rakṣaṇaś caiva śrāddhasya atithes tu vidhiś ca yaḥ |
bhūtānāṃ devatānāṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
evaṃ kāryaṃ ca samyak tu sarvam etad bhaviṣyati |
evaṃ datvā varaṃ teṣāṃ brahmā pitṛgaṇaiḥ saha |
bhūtānugrahakṛd devaḥ sa cacāra yathāsukham || iti |
tathā kratusmṛti-bhaviṣyatpurāṇayoḥ |
sadaivaṃ bhojayec chrāddhaṃ tatpūrvvaṃ ca pravartayet |
anyathā hy avalumpanti sadevāsurarākṣasāḥ ||
atra viśvān devān uddiśya kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddhāṅgabhūtaṃ karma daivam ity ucyate | tena sahitaṃ sadaivaṃ, sadaiveti niyamena tatpūrvaṃ daivapūrvaṃ pravartayet kuryāt | yadi tan na kriyate tadā śrāddhaphalaṃ rakṣāṃsy apaghnanti svayaṃ vā ādadatte | atra khalu daivena karmaṇā saha vartate śrāddhaśabdavācyaṃ karmeti samāsārthāvagamāt śrāddhād bhedenāpradhānatvena ca daivasya vyapadeśād aṅgatvam avagamyate | śrāddharakṣārthatvābhidhānenopakārakatvāvagamāt | manusmṛtāv api |
daivakāryād dvijātīnāṃ pitṛkāryaṃ viśiṣyate |
daivaṃ hi pitṛkāryasya parvam āpyāyanaṃ smṛtam ||
[136]        teṣām ārakṣabhūtaṃ tu pūrvaṃ daivaṃ niyojayet |
rakṣāṃsi hi vilumpanti śrāddham ārakṣavarjitaṃ ||
asyārthaḥ | pitṛkārye viśiṣyate pradhānatayā śreṣṭhatāṃ labhate | katham ity ata āha daivaṃ hi pitṛkāryasya pūrvaṃ kriyamāṇaṃ vighnakārakarākṣasanivāraṇadvāreṇāpyāyananirvāhikaṃ bhavatīti | anenāpi vaiśvadevikam aṅgakarmaiveti jñāyate, pitrādidaivatyamātāmahadaivatya - pradhānadvayavatyamāvāsyāśrāddhaprayoge “tantraṃ vā vaiśvadevikam” iti yājñavalkyena pradhānapravṛttatvābhidhānāc ca tatpūrvaṃ pravartayed ity uktaṃ | ato vakṣyamāṇān padārthān pratyekaṃ vaiśvadevasthāne kṛtvā pitṛsthāne kuryāt | tathā ca devalaḥ |
yatra tat kriyate karmma paiṭake brāhmaṇān prati |
tat sarvaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ vaiśvadaivatyapūrvakaṃ ||
paitṛke pitṛdevatye karmaṇi śrāddha ity arthaḥ |
viṣṇudharmottare ’pi |
ādau samarcayed viprān vaiśvadevaniveśitān |
niveśitāṃś ca pitrarthe tataḥ paścāt samarcayet ||
vṛddhaśātātape ’pi |
udaṅmukhas tu devānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ |
pradadyāt pārvaṇaśrāddhe daivapūrvaṃ vidhānataḥ ||
pārvaṇagrahaṇād amāvāsyāśrāddha eva vaiśvadevapūrvakatvaṃ niyamyate | ekoddiṣṭābhyudayikanityaśrāddheṣu vaiśvadevikābhāvo vakṣyate bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
tapyamānās tapas tīvraṃ proṣitā brahmaśāsanāt |
viśvedevās tu rakṣārthe pitṛyajñe ca sarvadā |
ataḥ pūrvaṃ pradātavyaṃ tebhyo ’nnaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi ||
annagrahaṇaṃ gandhādīnām apy upalakṣaṇārtham | evaṃ sarveṣāṃ śrāddhasaµbandhināṃ padārthānāṃ vaiśvadevapūrvatvaprāptau kvacid apavādaḥ pradṛśyate brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
paścād visarjayed devān pūrvaṃ paitāmahān dvijān |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ saha devaiḥ kramaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
paitāmahagrahaṇaṃ paitraprapaitāmahayor apy upalakṣaṇārthaṃ |
śātātapasmṛti-viṣṇudharmottarayoḥ |
viśvedevaniviṣṭānāṃ caramaṃ hastadhāvanaṃ |
visarjanaṃ ca nirdiṣṭaṃ teṣu rakṣā yataḥ sthitā |
sarvam anyat pradātavyam ādau teṣāṃ narādhipa ||
'caramaṃ' pāścātyam | 'hastadhāvanaṃ' bhojanānnāvaliptahastakṣālanārthajaladānam |
tad evam etad brahmavaivartādyukaṃ viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ śrāddhe devatātvaṃ nityaśrāddhādāv apohyate | āha śātātapaḥ |
nityaśrāddham adaivaṃ syād ekoddiṣṭaṃ tathaiva ca |
mātṛśrāddhaṃ tu yugmaiḥ syād adevaṃ prāṅmukhaiḥ pṛthak |
yojayed devapūrvaṇi śrāddhāny anyāni yatnataḥ ||
mātṛśrāddham atrābhyudayikapūrvavarti vivakṣitaṃ | brāhmaṇayugmatvaprāṅmukhopa-veśanaliṅgāt | tac ca yadāpi pitrā mātāmahādiśrāddhadvayāt pṛthagbhinnakāle ’nuṣṭhīyate tadānīm adaivaṃ vaiśvadaivarahitaṃ kartavyam | yadā punar ekasminn eva kāle śrāddhatrayaṃ kriyate tadā tantreṇāvṛttyā vā vaiśvadevikānuṣṭhānam iti śrāddhatrayaṃ prakurvīta | "tantraṃ vā vaiśvadevikam" iti
vaśiṣṭhavacanād avagantavyaṃ | tatra bhinnakāle śrāddhatrayānuṣṭhānaṃ |
pūrvedyur mātṛkaṃ śrāddhaṃ karmāhe pitṛkaṃ tathā |
uttaredyuḥ prakurvīta mātāmahagaṇasya tu ||
ityādibhir vacanair ābhyudayikaśrāddhaprakaraṇe darśayiṣyate | pṛthaganuṣṭīyamānamātṛśrāddhavyatiriktāny ābhyudayikanimittakāni śrāddhāni vikalpena vaiśvadevavihīnāni kāryāṇi | tathācābhyudayikaṃ prakṛtya mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe uktaṃ |
viśvadevavihīnaṃ tu kecid icchanti mānavāḥ |
yugmāś cātra dvijāḥ kāryas te pūjyāś ca pradakṣiṇam ||
kecidgrahaṇād atra vikalpo gamyate |
śātātapa-bhaviṣyatpurāṇayoḥ |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ tan naimittikam ucyate |
tad apy adaivaṃ kartavyam ayugmān āśayet dvijān ||
brahmapurāṇe |
pārvaṇaṃ daivapūrvaṃ syād ekoddiṣṭaṃ surair vinā |
tathā ekoddiṣṭaṃ daivahīnam ekārghyaikapavitrakam ityādi caikoddiṣṭapra[139]karaṇe vakṣyate | atraikoddiṣṭeṣu vaiśvadevikakarmābhāve kāraṇaṃ nāgarakhaṇḍe ’bhihitaṃ |
vṛtrāsurasya saṅgrāme pramītānāṃ divaukasāṃ |
nivāpāya gayākūpam avāpa tridaśeśvaraḥ ||
nagare nagare tatra caturdaśyāṃ mahālaye |
śrāddhāni śraddhayā kartum ārebhe vidhivad vṛṣā ||
śrāddhamantrair athāhūtān agniṣvāttādikān pitṝn |
anyān api svavargīyān pretān śastrahatāṃś ca tān ||
brāhmaṇānāṃ śarīreṣu viśataḥ saṃdadarśa saḥ |
viśvān devāṃs tu nāpaśyat tadarthaṃ ca vyalambata ||
atha śakreṇa sahasā dṛṣṭaḥ pṛṣṭaś ca nāradaḥ |
viśveṣāṃ prāha devānām anāgamanakāraṇaṃ ||
gayāyāṃ kurvataḥ śrāddhaṃ brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ |
viśvedevā mayā dṛṣṭāḥ tat kimarthaṃ vilambase ||
iti nāradavākyena kruddho vṛddhaśravās tataḥ |
viśvedevair vinā śrāddham ekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ ||
cakre śatakratus te ’pi pretāḥ prāpuḥ parāṃ gatiṃ |
evaṃvidhaṃ ca samayaṃ śrāvayāmāsa tān dvijān ||
viśve hi devāḥ śrāddhasya rakṣaṇārthaṃ vyavasthitāḥ |
tair vinā śrāddhasaṃrakṣā mayaiva hi vidhāsyate ||
[140]        avaiśvadevikaṃ śrāddham ata ūrddhvaṃ pravartatām |
iti tasmin vadaty ucair viśvedevāḥ samāyayuḥ ||
puruhūta purā hūtā vayaṃ devena vedhasā |
tvayā tu caramaṃ nātha vimṛśyaivaṃ prasīda naḥ ||
maghavann aghavanto ’dya na bhavāmaḥ kathaṃcana |
bahiḥ karoṣi śrāddhe ’dya kasmād asmān divaṣpate ||
iti bāṣpāmbukallolakṣālitaḥ kṣitiśokataḥ |
abhyarthanābhis teṣāṃ sa prasasāda na sīdatām ||
atha bāṣpoṣmaṇas tasmād aṇḍāny āsann anekaśaḥ |
jajñire puruṣās tebhyo lakṣaśeo rākṣasopamāḥ ||
viśvair devais tathādiṣṭās te jagadgrāsalālasāḥ |
asmadvirahitaṃ śrāddham āhāro vo bhaviṣyati ||
jātāḥ kṣity ūṣmajair aṇḍais tena kūṣmāṇḍasaṃjñitāḥ |
viharadhvam iti procya viśvedevās tiro dadhuḥ ||
atha kūṣmāṇḍasaṃkīrṇaviśvam ālokya viśvasṛk |
āhūya viśvān indraṃ ca vyavasthāṃ vidadhe vidhiḥ ||
ekoddiṣṭābhidhānāni sadā śrāddhāny ataḥ paraṃ |
viśvair devair vinā santu satyavāg astu vṛtrahā ||
avaiśvadevikaṃ śrāddham anyat kūṣmāṇḍatṛptaye |
bhavitā bhasmarekhābhiḥ pātrarakṣā ca yatra na ||
[141]        kūṣmāṇḍībhiratoragniḥ (?) salilair abhimantritaiḥ |
aprokṣitaḥ śrāddhapāko bhavadbhir bhujyatāṃ sukham |
ity uktvā prīṇaṇaṃ cakre kūṣmāṇḍānāṃ pitāmahaḥ |
agnaye vāyave ’rkāya pradadau pravibhajya tān ||
pitṛpakṣe caturdaśyām ekoddiṣṭavidhānataḥ |
śrāddhaṃ śastrahatasyaiva kāryaṃ nānyasya kasyacit ||
ity ūcivān viriñco ’ntardadhe sarvān visarjya tān |
avaiśvadevikaṃ śrāddham ekoddiṣṭaṃ tato ’bhavat ||
athaiteṣāṃ viśveṣāṃ nāmāny ucyante | tatra tāvan mahābhārate ||
viśve nāgnimukhā devāḥ saṃkhyātāḥ pūrvam eva te |
teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi bhāgārhāṇāṃ mahātmanām ||
sahaḥ kṛtir vipāpmā ca puṇyakṛt pāvanas tathā |
grāṣṇikṣamaḥ samūhaś ca divyabhānus tathaiva ca ||
vivasvān vīryavān hrīmān kīrtimān kṛta eva ca |
vidhyarthaḥ somapūrvaś ca sūryaśrīś ceti nāmataḥ ||
somāya sūryaḥ sāvitro dattātmā puṇḍarīyakaḥ |
uṣṇīlābho nabhodaś ca viśvāyur dīptir eva ca ||
samuhanaḥ suveṣaś ca yamāriḥ śaṅkaro bhavaḥ |
īśaḥ kartā kṛtitir dhātā bhuvano divyakarmakṛt ||
gaṇitaḥ pañcavīryaś ca prācīno rañjitas tathā |
saptakṛt somavarcāś ca viśvakṛt kavir eva ca ||
anugoptā sugoptā ca naptā ceśvara eva ca |
[142]        jitātmā munivīryaś ca dṛptaromā prabhaṅkaraḥ ||
atikarmā pratītaś ca pradātā cāṃśumāṃs tathā |
śailābhaḥ paramakrodhau dhāroṣṇī bhūpatis tathā ||
sajīvo vikarī caiva viśvedevāḥ sanātanāḥ |
kīrtitās te mahābhāgāḥ kālasya gatigocarāḥ ||
ete ca mahābhāratoktå sahakṛtyādināmāno viśvedevāḥ yathoktair nāmabhiḥ saṃbuddhyādivibhaktyantaiḥ pratipadārthaṃ saµkīrtya śrāddhe samuccayena viniyojyāḥ | saṃkṣepeṇa vā sakṛt sarvān api buddhāv āropya sahakṛtyādayo vikarīparyantā viśvedevā idaṃ vaḥ pādyam ityādiprakāraḥ prayogo vidheyaḥ | ete ca parasparam eva samuccīyante na tu bṛhaspatyādyuktapurūrava-ārdravādibhiḥ saheti | yatas teṣāṃ nāmāni vakṣyāmi bhāgāhāṇāṃ mahātmanām ity anena bhāgaśabdavācyahaviḥsaṃbandhavidhāyakena śāstreṇa teṣām iti sarvanāmarūpaikapadopāttānām eteṣām eva sahakṛtyādīnāṃ parasparasāpekṣāṇām eva prayoganirvartakatvam avagamyate |
nanu śāstrāntaravihitapurūrava-ārdravādisāpekṣāṇām iti na vācyaṃ | iṣṭiśrāddhe kratur dakṣa ityādinā vacanena viśeṣavihitapurūrava-ārdravādibhir eva sarveṣāṃ śrāddhānām avaruddhatvāt sahakṛtyādayo na kvacid avakāśaṃ labhanta iti na vaiśvadevike devatātvena kīrtanāha iti | ānarthakyaprahatānāṃ viparītaṃ balābalam iti nyāyāt sāmānyato vihitā api viśeṣato vihitān purūrava-ārdravādīn bādhitvā devatātvena niviśante | te ca viśeṣagrahaṇābhāvāt sarveṣv api śrāddheṣv iti manyamānaṃ prativiśeṣagrahaṇam ucyate | prakṛtau vā dviruktatvād iti nyāyāt prakaraṇāt pārvaṇa eva [143] niviśante | na pārvaṇavikṛtibhūteṣu karmāṅgādiśrāddheṣu |
nanv atideśād bhaviṣyati |
na | ātideśikaiḥ kratvādibhir bādhāt | tad evaṃ pārvaṇaśrāddhe sahakṛtyādīnāṃ vaiśvadevike karmaṇi purūrava-ārdravābhyāṃ saha vikalpena devatātvaṃ siddham | athaiteṣāṃ yair nāmabhiḥ karmasamāvāyaḥ prasiddhas tāny ucyante | bṛhaspatismṛtau |
kratur dakṣo vasuḥ satyaḥ kāmaḥ kālas tathaiva ca |
dhuriś ca rocanaś caiva tathā caiva purūravāḥ |
ārdravaś ca daśaite tu viśvedevāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
ete eṣu śrāddheṣu vibhajya viniyojyāḥ |
uktaṃ bṛhaspatismṛtāv eva |
iṣṭiśrāddhe kratur dakṣaḥ satyo nāndīmukhe vasuḥ |
naimittike kālakāmau kāmye ca dhurirocanau |
purūravā ārdravaś ca pārvaṇe samudāhṛtau || iti |
śaṅkhasmṛtāv api |
iṣṭiśrāddhe kratur dakṣaḥ saṃkīrtyau vaiśvadevike |
nāndīmukhe satyavasū kāmye ca dhurirocanau ||
purūravārdravau caiva pārvaṇe samudāhṛtau |
naimittike kālakāmāv iti sarvatra kīrtayet || iti |
iṣṭiśrāddhaśabdenātra karmāṅgaṃ śrāddhaṃ gṛhyate | tathā cādhānasomayāganiṣekādi-saṃskārādibhūtam api śrāddhaṃ labhyate | karmāṅgaṃ ca śrāddhabhedaprakaraṇe vakṣyate |
[144]        niṣekakāle some ca sīmantonnayane tathā |
jñeyaṃ puṃsavane śrāddhaṃ kamāṅgaṃ vṛddhivat kṛtam || iti |
atra karmāṅgam iti paribhāṣyate | vṛddhivad iti nāndīmukhe daivatattvasvāhāśabdaprayogo dadhivadarādimiśrapiṇḍavattvādayo vṛddhiśrāddhadharmā atidiśyante | vihitaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ yājñavalkyādismṛtiṣu | evaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya vṛddhav nāndīmukhān pitṝn yajetetyādibhir vacanaiḥ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ ca vṛddhavaśiṣṭhādibhir darśitaṃ | putrajanmavivāhādau vṛddhiśrāddham udāhṛtam iti | atrābhyudayikaśrāddhaviśeṣe vṛddhiśrāddhaśabdaḥ paribhāṣyate | ādiśabdena garbhādhānapuṃsavanasīmantonnayana- vyatiriktānām annaprāśanacūḍākaraṇādisaṃskārakarmaṇāṃ grahaṇaṃ | garbhādhānādisaṃskāratrayāṅgabhūtasyābhyudayikasya karmāṅgaśabdenābhidhānāt | etac ca vṛddhiśrāddhād bhinnaṃ karmāṅgaṃ vṛddhivad iti pāraskare ’tideśe darśanāt | bhede hy atideśo bhavati | śrāddhabhedoddeśe bhedābhidhānāc ca | kāmyapārvaṇādibhedās tattatprakaraṇe vakṣyante |
nanu naimittike kālakāmāv ity uktaṃ ekoddiṣṭaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ naimittikaśabdenocyate, ekoddiṣṭaṃ ca yac chrāddhaṃ tan naimittikam ucyate ity evaṃ paribhāṣitatvāt | ekoddiṣṭaṃ daivahīnam ekārghyaikapavitrakam ity evaṃ caikoddiṣṭe vaiśvadevikaniṣedhāt kathaṃ kālakāmau | na ca nimitte bhavaṃ naimittikam ity evaṃ vyutpattyā navānnalābhādinimittavihitam api naimittikaśabdenābhidhātuṃ śakyaṃ | tasya sāmānyaśāstrasthānīyasya yogasya viśeṣaśāstrarūpiṇyā paribhāṣayā bādhasya nyāyyatvāt |
satyam evaṃ tathāpi prakṛte virodhāt yogam evāśritya naimittikaśabdena navānnalābhādinimittaviśeṣe kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddham ucyate | yad uktam ādityapurāṇe |
[145]        viśvedevau kratur dakṣaḥ sarvasv iṣṭiṣu kīrtitau |
nityaṃ nāndīmukhaśrāddhe vasusatyau ca paitṛke ||
navānnalābhe dvau devau kālakāmau sadaiva hi |
api kanyāgate sūrye śrāddhe ca dhurirocanau |
purūravārdravau caiva viśvedevau tu pārvaṇe || iti |
sarvāsv iṣṭiṣu sarveṣu karmāṅgaśrāddheṣu, navānnalābhe navānnalābhanimitte sati | atrāpi śrāddhavidhiḥ śrāddhakālaprakaraṇe vrīhiyavapākair ityādinā vakṣyate | smṛticandrikākāras tu navānnalābhe sati navānnabhojanopakramāt pūrvaṃ pitṛbhaktyā navānnena śrāddhe kriyamāṇe dvau kālakāmau vaiśvadevike karmaṇi devatātvena kīrtanīyāv ity āha |
etac ca nimittādhikāreṇa kriyamāṇānāṃ vaiśvadevikānvitānām uparāgādiśrāddhānām upalakṣaṇārthaṃ | kanyāgate sūrya iti bhādrapadāparapakṣe, etac ca kāmyaśrāddhopalakṣaṇārtham | tad evaṃ virodhād vacanāc ca naimittikaśabdena navānnalābhādinimitte kriyamāṇaṃ śrāddham ucyata iti siddhaṃ | atha vā naimittike kālakāmāv ity atra naimittikaśabdenaikoddiṣṭābhidhāyinā ekoddiṣṭayuktaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇam ucyate | tat hi ekoddiṣṭapārvaṇarūpapradhānadvayātmakaṃ ato viśvadevikaṃ tatrāstīti kālakāmayor vidhir yuktaḥ | śaṅkhadharahariharau punar evam āhatuḥ | kecit pratyabdam ekoddiṣṭam icchanti| anye tu pārvaṇaṃ | ato naimittikaśabdavācyasyaikoddiṣṭasya sthāne kriyamāṇaṃ kṣayāhikaṃ pārvaṇam api naimittikaśabdaṃ labdhum arhatīti tasmin kālakāmavidhir iti | iyaṃ vā anayor utprekṣā prāguktarītyā vacanārthavyavasthopapatter nāvicāryaṃ grahītavyā |
[146]        athedaṃ cintyate | kiṃ purūrava-ārdravayoḥ pārvaṇaprayoge samuccitayoḥ prayoga uta vikalpitayor iti | evam iṣṭiśrāddhe kratu-dakṣayoḥ, evaṃ nāndaumukhādivihita-satyavasvādiṣu yugmeṣv api vikalpanīyam | tatra tāvad vikalpitayor iti gṛhyate | vyavasthāpane kratur dakṣa iti pṛthag ekaikasya karmasaṃbandhāvagamāt | na tv ekaikasya pṛthagdevatātvāt dravyadevatāsaṃbandhabhedāt karmaṇa eva bhedaḥ syāt | ko vikalpasyāvakāśaḥ | ekakarmānvayinor hy ekadvāratve vikalpābhidhā | astu nāma karmabhedaḥ ekaprayogānanvayamātreṇa vikalpābhidhā | vastutas tu na devatābhedāt karmabhedaḥ | śabdāntarābhyāsasaṃkhyāguṇaprakriyānāmadheyānāṃ karmabhedakatvābhidhānāt |
nanu devatāpi guṇa iti bhedikā bhavitum arhati |
maivaṃ | na hi guṇa ity eva bhedakaḥ kiṃ tv ekasmin karmaṇy alabhamāno ’nvayam| anyathā dravyavikalpo devatāvikalpaḥ tayoḥ samuccayo vā kvacin na syāt | dṛśyate cāgnihotrādīnāṃ dravyādau vikalpaḥ samuccayaś ca | some ca devatāsamuccayaḥ | na ca vācyaṃ | yathā saptadaśaprājāpatyānityān ity atra pṛthaktvaniveśinyā saṃkhyayā dravyadevatāsaṃbandhabheda evam atrāpi devatābhedena saṃbandhabhedāt karmabheda iti | yata evaṃ manyamāno bhavān indravāyvādyanekadevatānvitaṃ somayāgam eva smaratu |
nanu smṛta evaṃ kiṃ tu tatrendravāyvādīnām ekayāgasaṃbandhitvenāvagatadravya-saṃbandhāvagamāt na karmabhedakatvaṃ | iha tu na tatheti bhedakatvam iti |
na | utpattivākye hi śrūyamāṇo guṇo bhedakaḥ |
nanūtpanne karmaṇi guṇaṃ vidhātuṃ pravṛtte vākye tasya hi karmabhedakatvena kvāpi vikalpasamuccayau syātāṃ |
ato vikalpitayoḥ prayoga iti prāpte ucyate |
yat tatra kriyate karma [147] paitrikair brāhmaṇān prati |
tat sarvaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ vaiśvadevikapūrvakam ||
ityādiṣu devalādivākyeṣu vaiśvadevikaṃ kartavyam ity evaṃvidhe utpattividhāv avagate viśveṣāṃ devānām ekapadopādānād anyonyasahitānāṃ devatātve ’vagate sati teṣāṃ samuccayo 'vagamyamāno na guṇavākyasthena pṛthakprayogeṇa bādhituṃ śakyate |
nanv ekapadopādānena svarasataḥ sarveṣām eva viśveṣāṃ sāhityam avagatam | tac ced vyavasthāvacanena tricaturādiṣu viśvedeveṣu bhagnaṃ tadā bhagnam evāsya sāhityapratipādanaparatvam iti kathaṃ samuccayaḥ |
ucyate | sāmānyato ’vagamyamāno vacanārtho yāvaty aṃśe vacanāntareṇa bādhitas tad anyasminn aṃśe vyavatiṣṭhatīti samuccitayor devatātvam |
astv evam idaṃ tu saṃdihyate | kim ekaikaṃ svena svena nāmnā pṛthagvibhaktyantenoddiśyaikaiko vaiśvadevikapadārtho ’nuṣṭheyaḥ uta dvau dvau dvandvasamāsaprayuktena nāmadvayenoddiśyeti | tadartham idaṃ vicintyate | kiṃ sahitayor devatātvam uta devatayoḥ sāhityam iti | yadi devatayoḥ sāhityaṃ tadā pratyekaṃ devatātvāt pratyekam uddeśe tatsaṃpūrvatvāt pṛthak pṛthak caturthyādiprayogaṃ kṛtvā padārtho ’nuṣṭheyaḥ | uktaṃ hi | etasyā iti saṃkalpa uddeśo ’trābhidhīyate iti | yadā tu sahitayor devatātvaṃ tadā purūrava-ārdravasaṃjñakayor agnisomayor ivaikadevatātvarūpādhiṣṭhānabhūtatvād dvandvasamāsenoddeśaṃ kṛtvā prayogo vidheyaḥ | tatra “dṛṣṭiśrāddhe kratur dakṣaḥ saṃkīrtyo vaiśvadevike” ityevaṃvidhena śaṅkhādivacanena devatāvidhiḥ, vaiśvadevikapūrvakam ityevaṃvidhena devatādivacanena karmavidhiḥ, kramavidhir vā vaiśvadevikaśabdas tatpratyakṣanyāyena nāmadheyam iti vā manyamānaḥ ekaikasya pṛthagvibhaktyantena nāmnoddeśam āha | na ca vācyaṃ | taddhitacaturthyādividhāyakabhāvārthā[148]nām aśravaṇāt kathaṃ devatāvidhiḥ | itthaṃ vaiśvadevike ity anenānūdite bhāvārthe kratudakṣādīnāṃ vidhau teṣāṃ dravyarūpatvābhāvāt pariśeyyād devatātvena viniveśe taddhitacaturthyādīnām anupayogāt | evaṃ prāpte ’bhidhīyate | yatra taddhitabhāvārthavidhāyakās tatra na vidhir anyatra vidhir ity etat kasya hṛdayaṃ praveśaṃ labhet |
nanu yatraite sarve tasya kramaparatvam avagamyate | yāvat yāvad vidheyāntaram upādīyate tāvat tāvad vidhiśaktis tatra tatra saṃcaratīti nyāyāt |
satyam evaṃ | karmavidhau tu sarvaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ karma vidhātuṃ śakyam iti tatra devanāvidhau na doṣaḥ | tatra ced vihitā devatāḥ dvayor ekapadopādānāt sāhityaviśiṣṭayor devatātvam ity agnīṣomavat purūrava-ārdravayor dvandvasamāsenoddiśya prayogo vidheyaḥ | nāmadheyatāpi tatprakhyaśāstrāntaropapādanān nirastaiveti |
nanu purūrava-ārdravaprabhṛtinām advandvanirdeśo vivakṣita eva nāmadvayasyāprayojyatvāt tat kutas tadanurodhād vikalpasamuccayanyāsasamāsānāṃ prasaṅgaḥ | yadi punaḥ prayojyaṃ syāt tadā etannāmadayānabhijñasya devatānabhijñatvāc chrāddhe nādhikāra eva syāt | darśitaś ca tasyāpy adhikāraḥ |
utpattināma vai teṣāṃ na vidurye dvijātayaḥ |
ayam uccāraṇīyas taiḥ ślokaḥ śraddhāsamanvitaiḥ ||
āgacchantu mahābhāgā viśvedevā mahābalāḥ |
ye yatra vihitāḥ śrāddhe sāvadhānā bhavantu te ||
ity anena śāstreṇa yadi ca nāmadvayaṃ prayojyaṃ syāt tadā viśvedevāsa āgata śṛṇu tām imaṃ havam ityādimantreṇa śrūyamāṇaṃ bahuvacaṇaṃ virudhyeta | tasmād viśvedevā ity etacchabdollikhanair vā arghyādidānaṃ kartavyaṃ |
nanv anuṣṭhā[149]nakāle nāmadvayānuccāraṇe tatkathanasya kiṃ prayojanam iti cet samudradhyānavad anucintanamātram iti brūmaḥ |
atrocyate | nāmadvayāprayoge ’pi vyavasthāvākyasya devatāsaṃkhyāpratipādana-paratvād vastuvṛtyā dvayor eva devatātvāt mantrasya bahuavacanavirodhas tadavastha eveti | athaitad bhūyaḥ pramukhasya bhiṃṇasya (?) vivakṣitatvāt pūjārthatvād vā na virodhaḥ | tarhi nāmadvayaprayoge ’pi tasyaiva vivakṣitatvād avirodha iti kratudakṣādināmadvandvāni prayojyāny eva | evaṃ ca sati “iṣṭiśrāddhe kratur dakṣaḥ saṃkīrtyo vaiśvadevike” iti saṃkīrtanavidhir abhyupapannaḥ syāt | yat tu harihareṇāvivakṣitatvābhidhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tad dvitvābhiprāyeṇa na nāmābhiprāyeṇa | ato nāmadvayaprayoge ’pi bahuvacanam eva prayoktavyaṃ na dvivacanam iti tasyābhiprāyo veditavyaḥ | yad atra smṛticandrikākāreṇa bahuvacanaprayoge kāraṇam uktaṃ viśvān devān āvāhayiṣya iti pṛcchatīti smṛtipurāṇeṣu bahuṣu bahuvacanāntaprayogadarśanād iti tad astu nāma | mantrasthānīyasya praiṣasya ekaikaprayogasamavāyāt preṣavākyābhiprāyeṇedam uktam ity abhiprāyāntarotprekṣāsaṃbhavāt | yat tu
niraṅguṣṭhaṃ gṛhītvā tu viśvān devān samāhayet | ityādibahuvacanaprayogadarśanād ity uktaṃ, tat sarvaprayogasamavāyiviśvedevānusandhānenāpi vidhivākye bahuvacanopapatter asādhakameva | itaś cāsādhakaṃ |
naimittike kālakāmau kāmye ca dhurirocanau |
ityādidvivacanāntatayāpi prayogadarśanāt | itaś ca bahuvacanāntaprayogaḥ | prāyeṇa viśvaśabdasya bahvarthavācakatvāt bahuvacanāntatayā prayoge prāpte tadviśeṣaṇībhūtānāṃ purūrava-ādravādiśabdānāṃ tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena prapujyamānānāṃ bahuvacanāntatā uciteti | idam ucyate |
[150]        kim ekaike brāhmaṇapurobhāgasthāpitaikaikapātrastham annaṃ pṛthak pṛthag viśvān devān uddiśya tyājyaṃ uta sakṛd evoddiśyeti | tatra devataikyāt sahatyāgo lāghavād iti nyāyāt sakṛd iti |
athocyeta | pṛthakparikalpanasya dṛṣṭārthatvānurodhāt pṛthaktyāga iti |
tan na | tasya pṛthagbhojanārthatvenāpi dṛṣṭārthatvanirvāhāt | ekapātrapariveṣitānāṃ nānājātīyānām annānāṃ sakṛd buddhau niveśavan nānāpātrapariveṣitānām api niveśasambhavāt sakṛttyāgopapattiḥ | evaṃ ca parikalpanāṅgānurodhena pradhānāvṛttir na bhaviṣyati | evaṃ prāpta ucyate | āpastambādibhir brāhmaṇānām āhavanīyasthānīyatvābhidhānāt tatprāptiparyantas tāvad gandhādityāgavad annatyāgaḥ kāryaḥ | te ca pṛthaṅniveśitāḥ pṛthaṅniveśitāny annāni na sakṛt prāpayituṃ śakyante | prāptīnāṃ pṛthag iṣṭatvāt | tasmāt pṛthak tyājyāny annāni | agatyā ca tyāgāvṛttir na doṣāya ity ekaikasminn adhiṣṭhāne purūrava-ārdravasaṃjñakebhyo viśvebhyo devebhya idam annaṃ na mametyādiprayogais tyāgo vidheyaḥ | atraiteṣu vicāreṣv evam avasthānām uddeśa ity etāvaty evāṃśe tātparyaṃ | yat punar evaṃbhūtaḥ prayogo ’py evaṃbhūta ity upanyastaṃ tatprayogasya vicāraprayojanabhūtatvābhiprāyeṇa na sākṣād vicāryatvābhiprāyeṇeti | ato devatāsvarūpavicārāya prakṛtiprakaraṇe saṅgatir vicāraṇam iti |
iti vaiśvadevikadevatānirṇayaḥ |
atha vikirabhuktocchiṣṭayor devatāḥ |
manu-viṣṇū |
asaṃskṛtapramītānāṃ tyāgināṃ kulayoṣitām |
ucchiṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ syād darbheṣu vikiraś ca yaḥ ||
[151]
hārītaḥ |
arūḍhadantā ye nāma mṛtā garbhād viniḥsṛtāḥ |
mṛtā ye cāpy asaṃskārās teṣāṃ bhūmau pradīyate ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
annaprakiraṇaṃ yat tu manuṣyaiḥ kriyate bhuvi |
tena tṛptim upāyānti ye piśācatvam āgatāḥ ||
ye cādantāḥ kule bālāḥ kriyāyogyā hy asaṃskṛtāḥ |
vipannās te ’nnavikirasaṃmārjanajalāśinaḥ ||
manuḥ |
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigatam ajihmasyāśaṭhasya ca |
dāsavargasya tat pitrye bhāgadheyaṃ pracakṣate ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
prāk saṃskārāt pramītānāṃ saprekṣāṇām iti śrutiḥ |
bhāgadheyaṃ manuḥ prāha ucchiṣṭoccheṣaṇe ubhe ||
uccheṣaṇaṃ bhūmigataṃ vikiraṃ lepanodakam |
anupreteṣu visṭajed aprajānām anāyuṣām ||
iti vikirabhuktocchiṣṭayor devatāḥ |
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaśrīmahādevasya sakalaśrīkaraṇādhipatipaṇḍita-
śrīhemādriviracite caturvargacintāmaṇau pariśeṣakhaṇḍe śrāddhakalpe
devatānirṇayaprakaraṇaṃ nāma tṛtīyo ’dhyāyaḥ |
[152]
atha caturtho ’dhyāyaḥ |
śrāddhadeśakathanaprakaraṇam |
yat puṇyācaritapravāhasaralasrotasvinīnirmita-
śrīmattīrtharasāvagāhavigalatpāpopatāpo janaḥ |
romāñcair upacīyate sa sukṛtau hemādrir uddhāradhīḥ
kartuṃ śrāddhapadārthaniścayam atho yatnaṃ samālambate ||
tad evam ukte prakaraṇe śraddhayā pitrādirūpadevatoddeśena brāhmaṇabhojanādi śrāddhapadārtha ity etat siddhavat kṛtyoddeśyapitrādinirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ | athāmum eva śrāddhaśabdārthaṃ vicārya niścetuṃ prakaraṇāntaram ārabhyate | tatra tāvac chrāddhanirvacanena vicāryo ’rthaḥ prastūyate |
bṛhaspatismṛtau |
saṃskṛtaṃ vyañjanāḍhyaṃ ca payomadhughṛtānvitam |
śraddhayā dīyate yasmāt śrāddhaṃ tena nigadyate ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
śrāddhe śraddhā yato mūlaṃ tena śrāddhaṃ prakīrtitam |
tasmin prakriyamāṇe tu na kiṃcid vyarthatāṃ vrajet ||
mūlam iti śreṣṭham aṅgam | tathā ca kātyāyanakṛte śrāddhakalpe | śraddhānvitaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurvīteti vihitaṃ | na hy atra śraddhānimittatvena cotpadyate, kiṃ tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurvīta sa śraddhayānvitaḥ san kurvīteti śraddhāyāḥ śrāddhāṅgatvam ucyate | tathā ca bārhaspatya-pārāśareṇa tṛtīyā[153]vibhaktyā śrāddhaviśeṣaṇatvena śraddhākā |
deśe kāle ca pātre ca vidhinā haviṣā ca yat |
tilair darbhaiś ca mantraiś ca śrāddhaṃ syāt śraddhayānvitam ||
ata eva manusmṛtau śraddhāyogena vaiśiṣṭam uktam |
yad yad dadāti vidhivat samyak śraddhāsamanvitaḥ |
tatpitṛṇāṃ ca bhavati paratrānantam akṣayam || iti |
nandipurāṇe ’pi |
śraddhā pavitraṃ sarveṣāṃ pavitrāṇāṃ prakīrtitam |
śraddhaiva dharmaṃ paramaṃ pāvanaṃ caiva sarvadā ||
śraddhābhūtāni bhūtāni pavitrāṇi sadaiva tu |
śraddhā tu mātā bhūtānāṃ śraddhā śrāddheṣu śasyate ||
ata eva yamasmṛtau śraddhābhāvena vaiguṇyam uktam |
vidhihīnam amṛṣṭānnaṃ mantrahīnam adakṣiṇam |
aśraddhayā hutaṃ dattaṃ tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate ||
baudhāyano ’py āha |
śraddhayā bodhyate buddhiḥ śraddhayā śodhyate manaḥ |
śraddhayā prāpyate brahma śraddhā pāpavimocanī |
tasmād aśraddadhānasya havir nāśnanti devatā || iti |
nanu sarvasyāpi satkarmaṇaḥ śraddhāṅgam, tat kena viśeṣeṇāsyaiva śrāddhaśabdo ’yam |
tathā ca viṣṇudharmottare |
[154]        śraddhānvitena manasā yad yat kiṃcit samācaret |
tat tad bahuphalaṃ tasya jāyate lokayor dvayoḥ ||
śraddhāmayo ’yaṃ puruṣo yo yacchabdaḥ sa eva saḥ |
devaśraddhā narā devāḥ kathitā devabhājinaḥ ||
pitṛśraddhāś ca pitaro daityaśraddhā diteḥ sutāḥ |
pāpaśraddhās tathā pāpā vijñeyā narakaṃgamāḥ ||
tasmāc chraddhāṃ samāsthāya dharmaṃ dharmī samācaret |
puṇyaṃ bahuphalaṃ tasya śraddhām āsthāya yat kṛtam ||
śraddhā pavitraṃ paramaṃ hi loke
śraddhā śarīraṃ puruṣasya coktam |
śraddhāṃ samāsthāya narās tu kṛtvā
puṇyāni puṇyāṃ gatim āpnuvanti ||
bṛhatpārāśare |
śraddhāpūtaṃ pradātavyaṃ pātre dānam ayācitam |
yācite ’pi hi dātavyaṃ pūtaṃ svaṃ śraddhayā dhanam || iti |
tad evaṃ sarvasyāpi satkarmaṇaḥ śraddhārūpāṅgayuktatvāt katham asyaiva śrāddhaśabdo nāmadheyam iti |
ucyate | trividhā śabdasyārtheṣu vṛttiḥ rūḍhyā yogena yogarūḍhyā ceti | tatra yaḥ samudāyaśaktyā vartate sa tatra prarūḍha iva bhavatīti rūḍhyā vartate | yas tv avayavaśaktyā yāvad yogaṃ varttate sa tu avayavārthayogād vartata iti yogena vartata ity ucyate | yas tv avayavaśaktyā [155] vartamāno 'pi niyataprayogāt yāvat yogaṃ vartate sa yogarūḍhyā vartata ity ucyate | tathā ca śrāddhaśabdo yogarūḍhyā vartata ity asyaiva nāmadheyaṃ |
nanu vipratiṣedhān na yogarūḍhir ghaṭate | yady avayavaśaktyā vartate na tarhi samudāyaśaktyeti vipratiṣedhaḥ |
satyaṃ | avayavaśaktyaiva vartamānaḥ prayoganiyamāt saṅkucitāvayatraśaktitvena nātyantayogaparatantra iti rūḍhyeva vartata iti gauṇavādād avirodhaḥ |
kecit punar āhuḥ | pratyakṣaparokṣānivṛttiyogenaiva śabdo ’rthe vartate | yatrāvikṛtair evāvayavair vartate | yathā nighaṇṭuśabdas tatraiva so 'pi parokṣayā vṛttyā vartate ity ucyate | tatrāyaṃ śrāddhaśabdaḥ pratyakṣayaiva vṛttyā śraddhāyogena karmaviśeṣe vartate | kiṃ tu prayoganiyamāt saṅkucitāvayavaśaktitvān nānyatra vartate | yathā gośabdo gacchatīti gaur ity anayā vyutpattyā pravartamāno yogiko ’pi na aśvādiṣu vartate evaṃ dravyādāv aprasiddheḥ śrāddhaśabdaḥ karmaṇo nāmadheyaṃ |
nanu karmaṇy api prasiddhabhāvāt vidheś ca pravṛttyarthatvād guṇavidhitve ca guṇāṃśe puruṣapravṛttiviśeṣajanakatvena prayojanavattvāt dravyābhidhānaṃ bhavet | tad uktaṃ |
prasiddher balavattvena prayojanavaśena ca |
adhikatvāt pravṛtteś ca guṇarūpaṃ vidhīyate || iti |
athocyate | na tāvat karmaṇi guṇavidhiḥ samabhivyāhṛtakarmaviśeṣāśruteḥ | na ca karotivākye bhāvanāmātre vidhiḥ | siddharūpasya sākṣād bhāvanānanvayāt | nāpi phale guṇavidhiḥ | āśrayarūpakarmānanvitasya phalānvayāsambhavāt | praṇayanādyāśrita eva hi godohanādiḥ phale vidhīyate|
tan na | nāmadheyatve ’pi hi kasya nāmadheyam iti paryanuyogasāmyāt | nāmāpi hi na bhāvanāmātrasya saṃmatam | sarvakarmanāma[156]tvāpatteḥ | atha
pramītasya pituḥ putraiḥ śrāddhaṃ deyaṃ prayatnataḥ |
ye yajanti pitṝn devān brāhmaṇāṃś ca hutāśanān ||
ityādiprakaraṇāmnātavākyāvagatasya karmaṇo nāmadheyaṃ manyase tadā tadavagata eva karmaṇi phale vāyaṃ guṇavidhir iti mamāpi niḥsaraṇasaraṇiḥ sulabheva | śraddhāyogalakṣaṇaṃ nāma pravṛttinimittaṃ ca tulyam eva |
śrāddhamāsaṃ tu kartavyam iti vedavidāṃ sthitiḥ |
ity atra dravyasāmānādhikaraṇyadarśanāc ca gavādivat śrāddhaṃ deyam iti deyatvāvagamāc ca | na hi dānādikarmaṇo deyatvaṃ saṃbhavati |
lākṣaṇiko dravye prayoga iti cen na | lakṣaṇāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt | vākyāntarāvagate ca karmaṇi guṇe vidhīyamāne na matvarthalakṣaṇā | padaikavākyatve hi sā na vākyaikavākyatve, nityanaimittikakāmyādivyapadeśo ’pi camasagodohanādiṣv iva dravye ’pi na virudhyate | tasmān naitat karmanāmadheyam iti prāpta ucyate | tad arthaśāstrāntarasadbhāvād agnihotrapadavan nāmatā | yathā hi yad agnaye ca prajāpataye ca sāyaṃ juhotītyādinā prakaraṇāmnātena śāstrāntareṇa vidhitsitadevatāprāptiḥ evam atrāpi māṣayavatilagodhūmamāṃsādidravyaṃ pitrādibhyaḥ śraddhayā deyam ityādinā śrāddhaśabdavihitadravyaprāptir asty eva | prasiddhyabhāvāc ca na guṇavidhiḥ | prasiddher eva hi somenetyādir guṇavidhiḥ | yat punaḥ kvacit śrāddhaśabdasyāmaśabda-sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ talakṣaṇāvṛttyā | deyatvaṃ ca dravyasya lakṣaṇayā dravye śrāddhaśabdaṃ prayujyoktaṃ | nityanaimittikādivyapadeśaś ca karmaṇi bhūyasīṃ prasiddhiṃ gataḥ svarasavṛttyā na karmapakṣapātaṃ jahāti |
nanu sarvatra pradhānakarmaṇo yāgādeḥ karaṇatvān nāmatve śrāddham iti dvitīyāntatā na syāt | uktaṃ hi | vidhāne cānuvāde ca yāgaḥ karaṇam iṣyate iti |
[157]        ucyate | yathā dārvādidvaidhībhāvaphaloddeśapravṛttāyāṃ paraśūdyamana-nipatanātmikāyāṃ karaṇībhūto ’pi paraśus taddārudhvaṃsasaṃyogavibhāga-paramparārūpakriyāphalabhāgitvād anīpsitakarmabhūtas tathā svargādiphaloddeśa-pravṛttāyāṃ bhāvanāyāṃ karaṇībhūto ’pi yad yajyādyarthas tadbhāvanānirvartyatvena karmety agnihotram ityādikaṃ tadavasthāvivakṣāyāṃ dvitīyāntatopapadyate | bhāvanāsvarūpanirvartyasya yāgāder nirvarttakabhāvanāyāḥ svargādiphalakatvāvasthāyāṃ karaṇatvam ity ekaṃ prati karmatvakaraṇatvayor na virodhaḥ | svataḥ pūrvāparībhūtasya sādhyaikasvabhāvasya dhātvarthasyaiva bhāvanārūpeṇa karotyarthena sākṣād anvayaḥ | siddhasya dravyāder bhāvārthadvāreṇeti vyavahitatvāj jaghanya iti karmaṇo nāmadheyaṃ śrāddhaśabdaḥ | āpastambavacanād api karmanāmatvam avagamyate | athaitan manuḥ śrāddhaśabdaṃ karma provāca prajāniḥśreyasārthaṃ tatra pitaro devatā iti | evaṃ sthite vicāryate | kim idaṃ piṇḍadānasya brāhmaṇabhojanasya vā kevalasya nāmadheyaṃ uta prakṛtānāṃ piṇḍadānabrāhmaṇabhojanāgnaukaraṇarūpāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ samudāyasyeti | tatra tāvat karkopādhyāyaḥ piṇḍadānasyaiva nāmadheyam ity āha | tathā hi | pradhānasya hi idaṃ nāmadheyam adhikāravākyagatatvāt | piṇḍadānaṃ ca pradhānatayā asmin prakaraṇe ’vagamyate | tathā ca liṅgadarśanaṃ |
api naḥ sa kule bhūyāt yo no dadyāt trayodaśīṃ |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca || iti |
atra khalu śrāddhadevatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ piṇḍaviṣaye prārthanātiśayadarśanāt piṇḍadānaṃ pradhānam avagamyate | mahābhārate gayāyāṃ piṇḍe pradīyamāne piṇḍagrahaṇāya pitṝṇāṃ hastotthānasyābhidhānāc ca | tathā śrāddha[158]kartavyatāyāṃ vivakṣāyāṃ smṛtiṣu piṇḍanirvapaṇasya kartavyatoktā,
sahapiṇḍakriyāyāṃ tu kṛtāyām asya dharmataḥ |
anayaivāvṛtā kāryaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ sutaiḥ ||
ityādinā vacanena brāhmaṇabhojanāsaṃbhave piṇḍadānamātravidhānāc ca | tad evam etair liṅgadarśanaiḥ piṇḍadānasya prādhānyāvagamād asyaiva śrāddhaśabdo nāmadheyam iti |
nanu brāhmaṇabhojanasyāpi pitṛrūpapradhānadevatābhisaṃbandhāt prādhānyam asty eva |
maivaṃ | bhojanasya “yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe” iti vacanāt bhojanakriyāsāmarthyāc ca sākṣāt brāhmaṇasaṃbandhāt pitrarthatve pramāṇābhāvāt |
nanv evaṃ sati piṇḍapitṛyajñavad upacāraḥ pitryatvābhidhānaṃ syāt |
na | śrāddhārthabrāhmaṇārthatvena tadupapatteḥ |
nanu piṇḍadānamātre śrāddhaśabdārthe piṇḍābhojane ’pi śrāddham anena bhuktam iti prayogaḥ kathaṃ |
lakṣaṇayā śrāddhāṅgabhūtabhūjyartha iti brūmaḥ | tat siddhaṃ piṇḍadānasyaiva nāmadheyam iti | evaṃ ca kvacit prayoge piṇḍadānāniṣpattau prayoge vighne samutpanne sati piṇḍadānārthaṃ punar brāhmaṇabhojanābhyāvṛttiḥ pradhānārthatvād aṅgānāṃ | aṅgaṃ hi taddānasya brāhmaṇabhojanādi |
anye tv āhuḥ | brāhmaṇabhojanam eva pradhānam iti tasyaiva śrāddhaśabdānāmadheyam iti| tathācāpastambasūtre | athaitan manuḥ śrāddhaśabdaṃ karma provāca prajāniḥśreyasārthaṃ tatra pitaro devatā brāhmaṇās tv āhavanīyārthe iti | āhavanīyārthe āhavanīyakārye prakṣipyamāṇahavirdhāraṇalakṣaṇe vartate | na cābhuñjānās te tat kāryaṃ kartuṃ śakuvantīty arthāt brāhmaṇabhojanaparyantatāsya vākyasya bhavati | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
tato yathāsukhaṃ vācyaṃ bhuñjīraṃs te ’pi vāgyatāḥ | iti |
tad evaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanātmakaprayojyavyāpāraviṣayakasya prayojakavyāpārasya [159] gauṇāhavanīyaprāptiparyantatvāt adhigatahomābhidhānasya śrāddhaśabdo nāmadheyam iti viśvarūpa- medhātithi- vāmadeva-prabhṛtīnāṃ mataṃ | vihitaś cāyaṃ vyāpāraviśeṣaḥ phalasaṃbandhitvena śrāddhaprakaraṇe bahīṣv api smṛtiṣu |
ekaikam api vidvāṃsaṃ daive pitrye ca bhojayet |
puṣkalaṃ phalam āpnoti tasmāt yatnena bhojayet || iti |
śaunakagṛhye ’pi |
navāvarān śrotriyān bhojayed ayujo veti |
manusmṛtāv api |
dvau deve pitṛkārye trīn ekaikam ubhayatra vā |
bhojayet susamṛddho ’pi na prasajyeta vistare || iti |
tad evaṃ yajamānaniṣṭhasya brāhmaṇabhojanākhyasya prayojakavyāpārasya śrāddhaśabdo nāmadheyaṃ |
yat tu harihareṇa viśvarūpādīnāṃ matam upanyastaṃ prayojakavyāpārāt vinābhūtatvāt prayojyavyāpārasya brāhmaṇakarṭakaṃ prayojyavyāpārarūpam api bhojanaṃ śrāddhaśabdavācyaṃ | ateva śrāddhaṃ bhuktam aneneti śrāddhaśabdasya bhujisāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ dṛśyate | tathā | eṣām anyatamo yaś ca bhuñjīta śrāddham arcita ityādinirdeśaś ca | api ca jīvatpitṛkaśrāddhe jīvadbhiḥ piṇḍadānābhāve niyate bhojanavidhānaṃ bhojanatadanukūlavyāpārayoḥ prādhānyabodhakaṃ | vipravad vāpi tacchrāddhe svakaṃ pitaram āśayed iti | pitāmaho vā tacchrāddhaṃ bhuñjītetyādi |
tad etan matam ayuktam ivāvagamyate | yataḥ prayojyavyāpārasyādhikārivyāpāra-tvābhāvāt tasya phalāsaṃbandhe pradhānatvānupapattau kutaḥ pradhāna-karmābhidhā[160]yakaśrāddhaśabdābhidheyatvaṃ |
nanv evaṃ tarhi ṛtvigvyāpārāṇām āgneyādīnāṃ pradhānatvābhāve darśapūrṇamāsaśabdābhidheyatvaṃ na syāt |
maivaṃ | yajamānavyāpāratvenaiva vihitasyāgneyāder vākyāntaravihitayajamānāpādita-ṛtvigvyāpāranirvartyatve ’pi yajamānabhāvanārūpavyāpāranirvartyatvāt pradhānakarmābhidhāyakadarśapūrṇamāsa-śabdābhidheyatvaṃ na virudhyate | bhojanasya punar ayajamānavyāpāranirvartyasya yajamānavyāpāratve ’bhidhānāt yajamānanirvartyabhāvanāvyāpyatve ’pi na śrāddhaśabdābhidheyatvaṃ | yathā dānabhāvanāvidhau pratigrahasya vyāpyatvād bhāvanāntarbhāve ’pi na dānaśabdavācyatvam | atrāpi hi svasvatvanivṛttipūrvakaṃ parasvatvotpādanaṃ dānaśabdārthaḥ | parasvatvāpādanaṃ ca na pratigraham anāpādya siddhyati | pratigraheṇa hi parasvatvaṃ niṣpadyate | ataḥ pratigrahasyāpi dānabhāvanāvyāpyatvam asty eva | kiṃ tu yajamānavyāpāratvena vidhyabhāvād apradhānatvaṃ | pratigrahe yajamānaniyogāsaṃbhavāt | svavyāpāre hi puruṣaḥ kartṛtvena niyujyata iti nyāyāt | na ca pratigraho yajamānavyāpāra iti |
atha śrāddhadeśāḥ |
tatra manuḥ |
śuciṃ deśaṃ viviktaṃ ca gomayenopalepayet |
dakṣiṇāpravaṇaṃ caiva prayatnenopapādayet ||
avakāśeṣu cokṣeṣu nadītīreṣu caiva hi |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti dattena pitaraḥ sadā ||
yamasmṛtau |
dakṣiṇāpravaṇaṃ snigdhaṃ viviktaṃ śubhalakṣaṇam |
[161]        śuciṃ deśaṃ parīkṣyāśu gomayenopalepayet ||
agāreṣu vivikteṣu tīrtheṣu ca nadīṣu ca |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti dantena pitaraḥ sadā ||
nadītīreṣu tīrtheṣu svabhūmau ca prayatnataḥ |
upaddharanitambeṣu tathā parvatasānuṣu |
gomayenopalipteṣu vivikteṣu gṛheṣu ca ||
upaddharam atra parvatāntikam |
pṛthvīcandrodaye viṣṇudharme |
dakṣiṇāpravaṇe deśe tīrthādau ca gṛhe ’pi vā |
bhūsaṃskārādisaṃyukte śrāddhaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ || iti ||
skānde |
tulasīkānanacchāyā yatra yatra bhaved dvija |
tatra śrāddhaṃ pradātavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptihetave ||
devalo ’py āha |
śrāddhasya pūjito deśo gayā gaṅgā sarasvatī |
kurukṣetraṃ prayāgaś ca naimiṣaṃ puṣkarāṇi ca ||
nadītaṭeṣu tīrtheṣu śaileṣu pulineṣu ca |
vivikteṣu ca tuṣyanti datteneha pitāmahāḥ ||
śaṅkhasmṛtāv api |
gaṅgāyamunayos tīre payoṣṇy amarakaṇṭake |
narmadābāhudātīre bhṛguliṅge himālaye ||
gaṅgādvāre prayāge ca naimiṣe puṣkare tathā |
[162]        saṃnihatyāṃ gayāyāṃ ca dattam akṣayatāṃ vrajet ||
api jāyeta so ’smākaṃ kule kaścin narottamaḥ |
gayāśīrṣe vaṭe śrāddhaṃ yo no dadyāt samāhitaḥ ||
eṣṭavyā bahavaḥ putrā yady ekeo ’pi gayāṃ vrajet |
yajeta vāśvamedhena nīlaṃ vā vṛṣam utsṛjet || iti |
atha niṣiddhadeśāḥ |
pṛthvīcandrodaye skānde |
triśaṅkor varjayed deśaṃ sarvaṃ dvādaśayojanam |
uttareṇa mahānadyā dakṣiṇena tu kīkaṭāt ||
deśas traiśaṅkavo nāma śrāddhakarmaṇi varjitaḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
kāraskarāḥ kaliṅgāś ca sindhor uttaram eva ca |
praṇaṣṭāśramadharmāś ca deśā varjyaḥ prayatnataḥ || iti |
kāraskaro deśaviśeṣaḥ | kīkaṭo magadhaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
parakīyagṛhe yas tu svān pitṝṃs tarpayej jaḍaḥ |
tadbhūmisvāminas tasya haranti pitaro balāt ||
yamasmṛtau |
parakīyapradeśeṣu pitṝṇāṃ nirvapet tu yaḥ |
tadbhūmisvāmipitṛbhiḥ śrāddhakarma vihanyate ||
aṭavyaḥ parvatāḥ puṇyā nadyas tīrthāni yāni ca |
sarvāṇy asvāmikāny āhur na hi teṣu parigrahaḥ ||
[163]        rukṣaṃ kṛmiyutaṃ klinnaṃ saṃkīrṇāniṣṭagandhikaṃ |
deśaṃ tv aniṣṭaśabdaṃ ca varjayec chrāddhakarmaṇi ||
aniṣṭaśabdaṃ pratikūlaśabdam ity arthaḥ |
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaśrīmahādevasya sakalaśrīkaraṇādhipatipaṇḍita
śrīhemādriviracite caturvargacintāmaṇo pariśeṣakhaṇḍe śrāddhakalpe
śrāddhadeśakathanaṃ nāma caturtho ’dhyāyaḥ |
[164]
atha pañcamo’dhyāyaḥ |
śrāddhakālanirṇayaprakaraṇam |
nameror meror vā mahimaharaṇe dhaurikamitir
harīśaśrīramyaprakṛtir aharīśapratikṛtiḥ |
manīṣī hemādrir guṇagarimagambhīracaritaḥ
karotīha śrāddhe hitavihitakālaprakaraṇam ||
athādhikaraṇatvena kṛtsnaprayogāṅgatvena sārūpyād deśānantaraṃ śrāddhakālo ’bhidhīyate |
garuḍapurāṇe |
viśvaṃ sṛjaty avaty atti kālātmaiva janārdanaḥ |
ato ’rthaṃ kālam eveha paramaṃ kāraṇaṃ viduḥ ||
na hi kālam anāśritya loke vede ’pi kutracit |
vidhayaḥ pratiṣedhāś ca sambhavanti viśāṃpate ||
kāla eva tataḥ sādhyaḥ sādhanaṃ puṇyapāpayoḥ |
tasmāt karmāṇi puṣyeṣu kuryāt kāleṣu dhārmikaḥ |
nākāle viduṣā kiṃcit kartavyam iti niścayaḥ ||
puṇyeṣu puṇyasādhanatvenāmnāteṣu, kāleṣu kālāvayaveṣu |
nanv amūrtatvanityatvavibhutvair hetubhir niravayavatvāt kālasya katham avayavā ity etacchaṅkāparihāro devīpurāṇe |
nityo vibhuḥ sthitaḥ kālo hy avasthās tasya hetujāḥ |
nimittādiviśeṣaiḥ sa loke puṇyaphalapradaḥ || iti ||
[165]        asyārthaḥ | satyam asya na santy evāvayavāḥ svābhāvikāḥ yato ’yaṃ nityaḥ te cānityasyaiva bhavanti | yataḥ sāvayavaṃ nāmāvayavajanyam | anyaṃ ca
vināśād anyad vinaśyaty eva |
nanv avayavasamavetamātraṃ sāvayavaṃ na tu tajjanyaṃ |
maivam | sāmānyasyāpi sāvayavatvaprasaṅgāt | kiṃ ca kālasyāvayavās tajjātīyā avayavinam apekṣya vālpaparimāṇā vaktavyāḥ | tathā cāsya vibhutvaikatvayor hāniḥ | liṅgaviśeṣāc ca asyaikatvavibhutve kaṇādākṣapādādimunisammate smṛtipurāṇādiprasiddhe ceti | ato na santy evāsyāvayavāḥ svābhāvikāḥ | tathāpy aupādhikāḥ saṃbhavantītyāha avasthās tasya hetujā iti | avasthāḥ pūrvāhṇamadhyāhnāparāhṇadivasakṣapāpakṣamāsa-ṛtvayanasaṃvatsarādirūpavibhāgātmikāḥ, hetuḥ raviparispandapracayaviśeṣarūpopādhi-bhedaḥ, tajjāḥ tadavacchedanibandhanāḥ | tāś cāsya nityanaimittikakāmya-karmāṅgatvopādhibhedena trividhāḥ | ato ’yaṃ kālo nityo naimittikaḥ kāmya iti tridhā vyapadiśyate | sāyam evaṃ tridhā vibhakaḥ puṇyaphalaprada iti | atra śatapathaśrutiḥ | vasanto grīṣmo varṣāḥ | te devā ṛtavaḥ śarad dhemantaḥ śiśiras te pitaro ya evāpūryyate ’rddhamāsaḥ sa devā yo ’pakṣīyate sa pitaro ’hareva devā rātriḥ pitaraḥ punar ahnaḥ pūrvāhṇo devā aparāhṇaḥ pitara iti | atrottarāyaṇadakṣiṇāyanaśuklakṛṣṇapakṣāhorātra-pūrvāhṇāparāhṇavibhāgena devapitṛsaṃbandhaḥ kālasyocyate |
hārīto ’thāha |
saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatis tasyodagayanaṃ śukto ’haḥ pūrvahṇaś ca devānāṃ dakṣiṇāyanaṃ tāmisro rātry aparākṣaś ca tat pitṛṇāṃ [166] nānyabhāvaḥ kṛtsnabhāvād ubhayathā ijyante vyāmiśratvād aharahar athāpy atrodāharanti | yasyeme vartmanī havyakavye vahato devā yasyārddhaṃ pitaro ’rddhaṃ tathaiva nimeṣā ṛtavo yasya mūlaṃ taṃ vai yajantam amṭatam iyād iti |
asyārthaḥ | yo ’sau prajāpatir nāma devatāviśeṣaḥ śrūyate sa evāyaṃ saṃvatsarātmakaḥ kālaḥ | tasyaitasya bhāgadvayaṃ bhavati | tatrodagayanaṃ śuklo ’haḥ pūrvāhṇa ity eko bhāgaḥ | udagayanaṃ sūryopabhujyamānamakarādirāśiṣaṭkopalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ, śuklaḥ pakṣaḥ amāvāsyottarāṃ pratipadam ārabhya paurṇamāsīparyantaḥ kālaḥ | ahar dinaṃ | pūrvāhṇo dvidhākṛtasyāhnaḥ pūrvo bhāgaḥ | tathā ca skandapurāṇe |
āvartanāt tu pūrvāhṇo hy aparāhṇas tataḥ param || iti |
āṅ maryādāyām | āvartanāc chāyāyāḥ parivartanaṃ maryādīkṛtya yaḥ kālaḥ sa pūrvāhṇaḥ | tad etat saṃvatsarāvayavajātaṃ devānāṃ | atra daivikāni karmāṇi kartavyānīty arthaḥ | dakṣiṇāyanaṃ tāmisro rātriś ceti dvitīyo bhāgaḥ | dakṣiṇāyanaṃ sūryopabhujyamānakarkaṭakādirāśiṣaṭkopalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ | tāmisraḥ kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ paurṇamāsyuttarāṃ pratipadam ārabhyāmāvāsyāparyantaḥ kālaḥ | rātrir meruvyavahitasūryadarśanāvacchinnaḥ kālaḥ | tad etat saṃvatsarāvayavajātaṃ pitṝṇāṃ | atra pitryaṃ karma kartavyam ity arthaḥ | evaṃ daivatvena pitryatvena ca dvaividhyam eva kālasya nānyabhāvo nānyarūpatvaṃ | kasmād ity āha kṛtsnabhāvāt | yasmāt saṃvatsarātiriktakālābhāvāde tāvān eva sarvaḥ kālaḥ | ato daivapitryarūpapūrvāhṇāparāhṇā-tmakakālavyāmiśratvād daivapitryarūpatvena devāś ca pitaraś cobhayathā pūrvāhṇāparāhṇavibhāgenāharahaḥ pratidinam ijyante tyajyamānasya havyasya kavyasya [167]codṛśyatvaṃ prārthante |  athāsyārthasya nedaṃ prathamavākyamiti saṃvādaṃ darśayati | athāpy atrodāharatīti | atrāsmin viṣaye pūrve maharṣayaḥ udāharanti paṭhanti cety arthaḥ | yasyeme vartmanau havyakavye vahataḥ | yasya saṃvatsarātmakasya prajāpater devān pitṝṃś ca prati havyakavye vayataḥ (?) uttarāyaṇadakṣiṇāyanātmake devayānapitṛyānātmake vā dve vartmanī dvau mārgau yasyārdhaṃ devāś cārddhaś ca pitaraḥ | yasya ca mūlaṃ nimeṣyāḥ (?) | nimeṣaḥ kāṭhākalādayaḥ mūlam avayavarūpatvenārambhakā ity arthaḥ | tathā yasya ṛtavo mūlaṃ | ṛtavaḥ ṣaṭ | te ca śrutāv uktāḥ | madhuś ca mādhavaś ca vāsantikāv ṛtū | śukraś ca śuciś ca grauṣmāv ṛtū | nabhaś ca nabhasyaś ca vārṣikāv ṛtū | iṣaś corṣṇaś (?) ca śāradāv ṛtū | sahaś ca mahasyaś (?) ca haimantikāv ṛtū | tapaś ca  tapasyaś ca śaiśirāv ṛtū | madhuś ca mādhavaś ca caitraś ca vaiśākhaś ca dvau māsau vāsantikau vasantasaṃbandhinau, ṛtū ṛtvavayavau | anena caitravaiśākhamāsadvayātmako vasantākhyaṛtur bhavatīty uktaṃ bhavati | evam uttare grīṣmādayaḥ | ta ete yasya saṃvatsarasya mūlaṃ taṃ saṃvatsaraṃ (?) prāpya yathāvibhāgaṃ devāṃś ca pitṝṃś ca yajantaṃ puruṣam amṛtaṃ mokṣādiphalābhimukhena iyāt āgacchatīty arthaḥ |
iti devapitryavibhāgena kālanirūpaṇaṃ |
atha śrāddhaviśeṣaḥ
tatra yājñavalkaḥ |
abhāvāsya ekāha didaḥ kṛṣṇapa tho’yavadarthaṃ [line very blurred] |
dravyaṃ brāhmaṇasampattir viṣuvat sūryasaṃkramaḥ ||
vyatīpāto gajachāyā grahaṇaṃ candrasūryayoḥ |
[168]        śrāddhaṃ prati ruciś caiva śrāddhakālāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
amāvāsyādikālāḥ svasvaprakaraṇe ekaikaśo vakṣyante | rucir icchā sā yadā śrāddhānuṣṭhānaviṣayā bhavati tadupalakṣito ’pi kālaḥ śrāddhāṅgaṃ bhavati | asyānimittatvaṃ pūrvoktaśrāddhānimittatvanirākaraṇanyāyenaiva nirākaraṇīyaṃ | tatrāmāvāsyocyate | amāvāsyā kṛṣṇapakṣasya pañcadaśī tithiḥ | asyāś ca lakṣaṇaṃ gobhilenoktaṃ | yaḥ paramo viprakarṣaḥ sūryacandramasoḥ sā paurṇamāsī, yaḥ paramaḥ saṃnikarṣaḥ sāmāvāsyeti | tatrāmāvāsyāśabdanirvacanaṃ śatapathaśrutau |
te devā abruvan | na vā imam anyat somāddhinuyāt somam evāsmai saṃbharāmeti tasmai somaṃ samabharann eṣa vai somo rājā devānām annaṃ yac candramāḥ sa yatraiṣa etāṃ rātriṃ na purastān na paścād dadṛśe tad imaṃ lokam āgacchati sa ihevāpaśvauṣadhīś ca praviśati sa vai devānāṃ vasv annaṃ hy eṣāṃ tad yad eṣa etāṃ rātrim ihāmāvasati tasmād amāvāsyā nāmeti |
atra prathamavākye vṛtravadhānantaraṃ proṣitendrasamāgame devānām ukte vasunendreṇa amā saha nivāsād amāvāsyeti | dvitīyavākye tu vasoḥ candramaso ’syāṃ rātrāv asmin loke nivāsād amāvāsyeti dvidhā nirvacanam |
padmapurāṇe |
agniṣvāttā iti khyātā yajvāno yajñasaṃsthitāḥ |
acchāmā nāma teṣāṃ tu mānasī kanyakā matā ||
[169]        tam athāmāvasuṃ nāma pitaraṃ vīkṣya sāṅganā |
vavre varārthinī saṅgaṃ kusumāyudhapauḍanāt ||
yogād bhraṣṭhā tu tenāsau vyabhicāreṇa bhāminī |
dharān na spṛśate pūrvaṃ papātātha bhuvastale ||
pitā cāmāvasur yasmād icchāṃ cakre na tāṃ prati |
dhairyeṇa tasya sā loke amāvāsyeti viśrutā |
pitṝṇāṃ vallabhā yasmād dattasyākṣayakāriṇī ||
paramasaṃyamipitṛgaṇābhimatabrahmacaryāpracyutihetubhūtāmāvasudhairyanirvahaṇa-pratītamahimatvād amāvāsyety ucyate ity etad itihāsatātparyaṃ |
skānde nāgarakhaṇḍe |
amā nāma kalāraśmiḥ sahasrapramukhasthitaḥ |
yasyāś ca tejasā sūryaḥ proktas trailokyadīpakaḥ ||
tasmin viśati yenendur amāvāsyā tataḥ smṛtā |
akṣayā dharmakṛtye sā pitṛkṛtye viśeṣataḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
amā nāma kalāraśmiḥ sahasrapramukhasthitaḥ |
trailokyadīpakaḥ proktaś candraloke pratiṣṭhitaḥ ||
yasmāt somo viśaty asyām amāvāsyā tataḥ smṛtā |
matsya-vāyupurāṇayoḥ |
amā vasetāṃ ṛte tu yadā candradivākarau |
eṣā pañcadaśī rātrir amāvāsyā tatas tu sā ||
amā saha ekatrety arthaḥ |
[170]        āśritya tām amāvāsyāṃ paśyatas tu samāgatau |
anyonyaṃ candrasūryau tau yadā tad darśa ucyate ||
sā ca dvividhā | tathā ca skandapurāṇe |
rākā cānumatiś caiva paurṇamāso dvidhā smṛtā |
sinīvālī kuhūś caiva amāvāsyā dvidhaiva tu |
tayoś ca lakṣaṇaṃ śrutau | yā pūrvā paurṇamāsī sānumatir yottarā sā rākā, yā pūrvāmāvāsyā sā sinīvālī, yottarā sā kuhūr iti vijñāyate | atra caturdaśīmiśrā sinīvālīty uktaṃ bhavati | asyāṃca trimuhartādhikacaturdaśīmiśrāyāṃ candro bhavatīti jyotirvidvacanāt prāyikaṃ candrodayopalakṣiteyam ity abhipretyāha vyāsaḥ |
dṛṣṭacandrā sinobālī naṣṭacandrā kuhaḥ smṛtā |
sinīvālī sadā pitrye kuhūr daive tu karmaṇi || iti |
athānayor nirvacanaṃ parvasaṃdhiṃ prakṛtya |
sinīvālīpramāṇas tu kṣīṇaśeṣo niśākaraḥ |
amāvāsyāṃ viśaty arkaḥ sinīvālī tathā smṛtā ||
sinī sitā avasiteti yāvat | alpo vālo vālī | sinī cāsau vālī ca sinīvālī tatparimāṇaḥ sinīvālīpramāṇaḥ vālāgramātra ity arthaḥ |
kuhūr iti pikenoke yāvatkālaḥ samāpyate |
tatkālasaṃmitā hy eṣā amāvāsyā kuhūḥ smṛtā || iti |
kokiloccāritakuhūśabdaparimitaparvesaṃdhyupalakṣitam ahorātraṃ kuhūḥ | nityaś cāyaṃ śrāddhakālaḥ |
[171] tathā ca viṣṇudharmottare |
śrāddhakālam amāvāsyāṃ nityaṃ pārthivasattama |
paurṇamāsīṃ tathā māghīṃ śrāvaṇīṃ ca pracakṣate || iti |
manusmṛtāv api |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya vipraś candrakṣaye ’gnimān |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryān māsānumāsikaṃ ||
candrakṣayo ’māvāsyā tasyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | tatrāpi na sarvasyāṃ velāyāṃ kiṃ tarhi pitṛyajñaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñaṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ | vipragrahaṇam upalakṣaṇaṃ | piṇḍānvāhāryakam iti śrāddhasya nāmadheyaṃ | māsaś cānumāsaś ca tayor bhavaṃ māsānumāsikaṃ | māsānumāsaśabdo māsagatāṃ vīpsām ācaṣṭe māsi māsi kartavyam iti | ataś ca nityatāsiddhiḥ |
mārkaṇḍeye |
kāryaṃ śrāddham amāvāsyāṃ māsi māsy uḍupakṣaye |
tathāṣṭakāsv apy avaśyam iṣṭaṃ kālaṃ nibodha me |
uḍupaś candraḥ | iṣṭaṃ kālaṃ nibodha me ity eṣā vakṣyamāṇasyecchikasya kālasya pratijñā |
brahmapurāṇe |
śrāddhaṃ deyam uśantīha māsi māsy uḍupakṣaye |
paurṇamāsīṣu ca śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ ṛkṣagocare ||
ṛkṣagocare māsanakṣatrasaṃyoge | atrāpi vīpsayā nityatvaṃ |
viṣṇupurāṇe ’pi |
māsi māsy asite pakṣe pañcadaśyāṃ nareśvara |
[172]        tathāṣṭakāsu kurvīta kāmyān kālān śṛṇuṣva me || iti |
atra kāmyān kālān iti vakṣyamāṇānāṃ kāmyatvaṃ vadan pūrvoktānāṃ nityatvam anusaṃdhatte |
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
śrāddhaṃ candrakṣaye ’vaśyaṃ sadā kāryaṃ vipaścitā |
yadi vecchati sa svargaṃ santānaṃ ca tathā nṛpa ||
śītārtā yadvad icchani vahniṃ prāvaraṇāni ca |
pitaras tadvad icchanti kṣukṣāmāś candrasaṃkṣayaṃ ||
daridropahatā yadvad dhanaṃ vāñchanti mānavāḥ |
pitaras tadvad icchanti tu (?) kṣāmāś candrasaṃkṣayam ||
yathā dṛṣṭiṃ pravāñchanti karṣakāḥ śasyavṛddhaye |
tathātmatṛptaye prītāḥ pravāñchantīndusaṃkṣayam ||
yathoṣaś cakravākaś ca vāñchanti ravidarśanam |
pitaras tadvad icchanti śrāddhaṃ darśasamudbhavam ||
jalenāpi ca yaḥ śrāddhaṃ śākenāpi karoti na |
darśāhe pitaras tasya yānti śāpapradāyinaḥ ||
tathā |
amāvāsyādine prāpte gṛhadvāraṃ samāsthitāḥ |
vāyubhūtāḥ pravāñchanti śrāddhaṃ pitṛagaṇā nṛṇām ||
yāvad astamayaṃ bhānoḥ kṣutpipāsāsamākulāḥ |
tataś cāstaṃ gate bhānau nirāśā duḥkhasaṃyutāḥ |
niḥśvasya suciraṃ yānti garhayantaḥ svavaṃśajam || iti |
[173] asyāmāvāsyāśrāddhasyāvaśyakatvārtham itihāse |
viṣṇudharmottare |
saśarīro nṛpaḥ svargaṃ tadā prāpya purūravāḥ |
śakreṇābhyarcitaṃ kāle vijahāra yathāsukham ||
devodyāneṣu sarveṣu urvaśyā sahitas tadā |
nāpavārayituṃ śakyaḥ kadācid api pārthivaḥ ||
amāvāsyām amāvāsyāṃ niyataṃ candrabhāskarau |
draṣṭuṃ yāti mahīpālas tv akarāśigatau sadā ||
sahorvaśyā tu pautrasya yadā vasati candramāḥ |
tadā sudhārasaṃ devaḥ snehāt sravati mānavaḥ ||
somāmṛtarasaṃ taṃ ca pibanti pitaras tadā |
śrāddhakālas tadā prokto nṛṇāṃ nṛpavarottama ||
tadā śrāddhaṃ hi yad dattaṃ sudhārasavimiśritam |
tad aśnanti mahīpāla pitaro jalasaṃnibhāḥ ||
amāvāsyām amāvāsyāṃ tadā śrāddhaṃ prayatnataḥ |
kuruṣva bhūmipaśreṣṭha sarvakāmān avāpsyasi ||
ye cānye ’pi kariṣyanti tadā śrāddhaṃ narādhipa |
teṣām āyur yaśaḥ svargaṃ dāsyanti prapitāmahāḥ ||
rājyakāmaḥ svargakāmaḥ puṣṭikāmas tathaiva ca |
pitṝn saṃtarpayec chrāddhe candrasūryasamāgame ||
[174]        vasanti satataṃ rājan pitaraś candramaṇḍale |
teṣāṃ māsam ahorātravibhāgaṃ tatra me śṛṇu ||
kṛṣṇapakṣāṣṭamīmadhye teṣāṃ tūdayate raviḥ |
śuklāṣṭamyāṃ tathaivāstaṃ teṣāṃ yāti divākaraḥ ||
amāvāsyā sadā teṣāṃ madhyāhno jāyate nṛpa |
tasmāt śrāddhāni deyāni tasmin kāle prayatnataḥ ||
athāsyās tither vāraviśeṣeṇa puṇyayogatamatvam āha vyāsaḥ |
amā vai somavāreṇa ravivāreṇa saptamī |
caturthī bhaumavāreṇa viṣuvat sadṛśaṃ phalam ||
amā amāvāsyā |
śaṅkho ’pi |
amāvāsyā tu somena saptamau bhānunā saha |
caturthī bhūmiputreṇa somaputreṇa cāṣṭamī ||
catasras tithayas tais tu tulyāḥ syur grahaṇādibhiḥ |
sarvam akṣayam atroktaṃ snānadānajapādikam ||
bhūmiputro maṅgalaḥ | somaputro budhaḥ |
atha nakṣatrayoge puṇyatamatvaṃ |
brahmapurāṇe |
amāvāsyāyāṃ śravaṇaṃ rakṣoghnaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ yadā |
tadā pitṛbhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ tu dattam akṣayyam ucyate || iti |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
[175]        amāvāsyā yadā maitraviśākhāsvātiyoginī |
śrāddhe pitṛgaṇas tṛptiṃ tadāpnoty aṣṭavārṣikīm ||
maitram anurādhānakṣatram |
amāvāsyā yadā puṣye raudraṛkṣe punarvasau |
dvādaśābdāṃ tadā tṛptiṃ prayānti pitaro ’rcitāḥ ||
raudram ārdrānakṣatram |
vāsavājaikapādarkṣe pitṝṇāṃ tṛptim icchatām |
vāruṇenāpy amāvāsyā devānām api durlabhā ||
vāsavaṃ vasudaivatyaṃ dhaniṣṭhānakṣatram | ajaikapādaṛkṣaṃ pūrvabhādrapadā nakṣatram | vāruṇaṃ śatabhiṣānakṣatram |
māghāsite pañcadaśī kadācit
upaiti yogaṃ yadi vāruṇena |
ṛkṣeṇa kālaḥ sa paraḥ pitṝṇāṃ
na hy alpapuṇyair nṛpa labhyate ’sau ||
kāle dhaniṣṭhā yadi nāma tasmin
bhavet tu bhūpāla tadā pitṛbhyaḥ |
dattaṃ jalānnaṃ ca dadāti tṛptiṃ
varṣāyutaṃ tat kulajair manuṣyaiḥ ||
tasmin kāle māghe māsi asitapañcadaśayām ity arthaḥ |
tatraiva cet bhādrapadās tu pūrvā
kāle tadā yat kriyate pitṛbhyaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ parāṃ tṛptim upetya te ca
yugaṃ samagraṃ pitaraḥ svapantīti ||
tatraiva tasyāṃ māghapañcadaśyām ity arthaḥ |
athāmāvāsyādvaidhanirṇayaḥ |
tatrāha kātyāyanaḥ |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṣīṇe rājani śasyate |
vāsarasya tṛtīye ’ṃśe nātisaṃdhyāsamīpataḥ ||
atra tridhāvibhaktasya vāsarasya tṛtīye ’ṃśe | indukṣayas tu kadā bhavatīty apekṣāyāṃ sa evāha |
aṣṭame 'ṃśe caturdaśyāḥ kṣīṇo bhavati candramāḥ |
amāvāsyāṣṭame ’ṃśe tu punaḥ kila bhaved aṇuḥ ||
iha khalu grahacāravido rāśitriṃśāśair dvādaśabhir dvādaśabhiś candramasaḥ sūryeṇa sāvikarṣe [? letter missing] viprakarṣe vā ekaikā tithir bhavatīti varṇayanti | ataś caturdaśī vāmāvāsyā vā dvādaśāṃśair bhavati | tatra caturdaśyaṣṭamāṃśānte candraḥ kṣīyate| amāvāsyāṣṭamāṃśānte cāṇur bhūtvā punar jāyate | kilaśabdo ’tra jyautiṣikaiḥ kilaitad vyākhyātam iti | etam artham āha śatapathaśrutiḥ |
sa eṣa saṃvatsaraḥ prajāpatiḥ ṣoḍaśakalas tasya rātraya eva pañcadaśakalāḥ | dhruvaivāsya ṣoḍaśī kalā sā rātribhir eva ca pūryate na ca kṣīyate | somaḥ amāvāsyāṁ rātrim etayā ṣoḍaśyā kalayā sarvam idaṃ prāṇabhṛd anupraviśya tataḥ prātar jāyate| tasmād etāṃ rātriṃ prāṇabhṛtaḥ prāṇaṃ na vicchindyād api kṛkalāsasyaitasyā eva devatāyā apacityā iti |
atrāyam āśayaḥ | ṣoḍaśakalasyāsya candrarūpasya prajāpateḥ kṛṣṇapakṣapratipadam ārabhya caturdaśyantāsu tithiṣu kramād ekaikakalākṣayeṇa caturdaśakalāḥ kṣīṇā bhavanti | caturdaśī[177]tṛtīyāṃśe tu pañcadaśy api kalā kṣīyate | tato ’māvāsyāyāṃ dhruvayā avinaśvarayā ṣoḍaśakalayā bhūrlokam āgatya sarvaṃ prāṇijātaṃ saṃviśati | amāvāsyātṛtīyāṃśe tu tayaiva ṣoḍaśyā kalayā divam āruhya punaḥ prādur bhavatīti | amāvāsyāviśeṣe candrakṣayasya kālam āha kātyāyana eva |
āgrahāyaṇy amāvāsyā tathā jyaiṣṭhasya yā bhavet |
viśeṣam asyāṃ bruvate candracāravido janāḥ ||
atrendur ādye prahare ’vatiṣṭhate
caturthabhāgonakalāvaśiṣṭhaḥ |
tadanta eva kṣayam eti kṛtsnam
evaṃ jyotiścakravido vadanti ||
asyārthaḥ | vṛṣasthe candre jyeṣṭhasyāmāvāsyānte vṛścikasthe ca agrahāyaṇyamāvāsyānte ca candrasya paramoccanīcasthāne tadvaśāc candrasya gativailakṣaṇyāt yad anyāsv amāvāsyāsv aṣṭame prahare ’sya rūpaṃ tad anayor amāvāsyayoḥ prathama eva bhavatīti | ye tu candrādarśana eva piṇḍapitṛyajñādikaṃ manyante tasminn akṣaye ’pi tān nirākartuṃ kadācit tṛtīyāyām api candrādarśanaṃ darśayati |
yasminn abde dvādaśaikaś ca yavyas
tasmin tṛtīyāparato na dṛśyate |
evaṃ cāraṃ candramaseo viditvā
kṣīṇe tasminn aparāhṇe ca dadyāt ||
[178] asyārthaḥ | yavyo māsaḥ yasminn abde dvādaśādhiko bhavati tasminn abde malamāsānantaratṛtīyāyā aparasyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ candro na dṛśyate | na caitāvatā candrādarśanavihitaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñas tatra kriyate | ataś candracāraṃ viditvā tasmin kṣīṇe ’pi caturdaśīmiśrāyām amāvāsyāyām aparāhṇe piṇḍapitṛyajñādi kuryād iti | ataś caturdaśyante ’pi piṇḍānvāhāryakārtho nirvāpaḥ kartavya iti sthitiḥ | pūrvapakṣam avatārayanti | naitad asti caturdaśyante nirvāpa iti piṇḍapitṛyajñānantaraṃ vihitaṃ | piṇḍapitṛyajñaś cāmāvāsyāyām aparāhṇe vihitaḥ | amāvāsyāyām aparāhṇe piṇḍapitṛyajñena carantīti kalpasūtrakāravacanāt |
yat tu mataṃ | aupavasathyadinopalakṣaṇārtho ’māvāsyāśabda iti |
tan na | lakṣaṇāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt | vidhāv anyāyyatvāc cāsyāḥ | yā tu śrutiḥ | tasmāt pūrvedyu pitṛbhyaḥ kriyata uttaram ahar devān yajati pitṛbhya eva tadyajñaṃ nikrīna (?) yajamānaḥ pratanuta iti tathāgnyanvādhānaṃ prakṛtyāparāhṇe piṇḍapitṛyajñena carantīti kalpasūtrakāravacanam | tad etad yasminn aupavasathye ’hany aparāhṇe amāvāsyāsti tadviṣayam iti kṛtvā na pūrvadinamātraparaṃ sallakṣaṇāyāṃ pramāṇaṃ bhavitum arhati |
anye tu pūrvapakṣayanti | candrodayopalakṣitadināntargatāyām amāvāsyāyām api piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ na kartavyaṃ | tasya piṇḍapitṛyajñānantaraṃ vihitatvāt | piṇḍapitṛyajñaś ca candrādarśanavatyāmamāvāsyāyāṃ vihitaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ | yad evaiṣa na purastān na paścād dadṛśe ’thaibhyo dadātīti | sūtrakāravacanaṃ | yad ahaś candramasaṃ na paśyanti tad ahaḥ pitṛyajñaṃ kuruta iti | nātrādarśanaṃ kṣayopalakṣaṇaparaṃ | kṣīṇe rājanīty asyādarśane [179] ’py anatikramāt | na ca kramo vivakṣitaḥ, pramāṇābhāvāt |
atha mataṃ | yadaikasmin dine dvayaṃ tadāyaṃ kramo nānyadeti | yadā caupavasathya eva candrādarśanaṃ tadaikasmin dine dvayam anyadā piṇḍapitṛyajñaṃ yajanīyadinaṃ prakṛtya kuruta iti |
tan na | tadādyutkarṣanyāyenānvāhāryakasyāpy utkaṣāt |
yat tu laugākṣivacanam,
sinīvālī dvijaiḥ kāryā sāgnikaiḥ pitṛkarmaṇi |
strībhiḥ śūdraiḥ kuhūḥ kāryā tathā cānagnikair dvijaiḥ || iti ||
tat piṇḍapitṛyajñapiṇḍānvāhāryakāny apitṛkāryaviṣayam astu | atredam ucyate | caturdaśyante na nivvopa [nirvāpa?] iti |
tad ayuktaṃ | yataś chandogapariśiṣṭe piṇḍapitṛyajñaṃ prakṛtyāha kātyāyanaḥ |
yathoktaṃ dṛśyamāne ’pi tac caturdaśyapekṣayā |
amāvāsyāṃ pratīkṣeta tadante vāpi nirvapet || iti |
yac ca dṛśyamāne ’pi candre piṇḍapitṛyajñaṃ kuryād iti śrutāv ukaṃ tac caturdaśīvivakṣayā | ato ’māvāsyāṃ pratīkṣeta caturdaśyante eva eṣāṃ nirvaped iti | anena candradarśanadine pitṛyajñaḥ śrutyanugṛhīta eveti ca darśitam | adarśanaśruteḥ kṣayopalakṣaṇatvaṃ cāha sa eva |
yad uktaṃ yad ahas tv eva darśanaṃ naiti candramāḥ |
tat kṣayāpekṣayā jñeyaṃ kṣīṇe rājani cetyapi || iti |
yac cānyat pūrvapakṣitaṃ | candrodayopalakṣitadināntargatāyām amāvāsyāyām api na piṇḍapitṛyajñapiṇḍānvāhāryaka iti | tad api yac coktaṃ [180] dṛśyamāne ’pītyādinā kātyāyana-vacanena nirākṛtam eva | dṛśyamāne ’py anena gobhilavacanena hārītenāpi nirākṛtam |
yasyāṃ saṃdhyāgataḥ somā mṛṇālam iva dṛśyate | aparāhṇe kṣayas tasyāṃ piṇḍānāṃ karaṇaṃ dhruvam
iti nirdeśaḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñaṃ nātivartitum īhate | punaḥ kātyāyanaḥ |
yadā caturdaśīyāmaṃ turīyam anupūrayet |
amāvāsyā kṣīyamāṇā tadaiva śrāddham iṣyate || iti |
kiṃ ca | yajanīyadināt pūrvadine hi piṇḍapitṛyajñaśrāddhe vihite tasmāt pitṛbhyaḥ pūrvedyuḥ kriyata iti | yadā ca pratipaddine pūrvāhṇe ’tha vā madhyānhe 'māvāsyā samāpyate tadā tadahar eva yāga iti pūrvasyāṃ candrodayavatyāṃ | evaṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñādi | sinīvālī dvijaiḥ kāryā sāgnikaiḥ pitṛkarmaṇīty asya piṇḍānvāhāryakāryavyatiriktaviṣayatve pramāṇābhāvāc ceti | tad evam amāvāsyādvaidhe sinīvālyāṃ śrāddham iti siddham | asyāpavādo baudhāyanenoktaḥ |
madhyāhnāt parato yatra caturdaśy anuvartate |
sinīvālī tu sā jñeyā pitṛkārye tu niṣphalā || iti |
bṛhaspatir apy āha |
madhyāhnādyā tv amāvāsyā parastāt saṃpravartate |
bhūtaviddhā tu sā jñeyā na sā pañcadaśī bhavet ||
tataś ca |
bhūtaviddhām amāvāsyāṃ mohād ajñānato ’pi vā |
śrāddhakarmāṇi ye kuryus teṣām āyuḥ prahīyate ||
[181] ity etat kārṣṇājinivacanam aparāhṇapraviṣṭacaturdaśīviddhasinīvālīviṣayam eva vyākhyeyaṃ | anenābhiprāyeṇāha vṛddhamanuḥ |
yasyām astaṃ ravir yāti pitaras tām upāsate |
tithiṃ tebhyo yato dattas tv aparāhṇaḥ svayaṃbhuvā || iti |
anenāparāhṇavyāpinī cet sinīvālī tarhy eva sā grāhyā anyadā tu kuhūr eva grāhyety uktaṃ bhavati | tad āha hārītaḥ |
aparāhṇaḥ pitṝṇāṃ tu yāparāhṇānuyāyinī |
sā grāhyā pitṛkārye tu na pūrvās tānuyāyinī ||
evam aparāhṇavyāpinyāḥ sinīvālyāḥ pitṛkāryārthaṃ grahaṇe siddhe pūrvāhṇavyāpinyāś cāparāhṇavyāpakatve ’syāvaśyakatvāt siddhe tasyā api grahaṇe puṇyatamatvaṃ pratipādayitum āha baudhāyanaḥ |
ghaṭikaikāpy amāvāsyā pūrvāhṇavyāpinī yadi |
bhūtaviddhaiva sā kāryā pitrye karmaṇi sarvadā || iti |
amum evārthaṃ prakārāntareṇāha jābālaḥ 1
pratipatsv apy amāvāsyā pūrvavyāpinī yadi |
bhūtaviddhaiva sā kāryā pitrye karmaṇi sarvadā || iti |
pūrvadine pūrvāhṇa eva pravṛttāparadine pūrvāhṇa evaṃ samāpyate tadā sinīvālyeva kartavyā ity arthaḥ | yat tu hārītavacanaṃ |
tulāmakaramīneṣu kanyāyāṃ mithune tathā |
bhūtaviddhā tv (?) amāvāsyā pūjyā bhavati yatnataḥ || iti |
[182]
tat vratopavāsādiviṣayam iti manyante | atha yathoktasinīvālīparityāgena kuhūgrahaṇaparāṇi dṛśyante tatra kaiścin nibandhakārair hārītanāmnā vacanaṃ likhitaṃ |
pūrvāhṇe ced amāvāsyāparāhṇena bhaved yadi |
pratipady api kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ śrāddhavido viduḥ || iti |
atra harihareṇoktaṃ | anirjñātamūlam idaṃ vacanaṃ parasparavyāhatapadopabandhaṃ ceti | tathāhi pūrvāhṇe ced ity etāvat pratipaddinaviṣayaṃ | pūrvāhṇaś ca divasasya tṛtīyabhāgo vā pūrvārddhaṃ vā | evaṃ ca sati sarvathā kasmiṃścid aparāhṇe na bhaved ity etan na yujyate pratipaddine pūrvāhṇamātrāvasthitāyāś caturdaśīdine ’parāhṇasaṃbandhāvaśyakatvāt | ṣaṭghaṭikādhikasya tithihrāsasyābhāvāt | atha yadi pratipaddināparāhṇa eva na bhaved ity ucyate tarhi pūrvāhṇe ced ity anarthakaṃ syāt | atha yadi pūrvāhṇaśabdasya mukhyārtham abhyupetya kaścid aviruddho ’rthaḥ kalpyate tadā smṛtimahārṇave tu kalpitaḥ |
tathā hi | tithivṛddhau pratipadyuktāyām amāvāsyāyāṃ śrāddham uktaṃ | tac ca pitṛyajñaṃ nirvartyetyādinā piṇḍapitṛyajñānantaram eva manunā vihitaṃ | piṇḍapitṛyajñaś cāhatya śrutyāparāhṇe vihito nānyathā kartuṃ śakyaḥ | tasya ca tither ekakālatvāt tenaiva sakalāparāhṇavyāptāvamāvāsyāśrāddhasya kā gatir ity ata uktaṃ pūrvāhṇa iti nāparāhṇe kṛtsna ity arthaḥ | tadā pratipady api piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ kartavyam iti | smṛticandrikākāras tv āha |
yatra pūrvedyuś candrādarśanāsaṃbhavena piṇḍapitṛyajñotkarṣas tatra pratipadi [183] piṇḍapitṛyajñavidhānārtham iti |
idaṃ tu vyākhyānam aparisphuṭārthaṃ mandaṃ cety upekṣyate | athānyāny api harihareṇa kuhūgrahaṇaparāṇy evānirjñātakartṛtvena mahārṇavalikhitatvena copālabhya kaiścid uktānīty uktvopanyastāni |
aruṇodayavelāyām amāvāsyā yadā bhavet |
sa kālaḥ paramo jñeyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayaṃ ||
ghaṭikaikā tv amāvāsyā pratipatsu na cet tadā |
bhūtaviddhā tu sā kāryā daive pitrye ca karmaṇi ||
muhūrtam apy amāvāsyā pratipady api ced bhavet |
tadā tad uttamaṃ jñeyaṃ parvaśeṣaṃ tu parvavat || iti |
atrāruṇodayavelāyām ity etat sāmānyapravṛttaṃ viśeṣollekhapravṛttavādhāyāsamarthaṃ | ataḥ kasmiṃścic chrāddhaviśeṣe pravartate | pitrye karmaṇīdam astu | evam anyad api muhūrtam apy amāvāsyety etat tulāpuruṣādidānaviṣayam | atha vā,
trimuhartāpi kartavyā pūrvā darśā ca bahvṛcaiḥ |
kuhūr adhvaryubhiḥ kāryā yatheṣṭaṃ sāmagāyibhiḥ ||
ity etadvacanānusāriṇī vyavasthāstu |
atha kutapavyāptiprayuktapūrvaparāmāvāsyāgrahaṇaparāṇi |
tatra hārītaḥ |
bhūtaviddhāpy amāvāsyā pratipanmiśritāpi vā |
pitrye karmaṇi vidvadbhir grahyā kutapakālikī || iti |
smṛtyantare ’pi |
madhyāhnavyāpinī yā tu tithiḥ pūrvāparāpi vā |
tadahaḥ karma kurvīta hrāsavṛddhau na kāraṇam || iti |
[184] āha pracetāḥ |
sinīvālī kuhūś caiva śrutyukte śrāddhakarmaṇi |
syātāṃ na cet tu madhyāhne śrāddhādi syāt kathaṃ tadā ||
tithikṣaye sinīvālī tithivṛddhau kuhūr matā |
sāmveti ca kuhūr jñeyā vedavedāṅgavedibhiḥ || iti |
atra śrāddhāṅgatvena kutapo ’parāhṇaś ca vihitaḥ |
tatra ye ’parāhṇam ādriyante yatra piṇḍapitṛyajñapiṇḍānvāhāryakādikaṃ viśeṣato ’parāhṇe vihitaṃ te ca tac cāparāhṇavyāptiṃ puraskṛtya sinīvālyāṃ kuhvāṃ vā śrāddhaṃ kuryaḥ | ye tu kutapam ādriyante ’nagnikādayaḥ yac ca viśeṣataḥ kutapavihitam ekoddiṣṭam āmaśrāddhādi te tac ca kutapavyāptiṃ puraskṛtya pūrvasyāṃ parasyāṃ vā amāvāsyāyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryuḥ | tadā vihitakālasamuccayo labhyate | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
karmaṇo yasya yaḥ kālas tatkālavyāpinī tithiḥ |
tayā karmāṇi kurvīta hrāsavṛddho na kāraṇam || iti |
atra ye ’nye’pi kecana sinīvālīniṣedhās te tithivṛddhiviṣayatvenānagnika-viṣayatvenāparāhṇavyāptirāhityaviṣayatvena dānādiviṣayatvenāpavādatvenopasaṃhāratvena kalpasūtravibhāgena vā sati saṃbhave vyākhyeyāḥ | kuhūvidhayo ’py evaṃ na tu sahasā vihitapratiṣiddhatvena vikalpo ’bhyupeyaḥ |
śrutidvaidhaṃ tu yatra syāt tatra dharmāv ubhau smṛtau |
smṛtidvaidhe tu viṣayaḥ kalpanīyaḥ pṛthak pṛthak ||
ity amāvāsyānirūpaṇaṃ |
[185]
athāṣṭakāḥ |
aṣṭakāśabdena mārgaśīrṣādimāsacatuṣṭayāparapakṣāṣṭamyaḥ | hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakā iti śaunakasmaraṇāt | keṣāṃcinmate tisra evāṣṭakās tisraś cānvaṣṭakāḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
amāvāsyās tisro ’ṣṭakās tisro ’nvaṣṭakāḥ || iti |
aṣṭakānām anu paścād bhavantīty anvaṣṭakāḥ | aṣṭakānām uparitanā navamyaḥ | kūrmapurāṇe |
amāvāsyāṣṭakās tisraḥ pauṣamāsādiṣu triṣu |
tisraś cānvaṣṭakāḥ puṇyā māghī pañcadaśī tathā || iti |
viṣṇudharmottare |
agrahāyaṇyatikrāntau kṛṣṇās tisro ’ṣṭakās tathā || iti |
atha pūrvoktāṣṭakācatuṣṭayavyatiriktāpi bhādrapadāparapakṣe ’ṣṭakā | tasyāś cetihāsapūrvakam utpattiḥ praśaṃsā cocyate | padmapurāṇe |
agniṣvāttā iti khyātā yajvāno yajñasaṃsthitāḥ |
acchodā nāma teṣāṃ tu mānasī kanyakā matā ||
tam athāmāvasuṃ nāma pitaraṃ vīkṣya sāṅganā |
vavre varārthinī saṅgaṃ kusumāyudhapīḍanāt (?) ||
yogād bhraṣṭā tu tenātha vyabhicāreṇa bhāminī |
dharān na spṛśate pūrvaṃ papātātha bhuvastale ||
[186]         acchodādhomukhī dīnā lajjitā tapasaḥ kṣayāt |
sā pitṝn prārthayāmāsa punar ātmasamṛddhaye ||
vilapyamānā pitṛbhir idam uktā tapasvinī |
idam ūcur mahābhāgāḥ prasādaṃ śubhayā girā |
aṣṭāviṃśe bhavitrī tu dvāpare matsyayonijā |
nāmnā satyavatī khyātā vyāsasya jananī śubhā ||
vyatikramāt pitṝṇāṃ tvaṃ kaṣṭaṃ kulam avāpsyasi |
tasmād rājño vasoḥ kanyā tvam avaśyaṃ bhaviṣyasi ||
prauṣṭhapadyaṣṭakā bhūyaḥ pitṛloke bhaviṣyasi |
āyurārogyadā nityaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradā ||
brahmavaivarta-vāyupurāṇayoḥ |
atha kālaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrāddhakarmaṇi pūjitam |
kāmyanaimittikājasraśrāddhakarmavidhāyibhiḥ ||
putradā dharmamūlaṃ syur aṣṭakās tisra eva ca |
kṛṣṇapakṣe viśiṣṭā hi pūrvā caindrī udāhṛtā ||
prājāpatyā dvitīyā syāt tṛtīyā vaiśvadaivikī |
ādyāpūpaiḥ sadā kāryā māṃsair anyā bhavet tadā ||
śākaiḥ kārya tṛtīyā syād eṣa dravyagato vidhiḥ |
[187]         aṣṭakā ca pitṝṇāṃ vai nityam eva vidhīyate ||
yā vāpy anyā caturthī syāt tāṃ ca kuryād viśeṣataḥ |
tāsu śrāddhaṃ budhaḥ kuryāt sarvasvenāpi nityaśaḥ ||
paratreha ca sarveṣu nityam eva sukhī bhavet |
pūjakānāṃ sadotkarṣo nāstikānām adhogatiḥ ||
pitaraḥ sarvakāleṣu tithikāleṣu devatāḥ |
sarve puruṣam āyānti nipānam iva dhenavaḥ ||
yasya te pratigaccheyur aṣṭakābhir apūjitāḥ |
moghas tasya bhavel loko labhdhaṃ cāsya vinaśyati |
devāṃs tu dāyino yānti tiryag gacchanty adāyinaḥ ||
atra caturthī phālgunakṛṣṇapakṣāṣṭamī "hemantaśiśirayoś caturṇām aparapakṣāṇām aṣṭamīṣv aṣṭakāṣṭamī" iti śaunakasmaraṇāt | pauṣamāsakṛṣṇāṣṭamīm ārabhya tisro ’ṣṭakā ity etanmataparigrahe caturthī bhādrapadāparapakṣāṣṭamīsaṃnihitā darśitā padmapurāṇe itihāsānusārāt | brahmapurāṇe ca catasṛṣv aṣṭakāsu krameṇādhidevatānirūpaṇe pitṛtarpaṇadravyanirūpaṇe ca bhādrapadāparapakṣāṣṭamyā grahaṇāt |
aindryāṃ tu prathamāyāṃ ca śākaiḥ saṃtarpayet pitṝn |
prājāpatyā dvitīyā syān māṃsaiḥ śuddhaiś ca tarpayet ||
vaiśvadevyāṃ tṛtīyāyām apūpaiś ca yathākramaṃ |
varṣāsu madhye śākaiś ca caturthyām eva sarvadā || iti |
[188] atra śākamāṃsāpūpaśabdāḥ śrāddhe vihitasya sarvasyāpi bhojyasya tattaddravyapradhānatvapratipādanaparāḥ na tu dravyāntaranivṛttiparāḥ | yato vidher ekavidhyantarābādhenaiva tāvad gatiṃ nyāyyāṃ manyante | kim u bahutaravidhyantarābādhena dṛṣṭāvirodhena ca | dṛṣṭaṃ hi śākapradhānasya bhojyasya tarpakatvaṃ na tu kevalasya śākasyeti | āha vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
śrāvaṇyāgrahāyaṇyoś cāṣṭakāsu pitṛbhyo dadyāt |
aṣṭakānvaṣṭakāsv api cāvaśyakam eva śrāddhaṃ | yad āha pitāmahaḥ |
amāvāsyāvyatīpātapaurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca |
vidvān śrāddham akurvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate || iti |
vāyupurāṇe "tiryag gacchanty adāyinaḥ" ity aṣṭakāśrāddham akurvāṇasya tiryagyoniprāptirūpāniṣṭābhidhānāc ca | viṣṇunā amāvāsyās tisro ’ṣṭakās tistro ’nvaṣṭakāḥ māghīprauṣṭhapadyūrddhaṃ kṛṣṇatrayodaśī vrīhiyavapāko vety anukramyoktaṃ |
etāṃś ca śrāddhakālān vai nityān āha prajāpatiḥ | iti |
viṣṇudharmottare |
śrāddhakālam amāvāsyā nityaṃ pārthivasattama |
paurṇamāsī tathā māghī śrāvaṇī ca nṛpottama ||
proṣṭhapadyām atītāyāṃ tathā kṛṣṇatrayodaśī |
āgrahāyaṇyatikrāntau kṛṣṇās tisro ’ṣṭakās tathā ||
śākaiś ca prathamā kāryā māṃsaiś ca tadanantarā |
[189]         tṛtīyā ca tathāpūpair nityam eva vijānatā ||
etāni śrāddhakālāni nityāny āha prajāpatiḥ |
śrāddham eteṣv akurvvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate || iti |
devalaḥ |
manunā śrāddhakalpo ’yaṃ mānavaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
bahupiṇḍāṣṭakā tu syād ekapiṇḍā tu nāṣṭakā || iti |
atra bahupiṇḍaikapiṇḍaśabdau pārvaṇaikoddiṣṭavidhiniṣedhaparau brahmapurāṇe |
aṣṭakāsu ca kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ haimantikāsu vai |
anvaṣṭakāsu kramaśo mātṛpūrvaṃ tad iṣyate || iti |
atra smṛtiḥ |
anvaṣṭakāsu vṛddhau ca pratisaṃvatsaraṃ tathā |
mātṛśrāddhaṃ pṛthak kuryād anyatra patinā saha || iti |
śaṅkhasmṛtiḥ |
anvaṣṭakā tathā mātuḥ śrāddhaṃ caiva mṛtāhani |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tathā muktvā strīṣu nānyat pṛthag bhavet || iti ||
viṣṇudharmottare cāsmin mātṛśrāddhe karṣūvidhir uktaḥ |
anvaṣṭakāsu ca strīṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ tathaiva ca |
aṣṭakāvidhinā hutvā krameṇaitāsu pañcakaṃ ||
mātre rājan pitāmahyai śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ yathāvidhi |
tathaiva prapitāmahyai vaiśvadevapuraḥsaram ||
[190]         piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tasyaiva pitṛvan nṛpa |
bhuktavatsu tu vipreṣu tatra kāryaṃ nibodha me ||
prādeśamātrā rājendra caturaṅgulam āyatāḥ |
tāvad eva samutsedhāḥ ṣaṭkarṣūs tatra kārayet ||
pratyekaṃ karṣūmūle tu paristīrya hutāśanaṃ ||
agnīsomayamānāṃ ca yāgaṃ kṛtvā yathā purā |
karṣūtritayamūle tu puruṣāṇāṃ tu kārayet ||
pitṛnirvapaṇaṃ prāgvad ekaikasmin yathāvidhi |
karṣūṇāṃ tritayaṃ tatra kṣīrānnābhyāṃ prapūrayet ||
karṣūtritayamūle ca strīṇāṃ nirvapaṇaṃ bhavet |
dadhnā māṃsena payasā karṣūṇāṃ caiva pūraṇaṃ ||
annodakavimiśreṇa kāryaṃ manujapuṅgava |
bhavatībhyo bhavatv etad akṣayyam iti na tyajet ||
ity aṣṭakānirūpaṇaṃ |
atha vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ |
vṛddhir nāma putrajanmādinimittopalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ | tathā ca vṛddhavaśiṣṭhaḥ |
putrajanmavivāhādau vṛddhiśrāddham udāhatam || iti |
jābāliḥ |
yajñodvāhapratiṣṭhāsu mekhalābandhamokṣayoḥ |
[191]         putrajanmavṛṣotsarge vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ samācaret || iti |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
jātasya jātakarmādikriyākāṇḍam aśeṣataḥ |
putrasya kurvīta pitā śrāddhaṃ cābhyudayātmakaṃ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
svapitṛbhyaḥ pitā dadyāt sutasaṃskārakarmasu |
piṇḍānodvahanāt teṣāṃ tasyābhāve tu tatkramāt || iti |
atra saṃskārāḥ |
garbhādhānapuṃsavansīmantonnayanajātakarmanāmakaraṇaniṣkramaṇānnaprāśana-cauḍopanayanavratacaryādhyayanasamāvartanavivāhayajñadānādi | tatraiteṣu sutasaṃskāreṣu pitā svapitṛbhyo dadyāt | śeṣeṣu saṃskāreṣu svayam evādhikārīty etad vistareṇoktam evādhikāranirūpaṇe |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
kanyāputravivāheṣu praveśe navaveśmanaḥ |
nāmakarmaṇi bālānāṃ cūḍākarmādike tathā ||
sīmantonnayane caiva putrādimukhadarśane |
nāndīmukhaṃ pitṛgaṇaṃ pūjayet prayato gṛhī ||
putrādimukhadarśane putrajanmanīty arthaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
karmaṇy athābhyudayike maṅgalye cātiśobhane |
janmany athopanayane vivāhe putrakasya ca ||
pitṝn nāndaumukhān nāma tarpayed vidhipūrvakam |
[192] vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
pūrvedyur mātṛkaṃ śrāddhaṃ karmāhe paitṛkaṃ tathā |
uttaredyuḥ prakurvīta mātāmahagaṇasya tu ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
pṛthagdineṣv aśaktaś ced ekasmin pūrvavāsare |
śrāddhatrayaṃ prakurvīta vaiśvadevaṃ ca tāntrikam ||
ekasminn api dine kriyamāṇānāṃ kālabhedenānuṣṭhānaṃ kāryam ity āha śātātapaḥ |
pūrvāhṇe mātṛkaṃ śrāddham aparāhṇe ca paitṛkam |
tato mātāmahānāṃ ca vṛddhau śrāddhatrayaṃ smṛtam |
evaṃvidhasyāpi kālabhedasyāsaṃbhave āha vṛddhamanuḥ |
alābhe bhinnakālānāṃ nāndīśrāddhatrayaṃ budhaḥ |
pūrvedyur vai prakurvīta pūrvāhṇe mātṛpūrvakam ||
baudhāyanasūtre |
vedakarmāṇi prayokṣyan pūrvedyur eva yugmān brāhmaṇān bhojayed iti nāndīmukhā evaite uktā bhavanti | naikāhena pitryaṃ daivaṃ ca kurvanti | yasyaikāhnā pitryaṃ daivaṃ ca kurvanti prajā asya pramāyukā bhavanti tasmāt pitṛbhyaḥ pūrvedyuḥ karoti pitṛbhya eva tad yajñaṃ niṣkrīya yajamānaḥ pratanuta iti | brāhmaṇam apy atra pūrvam upanyastaṃ |
sakalaśrāddhakāloddeśasthānīye yājñavalkyavacane vṛddhiśabdasyāśeṣanāndaumukha-śrāddhakālopalacaṇaparatvāt karmāṅgaśrāddhakālā api saṃgṛhītāḥ |
[193] tenāha pāraskaraḥ |
niṣekakāle some ca sīmantonnayane tathā |
jñeyaṃ puṃsavane śrāddhaṃ karmāṅgaṃ vṛdbhivat kṛtam ||
niṣekakālaḥ ṣoḍaśarātrātmako bhāryāyā ṛtukālaḥ | tatra puṇyanakṣatrānvite garbhādhānākhyakarmāṅgaśrāddhaṃ kāryam | somaśabdo ’trāgnihocādikarmaṇām upalakṣaṇārthaḥ |
iti vṛddhiśrāddhanirūpaṇam |
atha kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ |
tatra vājasaneyasūtre kātyāyanaḥ |
aparapakṣe śrāddhaṃ kurvītārddhaṃ vā caturthyā yad ahaḥ sampadyeteti | apaprapakṣe pratipatprabhṛtyamāvāsyānte kṛṣṇapakṣe ūrddhvaṃ vā caturthyā yasmin vā ahani śrāddhasādhanasaṃpattis tasminn ahani śrāddhaṃ kurvīteti |
gautamo ’pi |
amāvāsyāyāṃ pitṛbhyo dadyāt pañcamīprabhṛti vāparapakṣasya yathāśraddhaṃ sarvasmin vā dravyadeśabrāhmaṇasaṃbhave kālaniyamaḥ śaktita iti |
aparapakṣe ekasminn ahani śrāddham ityekaḥ pakṣaḥ | pratyaham ity aparaḥ | kālaniyamaḥ śaktita iti tṛtīyaḥ | yathāsāmarthyaṃ kālavyavasthety arthaḥ | atra nityaṃ śrāddhaṃ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ | “śākenāpi nāparapakṣam atikrāmet” iti | yatt u mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇādiṣu “pratipaddhanalābhāya dvitīyā paśusaṃpade" ityādibhir vacanair aparapakṣāntargatāsu tithiṣu pratyekaṃ phalasaṃbandha ukto na tenāparapakṣe śrāddhasya nityatā híyate | darśapūrṇamāsavan nityatvakāmyatvayor upapatteḥ | yadāpi caitad ekasminn evāhani kriyate [194] tadāpy amāvāsyāśrāddhāt pṛthag eva kāryaṃ | “amāvāsyāṣṭakā vṛddhiḥ kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ” ityatra yājñavalkyavacane amāvāsyātaḥ kṛṣṇapakṣasya pṛthag upanyāsāt |
anena yad atra kaiścid uktaṃ amāvāsyādiśrāddhena mahāparapākṣikaṃ śrāddhaṃ vikalpata iti, tad apāstam | yat tu nigamasmaraṇam, “aparapakṣe yad ahaḥ saṃpadyetāmāvāsyāyāṃ viśeṣeṇeti", tat pṛthaktvāpṛthaktvobhayapakṣāvirodhīti na vikalpe pramāṇam iti smṛticandrikākāraḥ |
kecit tu vikalpam eva manyante |
āha āpastambaḥ | "māsi māsi kāryam aparapakṣasyāparāhṇaḥ śreyāṃs tathāparapakṣasya jaghanyāny ahānīti” | jaghanyāni daśamyādīni | naitat pūrveṣāṃ navamyādīnām aśreyastvābhidhānārthaṃ kiṃ tu pūrvebhyaḥ pareṣāṃ śreṣṭhatamattvapratipādanārthaṃ | tad etac caturdaśīṃ varjayann āha manuḥ |
kṛṣṇapahṣe daśamyādau varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śrāddhe praśastās tithayo yathaitā na tathetarāḥ || iti |
atra caturdaśī nātyantaṃ varjanīyā kiṃ tu śastrādihatavyatiriktadaivatyaśrāddha evety āha yājñavatakyaḥ |
pratipatprabhṛtiṣv ekāṃ varjayitvā caturdaśīm |
śastreṇa tu hatā ye vai teṣāṃ tatra pradīyate || iti |
śastragrahaṇam anyeṣām api keṣāṃcin maraṇakāraṇānām upalakṣaṇārtham iti vakṣyate |
[195]
atha bhādrapadāparapakṣaḥ |
atra yady apy aparapakṣe śrāddhaṃ kurvīta, śākenāpi nāparapakṣam atikrāmed ityādibhir dvādaśāparapakṣagrāhakaiḥ sāmānyavacanaiḥ bhādrapadāparapakṣo ’pi gṛhīta eva | tathāpi tasya puṇyatamatvapratipādanārthaṃ tadatikrame pratyavāyapratipādanārthaṃ ca smṛtipurāṇādiṣu viśeṣatas tasyābhidhānaṃ kṛtam | tad yathā viṣṇudharmottare |
uttarād ayanād rājan śreṣṭhaṃ syād dakṣiṇāyanam |
yāmyāyanāc caturmāsaṃ tatra supte tu keśave ||
prauṣṭhapadyāḥ paraḥ pakṣas tatrāpi ca viśeṣataḥ |
pañcamyūrddhaṃ tu tatrāpi daśamyūrddhvaṃ tatopyati |
maghāyuktā tu tatrāpi śastā rājaṃs trayodaśīti ||
uttarāyaṇād dakṣiṇāyanaṃ śrāddhakaraṇe śreṣṭham | sarvasmād dakṣiṇāyannād api tanmadhyavartyupendranidrādinādāṣāḍhyāḥ paurṇamāsyāḥ prabhṛti māmacatuṣṭayaṃ śreṣṭham | tasmād api sarvasmāt proṣṭhapadasya māsasyāparapakṣaḥ | tatrāpi pañcamyā ūrddhvaṃ daśadivasāḥ, tatrāpi daśamyā ūrddhvaṃ pañca | tatrāpi maghāyuktā trayodaśīti
brahmapurāṇe |
aśvayukkṛṣṇapakṣe tu śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ dine dine |
tribhāgahīnaṃ pakṣaṃ vā tribhāgaṃ tv arddham eva vā ||
śyāmākais tu viśeṣeṇa prasiddhais tu pitṛpriyaiḥ |
[196] atra viṣṇudharmottare "prauṣṭhapadamāsaṃbandhini kṛṣṇapakṣe śrāddham" ity uktam, brahmapurāṇe tu "aśvayuṅmāsasaṃbandhini" iti, so 'yam anayor visaṃvādaḥ parihriyate | tathāhi iha khalu śuklapakṣapratipady upakramya māsanāmapravartikāṃ paurṇamāsīṃ madhyāvayavīkṛtyāmāvāsyāntacāndramāsaṃ dākṣiṇātyāḥ parikalpayanti | auttarās tu kṛṣṇapakṣapratipady upakramya nāmapravartakapaurṇamāsyantam | evaṃ ca sati dākṣiṇātyavyavahāreṇa prauṣṭhapadayuktāyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ prauṣṭhapadāmāvāsyādyapakṣasamāptau taduttaraḥ pakṣaḥ prauṣṭhapadyo bhavati | uttaravyavahāreṇa tu tasyām eva paurṇamāsyāṃ prauṣṭapadamāsasamāptau taduttaraḥ pakṣaḥ aśvayuṅmāsamadhye bhavatītyeka eva vyavahāradvayena vyapadeśadvayaṃ labhate |
ādityapurāṇe |
prāvṛṭṛtau yamaḥ pratān pitṝṃś cātha yamālayāt |
visarjayati mānuṣye kṛtvā śūnyaṃ svakaṃ puram ||
kṣudhārtāḥ kīrtayantaś ca duṣkṛtaṃ te svayaṃ kṛtam |
kāṅkṣanti putrapautrebhyaḥ pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyutam ||
tasmāt tāṃs tatra vidhinā tarpayet pāyasena tu |
madhvājyatilamiśreṇa tathā śītena cāmbhasā ||
grāsamātraṃ paragṛahād annaṃ yaḥ prāpnuyān naraḥ |
bhikṣāmātreṇa yaḥ prāṇān saṃdhārayati vā svayam ||
yo vā saṃdhārayed dehaṃ pratyahaṃ svātmavikrayāt |
śrāddhaṃ tenāpi kartavyaṃ tailair dravyaiḥ sumañcitaiḥ || iti |
[197] nāgarakhaṇḍe pitṝn prati brahmavacanam |
āṣāḍhyāḥ pañcame pakṣe kanyāsaṃsthe divākare |
yo vai śrāddhaṃ naraḥ kuryād ekasminn api vāsare |
tasya savatsaraṃ yāvat tṛptāḥ syuḥ pitaro dhruvam |
śāṭyāyanir apy āha |
nabhasyasyāpare pakṣe tithiṣoḍaśakas tu yaḥ |
kanyāgatānvitaś cet syāt sa kālaḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
brahmapurāṇa-mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇayoḥ |
divyabhaumāntarīkṣāṇi sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca |
piṇḍam icchanti pitaraḥ kanyārāśigate ravau ||
kanyāgate savitari yāny ahāni tu ṣoḍaśa |
kratubhis tāni tulyāni devo nārāyaṇo ’bravīt ||
rājasūyāśvamedhābhyāṃ ya icched durlabhaṃ phalam |
apy ambuśākamūlānnaiḥ pitṝn kanyāgate ’rcayet ||
yady api kanyāgate māsamātraṃ bhavati tathāpy aparapakṣasyaiva paitryatvād vacanāntarasaṃvādāc ca tatraiva śrāddham ity anusaṃdheyam |
ādityapurāṇe |
kanyāgate savitari yāny ahāni tu ṣoḍaśa |
kratubhis tāni tulyāni pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti |
atra yady api bahuṣu vacaneṣu pakṣasyaiva śrāddhakālatvam uktaṃ, pakṣaś ca pañcadaśatithyātmaka eva | tathāpi kadācit tithivṛddhau ṣoḍaśadinātmako bhavati | tatraikadinahānir mābhūd ity anenābhiprāyeṇātra tithiṣoḍaśaka [198] ity uktaṃ | amāvāsyottarāṃ pratipadam api vā saṃgrahītuṃ pūrvāṃ paurṇamāsīṃ vā tasyāṃ hi nāndīmukhānāṃ śrāddhaṃ vakṣyate | atra pakṣamadhye yasmin kasmiṃścid apy ahani kanyāsaṃkrame sati pratipadādiṣoḍaśāpy ahāny upādeyāni | na tu saṃkramaṇāt pūrvāṇi heyānīty evaṃ veditavyam | ata evāha bṛhanmanuḥ |
madhye vā yadi vāpy ante yatra kanyāṃ ravir vrajet |
sa pakṣaḥ sakalaḥ pūjyaḥ śrāddhaṃ tatra vidhīyate ||
atra kanyāgatattvasyoktiḥ praśastataratvapratipādanārthā, na tu kanyāgatatvarāhitye tatparityāgārthā | tathā cādityapurāṇe |
pakṣāntare ’pi kanyāsthe ravau śrāddhaṃ praśasyate |
kanyāgate pañcame tu viśeṣeṇaiva kārayet ||
āṣāḍhīm avadhiṃ kṛtvā yaḥ syāt pakṣas tu pañcamaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ tatra prakurvīta kanyāṃ gacchati vā na veti ||
kārṣṇājiniḥ |
putrān āyus tathārogyam aiśvaryyam (?) atulaṃ tathā |
prāpnoti pañcame datvā śrāddhaṃ kāmāṃś ca puṣkalān ||
evaṃ bhādrapadāparapakṣe kṛte viśiṣṭeṣṭaprāptir uktā | tasminn akṛte tv aniṣṭaprāptim āha |
kārṣṇājiniḥ |
pretās taṃ caiva hiṃsanti pañcamaṃ yo vyatikramet |
tasmān nātikramed vidvān pañcame paitṛkaṃ vidhim ||
[199] nāgarakhaṇḍe |
āṣāḍhyāḥ pañcame pakṣe yaḥ śrāddhaṃ na kariṣyati |
śākenāpi daridro ’pi so ’ntyajatvam upeṣyati ||
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ bhojyaṃ sparśanaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ tathā |
tena sārddhaṃ na kartavyaṃ havyaṃ kavyaṃ kadācana ||
ye na śrāddhaṃ kariṣyanti te te pāpatarā narāḥ |
bhaviṣyanti na saṃdehaḥ santatyā ca vivarjitāḥ |
na sukhaṃ dhanadhānyaṃ ca teṣāṃ bhāvi kadācana || iti |
brahmapurāṇe |
sūrye kanyāgate kuryāt śrāddhaṃ yo na gṛhāśramī |
dhanaṃ putrāḥ kutas tasya pitṛniḥśvāsapīḍayā ||
na santi pitaraś ceti kṛtvā manasi yo naraḥ |
śrāddhaṃ na kurute tatra tasya raktaṃ pibanti te ||
atha kenacit prakāreṇa śrāddham antareṇaivokasya pakṣasyātikrame tannimittasya śrāddhasya taduttaradineṣv avadhiviśeṣāvacchinneṣu kartavyatocyate yamasmṛtau |
haṃse śrāddhaṃ tu kanyāsthe śākenāpi gṛhe vasan |
pañcamyor antare dadyād ubhayor api pakṣayoḥ ||
haṃsaḥ sūryaḥ | ubhayapakṣapañcamyor antara iti bhādrapadakṛṣṇapañcamyāḥ parastād āśvinaśuklapañcamyāḥ pūrvam antarālakāla ity arthaḥ | anenāśvinaśuklapañcamīparyantaṃ śrāddham abhyanujñātaṃ bhavati |
brahmapurāṇe |
[200]         yāvac ca kanyātulayoḥ kramād āste divākaraḥ |
tāvac chrāddhasya kālaḥ syāt śūnyaṃ pretapuraṃ tadā ||
tathā |
kanyāgate savitari pitaro yānti vai sutān |
śūnyā pretapurau sarvā yāvad vṛścikadarśanam ||
tato vṛścikasamprāptā nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ |
punaś ca bhavanaṃ yānti śāpaṃ datvā sudāruṇam ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
na dadāti naraḥ śrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ candramaṅkṣaye|
kṣutpipāsāparītāśāḥ pitaras tasya duḥkhitāḥ ||
pretapakṣaṃ pratīkṣante guruvāñchāsamanvitāḥ |
karṣakā jaladaṃ yadvad divānakam atandritāḥ ||
pretapakṣe ’py atikrānte yāvat kanyāgato raviḥ |
tāvac chrāddhaṃ pravāñchanti dattaṃ vai pitaraḥ sutaiḥ ||
tatas tulāgate 'py eke sūrye vāñchanti pārthiva |
śrāddhaṃ svavaṃśajair dattaṃ kṣutpipāsāsamākulāḥ ||
tasminn api vyatikrānte kāle vṛścikage ravau |
nirāśāḥ pitaro dīnās tato yānti nijālayam ||
māsadvayaṃ pratīkṣante gṛhadvāraṃ samāśritāḥ |
vāyabhūtāḥ pipāsārtāḥ kṣutkṣāmāḥ pitaro nṛṇām ||
[201]        yāvat kanyāgataḥ sūryaḥ tulāsthaś ca mahīpate |
sumantur āha |
kanyārāśau mahātejā yāvat tiṣṭhed vibhāvasuḥ |
tasmāt kālāt tu vai deyaṃ vṛścikaṃ yāvad āgamaḥ ||
yeyaṃ dīpānvitā rājan khyātā pañcadaśī bhuvi |
tasyāṃ dadyān na ced dattaṃ pitṝṇāṃ vai mahālaye ||
atra mahālayo bhādrapadāparapakṣaḥ |
yat tu jātūkarṇyenoktaṃ |
ākāṅkṣanti svapitaraḥ pañcamaṃ pacamāśritāḥ |
tasmāt tatraiva dātavyaṃ dattam anyatra niṣphalam || iti |
tat phalātiśayahāniparaṃ, na sarvathā phalahānipratipādakam |
athātra trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ prapañcenodyate |
manusmṛtau |
yat kiṃcin madhunā miśraṃ pradadyāt tu trayodaśīm |
tad apy akṣayam eva syāt varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
yamaḥ |
gāthāś ca pitṛbhir gītāḥ kīrtayanti purāvidaḥ |
api naḥ svakule bhūyāt yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
atha pitṛgīte gāthe bhavataḥ |
viṣṇuḥ |
api jāyeta so 'smākaṃ kule kaścin narottamaḥ |
[202]        prāvṛṭkāle site pakṣe trayodaśyāṃ samāhitaḥ ||
madhūnvaṭena (?) yaḥ śrāddhaṃ pāyasena samācaret |
kārtikaṃ sakalaṃ vāpi pracchāye kuñjarasya ca ||
paiṭhīnasir apy āha |
chāgena sarvalohena varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca |
putro vā yadi vā pautro yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm ||
jāyeta kim asau kaścid dhārmiko vaṃśavardhanaḥ |
mahābhārate |
api naḥ svakule bhūyāt yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm |
maghāsu sarpiṣā yuktaṃ pāyasaṃ dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
jātaṃ putraṃ praśaṃsanti pippalaṃ śakunā dūva ||
madhumāṃsena khaḍgena payasā pāyasena vā |
eṣa dāsyati naḥ śrāddhaṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
yo dadāti gayāsthaś ca sarvam ānantyam aśnute |
tathā varṣātrayodaśyāṃ maghāsu ca viśeṣataḥ ||
prauṣṭhapadyām atītāyāṃ maghāyuktāṃ trayodaśīm |
prāpya śrāddhaṃ hi kartavyaṃ madhunā pāyasena ca ||
[203] praśastatarā caiṣā tithir bhādrapadāparapākṣikābhyo ’pi tithibhyaḥ | tathā ca brahmapurāṇe |
yo vā saṃvarddhayed dehaṃ pratyahaṃ svātmavikrayāt |
śrāddhaṃ tenāpi kartavyaṃ tais tair dravyaiḥ susaṃcitaiḥ |
trayodaśyāṃ prayatnena varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
nāsmāt parataraḥ kālaḥ śrāddheṣv anyeṣu vartate
yad atra pitaro dattaṃ gṛhṇanti smṛtam akṣayam ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
uttarād ayanād rājan śreṣṭhaṃ syād dakṣiṇāyanam |
cāturmasyam tu tatrāpi prasupte keśave ’dhikam ||
prauṣṭhapadyāḥ paraḥ pakṣas tatrāpi ca viśeṣataḥ |
pañcapūrvaṃ tu tatrāpi daśapūrvaṃ tatopyati |
maghāyuktā ca tatrāpi śastā rājan trayodaśīti ||
atra pañcapūrvaṃ pañcamīm ārabhya, daśapūrvaṃ daśamīm ārabhyety arthaḥ | āvaśyakaṃ cedaṃ śrāddham uktam viṣṇudharmottare |
śrāddhakālam amāvāsyā nityaṃ pārthivasattama |
paurṇamāsī tathā māghī śrāvaṇī ca nṛpottama ||
prauṣṭhapadyām atītāyāṃ tathā kṛṣṇā trayodaśī |
aṣṭakānvaṣṭakās tisras tathaiva ca nṛpottama |
etāni śrāddhakālāni nityāny āha prajāpatiḥ |
[204]         śrāddham eteṣv kurvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate || iti |
