viṣṇur api amāvāsyās tisro ’ṣṭakās tisro ’nvaṣṭakāḥ | māghīprauṣṭhapadyūrdhvaṃ kṛṣṇā trayodaśīty upanyasya etāni śrāddhakālānīty amum eva ślokam uktavān | athātra niṣedhavacanāni | jyotirbṛhaspatiḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśyāṃ yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute naraḥ |
pañcatvaṃ tasya jānīyāt jyeṣṭhaputrasya niścitaṃ ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
gṛhī trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ na kuryāt putravān api |
upavāsaṃ ca saṃkrāntau grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ ||
trayodaśyāṃ tu vai śrāddhaṃ na kuryāt putravān gṛhī |
neṣyate copavāsaś ca kaiścid apy ayanadaye ||
āpastambaḥ |
trayodaśyāṃ bahuputro bahumitro darśanīyāpatyo yuvamāriṇas tu bhavantīti |
kātyāyanaś cāha |
yuvānas tatra mriyanta iti |
skānde nāgarakhaṇḍe |
yo vāñchati naro muktiṃ pitṛbhiḥ saha cātmanaḥ |
asantānaś ca yas tasya śrāddhe proktā trayodaśī |
santānayukto yaḥ kuryāt tasya vaṃśakṣayo bhavet ||
[204]                 ānartta uvāca |
trayodaśyāṃ kṛte śrāddhe kasmād vaṃśakṣayo bhavet |
etan me sarvam ācakṣa vistareṇa mahāmune ||
bhartṛyajña uvāca |
āsīd rathantare kalpe pūrvaṃ pārthivasattama |
sitāśvo nāma pañcāladeśīyaḥ pitṛbhaktimān ||
madhunā kālaśākena khaḍgamāṃsena kevalam |
sa hi śrāddhaṃ trayodaśyāṃ kurute pāyasena vā ||
kadācid brāhmaṇair bhūyaḥ sa pṛṣṭaḥ kautukānvitaiḥ |
anyeṣu vidyamāneṣu vicitreṣu kuto nṛpa ||
asvādukhaḍgamāṃsaṃ ca kālaśākaṃ ca yacchasi |
apratyākhyeyam atyājyaṃ śrāddhe dattaṃ havir dvijaiḥ ||
iti tad bhujyate ’smābhis tadā me kāraṇaṃ vada |
etac chrutvā vacas teṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ vaco ’bravīt ||
avācyam api vakṣyāmi śṛṇudhvaṃ susamāhitāḥ |
aham āsan purā hiṃsro lubdhakaḥ pitṛbhaktimān ||
bhraman kadācit prāpto ’haṃ agniveśmāśramaṃ niśi |
śiṣyānupadiśan vipro dūrasthena mayā śrutaḥ ||
agniveśma uvāca |
ṛkṣe paitre yadā candro haṃsaś caiva kare sthitaḥ |
trayodaśyāṃ tu sā chāyā vijñeyā kuñjarāhvayā ||
śrāddheṣv atipraśastā sā pitṝṇāṃ tṛptikāriṇau |
tatprātaḥ śrāddhūsaṃbhārāḥ saṃbhriyantām ataḥ śubhāḥ ||
[206] “paitram ṛkṣaṃ” maghā | "haṃsaḥ " sūryaḥ | "kare" haste |
sitāśva uvāca |
iti tasya muner vākyam ākarṇyedam acintayam |
ahaṃ cāpi kariṣyāmi prātaḥ śrāddhakriyām iti ||
tataḥ prabhāte śrāddhīyadravyārthaṃ paryaṭan vane |
khaḍgamāṃsaṃ kālaśākaṃ madhu ca prāptavān aham ||
yathopapannais tair dravyais trayodaśyāṃ samāhitaḥ |
nijān pitṝn samuddiśya svajātīyān tv abhojayam ||
śrāddhena tādṛśenāpi pitaro dvādaśābdikīm |
tṛptim āpur ahaṃ cāpi nṛpo jātismaro ’bhavam ||
ato maghātrayodaśyām etair eva hi sādhanaiḥ |
karomi śraddhayā śrāddhaṃ tatprabhāvam anusmaran ||
tasya tad vacanaṃ śrutvā te cānye ca janā bhuvi |
nabhasyasya trayodaśyāṃ śrāddhaṃ cakrur mudānvitāḥ ||
prāpuś ca paramāṃ siddhiṃ spardhamānāḥ surottamaiḥ |
īrṣālavas tatas teṣu vasurudrādayaḥ surāḥ ||
asaṃśayaṃ trayodaśyāṃ śrāddhakartṝn kṣudhānvitāḥ |
adyaprabhṛti yaḥ śrāddhaṃ trayodaśyāṃ kariṣyati ||
kanyāsaṃsthe sahasrāṃśau tasya syād vaṃśasaṃkṣayaḥ |
iti śāpena devānāṃ nirdagdheyaṃ mahātithiḥ |
tataḥ prabhṛti naitasyāṃ kriyate śrāddham uttamam ||
yaḥ pramādena kurute tasya syād aṃśasaṃkṣayaḥ |
[207]        etat trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ vaṃśacchedasya kāraṇam ||
ataḥ śrāddhaṃ vinā deyaṃ taddine madhupāyasam |
khaḍgamāṃsaṃ kālaśākaṃ vārdhrīṇasāsyam eva ca ||
vārdhrīṇaso vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇo vṛddhacchāgaḥ |
tasyābhāve pradātavyaṃ kṣīrodanam anuttamam |
tasminn ahani viprebhyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ tuṣṭaye nṛpa |
tasyābhāve ’pi dātavyaṃ jalaṃ tilavimiśritam || iti |
athātra kecid āhuḥ | trayodaśīśrāddhaniṣiddhavākyānām aprāmāṇyam eva | yat kiṃcin madhunā miśram ityādimanuvacanavirodhāt | manusmṛtir hi svādhyāyāt prasiddhatvāt bahubhiḥ saṃpradāyena paṭhyamānatvāt apabhraṣṭapāṭhāsaṃbhavāt mūlāntarā veti kṣudratarebhyo nāmamātrenāprasiddhebhyaḥ kenacid apy apaṭhyamānebhyo ’navasthitapāṭhebhyaḥ saṃbhāvitabhrāntyādimūlebhyaḥ svavirodhapratyakṣaśrutivad balīyasīti |
ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
vedārthopanibandhṛtvāt prāmāṇyaṃ tu manoḥ smṛtam |
manvarthaviparītā tu yā smṛtiḥ sā na śasyate || iti |
manur apy āha |
yaḥ kaścit kasyacid dharmā manunā parikīrtitaḥ |
sa sarve vihito vede sarvajñānamayo hi saḥ || iti |
ucyate | yat tāvad uktaṃ manusmṛteḥ śrutitulyatvena vidheḥ smṛtyantarebhyo balīyastvam iti tatkalpamātraṃ | bṛhaspativacanaṃ (?) manvarthaviparītānāṃ buddhādismṛtīnām aprāmāṇyam āha |
nanu śiṣṭatraivarṇikasvīkṛtānāṃ [208] kātyāyanādismṛtīnāṃ vedārthapratibaddhatvād ity aprāmāṇyahetvavidyamānatvāt ato manuśabdenātra sarve vedārthasmartāraḥ kātyāyanādayo ’bhyupalakṣyante | atha vā manajñāna iti dhātupāṭhāt vedārthajñānavān munir manur ucyate |
anye tv āhuḥ | kasyacid api śrāddhasya niṣedhānupapatteḥ niṣedhavākyānām aprāmāṇyam iti | tathāhi na tāvan nityasya śrāddhasya niṣedho ’yaṃ |
yata āha manuḥ |
dadyād ahar ahaḥ śrāddham annādyenodakena vā |
payomūlaphalair vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ prītim āharan || iti |
dakṣo ’py āha |
asnātvā vāpy ahutvā vā adatvā yaḥ samaśnute |
devādīnām ṛṇo bhūtvā aprajaḥ sa vrajaty adhaḥ || iti |
ahar ahaḥ svādhyāyaṃ kuryād udapātrāt (?) tathainaṃ pitṛyajñaṃ samāpnotīti | evam etair vacanair nityasya śrāddhasyāhar ahar vidhānād akaraṇād adhogamanābhidhānāt trayodaśyāṃ tadanuṣṭhānam arhati | nāpi naimittikasya niṣedhaḥ | na tāvan mṛtāhādinimittakasya |
yata āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
mṛtāhani tu dātavyaṃ pratimāsaṃ tu vatsaram |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivamādyam ekādaśe ’hani || iti |
kātyāyano ’py āha | tataḥ saṃvatsare saṃvatsare pretāyānnaṃ dadyāt | yasminn ahani pretaḥ syād iti siddhāntenaikoddiṣṭaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | evam anyeṣv api smṛtipurāṇeṣu ca | mṛtāhādivihitasya mṛtāhādiṣv apekṣitatvāt tadanuṣṭhānaṃ na pratiṣedhaḥ [209] pratiṣeddhum arhati | nāpi vṛddhyādinimittasya "putrajanmani kartavyaṃ jātakarma samaṃ naraiḥ” ityādibhir vākyaiḥ putrajanmādikāleṣu jātakarmavad āvaśyakatvena vihitatvāt tadanuṣṭhānam api pratiṣedhāḥ pratiṣeddhum arhanti | nāpi trayodaśīnimittakasya pārvaṇasyaiva, yataḥ
anye trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ putravaty aśubhāya vai |
ekasyaiva na tat kuryāt pārvaṇaṃ tu samācaret ||
iti tasyāvasyakatvena vidhānāt ekasyaiva vargasya na kuryāt pārvaṇaṃ samācared iti pitṛvargasya mātāmahavargasya ca ity evaṃ vargadvayasya kuryād ity arthaḥ | na caikavargāya dīyamānasya śrāddhasya niṣedhaḥ | tathāvidhasya tannimittakasya śrāddhasya prāpter abhāvāt |         pitaro yatra pūjyante tatra mātāmahā dhruvam
iti dhaumyavacanena vargadvayasyaiva śrāddhavidhidarśanāt | nāpi kāmyasya, tasyāpi trayodaśīsaṃbandhenaiva viśeṣavihitatvāt | tad evaṃviṣayāsaṃbhavāt pratiṣedhavākyānām aprāmāṇyam ity ucyate | nityasya naimittikasya vaiśeṣikasya ca kadācit pratiṣedhaḥ | yathā sūtakādau viśeṣavihitatvasyāpi trayodaśīnimittakasya viśeṣavacanena tanmātraviṣayakena pratiṣedhaḥ saṃbhavati | yathā nātirātre ṣoḍaśinaṃ gṛhṇātīty atra grahaṇapratiṣedhaḥ | trayodaśīśrāddhasya ca nityavad yathākathaṃcidanuṣṭhāne prāpte satyām api dhaumyasmṛtau vyāmohād ekavargīyaśrāddhasyānuṣṭhāne pratiṣedhasaṃbhavāt | tasmād viṣayāsaṃbhavāt pratiṣedhavākyānāma prāmāṇyam iti mataṃ | tasmād atra vyavasthā vaktavyā | tatra tāvac chaṅkhadharaḥ | maghātrayodaśīśrāddhavidhivākyāni (?) vargoddeśenānuṣṭhīyamānaṃ śrāddham āśrayante | niṣedhās tv ekavargodeśenānuṣṭhīyimānaṃ (?) śrāddham āśrayante | ata eva kārṣṇājiniḥ |
śrāddhaṃ naivaikavargasya trayodaśyām upakramet |
[210]        na tṛptās tatra ye yasya prajā hiṃsanti tasya te || iti |
ekasya vargasya pitrādimātrasya mātāmahādimātrasya vā nopakramet | kiṃ tu mātāmahādisahitasya kuryāt | ṣaṇṇāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ śrāddhākaraṇe trayodaśīpratiṣedhadoṣa ity arthaḥ | anye tu santi pramītānāṃ pitṛbhāvaṃ gatānāṃ vargāḥ pitṛvyavargo bhrātṛvargo mātulavarga ityevamādayaḥ | atha vā pitṛpakṣīyāṇāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ vargo mātṛpakṣīyāṇāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ cety evaṃ vargadvayaṃ | eṣāṃ vargāṇāṃ madhye śrāddhe labdhādhikāreṇa puruṣeṇa dināntare kasyacid vargasya śrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ kariṣyate vā kasyacid ity evaṃ kiṃcit kāraṇam anusaṃdhāya maghātrayodaśyāṃ na kartavyaṃ | kiṃ tu sarveṣāṃ vargāṇāṃ kartavyaṃ | yato maghātrayodaśyāḥ pitṛtṛptikāraṇabhūtānāṃ tithīnāṃ madhye śreṣṭhatamatvāt sarveṣāṃ pitṝṇāṃ madhumiśrapāyasādidravyeṇa dīyamānasya śrāddhasyāpekṣitatvāt | yebhya eva na dīyate ta eva kupitāḥ śrāddhakartuḥ prajāṃ nāśayanti | tad evaṃ niṣedhavākyānām ekasyaiva vargasya kriyamāṇaṃ śraddhaṃ viṣaya iti | anye tv āhuḥ, putravatā gṛhiṇā kriyamāṇaṃ trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ viṣaya iti |
jyotirbṛhaspatiḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe trayodaśyāṃ yaḥ śrāddhaṃ kurute naraḥ |
pañcatvaṃ tasya jānīyāt jyeṣṭhaputrasya niścitaṃ ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
gṛhau trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ na kuryāt putravān api |
upavāsaṃ ca saṅkrāntau grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ ||
smaranti ca |
[211]        trayodaśyāṃ tu vai śrāddhaṃ na kuryāt putravān gṛhī |
neṣyate cāpavāsaś ca kaiścid apy ayanadvaye ||
āpastambaḥ |
trayodaśyāṃ bahuputro bahumitro darśanīyāpatyo yuvamāriṇas
tu bhavanti || iti |
kātyāyanaḥ: "yuvānas tatra mriyante" iti |
kecit punaḥ putramaraṇarūpeṇa doṣeṇa na trayodaśīśrāddhanivṛttiṃ manyante | te hy āhuḥ | naiyogikatvād asya phalasya kāmyamānam evaitad bhavati | na tv evam eva | tad uktaṃ,
kāmyamānaṃ phalaṃ jñātaṃ nānicchos tad bhaviṣyati || iti |
tasmāt kriyamāṇe ’pi śrāddhe na putramaraṇarūpaṃ kāmyamānaṃ karmaphalaṃ bhavatīti gṛhiṇā putravatāpi kartavyaṃ trayodaśīśrāddham iti |
tad ayuktaṃ |
trayodaśyāṃ tu vai śrāddhaṃ na kuryāt putravān gṛhī |
tyādiniṣedhavākyāpekṣitānartharūpaphalaviśeṣaprāpaṇaṃ,
pañcatvaṃ tasya jānīyāj jyeṣṭhaputrasya niścitaṃ |
ityādivākyaiḥ kriyate na punaḥ kasyacit putramaraṇecchor abhicārarūpaṃ trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ vidhīyate | niṣedhyamānakriyāphalaṃ cānartharūpatvān na kaścid icchati | na ca sakṛduccaritaṃ vākyaṃ kaṃcit puruṣaṃ praty arthatvena kaṃcic ca praty anarthatvena putramaraṇaṃ bodhayituṃ śaktaṃ, vākyabhedaprasaṅgāt | yat tu kaiścid uktaṃ maghātrayodaśyāṃ śrāddhācaraṇasya dharmarūpatvāt tadanuṣṭhāne putramaraṇādyaniṣṭaṃ bhavatīti na yuktam iti | tathā tṛptāḥ santaḥ pitaro ’niṣṭaṃ phalaṃ prayacchantīty api na yuktam iti | tan na vaktavyaṃ | yataḥ [212] śāstrabodhito ’rtho na yuktimātreṇa nivartayituṃ śakyate | śāstraikagamyasyārthasya yuktyaviṣayatvāt | yat tu mahābhārate dānadharmeṣu |
jñātīnāṃ tu bhavec chreṣṭhaḥ kurvan śrāddhaṃ trayodaśīm |
nāvaśyaṃ tu yuvāno ’sya pramīyante narā gṛhe ||
iti yuvamaraṇarūpasya doṣasya āpastambādyuktasya nirākaraṇaṃ kṛtaṃ | tad api nātyantaṃ kṛtam | nāvaśyam ity abhidhānena doṣasya pākṣikatvābhyanujñānāt | yat tv avaśyaṃ na mriyanta eveti smṛticandrikākārasya vyākhyānaṃ tac chaṅkhadhara-hariharādīnāṃ na saṃmatam iti |
anye tu,
ayanadvitaye śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaddvitaye tathā |
yugādiṣu ca sarvāsu piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte ||
iti pulastyavacanād bhādrapadāparapakṣatrayodaśyāś ca yugāditvāt piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ vinā śrāddhaṃ kartavyam iti niṣedhavākyānāṃ piṇḍadānasahitaṃ śrāddhaṃ viṣaya ity āhuḥ |
kecit tu pāyasādidravyavyatiriktadravyeṇa śrāddhācaraṇaṃ niṣiddham ity evaṃvidhaṃ viṣayam āhuḥ |
tad ayukaṃ, pāyasādidravyavyatiriktadravyeṇāmaśrāddhakaraṇe nāgarakhaṇḍe doṣasyoktatvāt |
hariharas tv atraivaṃ kāraṇam uktavān |
tad ayuktaṃ, apadārthavākyārthāpatteḥ | putravān gṛhī trayodaśīśrāddhaṃ na kuryād ity ete padārthaḥ | pāyasavyatirikadravyeṇa śrāddhaṃ na kartavyam iti vākyārtha iti, hariharaḥ svayaṃ tv īdṛśīṃ vyavasthām āha | bhādrapadāparapakṣāntargatamaghānvitatrayodaśyāṃ nityaṃ mahāphalaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ | tadartham eva ca pitṝṇāṃ paurāṇikīṣu gāthāsu putraprārthanaṃ mahatā prabandhena śrūyate | atas trayodaśīmātrasaṃbandhīni niṣedhavākyāni sāmānyaśāstrarūpatvād bhādrapadāparapakṣatrayodaśīṃ parityajya śeṣāsv ekādaśasu trayodaśīṣu kṛtārthayany ātmānam iti | etat tu viśeṣato bhādrapadāparapakṣatrayodaśyām eva śrāddha[213]niṣedhasya nāgarakhaṇḍetihāse spaṣṭam eva darśitatvāt | anatilāślāghyaṃ kāmyaśrāddhaviṣayatvena vā pratiṣedhavacanāni vyavasthāpanīyāni | yataḥ kāmasaṃbandha eva śrāddhe doṣaḥ smaryate |
tathā hi trayodaśīśrāddhakartur jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ bahuputratvādiphalaṃ saṃkīrtya vyāsa-āpastambābhyāṃ yuvamaraṇalakṣaṇo doṣo ’pi tatraivoditaḥ | tad evaṃ niṣedhavākyānām etāvanto viṣayāḥ, ekavargaśrāddhaṃ piṇḍavac chrāddhaṃ putravad gṛhamedhikartṛkaṃ śrāddhaṃ kāmyaśrāddham iti | vidhivākyānāṃ cānyāni
śrāddhāni viṣaya iti manyante |
iti maghātrayodaśīśrāddhanirūpaṇaṃ |
athāparapakṣa eva caturdaśīśrāddhaṃ |
tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratipatprabhṛtiṣv ekāṃ varjayitvā caturdaśīṃ |
śastreṇa tu hatā ye vai tebhyas tatra pradīyate ||
mārkaṇḍeya-brahmapurāṇyoḥ |
yuvānaḥ pitaro yasya mṛtāḥ śastreṇa vā hatāḥ |
tena kāryaṃ caturdaśyāṃ teṣāṃ tṛptim abhīpsatā ||
brahmavaivartta-vāyupurāṇayoḥ |
yuvānas tu gṛhe yasya mṛtās teṣāṃ pradāpayet |
śastreṇa vā hatā ye vai teṣāṃ dadyāc caturdaśīṃ ||
vṛddhamanuḥ |
caturdaśīṃ varjayitvā śrāddhaṃ śrāddhaviśāradāḥ |
[214]        prīyante pitaras tasya ye ca śastrahatā raṇe ||
kāmyaprakrame ca manuḥ |
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ tu suprajāḥ |
prīyante pitaras tasya ye ca śastrahatā raṇe || iti |
śastragrahaṇam upalakṣaṇaṃ |
brahmapurāṇe |
prāyo’naśanaśastrāgniviṣodbandhaninān tathā |
caturdaśyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ tṛptyartham iti niścayaḥ ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
yeṣāṃ ca śastramṛtyuḥ syād apamṛtyur athāpi vā |
upasargīt mṛtānāṃ ca viṣamṭayum upeyuṣāṃ ||
vahinā ca pradagdhānāṃ jalamṛtyum upeyuṣāṃ
sarpavyāghrahatānāṃ ca śṛṅgair uddbandhanair api ||
śrāddhaṃ teṣāṃ prakartavyaṃ caturdaśyāṃ narādhipa |
teṣāṃ tasmin kṛte tṛptis tatas tatpakṣajā bhavet ||
apamṛtyur akālamṛtyur aprāptajarasām eva mṛtyuri ty arthaḥ | yakṣabhūtagrahādyupasṛṣṭānāṃ yan maraṇaṃ tad upasargān maraṇaṃ | tatpakṣajā sarvasminn aparapakṣe śrāddhakaraṇāt yāvatī tṛptis tāvatīty arthaḥ |
marīcir api |
viṣaśastraśvāpadāhitiryagbrāhmaṇaghātināṃ |
caturdaśyāṃ kriyā kārya anyeṣāṃ tu vigarhitā ||
viṣādibhir ghāto yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ | anena śastrādihatānām eva [215] caturdaśīti kālamātraṃ niyamyate | na tu caturdaśyām eva śastrādihatānāṃ | kiṃ tu teṣām anyasminn api kāle bhavaty eva śrāddham iti | pracetā api |
vṛkṣārohaṇalohādyair vidyujjvālāviṣādibhiḥ |
nakhadaṃṣṭrair vipannānāṃ teṣāṃ śastā caturdaśī ||
ādiśabdena giriśikharādiviṣamasthalārohaṇapatana aguḍādyabhighātodbandhanopaghātādayaḥ prāguktasadharmāṇo maraṇopāyāḥ saṃgṛhyante | yat tūktaviruddhārthaṃ śākaṭāyanavacanam
jalāgnibhyāṃ vipannānāṃ saṃnyāse vā gṛhe pathi |
śrāddhaṃ kurvīta teṣāṃ vai varjayitvā caturdaśīm ||
iti, tat prāyaścittādyarthavihitajalāgnyādimaraṇayuktaprāṇiviṣayaṃ | ye hi pāpamṛtyavas teṣām atra caturdaśyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ | vṛkṣārohaṇetyādau pracetovacane apamṛtyuprāyapāṭhāt | ata eva pramīte patyau vihitāgnipraveśavidhinānumaraṇakṛtāṃ yoṣitām api caturdaśīśrāddhaṃ na kartavyaṃ | tad evam avihitair jvalanādibhir mṛtānāṃ manu-brahmapurāṇavacana-prāmāṇyāt saṃgrāmaprāyopaśeśanamṛtānāṃ ca kṛṣṇacaturdaśīśrāddham iti siddhaṃ | athaitat sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvam apy ekoddiṣṭam eva kartavyam | yad āha gārgyaḥ |
caturdaśyāṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt paraṃ |
ekoddiṣṭavidhānena tat kāryaṃ śastraghātinaḥ || iti |
manuḥ |
ekapiṇḍīkṛtānāṃ tu pṛthaktvaṃ nopapadyate |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ mṛte kṛṣṇacaturdaśīm || iti |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
[216]        samatvam āgatasyāpi pituḥ śastrahatasya tu |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ sutaiḥ kāryaṃ caturdaśyāṃ mahālaye ||
pretatvaparityāgena pitṛtvaṃ prāptaiḥ pitāmahādibhiḥ saha sapiṇḍīkaraṇāt pretatvaparityāgena pitṛtvaprāpyā sārūpyam āgatasyāpīty arthaḥ | śastrahatasya pitur mahālaye caturdaśyām ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhe kṛte ’pi dināntare pārvaṇaṃ śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ | ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhena pitāmahāditṛptyasiddheḥ |
nanv evaṃ mṛtāhe 'py ekoddiṣṭaśrāddhe kṛte pārvaṇaśrāddham api pitāmahāditṛptisiddhyarthaṃ kartavyaṃ syāt |
maivaṃ | pitur mṛtāhe pitāmahādes tarpaṇīyatvāsmaraṇāt tattṛptyarthaṃ śrāddhasyānanuṣṭheyatvāt | mahālaye tu |
kāṅkṣanti putrapautrebhyaḥ pāyasaṃ madhusaṃyutaṃ |
tasmāt tāṃs tatra vidhinā tarpayet pāyasena tu ||
madhvājyatilamiśreṇeti pitāmahāder api tarpaṇīyatvasmaraṇāt tattṛptaye dināntare pārvaṇaśrāddhaṃ kāryam iti | yasya tu pitāmaho ’pi śastrādinā hatas tasya mahālaye pitāmahaśrāddham api caturdaśyām ekeoddiṣṭarūpaṃ kāryaṃ | tathā ca smṛtyantare: "ekasmin dvayor vaikoddiṣṭavidhiḥ" iti | ekasmin pitari pitāmahe (?) vā śastrādinā hate pitrāditrayamadhye dvayor vā śastrādinā hatayoś caturdaśyām ekoddiṣṭavidhinā pratyekaṃ śrāddhaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ | dvayor ekoddiṣṭavidhānena pratyekaṃ śrāddhe kṛte ’pi prapitāmahatṛptisiddhyarthaṃ dināntare pārvaṇaśrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ | pitrādiṣu triṣv api śastrādihateṣu naikoddiṣṭavidhir iti gamyate |
[217]         atra kecid āhuḥ | pṛthag ekoddiṣṭāni kāryyāṇīti |
anye tv āhuḥ | ekasmin dvayor vety abhidhānāt triṣu pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmaheṣu śastrādinā hateṣu naikoddiṣṭavidhir iti | tena triṣu śastrādihateṣu caturdaśyāṃ pārvaṇavidhir eva |
yuktaṃ caitat | sapiṇḍīkṛtānāṃ śastrādihatānāṃ trayāṇām api caturdaśīrūpavihitakāla-saṃbhave saty aśastrahatāprāptikṛtakālāsambhavanibandhanasyaikoddiṣṭavidher anavatārāt |
anenaivābhiprāyeṇa aparārkeṇoktaṃ | “tatra caikasya śastrahatatve ekoddiṣṭavidhānaṃ, na tu trayāṇāṃ tathātve tatra tu pārvaṇam eva" iti | trayāṇāṃ tathātve naikoddiṣṭavidhānaṃ, kiṃ tu tatra pārvaṇam eveti tasyārthaḥ |
tad ayuktaṃ | ekasmin dvayor vety etasyopalakṣaṇatvāt | ekoddiṣṭavidheś ca śastrādihatatvanimittatvāt na kālāsambhavaḥ, nibandhanakālāsaṃbhavasyāśrutatvāt | ekoddiṣṭavidhes tu tannibandhanatve kalpanāmātramūlatvāpatteḥ | ata eva devasvāminābhihitaṃ | triṣv api śastrādihateṣu pṛthagekoddiṣṭatrayam eva kāryaṃ, na tu pārvaṇaṃ, āhatyavacanābhāvād iti | atra śastrādihatānāṃ caturddaśyām ekoddiṣṭam iti vākyārthāvagamāt mṛtāhādau pārvaṇam eva kartavyaṃ | ye tu caturdaśyām eva śastrādibhir mṛtās teṣāṃ mṛtāhe kiṃ pārvaṇam ekoddiṣṭaṃ veti saṃśaye ’bhidhīyate | caturdaśīnimittakasya mṛhatāhanimittakasya ca śrāddhasya kālasamavāyaprakaraṇe vakṣyamāṇanyāyena bhede ’pi yugapadanekanimittopanipāte ekenaiva naimittikena kutapādikālāvarodhe dvitīyasyānavakāśe deśakālakartṛdevataikye viśeṣāgrahaṇāt tantreṇa naimittikānuṣṭhāne sati svakulavṛddhaparamparānusārāt mṛtāhe ’pi yair ekoddiṣṭapakṣaparigrahaḥ kṛtas teṣāṃ mṛtāhanimittakam api caturdaśyām [218] ekoddiṣṭam eva | yais tu pārvaṇapacaparigrahaḥ kṛtaḥ sa tair uditānuditahomavat yāvajjīvaṃ parigṛhīta eveti caturdaśyām api na tair asau parityājyaḥ | yas tu caturdaśyāṃ pārvaṇatvaniṣedhaḥ śrāddhasya, sa caturdaśīnimittakasya, na mṛtāhanimittakasyāpi |
nanu caturdaśyāṃ na pārvaṇam ity atra caturdaśīnimittakaṃ na caturdaśyām iti viśeṣaṇe ’dhyāhāravākyabhedau syātāṃ |
maivaṃ | prakṛtatvādinā viśeṣalābhasyopanyastadoṣānāspadatvāt | yac ca nāgarakhaṇḍe caturdaśyāṃ kriyamāṇasya pārvaṇasya vaiphalyam uktaṃ tad api caturdaśīnimittakasyaiva, na mṛtāhanimittakasyeti |
atha caturdaśīnimittamekoddiṣṭaṃ mṛtāhanimittakaṃ ca pārvaṇam iti śrāddhadvayaṃ kuryyād ekameva vā aṅgavaidharmyāt gṛhyamāṇaviśeṣatvena tantrānuṣṭhānāsambhavāt saṃśayaḥ | tatra samāne ’hani naikaḥ śrāddhaddayaṃ kuryād ity asya niṣedhasya bhinnanimittakaśrādbhagocaratvābhāvāt śrāddhadayaṃ kuryāt |
maivaṃ | devataikyāt pradhānadvayasya mahānuṣṭhāne bhūyasāṃ śrāddhadharmāṇām anugrahāya pārvaṇadharmair ekoddiṣṭadharmabādhe pārvaṇena naimittikasya siddhau na bhedena śrāddhadvayānuṣṭhānam iti |
nanu kāmyam api caturdaśīśrāddham asti kāmapravaṇapravṛttitvāc ca narāṇāṃ kāmapravṛtter utkaṭatvāc caturdaśīnimittake śrāddhe ’nuṣṭheye saty ekoddiṣṭadharmā eva prāpnuvanti |
bhaved evaṃ yadi bhūyasām anugraho ’tra viśeṣeo nāvagamyeta | astu vāsyāṃ tithau nimittabhede śrāddhadvayam iti, mahālayapakṣe ca yathākathaṃcic caturdaśyāṃ śrāddhāsaṃbhave tatpakṣamadhye kasmiṃścid api dine kartavyaṃ | praśastataramukhya-kālāsaṃbhave ’pi tatpakṣakartavyasya śrāddhasyāvaśyakatvāt | dināntare ca śastrahatānām api pārvaṇam eva, caturdaśyām evaikoddiṣṭavidhānāt | caturdaśyāṃ [219] śastrādihatānām ekoddiṣṭam eva kartavyam ity atra kāraṇam uktaṃ nāgarakhaṇḍe |
ānarta uvāca |
kasmāc chastrahatānāṃ ca proktā śrāddhe caturdaśī |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ kutaḥ śrāddhaṃ kāraṇaṃ prabravīhi me ||
bhartṛyajña uvāca |
bṛhatkalpe purā rājan hiraṇyākṣeo mahāsuraḥ |
babhūva balavān śūraḥ sarvadevabhayaṃkaraḥ ||
brahmā pratoṣitas tena vidhāya vividhaṃ tapaḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ nabhasye māsi saṃsthite ||
brahmovāca |
parituṣṭo ’smi te vatsa prārthayasva (?) yathepsitaṃ |
adeyam api dāsyāmi durlabhaṃ yat surāsuraiḥ ||
hiraṇyākṣa uvāca |
bhūtāḥ pretāḥ piśācāś ca rākṣasā daityadānavāḥ |
bubhukṣitāḥ pradhāvanti māṃ nityaṃ padmasaṃbhava ||
pitṛpakṣe kṛte śrāddhe kanyāsaṃsthe divākare |
etasminn ahani prāyas tṛptiḥ syād varṣasaṃbhavā ||
tad eṣām atra divase tṛptyupāyaṃ kuru prabho |
brahmovāca |
[220]        yaḥ kaścin mānavaḥ śrāddhaṃ svapitṛbhyaḥ pradāsyati |
pitṛpakṣe caturdaśyāṃ nabhasye māsi saṃsthite |
pretānāṃ rākṣasānāṃ ca bhūtānāṃ tad bhaviṣyati ||
evam uktā tato brahmā gataś cādarśanaṃ nṛpa |
hiraṇyākṣo ’pi saṃhṛṣṭaḥ svīyam eva gṛhaṃ yayau ||
yac ca śastrahatānāṃ ca tasminn ahani dīyate |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ naraiḥ śraddhaṃ tat te vacyāmi kāraṇaṃ ||
saṃkhye śastrahatā ye ca nirvikalpena cetasā |
yudhyamānā na te martye jāyante manujāḥ punaḥ ||
parāṅmukhā ye hanyante palāyanaparāyaṇāḥ |
te bhavanti narāḥ pretā etad āha pitāmahaḥ ||
sanmukhā api ye dainyaṃ hanyamānā vadanti ca |
paścāttāpaṃ ca vā kuryaḥ prahārair jarjarīkṛtāḥ ||
te ’pi pretā bhavantīha manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ’bravīt |
kadācic cittacalanaṃ śūrāṇāṃ saṃprajāyate ||
teṣām api dine tatra deyaṃ pretatvaśaṅkayā |
apamṛtyugatānāṃ ca sarveṣām eva dehināṃ ||
pretatvaṃ jāyate yasmāt tasmāt teṣāṃ hi tad dinaṃ |
śrāddhārhaṃ pārthivaśreṣṭha viśeṣeṇa prakīrtitaṃ ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ prakartavyaṃ tasmāt tatra dine naraiḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrddhvaṃ tat te vakṣyāmi kāraṇaṃ ||
yadi pretatvam āpannaḥ kadācit tatpitā bhavet |
[221]        tṛptyarthaṃ tasya kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ tasya dine nṛpa ||
pitāmahādyās tatrāhni śrāddhaṃ nārhanti kutracit |
atha ced bhrāntito dadyāt hriyate rākṣasais tu tat ||
brahmaṇo vacanād rājan bhūtapretaiś ca dānavaiḥ |
tenaikoddiṣṭam evātra kartavyaṃ na tu pārvaṇaṃ ||
pitṛpakṣe caturdaśyāṃ kanyāsaṃsthe divākare |
etasmāt kāraṇāc chrāddhaṃ pārvaṇaṃ na vidhīyate |
tasminn ahani saṃprāpte vyarthaṃ śrāddhaṃ bhaved yataḥ ||
iti caturdaśīśrāddhanirūpaṇaṃ |
athaitad bhādrapadāparapakṣaśrāddhaṃ malamāse na kartavyam ity ucyate kāṭhakagṛhye |
yasmin māse na saṃkrāntiḥ saṃkrāntidvayam eva ca |
malamāsaḥ sa vijñeyo māse triṃśattame bhavet ||
bārhaspatye jyotiḥśāstre |
yasmin māse na saṃkrāntiḥ saṃkrāntidvayam eva ca |
saṃsarpāṃhaspatīmāsāv adhimāsaś ca ninditaḥ ||
nandipurāṇe |
amāvāsyām ahorātre yadā saṃkramate raviḥ |
sa tu māsaḥ pavitraḥ syād vyatīte hy adhiko bhavet ||
āha bhṛguḥ |
ekarāśisthite sūrye yadā darśadvayaṃ bhavet |
[222]        havyakavyakriyāhantā tadā jñeyo ’dhimāsakaḥ || iti |
paiṭhīnasir api |
śrautasmārtakriyāḥ sarvā dvādaśe māsi kīrtitāḥ |
trayodaśe tu sarvās tā niṣphalā iti saṃjñitāḥ |
tasmāt trayodaśe māsi kuryāt tā na kathaṃcana || iti |
uktaṃ sumantunā |
na kuryād adhike māsi karmī karma kadācana |
malaṃ vadanti kālasya māsaṃ kālavido ’dhikam || iti |
gṛhyapariśiṣṭe ’pi |
malimlucas tu māso vai malinaḥ pāpasaṃbhavaḥ |
garhitaḥ pitṛdevebhyaḥ sarvakarmasu saṃtyajet ||
śātātapaḥ |
vatsarāntargataḥ pāpo yajñānāṃ phalanāśakṛt |
nairṛtyayātudhānādyaiḥ samākrānto vināśakaḥ ||
malimlucaḥ samākhyātaḥ sūryasaṃkrāntivarjitaḥ |
malimluca iti khyāto garhitaḥ sarvakarmasu ||
satyavrataḥ |
malimlucas tu yo māsaḥ sa māsaḥ pāpasaṃjñitaḥ ||
na caiṣāṃ vacanānāṃ pitṛkāryavyatiriktaviṣayatvaṃ mantavyaṃ | havyakavyakriyāhanteti garhitaḥ pitṛdevebhya iti ca pitṛkāryasyāpi niṣedhāt | yat tu jyotiḥparāśareṇoktaṃ,
upākarma tathotsargaṃ pravāsādyutsavāṣṭakāḥ |
[223]        māsavṛddhau parāḥ kāryā varjayitvā tu paitṛkam || iti |
tat prasavanimittakapaitṛkamātraviṣayaṃ | uktaṃ hi matsyapurāṇe |
candrasūryagrahe caiva maraṇe putrajanmani |
malamāse ’pi deyaṃ syāc chrāddham akṣayakārakaṃ ||
aṣṭakāgatasya tu paitṛkasya nātra pratiprasavaḥ | tatra sākṣānniṣedhasya darśayiṣyamāṇatvāt |
nanv āvaśyakatvād aparapakṣaśrāddhasya malamāse’pi kartavyatā na virudhyate | uktaṃ hi gṛhyapariśiṣṭe |
malaṃ vadanti kālasya māsaṃ kālavido ’dhikaṃ |
nehetātra viśeṣejyām anyatrāvaśyakād vidheḥ || iti ||
naitad evaṃ, asya vacanasyānanyagatikanityaviṣayatvāt | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
ananyagatikaṃ nityaṃ kuryān naimittikaṃ tathā || iti |
yasya nityasya mukhyakālātikrame jaghanyasya kālāntarasyānabhyanujñānād atyantalopaḥ, prāyaścittaṃ ca prasajyate tatkarmānanyagatiḥ | tat malamāse ’pi kuryāt | bhādrapadāparapakṣaśrāddhasya uttarakālābhyanujñādarśanān nānanyagatikatvam | ato malamāse na kartavyaṃ | ata evāsya malamāse spaṣṭo niṣedho dṛśyate | tatra tāvat kāṭhakagṛhyapariśiṣṭe |
tathā male ’nanyagatiṃ nityāṃ naimittikīṃ kriyāṃ |
somayāgādikarmāṇi nityāny api malimluce ||
[224]        puṣṭīṣtyāgrayaṇādhānacāturmasyādikāny api |
mahālayāṣṭakāśrāddhopākarmotsargakarma yat |
spaṣṭaṃ māsaviśeṣākhyāvihitaṃ varjayen male ||
mahālayo bhādrapadāparapakṣaḥ | bhṛgusmṛtāv api |
vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ tathā somam agnyādheyaṃ mahālayaṃ |
rājābhiṣekaṃ kāmyaṃ ca na kuryād bhānulaṅghite ||
jyotiḥpitāmaho ’pi |
māsaḥ kanyāgate bhānāv asaṃkrānto bhaved yadi |
daivaṃ pitryaṃ tadā karma tulāsthe kartur akṣayam || iti |
devalaḥ |
arke tu tasya kanyāsthe śrāddhapakṣaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
sinīvālīm atikramya yadā kanyāṃ vrajed raviḥ |
tadā kālasya vṛddhatvād atītaiva pitṛkriyā || iti |
tasmān malamāsāntargate bhādrapadāparapakṣe śrāddhaṃ na kartavyaṃ | yat tu jyotiḥsiddhāntam,
ghaṭakanyāgate sūrye vṛścike vāpi dhanvani |
makare vātha kumbhe vā nādhimāseo vidhīyate || iti |
tat saṃvatsaramadhye malamāsadvaye sati veditavyaṃ |
tathā ca brahmasiddhānte |
māsadvaye ’bdamadhye tu saṃkrāntir na yadā bhavet |
prākṛtas tatra pūrvaḥ syād adhimāsas tathottaraḥ ||
[225]        atra siṃhasaṃkrāntim ārabhya mīnasaṃkramaṇāvadhike kāle yo māsaḥ pūrvaḥ sa prākṛta eva, tat punar vaikṛto malamāso bhavatīty arthaḥ | “caitrād arvāṅ nādhimāsaḥ paratas tv adhiko bhavet” ity asyāpi brahmamiddhāntavacanasyāyamevārtho vyākhyeyaḥ | tasmāt malamāsāntargate mahālayāparapakṣe śrāddhaṃ na kartavyam iti sthitaṃ |
nanu kiṃ tadā lupyata eva tat, kālāntare vā kriyamāṇam apuṇyakālaṃ syāt |
na hi | yatas taruttarakāle kartavyam iti | ata āha parāśaraḥ |
raviṇā laṅghito māsaś cāndraḥ khyāto malimlucaḥ |
tatra yad vihitaṃ karma uttare māsi kārayet ||
tathā |
pakṣadvaye ’rkasaṃkrāntir yadā na syāt sitāsite |
tadā tanmāsikaṃ kāryam uttare māsi kārayet ||
evaṃ ṣaṣṭidino māsas tadarddhaṃ ca malimlucaṃ |
tyaktvā taduttare kur yāt pitṛdevādikāḥ kriyāḥ ||
prajāpatiḥ |
upākarma ca havyaṃ ca kavyaṃ parvotsavaṃ tathā |
uttare niyataṃ kuryāt pūrve tan niṣphalaṃ bhavet ||
jyotiḥśāstre ’pi |
ṣaṣṭhyā tu divasair māsaḥ kathito bādarāyaṇaiḥ |
pūrvam ardhaṃ parityajya uttarārdhaṃ praśasyate ||
amāvāsyādvayaṃ yatra ravisaṃkrāntivarjitaṃ |
[226]        malimlucaḥ sa vijñeya uttaras tūttamābhidhaḥ ||
atha malamāse śrāddhakarmaṇāṃ pratiprasavāḥ | āha yamaḥ |
garbhe vārddhuṣike kṛtye śrāddhakarmāṇi māsike |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇe nitye nādhimāso vidhīyate ||
tīrthajñānaṃ japo homo yavavrīhitilādibhiḥ |
jātakarmāntyakarmāṇi navaśrāddhaṃ tathaiva ca ||
maghātrayodaśīśrāddhaṃ śrāddhāny api ca ṣoḍaśa |
candrasūryagrahe snānaṃ śrāddhadānajapādikaṃ |
kāryāṇi malamāse ’pi nityaṃ naimittikaṃ tathā ||
nāradīyapurāṇe |
nityanaimittike kuryāt prayataḥ san malimluce |
tīrthasnānaṃ gajacchāyāṃ pretaśrāddhaṃ tathaiva ca ||
kāṭhakapariśiṣṭe ’pi |
pretasaṃskāram ārabhya śrāddhapiṇḍodakakriyāḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇāntāś ca yathākālam upasthitāḥ ||
yavavrīhitilair homo jātakarmādikāḥ kriyāḥ |
maghātrayodaśīśrāddhaṃ pratyupasthitahetukaṃ |
ananyagatikatvena kartavyaṃ syān malimluce ||
saurapurāṇe |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ tan naimittikam ucyate |
tat kāryaṃ malamāse ’pi kālādhike ’pi dharmataḥ ||
[227] vyāsaḥ |
jātakarmaṇi yac chrāddhaṃ darśaśrāddhaṃ tathaiva ca |
malamāse ’pi kartavyaṃ vyāsasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
śātātapaḥ |
pratisaṃvatsare śrāddhe nādhimāsaṃ vivarjayet |
malamāse ’pi kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ yat prativatsaraṃ ||
etadādy asāṃvatsarikaviṣayaṃ | malamāse sāṃvatsarikaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṛtvā taduttaramāse punas tad evāvartanīyam ity evaṃvidhamatānusāri cedaṃ vacanam atra vacanāni |
vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
asaṃkrānte ’pi kartavyam ābdikaṃ prathamaṃ dvijaiḥ |
tathaiva māsikaṃ śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tathā ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
malamāsamṛtānāṃ ca śrāddhaṃ yat prativatsaraṃ |
malamāse ’pi kartavyaṃ nānyeṣāṃ tu kadācana ||
yamaḥ |
ābdikaṃ prathamaṃ yat syāt tat kurvīta malimluce |
trayodaśe tu saṃprāpte kurvīta punar ābdikaṃ ||
laghuhārītaḥ |
pratyabdaṃ dvādaśe māsi kāryā piṇḍakriyā dvijaiḥ |
kvacit trayodaśe ’pi syād ādyaṃ muktvā tu vatsaraṃ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ābdikāhani saṃprāpte adhimāso bhaved yadi |
[228]        śrāddhadvayaṃ prakurvīta evaṃ kurvan na muhyati ||
tad evaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāntāni pretaśrāddhāni putrajanmanimittakam uparāganimittakam ādyaṃ sāṃvatsarikam anādyam api malamāsamṛtasāṃvatsarikaṃ gajacchāyāśrāddham amāvāsyāśrāddhaṃ pratyaham anuṣṭhīyamānaṃ ca nityaśrāddhaṃ varjayitvānyac chrāddhaṃ malamāse nānuṣṭheyam iti sthitaṃ |
iti malamāsanirūpaṇapūrvako malamāse ’parapakṣādiśrāddhāpavādaḥ |
paryavasitaṃ cāparapakṣanirūpaṇaṃ |
athāyanādayaḥ śrāddhakālāḥ |
tatra yady api yājñavalkyavacane dravyabrāhmaṇasaṃpattibhyāṃ vyavadhāya ayanaviṣuvatsaṃkāntayo nirūpitās tathāpy atra saṃkrāntitvasādharmyād avyavasthānena nirūpyante |
tatra viṣṇupurāṇe |
upaśave candramaso raveś ca
triṣv aṣṭakāsv apy ayanadvaye ca |
pānīyam apy atra tilair vimiśraṃ
dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ prayato manuṣyaḥ ||
śrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ tena samāsahasaṃ
rahasyam etat pitaro vadanti |
atra kila pānīyam apītivacanād āvaśyakatā śrāddhadvayasyocyata iti manyante |
[229]        yat tu |
ādityasaṃkramaś cobhe ayane viṣuvaddvayaṃ |
vyatīpāto ’tha janmarkṣaṃ candrasūryagrahas tathā |
etāṃs tu śrāddhakālān vai kāmyān āha prajāpatiḥ |
iti viṣṇuvacanena grahaṇādeḥ kāmyaśrāddhakālatvam uktam |
tan na, nityatvanirākaraṇakṣamaṃ, vākyadvayabalenāgnihotrādivan nityatvakāmyatvayor avirodhāt | āha śaṅkhaḥ |
hasticchāyāsu yad dattaṃ yad dattaṃ rāhudarśane |
viṣuvaty ayane caiva sarvam ānantyam ucyate ||
atrāyanadvayaṃ viṣuvatsūryasaṃkrama ity asmin yājñavalkyavacane ca ayanayor viṣuvatoś ca saṃkrāntipadenaiva saṃgrahasiddhāv api dvayor api yat pṛthagabhidhānaṃ tat saṃkānyantarebhyo viṣuvaḥ, viṣuvād ayanam iti yathāpūrvaṃ prāśastyātiśayapratipādanārtham iti mantavyaṃ | tathā ca viṣṇudhamottare |
śrāddhaṃ saṃkramaṇe bhānoḥ praśastaṃ pṛthivīpate |
viṣuvaddvitayaṃ tatrāpy ayane dve viśeṣataḥ ||
viṣuvator ayanayoś ca lakṣaṇaṃ | nāgarakhaṇḍe |
yadā syān meṣago bhānus tulāṃ cātha yadā vrajet |
tadā syād viṣuvākhyas tu kālaś cākṣayakārakaḥ ||
makare karkaṭe caiva yadā bhānur vrajen nṛpa |
[230]        tadāyanābhidhānaś ca viṣuvat sa viśiṣyate ||
asmiṃś ca kāle piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ vinaiva śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ | yad āha pulastyaḥ |
ayanadvitaye śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaddvitaye tathā |
yugādiṣu ca sarvāsu piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte ||
brahmapurāṇe sarvāsv api saṃkrāntiṣu piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ vinā śrāddham uktam |
ayanadvitayaṃ śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaddvitaye tathā |
saṃkrāntiṣu ca sarvāsu piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte || iti |
eteṣu kāleṣu piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ vinā śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ | etatkālanimittaka eva śrāddhe piṇḍanirvapaṇapratiṣedho 'yaṃ, na mṛtāhādinimittake prasaṅgād ayanādiṣu patite | yasmād ayanadvitaye śrāddham ity anenāyanādibhir nityānvitaṃ śrāddham avagamyate | tannimittakam eva ca tair nityānvitaṃ nānyat | nityānvayaś cānityānvayād balīyān bhavati |
kiṃ ca | atra hi vidhīyamānasya śrāddhasya piṇḍanirvapaṇādṛta iti viśeṣaṇaṃ na prasaṅgāt tatkālapatitaṃ mṛtāhādiśrāddham āśrayitum arhati | yady evaṃvidhaṃ śrāddham eteṣu kāleṣu vidhīyate tadā bhāvārthavidhir ity eṣa guṇo bhavati |
atha saṃkrāntitadbhedatatkālaparimāṇādy ucyate | nāgarakhaṇḍe |
raveḥ saṃkramaṇaṃ rāśau saṃkrāntir iti kathyate |
snānadānajapaśrāddhahomādiṣu mahāphalaṃ ||
rāśau meṣādisaṃjñe jyotiḥśāstraprasiddhe |
[231]        devīpurāṇe |
samāyanaṃ ṛtur māsapakṣāhādi krameṇa tu |
sūkṣmasthūlavibhāgena devī sarvagatā vibho ||
dvādaśaiva samākhyātāḥ samā saṃkrāntikalpanāḥ |
saptadhā sā tu boddhavyā ekaikā tu yathā śṛṇu ||
mandā mandākinī dhvāṅkṣī ghorā caiva mahodarī |
rākṣasī miśritā proktā saṃkrāntiḥ saptadhā nṛpa ||
mandā dhruveṣu vijñeyā mṛdā mandākinī yathā |
kṣipre dhvāṅgī vijānīyād ugre ghorā prakīrtitā ||
carair mahodarī jñeyā dāruṇarkṣaiś ca rākṣasī |
miśritā caiva nirdiṣṭā miśritarkṣais tu saṃkrame ||
tricatuḥpañcasaptāṣṭanavadvādaśa eva ca |
krameṇa ghaṭikā hy etās tat puṇyaṃ pāramārthikaṃ ||
atītānāgato bhogo nāḍyaḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ |
eṣa sthūlavibhāgas tu muktikāmasya kīrtitaḥ ||
paramārthena yā saṃkhyā kathayāmi nṛpottama |
svasthe nare sukhāsīne yāvat spandati locanaṃ |
tasya triṃśattamaṃ bhāgaṃ tat paraṃ parikīrtitaṃ ||
tatparāc chatabhāgas tu truṭir ity abhidhīyate |
truṭyāḥ sahasrabhāgārdhaṃ tat kālaṃ ravisaṃkrama iti ||
[232]        samāyanetyādi | ‘devī’ saṃkrāntirūpā, sā samāyanādibhiḥ sthūlaiḥ sūkṣmaiś cāvayavaiḥ 'sarvagatā' sarvavyāpinī | saṃvatsarādayaḥ saṃkrāntyavayavāḥ | katham ity āha dvādaśaivetyādi | dvādaśaiva saṃkrāntikalpanāḥ, 'samā' saṃvatsaro bhavati | ayam āśayaḥ, dvādaśabhiḥ saṃkrāntibhir ekaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ | ṣaṣbhiḥ saṃkrāntibhir ayanaṃ | saṃkrāntidvayena ṛtuḥ | ekasaṃkrāntyavacchinnaḥ kālo māsaḥ | saṃkrāntyardhaḥ pakṣaḥ | saṃkrāntes triṃśāṃśo ’horātram | ādiśabdena yāmārdhayāmamuhūrtādayaḥ | evam ayaṃ saṃkrāntirūpas tapanaparisyandaḥ śaktyātmikā devīti | ekaikasyāḥ saṃkānter dhruvādinakṣatrasaṃyoge puṇyakālaparimāṇārthaṃ mandādisaṃjñām āha | mandetyādi | 'dhruvāṇi' rohiṇyuttarātrayaṃ ceti catvāri | mṛdūni citrānurādhāmmṛgaśirorevatīti catvāri | 'kṣiprāṇi' aśvinī puṣyo hasto ’bhijid iti catvāri | 'ugrāṇi' pūrvatrayaṃ bharaṇī magheti pañca | 'carāṇi' śravaṇā dhaniṣṭhā śatatārakā punarvasuḥ svātīti pañca | 'dāruṇāni' mūlaṃ jyeṣṭhādrāśleṣeti catvāri | 'miśre' viśākhā kṛttiketi dve | ekaikā saptadheti nirdeśāt dhruvādini dinanakṣatrāṇi vijñāyante, na mahānakṣatrāṇi | teṣu na ekaikasyāḥ saptadhātvam upapannaṃ | puṇyakālaparimāṇam āha |
tricatuḥpañceti | mandāyāṃ tisro ghaṭikāḥ puṇyakālaḥ | mandākinyāṃ catasra | dhvāṅkṣayāṃ pañca | ghorāyāṃ sapta | mahodaryā aṣṭau | rākṣasyāṃ nava | miśrāyāṃ dvādaśeti | tatra kṛte dānayajñatapaḥśrāddhādikarmaṇi saṃkrāntikālanimittakaṃ puṇyaṃ pāramārthikam atiśayitam ityarthaḥ | itaḥ kiṃcidūnaphalaḥ sarvasaṃkrāntiṣu pañcadaśaghaṭikātmakaḥ puṇyakālo 'stīty āha atītānāgata ityādi | 'bhogaḥ' vyāptiḥ, ‘atīte' saṃkramaṇāt [233] pūrvasmin | ‘anāgate' parasmin kāle | saṃkrāntisamayāt pūrvataḥ parataś ca pañcadaśa ghaṭikāḥ puṇyakāla ity arthaḥ | atītānāgatakālavyāptiś ca pūrvokteṣu vyādiśyamāneṣv api veditavyā | atra puṇyakālasya pūrvabhāvitvaṃ paścādbhāvitvaṃ ca vakṣyamāṇavyavasthayānusartavyam | evaṃ sthūlaṃ puṇyakālam uktvā praśastatamaphalaṃ sūkṣmakālam āha paramārthenetyādi | truṭisahasratamabhāgārdhasya durlabhatve ’pi tatsaṃnihitakālagrahaṇārthapratipādanaṃ | āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
saṃkrāntisamayaḥ sūkṣmo durjñeyaḥ piśitekṣaṇaiḥ |
tadyogāc cāpy adhaś cordhvaṃ triṃśannāḍyaḥ pavitritāḥ ||
atra triṃśannāḍyaḥ pūrvaṃ pañcadaśa, paścāt pañcadaśety evaṃsaṅkalanayā draṣṭavyāḥ | evaṃ hi sati devīpurāṇena saha saṃvādo bhavati | brahmavaivarte |
meṣādirāśiṣu raviḥ kramād gacchati saṃkramāt |
dvādaśaiva bhavanty eṣāṃ dvija nāmāni me śṛṇu |
meṣādirāśiṣu sūryagatyā tadrāśisaṃjñakā eva dvādaśa saṃkramāḥ syuḥ | teṣām eva prāśastyatāratamyabhogapaurvāparyādyupadeśārthaṃ viṣṇupadādisaṃjñācatuṣṭayam āha |
ekaṃ viṣṇupadaṃ nāma ṣaḍaśītimukhaṃ tathā |
viṣuvac ca tṛtīyaṃ ca ayane dakṣiṇottare ||
kumbhāligohariṣu viṣṇupadaṃ vadanti
[234]                strīcāpamīnamithune ṣaḍaśītivaktraṃ |
arkasya yānam ayanaṃ śaśidhāmni yāmyaṃ
saumyaṃ jhāṣe tu viṣuvaṃ tv ajataulinoḥ syāt ||
'kumbhaḥ' prasiddhaḥ | aliḥ' vṛścikaḥ | 'go' vṛṣaḥ | 'hariḥ' siṃhaḥ | eṣu rāśiṣu raver ayanaṃ saṃkramaṇaṃ 'viṣṇupadaṃ' viṣṇupadasaṃjñaṃ bhavati | 'strī' kanyā | 'cāpaṃ' dhanuḥ | 'mīnamithunau' prasiddhau | eteṣu ṣaḍaśītimukhaṃ | 'śaśidhānni' karkaṭake, yāmyāyanaṃ | 'jhaṣe' makare | saumyāyanaṃ, 'ajaḥ' meṣaḥ | 'taulī' tulādhāraḥ | tayor viṣuvasajñaṃ |
vasiṣṭho ’pi |
ayane dve viṣuve caiva catasraḥ ṣaḍaśītayaḥ |
catasro viṣṇupadyaṃ ca saṃkrāntyo dvādaśa smṛtāḥ ||
jhāṣakarkaṭasaṃkrāntī dve tūdagdakṣiṇāyane |
viṣuvato tulāmeṣau tayor madhye tato ’parāḥ ||
vṛṣavṛścikakumbheṣu siṃhe caiva yadā raviḥ |
etad viṣṇupadaṃ nāma viṣuvād adhikaṃ phalaṃ ||
kanyāyāṃ mithune mīne dhanuṣy api raver gatiḥ |
ṣaḍaśītimukhā proktā ṣaḍaśītiguṇā phale ||
laugākṣiḥ |
yasyāyane viṣṇupade tathādau
dānādyanantaṃ viṣuve tu madhye |
vadanty atīte ṣaḍaśītivaktra-
maharṣayaḥ khalv ayane ca saumye ||
[235] baudhāyanaḥ |
bhaviṣyaty ayane viṣṇor vartamāne viṣūvati |
ṣaḍaśītimukhe ’tīte vyatīte cottarāyaṇe ||
devīpurāṇe |
ṣaḍaśītimukhe ’tīte madhye ca viṣuvaddvaye |
bhaviṣyaty ayane puṇyam atīte cottarāyaṇe ||
vṛddhavaśiṣṭhaḥ |
atītānāgate puṇye dve udagdakṣiṇāyaṇe |
tūparāge tu tatkālaṃ vyatīte cottarāyaṇe ||
vāyupurāṇe |
madhye dānaṃ viṣuvati dakṣiṇe vaiṣṇave puraḥ |
ṣaḍaśītimukhe ’tīte vyatīte cottarāyaṇe ||
atra sarveṣu vacaneṣv atīte cottarāyaṇa ity atra cakārād anāgate ceti smṛtinibandhakārā manyante | uktasya pūrvottarādipuṇyakālasya parimāṇam āha baudhāyanaḥ |
nāḍyo viṣuvati proktā daśa pūrvā daśāparāḥ |
puṇyaḥ kālo ’rkasaṃkāntau snānadānajapādiṣu ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
triṃśat karkaṭake nāḍyo makare viṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ |
vartamāne tulāmeṣe nāḍyas tūbhayato daśa ||
ṣaḍaśītyāṃ vyatītāyāṃ ṣaṣṭir uktās tu nāḍikāḥ |
puṇyās tu viṣṇupadyāś ca prākpaścād api ṣoḍaśa ||
[236] brahmavaivarte |
viṣuvat ṣaṇmuhūrtaṃ syāt ṣaḍaśītimukhe trayaṃ |
tathā viṣṇupade trīṇi puṇyāni kavayo viduḥ ||
triṃśat karkaṭake nāḍyo makare tu daśādhikāḥ |
bhaviṣyaty ayane puṇyā atīte cottarāyaṇe ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhaviṣyaty ayane puṇyās triṃśad eva tu dakṣiṇe |
atīta uttare nāḍya iti prāhuḥ purāvida iti ||
gautamaḥ |
arvāk ṣoḍaśa nāḍyas tu parataḥ ṣoḍaśaiva tu |
tā eva dakṣiṇe pūrvam atīte cottarāyaṇe |
marīciḥ |
nāḍyaḥ ṣoḍaśa pūrveṇa saṃkrānter apareṇa vā |
rāhor darśanamātreṇa puṇyakālaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
saṃkrānteḥ puṇyakālas tu ṣoḍaśobhayataḥ kalāḥ |
candrasūryoparāge tu yāvad darśanagocarāḥ ||
atra keṣucid vacaneṣu puṇyakālaparimāṇam adhikam uktaṃ | keṣucin nyūnam uktaṃ | tatra yan nyūnaṃ tat phalātiśayapratipādanārthaṃ | yat tv adhikaṃ tat puṇyakālatvamātra-pratipādanārthaṃ | na punar nyūnam adhikanirākaraṇārtham adhikaṃ vā nyūnanirākaraṇārtham iti mantavyaṃ | ata evāha śātātapaḥ |
yā yāḥ saṃnihitā nāḍyas tās tāḥ puṇyatamā matāḥ |
[237] evaṃ ca sati sarveṣāṃ puṇyakālāvadhivacanānāṃ avirodhe sati virodhaparihārārthaṃ vacanārjavabhaṅgo na kartavyo bhavati | atrāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ahni saṃkramaṇe puṇyam ahaḥ kṛtsnaṃ prakīrtitaṃ |
rātrau saṃkramaṇe puṇyaṃ dinārdhaṃ snānadānayoḥ || iti |
anena saṃkramaṇasaṃnihito ’pi rātrirūpaḥ puṇyakālo na bhavati | kiṃ tu dinārdharūpa evety uktaṃ bhavati | so ’pi yaḥ saṃkramaṇasaṃnihitaḥ sa eva grāhyaḥ | tadāha gobhilaḥ |
rātrau saṃkramaṇe bhānor divā kuryāt tu tatkriyāṃ |
pūrvasmin paratā vāpi pratyāsatteś ca tatphalam || iti |
pratyāsatter ity anenedam uktam, yadi pūrvarātre saṃkramaṇaṃ bhavati, tadā pūrvasya dinasyottarārdhaṃ puṇyaṃ | yady apararātre tadā aparasya dinasya pūrvārdham iti | yadi madhyarātre tadā viśeṣagrahaṇābhāvāt pūrvasyottarārdham uttarasya ca pūrvārdham ity etad dvayam api puṇyāham iti | tadāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
ardharātrād adhas tasmin madhyāhnasyopari kriyā |
ūrdhvaṃ saṃkramaṇe bhānor udayāt praharadvayaṃ ||
pūrṇe ced ardharātre tu yadā saṃkramate raviḥ |
tadā dinadvayaṃ puṇyaṃ muktvā makarakarkaṭau || iti |
makarakarkaṭaviṣaye tu yajñapārśvavacanaṃ |
[238]        āsannasaṃkramaṃ puṇyaṃ dinārdhaṃ snānadānayoḥ |
rātrau saṃkramaṇe bhānor viṣuvaty ayane dine |
ayane dina iti | dakṣiṇottarāyaṇayos tu dina evaṃ saṃkramaṇe sati dine puṇyakālaḥ | tena rātrāv ayanasaṃkrame rātrāv eva praśastataraḥ puṇyakālaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
astaṃgate yadā sūrye jhaṣaṃ yāti divākaraḥ |
pradoṣe vārdharātre vā tadā puṇyaṃ dināntaram ||
kārmukaṃ tu parityajya jhaṣaṃ saṃkramate raviḥ |
pradoṣe vārdharātre vā tadā bhogaḥ pare ’hani ||
skandapurāṇe |
dhanurmīnāv atikramya kanyāṃ ca mithunaṃ yadā |
pūrvāparavibhāgena rātrau saṃkramate yadā |
dinānte pañca nāḍyas tu tadā puṇyatamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
udaye (?) ca tathā pañca daive pitrye ca karmaṇi ||
atra puṇyatamatvābhidhānād dinārdhapakṣeṇa mahāvirodhaḥ |
nigamaḥ |
viṣṇupadyāṃ dhanurmīnanṛyukkanyāsu vai yadā |
parataḥ pūrvato vāpi rātrau saṃkramaṇaṃ bhavet ||
pūrvāhṇe pañcanāḍyas tu puṇyāḥ proktā manīṣibhiḥ |
aparāhṇe tu pañcaiva śraute smārte ca karmaṇi || iti ||
'nṛyuk' mithunarāśiḥ | tithibhedanibandhanam api saṃkrāntibhogasya pūrvāparavibhāgam āha |
[239]        ādau puṇyaṃ vijānīyād yady abhinnā tithir bhavet |
ardharātre vyatīte tu vijñeyam apare ’hani |
ardharātrād anantaram api yadi pūrvatithimadhye saṃkramas tadā pūrvam eva dinaṃ puṇyaṃ | aparatithisaṃkrame tv aparam eveti vacanārthaḥ | atha rātrau niṣiddhasya snānādeḥ saṃkrāntiviśeṣapratiprasavābhidhānārthaṃ niṣedhas tāvad ucyate | bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
rātrau snānaṃ na kurvīta dānaṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ |
naimittikaṃ tu kurvīta snānaṃ dānaṃca rātriṣu ||
viṣṇuḥ |
vivāhavratasaṃkrāntipratiṣṭhā-ṛtujanmasu |
tathoparāgapātādau snāne dāne niśā śubhā ||
'ṛtuḥ' garbhādhānaṃ | candrārkadṛṣṭisaṃnipātasamayaḥ pātaḥ |
gobhilaḥ |
rāhudarśanasaṃkrāntivivāhātyayavṛddhiṣu |
snānadānādikaṃ kuryur niśi kāmyavrateṣu ca ||
'atyayaḥ' bandhumaraṇaṃ | atra saṃkrāntiśabdena makarakarkaṭāv eva gṛhyate, rātrau saṃkramaṇe dinasya puṇyatāṃ vadatā vaśiṣṭhena muktvā makarakarkaṭāv ity abhidhānāt | ato makarakarkaṭayor eva rātrau snānādeḥ pratiprasava iti gamyate | saṃkrānyantareṣu gobhilādivacanānusārād asty eva rātrau snānāder niṣedha iti |
atra manyante | muktvā makarakarkaṭāv ity eṣa pūrvadinobhayagrahaṇāpavādo, na tu sarvātmanā dināpavādena rātri[240]grahaṇopadeśaḥ | baudhāyanādivacaneṣu rātrau makarasaṃkramaṇe paradinasyaiva puṇyāhatvābhidhānāt dinārdhagrahaṇavacanāni tu kenacit prakāreṇa rātrau saṃkramaṇasaṃnihitaṣoḍaśanāḍikādiparimitakāle snānadānādyasaṃbhave vyavahitam api dinārdhaṃ grāhyam ityevaṃparāṇīti sarvāsv api saṃkrāntiṣu rātrau snānadānādipratiprasava ity āhuḥ |
anye tu ravisaṃkramaṇe dinaṃ puṇyaṃ, grahāntarasaṃkramaṇe tu rātrir eva puṣyety āhuḥ | grahāntarasaṃkramaṇasamayasyāpi puṇyatām āha jaiminiḥ |
nakṣatrarāśyo ravisaṃkrame syur
arvāk paratrāpi rasendunāḍyaḥ
puṇyās tathendos tridharāpalair yug
ekaiva nāḍī munibhiḥ śubhoktā ||
nāḍyaś catastraḥ sapalāḥ kujasya
budhasya tisro manavaḥ palāni |
sārdhāś catasraḥ palasaptayuktā
guroś ca śukre sapalāś catasraḥ ||
dvir nāganāḍyaḥ palasaptayuktāḥ
śanaiścarasyābhihitāḥ supuṇyāḥ |
ādyantamadhye japadānahomaṃ
kurvann avāpnoti narendradhāma ||
nakṣatre rāśau vā sūryasya saṃkramaṇe sati, pūrvataḥ parato ’pi rasendunāḍyaḥ ṣoḍaśaghaṭikāḥ puṇyakālaḥ | indos tu tridharāpalair yuk trayodaśabhiḥ palair yuktā ekaiva ghaṭikā | 'kujasya' bhaumasya tu ekapalayuktāś [241] catasro ghaṭikāḥ | budhasya caturdaśapalayuktās tistraḥ | ‘guroḥ' bṛhaspateḥ, saptatriṃśatpalayuktāś catasraḥ| śukrasya palādhikāś catasraḥ | śanaiścarasya palasaptayuktāḥ dvir nāganāḍyaḥ | 'nāgāḥ' aṣṭau, dvirāvṛttā nāgā dvirnāgāḥ | tad eteṣu rātrityāgaparigrahapakṣeṣu kṣodakṣamo grāhyaḥ |
iti saṃkrāntinirūpaṇam |
atha vyatīpātaḥ |
tatra praśaṃsādi yājñavalkyaḥ |
śatam indukṣaye dānaṃ sahasraṃ tu dinakṣaye |
viṣuve śatasāhasraṃ vyatīpāte tv anantakam ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
darśe śataguṇaṃ dānaṃ tacchataghnaṃ dinakṣaye |
tacchataghnaṃ ca saṃkrāntau śataghnaṃ viṣuve tataḥ ||
yugādau tacchataguṇam ayane tacchatāhataṃ |
somagrahe tacchataghnaṃ ravigrahe tv asaṃkṣayaṃ |
vyatīpāte tv anantaṃ ca dānaṃ vedavido viduḥ ||
'śataghnaṃ' śataguṇam ity arthaḥ | vyatīpāto ’tra viṣkambhādiṣu yogeṣu saptadaśo yogaḥ | asyotpattim āha gālavaḥ |
candrārkayor nayanavīkṣaṇajātamūrttiḥ
kālānaladyutinibhaḥ puruṣo ’tiraudraḥ |
[242]        astrodyato ’nunipataṃś ca nirīkṣyamāṇaḥ
saṅghātayevam iti ca vyatipātayogaḥ ||
asya cotpattibhramaṇapatanasamayeṣu patanānantaraṃ ca kriyamāṇaṃ snānadānaśrāddhādikaṃ mahāphalaṃ bhavati | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
utpattau lakṣaguṇaṃ, koṭiguṇaṃ bhramaṇanāḍikāyāṃ tu |
arbudaguṇitaṃ patane, japadānādyakṣayaṃ patite ||
utpattyādimānaṃ ca jyotiḥśāstre |
viṃśatir dviyutotpattau bhramaṇe caikaviṃśatiḥ |
patane daśanāḍyas tu patite saptanāḍikāḥ ||
vṛddhamanunā tu prakārāntareṇa vyatīpāto darśitaḥ |
śravaṇāśvidhaniṣṭhādrānāgadaivatamastake |
yady amā ravivāreṇa vyatīpātaḥ sa ucyate ||
nāgadaivatam aśleṣā | mastaka iti śravaṇādibhiḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | kecit tu mastakaṃ mṛgaśira iti vyācakṣate | 'amā' amāvāsyā | sā yady eteṣu nakṣatreṣv ādityavārayuktā bhavati tadā sa eva yogo vyatīpātasaṃjño bhavati | śāstrāntare |
pañcānanasthau gurubhūmiputrau
meṣe raviḥ syād yadi śuklapakṣe |
[243]        pāśābhidhānā karabheṇa yuktā
tithir vyatīpāta itīha yogaḥ ||
asmin hi gobhūmihiraṇyavastra-
dānena sarvaṃ parihāya pāpaṃ |
śūratvam indratvam anāmayatvaṃ
martyādhipatyaṃ labhate manuṣyaḥ ||
'pañcānanaḥ' siṃhaḥ | 'gurubhūmiputrau' bṛhaspatyaṅgārakau | 'pāśābhidhānā' dvādaśī | 'karabhaṃ' hastanakṣatraṃ | āha bhṛguḥ |
krāntisāmyasamayaḥ samīritaḥ
sūryaparvasadṛśo munīśvaraiḥ |
tatra dattahutajaptapūjana-
yāgakoṭiguṇam āha bhārgavaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ | sūryacandramasoḥ krāntisāmye puṇyakāladvayaṃ bhavati | eko vyatīpātākhyaḥ | aparo vaidhṛtyākhyaḥ | tatra krāntisāmyalakṣaṇasya vyatīpātasya gaṇḍottarārdhād ārabhya kramāt sārdheṣu pañcayogeṣu saṃbhavo ’sti | vaidhṛtisaṃjñasya tu śuklayogād ārabhya kramāt sārdheṣu pañcayogeṣu saṃbhavaḥ | tatparvakālasaṃkhyā ca daśaghaṭikābhyaḥ samārabhyaikasaptatyadhikaśataparyantaṃ saṃbhāvyate |
tathā ca vedāṅge jyotiṣe |
[244]        gaṇḍottarārdhād vyatipātasaṃbhavaḥ
śuklādito vaidhṛtisaṃjñako bhavet |
sārdheṣu pañcasu pareṣu tatheṣyate saḥ
evaṃ dvayaṃ tat sukṛtaikasādhakaṃ ||
āha viṣṇuḥ |
vaidhṛtau ca vyataīpāte dattam akṣayakṛt bhavet |
bharadvājaḥ |
vyatīpāte vaidhṛte ca dattasyānto na vidyate |
vyatīpāte viśeṣeṇa sa hi sūkṣmaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
sthūlaprakāreṇa prasiddhas tu saptaviṃśatitamo yogo vaidhṛta iti | atra ca vyatīpāte śrāddhaṃ kartavyam ity āha pitāmahaḥ |
amāvāsyāvyatīpātapaurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca |
vidvān śrāddham akurvāṇaḥ prāyaścittīyate tu saḥ ||
atra prāyaścittīyate iti vacanaṃ śrāddhasyāvaśyakatāṃ gamayati ||
iti vyatīpātanirūpaṇaṃ ||
atha gajacchāyā |
skandapurāṇe |
yadenduḥ pitṛdaivatye haṃsaś caiva kare sthitaḥ |
tithir vaiśravaṇī yā ca gajacchāyeti sā smṛtā ||
‘induḥ' candramāḥ | ‘pitṛdaivatye' nakṣatre maghāyāṃ | 'haṃsaḥ' sūryaḥ | 'karaḥ' hastaḥ | 'tithir vaiśravaṇī' trayodaśī |
[245] narasiṃhapurāṇe |
haṃse haṃsasthite yā tu maghāyuktā trayodaśī |
tithir vaiśravaṇī nāma sā chāyā kuñjarasya tu ||
brahmapurāṇe |
yogo maghātrayodaśyoḥ kuñjaracchāyasaṃjñakaḥ |
bhaven maghāyāṃ saṃsthe ca śaśiny arke karasthite || iti |
ayam arthaḥ | sūrye hastasthite candrādhiṣṭhitābhir maghābhis trayodaśyā yogo gajacchāyasaṃjñakaḥ | sa ca bhādrapadāparapakṣe saṃbhavati | vibhāṣāsenāsurācchāyeti chāyāśabdasya napuṃsakatvaṃ |
haṃse haṃsasthite yā tu amāvāsyā karānvitā |
sā jñeyā kuñjaracchāyā iti baudhāyano ’bravīt ||
'haṃsaḥ' sūryaḥ | haṃsadevatatvāt hastanakṣatram api haṃsaḥ | 'karānvitā' candrayuktahaṃsānvitā |
saiṃhikeyo yadā sūryaṃ grasate parvasaṃdhiṣu |
gajacchāyā tu sā proktā tasyāṃ śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
‘saiṃhikeyaḥ’ rāhuḥ | ‘parvasaṃdhiṣu' amāvasyāpratipadoḥ saṃdhiṣu | 'gajacchāyā' prasaṅgād deśarūpāpi gajacchāyocyate |
manuḥ |
api naḥ sa kule bhūyād yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ prākchāye kuñjarasya ca ||
prācīcchāyā yasmin deśe sa prākchāyo deśaḥ tasmin kuñjaraḥ prasiddhaḥ |
mahābhārate ’pi |
ajena sarvalohena varṣāsu niyatavrataḥ |
[246]        hasticchāyāsu vidhivat karṇavyajanavījitaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ dadyād iti śeṣaḥ |
'sarvalohena' lohitasarvāṅgena | karṇavyajanavījita ity anenaitad vacanaṃ pratyakṣagajacchāyāviṣayam ity avagamyate | mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
vanaspatigate some chāyā tu prāṅmukhī bhavet |
gajacchāyā tu sā proktā tasyāṃ śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet ||
amāvāsyāparāhṇa ity arthaḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
ghṛtena bhojayed viprān ghṛtaṃ bhūmau samutsṛjet |
chāyāyāṃ hastinaś caiva dadyāc chrāddhaṃ na śocati ||
viṣṇudharmottare pitṛvākyaṃ |
gayāyāṃ darśane rāhoḥ khaḍgamāṃsena yoginam |
bhojayet tu kule ’smākaṃ chāyāyāṃ kuñjarasya ca ||
akalpakālikī tṛptis tenāsmākaṃ bhaviṣyati |
dātā sarveṣu lokeṣu kāmacārī bhaviṣyati ||
yadaitat pañcakaṃ na syād ekenāpi vayaṃ tadā |
tṛptiṃ prāpsyāmo mahatīṃ kiṃ punaḥ sarvasampadeti ||
atra śrāddhabhoktur doṣaḥ smṛtyantare darśitaḥ |
kṛṣṇājinapratigrāhī vikrayī caiva retasaḥ |
gajacchāyāśrito bhuktvā na bhūyaḥ puruṣo bhavet || iti |
brahmapurāṇe 'py uktaṃ |
[247]        mṛtake sūtake caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
chāyāyāṃ kuñjarasyātha bhuktvā tu narakaṃ vrajet || iti |
ayaṃ ca gajacchāyāyāṃ bhojane doṣaḥ |
kenacit pratyakṣagajacchāyāyām ity uktaṃ | kenacit pāribhāṣikagajacchāyāyām iti| tatra vispaṣṭo viśeṣahetur nopalabhyate | iti sārvatrika evaṃ doṣaḥ |
iti gajacchāyānirūpaṇaṃ |
atha candrasūryayor grahaṇaṃ |
tatra praśaṃsādi śātātapaḥ |
snānaṃ dānaṃ tapaḥ śrāddham anantaṃ rāhudarśane |
āsurīṃ rātrim anyatra tasmāt tāṃ parivarjayet ||
devalaḥ |
yathā snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca sūryasya grahaṇe divā |
somasyāpi tathā rātrau snānaṃ dānaṃ vidhīyate ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
candre vā yadi vā sūrye dṛṣṭe rāhau mahāgrahe |
akṣayaṃ kathitaṃ puṇyaṃ tatrārke tu viśeṣataḥ ||
'dṛṣṭe' darśanayogya ity arthaḥ |
vyāsaḥ |
sarvaṃ bhūmisamaṃ dānaṃ sarve brahmasamā dvijāḥ |
sarvaṃ gaṅgāsamaṃ toyaṃ rāhugraste divākare |
[248]        indor lakṣaguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ raver daśaguṇaṃ tataḥ |
gaṅgātoye tu saṃprāpte indoḥ koṭīraver daśa ||
devīpurāṇe |
kārttike grahaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ gaṅgāyamunasaṃgame |
mārge tu grahaṇaṃ puṇyaṃ devikāyāṃ mahāmune ||
pauṣe tu narmadā puṇyā māghe sannihatī śubhā |
phālgune varaṇā puṇyā caitre puṇyā sarasvatī ||
vaiśākhe grakṣaṇe (?) puṇyā candrabhāgā saridvarā |
jyeṣṭhe tu kauśikī puṇyā āṣāḍhe tāpikā nadī ||
śrāvaṇe sindhunāmā ca prauṣṭhe śreṣṭhā ca gaṇḍakī |
āśvine sarayūḥ śreṣṭhā bhūyaḥ puṇyā tu narmadā ||
godāvarī mahāpuṇyā candre rāhusamanvite |
sūrye ca śaśinā graste tamorūpe mahāmate ||
narmadātoyasaṃsargāt kṛtakṛtyo bhaven naraḥ |
spṛśanti cāvagāhanti narāḥ prakṛtimānuṣāḥ ||
smṛtvā śatakratuphalaṃ dṛṣṭvā godānajaṃ phalam |
spṛṣṭvā gomedhajaṃ puṇyaṃ pītvā sautrāmaṇīṃ labhet |
snātvā vājimakhaṃ puṇyaṃ prāpnuyād avicārataḥ ||
ravicandroparāge ca ayane cottare tathā |
evaṃ gaṅgāpi draṣṭavyā tadvad devī sarasvatī ||
gaṅgāyamunam ity atra sarvo ’pi dvandvo vibhāṣayaikavad bhavatīty ekavadbhāvaḥ |
[249]        atra yady api pauṣe tu narmadā puṇyety ātideśapradhāno vyapadeśo dṛśyate tathāpi kārttike grahaṇaṃ śreṣṭham ityādyupakramānurodhāt sarvatra grahaṇakālasyaiva prādhānyam avagantavyam |
vyāsaḥ |
sūryavāre bhaved bhānoḥ some somagrahas tathā |
cūḍāmaṇir iti khyātas tatrānantaṃ phalaṃ bhavet ||
vāreṣv anyeṣu yat puṇyaṃ grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
tat puṇyaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ yoge cūḍāmaṇau smṛtam ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
rāhoś ca darśane dattaṃ śrāddham ācandratārakam |
guṇavat sarvakāmīyaṃ pitṝṇām upatiṣṭhate ||
ṛṣyaśṛaṅgaḥ |
rāhugraste tu vai sūrye yas tu śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet |
tena vai sakalā pṛthvī dattā viprasya vai kare ||
vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
tridaśāḥ sparśasamaye tṛpyanti pitaras tathā |
manuṣyā madhyakāle tu muktikāle ca rākṣasāḥ || iti |
grahaṇasyādimadhyāvasāneṣu kṛte śrāddhe kramāt tridaśādayas tṛpyantīty arthaḥ |
liṅgapurāṇe śātātapaḥ |
sarvasvenāpi kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ vai rāhudarśane |
akurvāṇas tu tacchrāddhaṃ paṅke gaur iva sīdati ||
[250]        itivacanaṃ śrāddhasyāvaśyakatāṃ gamayati | kūrmapurāṇe cāsya śrāddhasya naimittikatvenāvaśyakatāṃ pratipādya kāmyatvam api pratipāditam |
amāvāsyāṣṭakās tisraḥ pauṣamāsādiṣu triṣu |
tisraś cānvaṣṭakāḥ puṇyā māghī pañcadaśī tathā ||
trayodaśī maghāyuktā varṣāsu ca viśeṣataḥ |
tasya pākaśrāddhakālā nityāḥ prokā dine dine ||
naimittikaṃ tu kartavyaṃ grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ |
bāndhavānāṃ ca maraṇe nārakī syād ato ’nyathā ||
kāmyāni caiva śrāddhāni śasyante grahaṇādiṣu |
ayane viṣuve caiva vyatīpāte 'py anantakam ||
saṃkrāntyām akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ tathā janmadineṣv api |
nakṣatreṣu ca sarveṣu kāryaṃ kāmyaṃ viśeṣataḥ || iti |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
viśiṣṭabrāhmaṇaprāptau sūryendugrahaṇe ’yane |
viṣuve ravisaṃkrāntau vyatīpāte ca putraka |
śrāddhārhadravyasaṃprāptau tathā duḥsvapnadarśane |
janmarkṣagrahapīḍāsu śrāddhaṃ kurvīta cecchayā || iti |
atra sūryagrahaṇādikāleṣu tathā 'icchayā' svargaphalakāmanayā, śrāddhaṃ kuryād iti icchayety asyārthaḥ, na punar yadīcchati kartuṃ tarhi kuryāt na cen na kuryād iti |
[251]        matsyapurāṇe |
candrasūryagrahe caiva maraṇe putrajanmani |
malamāse ’pi deyaṃ syād dattam akṣayakārakam ||
śātātapaḥ |
āpady anagnau tīrthe ca candrasūryagrahe tathā |
āmaśrāddhaṃ dvijo dadyāc chūdro dadyāt sadaiva tu ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
sūryagrahau yadā rātrau divā candragrahas tathā |
tatra snānaṃ na kurvīta dadyād dānaṃ na ca kvacit ||
iti grahaṇanirūpaṇaṃ |
atha yugādayaḥ |
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyā yā navamī kārttikasya tu |
paurṇamāsī ca māghasya nabhasye ca trayodaśī |
yugādayaḥ smṛtā hy etā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ ||
nabhasyo bhādrapadaḥ | atra trayodaśīvyatiriktāḥ sarvāḥ śuklāḥ | tathā ca brahmapurāṇe |
vaiśākhaśuklapakṣe tu tṛtīyāyāṃ kṛtaṃ yugam |
kārttike śuklapakṣe tu tretā ca navame ’hani ||
atha bhādrapade kṛṣṇatrayodaśyāṃ tu dvāparam |
māghe tu paurṇamāsyāṃ ca ghoraṃ kaliyugaṃ tathā |
yugārambhās tu tithayo yugādyās tena saṃsmṛtāḥ ||
kṛtayugaṃ pravṛttam iti śeṣaḥ | atra kecit,
dve śate dve tathā kṛṣṇa[252]yugādau kavayo viduḥ |
iti nāradīyapurāṇavacanāt yugādidvayasya kṛṣṇapakṣasthatvaṃ manyamānā māghasyāmāvāsyām eva yugādim āhuḥ |
tathā ca nāgarakhaṇḍe |
adhunā śṛṇu rājendra yugādyāḥ pitṛvallabhāḥ |
yāsāṃ saṃkīrtanenāpi kṣīyate pāpasaṃcayaḥ ||
navamī kārttike śuklā tṛtīyā mādhave sitā |
amāvāsyā ca tapaso nabhasye ca trayodaśī |
tretākṛtakalīnāṃ tu dvāparasyādayaḥ kramāt ||
snāne dāne jape home viśeṣāt pitṛtarpaṇe |
kṛtasyākṣayakāriṇyaḥ sukṛtasya mahāphalāḥ ||
'mādhave' vaiśākhe, 'sitā' śuklā, 'tapasaḥ' māghasya |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
vaiśākhamāsasya tu yā tṛtīyā
navamy asau kārttikaśuklapakṣe |
nabhasyamāsasya ca kṛṣṇapakṣe
trayodaśī pañcadaśī ca māghe ||
pānīyam apy atra tilair vimiśraṃ
dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ prayato manuṣyaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ tena samāsahasraṃ
rahasyam etat pitaro vadanti ||
yāsau tṛtīyā yāsau navamītyādisaṃbandhaḥ |
bhaviṣyottare |
[253]        vaiśākhasya tṛtīyā yā samā kṛtayugena sā |
navamī kārttike yā tu tretāyugasamā tu sā ||
trayodaśī nabhasye tu dvāpareṇa samā matā |
māghe pañcadaśī rājan kalikālasamā smṛtā ||
etāś catasro rājendra yugānāṃ prabhavāḥ śubhāḥ |
yugādayas tu kathyante tenaitāḥ pūrvasūribhiḥ ||
upavāsas tapo dānaṃ śrāddhaṃ homo japas tathā |
yad atra kriyate kiṃcit sarvaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet ||
kṛtayugena sameti | samagre ’pi kṛtayuge pratyahaṃ kriyamāṇena karmaṇā yāvat puṇyaṃ bhavati tāvad asyām eva tithāv ity arthaḥ | koṭiśabdo ’tra bāhulyātiśayaparaḥ |
matsyapurāṇe |
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyā yā navamī kārttikasya tu |
pañcadaśy api māghasya nabhasye tu trayodaśī |
yugādayaḥ smṛtā hy etā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
tṛtīyā rohiṇīyuktā vaiśākhasya tu yā sitā |
maghābhiḥ sahitā kṛṣṇā nabhasye tu trayodaśī |
yugādayaḥ smṛtā hy etā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyāyāṃ navamyāṃ kārttikasya tu |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ tu śuklāyāṃ saṃkrāntividhinā naraiḥ ||
[254]        trayodaśyāṃ bhādrapade mādhe candrakṣaye ’hani |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ pāyasena dakṣiṇāyanavac ca tat ||
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ vinetyarthaḥ |
iti yugādayaḥ |
atha yugāntāḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
sūryasya siṃhasaṃkrāntyām antaḥ kṛtayugasya tu |
atha vṛścikasaṃkrāntyām antas tretāyugasya tu ||
jñeyas tu vṛṣasaṃkrāntyāṃ dvāparāntaś ca saṃjñayā |
tathā ca kumbhasaṃkrāntyām antaḥ kaliyugasya tu |
yugādiṣu yugānteṣu śrāddham akṣayam ucyate ||
iti yugāntāḥ |
atha manvantarādayaḥ |
tatra mātsya-brahmapurāṇyoḥ |
aśvayukśuklanavamī dvādaśī kārttikasya ca |
caitrasya tu tṛtīyā yā tathā bhādrapadasya tu ||
phālgunasya tv amāvāsyā pauṣasyaikādaśī tathā |
śrāvaṇasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathāṣāḍhasya pūrṇimā ||
āṣāḍhasya tu daśamī māghamāsasya saptamī |
kārttikaī phālgunī caitrī jyeṣṭhe pañcadaśī tathā |
manvantarādayaś caitā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ ||
[255]        snānaṃ dānaṃ japo hāmaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛtarpaṇam |
sarvam evākṣayaṃ vidyāt kṛtaṃ manvantarādiṣu ||
amāvāsyāṣṭamīvyatirekeṇa sarvāḥ śuklā eva |
nāradīyapurāṇe śrāvaṇasyāmāvāsyā bhādrapadasya kṛṣṇāṣṭamī manvantarādaya iti pradipāditam | tad yathā |
āśvine śuktanavamī dvādaśī kārttike tathā |
tṛtīyā caitramāsasya tathā bhādrapadasya ca ||
śrāvaṇasyāpy amāvasyā pauṣasyaikādaśī tathā |
āṣāḍhasyāpi daśamī māghamāsasya saptamī ||
nabhasyasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathāṣāḍhīyapūrṇimā |
kārttikī phālgunī caitrī jyeṣṭhe pañcadaśī tathā |
manvantarādayo hy etā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe māghaśuklatṛtīyāpi manvantarādir ity uktaṃ |
manvādir api te vatsa tāḥ śṛṇuṣva narādhipa |
pitṝṇāṃ vallabhā nityaṃ sarvapāpakṣayāvahāḥ ||
yāsu toyam api kṣmāyāṃ pradattaṃ tilamiśritam |
pitṛbhyo ’kṣayatāṃ yāti śraddhāpūtena cetasā ||
aśvayukśuklanavamī dvādaśī kārttikasya ca |
tṛtīyāpi ca māghasya tathā bhādrapadasya ca ||
amāvāsyā tapasyasya pauṣasyaikādaśī tathā |
[256]        tathāṣāḍhasya daśamī māghamāsasya saptamī ||
śrāvaṇasyāṣṭamī kṛṣṇā tathāṣāḍhasya pūrṇimā |
tathā kārttikamāsasya tathā vai phālgunasya ca ||
caitrasya jyaiṣṭhamāsasya pañcaitāḥ pūrṇimā nṛpa |
manvantarādayaḥ proktā āsāṃ pūrṇāś ca yā tava ||
āsu tāyam api snātvā tiladarbhavimiśritam |
pitṝn uddiśya yo dadyāt sa gatiṃ paramāṃ labhet ||
iha loke pare caiva pitṝṇāṃ ca prasādataḥ |
kiṃ punar vividhair annai rasair vastrais tu dakṣiṇaiḥ ||
mātsya-brāhmapurāṇayoḥ |
yasyāṃ manvantarasyādau ratham āpa divākaraḥ |
māghamāsasya saptamyāṃ sā tu syād rathasaptamī ||
pānīyam apy atra tilair vimiśraṃ
dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ prayato manuṣyaḥ |
śrāddhaṃ kṛtaṃ tena samāsahasraṃ
rahasyam etat pitaro vadanti ||
iti manvantarādayaḥ |
atha kalpādayaḥ |
tatra nāgarakhaṇḍe |
atha kalpādayo rājan kathyante tithayaḥ śubhāḥ |
yāsu śrāddhe kṛte tṛptiḥ pitṝṇām akṣayā bhavet ||
[257]        caitraśuklapratipadi śvetaḥ kalpaḥ purābhavat |
tasya śuklatrayodaśyām udānaḥ samajāyata ||
kalpas tu nārasiṃhākhyaḥ kṛṣṇāyāṃ pratipady abhūt |
atha kṛṣṇatrayodaśyāṃ gaurīkalpo ’py akalpayat ||
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyāyāṃ śuklāyāṃ nīlalohitaḥ |
caturdaśyāṃ tu śuklāyāṃ pravṛtto gāruḍābhidhaḥ ||
samānas tu dvitīyāyāṃ kṛṣṇāyām upapadyate |
māheśvaraś caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe samāgamat ||
jyeṣṭhaśuklatṛtīyāyāṃ vāmadevasya saṃbhavaḥ |
paurṇamāsyāṃ tu tasyaiva kaurmaḥ pravavṛte nṛpa ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe tṛtīyāyām āgneyaḥ samagacchata |
pitṛpakṣe tv amāvāsyāṃ tasyaivāśritya paprathe ||
śucau śuklacaturthyāṃ tu kalpo rathyantaro ’bhavat |
tasyāṃ tasyaiva kṛṣṇāyāṃ somakalpaḥ samāpatat ||
śrāvaṇe śuklapañcamyāṃ rauravaḥ samavartata |
tasyaiva kṛṣṇapañcamyāṃ mānavaḥ samapadyata ||
ṣaṣṭhīṃ prauṣṭhapade śuklāṃ prāpya prāṇādhipo ’bhavat |
sitetarāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ tu tasya tatpuruṣāhvayaḥ ||
bahukalpas tu saptamyāṃ śuklāyām āśvinasya tu |
kṛṣṇāyām api vaikuṇṭhaḥ praviveśa viśāṃpate ||
kārttikasya sitāṣṭamyāṃ kalpaḥ kandarpasaṃjñakaḥ |
asitāyāṃ punar jajñe lakṣmīkalpasya kalpanā ||
[258]        mārge śuklanavamyāṃ tu kalpaḥ saṅgho ’bhyapadyata |
asitāyāṃ tu sāvitrīkalpaḥ prārambham abhyagāt ||
pauṣe daśamyāṃ śuklāyāṃ īśānaḥ prādur āsa ha |
asitāyām aghorasya kalpasyopakramo ’bhavat ||
ekādaśyāṃ tu śuklāyāṃ māghe vyānaḥ prajajñivān |
tasyām eva tu miśrāyāṃ vārāhaḥ prāpa bhūpate ||
sārasvatas tu dvādaśyāṃ śuklāyāṃ phālgunasya ca |
kṛṣṇāyām atha vai rājan virājas tu mahāmate ||
iti triṃśadamī (?) kalpās tithayaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ |
ārambhatithayas teṣām uktāḥ puṇyatamā mayā |
tāsu śrāddhe kṛte puṇyam ākalpāya prakalpate ||
matsyapurāṇe |
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyā yā kṛṣṇā yā phālgunasya ca |
pañcamī caitramāsasya tasyaivāntyā tathā parā ||
śuklā trayodaśī māghe kārttikasya ca saptamī |
navamī mārgaśīrṣasya saptaitāḥ saṃsmarāmy ahaṃ |
kalpānām ādayo hy etā dattasyākṣayakārikāḥ ||
‘antyā’ amāvāsyā | vaiśākhatṛtīyā, phālgunatṛtīyā, caitrapañcamī, caitrāmāvāsyeti kṛṣṇāḥ | māghatrayodaśī, kārttikasaptamī, mārgaśīrṣanavamī etāḥ śuklā: |
iti kalpādayaḥ |
[259]
atha dravyabrāhmaṇasampattyopalakṣitaḥ kālaḥ |
tatra hārītaḥ |
tīrthadravyopapattau tu na kālam avadhāraya |
pātraṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ prāpya sadyaḥ śrāddhaṃ vidhīyate ||
'tīrthaṃ' gayādi, 'dravyaṃ' annādi vakṣyamāṇaṃ | śrotriyetyādiviśevaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ ca brāhmaṇaṃ prāpya na kālāntaraṃ pratīkṣeta, kiṃ tu tasminn eva kāle śrāddhaṃ kuryāt | atra sadya iti vacanād āvaśyakatvaṃ pratīyate | mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
viśiṣṭabrāhmaṇaprāptau sūryendugrahaṇe ’yane |
śrāddhārhadravyasaṃpattau tathā duḥkhapradarśane |
janmarkṣagrahapīḍāsu śrāddhaṃ kurvīta cecchayā || iti ||
atrecchāpadaṃ yathā nityatvena na virudhyate tathā vyākhyātam eva grahaṇaprakaraṇe |
brahmapurāṇe |
yadā ca śrotriyo ’bhyeti ge (? gehe) vedavid agnicit |
tenaikenāpi kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ viṣuvavac chubham ||
śrāddhīyadravyasaṃprāptir yadā syāt sādhusaṅgamaḥ |
pārvaṇena vidhānena śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ dvijottamaiḥ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe dravyabrāhmaṇasaṃpattyupalakṣite kāle kāmyam api śrāddham uktaṃ | tathā hi tatra 'kāmyān kalpān śṛṇuṣva' ity asyānantaraṃ bhavati vacanaṃ |
śrāddhārham āgataṃ dravyaṃ viśiṣṭam atha vā dvijaṃ |
śraddhaṃ kurvīta vijñāya vyatīpāte ’yane tathā |
iti dravyabrāhmaṇasaṃpattyupalakṣitakālanirūpaṇaṃ |
[260]
atha prakīrṇakālaḥ |
matsyapurāṇe |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi viṣṇunā yad udīritam |
śrāddhaṃ sādhāraṇaṃ nāma bhuktimuktiphalapradam ||
ayane viṣuve yugme sāmānye vārkasaṃkrame |
amāvāsyāṣṭakāyāṃ vā kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ ||
ādrāmaghārohiṇīṣu dravyabrāhmaṇasaṅgame |
gajacchāyāvyatīpāte viṣṭivaidhṛatavāsare ||
tathā |
vaiśākhyām uparāge tu tathotsavamahālaye
brahmapurāṇe |
śrāddhaṃ deyam uśantīha māsi māsy uḍupakṣaye | |
paurṇamāse ’pi ca śrāddhaṃ kartavyam ṛkṣagocare ||
nityaṃ śrāddhaṃ vidheyaṃ ca manuṣyair iha gopate |
vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ ca kartavyaṃ jātakarmādike budhaiḥ ||
kanyāgate savitari dināni daśa pañca ca |
pārvaṇeneha vidhinā śrāddhaṃ tatra vidhīyate ||
āha jātūkarṇaḥ |
grahoparāge ca sute ca jāte
pitrye maghāyām ayanadvaye vā |
[261]        nityaṃ ca śaṅkhe ca tathaiva padme
dattaṃ bhaven niṣkasahasratulyam ||
atra yan nityaṃ dattam ity anvayaṃ manyamānā grahoparāgādiṣu śrāddhāder nityatāṃ bruvate | atha vā 'nityam' ahar ahar yāvajjīvaṃ | 'pitrye maghāyām' iti bhādrapadāparapakṣasthitāsu maghāsv ity arthaḥ | śaṅkhapadmayor lakṣaṇam api sa evāha |
śaṅkhaṃ prāhur amāvāsyāṃ kṣīṇasomāṃ dvijottamāḥ |
aṣṭakāsu bhavet padmaṃ tayor dattaṃ tathākṣayam || iti |
śaṅkhas tv āha |
yadā viṣṭirvyatīpāto bhānuvāras tathaiva ca |
padmakaṃ nāma tat proktam ayanāc ca caturguṇam || iti |
viṣṇudharmottare |
aśvinasyāpare pakṣe prathame kārttikasya ca |
yas tu śrāddhaṃ sadā kuryāt so ’śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
nidrāṃ tyajati sarvātmā tasmin kāle janārdanaḥ |
tatra śrāddham athānantaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
tathā |
akṣayaṃ ca tathā śrāddhaṃ vijñeyaṃ rāhudarśane |
vrīhipāke ca kartavyaṃ yavapāke ca pārthiva |
na tau tyājyau mahārāja vinā śrāddhaṃ kathaṃcana ||
śātātapaḥ |
navodake navānne ca gṛahapracchādane tathā |
[262]        pitaraḥ spṛhayanty annaṃ varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca |
tasmād dadyāt sadā yukto vidvatsu brāhmaṇeṣu ca ||
āha pitāmahaḥ |
amāvāsyāvyatīpāta paurṇamāsyaṣṭakāsu ca |
vidvān śrāddham akurvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate ||
iti prakīrṇakālāḥ |
atha kāmyaśrāddhakālāḥ |
tatra tāvat tithayaḥ | āha kātyāyanaḥ |
atha kāmyāni bhavanti | striyaḥ pratirūpāḥ pratipadi | dvitīyāyāṃ strījanma | aśvāḥ tṛtīyāyāṃ | caturthyāṃ kṣudrapaśavaḥ | putrāḥ pañcamyāṃ | ṣaṣṭhyām adhvabhāgī | kṛṣiḥ saptamyāṃ | vāṇijyam aṣṭamyāṃ | ekaśaphaṃ navamyāṃ | daśamyāṃ gāvaḥ | paricārakā ekādaśyāṃ | dvādaśyāṃ dhanadhānyaṃ kupyaṃ jātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ | hiraṇyāni trayodaśyāṃ | yuvānaḥ śastrahatasya caturdaśyāṃ | amāvāsyāyāṃ sarvam || iti |
āpastambaḥ |
sarveṣv evāparapakṣasyāhaḥsu kriyamāṇe pitṝn prīṇāti | kartus tu kālaniyamāt phalaviśeṣāḥ | prathame ’hani kriyamāṇe strīprāyam apatyaṃ jāyate | dvitīye straiṇaṃ | tṛtīye brahmavarcasaṃ | caturthe kṣudrapaśuḥ | pañcame pumān bahvapatyo na cānapatyaḥ pramīyate | ṣaṣṭhe 'dhvaśīlo ’kṣaśīlaś ca | saptame kṛṣer āptiḥ | aṣṭame puṣṭiḥ | navame ekakhuram | daśame vyavahārasiddhiḥ | ekādaśe kṛṣṇāyasaṃ trapuśīsam | dvādaśe paśumān | trayodaśe bahumitro [263] darśanīyāpatyo yuvaparimāṇas tu bhavati | caturdaśe āyudhasiddhiḥ | pañcadaśe puṣṭiḥ |
satataṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvann āpnotīty adhikāre viṣṇuḥ |
gṛhe sarūpāstriyaḥ pratipadi | kanyāṃ varāṃ dvitīyāyām | aśvāṃs tṛtīyāyām | yaśaś caturthyām | śriyaṃ pañcamyām | dyūtavijayaṃ ṣaṣṭhyām | kṛṣiḥ saptamyām | vāṇijyam aṣṭamyām | paśūn navamyām | vājino daśamyām | putrān brahmavarcasvinas tv ekādaśyām | kanakarajate dvādaśyām | saubhāgyaṃ trayodaśyām | sarvān kāmān pañcadaśyām | śastrahatānāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi caturdaśī praśastā |
śrāddhaṃ kuryād ity adhikāre hārītaḥ |
pañcamīṃ putrakāmaḥ | ṣaṣṭhīṃ dhanakāmaḥ | saptamīṃ paśukāmaḥ | aṣṭamīṃ rājyakāmaḥ | navamīṃ jetukāmaḥ | daśamīm annakāmaḥ | ekādaśīṃ vṛddhikāmaḥ | dvādaśīṃ strīkāmaḥ | trayodaśīṃ yaśaskāmaḥ | caturdaśīṃ bhūtikāmaḥ | svastikāmo ’māvāsyāyāṃ sarvakāmaḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
pañcamyāṃ putrakāmo yajeta | ṣaṣṭhyāṃ paśukāmaḥ | saptamyāṃ vṛddhikāmaḥ | aṣṭamyām annādyakāmaḥ | navamyāṃ brahmavarcasakāmaḥ | daśamyāṃ sarvakāmaḥ | ekādaśyāṃ dhanakāmaḥ | dvādaśyāṃ kṣetrakāmaḥ | trayodaśyāṃ strīvaśitvaṃ | caturdaśyāṃ śastreṇa hatānāṃ | sarvakāmo ’māvāsyāyām |
manuḥ |
kurvan pratipadi śrāddhaṃ surūpān labhate sutān |
kanyakāṃ tu dvitīyāyāṃ tṛtīyāyāṃ tu vandinaḥ ||
[264]        paśūn kṣudrāṃś caturthyāṃ tu pañcamyāṃ śobhanān sutān |
ṣaṣṭhyāṃ dyūtaṃ kṛṣiṃ cāpi saptamyāṃ labhate naraḥ ||
aṣṭamyām api vāṇijyaṃ labhate śrāddhadaḥ sadā |
navamyām evaikakhuraṃ daśamyāṃ dvikhuraṃ tathā ||
ekādaśyāṃ tathā raupyaṃ brahmavarcasvinaḥ sutān |
dvādaśyāṃ jātarūpaṃ ca rajataṃ kūpyam eva ca ||
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ trayodaśyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ tu suprajāḥ |
prīyante pitaraś cāsya ye ca śastrahatā raṇe ||
pakṣatyādivinirdiṣṭān vipulān manasaḥ priyān |
śrāddhadaḥ pañcadaśyāṃ tu sarvān kāmān samaśnute ||
'vandinaḥ' stāvakān |
brahmapurāṇa-mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇayoḥ |
kanyāgate savitari dināni daśa pañca ca |
pārvaṇeneha vidhinā śrāddhaṃ tatra vidhīyate ||
pratipad dhanalābhāya dvitīyā dvipadapradā |
varārthinī tṛtīyā tu caturthī pāpanāśinī ||
śriyaṃ prāpnoti pañcamyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ pūjyo bhaven naraḥ |
gaṇādhipatyaṃ saptamyāṃ aṣṭamyāṃ buddhim uttamām ||
śriyo navamyāṃ prāpnoti daśamyāṃ pūrṇakāmatām |
bedāṃs tathāpnuyāt sarvān ekādaśyāṃ kriyāparaḥ ||
[265]        dvādaśyāṃ jayalābhaṃ ca prāpnoti pitṛpūjakaḥ |
prajāṃ medhāṃ paśūn vṛddhiṃ svātantryaṃ puṣṭim uttamām ||
dīrgham āyur athaiśvaryaṃ kurvāṇas tu trayodaśīm |
avāpnoti na saṃdehaḥ śrāddhaṃ śraddhāparo janaḥ ||
yuvānaḥ pitaro yasya mṛtāḥ śastreṇa vā hatāḥ |
tena kāryaṃ caturdaśyāṃ teṣāṃ tṛptim abhīpsatā ||
śrāddhaṃ kurvann amāvāsyāṃ yatnena puruṣaḥ śuciḥ |
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti svarge vāsaṃ samaśnuts ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
striyaḥ surūpāś ca gṛhe kanyājanma tathā dhanam |
sarvān kāmān yaśaś caiva śriyaṃ dyūtajayaṃ kṛṣiṃ ||
vāṇijyaṃ ca paśūṃś caiva vājinaś ca tathā sutān |
suvarṇaraupyasaubhāgyaṃ prāpnoti śrāddhadaḥ kramāt ||
pratipatprabhṛtiṣv etad yāvad rājan trayodaśīm |
caturdaśyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ ye narāḥ śastraghātitāḥ ||
śrāddhaṃ sadā pañcadaśīṣu kurvan
kāmān samagrān labhate manuṣyaḥ |
tasmāt prayatnena narendra kāryaṃ
śrāddhaṃ sadā pañcadaśīṣu tajjñaiḥ ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
[266]        kanyāgate savitari kṛṣṇapakṣe ’ṣṭamī tu yā |
sā ca pāpaharā puṇyā śivasyānandavardhanī ||
snānaṃ dānaṃ japo homaḥ pitṛdevābhipūjanam |
sarvaṃ prītikaraṃ syād dhi kṛtaṃ tasyāṃ trilocane ||
viśeṣataḥ kṛtaṃ śrāddhaṃ homaś ca vidhivan mune |
tasmāt śrāddhaṃ prayatnena tasyāṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
ekabhaktaṃ tu pañcamyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāṃ naktaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
upavāsas tu saptamyām aṣṭamyāṃ pūjayec chivaṃ ||
pūjayitvā śivaṃ bhaktyā pitṛśrāddhaṃ tu kalpayet |
kṛtvā tu vidhivac chrāddhaṃ bhuñjīta pitṛsevitam ||
yas tv asyāṃ kurute śrāddhaṃ pūjayitvā trilocanaṃ |
tasya varṣāṇi tṛptāḥ syuḥ pitaro daśa pañca ca ||
iti kāmyās tithayaḥ |
atha nakṣatrāṇi |
tatra manuḥ |
yukṣu kurvan dinarkṣeṣu sarvān kāmān samaśnute |
ayukṣu ca pitṝn arcan prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām ||
'yukṣu' sameṣu | 'ayukṣu' viṣameṣu |
yamaḥ |
yukṣu kurvan dinarkṣeṣu sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
gajacchāyāṃ pitṝn arcan prajāṃ prāpnoti puṣkalām ||
[267]        yājñavalkyaḥ |
svargaṃ hy apatyam ojaś ca śauryaṃ kṣetraṃ balaṃ tathā |
putraṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ sasaubhāgyaṃ samṛddhiṃ mukhyatāṃ śrutam ||
pravṛttacakratāṃ caiva vāṇijyaprabhṛtīṃs tathā |
arogitvaṃ yaśo vītaśokatāṃ paramāṃ gatim ||
dharmaṃ vidyāṃ bhiṣaksiddhiṃ kupyaṃ gāś cāpy ajādikam |
aśvān āyuś ca vidhivat yaḥ śrāddhaṃ saṃprayacchati ||
kṛttikādibharaṇyantaṃ sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
āstikaḥ śraddadhānaś ca vyapetamadamatsaraḥ ||
'pravṛttacakratā' apratihatājñāśāstitvaṃ |
viṣṇuḥ |
svargaṃ kṛttikāsu | apatyaṃ rohiṇīṣu | brahmavarcasaṃ saumye | raudrāṇāṃ siddhiṃ raudre | bhuvaṃ punarvasau | puṣṭiṃ puṣye | śriyaṃ sārpe | sarvān kāmān paitrye | saubhāgyaṃ bhāgye | dhanam āryamṇe | jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ haste | rūpavataḥ sutāṃs tvāṣṭre | vāṇijyasiddhiṃ svātau | kanakaṃ viśākhāsu | mitrāṇi maitre | rājyaṃ śākre | kṛṣiṃ mūle | samudrayānasiddhim āpye | sarvān kāmān vaiśvadeve | śraiṣṭhyam abhijiti | sarvān kāmān śravaṇe | balaṃ vāsave | ārogyaṃ vāruṇe | kupyadravyam āje | gṛham āhibradhne | gāḥ pauṣṇe | turaṅgamā āśvine | jīvitaṃ yāmye |
'saumyaṃ' mṛgaśiraḥ | 'raudraṃ' ādrā | 'sārpam' aśleṣā | 'paitryaṃ' maghā | 'bhāgyaṃ' pūrvaphālgunyaḥ | 'āryamṇam' uttarāphālgunyaḥ | 'tvāṣṭraṃ' citrā | 'maitram' anurādhā | 'śākraṃ' jyeṣṭhā | 'apyaṃ' pūrvāṣāḍhā | 'vaiśvadevam' uttarāṣāḍhā | 'vāruṇaṃ' śatabhiṣā | 'vāsavaṃ' dhaniṣṭhā | [268] 'āja' pūrvā bhādrapadā | 'āhibradhnaḥ' uttarā bhādrapadā | 'pauṣṇaṃ' revatī | 'āśvinam' aśvinī | ' yāmyaṃ' bharaṇī |
viṣṇudharmottare |
svargaṃ hy apatyāni tathā brahmavarcasam eva ca |
raudrāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ siddhiṃ bhuvaṃ puṣṭiṃ tathā śriyam ||
sarvān kāmāṃś ca saubhāgyaṃ dharmaṃ jñātipradhānatām |
rupayuktāṃś ca tanayān vāṇijyaṃ dhanasaṃpadam ||
kanakaṃ suhṛdo rājyaṃ saphalāṃ ca tathā kṛṣim |
samudrayānalābhaṃ ca sarvān kāmāṃs tathaiva ca ||
śraiṣṭhyaṃ kāmāṃs tathā sarvān balam ārogyam eva ca ||
kupyadravyaṃ gṛhaṃ gāś ca turagāṃś caiva jīvitaṃ ||
kṛttikādibharaṇyante kramād uḍugaṇe naraḥ |
ekaikasmin kramāt kurvan śrāddham āpnoty asaṃśayam ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
kṛttikāsu pitṝn arcan svargam āpnoti mānavaḥ |
apatyakāmo rohiṇyāṃ saumye tejasvitāṃ labhet ||
śauryam ādrāsv avāpnoti kṣetrādi ca punarvasau |
puṣṭiṃ puṣye sadābhyarcya aśleṣāsu varān sutān ||
maghāsu svajanaśraiṣṭhyaṃ saubhāgyaṃ phalgunīṣu ca |
pradānaśīlo bhavati sāpatyaś cottarāsu ca ||
[269]        prayāti śreṣṭhatāṃ yasya haste śrāddhaprado naraḥ |
rūpayuktas tu citrāsu tathāpatyāny avāpnuyāt ||
vāṇijyalābhadā svātir viśākhā putrakāmadā |
kurvantaś cānurādhāsu labhante cakravarttitām |
ādhipatyaṃ ca jyeṣṭhāsu mūle cārogyam uttamam ||
āṣāḍhāsu yaśaḥprāptim uttarāsu viśokatām |
śravaṇe ca śubhān lokān dhaniṣṭhāsu dhanaṃ mahat ||
vedavittvam abhijiti bhiṣaksiddhiṃ ca vāruṇe |
ajādikaṃ prauṣṭhapade vinded bhāryāṃ tathottare ||
revatīṣu tathā kupyam aśvinīṣu turaṅgamān |
śrāddhaṃ kurvaṃs tathāpnoti bharaṇīṣv āyur uttamaṃ ||
tasmāt kāmyāni kurvīta ṛkṣeṣv eteṣu tattvavit |
brahmavaivarte |
yamas tu yāni śrāddhāni provāca śaśibindave |
tāni tvaṃ śṛṇu kāmyāni nakṣatreṣu pṛthak pṛthak ||
śraddhāyoge tu yaḥ śrāddhaṃ karoti satataṃ naraḥ |
agnidātā sudhīmān yo yajate sa gatajvaraḥ ||
apatyakāmo rohiṇyāṃ saumye tejasvitāṃ labhet |
krūrāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ siddhiṃ ārdrāyāṃ śrāddham ācaran ||
[270]        kṣetrabhāgī bhavet putrī śrāddhaṃ kurvan punarvasau |
puṣṭikāmaḥ punaḥ puṣye śrāddhaṃ kurvīta mānavaḥ ||
aśleṣāsu pitṝn arcan vīrān putrān avāpnuyāt |
śreṣṭho bhavati jñātīnāṃ maghāsu śrāddham ācaran ||
phālgunīṣu pitṝn arcan saubhāgyaṃ labhate naraḥ |
pradhānaśīlaḥ so ’vaśyam uttarāsu karoti yaḥ ||
sa sarvamukhyo bhavati haste yas tarpayet pitṝn |
citrāyāṃ caiva yaḥ kuryāt paśyed rūpavataḥ sutān ||
svātīṣu caiva yaḥ kuryād vāṇijyāl lābham āpnuyāt |
putrārthī tu viśākhāsu śrāddham īheta mānavaḥ ||
anurādhāsu kurvāṇo naraś cakraṃ pravartayet |
ādhipatyaṃ labhec chreṣṭhaṃ jyeṣṭhāsu satataṃ tu yaḥ ||
mūlenārogyam icchanti āṣāḍhāsu maheśvaraḥ |
uttarāsu tv āṣāḍhāsu tīrṇaśoko bhaven naraḥ ||
śravaṇe manulokeṣu prāpnuyāt paramāṃ gatim |
rājyabhogaṃ dhaniṣṭhāsu prāpnuyād vipulaṃ dhanam ||
śrāddhaṃ tv abhijite kurvan vedān sāṅgān avāpnuyāt |
nakṣatre vāruṇe kurvan bhaiṣajye siddhim āpnuyāt ||
pūrve prauṣṭhapade bhakto vindetājādikaṃ bahu |
uttarāsv anatikramya vinded gāvaś ca sahasraśaḥ ||
bahukupyakṛtaṃ dravyaṃ vindet kurvaṃs tu revatīm |
aśvān āśvayuje bhuṅkte bharaṇyām āyur uttamam ||
[271]        imaṃ śrāddhavidhiṃ kurvan śaśibindur mahīm imām |
kṛtsnāṃ ca lebhe sotkṛṣṭo labdhvā ca praśaśāsa tām ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
svargaṃ ca labhate kṛtvā kṛttikāsu dvijottamaḥ |
apatyam atha rohiṇyāṃ saumye tu brahmavarcasaṃ ||
raudrāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ siddhim ārdrāyāṃ śauryam eva ca |
punarvasau kṣetrasiddhiṃ puṣye poṣam avāpnuyāt |
sarvān kāmāṃs tathā sārpe paitrye saubhāgyam eva ca ||
āryamṇe tu dhanaṃ vindyāt phālgunyāṃ pāpanāśanaṃ |
jñātiśraiṣṭhyaṃ tathā haste citrāyāṃ ca bahūn sutān ||
vāṇijyasiddhiṃ svātau tu viśākhāsu suvarṇakam |
maitre bahūni mitrāṇi rājyaṃ śākre tathaiva ca ||
mūle kṛṣiṃ labhet yānasiddhim āpye samudrataḥ |
sarvān kāmān vaiśvadeve śraiṣṭhyaṃ tu śravaṇe punaḥ ||
dhaniṣṭhāyāṃ tathā kāmān vāruṇe ca paraṃ balam |
ajaikapāde kupyaṃ syād āhibradhne gṛhaṃ śubham ||
revatyāṃ bahavo gāvaḥ aśvinyāṃ turagāṃs tathā |
yāmye tu jīvitavyaṃ syāt yaḥ śrāddhaṃ saṃprayacchati ||
atrānyāny api brahmapurāṇādivacanāni vailakṣaṇye ’pi sarvathā pūrvoktānatiriktārthānīti na likhyante |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
[272]        maghāsu kurvan śrāddhāni sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
pratyakṣam arcitās tena bhavanti pitaraḥ sadā ||
iti kāmyāni nakṣatrāṇi |
atha vārāḥ |
atra viṣṇuḥ |
satatam āditye ’hni śrāddhaṃ kurvann ārogyam āpnoti | saubhāgyaṃ cāndre | samaravijayaṃ kauje | sarvān kāmān baudhe | vidyām abhīṣṭāṃ jaive | dhanaṃ śaukre | jīvitaṃ śanaiśvare |
'kauje' maṅgaladine | 'jaive' bṛhaspatidine |
viṣṇudharmottare |
atha kāmyāni vakṣyāmi śrāddhāni tava pārthiva |
ārogyam atha saubhāgyaṃ samare vijayaṃ tathā ||
sarvān kāmāṃs tathā vidyāṃ dhanaṃ jīvitam eva ca |
ādityādidineṣv evaṃ śrāddhaṃ kurvan sadā naraḥ |
krameṇaitad avāpnoti nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
ādityavāre tv ārogyaṃ candre saubhāgyam eva ca |
kuje sarvatra vijayaṃ sarvān kāmān budhasya tu ||
vidyām abhīṣṭāṃ ca gurau dhanaṃ vai bhārgave punaḥ |
śanaiśvare labhed āyur ārogyaṃ ca sudurlabham ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
brahmovāca |
ye tv ādityadine prāpte śrāddhaṃ kurvanti mānavāḥ |
saptajanmāni te jātāḥ saṃbhavanty avirodhinaḥ ||
[273]        pañcatāraṃ bhaved yatra nakṣatraṃ govṛṣadhvaja |
vāre tu devadevasya sa vāraḥ putradaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
upavāso bhavet tatra śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ tathā bhavet |
prāśanaṃ cāpi piṇḍasya madhyamasya prakīrtitam ||
sopavāsas tu vai bhaktyā pūjayec caiva gopatim |
dhūpamālyopahārais tu divyagandhasamanvitaiḥ ||
evaṃ pūjya vivasvantaṃ tasyaiva purato niśi |
bhūmau svapiti vai vīra japan śvetaṃ mahāmate |
prātar utthāya ca snānaṃ kṛtvā dattvārghyam uttamam |
raktacandanasaṃmiśraiḥ karavīrair gaṇādhipam ||
saṃpūjya grahabhūteśam āsīnaṃ tu trilocanaṃ |
vīraṃ ca pūjayitvā tu tataḥ śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet ||
pañcabhir brāhmaṇair deva divyair bhaumaiś ca suvrata |
magasaṃjñais tatra daivaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ parikalpayet ||
pitryāṃs tu brāhmaṇān bhaumān prakalpyāndhakasūdana |
kuryād evaṃ tataḥ śrāddhaṃ pārvaṇaṃ bhāskarapriyam ||
śrāddhe samāpte vidhinā tv adyāt piṇḍaṃ tu madhyamam |
purato devadevasya sthitvā mantreṇa suvrata ||
sa eṣa piṇḍo deveśa yo ’bhīṣṭas tava sarvadā |
aśnāmi puratas tubhyaṃ yena me santatir bhavet |
prasādāt tava devasya yan me ’bhyarthayate manaḥ |
itthaṃ saṃpūjito yatra bhāskaraḥ putrado bhavet |
[274]        ato ’yaṃ putrado vāro devasya parikīrtitaḥ ||
evam atra sadā yas tu bhāskaraṃ pūjayen naraḥ |
upavāsaparaḥ śrāddhe sa putraṃ labhate dhanaṃ ||
dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ hiraṇyaṃ ca ārogyaṃ sukham uttamaṃ |
sūryalokaṃ svayaṃ prāptas tato rājā bhaven nṛpa ||
prabhayā dvijasaṅkāśaḥ kāntyā jambukasannibhaḥ |
vīryeṇa gopates tulyo gāmbhīrye kalaśodbhavaḥ ||
‘avirodhinaḥ’ śatruśūnyāḥ | rohiṇyaśleṣāmaghāhastākhyāni catvāri nakṣatrāṇi pañcatārāṇi | śreṣṭho gauḥ 'govṛṣaḥ', sa dhvajo yasya sa ‘govṛṣadhvajaḥ’| vṛṣavyāghrapuṅgavakuñjarāṇāṃ śreṣṭhyavācitā uttarapade, bharatarṣabhādiṣu darśanāt | ‘devadevaḥ' sūryaḥ | upavāso ’tra śanaiścaradine | śrāddham ādityavāre | etac ca prātar utthāyetyādinā granthena vyaktībhaviṣyati | madhyamapiṇḍaprāśanam atra yajamānasyaiva pratīyate, śrāddhāntareṣu tu patnyā vihitatvāt tat kṛtam eva | atra tu dampatyoḥ putrakāmayoḥ sahādhikārād viśeṣānabhidhānāc ca dvayor api piṇḍaprāśanam iti yuktaṃ | dṛśyate ca putrakāmakarmāntare dvayor api śeṣaprāśanaṃ | tad yathā śatapathe “kṣīrodanaṃ pācayitvā sarpiṣmantam aśnīyātām” ityādi | tathātraiva homānantaraṃ hutvoddhṛtya prāśnāti prāśyetarasyāḥ prayacchatīti | ‘gopatiḥ’ sūryaḥ, sa suvarṇādipratimāyāṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇoktaprakāreṇa vyomni vā pūjanīyaḥ | 'śvetaḥ' mahāśvetākhyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ sūryamantraḥ khagolkāya namaḥ ity evaṃ prasiddhaḥ | arghyadānam ambarasthasūryābhimukho bhūtvā sthaṇḍile kuryāt | 'pūjayitvā' iti punaḥ pūjanopādānāt pūjanāntarāvagatau [275] grahabhūteśādayaḥ pañcadevatāḥ praṇavādibhiś caturthyantaiḥ svasvanāmabhiḥ pīṭhe pūjyāḥ | 'divyā magāḥ' bhojakākhyā brāhmaṇaviśeṣatvenottarāpathe prasiddhāḥ, tadanye 'bhaumāḥ' | atra vaiśvadevīkasya sthāne magānupaveśya pitryasthāne brāhmaṇāntarāṇy upaveśayet | evam atra 'sadā' iti viśiṣṭaphalalābhārtham āvṛttikathanaṃ | 'prabhā' aujvalyam | 'dvijaḥ' candramāḥ | 'kāntiḥ' tejaḥ | ‘jambukaḥ' varuṇaḥ | varuṇo vai jambuka iti | varuṇaś cādityaviśeṣaḥ, varuṇo māghamāse tu tapatīti darśanāt | 'gopatiḥ' śambhur ity arthaḥ | ‘kalaśodbhavaḥ' agastyaḥ, sa ca gambhīratayā vivakṣitaḥ |
padmapurāṇe |
śukrāṅgārakayuktā yā caturthī jāyate tadā |
śraddhayā śrāddhakṛd vipro na preto jāyate mṛtaḥ ||
skandapurāṇe |
amāyāṃ yadi somaḥ syāt saptamyāṃ vai divākaraḥ |
caturdaśyāṃ caturthyāṃ vā vāraḥ syān maṅgalasya cet ||
tadā śrāddhaṃ prakartavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptim icchatā |
ṣaṣṭivarṣasahasrāṇi ṣaṣṭivarṣaśatāni ca ||
nandanti pitaraḥ svarge vimānāmbaram āsthitāḥ |
tāvantam eva kālaṃ hi śrāddhakartus tathā phalaṃ ||
iti kāmyavārāḥ |
[276]
atha kāmyāḥ prakīrṇakālāḥ |
tatra viṣṇupurāṇe |
māsi māsy asite pakṣe pañcadaśyāṃ nareśvara |
tathāṣṭakāḥ prakurvīta kāmyān kālān śṛṇuṣva me ||
śrāddhārham āgataṃ dravyaṃ viśiṣṭam atha vā dvijaṃ |
śrāddhaṃ kurvīta vijñāya vyatīpāte ’yane tathā ||
viṣuve cāpi saṃprāpte grahaṇe śaśisūryayoḥ |
samasteṣv eva bhūpāla rāśiṣv ’rke 'nugacchati ||
nakṣatragrahapīḍāsu duṣṭasvapnāvalokane |
icchāśrāddhāni kurvīta navasasyāgame tathā ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
kāryaṃ śrāddham amāvāsyāṃ māsi māsy uḍupakṣaye |
tathāṣṭakāsv apy avaśyam icchākālaṃ nibodha me ||
viśiṣṭabrāhmaṇaprāptau sūryendugrahaṇe ’yane |
viṣuve ravisaṃkrāntau vyatīpāte ca putraka |
śrāddhārhadravyasaṃprāptau tathā duḥsvapnadarśane |
janmarkṣagrahapīḍāsu śrāddhaṃ kurvīta cecchayā ||
viṣṇur apy āha |
saṃkrāntiviṣuve caiva viśeṣeṇāyanadvayaṃ |
vyatīpāto ’tha janmarkṣaṃ candrasūryagrahas tathā ||
ity etān śrāddhakālāṃs tu kāmyān āha prajāpatiḥ |
śrāddham eteṣu yad dattaṃ tad ānantyāya kalpyate ||
[277]         brahmavaivarte |
amāvāsyāṃ prayatnena śrāddhaṃ kuryāc chuciḥ sadā |
sarvān kāmān avāpnoti svargaṃ cānte samaśnute ||
ghṛtaṃ dadyād amāvāsyāṃ somasyāpyāyanaṃ mahat |
evam āpyāyitaḥ somaḥ trīn lokān dhārayiṣyati ||
siddhacāraṇagandharvaiḥ stūyate nityapūjitaḥ |
sarvaiḥ puṣpair manojñaiś ca sarvakāmaparicchadaiḥ ||
nṛtyavāditragītaiḥ syād apsarobhiḥ sahasraśaḥ |
upakrīḍed vimānasthaḥ pitṛbhakto dṛḍhavrataḥ |
stuvanti devagandharvāḥ siddhasaṃghāś ca taṃ sadā ||
pitṛbhaktas tv amāvāsyāṃ sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
pitṛdevān yajan bhaktyā yato ’syām akṣayaṃ phalaṃ ||
tathā |
puṣṭiṃ prajāṃ smṛtiṃ medhāṃ putrān aiśvaryam eva ca |
kurvāṇaḥ paurṇamāsyāṃ tu sarvaṃ saṃpūrṇam aśnute ||
brahmapurāṇe |
nāndīmukhānāṃ pratyabdaṃ kanyārāśigate ravau |
paurṇamāsyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ varāhavacanaṃ yathā ||
bhādrapadamāsāntargate yatra kvacana dine kanyārāśau ravisaṃkramaṇe jāte tanmāsamadhyavartinyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ nāndīmukhānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ kartavyam, tac cāvaśyakaṃ pratyabdam iti vacanāt | nāndīmukhaśabdena cātra trayaḥ prapitāmahāt pare vivakṣitāḥ | yasmād aśminn eva purāṇe asya vacanasyānantaram evoktaṃ |
[278]        ye syuḥ pitāmahād ūrdhvaṃ te syur nīndīmukhās trayaḥ |
prasannamukhasaṃjñās tu maṅgalīyā yatas tu te || iti |
atra pitāmahaśabdena prapitāmaho lakṣaṇīyaḥ | yad uktaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
trayo hy aśrumukhā hy ete pitaraḥ saṃprakīrttitāḥ |
tebhyaḥ pūrve trayo ye tu te tu nāndīmukhāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
asya nāndīmukhavacanasyaitat paurṇamāsīnimittakaśrāddhavidhinaikavākyatāpannatvāt śrāddhāntare nāndīmukhaśabdena prapitāmahāt pare trayo na grāhyāḥ |
athaiteṣāṃ nāndīmukhasaṃjñā kimartha | atha yathā vasvaḥ pitaro jñeyā ityādau vasvādiśabdo vasvādyabhedadṛṣṭividhānārthaḥ, tathā ca vārāhakalpīyapitṛgaṇābheda-prakaraṇoktanāndīmukhākhyapitṛgaṇābhedadṛṣṭividhānārtha ucyate |
tan na | yato ’tra prasannamukha ityādinā nandanamukhayogān nāndīmukhaśabda-niṣpattir abhipreteti darśitaṃ | ato nābhedadṛṣṭyupadeśārthaḥ |
atha yathā kauṇḍapāpīnīye sāmahome ’gnihotram iti paraśabdaḥ paratra prayujyamānaḥ parasārūpyabhāvaṃ gamayan naiyamikāgnihotradharmātideśārthaḥ, tathātra nāndīmukhaśabdo ’pi putrajanmādikālavihita-prasiddhanāndīmukhadharmātideśārtha iti |
