tan na | uktottaratvāt | na hy ayaṃ paraśabdaḥ parasārūpyavivakṣayā prayuktaḥ, kiṃ tu nandanamukhatvayogād iti | ato ’sya praśaṃsaiva prayojanaṃ manyāmahe |
[279]        hārītaḥ |
aṣṭakā māghābhyudayās tīrthayātro ’papattayaḥ |
pitṝṇām atireko ’yaṃ māsi kārye dhruvaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
brahmavaivarta-vāyupurāṇayoḥ |
atha kālaṃ pravakṣyāmi śrāddhakarmaṇi pūjitam |
kāmyanaimittikājasraśrāddhakarmavidhāyibhiḥ ||
putradā dhanamūlaṃ syur aṣṭakās tisra eva ca |
iti kāmyāḥ prakīrṇakālāḥ |
atha vaiśeṣikāḥ śrāddhakālāḥ |
tatra yājñavalkyavacanāpabaddhakāla prakramamanusaradbhirasmābhirvṛddhiśrāddhakālāḥ pūrvam eva nirūpitāḥ | athedānīṃ sāṃvatsarikaśrāddhakālāḥ kathyante | tatra tāvat kṣayāhanirṇayaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ kāryaṃ mātāpitror mṛtāhani |
pitṛvyasyāpy aputrasya bhrātur jyeṣṭhasya caiva hi ||
mātrādīnāṃ śrāddhaṃ kāryam ity anenārthāt putrādīnāṃ kartavyam uktaṃ | etac cādhikāramātropalakṣaṇārthaṃ | bhrātur ity atrāputrasyety anuṣañjanīyaṃ | jyeṣṭhasyety anena kaniṣṭhasya bhrātur nāvaśyakaṃ sāṃvatsarikam iti darśitaṃ | tathā ca |
na putrasya pitā dadyān nānujasya tathāgrajaḥ |
iti niyatādhikāranirākaraṇāt snehādinā tv aniyatenādhikāreṇāputrasya kaniṣṭhasyāpi sāṃvatsarikaṃ kartavyam eva | tathā coktam |
[280]        api snehena kuryāc cet sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vinā || iti |
etac ca prāg evādhikāraprakaraṇe prapañcenoktam | prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
mṛtāhani pitur yas tu na kuryāt śrāddham ādarāt |
mātuś caiva varārohe vatsarānte mṛtāhani |
nāhaṃ tasya mahādevi pūjāṃ gṛhṇāmi no hariḥ ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
sarveṣām eva śrāddhānāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sāṃvatsaraṃ mataṃ |
kriyate yat khagaśreṣṭha mṛte ’hani budhaiḥ sadā ||
mṛte ’hani pitur yas tu na kuryāt śrāddham ādarāt |
mātuś ca khagaśārdūla vatsarānte mṛte ’hani ||
nāhaṃ tasya khagaśreṣṭha pūjāṃ gṛhāmi no hariḥ |
na brahmā na ca vai rudro na cānye devatāgaṇāḥ ||
tasmād yatnena kartavyaṃ varṣe varṣe mṛte ’hani |
nareṇa khagaśārdūla bhojakena viśeṣataḥ ||
bhojako yas tu vai śrāddhaṃ na karoti khagādhipa |
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ satataṃ varṣe varṣe mṛte ’hani ||
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ tāmisraṃ nāma nāmataḥ |
tato bhavati duṣṭātmā nagare śūkaraḥ khagaḥ ||
atra mṛtāhaśabdena maraṇāharvartinī tithir vivakṣitā saṃvatsarānte mukhyamṛtāhasyāsaṃbhavāt | na hi tāny evāhāni parivartante | na hy ahnāṃ prātisvikaṃ nāmāsti cihnaṃ | ato yatra kvacanāhni sāṃvatsarikaṃ [281] prāpnuyāt saṃvatsarāntimāhargrahaṇān neti cet |
na, mṛtāhargrahaṇānarthakyāpatteḥ | ato maraṇāharvartinī tithir vivakṣitā | ahargrahaṇaṃ rātrer apy upalakṣaṇārthaṃ | tena rātrimṛtasyāpi sā tithir labhyate | evaṃ ca saṃvatsarānte mṛtāhasaṃbandhinyāṃ tithau sāṃvatsarikaṃ kartavyam iti labhyate |
atra ca |
yāṃ tithiṃ samanuprāpya udayaṃ yāti bhāskaraḥ |
sā tithiḥ sakalā jñeyā dānādhyayanakarmasu ||
yāṃ tithiṃ samanuprāpya astaṃ yāti divākaraḥ |
sā tithiḥ sakalā jñeyā khānadānajapādiṣu ||
iti devalādivacanānusārād yathā karmakāleṣv avidyamānāyā api titheḥ saṃpūrṇatvakalpanayā grahaṇam, na tathā mṛtāharvartinyās tither maraṇasaṃbandhitayā grahaṇam, kiṃ tu maraṇakālinyā eva | ataḥ sāṃvatsarike mṛtāhaśabdenoktā maraṇakāliky eva tithir veditavyā |
tadāha nāradīyapurāṇe vasiṣṭhaḥ |
pāraṇe maraṇe nṝṇāṃ tithis tātkālikī smṛtā |
pitrye ’stamayavelāyāṃ spṛṣṭā pūrṇā nigadyate ||
'tātkālikī' yasmin kāle upavāsādivratapāraṇaṃ maraṇaṃ vā tatkālavartiny eva tayos tithiḥ | na punar atatkālavartinī | prātarādiṣu trimuhūrtādinā pitrye sāṃvatsarikādāv astamayavelāyām arhante spṛṣṭāpi maraṇakālikī tithiḥ saṃpūrṇā nigadyate | maraṇakālikyāṃ tithau sāṃvatsarike kartavye prāpte yadi sāstamayavelāyām api dinā[282]nte 'sti tadā sā kutapāparāhṇayor astīti matvā tadupalakṣita eva divase kutapādiṣu śrāddhaṃ kartavyam iti |
atha vātra mṛtāhaśabdenopalakṣaṇatrayaviśiṣṭam ahorātram abhipretaṃ |
tad āha vyāsaḥ |
māsapakṣatithispṛṣṭe yo yasmin mriyate ’hani |
prātyabdaṃ tat tathābhūtaṃ kṣayāhaṃ tasya taṃ viduḥ || iti |
tathābhūtam ity upalakṣaṇatrayopetaṃ | atra māsapakṣāhorātrāṇi cāndrāṇy eva grāhyāṇi, na saurasāvanāni | santi ca tāni brahmasiddhānte nirūpitāni |
darśād darśaś cāndras triṃśaddivasas tu sāvano māsaḥ |
ravisaṃkrāntiviśiṣṭaḥ sauro māso nigadyate tajjñaiḥ |
tathā |
cāndraḥ śuklādidarśāntaḥ sāvanas triṃśatā dinaiḥ |
ekarāśau ravir yavatkālaṃ māsaḥ sabhāskaraḥ || iti |
eṣu ca māseṣu madhye sāṃvatsarikādipitṛkārye ca cāndrasyaiva grahaṇam āha vyāsaḥ |
vivāhādau smṛtaḥ sauro yajñādau sāvanaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ābdike pitṛkārye ca māsaś cāndramasaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
laghuhārītaḥ |
cakravat parivarteta sūryaḥ kālavaśād yataḥ |
ataḥ sāṃvatsaraṃ śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ māsacihnitaṃ ||
māsacihnaṃ tu kartavyaṃ pauṣamāghādyam eva hi |
[283]        yatas tatra vidhānena māsaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ ||
pauṣādisaṃjñā cāndrasyaiva grāhyā | tatra yady api parasminn api dine karmakālavyāptisaṃbhavaḥ, tathāpi prathamātikrame kāraṇābhāvāt pūrvasminn evāhni śrāddhaṃ |
āha sumantuḥ |
dviraharvyāpinī cet svāt mṛtāhasya tu yā tithiḥ |
pūrvasyāµ nirvapet piṇḍam ity āṅgirasabhāṣitaṃ ||
vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
devakārye tithir jñeyā yasyām astamito raviḥ |
śivarahasya-saurapurāṇyoḥ |
prāyaḥ prānta upoṣyaḥ syāt tither daivaphalepsubhiḥ |
mūlaṃ hi pitṛtṛptyarthaṃ paitryaṃ ca maharṣibhiḥ ||
'mūlaṃ' ārambhaḥ |
nāradīyapurāṇe |
tithiprāntaṃ surākhyaṃ hi upoṣyaṃ kavayo viduḥ |
paitraṃ mūlaṃ titheḥ prokaṃ śāstrajñaiḥ kālakovidaiḥ ||
nigamaḥ |
pūrvāhṇikās tu tithayo devakārye phalapradāḥ |
aparāhṇikās tathā jñeyā pitrarthe tu śubhāvahāḥ ||
atrāparāhṇaḥ sūkṣmakālaḥ vakṣyamāṇeṣv aparāhṇeṣv anyatamo veditavyaḥ | ataḥ pūrvadinasaṃbandhinī mṛtāhatithiḥ sāṃvatsarike ’bhyupeyā sā ca [284] yady aparāhṇe trimuhūrtā bhavet tadaiva grāhyā | na tu trimuhūrtān nyūnā |tad āha baudhāyanaḥ |
udite daivataṃ bhānau pitryaṃ cāstamite ravau |
dvimuhūrte triahṇaś ca sā tithir havyakavyayoḥ || iti |
asyārthaḥ | bhānāv udite saty uttarakāle ahno muhūrtadvayaṃ 'daivataṃ’ devadevatyaṃ | tasmiṃś cāstam ite tatpūrvakālīnam ahno muhūrtatrayaṃ 'pitryaṃ' pitṛdevatyaṃ | atas tāvatkālavyāpinī yā tithir bhavati saiva kramāt havyakavyayor grāhyeti | tad evam astamayāt pūrvaṃ trimuhūrtā grāhyeti sthite, yad bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
vratopavāsasnānādau ghaṭikaikāpi yā bhavet |
udaye sā tithir grahyā viparītā tu paitṛka iti |s
'viparītā' astamayasaṃbandhinī | yac ca vyāsoktaṃ |
ahno ’stamayavelāyāṃ kalāmātrāpi yā bhavet |
saiva pratyābdike grāhyā itarā putrahānidā || iti |
yac ca vasiṣṭhoktaṃ |
pitrye ’stamayavelāyāṃ spṛṣṭā pūrvā nigadyate | iti |
tad etat pūrvadināparāhṇatrimuhūrtavyāpi, kiṃ punar nyāyena praśaṃsārtham | na tu ghaṭikādimātrakālīnāyāḥ parigrahārtham, tathāvidhāyāḥ karmāparyāptatvāt | uttaradine karmaparyāptāparāhṇe vyāptilābhāc ca | ata eva hārītaḥ | tathāhi |
puṣyeṇa yuktaś candro yasyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ sā pauṣī | sā yasmin māse [285] sa pauṣamāsaḥ | evaṃ māghādayo ’pi | tad āha pāṇiniḥ | nakṣatreṇa yuktaḥ kālaḥ | sāsmin paurṇamāsīti saṃjñāyām iti | na ca saurasāvanayoḥ evaṃvidhāsti saṃjñā, evaṃvidhapaurṇamāsīyoganiyamo vā ataḥ sāṃvatsarikādau cāndra eva māso grāhyaḥ |
yat punar vacanaṃ |
yasmin rāśau gate sūrye vipattiṃ yāti mānavaḥ |
tadrāśāv eva kartavyaṃ pitṛkāryaṃ mṛtāhani ||
tad etad adhimāsaviṣayaṃ |
adhimāse vipattiś cet sauraṃ mānaṃ samāśrayet |
sa eva divasas tasya śrāddhaṃ piṇḍodakādiṣu ||
vyākhyāto māsaḥ | asyārddhaṃ pakṣaḥ | tithis tu cāndram ahorātraṃ | tad uktaṃ brahmasiddhānte |
sāvanaṃ syād ahorātram udayād udayo raveḥ |
raves triṃśat tu rāśyaṃśas tithisaṃbhoga aindavaḥ ||
sūryodayāt sūryodayāntaraparyantaṃ sāvanam ahorātraṃ | meṣādisaṃjñakarāśitriṃśāṃśabhogo raveḥ rāśyaṃśaḥ saurasaṃjñaka ity arthaḥ | tithisambhoga iti | yāvatā kālena rāśer dvādaśatriṃśāśāś candramasā bhujyante tāvatkālas tithisaṃbhogaḥ, tac cāndram ahorātraṃ, tad etat pratipadādisaṃjñābhir vyavahriyate | evaṃ māsopalakṣitamāsaviśeṣaṇakṣayopetaṃ sāvanam ahorātraṃ mṛtāhaśabdenoktaṃ veditavyaṃ |
iti kṣayāhanirṇayaḥ |
[286]
atha kṣayāhadvaidhanirṇayaḥ |
tatra kṣayāhaśrāddhatithidvaidhe karmakālavyāpinī grāhyā | yad āha vyāsaḥ |
karmaṇo yasya yaḥ kālas tatkālavyāpinī tithiḥ |
tayā karmāṇi kurvīta hrāsavṛddhau na kāraṇaṃ ||
yadā tu tithisāmye dvayor api tithyor aparāhṇasaṃbandhas tadānīṃ pārvaṇetikartavyatāke sāṃvatsarike pūrvaiva tithiḥ |
aparāhṇe pitṝṇāṃ tu yā parāhṇānuyāyinī |
sā grāhyā pitṛkārye tu na pūrvāhṇānuyāyinī ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe ’pi |
vratopavāsaniyame ghaṭikaikā yadā bhavet |
sā tithiḥ sakalā jñeyā pitrarthe cāparāhṇikī || iti |
astādiśabdāś cāparāhṇaparā eva | tathā ca vṛddhamanuḥ |
yasyām astaṃ ravir yāti pitaras tām upāsate |
tithis tebhyo yato dattā hy aparāhṇe svayaṃbhuvā ||
manur api |
yasyām astaṃ ravir yāti pitaras tām upāsate |
sā pitṛbhyo yato dattā hy aparāhṇe svayaṃbhuvā ||
ata eva śivarahasya-brahmavaivarta-saura-nāradīyapurāṇeṣu |
darśe ca paurṇamāse ca pituḥ sāṃvatsaraṃ dinam |
pūrvaviddham akurvāṇo narakaṃ pratipadyate || iti |
[287]        yat tu yugmāgniyugabhūtānām ityādi nigamavākyaṃ, tad vratopavāsādiviṣayaṃ | tad āha vyāsaḥ |
dvitīyādiṣu yugmānāṃ pūjyatā niyamādiṣu |
ekoddiṣṭādivṛddhyādau vṛddhihrāsādicodanāt || iti |
ekoddiṣṭetyādi paitṛkaśrāddhopalakṣakaṃ | tad evaṃ tithisāmye pūrvā grāhyeti siddhaṃ | vaiṣamye tu āha baudhāyanaḥ |
sā tithis tad ahorātraṃ yasyām abhyudiyād raviḥ |
varddhamānasya pakṣasya hrāse tv astam iyād raviḥ ||
ckṣayāham eva viṣayīkṛtya sa evāha |
aparāhṇadvayavyāpinyatītasya yadā tithiḥ |
kṣaye pūrvā tu kartavyā vṛddhikārye tathottarā ||
yat tu vyāghreṇa tithisāmye ’pi paratithigrahaṇam uktaṃ |
kharvo darpas tathā hiṃsā trividhaṃ tithilakṣaṇaṃ |
kharvadarpau parau pūjyau hiṃsā syāt pūrvakālikī || iti ||
'kharvaḥ' sāmyaṃ | 'darpaḥ' vṛddhiḥ | 'hiṃsā' kṣayaḥ | tad etat sāmyaśāstraṃ kṣayāhavyatiriktatithiviṣayam āstāṃ | yac ca,
śuklapace tithir grāhyā yasyām abhyudito raviḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣe tithir grahyā yasyām astam ito raviḥ ||
iti mārkaṇḍeyavacanaṃ, tat tu śuklapakṣaśabdayor daivapitryakarmopalakṣakatvāt devapitryakarma[288]kālavibhāgamātraparatvena vyavasthāpanīyaṃ | tad evaṃ pārvaṇaikasaṃbandho ’parāhṇasparśaparādhīnanirṇayaḥ | pārvaṇetikartavyatākasya sāṃvatsarikasyāyam aṅgabhūtaḥ kālo vivecitaḥ | ekoddiṣṭasya mādhyāhnikatvāt pūrvottarayor madhyāhnasparśapuraskāreṇaiva ekoddiṣṭavidhiṃ nirvartya sāṃvatsarikaśrāddhāṅgakālanirṇayo vidheyaḥ | tatra manuḥ |
pūrvāhṇe daivikaṃ śrāddham aparāhṇe tu pārvaṇam |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu madhyāhne prātar vṛddhinimittakam ||
hārīto ’pi |
āmaśrāddhaṃ tu pūrvāhṇe ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu madhyataḥ |
pārvaṇaṃ cāparāhṇe tu prātar vṛddhinimittakaṃ ||
vṛddhagautamaḥ |
madhyāhnavyāpinī yā syāt saikoddiṣṭe tithir bhavet |
aparāhṇavyāpinī yā pārvaṇe sā tithir bhavet ||
madhyāhnavyāpinī yā tu tithiḥ pūrvā parāpi vā |
tasyāṃ pitṛbhyo dātavyaṃ hrāsavṛddhī na kāraṇaṃ ||
iti kṣayāhadvaidhanirṇayaḥ |
atha kṣayāhāparijñāne sāṃvatsarikaśrāddhakālāḥ |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
aruṇa uvāca |
na jānāti dinaṃ yas tu māsaṃ vāpy amarādhipa |
mṛtayos tu mahāprājña pitros tu sa kathaṃ naraḥ |
[289]        śrāddhaṃ karotu vai tābhyāṃ vidhivat vatsarātmakam ||
atra pitror ityupalakṣaṇam |
āditya uvāca |
mṛtāhaṃ yo na jānāti mānako vinatātmaja |
tena kāryam amāvāsyāṃ śrāddhaṃ sāmvatsaraṃ nṛpa ||
yasya proṣitasyāproṣitasya vā mṛtasya tithir na jñāyate *tuṣya (?) tanmāsāmāvāsyāyāṃ sāṃvatsarikaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
na jñāyate mṛtāhaś cet pramīte proṣite sati |
māsaś cet prativijñātas taddarśe syān mṛtāhani ||
proṣite ity ajñānakāraṇopalakṣaṇam | 'darśe' tanmāsāmāvāsyāyāṃ |
'mṛtāhani' tatra kartavyaṃ tad iti śeṣaḥ
asminn eva viṣaye ekādaśyāṃ śrāddham āha marīciḥ |
śrāddhavighne samutpanne avijñāte mṛtāhani |
ekādaśyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ ||
atra viśeṣata iti kṛṣṇapakṣasya praśastataratvābhidhānena śuklapakṣo ’py anujñāyata iti gamyate | tataś ca śuklakṛṣṇaikādaśyor vikalpe prāpte evaṃ vyavasthā | māse pakṣe ca vijñāte tithimātrasyāparijñāne tasya māsasya tatpakṣasthitāyām ekādaśyāṃ śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ, vijñāyamānapakṣaparityāge kāraṇābhāvāt | pakṣasyāpy aparijñāne kṛṣṇaikādaśyāṃ tadalābhe śuklaikādaśyām apīti |
[290]        bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
dinam eva vijānāti māsaṃ naiva tu yo naraḥ |
mārgaśīrṣe ’tha vā bhādre māghe vātha samācaret ||
yasya tu mṛtāhaḥ parijñāyate māsas tu na jñāyate tasya mārgaśīrṣe bhādrapade māghe vā tasminn ahani śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
yadā māso na vijñāto vijñātaṃ dinam eva tu |
tadā mārgaśire māsi māghe vā tad dinaṃ bhavet ||
yadā tu proṣitamṛtasya dinamāsāv ubhāv apy avijñātau syātāṃ tadā tatprasthānakālikayor dinamāsayos tatsāṃvatsarikaṃ kāryam | tad āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dinamāsau na vijñātau maraṇasya yadā punaḥ |
prasthānadinamāsau tu grāhyau pūrvoktayā diśā ||
ity asyāyam arthaḥ | prasthānatithyajñāne tanmāsajñāne ca tanmāsavartinyām amāvāsyāyāṃ sāṃvatsarikaṃ, prasthānatithijñāne māsājñāne ca mārgaśīrṣe māghe vā tasyām eva tithau kuryāt | prasthānatithimāsayor ubhayor apy ajñāne tu tanmaraṇaśravaṇakālikayos tithimāsayoḥ kuryāt | tad āha pracetāḥ "avijñātasmṛte amāvāsyāyāṃ śravaṇadivase vā" | 'śravaṇadivase' maraṇavārtakarṇanatithau | bhaviṣyottare ’pi |
[291]        mṛtavārtāśrutau grāhyau tau pūrvoktakrameṇa tu |
śravaṇatithismaraṇe ca mārgaśīrṣe māghe vā tasyām eva tithau kāryam | śravaṇatithimāsayor ubhayor api vismaraṇe māghasya mārgaśīrṣasya vā amāvāsyāyāṃ kāryaṃ | tad uktaṃ prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
mṛtasyāho na jānāti māsaṃ vāpi kathaṃcana |
tena kāryam amāvāsyāṃ śrāddhaṃ māghe ’tha mārgake ||
‘kathaṃcana' maraṇaprasthānaśravaṇarūpeṇa kena kenāpy upalakṣaṇenety arthaḥ |
bhaviṣyottare |
pravāsam antareṇāpi syātāṃ tau vismṛtau yadā |
tadānīm api tau grāhyau pūrvavan mṛtakādike ||
yasya tu proṣitasya maraṇvārtā tajjīvanavārtāpi nāgatā pañcadaśavarṣaparyantam āgamanaṃ nāsti tasya tadūrdhvaṃ pālāśībhiḥ samidbhiḥ kuśair vā śarīrapratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi dāhasaṃskāraṃ nirvartya tadānīṃtanapakṣamāsatithispṛṣṭe ’hani sāṃvatsarikaṃ kartavyam ity āha jātūkarṇaḥ |
pitari proṣite yasya na vārtā naiva cāgatiḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pañcadaśād varṣāt kṛtvā tu pratirūpakam ||
kuryāt tasya ca saṃskāraṃ yathoktavidhinā tataḥ |
tadādīny eva sarvāṇi śeṣakarmāṇi saṃcaret ||
etac ca proṣitamṛtaśrāddhaprayogaprakaraṇe vistareṇa darśayiṣyate |
iti kṣayāhāparijñāne sāṃvatsarikadinakālaḥ |
iti sāṃvatsarikakālanirūpaṇaṃ |
[292]
atha pretaśrāddhānāṃ kālāḥ |
tatra tāvan navasaṃjñakānāṃ śrāddhānām, tatra cāntardaśāhaṃ kriyamāṇānāṃ navaśrāddhasaṃjñāṃ niveśayann āśvalāyano daśāhaparyantaṃ navaśrāddhasaṃjñakatvam āha | nāgarakhaṇḍe bhartṛyajñaḥ |
trīṇi saṃcayanasyārthe tāni vai śṛṇu sāṃprataṃ |
yatra sthāne bhaven mṛtyus tatra śrāddhaṃ prakalpayet ||
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tato mārge viśrāmo yatra kāritaḥ |
tataḥ saṃcayanasthāne tṛtīyaṃ śrāddham iṣyate ||
pañcame saptame tadvad aṣṭame navame tathā |
daśamaikādaśe caiva navaśrāddhāni tāni ca |
vaitaraṇyām asaṃprāptaḥ pretas tṛptim avāpnuyāt ||
brahmapurāṇe |
tṛtīye ’hani kartavyaṃ pretadāhāvanau dvijāḥ |
sūtakānte gṛhaśrāddham ekoddiṣṭaṃ pracakṣate ||
āha kātyāyanaḥ |
caturthe pañcame caiva navamaikādaśe tathā |
yad atra dīyate jantos tan navaśrāddham ucyate ||
vyāsaḥ |
prathame saptame caiva navamaikādaśe tathā |
yat tu vai dīyate jantos tan navaśrāddham ucyate ||
āha śaṅkhaḥ |
ādyaṃ śrāddham aśudro ’pi kuryād ekādaśe ’hani |
[293]        kartus tātkālikī śuddhir aśuddhaḥ punar eva saḥ ||
'adyaṃ' navaṃ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
sadyaḥśauce pradātavyaṃ pretasyaikādaśe ’hani |
sa eva divasas tasya śrāddhaśayyāsanādiṣu ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
pretāya ca gṛhadvāri prathame bhojayed dvijān |
dvitīye ’hani kartavyaṃ khurakarma sabāndhavaiḥ ||
caturthe bāndhavaiḥ sarvair asthnāṃ saṃcayanaṃ bhavet |
pūrvān viprān prayuñjīta yugmāṃs tu śraddhayā śucīn ||
pañcame navame caiva tathaivaikādaśe ’hani |
yugmāṃś ca bhojayed viprān navaśrāddhaṃ tu tad viduḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
caturthe brāhmaṇānāṃ ca pañcame ’hani bhūbhṛtām |
navame vaiśyajātīnāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ daśamāt pare ||
atriḥ |
pretārthaṃ sūtikānte tu brāhmaṇān bhojayet tataḥ |
atra śrāddhanimittaṃ tu ekam ekādaśe ’hani |
evaṃ saty ekādaśāhikān navaśrāddhād aśaucāntavidhīyamānaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanaṃ karmāntaram iti gamyate |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
caturthe ’hani viprabhyo deyam annaṃ hi bāndhavaiḥ |
[294]        gāvaḥ suvarṇaṃ vittaṃ ca pretam uddiśya śaktitaḥ ||
'caturthe' saṃcayāhanīty arthaḥ |
yad iṣṭaṃ jīvataś cāsīt tad dadyāt tasya yatnataḥ |
tasya pretasya jīvato yat kiṃcid abhīṣṭam āsīt tad dadyād ity arthaḥ |
iti navaśrāddhakālāḥ |
atha ṣoḍaśaśrāddhānīty evaṃ prasiddhānāṃ pretaśrāddhānāṃ kālāḥ |
tatra brahmapurāṇe |
pitṝṇāṃ tyaktadehānāṃ śrāddhaṣoḍaśa sarvadā |
caturthe pañcame caiva navamaikādaśe tathā ||
tato dvādaśabhir māsaiḥ śrāddhā dvādaśasaṃkhyayā |
kartavyāḥ śrutitas teṣāṃ tatra viprāṃś ca tarpayet ||
jātūkarṇaḥ |
dvādaśa pratimāsyāni ādyaṃ ṣāṇmāsike tathā |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ caiva ity etac chrāddhaṣoḍaśam ||
ṣāṇmāsike ity etena vihitayoḥ śrāddhayor nirūpaṇārtham āha |
ekāhena tu ṣaṇmāsā yadā syur api vā tribhiḥ |
nyūnāḥ saṃvatsarāś caiva syātāṃ ṣāṇmāsike tadā ||
ekenāhnā tribhir vā yadā ṣaṇmāsā: nyūnāḥ syuḥ saṃvatsarāś ca tadā ṣaṇmāsābdike kārye ity arthaḥ |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
asthisaṃcayane śrāddhaṃ tripakṣe māsikāni ca |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
[295]        ṣāṇmāsikābdike śrāddhe syātāṃ pūrvedyur eva te |
māsikāni svakīye tu divase dvādaśe ’pi ca ||
tathā |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād arvāk kuryāc chrāddhāni ṣoḍaśa |
ekoddiṣṭavidhānena kuryāt sarvāṇi tāni tu ||
brahmapurāṇe |
dvādaśe ’hani māse ca tripakṣe ca tataḥ paraṃ |
māsi māsi tu kartavyaṃ yāvad āvatsaraṃ dvijāḥ |
tataḥ parataraṃ kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kramāt ||
kṛte sapiṇḍīkaraṇe caramaṃ procyate punaḥ |
tataḥ prabhṛti nirmuktaḥ pretatvāt pitṛtāṃ gataḥ ||
vyāghrapādaḥ |
ekādaśe caturthe ca māsi māsi ca vatsaraṃ |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ caivam ekoddiṣṭaṃ mṛtāhani ||
agnimatpretaśrāddhe viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
śrāddham agnimataḥ kāryaṃ dāhād ekādaśe ’hani |
dhruvāṇi tu prakurvīta pramītāhani sarvadā ||
traipakṣikād ūrddhvāni dhruvāṇi | ata evāha jātūkarṇaḥ |
ūrddhvaṃ tripakṣāt yac chrāddhaṃ mṛtāhany eva tad bhavet |
adhas tu kārayed dāham āhitāgner dvijanmanaḥ || iti |
hārītas tv asamarthaviṣaye māsikānāṃ kālavikalpān āha |
[296]        mukhyaṃ śrāddhaṃ māsi māsi aparyātāv ṛtuṃ prati |
dvādaśāhena vā kuryād ekāhe dvādaśātha vā || iti |
tatroparitane prakaraṇe vṛddhāv upasthitāyāṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇāpakarṣaṇaṃ vakṣyate | tannibandhanaṃ māsikānām apakarṣam apunarāvṛttiṃ ca tatraiva vakṣyāmaḥ |
iti ṣoḍaśaśrāddhānīty evaṃprasiddhaśrāddhānāṃ kālaḥ |
atha sapiṇḍīkaraṇakālaḥ |
tatra yathākālaṃ dvādaśasu māmikeṣv anuṣṭhiteṣu pūrṇe saṃvatsare tadūrdhvamāt sargiko ’nagnisapiṇḍīkaraṇasya kālaḥ | tathā ca mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe māsikāntāni śrāddhāny uktvābhihitaṃ |
atha saṃvatsare pūrṇe yathāvat kriyate naraiḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ samyak tatrāpi vidhir ucyate ||
asya ca kālasyānagnimatkartṛkānagnimaddaivatyasapiṇḍīkaraṇāṅgatvaṃ naimittikaṃ ca kālāntaram uktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād yajamāno hy anagnimān |
anāhitāgneḥ pretasya pūrvedyur bharatarṣabha ||
uktaṃ ca pulastyena |
niragniḥ sahapiṇḍatvaṃ pitur mātuś ca dharmataḥ |
pūrṇe saṃvatsare kuryād vṛddhir vā yadi vā bhavet ||
vṛddhiś cauḍamauñjīvivāhādirūpo ’bhyudayaḥ upalakṣaṇaṃ caitan nāndīśrāddhanimittabhūtasya karmamātrasya | etac [297] "cādhānādyupasaṃprāptāv" ity uśanovacane “kartuṃ nāndīmukhaṃ” iti śāṭyāyanivacane ca sphuṭībhavati | saiṣā yasmin dine vṛddhir āpadyate tataḥ prāg evāpakṛṣya sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā tadaṅgabhūtaṃ nāndīmukhasaṃjñakaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | anena pravṛttādhikārasyāparisamāpita-pretaśrāddhasya cauḍamauñjīvivāhādhānādau tadaṅgabhūte ca nāndīśrāddhe ’nadhikāro ’pi darśitaḥ | āha laghuhārītaḥ |
bhrātā vā bhrātṛputro vā sapiṇḍaḥ śiṣya eva ca |
sahapiṇḍakriyāṃ kuryāt kuryād ābhyudayaṃ tataḥ ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
tataḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vatsarād ūrdhvataḥ sthitam |
vṛddhir vā gāminī cet syāt tadavāg api kārayet ||
ādhānāṅgabhūtān nāndīśrāddhāt prāg apakarṣam abhipretyāhośanāḥ |
pituḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ vārṣikaṃ mṛtavatsare |
ādhānādyupasaṃprāptāv etat prāg api vatsarāt ||
atra cāpakṛṣyamāṇasapiṇḍīkaraṇe tadantam apakarṣe syād iti nyāyāt pūrvāṇi māsikāny apakṛṣya sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam | vṛddhavasiṣṭho ’py āha |
śrāddhāni ṣoḍaśā datvā na tu kuryāt sapiṇḍanam |
taddhānau tu yataḥ pretaḥ pitṛtvaṃ na prapadyate ||
apakarṣapakṣa evaitat | anapakarṣe kramavidhānād eva tatsiddheḥ | ata eva śāṭyāyaniḥ |
pretaśrāddhāni sarvāṇi sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ tathā |
[298]        apakṛṣya prakurvīta kartuṃ nāndīmukhaṃ dvijaḥ ||
yat tu laugākṣivacanam,
yasya saṃvatsarād arvāk sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ bhavet |
māsikaṃ codakumbhaṃ ca deyaṃ tasyāpi vatsaram || iti |
tat tu tadantāpakarṣanyāyena kṛteṣv api māsikeṣu “tāny eva tu punaḥ kuryāt” iti kalpasūtrakāravacanoktapunaranuṣṭhānaviṣayatvena vyākhyeyam | apakṛṣyety anuvṛttāv āhośanāḥ |
vṛddhiśrāddhavihīnas tu pretaśrāddhāni yaś caret |
sa śrāddhī narake ghore pitṛbhiḥ saha pacyate ||
anupasthitāyāṃ vṛddhau yaḥ pretaśrāddhāny apakarṣed ity arthād vṛddhim antareṇānapakarṣo ’yaṃ samarthaviṣayaḥ | asamarthaviṣaye tu vṛddhiśrāddhapratyupasthānaṃ vināpy apakṛṣyamāṇasya ca kālavikalpān āha gobhilaḥ | “pūrṇe saṃvatsare ṣaṇmāse tripakṣe vā yad ahar vā vṛddhir āpadyeta”|
atra kecit pūrṇe saṃvatsare yad ahar vā vṛddhir āpadyetety etayoḥ pakṣayor anagnikaikaviṣayatvaṃ | ṣaṇmāse tripakṣe vety etayos tu sāgnikaikaviṣayatvam iti vyavasthāṃ (?) manyante | udbhāvayanti cātra paiṭhīnasivacanam |
tripakṣe ’gnimataḥ kālo vṛddhīṣṭāpūrtakarmasu |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt pūrṇe saṃvatsare ’pi vā || iti |
agnimatsapiṇḍīkaraṇakālaprakaraṇe vakṣyamāṇāni vacanāny upanyasyanti | atredaṃ vaktavyam | yady agnimattvaviśeṣaṇavatas tripakṣādipakṣāḥ santi, santu te ’sya saṃvatsarānte kartuṃ samarthasyāpi mukhyakalpāḥ | anagnikatvasāmarthyaprayuktāḥ prakṛte gobhilavacanoktasaṃvatsarapakṣeṇaikavākyatāṃ gatāḥ santaḥ santv anukalpāḥ | vyāghras tv agnirahitānām api saha ṣoḍaśabhiḥ śrāddhaiḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇasya dvādaśāha eva praśastaḥ kāla ity āha |
ānantyāt kuladharmāṇāṃ puṃsāṃ caivāyuṣaḥ kṣayāt |
asthiteś ca śarīrasya dvādaśāhaḥ praśasyate ||
ity ananikasapiṇḍīkaraṇakālāḥ |
atha sāgnikasapiṇḍīkaraṇakālaḥ |
tatrāha laghuhārītaḥ |
anagnis tu yadā vīra bhavet kuryāt tadā gṛhī |
pretaś ced agnimān yas tu tripakṣe vai sapiṇḍanam ||
atra gṛhī śrāddhakartā yady āhavanīyarahitaḥ pretaś cāhavanīyavān bhavet (?) tadā sa kartā tasya pretasya mṛtāhadivasād ārabhya pakṣatraye pūrṇe sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt |
tathā |
yajamāno ’gnimān rājan pretaś cānagnimān bhavet |
dvādaśāhe bhavet kāryaṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ sutaiḥ ||
atra mṛtāhād ārabhya dvādaśe dine iti veditavyam | āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ekādaśāhaṃ nirvartya pūrṇaṃ darśād yathāvidhi |
prāk kurvītāgnimān vipro mātāpitroḥ sapiṇḍatām ||
asyābhiprāyaḥ | agnimatā hy amāvāsyāyāṃ piṇḍapitṛyajñaḥ piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ kartavyam | tac ca pramītamātāpitṛkasya sapatnīkapitrādidaivata (?) | na cākṛtasapiṇḍīkaraṇayos tayor darśaśrāddhādau devatātvam upapadyate | tasya [300] sapiṇḍīkaraṇottarakālikatvasmaraṇāt | ataḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñaśrāddhayor lopo mā bhūd iti matvā agnimān vipra ekādaśāhakartavyaṃ pretakāyaṃ nirvartya darśāt prāg eva yasmin kasminn apy ahani mātāpitroḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād iti | vipragrahaṇam upalakṣaṇam, kṣatriyādiṣv api nyāyasya tulyatvāt |
pakṣāntaram āha hārītaḥ "yā tu pūrvam amāvāsyā tasyām" iti |
ata evāha jābālaḥ |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt pūrvaṃ darśe ’gnimān sutaḥ |
parato daśarātrasya pūrṇatve tu tathāparaḥ ||
mṛtāham ārabhya yad daśarātraṃ tasmāt parato yaḥ prathamo darśe bhavet tasminn agnimān sutaḥ pitroḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | daśarātragrahaṇam aśaucakālopalakṣaṇārtham | tad evaṃ sāgnikasya trayaḥ sapiṇḍīkaraṇakālā uktā bhavanti | “dvādaśāhaḥ, daśāhāt parakālīnāyā amāvāsyāyāḥ pūrvaṃ kim apy ahaḥ, daśāhāt parāmāvāsyā ceti” ||
iti sāgnikasapiṇḍīkaraṇakālāḥ |
iti traivarṇikāṇām ||
atha śūdrasya sapiṇḍīkaraṇakālāḥ |
tatra sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ |
mantravarṇaṃ hi śūdrāṇāṃ dvādaśe ’hani kīrtitam || iti |
amāvāsyāśrāddhakāriśūdraviṣayam etad iti vṛddhāḥ | vacanabalād aśaucamadhya eva śrāddhakaraṇaṃ |
iti śūdrasya sapiṇḍīkaraṇakālāḥ |
iti sapiṇḍīkaraṇakālāḥ |
[301]
tad evam ete sapiṇḍānvāhāryakasādhāraṇasaṃjñakobhayavidhapārvaṇavṛddhikarmāṅga-kāmyasāṃvatsarikaśrāddhānāṃ navaśrāddhaṣoḍaśaśrāddhānīty evaṃ prasiddhānāṃ sapiṇḍīkaraṇasya cāsmin prakaraṇe kālāḥ pratipāditāḥ |
athānyeṣāṃ śrāddhakāleṣu vaktavyeṣv idam ucyate | tīrthaśrāddhaśuddhiśrāddha-
daivikaśrāddhayātrāśrāddhajīvacchrāddhagoṣṭhīśrāddhānāṃ tv adhikāravākyopadiṣṭā eva kālā iti | te viśeṣādhikāraprakaraṇa eva nirūpitāḥ | tatra ca akāle kāle vā tīrthaprāptis tadanantaram eva tīrthaśrāddhakālaḥ,
akāle ’py atha kāle vā tīrthe śrāddhaṃ yathā naraiḥ |
iti matsyapurāṇasmaraṇāt | pāpakṣayārthakṛc chuddhivratasamāptau śuddhiśrāddhakālaḥ,
śrāddhaṃ kuryād vratānte tu gohiraṇyānnadakṣiṇāḥ |
iti jābālavacanāt | prīṇanīyatattaddevatādhiṣṭhitaṃ tithivārādikaṃ daivikaśrāddhasya kālaḥ |
tan nityaśrāddhavac chrāddhaṃ darśādiṣu ca yatnataḥ |
iti bhaviṣyatpurāṇadarśanāt |
tīrthayātrādināt pūrvadinam, samāpitatīrthayātrasya gṛhapraveśānantaradinaṃ ca yātrāśrāddhasya kālaḥ | "gacched deśāntaram" ityādibhaviṣyatpurāṇasya "yaḥ kaś cit tīrthayātrām" ityādibrahmapurāṇa-agnipurāṇayoś ca śravaṇāt | ariṭadarśanānumitamaraṇakālasya mumukṣor uttarakālaḥ jīvacchrāddhakālaḥ | “jīvacchrāddhaṃ prakartavyaṃ” ityādibhaviṣyatpurāṇādipratipādanāt | kasyacid brahmaṇaḥ samāgamasamūhasamaya eva goṣṭhīśrāddhakālaḥ, "goṣṭhayāṃ yat kriyate śrāddham" ityādibhaviṣyatpurāṇokteḥ | saṇnyāsāṅgānāṃ ṣoḍaśaśrāddhānāṃ daivādiśrāddhāṣṭakadināt prācīnadinam eva kālaḥ, "daivādīnāṃ tu saṃnyāsād" ityādismaraṇāt |
iti devatāprakaraṇaṃ nirūpitaṃ |
athātaḥ saṃnyāsavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
"pūrvedyur nāndīmukhaṃ śrāddham" ityādinā śaunakavacanena durmaraṇamṛtānāṃ pātakaviśeṣavatāṃ mṛtāham ārabhya saṃvatsare pūrṇe yā kācic chuklaikādaśī sa eva nārāyaṇabalisaṃjñakasya śrāddhasya kālaḥ |
tathā ca bhaviṣyottare durmṛtyumṛtān pāpaviśeṣakāriṇaś cānukramyokaṃ |
pūrṇe saṃvatsare teṣām atha kāryaṃ dayālubhiḥ |
ekādaśīṃ samāsādya śuklapakṣasya vai tithim ||
ityādi |
iti tīrthaśrāddhādikālāḥ |
iti vaiśeṣikaśrāddhakālāḥ |
atha mukhyakālātikramakālāḥ |
te ca gauṇā vācanikānukalpikāś ceti dviprakārāḥ | tatredaṃ tāvad vicāryate | mukhyakālāsaṃbhave gauṇakālagrahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ na veti | nanu ko mukhyaḥ ko vā gauṇaḥ | yo vihitaḥ sa mukhyaḥ | yathā vṛddhiśrāddhādau prātarādiḥ | yaś cāvihitaḥ tatsamīpavartī teṣv evāṅgabhāvaṃ nīyamānaḥ saṅgavādir gauṇaḥ | tad āha trikāṇḍamaṇḍanaḥ |
svakālād uttaro gauṇaḥ kālaḥ pūrvasya karmaṇaḥ |
yad vāgāmikriyā mukhyakālasyāpy antarālavat |
gauṇakālatvam icchanti kecit prāktanakarmaṇi || iti ||
asyārthaḥ | pūrvottarayoḥ karmaṇor yaḥ svakālas tasmād uparitanaḥ | uttarakarmasvakālāc ca pūrvabhāvī pūrvaparayoḥ karmaṇor antarālakāla [303] iti yāvat | sa pūrvasmin karmaṇi gauṇo jñeyaḥ | yad vā yathoktāntarālavad āgāmikriyāmukhyakālasyāpi pūrvasmin karmaṇi gauṇakālatāṃ manyanta iti | kathaṃ punar uttarakarmakālasya pūrvakarmānuṣṭhānāgrahaṇam āśaṅkyeta yena svakālāt pracyutaṃ pūrvaṃ karma tāvad vinaṣṭaprāyaṃ uttaraṃ ca svakālāpracyutatvād avinaṣṭarūpaṃ | tatra yady uttarakarmakāle pūrvaṃ kriyate tadā vinaṣṭasamādhānārtham avinaṣṭavināśanaṃ prasajyeta | tan mṛtajīvanāśayā jīvanamaraṇasyopakramaḥ | uttarakarmakāle dvayor avakāśe satīdam ucyate na punar asaty api tasminn iti |
nanu mukhyakālasamīpavartinaḥ kālasyābhidheyāvinābhūtapravṛttilabhyatvāl lākṣikam evocitaṃ |
satyam eva | tathāpi tatra bālātapakālopalakṣaṇībhūtayātapamārdavādigulopanītayā laukikyā prātarādiśabdapravṛttyā tasya gauṇatvoktir na virudhyate |
nanu tasyānupadeśāt kathaṃ grāhyatvam āśaṅkyate |
pratinidhinyāyeneti brūmaḥ |
nanv evaṃ tarhi yathākathaṃcit sādṛśyān madhyāhādāv api kadācit prātarādiśabdāḥ prasajyeran |
kim atrāniṣṭaṃ, kriyata eva hi kadācid atyantasādṛśyavataḥ pracuragauṇaprayogasaṅginaḥ saṅgavāder alābhe | tad evam asmin saṃśaye kvacit pūrvāhṇe daivikaṃ śrāddham ityādikālasaṃyukte vākaye kālaṃ prati karmaṇo guṇabhāvena vidhānaṃ matvā pradhānabhūtavihitakālātikrame guṇabhūtakarmānugrahāya kālāntarānuṣṭhānaṃ na kāryam iti pūrvapakṣam abhipretya siddhāntitavān | kāle hi karma codyate na karmaṇi kālaḥ | ataḥ karmaṇām eva prādhānyaṃ kālam anūdya karmaṇo vidhānāt | tataḥ karma prati guṇabhūtavihitakālātikrame pradhānabhūtakarmānugrahārthaṃ gauṇakāle ’py anuṣṭhānaṃ kāryam | kālahāneḥ karmaṇo ’pi tyāgasyānyāya[304]tvāt | na hy ekavyañjanavināśe bhojanam eva saṃtyajyata iti |
tad ayuktaṃ | na tāvat karmaguṇībhāvenātra pūrvapakṣo ghaṭate, karmaṇaḥ phalasaṃbandhāt | phalavatsaṃnidhāv aphalaṃ tadaṅgam iti nyāyena kālasyaivāṅgatvāt | na ca karmaṇaḥ kālārthatayā parārthatvāt phalaśrutir arthatvānuvāda iti vācyam, kālasya phalavatkarmārthatvāvagamābhāvāt | nāpi kālabhāvasya guṇībhāvenoktaḥ siddhānto ghaṭate, asiddhahetutvāt sopanyāsāt | na hy atra kālānuvādena karma vidhīyate, kālasyānyataḥ prāptivirahāt |
nanu kālasya vākyāntareṇa prāptyabhāve ’māvāsyādirūpeṇa lokavyavahārāya devajñātasyānuvādo bhaviṣyati |
maivaṃ | lokasiddhakālasvarūpānuvādena karmavidhau tasya svayam eva phalatayā phalavatkarmāṅgatvābhāvena ca vidher ānarthakyaprasaṅgaḥ syāt | kiṃ cāsmin karmaṇy uddeśyasya kālasya na kamāntarasaṃbandho ghaṭate | tasyaitatkarmānuṣṭhānānantaram eva vivakṣitatvāt |
nanv evaṃ tarhi "yajeta svargakāma" ity atrāpi svargakāminam anūdya yāgavidher avadhāraṇāt kathaṃ na vidher ānarthakyaṃ |
ucyate | yady api svargakāmasyoddeśyatayā vākyānvayas tathāpi bhāvanāyāḥ prathamaṃ bhāvyasyāpekṣaṇatvāt puruṣasyaiva siddharūpatayā tadanarhatvāt svargakāmavat kāmanāviṣayabhūtaḥ svarga eva sādhyatvena samarthyate | svargakāmas tu pūrvasiddha evānūdyate | yathā lohitoṣṇīśam ṛtvijaṃ pracarantītyādau viśeṣyāṇām ṛtvijāṃ prāptatvād aprāptaviśeṣaṇamātraparaṃ vākyaṃ, evam ihāpi | tadukaṃ maṇḍanamiśraiḥ |
apekṣitatvād bhāvyasya kāmaśabdā hi tatparāḥ |
viśeṣaṇapradhānatvaṃ daṇḍītyādiṣu darśitam || iti |
[305] ataḥ kālānunmādena tatra karmavidhir ity uktaṃ | tad evaṃ yathoktapūrvottarapakṣāv ayuktaikāv atra tarhi tau yuktau ucyete | tatra tāvat pūrvapakṣaḥ | nityanaimittikeṣu āvaśyakatvena prāpteṣu tādṛgvidhasvarūpanirvāhārthaṃ yathā śaknuyād ity upabandhe vihitāyāṃ saṃpattau tatsadṛśapratinidhigrahaṇaṃ kartavyam iti sthite mukhyakālāsaṃpattau gauṇakālagrahaṇaṃ kāryam | tad āha trikāṇḍamaṇḍanaḥ |
mukhyakāle yadāvaśyaṃ karma kartuṃ na śakyate |
gauṇakāle ’pi kartavyaṃ gauṇo ’py atredṛśo bhavet || iti |
'īdṛśaḥ' mukhyakālasadṛśaḥ | anenāsya pratinidhitvayogyatā darśitā | smṛtayo ’pi gauṇakālagrahaṇaṃ darśayanti |
yathā kathaṃcit kartavyaṃ nityaṃ karma vijānatā |
na prāpte ’sya vilopo ’sti paitṛkasya viśeṣataḥ || iti |
tathā |
dinoditāni karmāṇi pramādād akṣatāni vai |
yāminyāḥ praharaṃ yāvat tāvat sarvāṇi kārayet |
saṃdhyārātryor na kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ khalu vicakṣaṇaiḥ ||
ityādikaḥ smārtasaṃdhyādikālaviśeṣaniṣedhaś cāgauṇatvena tatkālagrahaṇaprāptau satyām evāvakalpyate | nityanaimittike ca yathā kuryād ity upabandhasāmarthyān nityavad āmnātāny apy aṅgāny anityāni bhavanti | tad āha paiṭhīnasiḥ | "pradhānasahitāny aṅgāni bhavanti na bhavanti ca" iti |
nanu yadi mukhyakālātikrame gauṇakālagrahaṇam aviruddhaṃ tarhi darśapaurṇamāsapārvaṇaśrāddhādīnām amāvāsyādivihitakālālābhe pañcamyā[306]dikartavyatvaṃ prasajyeta | kā nāmaiṣā prasaktiḥ | abhimatam eva hi gauṇakālasvīkāravādinas tadanuṣṭhānaṃ | etac ca gauṇakālagrahaṇaṃ na kāmyeṣu | tatra hi sarvāṅgopahāraśaktasyaivādhikārapratipādanāt | gauṇakālagrahaṇena karmānuṣṭhāne kaścid viśeṣas trikāṇḍamaṇḍanenoktaḥ | prasaṅgād ucyate |
gauṇesv eteṣu kāleṣu karma coditam ācaret |
prāyaścittaprakaraṇaproktāṃ niṣkṛtim ācaret | kālāntare coditaṃ karmaiteṣu pūrvoktalakṣaṇeṣu gauṇeṣu kāleṣv ācaran prāyaścittaprakaraṇoktāṃ niṣkṛtiṃ prāyaścittam ācaret | pakṣāntaramapi tenaivoktaṃ|
prāyaścittam akṛtvā vā gauṇakāle samācaret |
atrākṛtvā ity atra pūrvakālatāpratītir avivakṣitā | tad evaṃ gauṇakālagrahaṇe prāpte vidhīyate | sarvatra saptamyantanirdiṣṭakālasyānupādeyatvenoddeśyaikasvabhāvasya nimittatvenābhidhānād adhikāriviśeṣaṇatve vihitakālam antareṇādhikārābhāvāt kālāntare kṛtam akṛtam eva syād iti gauṇakālagrahaṇaṃ na kāryyaṃ | vihitakālābhāve cādhikārābhāvaḥ sāntapanīyādhikāraṇe "prāpaṇāvanimittasya" ity atra sūtre pratipāditaḥ |
ata eva laugākṣiḥ |
gaṇitāt jñāyate kālaḥ kāle tiṣṭhanti devatāḥ |
varam ekāhutiḥ kāle nākāle lakṣakoṭayaḥ ||
yat tu trikāṇḍamaṇḍane gauṇakālagrahaṇam uktaṃ tatpūrvapakṣanyāyaśaivasiddhāntam abhimanya noktam ity upekṣaṇīyam | yat tu "yathā kathaṃcid" ityādi smaraṇaṃ na tatra gauṇakālagrahaṇaṃ pratipadyate kiṃ tv agrahaṇam eva | yatas tasyāyam artho ’vagamyate | svakālaṃ prāptasya karmaṇo mukhyadravyādyasampattivaśāt lopo ’nanuṣṭānaṃ na kāryaṃ | [307] kiṃ tu svakāla eva yathā kathaṃcin mukhyadravyādisadṛśadravyāntaropādānena kiṃcid viphalam api kartavyam iti | yac ca dinoditāni karmāṇītyādivacanaṃ, tena na sarveṣv api karmasu sarveṣāṃ ca gauṇakālānāṃ gauṇatvaprayuktyā grahaṇam ucyate | kiṃ tu keṣucid eva karmasu kiṃcid eva kālāntaram anukalpatvena mukhyakālavan nyāyād aprāptam eva vidhīyate | śrutāny eva tadvacanabalena saṃvatsarartumāsatithyādikālaviśeṣavihitāni karmāṇi tatkālātikrame kālāntare ’pi kartuṃ yujyante | yas tu saṃdhyārātryoḥ śrāddhaniṣedhaḥ so ’pi rāgaprāptasya bhakṣaṇāder iva nyāyābhāsādinā prāptasyopapadyate |
athavā
dravyaṃ brāhmaṇasampattiḥ śrāddhaṃ prati ruciś ca ha |
akāle yadi vā kāle tīrthaśrāddhaṃ sadā naraiḥ |
prātar evāhni kartavyam |
ityādibhir aparāhṇavyatiriktakālaprāptasya śrāddhasya saṃdhyādikāleṣv ayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | yat tu mukhyakālātikrame nityanaimittikānāṃ gauṇakāle ’nuṣṭhānaṃ dṛśyate tat kvacit tu garhāparihāramanaḥparitoṣādyarthaṃ, kvacic cānukalpatvena kālāntaravidher ity anavadyam | upadiṣṭakālātikrame cādhikārābhāvād eva mukhyadravyālābhān mukhyakālātikramo na kāryaḥ | etad eva ca saṃdehapūrvakam upanyasya trikāṇḍamaṇḍane niścitaṃ | mukhyakāle hi mukhyaṃ cet syāt kālātikramo na kāryaḥ | dhanaṃ naiva labhyate cet kāladravyayoḥ kasya mukhyatvaṃ gauṇatāpi vā |
mukhyakālam upāśritya gauṇam apy astu tad dhanaṃ |
na mukhyadravyalābhena gauṇakālapratīkṣaṇaṃ ||
tad evam etat siddhavacanam antareṇa gauṇagrahaṇaṃ na kāryam | sati tu vacane gauṇasya tasmāt parasyāpi vā kāyam iti |
[308] tatrāha ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
deye pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhe tu aśaucaṃ jāyate yadā |
āśauce tu vyatikrānte tebhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ vidhīyate ||
mukhyakālikeāpakramāt prāg yadā śrāddhādhikāriṇām aśaucaṃ jāyate, tadā mukhyakālapratyāsanne aśaucena cādūṣite aśaucāpagamānantarakāla eva paitṛkaṃ karma kāryam ity arthaḥ |
yat tv atrivacanam,
tad ahaś cet praduṣyeta kenacit sūtakādinā |
mṛtakānantaraṃ kuryāt punas tad ahar eva vā || iti |
tatra tithipakṣas tv ādiśabdoktanimittāntaravighnite mukhyakālaviṣaye iti pūrvavacanāvirodhāya kalpanīyaṃ | etac ca pakṣāntarakṣayāhakriyamāṇapratimāsika-śrāddhaviṣayaṃ | tatra māsaspṛṣṭakālābhāve ’pi pakṣatithimātraspṛṣṭasyāpi grāhyatvāt | ata evāha devalaḥ | pakṣa iti mṛtakasūtakābhyāṃ mṛtāhopaghāte tad atikramyāśaucanivṛttau śrāddhaṃ kartavyam ity ukaṃ | tatpākopakramān nimantraṇād vā pūrvam evāśaucavijñāne sati nottarakālaṃ | tathā ca brahmapurāṇe |
gṛhītamadhuparkasya yajamānasya ṛtvijaḥ |
paścād daśāhe patite nāśaucam iti niścayaḥ ||
tadvad gṛhītadīkṣasya traividyasya mahāmakhe |
snānam avabhṛthe yāvat tāvat tasya na vidyate ||
nivṛtte kṛcchrahomādau brāhmaṇādiṣu bhojane |
gṛhītaniyamasyāpi na syād anyasya kasyacit ||
[309]        naiṣṭhikasyātha vānyasya bhikṣārthaṃ prasthitasya ca |
vānaprasthasya vānyasya sādhikārasya sarvadā |
pratigrahādhikārāc ca nivṛttasya na vidyate ||
gomaṇḍalādau vaiśyānāṃ rakṣākālātyayād api |
nimantriteṣu vipreṣu prārabdhe śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
nimantraṇād dhi viprasya svādhyāyād viratasya ca |
dehe pitṛṣu tiṣṭhatsu nāśaucaṃ vidyate kvacit ||
api dātṛgrahītroś (?) ca sūtake mṛtake tathā |
avijñāte na doṣaḥ syāc chrāddhādiṣu kadācana ||
vijñāte bhoktur eva syāt prāyaścittādikaṃ tathā |
bhojanārdhe tu saṃbhukte viprair dātur vipadyate ||
yadā kaścit tadocchiṣṭaśeṣaṃ tyaktvā samāhitaḥ |
ācamya parakīyena jalena śucayo dvijāḥ ||
vivāhayajñayor madhye srutake sati vāntarā |
śeṣam annaṃ parair deyaṃ dātṝn bhoktṝṃś ca na spṛśet ||
atra
nimantriteṣu vipreṣu prārabdhe śrāddhakarmaṇi |
iti tad dvayopanyāsāt pākārambhāt prāk yadā nimantraṇāt prabhṛtyā karmasamāpter āśaucābhāvaḥ, yadā tu pākāt pūrvaṃ nimantraṇaṃ kṛtaṃ tadā pākārambhaprabhṛtyā karmasamāpter āśaucābhāvaḥ tadādipākopakramasyaiva prathamāvayavarūpatvena śrāddhārambhābhāvatvaṃ | bhojanārdhe tu sambhukre yadi dātuḥ kaścid vipadyata ity etasya tatkālamṛtadātṛsapiṇḍanibandhanāśaucaviṣayatvena śrāddhagṛhamṛtāśaucaviṣayatvena vā nimantriteṣu vipreṣv ityādinā pūrvoktena saha [310] virodhaparihāro vidheyaḥ | śeṣam annaṃ parair deyam ityetat tu bhoktṛgṛhavyatiriktajanmamaraṇaviṣayaṃ | dātṝn bhoktṝṃś ca na spṛśed ity atra doṣa iti śeṣaḥ |
āha kratuḥ |
pūrvasaṃkalpitaṃ dravyaṃ dīyamānaṃ na duṣyati |
uktam idam aśaucadūṣite ’dhikāriṇi mukhyakālam atikramyāśaucanivṛttau śrāddhaṃ kartavyam iti | gṛhamedhinaś ca bhāryayā sahaivādhikārāt tasyāṃ rajodarśananibandhanāśucitvadūṣitāyāṃ mukhyakālam atikramyāśucitvanivṛttāv eva śrāddhe kartavye prāpte rajasvalāpatinaivaikākinā mukhyakāla evāmaśrāddhaṃ kartavyam ity āhośanāḥ |
apatnīkaḥ pravāsī ca yasya bhāyā rajasvalā |
siddhānnena na kurvīta āmaṃ tasya vidhīyate || iti |
asyāmadravyaśrāddhavidheḥ kvacid upasaṃhārapūrvakam apavādaḥ |
śrāddhavighne dvijātīnām āmaśrāddhaṃ prakīrtitaṃ |
amāvāsyādiniyataṃ māsaṃ saṃvatsarād ṛte ||
māsikasāṃvatsarikaviṣaye āmaśrāddhaniṣedhaḥ | mṛtāhe prāpte yasya bhāryā rajasvalā bhavati tasminn eva dine annenaiva śrāddhaṃ kuryāt nāmena kālāntare vā |
smṛtyantare |
ābdike samanuprāpte yasya bhāryā rajasvalā |
pañcame ’hani tac chrāddhaṃ na kuryāt tanmṛte ’hani ||
tad bhartuḥ pratyābdike aputrā patnī svayam eva śrāddhakartrī yadi,
aputrā tu yadā bhāryā saṃprāpte bhartur ābdike |
rajasvalā bhavet sā [311] hi tat kuryāt pañcame ’hani ||
iti gotamasmaraṇāt | yad bhāryārajodarśanādirūpe śrāddhavighne saty āmaśrāddhaṃ kartavyam iti prakīrtitaṃ tad amāvāsyādikālikam eva na tu yanmṛtāhakālikaṃ māsikaṃ sāṃvatsarikaṃ ca tad apīty arthaḥ | kiṃ tu tat pakvānnenaiva kartavyam | tac caikabhāryeṇa |
ekoddiṣṭe tu saṃprāpte yadi vighnaḥ prajāyate |
anyasmiṃs tattithau tasmin śrāddhaṃ kuryāt prayatnataḥ ||
‘anyasmin’ anantare māsi | 'tattithau’mṛtatithau ‘tasmin' śukle kṛṣṇe vā mṛtas tasmin pakṣe | śrāddhaṃ vighnavaśād atikrāntaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | yadi vighna ity atrāpi ṛṣyaśṛṅgavacanāvirodhāyāśaucavyatiriktanimitteneti viśeṣo draṣṭavyaḥ | aśaucanimittena vighne jāte māsikaviṣaye ’pi sūtakānantaram eva śrāddhānuṣṭhānam ṛṣyaśṛṅgavacanoktam anusaṃdheyaṃ | ata eva devasvāmināpy uktaṃ |
etat ṛṣyaśṛṅgavacanaṃ sūtakāśaucaviṣayaṃ, nimittāntarais tu tadahar vighāte
ekoddiṣṭe tu saṃprāpte yadi vighnaḥ prajāyate |
ityādi smṛtyantaravacanam iti |
yat punar vyāsavacanaṃ,
śrāddhavighne samutpanne antarāmṛtasūtake |
amāvāsyāṃ prakurvīta śuddhāv eke manīṣiṇaḥ || iti |
'antarāmṛtasūtake' śrāddhaprayogamadhye pākopakramāt prāk mṛtake sūtake vā jāte | 'amāvāsyāṃ' amāvāsyāyām | 'śuddhau' śuddhyanantaraṃ | etad avijñātamṛtāhasāṃvatsarika-śrāddhaviṣayam | amāvāsyāgrahaṇaṃ śuklaikādaśyāḥ kṛṣṇaikādaśyāś copalakṣaṇam |
ata eva marīciḥ |
[312]        śrāddhavighne samutpanne avijñāte mṛte ’hani |
ekādaśyāṃ tu kartavyaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe viśeṣataḥ ||
kṛṣṇapakṣe yā ekādaśī tasyāṃ viśeṣataḥ kartavyam ity anvayaḥ, pitṛkāryeṣu kṛṣṇapakṣasyaiva grāhyatvāt | kṛṣṇaikādaśītāpy amāvāsyāyāḥ pitṛkāryaśraiṣṭhyaṃ daṇḍāpūpanyāyasiddhaṃ boddhavyam | yat tu ṣaṭtriṃśanmatam,
māsikābde tu saṃprāpte antarāmṛtasūtake |
vadanti śuddhau tat kāryaṃ darbhe vāpi vicakṣaṇāḥ || iti |
tāvatoktāny eva tu punaḥ kuryād iti vihitānumāsikaśrāddhaviṣayam, ekoddiṣṭarūpamāsike pūrvam evānukalpasyoktatvāt | atra śuddhyanantarakālo mukhyakālapratyāsannatvāc chreṣṭhaḥ | darśakālas tu tato jaghanyaḥ, mukhyakālapratyāsattyabhāvād iti mantavyaṃ |
tathā ca ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
śucībhūtena dātavyaṃ yā tithiḥ pratipadyate |
sā tithis tasya kartavyā na tv anyā vai kadācana || iti |
ayam arthaḥ | śucībhūtena tāvad dātavyaṃ | tena mukhyakāle śuddhyasaṃbhave śuddhyanantaraṃ yā tithiḥ pratipadyate labhyate sā tithis tasya kartavyā karmāṅgatayā neyā | yā mukhyā tithir aśaucadūṣitā kadācana vā nānuṣṭheyeti | aśaucādūṣitā tu mukhyā tithiḥ sadānuṣṭheyeti |
tithicchedo na kartavyo vināśaucaṃ yadṛcchayā |
piṇḍaṃ śrāddhaṃ ca dātavyaṃ vicchittiṃ naiva kārayet || iti |
'yadṛcchayā' svecchayā, 'śrāddhaṃ' brāhmaṇatarpaṇaṃ, caśabdenāgnaukaraṇam api [313] kartavyam iti, samuccityeti | śrāddhaśabdenātra brāhmaṇatarpaṇamātram upacārād ucyate, na tu mukhyārthatayā, agnaukaraṇabrāhmaṇatarpaṇapiṇḍadānātmakakatritayasamudāyasyaiva mukhyārthavāt | vicchittiṃ naiva kārayed ity asyāyam arthaḥ | kṛtsnasya śrāddhaśabdārthasya kartum aśakyatve ’pi taddeśamātraṃ vā yathāsāmarthyaṃ kuryāt | sarvathā karmavicchittiṃ naiva kuryād iti | ata eva nigamaḥ |
āhitāgneḥ pitrarcanaṃ piṇḍair eva brāhmaṇān api vā bhojayet || iti |
tatra yathāsāmarthyaṃ vyavasthā | tataś ca dvitīyapakṣāsāmarthye prathamoktapratītir jāyate | tad vādho mā bhūd ity etadartham evam abhyupagantavyaṃ |
nanu darśayiṣyamāṇais tridhācatudhādivibhāgavacanair yaugiko dṛṣṭāntasārūpyakalpanāmūlakaś vibhāgo bādhyatām |
satyaṃ | pāribhāṣikasya pratyakṣavacanopadiṣṭasya vānavakāśatvena pratyakṣatvena ca yaugikāt kālpanikāc ca balīyastvād eva bādhaḥ | kiṃ tu paribhāṣayā yaugikasya bādho mā bhūd ity etadartham eva yogasiddho 'py arthaḥ |
skandapurāṇe |
āvartanāt tu pūrvāhṇo hy aparāhṇas tataḥ param || iti |
'āvartanaṃ' dinam, iti dvidhāvibhaktasyāhno dvitīyo yo bhāgo ’parāhṇa ity ekaṃ matam | atha tridhāvibhakrasyāhṇas tṛtīyo bhāgo ’parāhṇa ity aparaṃ mataṃ | ucyate tatra śrutiḥ | pūrvāhṇo vai devānāṃ madhyandinaṃ manuṣyāṇām aparāhṇaḥ pitṝṇāṃ | tasmād aparāhṇe dadātīti | atra pañcadaśamuhartātmakadivasasya pañcadaśamuhūrtaparimitaṃ pūrvavāhṇādināmnas trīn bhāgān parikalpya kramād daivamānuṣapaitṛkeṣu karmasu viniyuṅkte śrutir [314] iti pratītir jāyate | atrāparāhṇakāle vihitasya pārvaṇaśrāddhasya vāsaratṛtīyāṃśamānataḥ kartavyatopadeśakālaṃ viniyogaprakaraṇe darśayiṣye | so ’pi tridhāvibhaktasyāhṇas tṛtīyo bhāgo ’parāhṇa ity anyatrapradarśyatvenānusandheyaḥ, iti sahādhikāribhāryādūṣaṇād bhāryāntarasya vā rajasvalābhāve tadviṣayavacanābhāvād rajodarśanarūpavighnāpagamakāla eva kartavyam | sa coktaḥ prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
saṃśuddhā (?) syāc caturthe ’hni snātā nārau rajasvalā |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca pañcame ’hani śudhyati ||
smṛtyantare |
mṛte ’hani tu saṃprāpte yasya bhāryā rajasvalā |
śrāddhaṃ tadā na kartavyaṃ kuryāt tat pañcame ’hani ||
adhikāribhāryāntarayuktena tv adhikārānapagamān mukhyakāla eva kartavyaṃ |
iti mukhyakālātikrame kālāḥ |
athātra kasmiṃścic chrāddhe prātaḥkālaḥ | kasmiṃścit pūrvahṇaḥ kasmiṃścin madhyāhnaḥ kasmiṃścid aparāhṇo vihitaḥ | sāyaṃkālaś ca niṣiddhaḥ | atas tadvivekārthaṃ pūrvāhṇād aparāhṇo viśiṣyata iti |
nanv atra śrāddhaviṣaye pūrvāhṇād aparāhṇasya vaiśiṣṭyamātram ucyate | na punar ahaḥ dvidhā vibhajya pūrvabhāgasya pūrvāhṇasaṃjñakatvam iti | athocyate | dvidhā vibhaktasya māsasya bhāgau pūrvāparau pakṣau bhavataḥ | tau cātra dṛṣṭāntīkṛtau svasārūpyād ahno ’pi dvidhāvibhaktasya pūrvāparayor bhāgayoḥ pūrvāparāhṇasaṃjñakatvaṃ gamayata iti | tatra vaiśiṣṭyamātreṇa sārūpyād dṛṣṭāntatvopapatteḥ |
satyaṃ | tathāvibhāgāntarānupādānāt sarvasārūpyānurodhāc ca [315] vivakṣitavibhāgapratītir jāyate | tadbādho mā bhūd ity etadartham evam abhyupagantavyaṃ |
iti tridhā vibhaktasya tṛtīyo bhāgo ’parāhṇa iti mataṃ |
atha viṣamair bhāgaiś caturdhā vibhaktasya tṛtīyo bhāgo ’parāhṇa ity aparaṃ |
tathā ca gobhilaḥ |
pūrvāhṇaṃ praharaṃ sārdhaṃ madhyāhnaṃ praharaṃ tathā |
ā tṛtīyād aparāhṇaṃ sāyāhnaṃ ca tataḥ param || iti |
atra sūryodayāt prabhṛti sārdhapraharaparimitaṃ dinabhāgaṃ pūrvāhṇam | tadūrdhvaṃ praharaparimitaṃ madhyāhnam | tadūrdhvaṃ tṛtīyapraharasamāptiparyantam ardhapraharaparimitam aparāhṇaṃ | tadūrdhvaṃ praharamātraṃ sāyāhnam āhur ity arthaḥ |
iti caturdhā vibhaktasya tṛtīyo bhāgo ’parāhṇa iti mataṃ |
atha samavibhāgaiḥ pañcadhā vibhaktasyāhnaś caturtho bhāgo ’parāhṇa ity aparam api mataṃ |
tatra śatapathaśrutiḥ |
ādityas tv eva sarva ṛtavaḥ | yadaivodety atha vasanto yadā saṅgavo ’tha grīṣmo yadā madhyandino ’tha varṣā yadāparāhṇo ’tha śaradyadevāstam ety atha hemantaḥ iti | evaṃ pañcadaśamuhūrtasaṃmitasya divasasya pañcadhā vibhāge kriyamāṇe trimuhūrtaparimitāḥ prātar ādayaḥ pañca kālā bhavanti | atra yady api tasmāt tu madhyandina evādadhītety anantaram evāmnāyanasyādhānasaṃbandho madhyandinavidhiḥ | śeṣabhūtayā pañcadhā vibhāgaḥ śrūyate | tathā hi prātarādivihitaiḥ karmabhir ākāṅkṣitasvakālaparimāṇa-jñāpa[316]katayopajīvyamānaḥ sarvakāmasaṃbandhitāṃ labhate | ata evāyaṃ trimuhūrtaparimāṇāvayavaḥ pañcadhā vibhāgaḥ sādhāraṇyenaiva dṛśyate | tathā ca prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
muhūrtatritayaṃ prātas tāvān eva ca saṅgavaḥ |
madhyāhnas trimuhūrtaḥ syād aparāhṇo ’pi tādṛśaḥ |
sāyāhnas trimuhūrto ’tha sarvakarmmabahiṣkṛtaḥ || iti |
ata eva tatkālavihitakarmamātropayogitām asya vibhāgasyāha parāśaraḥ |
lekhāprabhṛty athādityān muhūrtas traya eva ca |
prātas tu sa smṛtaḥ kālo bhāgaś cāhnaḥ sa pañcamaḥ ||
saṅgavas trimuhūrto ’tha madhyāhnas tatsamas tataḥ |
tatas trayo muhūrtāś ca tv aparāhṇo viśiṣyate ||
pañcamo ’pi dināṃśo yaḥ sa sāyāhṇa iti smṛtaḥ |
yad yad eteṣu vihitaṃ tat tat kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ || iti |
udayamānasya bhāsvanmaṇḍalasya rekhāvat prakāśamānaḥ prathamopalambhayogyabhāgo ‘lekhā' | 'ādityāt' ādityodayāt | tathā ca lekhāprabhṛty ādityodayād ārabhya ye tatra muhūrtās te prātaḥsaṃjñā bhavantīty uktaṃ bhavati |
uktaṃ hi skandapurāṇe |
lekhāprabhṛty athāditye trimuhūtotthite tu vai |
prātas tataḥ smṛtaḥ kālo bhāgaś cāhnaḥ sa pañcamaḥ || iti |
yad yad eteṣu prātarādikāleṣu karma vihitaṃ tat tad eteṣu yathokteṣu trimuhūrtaparimiteṣu kuryād ity arthaḥ |
[317]        atha yathoktavacanaprapañcamitāḥ pūrvāhṇādikālāḥ kalpā evocyante | tatra dvidhāharvibhāge bhāsvanmaṇḍālārdhāṃśanirīkṣaṇād ārabhyāṣṭamamuhūrta-pūrvārdhasamāptiparyantaḥ pūrvāhṇaḥ | tridhā vibhāge lekhāprakāśāt prabhṛti pañcamuhūrtāvasānāvadhiḥ | caturdhā vibhāge tata evārabhya ṣaṣṭhamuhūrtatṛtīyapāda-pūrvārdhasamāptiparyantaḥ | pañcadhā vibhāge tūdayād ārabhya ṣaṇmuhūrtaparimitasya kālasya pūrvāhṇatvaṃ vaktavyaṃ | tadā hi yaugikīṃ vṛttiṃ parikalpya pūrvāhṇaśabdasyārtho vācyaḥ | yady api dinādyasya muhūrtaṣaṭkasya prātaḥ aṅgavātmakasaṃjñādvayāvarodho ’sti tathāpy asau niyogavirodhīti na pūrvahṇasaṃjñāniveśaparipanthitāṃ pratipadyate |
iti caturvidhapūrvāhṇavivekaḥ |
atha madhyāhnakalpāḥ |
dvidhāvibhāge divasasya madhyamakṣaṇād ubhayato madhyāhnavihitakarmaprayoga-paryāpto madhyāhnaḥ | na cātra viruddhasaṃjñāntaraniveśo ’py abhyupeyaḥ | tridhā vibhāge ṣaṣṭhamuhūrtād ārabhya daśamamuhūrtāvasānāvadhir madhyāhnaḥ | caturdhā vibhāge ṣaṣṭhamuhūrtatṛtīyapādottarārdhād ārabhya daśamamuhūrtadvitīyapādapūvārdhasamāptiparyantaḥ | pañcadhā vibhāge tu ṣaṣṭhamuhūrtād ārabhya daśamamuhūrtasamāptiparyantaḥ |
iti caturvidhamadhyāhnavivekaḥ |
athāparāhṇakalpāḥ |
dvidhā vibhāge aṣṭamamuhūrtottarārdhād ārabhya paścimasaṃdhyārambhaparyanto [318] ’parāhṇaḥ | tridhā vibhāge tv ekādaśamuhartād ārabhya paścimasaṃdhyāparyantaḥ | catudhā vibhāge daśamamuhūrtadvitīyapādottarārdhād ārabhya dvādaśamuhūrtādyapādāvasānaparyantaḥ | pañcadhā vibhāge daśamamuhatād ārabhya dvādaśamuhūrtāvasānaparyantaḥ |
iti caturvidhāparāhṇavivekaḥ
atha sāyāhnakalpāḥ |
atha sāyaṃkālo dvidhā tridhā vibhāge tatkālavihitakarmaprayogaparyāpto ’stamayāt pūrvaṃ kalpanīyaḥ | caturdhā tu vibhāge dvādaśamuhūrtadvitīyapādād ārabhya sūryāstamayāvadhiḥ | pañcadhā vibhāge trayodaśamuhūrtād ārabhya sūryastamayaṃ sāyāhna iti |
ete ca pūrvāhṇādayo vakṣyamāṇaviniyogānatikrameṇa yathālābhaṃ grāhyāḥ | prāśastyatāratamyakrameṇa ca yo yaṃ kālam apekṣya sūkṣṇaḥ sa tasmāt praśastataraḥ | mahāvadhivacanena mahataḥ kālasya pūrvāhṇatve pramite tadekadeśasyāpi pramitam eva pūrvahṇatvaṃ | yathā himavat kuharād ārabhya pūrvasamudrasīmno jalapravāhaviśeṣasya gaṅgātve pramite pramitam eva tadantarvartinaḥ pravāhaikadeśasyāpi gaṅgātvaṃ | katham anyathā gaṅgāyāṃ snānaṃ kuryād aparāhṇe pitṛyajñam ācared iti vihitasya tadekadeśe ’nuṣṭhāne vidheḥ siddhir dṛśyate ca | kecit samudāyatadekadeśayoḥ sādhāraṇaśabdāḥ | yad uktam ācāryaiḥ |
bindau ca samudāye ca toyaśabdo yatheṣyate |
saṃsargidravyarūpatvāt sūktaṃ vākyaṃ padaṃ tathā || iti |
[319]        ato ’vāntarāvadhivacanapramitapūrvāhṇaikadeśāntaranivartakatvena vā svābhidheyasyāpi prāśastyapratipādanaparatvena cārthavatvaṃ vaktavyam | tatrādye pakṣe mahāvadhivacanānarthakyabādhau syātāṃ | ato ’vāntarāvadhivacanaṃ praśastataratvārtham iti niścīyate |
atha kutapakālāḥ |
skanda-vāyupurāṇayoḥ |
kuṃ yatra gopatir gobhiḥ kārtsnyena tapati kṣaṇe |
sa kālaḥ kutapo jñeyas tatra dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
'kuḥ' pṛthvīḥ tāṃ, 'gopatiḥ' sūryaḥ, 'gobhiḥ' svakīyaiḥ karaiḥ, ‘kārtsnyena' samagrāṃ, yasmin kṣaṇe 'tapati' uṣṇāṃ karoti, sa kālaḥ kutapo jñeyaḥ | tad anena kutapaśabdanirvacanaṃ kṛtaṃ | nirūpitaś ca kutapo ’yam iti | sarvacchāyāsaṅkocād divasamadhya eva hi sūryaḥ kṛtsnāṃ pṛthvīṃ tapati, ato divasamadhyaḥ kutapa ity evam asau nirddharito bhavati | sa ca muhūrtaparimito ’pi svalpam anusaṃdhāyopacāravṛttyā kṣaṇaśabdenoktaḥ | uktaṃ hi vāyupurāṇa eva |
muhūrtāt saptamād ūrdhvaṃ muhūrtān navamād adhaḥ |
sa kālaḥ kutapo jñeyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe ’pi |
ahno muhūrtā vikhyātā daśa pañca ca sarvadā |
tatrāṣṭamo muhūrto yaḥ sa kālaḥ kutapaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
khaḍgapātrādikaṃ tu kutapapadaśakyamadhyāhnavācy avasthitatvāt pṛthakkartum aśakyam iti madhyāhnaprasaṅgād ihaiva likhyate |
[320]        kālikāpurāṇe |
brāhmaṇāḥ kambalo gāvo rūpyāgnyatithayo ’pi ca |
tilā darbhāś ca madhyāhnaḥ sarvaite kutapāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
madhyāhnaḥ khaḍgapātraṃ ca tathā nepālakambalaḥ |
rūpyaṃ darbhās tilā gāvo dauhitraś cāṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pāpaṃ kutsitam ity āhus tasya santāpakāriṇaḥ |
aṣṭāv ete yatas tasya kutapā iti viśrutāḥ ||
kauṣītakinā tu tathānyaḥ kālakambala iti paṭhitaṃ |
iti kutapaḥ |
athaiteṣām aparāhṇādikālānāṃ pārvaṇādiṣu śrāddheṣu viniyogaḥ kathyate | tathāha śātātapaḥ |
āmaśrāddhaṃ tu pūrvāhṇe prātar vṛddhinimittakam |
manur apy āha |
pūrvāhṇe mātṛkaṃ śrāddham aparāhṇe tu pārvaṇaṃ |
ekoddiṣṭaṃ tu madhyāhne prātar vṛddhinimittakam || iti |
āha mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
śuklapakṣasya pūrvahṇe śrāddhaṃ kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ |
kṛṣṇapakṣāparāhṇe tu rohiṇaṃ tu na laṅghayet || iti |
atra śuklapakṣe pūrvāhṇāntargate kutapapūrvārdha eva pārvaṇam ekeoddiṣṭaṃ vā śrāddhum ārambhaṇīyaṃ | kṛṣṇapakṣe tv aparāhṇāntargate kutapottarārdha eva | [321] etac ca vacanaṃ pārvaṇe kutapasya pūrvardham ekoddiṣṭe tu parārdhaṃ prāpayituṃ | vaiparītyena hi pūrvāparārdhayoḥ madhyāhnāparāhṇo vidhyantarād eva prāptaḥ | atha vā śuklapakṣasyety anena tatpakṣavihitaṃ daivikaṃ śrāddham upalakṣya pūrvāhṇo vidhīyate | kṛṣṇapakṣety anena paitṛkam upalakṣyāparāhṇa iti | evaṃ ca vidhyantarāvirodhenaiva satyāṅgatau madhyāhnāparāhṇau ca bādhitau na bhaviṣyataḥ | tatrāparāhṇasya tāvad viniyogo vivicyate | śātātapaḥ |
darśaśrāddhaṃ tu yat proktaṃ pārvaṇaṃ tat prakīrtitaṃ |
aparāhṇe pitṝṇāṃ tu tatra dānaṃ praśasyate || iti |
yat piṇḍapitṛyajñānuṣṭhānarahitāgnikartṛkaṃ strīśūdrādikartṛkaṃ vā darśaśrāddhaṃ tatrāmāvāsyāyām aparāhṇe piṇḍapitṛyajñena carantīti kalpasūtrakāroktāparāhṇakālānuṣṭheyapiṇḍapitṛyajñottarakālatvaṃ vidheḥ, tadanusāreṇaiva tridhā vibhaktasyāhnas tṛtīyabhāgo yathālābham āsaṃdhyaṃ grāhyaḥ | tathā cāha manuḥ |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kṣīṇe rājani śasyate |
vāsarasya tṛtīye 'ṃśe nātisaṃdhyāsamīpataḥ || iti |
tathā |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya vipraś candrakṣaye ’gnimān |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryān māsānumāsikam || iti |
na caitad vaktavyam |
pitṛyajñaṃ tu nirvartya tarpaṇākhyaṃ tu yo’gnimān |
piṇḍānvāhāryakaṃ kuryāc chrāddham indukṣaye sadā ||
iti matyapurāṇavacanāt pañcamahāyajñāntarbhūtatarpaṇākhyapitṛyajñānantaryam anvāhāryakasya na [322] piṇḍapitṛyajñānantaryam iti | tadanurodhenāparāhṇagrahaṇam iti | yata āha laugākṣaḥ |
pakṣāntaṃ karma nirvartya vaiśvadevaṃ ca sāgnikaḥ |
piṇḍayajñaṃ tataḥ kuryāt tato ’nvāhāryakaṃ budhaḥ || iti |
ataḥ piṇḍapitṛyajñād ūrdhvam anuṣṭheyasya darśaśrāddhasya pitṛyajñasya samāpyanusāreṇa nātisaṃdhyāsāmīpyaparyanto vāsarasya tṛtīyāṃśo grāhyaḥ |
ity aparāhṇakālaviniyogaḥ
atha pūrvāhṇakālaviniyogaḥ |
tatra pūrvāhṇe daivikaṃ śrāddham iti daivikaśrāddhāṅgabhūte pūrvāhṇe vaktavyābhāvād āmaśrāddhāṅgabhūtaṃ pratyucyate | tatra cāmaśrāddhaṃ tu pūrvāhṇa ity ayaṃ pūrvāhṇavidhir dvijakartṛkāmaśrāddhaviṣaya eva na punaḥ śūdrakartṛkāmaśrāddhaviṣayo ’pīti kaścin niścikāya | sa hi manyate, ubhayakartṛkāmaśrāddhaviṣayatve ekatrāparāhṇasyātyantikaṃ bādhaṃ kṛtvānyatra pākṣikaṃ niveśyamānasyaikatra nityatvam anyatra pākṣikatvam iti vaiśamyam āpadyate |
tathā hi |
dvijāmaśrāddhe tāvad ātideśikasyāparāhṇasyātyantaṃ bādhaṃ kṛtvā pūrvāhṇaṃ niveśayet | pūrvāhṇopadeśo bhavatv ity upadeśasyādeśād balīyastvena nātideśikasyāparāhṇasya pākṣikaṃ bādhaṃ kṛtvā niveśayed iti | ato śūdrāmaśrāddhe tūpadeśāntareṇa tulyabalatvād aupadeśikasyāparāhṇasya pākṣikaṃ bādhaṃ kṛtvā niveśayet |
[323]        ato vaiṣamyaṃ | kathaṃ punaḥ dvijāmaśrāddhe aparāhṇa ātideśikaḥ, śūdrāmaśrāddhe caupadeśika iti |
ucyate | mukhyaṃ hi pārvaṇaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanātmakaṃ nityaṃ | tad dvijarūpakartṛviṣayam eva na śudrakartṛviṣayam, tasya brāhmaṇabhojanātmaka-śrāddhaniṣedhena nityam evāmaśrāddhavidhānāt | niṣedhaṃ bādhitvāpi yadi tatra pakvānnavidhir api niviśet tadā śūdraviṣaye niṣedhabādhasāpekṣeo dvijaviṣaye nirapekṣa iti vaiṣamyam āpadyeta | tad evaṃ brāhmaṇabhojanātmakaṃ yad brāhmaṇyaṃ yan mukhyaṃ pārvaṇaṃ tad dvijarūpakartṛviṣayaṃ nityaṃ ca | anityaṃ tu dvijānāṃ pākāsaṃbhavādinimittavihitam āmaśrāddhaṃ | evaṃ ca saty aparāhṇaprabhṛtayaḥ prakṛtāv upadiśyamānā dharmā brāhmaṇabhojanātmakasya nityasyaivopadeśato bhavitum arhanti nānyasya | dvijakartṛkāmaśrāddhasyāpi nityānityasaṃyogavirodhāt | yathopadiśyamānā dīkṣaṇīyādayo dharmā nityasyaivāgniṣṭomasaṃsthasya jyotiṣṭomasyopadeśato bhavanti | nānityasyānyasyāpi | yathā vā krayādayaḥ somadravyasādhyasya nityasyaiva jyotiṣṭomasya, nānityasya anyadravyasādhyasyāpi | kiṃ tv anitye ’tideśata eva bhavanti | ataś cānitye (?) dvijakartṛkāmaśrāddhe ’tideśata evāparāhṇaḥ | śūdrāmaśrāddhasya tu anaimittikatvena nityatvād upadeśata evāparāhṇa ity aupadeśikaḥ | pūrvahastena saha tatra vikalpato dvijāmaśrāddhatvātideśikatvena bādhate | ataś cāyaṃ pūrvāhaṇavidhiḥ ekatra nityo ’nyatra pākṣika ity evaṃvidhavaiṣamyākulīkṛtaḥ sann ekam eva viṣayam āśrayitum arhati | sa cānyasya vikalpaduṣṭaṃ śūdrāmaśrāddhaṃ parihṛtya dvijāmaśrāddham evāśrayate | evaṃ ca sati
madhyāhnāt parato yas tu kutapaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
āmamātreṇa tatraiva pitṝṇāṃ dattam akṣayaṃ ||
[324] iti vacanamatrāparāhṇānuvādakam, sādhāraṇenaiva vacanenāprāpyāparāhṇalābhāt | tasmāc chudrāmaśrāddhaṃ kutapottarārdhāt prabhṛti pravṛtte ’parāhṇe | dvijāmaśrāddhaṃ tu pūrvāhṇa iti |
iti pūrvāhṇaviniyogaḥ |
atha madhyāhnaviniyogaḥ |
tatra caikoddiṣṭaṃ tu madhyāhna ity atra yady api madhyāhnatvena ṣaṣṭhopakramāḥ pañca muhūrtā ekoddiṣṭakālatvenopadiṣṭās tathāpi kutapasyātiprāśastyāt prayatnena kutapena śrāddhārambhaṃ saṃpādayet | tad uktaṃ matsyapurāṇe |
madhyāhne sarvadā yasmān mandībhavati bhāskaraḥ |
tasmād anantaphaladas tatrārambho viśiṣyate ||
yathā kutape śrāddhārambho viśiṣṭas tathā rohiṇe samāptir apīty āha gautamaḥ |
prārabhya kutape śrāddhaṃ kuryād ārohiṇād budhaḥ |
vidhijño vidhim āsthāya rohiṇaṃ tu na laṅghayet || iti |
'rohiṇaṃ' navamo muhūrtaḥ | na laṅghayed ity etad api rohiṇāntaḥ samāptipraśaṃsārtham, na rohiṇātikramaniṣedhārtham, tasyāpi madhyāhnatvenāparāhṇatvena ca śrāddhāṅgavāt | ata eva matsyapurāṇe kutapāt prabhṛti muhūrtapañcakasya śrāddhāṅgatvam uktaṃ |
muhūrtāt kutapād ūrdhvaṃ yan muhūrtacatuṣṭayaṃ |
muhūrtapañcakaṃ hy etat svadhābhavanam iṣyate || iti |
[325] svadhābhavanam ity asyābhiprāyaḥ | svadhāśabdavataḥ pārvaṇasyaikoddiṣṭasya cāyaṃ kālo na punaḥ svāhāśabdavato daivikasya nāndīśrāddhasya veti | tayor hi pūrvāhṇaprātaḥkālayor eva vidhānāt |
iti madhyāhnaviniyogaḥ |
atha prātaḥkālaviniyogaḥ |
tatra prātar vṛddhinimittakam ity ayaṃ prātaḥkālavidhiḥ putrajanmanimittakavyatirikta- vṛddhiśrāddhaviṣayaḥ, tasya hi putrajanmarūpe nimitte sati vidhānāt | tadanantaram evānuṣṭheyatve janmanaś cāniyatakālatve niyatakālatvam evāvasīyate |
nanu nimitte (?) vihitam api śrāddhaṃ vaiśvānarīyasvakālaṃ prati kiṃ notkṛṣyate | utkarṣakāraṇābhāvād iti jānīhi, na tv asty āśaucam utkarṣakāraṇaṃ | na hi vaiśvānarīyeṣṭir āśaucadoṣanivṛttyartham ujjṛmbhitā |
athocyate |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaṃ pitṛkarma ca |
pretapiṇḍakriyāvarjam āśaucavinivṛttaye ||
ityādiniṣedhānurodhād utkarṣa iti |
tatra putre jāte śrāddhaṃ kuryād iti naimittikavidher viśeṣaśāstreṇa niṣedhabādhakatvāt | asti ca tatkāle nāsty aśaucam ity evaṃparo vacanaprapañcaḥ |
tatra tāvac chaṅkhalikhitau |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ |
[326]        bhūyas tatraiva jāyante tadahar vedayanti ca |
tasmāt ma divasaḥ puṇyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ prītivardhanaḥ ||
ādityapurāṇe ’pi |
devāś ca pitaraś caiva putre jāte dvijanmanām |
āyānti tasmāt tad ahaḥ puṇyaṃ pūjyaṃ ca sarvadā ||
tatra dadyāt suvarṇaṃ tu bhūmiṃ gāṃ turagaṃ rathaṃ |
chatraṃ vastraṃ ca mālyaṃ ca śayanaṃ cāsanaṃ gṛhaṃ ||
hārīto ’py āha |
jāte ca kumāre pitṝṇām āmodāt puṇyaṃ tad ahaḥ || iti |
'pitṝṇām āmodāt' atiśayena harṣotpatteḥ, 'puṇyaṃ' pāvanam, tad ahaḥ dānād eva | puṇyasya sa kāla ity arthaḥ | atra putrajanmadivasasyāśaucadūṣitatvābhāvāt tatkāla eva janmanimittaṃ śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ | nāśaucottarakālaṃ pratyutkarṣaṇīyam | atra yady api śaṅkhādivacane tad ahaḥ puṇyam iti pratibhāti tathāpi śuśrunābhyucchedāt (?) pūrvam eveti veditavyaṃ | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
prāṅnābhyucchedāt saṃskārapuṇyārthān kurvanti, nābhyām ucchinnāyām āśaucam || iti |
‘saṃskāraḥ’ jātakarma | 'puṇyārthān' guḍatilatailagobhūhiraṇyavastradhānyādidānāniti |
manur apy āha |
prāṅ nābhivardhanāt puṃso jātakarma vidhīyate || iti |
'vardhanaṃ' chedaḥ |
jaiminir api |
[327]        yāvat na naśyate nālaṃ tāvan nābhyeti sūtakaṃ |
chinne nāle tataḥ paścāt sūtakaṃ tu vidhīyate ||
viṣṇudharmottare mārkaṇḍeya āha |
acchinnanābhyāṃ kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ vai putrajanmani |
aśaucoparame kāryam atha vā niyatātmabhiḥ ||
putrajanmany ā nābhikartanāt puṇyaṃ tad ahaḥ | tatra jātakarmāmaśrāddhaṃ kuryāt, pātrāṇi sahiraṇyāni vā dadyāt, prajāpataye nābhyāṃ chinnāyām aśaucam | ato nālacchedāt pūrvam eva śrāddhaṃ | etac ca naimittikatvād rātrāv api kartavyaṃ | yad āha vyāsaḥ |
rātrau snānaṃ na kurvīta dānaṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ |
naimittikaṃ tu kurvīta snānaṃ dānaṃ ca rātriṣu ||
tathā |
grahaṇodvāhasaṃkrāntiyātrārtiprasaveṣu ca |
dānaṃ naimittikaṃ jñeyaṃ rātrāv api na duṣyati |
putrajanmani yātrāyāṃ sarvaryāṃ dattam akṣayam || iti |
etac ca tatkāle sutakāntaranimittāśaucasaṃnipāte saty api kartavyam | tad āha prajāpatiḥ |
sūtake tu samutpanne putrajanma yadā bhavet |
kartus tātkālikī śuddhiḥ pūrvāśaucena śudhyati ||
'kartuḥ' jātakarmakartuḥ | pūrvapravṛttajanmāśaucanivṛttau satyāṃ sa [328] karmaṇi śuddho bhavatīty arthaḥ | yadi punar idaṃ tatkāle jātakarmakartur anupapatteḥ śāvāśaucāntaranipātād vā na kṛtaṃ syāt tadā sūtakānantaraṃ kasmiṃścid dine prātaḥkāla eva kāryaṃ |
nanu svakālāt paribhraṣṭam idaṃ kim iti kālāntare kartavyaṃ | na hy uparāgādikarmāṇi nimittabandhanāni karmāṇi vā tatkālātikrame ’pi kriyante |
satyam evaṃ | idaṃ tu vacanāt kriyate | tathā ca viṣṇudharmottare |
acchinnanābhyāṃ kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ vai putrajanmani |
aśaucoparame kāryam atha vā niyatātmabhiḥ || iti |
tatrāśaucoparamo ’py asya kālaḥ | sa tu nālacchedapūrvakālāsambhavanibandhanaḥ | ity etat kutaḥ | vaijavāpāyanavacanād brūmaḥ |
janmano ’nantaraṃ kāryaṃ jātakarma yathāvidhi |
daivād atītakālaṃ ced atīte sūtake bhavet || iti |
iti prātaḥkālaviniyogaḥ |
atha niṣiddhakālāḥ |
tatra vyāghrapāda āha |
smārtakarmaparityāgo rāhor anyatra sūtake |
śrautakarmaṇi tat kālaṃ snānaṃ śuddhim avāpnuyāt ||
rāhusūtakād anyasmin sūtake smārtaṃ śrāddhādikarma na kurvīta | śrautaṃ tu darśapūrṇamāsāgnihotrādi snātvā sadya eva kuryād ity aparārkaḥ | sa hi jananāśauce rāhugrastacandrārkasākṣātpūrvakāle ca mṛte sūtakaśabdaprayogo ’stīty abhipretyaivaṃ vyākhyātavān | anyathaivaṃ vyācakṣate | sūtake [329] janmāśauce sarvakarmatyāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | "sa ca rāhor anyatra" sa ca rāhudarśananimittātmakakarmāṇi vihāyety arthaḥ | tatra prathamavyākhyāne janmamaraṇāśaucayor dvitīyavyākhyāne tu janmāśauce śrāddhaṃ na kartavyam iti siddhaṃ bhavati | karmaśabdaś ca pretapiṇḍakriyāvyatiriktakarmaviṣayaḥ | tathā ca paiṭhīnasiḥ |
dānaṃ pratigraho homaḥ svādhyāyaḥ pitṛkarma ca |
pretapiṇḍakriyāvarjam āśauce vinivartayet || iti |
manu-śātātapau |
rātrau śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta rākṣamī kīrtitā hi sā |
saṃdhyayor ubhayoś caiva sūrye caivācirodite || iti |
rakṣāṃsi asyāṃ caranti balavanti vā bhavantīti rākṣamīty ucyate | tasyāṃ hi rakṣobhiḥ śrāddham avalupyate | pūrvāparayor api saṃdhyayoḥ śrāddhaṃ na kartavyaṃ |
saṃdhyayor mānaṃ tu yājñavalkya āha |
udayāt prāktanī saṃdhyā muhūrtadvayam ucyate |
sā ca saṃdhyā trighaṭikā astād upari bhāsvate || iti |
sūrye caivācirodita iti yad uktaṃ tat saṃdhyāsamīpakālasyopalakṣaṇam | tenāstamayasaṃdhyāsamīpo ’pi kālo labhyate | tad anena prātaḥkālavihitaṃ pūrvasaṃdhyātatsamīpakālayoḥ prasaktaṃ vṛddhiśrāddhaṃ niṣidhyate | aparāhṇavihitaṇ ca pārvaṇaṃ paścimasaṃdhyātatsamīpakālayoś ca prasaktaṃ niṣidhyate | madhyama-saṃdhyātatsamaupakālayos tu kutapatvān na kvacin niṣedhaḥ |
[330] skandapurāṇe |
upasaṃdhyaṃ na kurvīta pitṛpūjāṃ kathaṃcana |
sa kāla āsuraḥ proktaḥ śrāddhaṃ tatra vivarjayet ||
anena saṃdhyāsāmīpyaniṣedhe sāyaṃkālāt pūrvaṃ śrāddhārambhasamāptyor asamarthaṃ prati saṃdhyāsamīpavyatiriktaḥ sāyaṃkālaḥ so ’nujñāto bhavati | yat tu,
sāyāhne trimuhūrtas tu sarvakarmabahiṣkataḥ |
iti prabhāsakhaṇḍavacanaṃ,
caturthe prahare prāpte śrāddhaṃ yaḥ kurute naraḥ |
āsuraṃ tad bhavec chrāddhaṃ dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
iti ca baudhāyanavacanaṃ |
divasasyāntime bhāge mandrībhūte divākare |
āsuraṃ tad bhavec chrāddhaṃ pitṝṇāṃ nopatiṣṭhate || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
prāyaḥ prānta upoṣyaḥ syāt tithir daivaphalepsubhiḥ |
mūlaṃ hi pitṛṭṛpṭyarthaṃ paitraṃ coktaṃ maharṣibhiḥ ||
'mūlaṃ' ārambhaḥ |
nāradīyapurāṇe |
tithiprāntaṃ surākhyaṃ hi upoṣyaṃ kavayo viduḥ |
paitryaṃ mūlaṃ titheḥ proktaṃ śāstrajñaiḥ kālakovidaiḥ ||
nigamaḥ |
pūrvāhṇikāsu cec chrāddhaṃ dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet |
iti hārītavacanaṃ tat sāyaṃkālāt pūrvam eva śrāddhārambhasamāptisamarthaṃ praty eva |
āha vyāghrapāt |
[331]        vidhijñaḥ śraddhayopetaḥ samyak pātre niyojakaḥ |
rātrer anyatra kurvāṇaḥ śreyaḥ prāpnoty anuttamam || iti |
'rātriḥ' saṃdhyā | saṃdhyātatsamīpakālaniṣedhasyāpavādam āha viṣṇuḥ |
saṃdhyārātryor na kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ khalu vicakṣaṇaiḥ |
tayor api ca kartavyaṃ yadi syād rāhudarśanam || iti |
'rāhudarśanaṃ' candroparāgaḥ |
iti niṣiddhakālāḥ |
atha pretakriyāsu niṣiddhakālāḥ
tatrāha gārgyaḥ |
pratyakṣaśavasaṃskāre dinaṃ naiva viśodhayet |
āśaucamadhye kriyate punaḥ saṃskārakarma cet ||
śodhanīyaṃ dinaṃ tatra yathāsaṃbhavam eva tu |
āśaucavinivṛttau cet punaḥ saṃṣkriyate mṛtaḥ ||
saṃśodhyaiva dinaṃ grāhyam ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarād yadi |
pretakāryāṇi kurvīta śreṣṭhaṃ tatrottarāyaṇaṃ |
kṛṣṇapakṣaś ca tatrāpi varjayet tu dinakṣayam ||
iha dvividhaḥ pretasaṃskāraḥ | ekaḥ pratyakṣaśarīrasya | pratikṛter aparaḥ | tatra pratyakṣaśarīrasaṃskāre śubhāśubhadinaparīkṣānavakāśaḥ | pratikṛti[332]saṃskārasya tu trayaḥ kālāḥ | āśaucamadhye, saṃvatsarāntaḥ, saṃvatsarād bahiś ceti | tatrāśaucamadhye pratikṛtisaṃskāraḥ sati saṃbhave vakṣyamāṇatithinakṣatrādivarjite kāle vidheyaḥ | yadā tv aśaucād bahiḥ saṃvatsaramadhye pratikṛtisaṃskāras tadānīṃ vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇārambhakālaśodhanam avaśyaṃ kāryaṃ | saṃvatsarād bahir api kriyamāṇe tasmin śodhanīya eva kālaḥ | kiṃ tu tatrottarāyaṇakṛṣṇapakṣau saṃpādyamānau guṇottarau bhavataḥ | dinakṣayadinaṃ tu sarvatra varjanīyam | marīciḥ |
nandāyāṃ bhārgavadine caturdaśyāṃ tripuṣkare |
tatra śrāddhaṃ na kurvīta gṛhī putradhanakṣayāt ||
pratipat ṣaṣṭhy ekādaśī ca nandā | 'bhārgavadinaṃ' śukravāraḥ | kṛttikāpunarvasūttarā-phālgunīviśākhottarāṣāḍhāpūrvabhādrapadākhyāni nakṣatrāṇi 'tripuṣkarāṇi' | śrāddhaśabdenātra pretakriyā vivakṣitā | nandādiśv eva madhye duṣṭatamāny āha sa eva |
ekādaśyāṃ tu nandāyāṃ sinīvālyāṃ bhṛgor dine |
nabhasyasya caturdaśyāṃ kṛttikāsu tripuṣkare ||
atra śrāddhaṃ na kurvītety anuṣaṅgaḥ | sinīvālyāṃ bhṛgor dina iti saṃbandhaḥ |
mahābhārate |
nakṣatreṇa na kurvīta yasmin jāto bhaven naraḥ |
na prauṣṭhapadayoḥ kāryaṃ tathāgneye ca bhārata |
dāruṇeṣu ca sarveṣu pratyare ca vivarjayet ||
[333]        janmanakṣatram atra śrāddhakartuḥ | 'prauṣṭhapadayoḥ' bhādrapadādvaye | 'āgneyaṃ' kṛttikā | ‘dāruṇāni' ādrāśleṣājyeṣṭhāmūlāni | janmanakṣatrāt pañcamaṃ caturdaśaṃ trayoviṃśaṃ ca 'pratyaram' |
jyotiḥparāśaraḥ |
sādhāraṇadhruvogramaitreṣu na śasyate manuṣyāṇāṃ pretakriyā kathaṃcit puṣkare yamalādhiṣṭhe ca |
'sādhāraṇe' kṛttikāviśākhe | 'dhruvāṇi' uttarātrayaṃ rohiṇyaś ca | ‘ugrāṇi’ pūrvātrayaṃ bharaṇī maghā ca | ‘maitrāṇi' mṛgacitrānurādharevatyaḥ | tripuṣkarāṇy uktāni | 'yamalādhiṣṭhaṃ' dhaniṣṭhā | mṛgacitrayor maitreṣu (?) uktatvāt |
vārāhapurāṇe |
caturthāṣṭamage candre dvādaśe ca vivarjayet |
pretakṛtyaṃ vyatīpāte vaidhṛte parighe tathā ||
karaṇe viṣṭisaṃjñe ca śanaiścaradineṣu ca |
trayodaśyāṃ viśeṣeṇa janmatārātraye tathā ||
utpattinakṣatraṃ daśamam ekonaviṃśaṃ ceti janmatārātrayaṃ |
āha kāśyapaḥ |
bharaṇy ādrā maghā śleṣā mūlaṃ tricaraṇāni ca |
pretakṛtye ’tiduṣṭāni dhaniṣṭhādyaṃ ca pañcakam ||
phālgunīdvitayaṃ rohiṇy anurādhā punarvasuḥ |
dve āṣāḍhe viśākhā ca bhāni dvicaraṇāni ca |
etāni kiṃcidduṣṭāni varjayet sati saṃbhave ||
[334] tad evam eṣu pretakṛtyamātrasya pratiṣedhe prāpte kvacid apavādam āha gobhilaḥ |
nandāyāṃ śukravāre ca caturdaśyāṃ trijanmasu |
ekādaśaprabhṛtiṣu naikoddiṣṭaṃ niṣidhyate ||
'trijanmāni' janmatārātrayaṃ |
vaijavāpaḥ |
pretasya sākṣād dagdhasya prāpte tv ekādaśe ’hani |
nakṣatratithivārādi śodhanīyaṃ na kiṃcana ||
yugamanvādisaṃkrāntidarśe pretakriyā yadi |
daivād āpatitā tatra na nakṣatrādiśodhanaṃ ||
iti pretakriyāsu niṣiddhakālāḥ |
atha piṇḍadāne niṣiddhakālāḥ |
tatrāha pulastyaḥ |
ayanadvitaye śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaddvitaye tathā |
yugādiṣu ca sarvāsu piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte || iti |
eteṣu kāleṣu śrāddhaṃ 'piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte' piṇḍadānaṃ vinā kartavyam iti |
matsyapurāṇe |
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyāyāṃ navamyāṃ kārttikasya ca |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ tu śuklāyāṃ saṃkrāntividhinā naraiḥ ||
atra saṃkrāntiṣv ity anenaivāyanādīnām api lābhe punas tadabhidhānaṃ tatkālakṛtasya piṇḍadānasyātininditatvadyotanārthaṃ | saṃkrāntividhinety [335] anena piṇḍānarāhityam uktaṃ | vaiśākhasya tṛtīyāyām ityādikaṃ sarvayugādyupalakṣaṇaṃ | "yugādiṣu ca sarvāsu" iti pulastyavacanāt | brahmapurāṇe |
ayanadvitaye śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaddvitaye tathā |
saṃkrāntiṣu ca kartavyaṃ piṇḍanirvapaṇād ṛte ||
vaiśākhasya tṛtīyāyāṃ navamyāṃ kārttikasya tu |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ tu śuklāyāṃ saṃkrāntividhinā naraiḥ ||
trayodaśyāṃ bhādrapade māghe candrakṣaye ’hani |
śrāddhaṃ kāryaṃ pāyasena dakṣiṇāyanavac ca tat ||
yadā ca śrotriyo ’bhyeti gehaṃ vedavid agnivit |
tenaikena tu kartavyaṃ śrāddhaṃ viṣuvaduttamam ||
'viṣuvato viṣuvacchrāddhāt uttamaṃ śreṣṭham | śrotriyāgamananimittakam api śrāddhaṃ prāyaḥpāṭhād apiṇḍam eva bhavitum arhati |
bṛhatpārāśaraḥ |
yugādiṣu maghāyāṃ ca viṣuvaty ayane tathā |
bharaṇīṣu ca kurvīta piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ na hi ||
maghāyuktāṃ bhādrapadāparapakṣatrayodaśīm adhikṛtya devīpurāṇe |
tatrāpi mahatī pūjā kartavyā pitṛdaivate |
ṛkṣe piṇḍapradānaṃ tu jyeṣṭhaputrī vivarjayet ||
'tatrāpi' bhādrapadāparapakṣatrayodaśyām api, 'pitṛdaivate rkṣe' maghānakṣatre jāte khati, ‘mahatī' śrāddhalakṣaṇā pūjā kartavyā | sa tu kartā 'jyeṣṭhaputravān' jīvatprathamaputraś ced bhavet tarhi tatra śrāddhaṃ kurvan piṇḍa[336]pradānaṃ varjayet | piṇḍarahitaṃ śrāddhaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | jyeṣṭhaputrīty anena maghākālikasya trayodaśīkālikasya ubhayasamavāyakālikasya ca piṇḍadānasya jyeṣṭhaputravināśakatvalakṣaṇo doṣaḥ sūcitaḥ |
tatra ca mahābhārate |
saṃkrāntāv upavāsena pāraṇena ca bhārata |
maghāyāṃ piṇḍadānena jyeṣṭhaḥ putro vinaśyati || iti |
jyotiḥpārāśaraḥ |
vivāhe vihite māsāṃs tyajeyur dvādaśaiva hi |
sapiṇḍāḥ piṇḍanirvāpaṃ mauñjībandhe ṣaḍ eva hi ||
asyāpavādaḥ |
mahālaye gayāśrāddhe mātāpitroḥ kṣaye ’hani |
yasya kasyāpi marttyasya sapiṇḍīkaraṇe tathā |
kṛtodvāho ’pi kurvīta piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ sadā || iti |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
piṇḍanirvāparahitaṃ yat tu śrāddhaṃ vidhīyate |
svadhāvācanalopo ’tra vikiraṃs tu na lupyate |
akṣayyadakṣiṇāsvastisaumanasyaṃ yathāsthitam ||
iti piṇḍadāne niṣiddhakālāḥ ||
atredaṃ vicāryate | sapiṇḍīkaraṇottarakālikāni mṛtāhāparapakṣāmāvāsyāṣṭakā- vyatīpātasaṃkrāntyuparāgayugamanukalpādigajacchāyādi[337]yuktāni śrāddhāni pratikālaṃ bhidyante na veti tadartham idaṃ vicāryate | kiṃ kālayuktāni vākyāni karmotpattividhaya utotpanne karmaṇi kālasya vidhaya iti | tatra yady api guṇavidhāne bhāvārthe vidhiḥ vākyārthavidhiś cāpādyate tathāpi viśeṣaṇe vidhiśaktisaṃkramāt lāghavāc ca guṇavidhaya eveti na karmabhedam āpādayanti | ataḥ karmaikyād yugapadbhāviṣv api vaikalpikeṣu kvacid ekasmin kāle ’nuṣṭhānaṃ na sarvasmin | kutas tu yugapadbhāviṣu pṛthaganuṣṭhānam iti kālasamavāye sakṛd ekam eva śrāddhaṃ kartavyam iti | na hi kasyacit kālarūpaguṇānurodhena pradhānāvṛttiḥ saṃmatā | yā tu sāyaṃprātarādikālānurodhena homāvṛttiḥ sā tatkālāvacchinnajīvanarūpanimittāvṛttyeti |
atrocyate | yatra śuddhotpattiḥ saṃnidhāv asti tatra guṇayukteṣu vākyeṣu tatpratyabhijñānād guṇamātravidhir bhavati | atra tu śuddhotpattyabhāvād agatyā viśiṣṭavidhau gauravādyadoṣaḥ | śuddhotpattikalpanāyāṃ tu sarvatra viśiṣṭavidhilopena kvacid guṇād bhedaḥ syāt | ato viśiṣṭavidhitvāt pratikālaṃ śrāddhabhedaḥ | nimittatvāc coparāgādīnāṃ tadbhede spaṣṭo bhedaḥ | atas tatkālāgamane tat tac chrāddham anuṣṭheyam iti sthitam |
athedānīṃ kālasamavāye nimittasamavāye ca saṃdihyate | kiṃ tadā tattatkālikānāṃ tattannimittakānāṃ śrāddhānāṃ madhye kiṃcid ekam evānuṣṭheyam uta sarvāṇi pṛthak pṛthak |
atha tantreṇeti | tatraikadine ’nekaśrāddhaniṣedhāt kiṃcid ekam eveti prāpta ucyate |
ekakāle gatāsūnāṃ bahūnām atha vā dvayoḥ |
tantreṇa śrapaṇaṃ kṛtvā kuryāc chrāddhaṃ pṛthak pṛthak ||
[338]        pūrvakasya mṛtasyādau dvitīyasya tataḥ punaḥ |
tṛtīyasya tataḥ kuryāt saṃnipāteṣv ayaṃ kramaḥ ||
iti bhṛguṇā samāne ’py ahani nānānimittānām anekeṣāṃ śrāddhānām anuṣṭhānasyoktatvāt “dvādaśāhaḥ praśasyate” ityādau nānānimittakānāṃ pretaśrāddhānām ekasminn evāhani anuṣṭhānasya vyutpāditatvāt,
naikaḥ śrāddhadvayaṃ kuryāt samāne ’hani kutracit |
iti asya niṣedhasya śraddhājaḍārabhyamāṇaikanimittaśrāddhavṛddhiviṣayatve sthite sarvāṇy api pṛthag anuṣṭheyānīti pakṣo gṛhyate | yat tu deśakālakartraikyād viśeṣagrahaṇābhāvenāgneyādiyāgāṅgānāṃ prayājādīnāṃ tantram anuṣṭhānam uktaṃ tad ekaprayogavidhigṛhīteṣv aṅgeṣv evocitaṃ na tu svatantreṣu pradhāneṣv api | yas tu kāmyāpūrvārtham anuṣṭhitayor darśapaurṇamāsayor nityāpūrvaprasaṅga uktaḥ so ’pi karmaikye vaktum ucitaḥ | yāv eva hy āgneyādisamudāyau kāmyāpūrvārthau tāv eva nityāpūrvārthau utpattivākyaikyād avagamāt | yas tu tatra bhedavyavahāraḥ sa kāmarūpanimittarūpopādhi-saṃbandhanibandhanapratītikālavyavasthābhedāśrayo na vastubhedāśrayaḥ | apūrvabhedas tu vāstavo ’dhikārabhedāt | ataś cāyam aupādhiko ’py apūrvabhedānuniṣpādīti kṛtvā bhedalakṣaṇo vicāritaḥ |
nanu tattatprakṛtyartharūpopādhibhedoparāgapratītibhedanibandhano vāstavo ’pi yāgadānahomabhāvanānāṃ bhedaḥ pratipāditaḥ |
satyaṃ | kiṃ tu prakṛtyarthoparāgapratyāyitp bhedas tatra bādhakābhāvād vāstavo ’ṅgīkṛtaḥ | prakṛte tūtpattiśāstraikatvāpāditakarmaikyapratirūpabādhaka-sadbhāvād avāstavaḥ |
ato ’tra karmaikyāt kāmyāpūvārtham anuṣṭhitābhyām eva darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ [339] nityānuṣṭhānasiddhau na pṛthak anuṣṭhānaṃ kriyate | bhinne tu karmaṇy anyāpūrvasyānyenāsiddhes tadartham anyasyāpy anuṣṭhānam iti | astu vātra kaścid vāstavo ’pi sūkṣmaḥ karmabhedaḥ | sa tu bhedāntarebhyo vilakṣaṇaḥ | na vasāmānyābhy[up]agataṃ (?) kim apy anyatrārthayitum arhatīti |
atrocyate | karmabhede ’pi deśakālakatrādyekatve sakṛdanuṣṭhānena sarvakarmasiddhir yugapad eva sarvapūrvaniṣpattir jāyate | na gṛhyate hi tadā viśeṣaḥ, idaṃ niṣpannaṃ nedam iti | abhyupagataṃ ca yāgabhede ’pi devataikye tantram anuṣṭhānaṃ | sānnāyye svapnanadītaraṇābhivarṣaṇāmedhya-pratimantraṇeṣu caivam ity atra yugapad anekāmedhyadarśanarūpa-nimittasamavāye pratinimittam ārabdhaṃ mano daridraṃ cakṣur ity etanmantrajaparūpanaimittikāvṛttau prāptāyāṃ viśeṣagrahaṇābhāvāt sakṛn naimittikānuṣṭhānam iti caikadeśe sthitam |
ataḥ śrāddhanimittasamavāye kālasamavāye ca deśaike ca kartraikye devataikye ca viśeṣagrahaṇābhāvāt tantreṇa sarvaśrāddhānuṣṭhānaṃ kartavyam iti | evaṃ ca sati saṃkrāntinimittakam amāvāsyānimittakaṃ vyatīpātanimittakāny etāni tantreṇa kariṣya iti saṅkalpaṃ kuryāt |
iti kālasamavāyanirṇayaḥ |
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaśrīmahādevīyasamastakaraṇādhīśvarasakala-
vidyāviśāradasakalaśrīkaraṇādhipatipaṇḍitaśrīhemādriviracite
caturvargacintāmaṇau pariśeṣakhaṇḍe śrāddhakalpe śrāddhakālanirūpaṇaṃ nāmapañcamo ’dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
[340]
atha ṣaṣṭho’dhyāyaḥ |
yat sārasvatanirjharavyatikaraprārabdhapūrormiṣu
ślāghyante ghanamīnakacchapakulaspardhānibaddhādarāḥ |
āmnāyasmṛtinītiśāstranikarāḥ so ’yaṃ vidhatte ’dhunā
śrāddhe yogyatadanyaviprakathanaṃ hemādrimantrīśvaraḥ ||
atha brāhmaṇaṇa nirūpyante |
atra deśakālānantaraṃ śrāddhaprayoge nirūpaṇīye haviḥprakṣepādhikaraṇa-bhūtāhavanīyasthānīyānāṃ prathamasaṃpādyatvāt dravyādibhyaḥ pūrvaṃ nirūpaṇaṃ kriyate | tatra caturbhir api varṇaiḥ kriyamāṇeṣu śrāddheṣu brāhmaṇānām evāmantraṇīyatvena vihitatvāt ta eva niyojyāḥ |
tatra brāhmaṇapraśaṃsā |
tatra taittirīyaśrutiḥ |
yāvatīr vai devatās tāḥ sarvā vedavidi brāhmaṇe vasanti | tasmād brāhmaṇebhyo vedavidbhyo dive dive namaskuryān nāślīlaṃ kīrtayet | etā eva devatā eva prīṇātīti |
namaskāro ’tra pūjopalakṣaṇārthaḥ |
śatapathaśrutir api |
dvayā vai devāḥ | devā ahaiva devā atha ye brāhmaṇāḥ śuśruvāṃso ’nūcānās te manuṣyadevās teṣāṃ dvedhā vibhakta eva yajña āhutaya eva devānāṃ dakṣiṇā manuṣyadevānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ śuśruvuṣām anūcānānām āhutibhir eva devān prīṇāti dakṣiṇābhir manuṣya[341]devān brāhmaṇāṃcchuśruvuṣo ’nūcānāṃs ta enam ubhaye devāḥ prītāḥ sudhāyāṃ dadhatīti |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
brāhmaṇā daivataṃ bhūmau brāhmaṇā divi daivatam |
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ paraṃ nāsti bhūtaṃ kiṃcij jagattraye ||
adaivaṃ devataṃ kuryuḥ kuryur daivam adaivatam |
brāhmaṇā hi mahābhāgāḥ pūjyante satataṃ dvijāḥ ||
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ samutpannā devāḥ pūrvam iti śrutiḥ |
brāhmaṇebhyo jagat sarvaṃ tasmāt pūjyatamā dvijāḥ ||
eṣām aśnanti vaktreṇa devatāḥ pitaras tathā |
ṛṣayaś ca tathā nāgā kiṃ bhūtam adhikaṃ tataḥ ||
yājñavalkāḥ |
tapas taptvāsṛjad brahmā brāhmaṇān vedaguptaye |
tṛpyarthaṃ pitṛdevānāṃ dharmasaṃrakṣaṇāya ca ||
matsyapurāṇe |
nāsti viprasamo devo nāsti viprasamo guruḥ |
nāsti viprāt paraḥ śatrur nāsti viprāt paro vidhiḥ ||
viṣṇudharmottare bhagavadvākyam |
brāhmaṇaiḥ pūjitair nityaṃ pūjito ’haṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
nirbhartsitaiś ca nirbhartsyas tair ahaṃ sarvakarmasu ||
[342]        viprāḥ parā gatir mahyaṃ yas tān pūjayate nṛpa |
tam ahaṃ svena rūpeṇa prapaśyāmi yudhiṣṭhira ||
skandapurāṇe devīṃ prati īśvaravacanam |
śrutīnām ākarā hy ete ratnānām iva sāgarāḥ |
viprā viprādhipamukhi pūjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
“viprādhipamukhi” candramukhīti devyāḥ saṃbodhanam |
yatra vedavido viprā na prāśnanty uttamaṃ haviḥ |
na tatra devā deveśi havir aśnanti karhicit ||
api nārāyaṇo ’nanto brahmā skando ’nalaḥ śikhī |
tat karma nābhinandanti yatra viprā na pūjitāḥ ||
yeṣāṃ prasādāt sulabham āyur dharmaḥ sukhaṃ dhanam |
śrīr yaśaḥ svargavāsaś ca tān viprān arcayed budhaḥ ||
iti brāhmaṇapraśaṃsā |
atha brāhmaṇalakṣaṇam |
atrāhur ācāryāḥ |
brāhmaṇatvajātyāśrayo brāhmaṇa iti | atra yady apy upadeśānapekṣākṣi-saṃnipātamātrānubhavanīyakṣatriyavaiśyādivyāvṛttabrāhmaṇatvābhimata-vyaktimātrānuvṛttadravyaguṇakarmasaṃsthānādikṛtavailakṣaṇyābhāvān manuṣyatvāvāntarasāmānyābhivyañjakasthūlatarakāraṇāsambhavas tathāpi viśuddhamātāpitṛsantatijatvopadeśasāpekṣabuddhiviśeṣasamudbhāsita-sūkṣmatarasauśīlyādyātmakabrāhmaṇatvābhivyañjakasaṃbhavaḥ parīkṣakāparīkṣakasākṣiko [343] ’sty eva | abhyupagataś ca ratnādāv upadeśasāpekṣo ’pi sāmānyābhivyañjakāvabhāsaḥ sarvaiḥ |
nanu viśuddhamātāpitṛsantatijatvaṃ nāma brāhmaṇajātīyamātāpitṛjatvam eva tathā copadeśe jñaptipratibandhakam anyonyāśrayatvam āpadyate | upadeśārthajñānādhīnaṃ brāhmaṇatvajñānaṃ brāhmatvajñānādhīnam upadeśārthajñānam iti |
maivaṃ | ayam atropadeśārthaḥ | brāhmaṇatvasāmānyavān brāhmaṇaḥ | brāhmaṇatvavyavasthāpakaṃ tu tajjanyatvam iti |
nanu tarhi yatra brāhmaṇaikajanyatvaṃ vyavatiṣṭhata ity evaṃvidhe vyavasthāpakopadeśe vyavasthāpakadharmagarbhīkṛtasya brāhmaṇatvasya vyavasthājñānābhāvāt tathābhūtasya vyavasthājñānasādhanatvāsaṃbhavād anyonyātmāśrayānavasthādyāpātaḥ |
maivaṃ | astu nāma vyavasthājñāpakatve ’nyonyāśrayādikaṃ kārakahetutve tu na tasya prasaṅgaḥ, sattāmātreṇa kārakatvāt | kārakasya cāyam upadeśaḥ | na jñāpakasya | tad evaṃ vyavasthākārakopadeśānantaraṃ brāhmaṇatvābhivyañjaka- pūrvoktadharmaviśeṣāvabhāsakṛto brāhmaṇatvasāmānyāvagamo ’sty eva |
nanv evaṃ satīdṛgvidhopadeśaśravaṇavato narasyādṛṣṭacaramavijñāta-kulanāmānam upalakṣitācāraveṣaviśeṣaṃ brāhmaṇapuruṣam upalabhyākṣisaṃnipātamātreṇa nirvicikitsā brāhmaṇatvabuddhir utpadyeta | tathā ca praśnaparīkṣe na syātāṃ | atha brāhmaṇatvavyavasthāpakasya viśuddhamātāpitṛsantatijattvalakṣaṇasyopādheḥ tadvyaktiniṣṭhatayānavagamāt tagatasāmānyānabhivyaktiḥ, tarhi vyavasthāpakasyopādheḥ jñāpakatvāpāte sati itaretarāśrayādikam āpatitam eva |
maivaṃ | upadeśadaśātikrāntam idaṃ liṅgatvena kārakasya jñāpakatvam aḍuṣṭam eva |
athocyate | anādāv iha saṃsāre duvāre makaradhvaje kule ca kāminīmūle kā jātiparikalpaneti |
tan na, pratikūlaṃ kulābhimānibhiḥ [344] puruṣais tādṛgvidhābhiḥ strībhiś ca rakṣyamāṇāni kulāny upaplutāny evānuvartamānāni dṛśyante | prapañcitaś cāyam arthas teṣu teṣu śāstreṣv ity uparamyate | na ca yajanayājanādhyayanādhyāpanadānapratigrahācārasaṃpadahiṃsāsatya- śaucādisaṃpannatvaṃ brāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ | asya hi yājanādhyāpana-pratigrahādīnām anyatamavaikalpe ’pi adhyayanayajanadānādimātrasadbhāve brāhmaṇajātīye brāhmaṇavyavahārasyānupaplavān na samuccitasya lakṣaṇatvaṃ | na cāsamuccitasya, kṣatriyādiṣu sattvāt | na ca yājanādhyāpanapratigraha-samuccayasya brāhmaṇalakṣaṇatvaṃ | pratigrahavarjanavratavato ’pratiṣiddhavrate brāhmaṇatvaṃ na syāt | etattrayavataḥ kṣatriyatvenābhimatasya ca brāhmaṇatvaṃ syāt | kiṃ ca vṛttyarthaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ pratigṛhṇīyād yājayed adhyāpayed veti brāhmaṇasya sataḥ pratigrahādayo vihitāḥ | paścādbhāvino na pūrvasiddhabrāhmaṇasaṃjñāpravṛttinimittaṃ bhavitum arhanti, pūpavad iti cen na, ananyagatinyāyalabhyānām arthānām anyagatimadarthaviṣayatvābhāvāt |
yat tu vahnipurāṇe vṛttāny eva brāhmaṇalakṣaṇam ity uktam,
na jātir na kulaṃ rājan na svādhyāyaḥ śrutaṃ na vā |
kāraṇāni dvijatvasya vṛttam eva hi kāraṇaṃ ||
kiṃ kulaṃ vṛttahīnasya kariṣyati durātmanaḥ |
kṛmayaḥ kiṃ na jāyante kusumeṣu sugandhiṣu ||
naikam ekāntato grāhyaṃ pāvanaṃ hi viśāṃpate |
vṛttam anviṣyatāṃ tāta rakṣobhiḥ kiṃ na padyate ||
bahunā kim adhītena naṭasyeva durātmanaḥ |
tenādhītaṃ śrutaṃ vāpi yaḥ kriyām anutiṣṭhati ||
[345]        kapālasthaṃ yathā toyaṃ candane ca yathā payaḥ |
dūṣyaṃ syāt sthānadoṣeṇa vṛttahīnaṃ tathā śrutaṃ ||
caturvedo ’pi durvṛttaḥ śūdrād alpataraḥ smṛtaḥ |
tasmād viddhi mahārāja vṛttaṃ brāhmaṇalakṣaṇam ||
satyaṃ damas tapo dānam ahiṃsendriyanigrahaḥ |
dṛśyante yatra rājendra sa brāhmaṇa iti smṛtaḥ ||
yac ca brahmāṇḍapurāṇe uktam,
jātakarmādibhir yas tu saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ śuciḥ |
vedādhyayanasaṃpannaḥ ṣaṭṣu karmasv avasthitaḥ ||
satyavāk vighasāśī tu śīlavāṃś ca gurupriyaḥ |
satyavratī satyaparaḥ sa vai brāhmaṇa ucyate |
tapaḥśrute ca yoniś cāpy etat brāhmaṇakāraṇaṃ ||
satyaṃ dānaṃ tapo homa ānṛśaṃsyaṃ kṣamā nṛṇāṃ |
tapaś ca dṛśyate yatra sa brāhmaṇa iti smṛtaḥ ||
'ṣaṭṣu karmasu' yajanayājanādhyayanādhyāpanadānapratigraheṣu |
'vighasāśī' avaśyabhojanīyātithiprabhṛtibhuktāvaśiṣṭabhojī | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
devebhyaś ca pitṛbhyaś ca bhṛtyebho (?) 'tithibhiḥ saha |
dattvā śiṣṭaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte tam āhur vighasāśinam || iti |
yac ca yama-śātātapābhyām uktam,
[346]        tapo dharmo dayā dānaṃ satyaṃ jñānaṃ śrutir ghṛṇā |
vidyā vinayam asteyam etad brāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ ||
yac ca vasiṣṭhenoktam,
yogas tapo dayā dānaṃ satyaṃ śaucaṃ śrutaṃ ghṛṇā |
vidyā vijñānam āstikyam etad brāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ ||
yan na santaṃ na vāsantaṃ nāśrutaṃ na bahuśrutaṃ |
na suvrataṃ na durvṛttaṃ veda kaścit sa brāhmaṇaḥ ||
yacca yamenoktam,
ahiṃsānirato nityaṃ juhvāno jātavedasaṃ |
svadāranirato dātā sa vai brāhmaṇa ucyate ||
śrutaṃ prajñānugaṃ yasya prajñā caiva śrutānugā |
asaṃbhinnāryamaryādaḥ sa vai brāhmaṇa ucyate ||
āśiṣo'rthārthaṃ pūjāṃ ca prasaṅgāṃs tu karoti yaḥ |
nivṛtto lobhamohābhyāṃ taṃ devā brāhmaṇaṃ viduḥ ||
'āśiṣaḥ' aśīrvādān | 'arthārthaṃ' dhanalābhāya | 'pūjāṃ' satkriyāṃ, devatāpūjāṃ vā | 'prasaṅgāḥ' tās tāḥ prastāvanāḥ, teṣu teṣu viṣayeṣu āsaktīr vā |
satyaṃ dānaṃ kṣamā śīlam ānṛśaṃsyaṃ dayā ghṛṇā |
dṛśyante yatra loke ’smin taṃ devā brāhmaṇaṃ viduḥ ||
'ghṛṇā' kutsitaviṣaye vivādaḥ |
tad etat sarvaṃ na brāhmaṇatvajāti[347]nirākaraṇena śīlavṛttāder eva brāhmaṇaśabdapravṛttinimittatvapratipādanaparam, kiṃ tu havyakavyabhoktṛtvārhatāpādakabrāhmaṇagataprāśastyapratipādanaparam | apaśavo ’nye go’śvebhyaḥ, paśavo go.aśvā ity etad yathā go’śvebhyaḥ prāśastyapratipādanaparaṃ |
ata evāha baudhāyanaḥ |
vidyā tapaś ca yoniś ca etad brāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ |
