vidyātapobhyāṃ yo hīno jātibrāhmaṇa eva saḥ ||
iti vidyādiyuktatvaṃ havyakavyārhapraśastabrāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ | vidyādirahite tu yad brāhmaṇatvaṃ tad brāhmaṇatvarūpajātiyogamātram ity arthaḥ | 'jātibrāhmaṇa eva' ity atra jātyaiva sa brāhmaṇa ity anvayavigrahau | ata eva vidyādiyogo brāhmaṇānāṃ pātratva eva smṛtipurāṇeṣu hetur ukto na brāhmaṇaśabdapravṛttāv iti |
iti brāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ |
atha pātrībhūtabrāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
na vidyayā kevalayā tapasā vāpi pātratā |
yatra vṛttam ime cobhe tad dhi pātraṃ prakīrttitaṃ ||
aṅgasmṛtipurāṇanyāyamīmāṃsāsahitā vedā vidyā |
tapolakṣaṇam āha devalaḥ |
atha tapovidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | tad yathā vratopavāsaniyamaiḥ śarīrottāpanaṃ tapa iti | vratādīny api tenaiva darśitāni | tatra brahmacaryaṃ satyavacanaṃ madhumāṃsavarjanaṃ maunam ayācanam ṛtukālābhigamanam ity evamādīni [348] vratāni | anaśanam upavāsaḥ | svādhyāyaśīlatvaṃ, mātāpitṛgobrāhmaṇaśuśrūṣā, puṇyakṣetrābhigamanaṃ, tīrthāvagāhanaṃ, śītoṣṇavarṣātapāvaśyāyānām apratīkāraḥ, kaṇṭakaśarkarādarbhadāruphalaka- balvajasikatābhūmiśayanaṃ, hemantaśiśirayor ārdrapaṭaprāvaraṇaṃ, varṣāsu jalaśayyā, grīṣmavasantayoḥ pañcāgnimadhyādhiṣṭhānena divasāvasthānaṃ, guḍalavaṇagorasasnehadhānyādīnāṃ svādūnām anupabhojanaṃ, phalamūlaśāka-puṣpakapiṇyākabhojanam iti niyamāḥ | nijavarṇāśramavihitadharmānuṣṭhānaṃ tapaḥ |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
svādhāyavān niyamavāṃs tapasvī jñānavic ca yaḥ |
kṣānto dāntaḥ satyavādī vipraḥ pātram ihocyate ||
'svādhāyaḥ' vedaḥ, tasyādhyāpanārthāvabodhavyākhyānādibhir yuktaḥ 'svādhāyavān' | 'niyamavān' yamaniyamasaṃpannaḥ | te ca |
brahmacaryaṃ dayā kṣāntir dhyānaṃ satyam akalkatā |
ahiṃsāsteyamādhurye damaś ceti yamāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
snānamaunopavāsejyāsvādhāyopasthanigrahāḥ |
niyamā guruśuśrūṣā śaucākrodhāpramādatāḥ ||
iti yājñavalkyoktā vijñeyā | tatra niṣiddhastrīsaṅgavarjanaṃ 'brahmacaryaṃ | prāṇimātravipatpratīkārecchā 'dayā' | dvandvaduḥkhasahiṣṇutā 'kṣāntiḥ' | abhimatadevānucintanaṃ 'dhyānaṃ' | bhūtahitāya yathārthavacanaṃ 'satyaṃ' | dambharāhityaṃ 'akalkatā' | avihitavadhavarjanaṃ 'ahiṃsā' | ahāryaparasvānapahāraḥ 'asteyaṃ' | paraprītikaraveṣabhāṣaṇaceṣṭāvattvaṃ 'mādhuryaṃ' | svairavṛttinivṛttiprayojanakaṃ parimitagrāsabhojanādibhir upāyair indriyamadāpaharaṇaṃ 'damaḥ | [349] nityanaimittikaṃ jalanimajjanādirūpaṃ 'snānaṃ' | niṣiddhavākpravṛttivarjanaṃ 'maunaṃ' | nityanaimittikam ahorātrānaśanaṃ 'upavāsaḥ' | āvaśyakaṃ devatām uddiśya svakīyadravyatyāgaḥ 'ijyā' | vedādhyayanaṃ 'svādhyāyaḥ' | avihitaprakārakaśukravimocanavyāpāra-vaimukhyaṃ 'upasthanigrahaḥ' | guror anukūlācaraṇaṃ 'guruśuśrūṣā' | bāhyābhyantaramalāpaharaṇaṃ 'śaucam' | hananabhartsanaśapanādikrūra-karmārambhanidānībhūtāntarvikāraviśeṣavarjanaṃ 'akrodhaḥ' | vihite niṣiddhārthānanuṣṭhāne ca sāvadhānatvaṃ 'apramādatāḥ' iti | jñānasyātmatattvajñānasya vedena lābhāt 'jñānavit' |
mahābhārate |
sāṅgāṃs tu caturo vedān yo ’dhīte vai dvijarṣabha |
ṣaḍbhyo nivṛttaḥ karmabhyas taṃ pātram ṛṣayo viduḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kiṃcid vedamayaṃ pātraṃ śūdrānnaṃ yasya nādare |
śūdradravyapratigrahasaṃpāditam annaṃ 'śūdrānnam' | sākṣācchradrānnasya varjane hi kā nāmātipātratā |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
brahmacārī bhavet pātraṃ pātraṃ vedasya pāragaḥ |
pātrāṇām uttamaṃ pātraṃ śūdrānnaṃ yasya nodare ||
vyāsaḥ |
kiṃcid vedamayaṃ pātraṃ kiṃcit pātraṃ tapomayaṃ |
asaṃkīrṇaṃ tu yat pātraṃ tat pātraṃ paramaṃ smṛtaṃ ||
yonisaṃkaravṛttisaṃkarapatitādisaṃsargarahitaṃ 'asaṃkīrṇam' |
ṛṣyaśṛṅgaḥ |
[350]        kiṃcid vedamayaṃ pātraṃ kiṃcit pātraṃ tapomayaṃ |
atithiparaṃ yat pātraṃ tat pātraṃ paramaṃ viduḥ ||
atithilakṣaṇaṃ vakṣyate |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
kṣāntyaspṛhā damaḥ satyaṃ dānaṃ śīlaṃ tapaḥ śrutaṃ |
etad aṣṭāṅgam uddiṣṭaṃ paramaṃ pātralakṣaṇaṃ ||
śīlalakṣaṇam upariṣṭād vakṣyate |
devalaḥ |
triśuklaḥ kṛśavṛttiś ca ghṛṇāluḥ sakalendriyaḥ |
vimukto yonidoṣebhyo brāhmaṇaḥ pātram ucyate ||
trīṇi kulavidyāvṛttāni 'śuklāni' viśuddhāni yasyāsau ‘triśuklaḥ’ | ‘ghṛṇāluḥ’ dayāluḥ | 'sakalendriyaḥ' ahīnādhikāṅgaḥ | 'yonidoṣair vimuktaḥ' yonisaṃkarakāraṇānām akartā |
vṛddhavasiṣṭho ’py āha |
svādhāyāḍhyaṃ yonimantaṃ praśāntaṃ
vaitānajñaṃ pāpabhīruṃ bahujñam |
strīṣu kṣāntaṃ dhārmikaṃ gośaraṇyaṃ
vrataiḥ klāntaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ pātram āhuḥ ||
'strīṣu kṣāntaḥ' strīviṣaye sahiṣṇur alolupa ity arthaḥ | 'gośaraṇyaḥ’ gośuśrūṣāparaḥ | ‘vrataiḥ klāntaḥ' vratānuṣṭhānena saṃśoṣitaśarīrendriyaḥ |
yamo ’py āha |
vidyāyukto dharmaśīlaḥ praśāntaḥ
kṣānto dāntaḥ satyavādī kṛtajñaḥ |
vṛttiglāno gohito gośaraṇyo
dātā yajvā brāhmaṇaḥ pātrarūpaḥ ||
'vṛttiglānaḥ' śiloñchādibhiḥ kṣāmaśarīraḥ | ‘yajvā' yāgānuṣṭhānavān |
iti pātrībhūtabrāhmaṇalakṣaṇaṃ |
atha pātrasaṃjñābhidhānaprasaṅgād uttamamadhyamādhamavibhāga-jñānopayoginyaḥ kulaśīlavṛttādiyogaviyoganibandhanā anyā api brāhmaṇānāṃ saṃjñāḥ kathyante |
tatrāha devalaḥ |
mātraś ca brāhmaṇaś caiva śrotriyaś ca tapaḥparaḥ |
anūcānas tathā bhrūṇa ṛṣikalpo ṛṣir muniḥ ||
ity ete ’ṣṭau samuddiṣṭā brāhmaṇāḥ prathamaṃ śrutau |
teṣāṃ tapaḥparaḥ śreṣṭho vidyāvṛttaviśeṣataḥ ||
vidyāvṛttātiśayakṛtād vaiśiṣṭhyād dhetor ity arthaḥ |
uddeśakrameṇaivaiteṣāṃ lakṣaṇāny āha sa eva |
brāhmaṇānāṃ kule jāto jātimātro yadā bhavet |
anupetaḥ kriyāhīno mātra ity abhidhīyate ||
'anupetaḥ' upanayanarahitaḥ |
ekādaśam atikramya vedasyācāravān dvijaḥ |
sa brāhmaṇa iti prokto nivṛttaḥ satyavān ghṛṇī ||
'ekādaśātikramaḥ' vedasya kiṃcinnyūnasyādhyayanaṃ | 'nivṛttaḥ' śāntaḥ |
ekāṃ śākhāṃ sakalāṃ vā ṣaḍbhir aṅgair adhītya vā |
ṣaṭkarmanirato vipraḥ śrotriyo nāma dharmavit ||
yamo ’pi |
oṃkārapūrvikās tisraḥ sāvitrīṃ yaś ca vindati |
caritabrahmacaryaś ca sa vai śrotriya ucyate ||
‘oṃkārapūrvikāḥ,’ mahāvyāhṛtīr iti śeṣaḥ |
[352]        brahmavaivarte ’pi śrotriyalakṣaṇam uktam |
janmanā brāhmaṇo jñeyaḥ saṃskārair dvija ucyate |
vidyayā cāpi vipratvaṃ tribhiḥ śrotriya ucyate ||
devalaḥ |
vedavedāṅgatattvajñaḥ śuddhātmā pāpavarjitaḥ |
śiṣṭaḥ śrotriyatāṃ prāptaḥ so ’nūcāna iti smṛtaḥ ||
anūcānaguṇopeto yajñasvādhyāyayantritaḥ |
bhrūṇa ity ucyate śiṣṭaiḥ śeṣabhojī jitendriyaḥ ||
vaidikaṃ laukikaṃ caiva sarvaṃ jñānam avāpya yaḥ |
aśramastho vanī nityam ṛṣikalpa iti smṛtaḥ ||
'laukikam' arthārjanādijñānaṃ |
ūrddharetās tapasy ugro niyatāśī na saṃśayam |
śāpānugrahayoḥ śaktaḥ satyasaṃdho bhaved ṛṣiḥ ||
satyā saṃdhā abhisaṃdhir abhiprāyo yasya asau 'satyasaṃdhaḥ' satyasaṃkalpa ity arthaḥ |
nivṛttaḥ sarvatattvajñaḥ kāmakrodhavivarjitaḥ |
dhyānastho niḥkriyo dātā triśuklaś ca smṛto muniḥ ||
'nivṛttaḥ' niṣiddhakāmyakarmabhyaḥ | 'niḥkriyaḥ' arthārjanādikriyārahitaḥ |
evam anvayavidyābhyāṃ vṛttena ca samucchritāḥ ||
triśuklā nāma viprendrāḥ pūjyante savanādiṣu |
saṃvartaḥ |
[353]        utpattiṃ pralayaṃ caiva bhūtānām agatiṃ gatim |
vetti vidyām avidyāṃ ca sa bhaved vedapāragaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
adhyāpayet tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ kṛtopanayanaṃ dvijaḥ |
sarahasyaṃ ca sakalaṃ vedaṃ bharatasattama |
tam ācāryaṃ mahābāho pravadanti manīṣiṇaḥ ||
śraha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ācinoti ca śāstrāṇi ācāre sthāpayaty api |
svayam ācarate yas tu tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate ||
manuḥ |
alpaṃ vā bahu vā yasya śrutasyopakaroti yaḥ |
tam apīha guruṃ vidyāt śrutopakriyayā tathā ||
sarvaṃ vā yadi vāpy ardhaṃ pādaṃ vā yadi vākṣaraṃ |
sakāśād yasya gṛhṇīyān niyataṃ tasya gauravam || iti |
śivadharme |
saṃskṛtaiḥ prākṛtair vākyaiḥ yaḥ śiṣyam anurūpataḥ |
deśabhāṣādyupāyaiś ca bodhayet sa guruḥ smṛtaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
ekadeśaṃ tu vedasya vedāṅgāny api vā guruḥ |
yo ’dhyāpayet svavṛttyarthaṃ upādhyāyaḥ sa ucyate ||
[354]         vyāsaḥ |
agnyādheyaṃ pākayajñam agniṣṭomādikaṃ tathā |
yaḥ karoti vṛto nūnam ṛtvijaṃ taṃ pracakṣate ||
śivadharmottare |
carācarasya kartāram anantavibhavaṃ vibhum |
ātmānam eva yo vetti sa cātmajña iti smṛtaḥ ||
śivadharmaratāḥ śāntāḥ śivajñānaparāyaṇāḥ |
śivadharmapravaktāro viprāḥ syuḥ śivayoginaḥ ||
manuḥ |
sāntānikaṃ yakṣyamāṇam adhvagaṃ sārvavedasaṃ |
gurvarthaṃ pitṛmātrarthaṃ svādhyāyārthyupatāpinaṃ |
navaitān snātakān vidyāt brāhmaṇān dharmabhikṣukān ||
‘sāntānikaḥ’ santānaprayojanaḥ vivāhārthīty arthaḥ | 'adhvagaḥ' atra dharmārthe pracalitaḥ | 'sārvavedasaḥ' sarvasvadakṣiṇayajñakṛt| 'upatāpī' vyādhipīḍitaḥ |
ādityapurāṇe |
akrodhanā dharmaparāḥ śāntāḥ śamadame ratāḥ |
nispṛhāś ca mahārāja te viprāḥ sādhavaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
saurapurāṇe |
gaṅgāyamunayor madhye madhyadeśaḥ prakīrtitaḥ |
tatrotpannā dvijā ye vai sādhavas te prakīrtitāḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
[355]        aṣṭavarṣā tu yā dattā śrutaśīlasamanvitā |
sā gaurī tatsuto yas tu sa gauraḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
kāṇḍam eṣāṃ kulavyākhyā parvāṇy āhur yugāni tu |
yas tu tan māpayet rakṣet sa kulīna iti śrutiḥ ||
devalaḥ |
dharmādharmavibhāgajño niviṣṭo vedaśāsane |
kriyālajjākṣamādhīmān āryo mleccho viparyaye ||
yamaḥ |
śrutvā spṛṣṭvā ca dṛṣṭvā ca bhuktvā ghrātvā ca yo naraḥ |
na hṛṣyati glāyati vā sa vijñeyo jitendriyaḥ ||
na pāṇipādacapalo na netracapalo dvijaḥ |
na ca vākcapalaś caiva iti śiṣṭasya lakṣaṇaṃ ||
iti praśastabrāhmaṇasaṃjñāḥ |
athābrāhmaṇāḥ |
āha śātātapaḥ |
abrāhmaṇās tu ṣaṭ proktā ṛṣiḥ śātātapo ’bravīt |
ādyo rājabhṛtas teṣāṃ dvitīyaḥ krayavikrayī ||
tṛtīyo bahuyājyaḥ syāt caturtho grāmayājakaḥ |
pañcamas tu bhṛtas teṣāṃ grāmasya nagarasya ca ||
anāgatāṃ tu yaḥ pūrvāṃ sādityāṃ caiva paścimām |
[356]        nopāsīta dvijaḥ saṃdhyāṃ sa ṣaṣṭho ’brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
vyāsaḥ |
brahmabījasamutpanno mantrasaṃskāravarjitaḥ |
jātimātropajīvī ca bhaved abrāhmaṇas tu saḥ ||
manuḥ |
nānṛg brāhmaṇo bhavati na vaṇik na kuśīlavaḥ |
na śūdraveṣaṇaṃ kurvan na steno na cikitsakaḥ ||
'anṛk' ṛgvarjitaḥ, vedaśūnya ity arthaḥ | 'kuśīlavaḥ' gītanṛtyavṛttiḥ |
devalaḥ |
kūpamātrodakagrāme vipraḥ saṃvatsaraṃ vasan |
śaucācāraparibhraṃśād brāhmaṇyād dhi pramucyate ||
atha durbrāhmaṇāḥ |
atrāha hārītaḥ |
pakṣimīnamṛgaghnā ye sarpakacchapaghātinaḥ |
nānājantuvadhāsaktāḥ proktā durbrahmaṇā hi te ||
tathā |
atha vedaś ca vedī ca triyugaṃ yasya sīdati |
sa vai durbrahmaṇaḥ proko brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ ||
'vedaḥ' vedādhyayanaṃ | 'vedī' vedasādhanakā darśapūrṇamāsādayo yāgāḥ | 'triyugaṃ' prapitāmahaprabhṛtipitṛparyantaṃ mithunatrayaṃ yāvat, ‘sīdati' avasīdati ucchidyata ity arthaḥ |
[357]        āha yamaḥ |
yasya vedaś ca vedī ca vicchidyete tripauruṣam |
sa vai durbrāhmaṇo nāma yaś caiva vṛṣalīpatiḥ ||
atha vṛṣalīpatiprabhṛtayaḥ |
atra vṛṣalīpatiśabdena śūdrāpatir eva yogasāmarthyāl labhyate | tasya ca nindā trayodaśavidhaputraprakaraṇe vistareṇa vivṛtā | pāribhāṣikaṃ tu vṛṣaṣalīpatim āhośanāḥ |
vandhyā ca vṛṣalī jñeyā vṛṣalī ca mṛtaprajā |
aparā vṛṣalī jñeyā kumārī yā rajasvalā ||
yas tv etām udahet kanyāṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
aśraddheyam apāṅkteyaṃ taṃ vidyād vṛṣalīpatim |
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
vṛṣo hi bhagavān dharmas tasya yaḥ kurute tv alaṃ |
vṛṣalaṃ taṃ vidur devāḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtaṃ ||
tathaiva strī vṛṣalī tat patiḥ vṛṣalīpatiḥ | 'alaṃ' vāraṇaṃ yaḥ kurute saḥ 'vṛṣalaḥ' dharmopaghātīty arthaḥ (?) |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
vṛṣalīty ucyate śūdrī tasyā yaś ca patir bhavet |
lālocchiṣṭasya saṃyogāt patito vṛṣalīpatiḥ ||
svaṃ vṛṣaṃ tu parityajya pareṇa tu vṛṣāyate |
vṛṣalī sā tu vijñeyā na śūdrī vṛṣalī bhavet ||
cāṇḍālī bandhakī veśyā rajasthā yā ca kanyakā |
[358]         ūḍhā yā ca svagotrā syāt vṛṣalyaḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ ||
pitur gṛhe tu yā kanyā rajaḥ paśyaty asaṃskṛtā |
patanti pitaras tasyāḥ sā kanyā vṛṣalī bhavet ||
yas tu tāṃ varayet kanyāṃ brāhmaṇo jñānadurbalaḥ |
aśraddheyam apāṅkteyaṃ taṃ vidyād vṛṣalīpatim ||
paradārābhigo mohāt puruṣo ’jñāna ucyate |
sa eva patito jñeyo yaḥ sadā sevate gṛhe ||
yamaḥ |
punarbhuvaḥ patir yas tu sa bhaved didhiṣūpatiḥ |
manuḥ |
parapūrvāpatiṃ dhīrā vadanti didhiṣūpatim |
dvijo ’gre didhiṣūś caiva yasya saiva kuṭumbinī ||
devalas tu |
jyeṣṭhasya bhāryāṃ saṃprāptāṃ sakāmāṃ didhiṣūpatiḥ |
dvijo ’gre didhiṣūś caiva yasya saiva kuṭumbinī ||
vṛddhamanuḥ |
bhrātur mṛtasya bhāryāyāṃ yo ’nurajyeta kāmataḥ |
dharmeṇāpi niyuktāyāṃ sa jñeyo didhiṣūpatiḥ ||
devalaḥ |
gūḍhaliṅgy avakīrṇī syāt yaś ca bhagnavratas tathā |
[359]  'gūḍhaliṅgī' brahmacaryādyāśramopadiṣṭadaṇḍādiliṅgarahitaḥ | 'bhagnavrataḥ' viplutabrahmacaryaḥ |
yamaḥ |
vratī yaḥ striyam abhyeti so ’vakīrṇī nirucyate |
“vratī” brahmacārī |
atha śūdratulyāḥ |
tatra devalaḥ |
anupāsitasaṃdhyā ye nityam asnānabhojanāḥ |
naṣṭaśaucāḥ patanty ete śūdratulyāś ca dharmataḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
udakyānvāsate yeṣāṃ ye ca kecid anagnayaḥ |
kulaṃ cāśrotriyaṃ yeṣāṃ sarve te śūdradharmiṇaḥ ||
‘udakyā' rajasvalā, sā yeṣāṃ snānabhojanadevapūjādikāle samīpe tiṣṭhati |
baudhāyanaḥ |
gorakṣakān vāṇijikāṃs tathā kārukuśīlavān |
viprān vārddhuṣikāṃś caiva rājan śūdravad ācaret ||
‘kāravaḥ' vardhakyādikarmakaravṛttyupajīvinaḥ | 'vārddhuṣikāḥ' vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāḥ |
manuḥ |
gorakṣakān vāṇijikāṃs tathā kārukuśīlavān |
viprān praiṣyān hīnavarṇaiḥ rājan śūdravad ācaret ||
[360] 'praiṣyān hīnavarṇaiḥ' śūdrādibhiḥ praiṣyān |
ye vyapetya svakarmabhyaḥ parapiṇḍopajīvinaḥ |
dvijatvam abhikāṅkṣanti tāṃś ca śūdravad ācaret ||
bhaviṣyapurāṇe |
na yasya vedo na japo na vidyā ca viśāmpate |
sa śūdra iva mantavya ity āha bhagavān manuḥ ||
vaiśvadevena ye hīnā ātithyena bahiṣkṛtāḥ |
sarve te vṛṣalā jñeyāḥ śrutavanto ’pi hi dvijāḥ ||
vaiśvadevagrahaṇaṃ pañcayajñopalakṣaṇārtham |
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
vṛṣo hi bhagavān dharmas tasya yaḥ kurute tv alaṃ |
vṛṣalaṃ taṃ vidur devāḥ sarvadharmabahiṣkṛtam ||
āha devalaḥ |
dvitīyasya pitur yo ’nnaṃ bhukvā pariṇato dvijaḥ |
avareḍha iti jñeyaḥ śūdradharmā sa jātitaḥ ||
'dvitīyasya' pratigrahītuḥ | kretur vā pitur annādinā yaḥ saṃvṛddhaḥ saḥ 'avareḍhaḥ' dattakakrītakādir ity arthaḥ |
anyam apy avareḍham āha manuḥ |
dvitīyena tu yaḥ pitrā savarṇāyāṃ prajāyate |
avareḍha iti jñeyaḥ śūdradharmā sa jātitaḥ ||
atha brāhmaṇabruvādayaḥ |
vyāsaḥ |
[361]        garbhādhānādibhir yuktas tathopanayanena ca |
na karmavān na cādhīte sa bhaved brāhmaṇabruvaḥ ||
saṃvartaḥ |
avratau vaiśyarājanyau śūdraś cābrāhmaṇās trayaḥ |
vedavratavihīnaś ca brāhmaṇo brāhmaṇabruvaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yaś ca kāṣṭhamayo hastī yaś ca carmamayo mṛgaḥ |
yaś ca vipro ’nadhīyānas trayas te nāmadhārakāḥ ||
garbhādhānādibhir yuktas tathopanayanādimān |
na karmavān na vādhīte sa bhaved brāhmaṇabruvaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
śūdrapreṣyo bhṛto rājñā vṛṣalo grāmayājakaḥ |
vadhabandhopajīvī ca ṣaḍ ete brahmabāndhavaḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
ātmārthaṃ yaḥ paced annaṃ na devātithikāraṇāt |
nārhaty asāv api śrāddhaṃ sa cokto brahmarākṣasaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
api vidyākulair yuktā hīnavṛttā narādhamāḥ |
kṣaudreṣu niratā nityaṃ yātudhānāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ |
deśaṃ gotraṃ kulaṃ vidyām annārthaṃ yo nivedayet |
[362]        vaivasvateṣu dharmeṣu vāntāśī saḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
sarvajñā vayam ity evam abhimānaratā narāḥ |
vāntāśinaḥ parityājyāḥ śrāddhe dāne ca lampaṭāḥ ||
atha kuṇḍagolakādayaḥ |
manu-śātātapau |
paradāreṣu jāyete dvau sutau kuṇḍagolakau |
patyau jīvati kuṇḍas tu mṛte bhartari golakaḥ ||
tau tu jātau parakṣetre prāṇināṃ pretya ceha vai |
niyuktau havyakavyāni nāśayetāṃ pradāyinām ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pāradāryam adharmaṃ tu tajjātau kuṇḍagolakau |
aśrāddheyāv abhojyānnau kṣetrajādyās tato ’śubhāḥ ||
āha yamaḥ |
jīvatpitari yo ’nyena jātaḥ kuṇḍaḥ sa ucyate |
tasya yo brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte sa kuṇḍāśī nigadyate ||
parāśaraḥ |
jīvato jārajaḥ kuṇḍo mṛte bhartari golakaḥ |
yas tayor annam aśnāti sa kuṇḍāśīti kathyate ||
kuṇḍagolakāv abhidhāyāha manuḥ |
yas tayor annam aśnāti sa kuṇḍāśy ucyate dvijaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
[363]        catuḥṣaṣṭipalaiḥ śuddhaiḥ kuṇḍaṃ prasthacatuṣṭayaṃ |
bhavet tad yas tu nigiret sa kuṇḍāśī pataty adhaḥ ||
ekasminn eva bhojane yaḥ prasthacatuṣṭayaparimāṇam annaṃ nigiret bhakṣayati sa kuṇḍāśī |
atha kāṇḍapṛṣṭhāḥ |
tatra hārītaḥ |
śūdrāputrāś ca dattā ye ye ca vai krītakāḥ smṛtāḥ |
sarve te maitriṇā proktāḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhā na saṃśayaḥ ||
svakulaṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kṛtvā yo vai parakulaṃ vrajet |
tena duścaritenāsau kāṇḍapṛṣṭho na saṃśayaḥ ||
svakulaṃ kāṇḍam ity uktaṃ tat kṛtvā pṛṣṭhatas tu yaḥ |
kulāntaraṃ vrajed yasmāt kāṇḍapṛṣṭhas tataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
yamo ’py āha |
āpaddatto hy apahnuto yaś ca syād vaiṣṇavīsutaḥ |
ity ete manunā proktāḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhās trayas tathā ||
māsopavāsādivratair viṣṇor ārādhanaparā vidhavā 'vaiṣṇavī', tasyāṃ tadavasthāyāṃ savarṇād utpannaḥ 'vaiṣṇavīsutaḥ’| vistīrṇāpado brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyavaiśyavṛttityāgopadeśānantaram āha nāradaḥ |
tasyām eva tu yo vṛttau brāhmaṇo ramate rasāt |
kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaś cyuto mārgāt so ’pāṅkteyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
vaiśyāpatiḥ kṛṣṇapṛṣṭhaḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭho bhaṭo bhavet |
[364]
atha baiḍālavratikādayaḥ |
manu-viṣṇū |
dharmadhvajī sadā lubdhaḥ chādmiko lokadambhakaḥ |
baiḍālavratiko jñeyo hiṃsraḥ sarvābhisaṃdhakaḥ ||
yasya dharmadhvajo nityaṃ suradhvaja ivocchritaḥ |
pracchannāni ca pāpāni baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam ||
yamaḥ |
yaḥ kāraṇaṃ puraskṛtya vratacaryāṃ niṣevate |
pāpaṃ vratena pracchādya baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam ||
arthaṃ ca vipulaṃ gṛhya hitvā liṅgaṃ nivartayet |
āśramāntaritaṃ rakṣet baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam ||
yo hy anyāyena vipulam arthaṃ saṃgṛhya rājādibhis tadapahāram āśaṅkamānaḥ pūrvāśramaliṅgāni parivartya atimānanīyayatyādyāśramasvīkāreṇa tad dravyaṃ rakṣati tasya tad vrataṃ baiḍālasaṃjñaṃ bhavati |
pratigṛhyāśramaṃ yas tu sthitvā tatra na tiṣṭhati |
āśramasya tu lopena baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam ||
datvodakena kanyāṃ tu kṛtvā caiva pratigraham |
kṛtārtho nārthavān yaś ca baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam ||
yo jalapūrvaṃ yogyāya kanyāṃ pradāya kṛtārthaḥ sann api tām eva bhūyo ’py anyasmai dātum icchan nārthavān akṛtārtha iva bhavati tasya taccaritaṃ baiḍālaṃ vrataṃ | yaś ca niyatakṛtyaparyāptaṃ pratigṛhyāpy asaṃtoṣād akṛtārtham ivātmānaṃ manyate tasyāpi tac caritaṃ baiḍālam |
[365]        yatīnām āśramaṃ gatvā pratyavāsya tu yaḥ punaḥ |
yatidharmavilopena baiḍālaṃ nāma tad vratam ||
adhodṛṣṭir naikṛtikaḥ svārthasādhanatatparaḥ |
śaṭho mithyāvinītaś ca bakavratacaro dvijaḥ ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
bauddhaśrāvakanirgranthāḥ pañcarātravido janāḥ |
kāpālikāḥ pāśupatāḥ pāṣaṇḍā ye ca tadvidhāḥ ||
anāśramī dvijo yaḥ syād āśramī vā nirarthakaḥ |
mithyāśramās tu ye viprā vijñeyāḥ śrāddhadūṣaṇe ||
devalaḥ |
devārcanaparo vipro vittārthī vatsaratrayam |
asau devalako nāma havyakavyeṣu garhitaḥ ||
tathā |
devatāṃśopajīvī ca nāmnā devalako bhavet |
bhavet karaṇasaṃjñaś ca saṅgheṣu vyavahāravān ||
agāradāhī sa jñeyaḥ pretadagdhā hy anekaśaḥ |
'pretadagdhā' pretadāhakaḥ |
sa cāpy agāradāhī syād yo dveṣād veśmadāhakaḥ |
kṣetrasasyāvanīnāñ ca dagdhāro ’raṇyadāhinaḥ ||
mahiṣīty ucyate bhāryā sā caiva vyabhicāriṇī |
[366]        tasyāṃ yo jāyate garbhaḥ sa vai māhiṣakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
mahiṣīty ucyate bhāryā sā caiva vyabhicāriṇī |
tasyāṃ yaḥ kṣamate doṣaṃ sa vai māhiṣakaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
gorasānāṃ ca vikretā govikretā ca vai dvijaḥ |
guḍalavaṇatilānāṃ vikretā duṣṭavikrayī ||
nikṛṣṭotkṛṣṭamadhye yo varṇeṣu anavagrahaḥ |
ācaraty aparācāraṃ varṇasaṃbhedakas tu saḥ ||
ekāko vyasanākrānto dhanya ity ucyate budhaiḥ |
vipra vārddhuṣikaṃ vindyāt tṛṇavṛddhyupajīvinam ||
viṣṇuḥ |
yas tu nindet paraṃ jīvaṃ praśaṃsaty ātmano guṇān |
sa vai vārddhuṣiko nāma sarvakarmasu garhitaḥ ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
samarghaṃ dhānyam ādāya mahārghaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
sa vai vārddhuṣiko nāma brahmavādiṣu garhitaḥ ||
yaḥ sagotrāṃ samārūḍhaḥ sa vipro gotravān bhavet |
rājapreṣakaro nityaṃ brāhmaṇo ’dhikṛtaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
putrācāryaḥ sa vijñeyo grāme yo bālapāṭhakaḥ |
putrād avāptavidyo vā putrācāryo nigadyate ||
[367]        yo bhrāmyati bhramaravad vibhavārthaṃ gṛhe gṛhe |
atilubdhatayā vipro bhrāmarī sa nigadyate |
yas tu duṣṭatapaścaryaḥ sa mūḍho nāma saṃjñayā ||
chandogapariśiṣṭe kātyāyanaḥ |
yaḥ svādhyāyāgnim ālasyād devādīn naibhir iṣṭavān |
nirākattāmarādīnāṃ sa vijñeyo nirākṛtiḥ ||
gautamaḥ |
adhītya vismṛto vedaṃ bhaved vipro nirākṛtiḥ |
devalaḥ |
ātmānaṃ dharmakṛtyaṃ ca putradārāṃś ca pīḍayet |
lābhād yaḥ pracinoty arthaṃ sa kadaryaṃ iti smṛtaḥ ||
uśanā |
rājñaḥ prabodhasamaye ghaṇṭāśilpas tu ghāṇṭikaḥ |
vṛddhagautamaḥ |
ātmānaṃ dharmakṛtyaṃ ca putradārāṃś ca pīḍayet |
mohād yaḥ pitarau bhṛtyān sa kadarya iti smṛtaḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
ye divaṃ bahubhiḥ pāpaiḥ chādayanti narādhamāḥ |
duṣprāpaṇāt tu dehasya durālānā hi te smṛtāḥ ||
‘divaṃ' paralokam, 'chādayanti' tiraskurvanti | 'dehasya duṣpāpaṇāt' duṣṭena karmaṇā nirvahaṇāt ||
yamaḥ |
[368]        paradravyopahartārastenāḥ syur brāhmaṇādhamāḥ |
duṣprāpaṇāt tu dehasya durālānā hi te smṛtāḥ |
ye vyapetāḥ svakarmabhyaḥ te nāstikāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
huṅkāraṃ kāsanaṃ caiva loke yac ca vigarhitam |
anukuryād anubrūyād vāgduṣṭaṃ taṃ vivarjayet ||
atha patitāḥ |
uśanāḥ |
asamānayājakāś ca śrutivikrayikāñ ca ye |
anyapūrvāprajātāś ca patitān manur abravīt ||
‘anyapūrvāḥ' punarbhūprabhṛtayaḥ |
yamaḥ |
śūdrasyādhyāpanād vipraḥ pataty atra na saṃśayaḥ ||
devalaḥ |
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitaś ca yaḥ |
tāv ubhau patitau viprau svādhyāyakrayavikrayāt ||
uktaṃ ca gārgyeṇa |
sodarye tiṣṭhati jyeṣṭhe kuryād yo dārasaṃgraham |
āvasathyaṃ tathādhānaṃ patitas tu tathā bhavet ||
vāyupurāṇe |
ātmārthaṃ yaḥ paced annaṃ na devātithikāraṇāt |
nārhaty asāv api śrāddhaṃ patito brahmarākṣasaḥ ||
[369]
atha ṣaṇḍāḥ |
devalaḥ |
ṣaṇḍako vātajaḥ ṣaṇḍaḥ ṣaṇḍaḥ klībo napuṃsakaḥ |
kīlakaś ceti ṣaṇḍo ’yaṃ ṣaṇḍabhedo vibhāṣitaḥ ||
teṣāṃ strītulyavākśiṣṭaḥ strīdharmāt ṣaṇḍako bhavet |
pumān bhūtvā svaliṅgāni paścāc chindyāt tathaiva ca ||
strī ca puṃbhāvam āsthāya puruṣācāravadguṇā |
vātajo nāma ṣaṇḍaḥ syāt strīṣaṇḍo vāpi nāmataḥ |
astrīliṅgo ’pi ṣaṇḍaḥ syāt ṣaṇḍas tu mlānamehanaḥ ||
sa kīlaka iti jñeyo yaḥ klaibyād ātmanaḥ striyam |
anyena saha saṃyojya paścāt tām eva sevate ||
atha nagnāḥ |
tatrāha parāśaraḥ |
nagnaḥ kāṣāyavastraḥ syān nagnaḥ kaupīnamātradhṛk |
nagnaḥ syān malavadvāsā nagnaś cārdhapaṭāvṛtaḥ ||
rañjakadravyaniryāsaḥ kaṣāyas tena raktaṃ vastraṃ kāṣāyaṃ tadvasāno nagna ity ucyate | guhyapradeśamātrācchādanaparyāptā purataḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ kaṭisūtragrathanopāyena vasanīyā vastracīrikā kaupīnaṃ tatparidhānādhikāritvenānukto gṛhasthādis tanmātradhṛṅ nagnaḥ | adhautavastro rajakādidhautavastrayukto vā malavadvāsāḥ | ekasyaivānavacchinnasya paṭasyārdhaṃ vasāno ’rdhaṃ prāvṛṇvāno 'rdhapaṭāvṛta ity ucyate khaṇḍapaṭavasanā vā |
[370]                bhṛgusmṛti-prabhāsakhaṇḍayoḥ |
nagnaḥ syān malavadvāsā nagnaḥ kaupīnakevalaḥ |
vikaccho ’nuttarīyaś ca dvikakṣo ’vastra eva ca |
nagnaḥ kāṣāyavastraḥ syān nagnaś cārdhapaṭaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
'kaupīnakevalaḥ' kaupīnamātradhṛk | parihitavāsaso guhyapradeśācchādanopayogī prāntaḥkacchaḥ sa yasya vimuktas trikacchaniṣaṅgarahitaḥ | 'anuttarīyaḥ' uttarīyarahitaḥ | kaṭibandhanavastraṃ kakṣā | parihitavastro parihitataduttarārdhena vastrāntareṇa vā tiryaksaṃmilanasaṃkucitavistareṇa kaṭibandhanaṃ dvitīyā kakṣā | tadvān ‘dvikakṣaḥ' | imāṃ ca dvitīyāṃ kakṣām āsurīśabdenāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
paridhānād bahiḥkakṣā nibaddhā hy āsurī bhavet || iti |
brahmavaivartte |
sarveṣām eva bhūtānāṃ trayī saṃvaraṇaṃ yataḥ |
ye vai tyajanti tāṃ mohāt te tu nagnāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
atha parivittyādayaḥ |
āha devalaḥ |
jyeṣṭhe cāpy aniviṣṭe ca jyeṣṭham utkramya yaḥ punaḥ |
vindeta parivittiś ca parivettā ca tāv ubhau ||
'jyeṣṭhe' bhrātari, 'aniviṣṭe' akṛtavivāhe, yadā kanīyān 'vindeta' dārasaṃgrahaṃ kuryāt, tadā jyeṣṭhaḥ 'parivittiḥ' itaraḥ 'parivetteti vacanārthaḥ |
manuḥ |
[371]        dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ kurute yo ’graje sthite |
parivettā sa vijñeyaḥ parivittis tu pūrvajaḥ ||
‘sthite’, dārāgnihotrasaṃyogaṃ vineti śeṣaḥ | agnihotrādyuttarakriyāprayojyam ādhānaṃ vivakṣitaṃ |
uktaṃ ca gāryeṇa |
sodarye tiṣṭhati jyeṣṭhe na kuryād dārasaṃgrahaṃ |
āvasathyaṃ tathādhānaṃ patitas tv anyathā bhavet ||
'āvasathyaṃ' āvasathyādhānaṃ, dāyavibhāgakāle kriyamāṇam aupāsanādhānam iti yāvat | vivāhakāle kriyamāṇasyaupāsanādhānasya na kuryād dārasaṃgraham ity anenaivārthān nirastatvāt | ata eva "aupāsanaṃ samādadhyāt svakāle parivedayann" iti brahmagarbhavacane ’pi svakālaśabdena dāyavibhāgakāla evoktaḥ na tu vivāhakālo 'pīty avagamyate | 'ādhānaṃ' gārhapatyādyādhānaṃ | 'patito bhavet' upapātakī bhaved ity arthaḥ, upapātakaparigaṇanāvacane parivettṛparivittyoḥ kīrtanāt |
laugākṣiḥ |
sodarye tiṣṭhati jyeṣṭhe yo ’gnyādheyaṃ karoti hi |
tayoḥ paryāhito jyeṣṭhaḥ paryadhātā kaniṣṭhakaḥ ||
sodaryagrahaṇād asodarye jyeṣṭhe tiṣṭhati kaniṣṭhasya dārasaṃgrahādau jāte ’pi na pātityadoṣa ity avagamyate | śātātapena tv asodaryaviṣaye doṣābhāvaḥ sākṣād evoktaḥ |
pitṛvyaputrasāpatnaparanārīsuteṣu ca |
jyeṣṭheṣv api ca tiṣṭhatsu bhrātṝṇāṃ tu kanīyasām |
[372]        dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
'pitṛvyaputrāḥ' pitṛbhrātṛsutāḥ | 'sāpatnāḥ' bhinnamātṛjāḥ | 'paranārīsutāḥ' dvyāmuṣyāyaṇaparibhāṣayā svajanakena parakṣetrotpāditā bhrātaraḥ | satsv apy eteṣu jyeṣṭheṣu bhrātṛṣu kaniṣṭhānāṃ pūrvam api dārāgnisaṃyogaṃ kurvatāṃ parivettṛtvasaṃjñako doṣo nāstīty arthaḥ | paranārīsutagrahaṇaṃ dattakāder apy upalakṣaṇārthaṃ | ata evāha yamaḥ |
pitṛvyaputrān sāpatnān paraputrāṃs tathaiva ca |
dārāgnihotradharmeṣu nādharmaḥ parivindane ||
paraputrān dānakrayādinā svapituḥ putratvam āpannān jyeṣṭhān bhrātṝn parivindato nādharmo ’sti | sodare ’pi jyeṣṭhe sati kadācit parivedane kaniṣṭhasya doṣo nāstīty āha śātātapaḥ |
klībe deśāntarasthe ca patite bhikṣuke ’pi vā |
yogaśāstrābhiyukte ca na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
'bhikṣukaḥ' pravrajitaḥ | 'yogaśāstrābhiyuktaḥ' viraktaḥ | kātyāyano ’py āha |
deśāntarasthaklībaikavṛṣaṇān asahodarān |
veśyābhisaktapatitaśūdratulyātirogiṇaḥ ||
jaḍamūkāndhabadhirakubjavāmanakhoḍakān |
ativṛddhān abhāryāṃś ca kṛṣisaktān nṛpasya ca ||
dhanavṛddhiprasaktāṃś ca kāmato ’kāriṇas tathā |
kuhakāṃs taskarāṃś cāpi parivindan na duṣyati ||
[373] ‘ekavṛṣaṇaḥ' rogapravṛddhavṛṣaṇaḥ | 'rogī' acikitsyarogaḥ | 'khoḍaka:' bhagnacaraṇadvayaḥ | 'abhāryān' naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇaḥ | 'kāmato 'kāriṇaḥ' svecchayaiva nityaṃ vivāham akurvāṇāḥ | 'kuhakāḥ' paravañcanāya vṛthodyogaparāḥ | atra ye sarvātmanā vivāhānadhikāriṇaḥ klībādayaḥ teṣu satsu kālaviśeṣam antareṇāpi parivedane na doṣaḥ | ye tu kālāntare saṃbhāvitavivāhādhikārāḥ deśāntarasthaviraktaveśyātisaktaśūdratulyakṛṣisakta- rājaparatantradhanavṛddhiprasaktakāmato’kārikuhakataskarāḥ teṣu satsu vakṣyamāṇakālapratīkṣāṃ kṛtvā parivedanaṃ kurvatāṃ na doṣo ’sti |
tatra deśāntarasthe jyeṣṭhe vaśiṣṭha āha |
aṣṭau daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi jyeṣṭhaṃ bhrātaram aniviṣṭam apratīkṣamāṇaḥ prāyaścittī bhavatīti |
deśāntarād āgatasya vivāhādāv adhikārasaṃbhavād ity abhiprāyaḥ | atra dharmārtham arthārthaṃ vā deśāntaragatasya jyeṣṭhasya dvādaśavarṣaṃ pratīkṣā |
tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
dvādaśaiva tu varṣāṇi jyāyān dharmārthayor gataḥ |
nyāyyaḥ pratīkṣituṃ bhrātā śrūyamāṇaḥ punaḥ punaḥ ||
dharmārthayor iti vidyāyā apy upalakṣaṇārthaṃ | ata eva gautamena naṣṭe bhartarīti prakramya dvādaśavarṣāṇi brāhmaṇasya vidyāsaṃbandheneti bhāryāyā bhartṛpratīkṣākālam uktvābhihitaṃ bhrātari caivaṃ jyāyasi yavīyān kanyāgnyādheyeṣv iti | 'naṣṭhe bhartari' kutra gata ity ajñāte bhartari atyantadūradeśāntaragata iti yāvat | 'vidyāsaṃbandhena' vidyāgrahaṇārthaṃ deśāntaragate bhartari, brāhmaṇabhāryayā dvādaśavarṣāṇi pratīkṣya tatkriyādikaṃ kāryam | [374] evaṃ brāhmaṇaḥ kaniṣṭho jyeṣṭhe bhrātari vidyāgrahaṇārthaṃ deśāntaragate dvādaśavarṣāṇi pratīkṣya vivāhāgyādhāne kuryāt | kāryāntarārthaṃ deśāntaragamane tu aṣṭau daśa veti pakṣadvayam | atredaṃ vivakṣitam | deśāntaragatasya svābhimataṃ kāryaṃ nirvartya pratyāgamanayogye kārye 'py anāgatasya jīvitasaṃdehe sati vivāhasaṃbhāvanānivṛttau taduttarakālaṃ kaniṣṭhena dārāgnihotrasaṃyoge kriyamāṇe na parivettṛtvarūpo doṣo bhavatīti | evam eva (?) viraktaveśyātisaktādiṣv api ca kiyantam api kālaṃ tathaivāvasthiteṣu tatsvabhāvatvāvadhāraṇena vivāhasaṃbhāvanānivṛttau taduttarakālaṃ kaniṣṭhena vivāhādī kriyamāṇe na doṣaḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa sumantunāpy uktaṃ |
vyasanāsakacitto vā nāstiko vātha vāgrajaḥ |
kanīyān dharmakāmas tu ādhānam atha kārayet ||
adhānagrahaṇaṃ vivāhasyāpy upalakṣaṇārthaṃ klībādayas tu svabhāvataḥ sarvathā vivāhānarhatvāt kiyantam api kālaṃ na pratīkṣaṇīyāḥ |
tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
unmattaḥ kilbiṣī kuṣṭhī patitaḥ klība eva vā |
rājayakṣmy āmayāvī ca na nyāyyaḥ syāt pratīkṣitum ||
khañjavāmanakubjeṣu gadgadeṣu jaḍeṣu ca |
jātyandhe badhire mūke na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
evaṃvidhe jyeṣṭhe sati kaniṣṭhasya parivedane doṣo nāstīty arthaḥ | evam ādhānādhikāriṇy apy akṛtādhāne jyeṣṭhe vidyamāne tadanujñātasya kaniṣṭhasya nāgnyādhāne parivetṛtvadoṣaḥ |
[375] tathā ca vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
agrajaś ca yadānagnir ādadhyād anujaḥ kathaṃ |
agrajānumataḥ kuryād agnihotraṃ yathāvidhi ||
ādhāne ’dhikāryy apy agrajo yadādhānavidhim atikramyāgnyādheyaṃ nādriyate tadā tadanumatiṃ gṛhītvā anuja ādhānaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
ayam evārthaḥ sumantunāpy uktaḥ |
jyeṣṭho bhrātā yadā tiṣṭhed ādhānaṃ naiva kārayet |
anujñātas tu kurvīta śaṅkhasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
jyeṣṭhabhrātṛgrahaṇaṃ pitur api pradarśanārtham |
tathā ca sa eva |
pitur yasya tu nādhānaṃ kathaṃ putras tu kārayet |
agnihotrādhikāro ’sti śaṅkhasya vacanaṃ yathā ||
tathā |
anujñātasya pitrā tu ādhānaṃ sarvadā bhavet |
pitrānujñātasya putrasyeti śeṣaḥ | pitṛgrahaṇaṃ pitāmahasyāpi pradarśanārtham |
ata evośanāḥ |
pitā pitāmaho yasya agrajo vāpy anagnimān |
tapo’gnihotramantreṣu na doṣaḥ parivedane ||
atrāpi pitrādyanujñātatasyeti śeṣaḥ | mantraśabdo vedopalakṣaṇārthaḥ |
tathā ca śātātapaḥ |
[376]        nāgnayaḥ parivindanti na vedā na tapāṃsi ca |
na ca śrāddhaṃ kaniṣṭhasya yā ca kanyā virūpikā ||
yathā vedādhyayanatapaḥśrāddharāhityena sthite ’pi jyeṣṭhe tāni tadanujñām antareṇāpi kaniṣṭhenānuṣṭhitāni parivedanahetavo na bhavanti | tathā jyeṣṭhānujñayāgnayo ’py ādhīyamānā parivedanahetavo na bhavantīty arthaḥ | yā ca kanyā virūpikety asyāyam arthaḥ | vivāhādhikāriṇyāṃ jyeṣṭhāyāṃ kanyāyāṃ vikṛtarūpāyām anūḍhāyāṃ sthitāyāṃ tadanujñayā vināpi kaniṣṭhāyā vivāhakālātikramabhītyā kṛto vivāho na doṣāyeti | evaṃ caivaṃvidhaviṣayavyatiriktaviṣaye kanyāsv api parivedane varaparivedanoktaṃ sarvam anusaṃdheyam | anujasya jyeṣṭhavivāhāt pūrvaṃ vivāhe tu jyeṣṭhānujñāyām api doṣo bhavatīty āha hārītaḥ |
sodarāṇāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ parivettā kathaṃ bhavet |
dārais tu parivindante nāgnihotreṇa nejyayā ||
agnihotrādiṣv anujñātā na parivindanta iti sthitaṃ | dāraiḥ punar anujñātā api parivindanta iti vacanārthaḥ |
vṛddhayājñavalkāḥ |
āvasathyam anādṛtya tretāyāṃ yaḥ pravartate |
so ’nāhitāgnir bhavati parivettā tathocyata iti ||
asyārthaḥ | 'āvasathyaṃ' aupāsanāgniṃ, 'anādṛtya' anādhāya, 'tretāyāṃ' [377] gārhapatyādyagnitrayādhāne, 'yaḥ pravartate so ’nāhitāgniḥ', eva 'bhavati' | tathā prathamam aupāsanāgnim anādhāya gārhapatyādyādhānakāraṇāt parivetteti gīyate |
iti parivittyādayaḥ |
ity apraśastabrāhmaṇasaṃjñāḥ |
iti brāhmaṇasaṃjñāprakaraṇaṃ |
atha śrāddhe praśastā brāhmaṇa nirūpyante |
tatra manu-śātātapau |
tatra ye bhojanīyāḥ syur ye ca varjyā dvijottamāḥ |
yāvantaś caiva yaiś cānyais tāvad vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ ||
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni dātṛbhiḥ |
arhattamāya viprāya tasmai dattaṃ mahāphalaṃ ||
yaḥ svakīyāṃ kṛtsnāṃ mantrabrāhmaṇātmikāṃ śākhām adhīte saḥ ‘śrotriyaḥ’ | śrāddhe viśvedevān uddiśya vihitāni havīṃṣi 'havyāni', pitṝn uddiśya vihitāni ‘kavyāni’ ‘arhattamāya' kulaśīlavidyādhārāyety arthaḥ | ‘tasmai’ śrāddhād anyad api 'dattaṃ mahāphalaṃ' bhavatīti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śrotriyāyaiva deyāni havyakavyāni nityaśaḥ |
aśrotriyāya yad dattaṃ tṛptiṃ nārhanti devatāḥ ||
āha manuḥ |
sahasraṃ hi sahasrāṇām anṛcāṃ yatra bhuñjate |
ekas tān mantravit prītaḥ sarvān arhati dharmataḥ ||
[378] 'anṛcāṃ' avedārthavidām iti jñeyam | yataḥ śrotriyāyaiva deyānīty ukter anadhīyānānāṃ prāptir eva nāsti | medhātithis tu anṛcā iti prathamābahuvacanāntaṃ pāṭhāntaraṃ darśitavān | tasmin pakṣe anṛcāḥ sahasraṃ yatra bhuñjata iti saṃbandhaḥ | 'mantravit' vedārthavettā | 'prītaḥ' tarpito bhojita iti yāvat | 'sarvān' 'tān' 'anṛcān' 'arhati' svīkarotīti abhedam āpadyate | abhede ca yat teṣu sahasrasaṃkhyeṣu bhojiteṣu phalaṃ tad ekasminn avāpyata ity arthaḥ |
jñānotkṛṣṭeṣu deyāni kavyāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
na hi hastāv asṛgdigdhau rudhireṇaiva śudhyataḥ ||
jñānena vidyayā utkṛṣṭā adhikā 'jñānotkṛṣṭāḥ', tebhyaḥ havyakavyāni deyāni netarebhya iti | yathā 'asṛjā' rudhireṇa 'digdhau' liptau, 'hastau' ‘rudhireṇa' prakṣālyamānau na nirmalau bhavataḥ, evam avidvadbrāhmaṇa-bhojanenopakriyamāṇaḥ pitaro na tat phalaṃ labhanta ity arthaḥ |
tathā |
dātṝn pratigrahītṝṃś ca kurute phalabhāginaḥ |
viduṣe dakṣiṇā dattā vidhivat pretya ceha ca ||
'viduṣe' yā 'dakṣiṇā' dīyate sā 'dātṛn pratigrahītṛn' api ‘phalabhāginaḥ’ ‘kurute’ | praśaṃsaiṣā īdṛśam etad viduṣe dānaṃ yat pratigrahītāram apy adṛṣṭaphalena yojayati kiṃ punar dātāram iti | ‘pretya’ svargaḥ phalaṃ, 'iha’, kīrtiḥ | bhojayed ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
śrotrīyāvāgrūpavayaḥśīlasaṃpannayuvabhyo dānaṃ prathamam
eke pitṛvat |
'vāksaṃpannāḥ' saṃskṛtabhāṣiṇaḥ | 'rūpasaṃpannāḥ' manojñaśarīrāḥ | [379] 'vayaḥsaṃpannāḥ' pariṇatavayasaḥ | te hi prāyeṇa praśāntaviṣayānurāgatayā viśuddhamatayo bhavanti | 'śīlasaṃpannāḥ' manovākkāyaiḥ sakalaprāṇihitakāriṇaḥ | vayaḥsaṃpannatvasyāpavādam āha | 'yuvabhya' taruṇebhyaḥ, 'prathamaṃ,' dānaṃ, prathamaśrāddhāni navaśrāddhānīty arthaḥ | prathamaṃ pradhānaṃ vā, yūnāṃ sakalaśrāddhaniyamasaṃpādanasamarthatvāt | 'eke pitṛvat' iti pitṛtulyān brāhmaṇān bhojayed ity eke manyante | pitaram uddiśya taruṇaṃ pitāmaham uddiśya sthaviraṃ, prapitāmaham uddiśya sthavirataram iti |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
pitṛbhyo dadyāt pūrvedyuḥ brāhmaṇān saṃnipātya yatīn gṛhasthān |
sādhūn apariṇatavayaso ’vikarmasthān śrotriyān śiṣyān antevāsinaḥ ||
‘pūrvedyuḥ’ śrāddhadināt pūrvasminn ahani, ‘brāhmaṇān’,‘saṃnipātya' āmantrya,
'yatayaḥ' pravrajitāḥ | tadasaṃbhave 'gṛhasthān' | sādhulakṣaṇam uktaṃ 'apariṇatavayasaḥ' nātisthavirān | 'avikarmasthān’ pratiṣiddhavarjakān | ye ca na śiṣyā cāntevāsinaḥ na sevakā ity arthaḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ |
snātakān eke yatīn gṛhasthasādhūn vā śrotriyān vṛddhān anavadyān svakarmasthānabhāve ’pi śiṣyān sadācārān |
kṛtasamāvartano ’kṛtavivāhaḥ 'snātakaḥ' | 'vṛddhaḥ' vidyātapobhyāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ | yeṣāṃ mātṛtaḥ pitṛtaś cāvadyaṃ doṣo nāsti te 'anavadyāḥ' | ‘svakarmasthāḥ' svāśramavihitaśrautasmārtakarmaniratāḥ, uktalakṣaṇabrāhmaṇābhāve 'sadācārān śiṣyān' api nimantrayet |
āpastambaḥ |
tulyaguṇeṣu vayovṛddhaḥ śreyān dravyakṛśaś ca | [2.17.10]
śamatapaḥśrutaśīlādibhis [380] tulyatve vayovṛddhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ | vayasāpi tulyatve dravyakṛśaḥ kṣīṇavṛttiḥ śreyān | sa ca yadi nijecchayā dravyakṛśo bhavati na punar ayogyatvenālabdhapratigrahatayā |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
prayatāya śucaye sarvatra deyaṃ havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca tasmāc chucir iti vijñāyate |
'prayatāya' saṃyatāya | 'śucaye' bāhyābhyantaraśaucayuktāya | 'sarvatra' janane maraṇe devabhojanādau ca | ayam arthaḥ śrutau vijñāyate | śrutipratīkaṃ paṭhati ‘tasmāc chuciḥ' iti |
āpastambaḥ |
śucīn mantravataḥ sarvakṛtyeṣu bhojayet |
'mantravantaḥ' adhītavedāḥ | 'sarvakṛtyeṣu' vihitabrāhmaṇabhojaneṣu śrautasmārteṣu karmasu | atra kecid yathā kanyā tathā havir iti kanyāsādharmyeṇa haviṣāṃ svaśākhīyapratipādyatvaṃ manyamānāḥ svaśākhīyam eva śrotriyatvādiguṇaviśiṣṭaṃ śrāddhe niyojayanti | tad asat, smṛtītihāsapurāṇagṛhya- kalpasūtreṣu kvacid api svaśākhīyaniyamāśravaṇāt, pratyuta smṛtyādiṣu ‘triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ' ityādibhir nānāśākhīyānāṃ nānāvedādhyāyinām eva vidhidarśanāc ca | na caiteṣāṃ śūdrakartṛkaśrāddhaviṣayatvam iti mantavyam, vyavasthāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt | nāpi kanyāsādṛśyadarśanamātreṇa niyamādhyavasthānaṃ yuktam, sādṛśyasyānyathāpy upapatteḥ | kiṃ ca kanyāyām eva tāvad ekaśākhādhyāyiniyamaḥ kuto ’vagataḥ yadbalena haviṣy api sādhyate | ācārād iti cet, [381] kanyādānapratigraheṣu svaśākhīyair eva saha vyavaharantaḥ paraśākhīyāṃś ca prayatnena pariharantaḥ śiṣṭā dṛśyante | tena jñāyate asty atra niyama iti | maivaṃ | śiṣṭavyavahārasya kulādijñānājñānamūlatvāt | tayoś ca saṃnidhānavyavadhānādhīnatvāt | prāyeṇa hi tulyaśākhinaḥ svādhyāyādhyayana-vedavratagṛhyakarmasaṃdhyopāsanādikriyākalāpavicāraṇārtham anyonyasaṃgamam abhilaṣantaḥ samānadeśavāsino bhavanti | saṃnidhānāc ca vijñātakulaśīlācārāḥ parasparaṃ yaunasaṃbandhavidhim ādriyante | anyaśākhīyāṃs tu deśāntaranivāsād avijñātasvarūpatayaiva na śraddadhate | aryāvarteṣu ca samānadeśavāsināṃ nānāśākhādhyāyinām apy upalabhyanta eva parasparam anavagīyamānāḥ sarvato vivāhasaṃbandhāḥ | avijñātaparasparāṇām ekaśākhādhyāyinām api nopalabhyante | ataḥ samānadeśavāsādisamutthaṃ kulādijñānam eva pariṇayanasaṃbandhapravartakam iti | yā tu keṣāṃcit saty api sādṛśye na dṛśyate saṃbandhapravṛttiḥ tatraitan nidānaṃ yeṣāṃ pūrvapuruṣaiḥ pūrvam atividūrānekadeśavāsibhir vigarhitetaretaradeśācāraiḥ kālāntare kathaṃcana sahasaṃvāsam āsādayadbhir api svasvakulaśreṇyahaṃkāraṃ samudvahadbhir anyonyaṃ na pravartate samudvāhavidhis tatas tatsantāneṣu saivāpravṛttiḥ kiyantaṃ kālam anuvartata iti | ato na kanyādāne nāpi havirdāne svaśākhīyadvijaniyama iti siddhaṃ | evaṃ ca sati vakṣyamāṇāni vacanāni samañjasārthāni syuḥ |
yamaḥ |
vedavidyāvratasnātāḥ śrotriyā vedapāragāḥ |
svadharmaniratāḥ kṣāntāḥ kriyāvantas tapasvinaḥ |
[382]        tebhyo havyaṃ ca kavyaṃ ca praśāntebhyaḥ pradīyate ||
vedān vedau vedaṃ cādhītya samāvṛttāḥ 'vedasnātakāḥ' | vidyāṃ vedārthavicāraṃ samāpya nivṛttāḥ 'vidyāsnātakāḥ' | vrataṃ brahmacaryaṃ samāpya nivṛttāḥ ‘vratasnātakāḥ | saty api vedapāragatve nityaṃ vedābhyāsaniratatva-pradarśanārthaṃ śrotriyagrahaṇaṃ | dvandaduḥkhasahiṣṇavaḥ 'kṣāntāḥ' | akrodhanāḥ 'śāntāḥ' |
manuḥ |
vedavidyāvratasnātān śrotriyān gṛhamedhinaḥ |
pūjayed dhavyakavyena viparītāṃś ca varjayet ||
'gṛhamedhinaḥ' gṛhasthāḥ |
tathā |
yatnena bhojayec chrāddhe bahvṛcaṃ vedapāragam |
śākhāntagam athādhvaryuṃ chandogaṃ cāsamāptikam ||
eṣām anyatamo yasya bhuñjīta śrāddham arcitaḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ tasya tṛptiḥ syāc chāśvatī sāptapauruṣī ||
vedapāragaśākhāntagāsamāptikaśabdair ekārthaiḥ sakalaśākhādhyetāra ucyante | ṛgvedādhyāyī 'bahvṛcaḥ'| vedasya pāraṃ paryavasānaṃ gacchatīti 'vedapāragaḥ' | nāyam ṛtvigviśeṣo ’dhvaryuḥ kiṃ tu adhvaryur vedasya yajurvedasyādhyetā puruṣaḥ 'adhvaryuḥ' | śākhāyā mantrabrāhmaṇātmikāyā antaṃ pāraṃ gacchatīti ‘śākhāntagaḥ’| 'chandogaḥ' sāmavedādhyāyī | asamāptir asyāstīti ‘āsamāptikaḥ' | eṣāṃ trividhānāṃ 'anyatamaḥ' eko ’pi, yasya śrāddhaṃ bhuñjīta tasya pitṝṇāṃ sāptapauruṣī tṛptiḥ syāt | 'arcitaḥ ' [383] 'sāptapauruṣī' | kālamahattvopalakṣaṇārthaṃ caitat | yāvat saptapuruṣā āgāminaḥ putrapautrādayo jātāḥ janiṣyante ca tāvat kālam iti medhātithiḥ | ‘śāśvatī’ anavacchinā |
nanu bahvṛcādiśabdair eva tattadvedādhyāyinaḥ pratyekaṃ vihitāḥ | ata eṣām anyatama iti kimartham |
kecid āhuḥ | ātharvaṇikanivṛttyartham etat | eṣāṃ bahvṛcādhvaryu-chandogānām evānyatamaḥ śrāddhe bhojanoyo na tu tadvyatirikraḥ kaścit | trayī vai vidyā ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmānīti śatapathe ’tharvāṅgirasāṃ vidyātvenānabhidhānāt |
tad ayukaṃ | ātharvaṇikaṃ vidadhadbhiḥ tadatikrame doṣaṃ cābhidadhadbhir bahubhir yama-śātātapādivacanaiḥ saha virodhāt | śatapathaśruteś cāyam arthaḥ | ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃjñakā trayy eva vidyā vaitānikakarmopayoginī nānyeti na punar etadatiriktā vicaiva na bhavatīti | śrūyate ca śatapathabrahmayajña eva | "brāhmaṇasvādhyāyasaṃśabdena pūrvikātharvāṅgirasāṃ praśaṃsā sa eva vidvān atharvāṅgiraso ’har ahaḥ svādhyāyam adhīte meda āhutibhir eva tad devāṃs tarpayati ta enaṃ tṛptās tarpayanti yogakṣemeṇa prāṇena retasā sarvātmanā sarvabhiḥ puṇyābhiḥ saṃpadbhir ghṛtakulyā madhukulyāḥ pitṝn svadhā abhivahantīti" śatapathaḥ | āśvamedhikakāṇḍe pāriplavabrāhmaṇe teṣāṃ vedasaṃjñāśabdatāpi dṛśyate | "atharvāṇo vedaḥ so ’yam ity atharvāṇām ekaṃ parva vyācakṣāṇa ivānudravet | aṅgiraso vedaḥ so 'yam ity aṅgirasām ekaṃ parva vyācakṣāṇa ivānudraved" iti | aṅgāni vedāś catvāra iti smṛtipurāṇādiṣv api atharvāṅgirasām eva caturthavedatvenābhidhānam abhimataṃ | ato nātharvaṇikanivṛttyartham idaṃ kiṃ tu pitṛkṛtye trayas tāvan nimantraṇīyāḥ | te ca chandogaṃ ceti caśabdena bahvṛco ’dhvaryuḥ chandoga ity evaṃ [384] trayaḥ sarvadā gṛhyeran nanv anyataravedādhyāyino ’pīti śaṅkānivṛttyartham ukram eṣām anyatama ityādi |
yamaḥ |
bahvṛco yas trisauparṇaś chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
pañcāgnir vā yajurvedī atharvā brahmaśīrṣakaḥ ||
ātmavic cāṅgavic caiva brahmadeyāsutas tathā |
ete sarve yathāvat syur ekadeśo ’pi vā bhavet ||
trayaḥ pare 'varāś caiva ātmā tatraiva saptamaḥ |
te saptapuruṣāḥ sarve tad annam upabhuñjate ||
trisuparṇaḥ ṛgyajuṣayoḥ ekadeśaḥ tadadhyayanāṅgaṃ vrataṃ ca tad asyāsti asau 'trisauparṇaḥ’| 'jyeṣṭhasāma' sāmaviśeṣaḥ | pañcāgnir nāma yajurvedavidyā tad adhyayanāṅgaṃ vrataṃ ca tatsaṃbandhāt puruṣaḥ 'pañcāgniḥ' | atha vā gārhapatyāhavanīyadakṣiṇāgnisabhyāvasathyāḥ agnayo yasya sa 'pañcāgniḥ' | brahmaśīrṣam atharvopaniṣadekadeśaḥ tadvettā 'brahmaśīrṣakaḥ' | 'ātmavit' ātmatattvavettā | 'brahmadeyā' brāhmavivāhoḍhā tasyām utpannaḥ ‘brahmadeyāsutaḥ' | ‘ete sarve’ bahvṛcādyāḥ | ‘ekadeśo ’pi' iti uktānāṃ sarveṣām asaṃbhave yāvanto labhyante tāvanto grāhyāḥ | 'pare' caturthāt puruṣāt prabhṛtayaḥ, 'avare' caturthāt pūrvaṃ trayaḥ pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahāḥ | 'ātmā' yajamānaḥ, ete sapta puruṣāḥ viśiṣṭapātre dattam annaṃ bhuñjate | yathā pitrādīnāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ śrāddham upakārakaṃ bhavati evaṃ yajamānasyāpi paraloke viśiṣṭaphalotpādakaṃ bhavati |
āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
[385]        yady ekaṃ bhojayec chrāddhaṃ chandogaṃ tatra bhojayet |
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni tritayaṃ tatra vidyate ||
aṭeta pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ saśailavanakānanām |
yadi labhyeta pitrarthe sāmnām akṣaracintakaḥ ||
ṛcā tu tṛpyati pitā yajuṣā ca pitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ pitāmahaḥ sāmnā chandogo ’bhyadhikas tataḥ ||
yama-śātātapau |
chandogaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe vaiśvadeve ca bahvṛvam |
puṣṭikarmaṇi cādhvaryuṃ śāntikarmaṇy atharvaṇam ||
puṣṭyarthaṃ karma puṣṭikarma | ariṣṭhopaśamanārthaṃ yat kriyate tac chāntikarma |
śātātapaḥ |
bhojayed yady athavāṇaṃ daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
anantam akṣayaṃ caiva phalaṃ tasyeti vai śrutiḥ ||
'anantaṃ' aparyavasānam | 'akṣayaṃ' akṛśaṃ |
yas tv anyaṃ bhojayec chrāddhe vidyamāneṣv atharvasu |
nirāśās tasya gacchanti devatāḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena śrāddhakāle tv atharvaṇam |
bhojayet havyakavyeṣu pitṝṇāṃ ca tad akṣayam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
agryaḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu śrotriyo brahmavid yuvā |
vedārthavit jyeṣṭhasāmā trimadhus trisuparṇikaḥ ||
[386]        karmaniṣṭhās taponiṣṭhāḥ pañcāgnibrahmacāriṇaḥ |
pitṛmātṛparāś caiva brāhmaṇāḥ śrāddhasaṃpade ||
'agryaḥ' mukhyaḥ adhyetṝṇām | 'sarveṣu' caturṣu vedeṣu | 'śrotriyaḥ' yathāvidhi svaśākhādhyāyī | 'brahmavit' upaniṣatpuruṣavit | 'yuvā' madhyamavayaskaḥ | ‘vedārthavit' mantrabrāhmaṇārthavettā | trimadhvākhyaṃ vrataṃ kṛtvā yas tadākhyam ṛgvedaikadeśam adhīte saḥ 'trimadhuḥ' | trisuparṇavratapūrvakaṃ tadākhyayajurvedabhāgādhyetā 'trisuparṇikaḥ' | 'karmaniṣṭhāḥ' vihitānuṣṭhānaparāḥ | 'śrāddhasaṃpade' śrāddhasamṛddhaye |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
ye ca bhāṣyavidaḥ kecid ye ca vyākaraṇe ratāḥ |
adhīyānāḥ purāṇaṃ ye dharmaśāstram athāpi vā ||
ye ca puṇyeṣu tīrtheṣu kṛtasnātāḥ kṛtaśramāḥ |
makheṣu ye ca sarveṣu bhavanty avabhṛthaplutāḥ ||
ye ca satyavratā nityaṃ svadharmaniratāś ca ye |
akrodhanāḥ kṣāntiparās tān śrāddheṣu niyojayet ||
matsyapurāṇe |
ātharvaṇaḥ vedavic ca jñātavaṃśaḥ kulānvitaḥ |
purāṇavettā brahmaṇyaḥ svādhyāyajapatatparaḥ ||
śivabhaktaḥ pitṛparaḥ sūryabhakto ’tha vaiṣṇavaḥ |
brahmaṇyo yogavic chānto vijitātmā suśīlavān |
etāṃs tu bhojayen nityaṃ daive pitrye ca karmaṇi ||
[387]        maṇḍalabrāhmaṇajñā ye ye sūktaṃ puruṣaṃ viduḥ |
tāṃs tu dṛṣṭvā naraḥ kṣipraṃ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
śatarudrīyajāpyeṣu niratā ye dvijottamāḥ |
pitṝn saṃtārayanty ete śrāddhe yatnena bhojitāḥ ||
tathā |
gāyatrījāpyanirataṃ havyakavyeṣu yojayet |
pāpaṃ tiṣṭhati no tasminn abbindur iva puṣkare ||
apāṃ binduḥ 'abbinduḥ' | 'puṣkaraṃ' padmapatraṃ |
brahmapurāṇe |
ṣaḍaṅgavid yājño yogī yajñatattvajña eva ca |
ayācitāśī vipro yaḥ śrāddhakalpavid eva ca ||
aṣṭādaśānāṃ vidyānām ekasyā api pāragaḥ |
trimadhuś ca trisauparṇaḥ pañcāgnir jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ ||
catvāro vedāś catvāra upavedāḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni smṛtayaḥ purāṇāni mīmāṃsā nyāya ity aṣṭādaśavidyāḥ |
cāndrāyaṇaiḥ parākair vā niyamair yaś ca saṃskṛtaḥ |
bārhaspatyaṃ nītiśāstraṃ śabdavidyāṃ ca vetti yaḥ ||
itihāsapurāṇaiś ca yaḥ pavitrīkṛtaḥ sadā |
akṛtyam api kurvāṇaḥ sa pavitraśatād varaḥ ||
bahunātra kim uktena itihāsapurāṇavit |
atharvasiraso ’dhyetā tāv ubhau pitṛbhiḥ purā |
[388]        tapaḥ kṛtvā niyogārthaṃ prārthitau pitṛkarmaṇi ||
mahābhārate |
ye ca puṇyeṣu tīrtheṣu abhiṣekakṛtaśramāḥ |
makheṣu ca sasatreṣu bhavanty avabhṛthaplutāḥ ||
abādhanā acapalāḥ kṣāntā dāntā jitendriyāḥ |
sarvadharmaratā ye ca śrāddheṣv etān niyojayet ||
akrodhanāḥ karmaparāḥ santo nityaṃ dame ratāḥ |
tādṛśāḥ sādhavo viprās tebhyo dattaṃ mahāphalaṃ ||
amāninaḥ sarvasahāḥ suhṛṣṭā vijitendriyāḥ |
sarvabhūtahitā maitrās tebhyo dattaṃ mahāphalaṃ ||
alubdhāḥ śucayo vaidyā hrīmantaḥ satyavādinaḥ |
svakarmaniratā ye ca tebhyo dattaṃ mahāphalaṃ ||
prajñāśrutābhyāṃ śīlena kulena ca samucchritāḥ |
tārayet tat kulaṃ kṛtsnam eko 'pīha dvijarṣabhaḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
akḷptānnaṃ ghṛṇiklāntaṃ kṛśavṛttim ayācakaṃ |
ekāntaśīlaṃ hrīmantaṃ sadā śrāddheṣu bhojayet ||
na vidyate kḷptam annaṃ yasyāsau 'akḷptānnaḥ' | 'ghṛṇā' dayā sā vidyate yasyāsau ‘ghṛṇī’| ‘klāntaḥ’vrataśrāntaḥ, ghṛṇī cāsau klāntaś ca 'ghṛṇiklāntaḥ’ | tīrthayātrāśīḷatayā vā ghṛṇibhiḥ sūryaraśmibhiḥ klānto 'ghṛṇiklāntaḥ' pratigrahādisaṅkucitatayā kṛśāvṛttir vartanaṃ yasyāsau‘kṛśavṛttiḥ’ | 'hrīmān’ lajjāvān | tathāvidhān viprān gṛhasthān vedārthavido nirabhimāninaḥ |
[389]        patnīputrasamāyuktān śrāddhakarmaṇi yojayet |
dehasyārdhaṃ smṛtā patnī na samagro vinā tathā |
na vāputrasya loko ’sti śrutir eṣā sanātanī ||
skānde prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
atha viprān pravakṣyāmaḥ śrāddhe vai ketanakṣamān |
viśiṣṭaḥ śrotriyo yogī vedavit jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
pañcāgniḥ karmaniṣṭhaś ca taponiṣṭho jitendriyaḥ ||
nandipurāṇe |
yatīn vā vālakhilyān vā bhojayec chrāddhakarmaṇi |
vānaprasthopakurvāṇau pūjayet paritoṣayet ||
gṛhasthaṃ bhojayed yas tu viśvedevās tu pūjitāḥ |
vānaprasthena ṛṣayo vālakhilyaiḥ purandaraḥ ||
yatīnāṃ pūjanād vāpi sākṣād brahmā supūjitaḥ |
āśramāḥ pāvanāḥ pañca upadhābhiratāśramāḥ |
catvāra āśramāḥ puṇyāḥ śrāddhe daive tathaiva ca ||
tathā |
śrāddhaṃ deyaṃ tu vipreṣu saṃyateṣv agnihotriṣu |
avadāteṣu vidvatsu śrotriyeṣu viśeṣataḥ ||
maṇḍalabrāhmaṇajñaś ca purāṇārthaviśāradaḥ |
akalkaḥ svalpasaṃtuṣṭaḥ pratigrahavivarjitaḥ |
[390]        ete śrāddhe niyoktavyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktimūrdhani ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
triṇāciketaḥ sauparṇī dharmadroṇasya pāṭhakaḥ |
sadyaḥ prakṣālako yaś ca paropakaraṇe rataḥ ||
mṛṣṭānnado mṛṣṭavāṃś ca sadā japaparāyaṇaḥ |
ete vai brāhmaṇā jñeyā viśeṣāḥ śrāddhasiddhaye ||
bārhaspatyasaṃhitāyām |
aṅgironāradabhṛgubṛhaspatyuditāḥ smṛtīḥ |
paṭhanti ye śraddadhānās te ’bhiyojyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
vedāntaniṣṭhāḥ śrāddheṣu vyākhyātāro viśeṣataḥ ||
pracetāḥ |
triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
jñānaniṣṭhas taponiṣṭhaḥ karmaniṣṭhas tathāparaḥ ||
dāntaś ca mātṛbhaktaś ca śraddhayā devapūjakaḥ |
śiloñchanādivṛttiś ca śrotriyasya sutas tathā |
ete viprā mahāpuṇyā havyakavyeṣu cāśritāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
nakṣatratithipuṇyāhān muhūrtān maṅgalāni ca |
na nirdiśanti ye viprās tair bhuktaṃ hy akṣayaṃ bhavet ||
prayatnād dhavyakavyāni pātrībhūte dvijanmani |
[391]        pratiṣṭhāpyāni vidvadbhiḥ phalānantam abhīpsubhiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
gobhūtilahiraṇyādi pātre dātavyam arcitam |
nāpātre viduṣā kiṃcid ātmanaḥ śreya īcchatā ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
asamānapravarako hy asagotras tathaiva ca |
asaṃbandhī ca vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ śrāddhasiddhaye ||
garuḍapurāṇe |
śrāddheṣu viniyojyāḥ syur brāhmaṇā brahmavittamāḥ |
ye yonigotramantrāntevāsisaṃbandhavarjitāḥ ||
'yonisaṃbandhāḥ' mātulādayaḥ | 'gotrasaṃbandhāḥ' sapiṇḍādayaḥ | 'mantrasaṃbandhāḥ' adhyāpakādayaḥ | 'antevāsisaṃbandhāḥ ' śilpaśāstropādhyāyāḥ |
iti praśastā brāhmaṇāḥ |
atha pāṅkteyāḥ |
tatra śaṅkhalikhitau |
atha pāṅkteyā vedavedāṅgavit pañcāgnir anūcānaḥ śāṅkhyayogopaniṣad-dharmaśāstravic chrotriyas triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇī jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ | śāṅkhyayogopaniṣaddharmaśāstrādhyāyī vedaparaḥ sadāgniko mātṛpitṛśuṣrūṣur dharmaśāstraratir iti |
'sāṅkhyaśāstram' kāpilam | 'yogaśāstram' pātañjalam | 'upaniṣacchāstram' vedāntāḥ | ‘sadāgnikaḥ' nityam agnihotā |
[392]         pāṅkteyanirūpaṇārthaṃ kulaśrutaśīlavṛttarūpaguṇān āha hārītaḥ |
sthitir avicchinnavedaveditā ayonisaṃkaritvam ārṣeyatvaṃ ceti kulaguṇāḥ | vedo vedāṅgāni dharmo ’dhyātmaṃ vijñānaṃ sthitir iti ṣaḍvidhaṃ śrutam | brahmaṇyatā devapitṛbhaktatā samatā saumyatāparopatāpitānasūyatā mṛdutāpāruṣyaṃ maitratā priyavāditvaṃ kṛtajñatā śaraṇyatā praśāntiś ceti trayodaśavidhaṃ śīlam | kṣamā damo dayā dānam ahiṃsā gurupūjanaṃ śaucaṃ snānaṃ japo homas tapaḥ svādhāyaḥ satyavacanaṃ saṃtoṣo dṛḍhavratatvam upavratitvaṃ ceti ṣoḍaśaguṇaṃ vṛttam | tasmāt kulīnāḥ śrutavantaḥ śīlavanto vṛttasthāḥ satyavādino ’vyaṅgāḥ pāṅkteyā iti |
asyārthaḥ | santatirūpeṇa cirakālāvasthānaṃ sthitir iti smṛticandrikākāraḥ | hariharas tv āha | śāstroktavarṇāśramādidharmeṣv avasthānam āpady api tato ’pracyavanaṃ sthitiḥ |
yad āhuḥ |
ayācyāpracalanam amārge cāpravartanam |
āpady api yato dhairyaṃ sa guṇaḥ sthitir ucyate iti ||
brāhmaṇyādivat pitṛpitāmahādiprabandhena toraṇāyaṇīyādi-caraṇākhyātṛtvenāvicchinnasya vedasya vettṛtvam avicchinnavedavittvam |
yathāhuḥ |
jātivad viprabandhena smaryate caraṇaṃ kule |
tadvedanam avicchinnavedavittā prakīrttitā ||
apare tu vedavedyor avicchinatvena pūrvapuruṣeṣv api vedādhyayanayāgānuṣṭhānayor [393] avyavacchedaṃ kulaguṇam āhuḥ | vijātīyāyām apatyānutpādanena dattakrītādirūpaparakulasaṃsargarāhityena cāsaṃkīrnayonitvam ayonimāsāṃkaryam, svīyapravaragatarṣijñātṛtvam ārṣeyatvam, “anūcānaputratvaparo yo vai jñāto ’nūcānaḥ sa ṛṣiḥ ārṣeya” iti śatapathaśrutidarśanāt |
ete tāvat kulaguṇāḥ śuddhihetavaḥ | ṛgādibhedena caturvidho vedaḥ | vedāṅgāni śikṣādīni tad uktaṃ |
śikṣā kalpo vyākaraṇaṃ nirutaṃ jyotiṣāṃ gatiḥ |
chandovicitir ity evaṃ vedāṅgāni ṣaḍ ūcire ||
'dharmaḥ' manvādidharmmaśāstram | 'adhyātmaṃ' ātmavivekaśāstram | 'vijñānaṃ' pramāṇaprameyādipadārthaviśeṣajñānahetur nyāyadarśanādi | sthitihetuvārtādiśāstraṃ ‘sthitiḥ' | adhītāvismaraṇaṃ vā 'sthitiḥ'| dharmādikramāvasthitaṃ vā yatretihāsādau kathyate sā 'sthitiḥ' | evaṃ ca vedaprabhṛticaturdaśavidyāsthānāny uktāni bhavanti | eteṣāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ śravaṇaṃ 'śrutaṃ' | brahmaṇi sādhutvaṃ 'brahmaṇyatā' | amāyayā devapitṛkarmānuṣṭhānaṃ 'devapitṛbhakatā' | jīvator vā pitror aśāṭyena śuśrūṣaṇaṃ 'pitṛbhaktatā' | vigalitarāgadveṣatā 'samatā' | sarvajanahṛdyacaritratvaṃ 'saumyatā' | vāgādibhiḥ parapīḍārāhityam 'aparopatāpitā' | paradoṣānāviṣkaraṇaṃ 'anasūyatā' | akaṭhinahṛdayatvaṃ 'mṛdutā' | kalahavirahitatvaṃ 'apāruṣyaṃ' | prasannamanaskatā 'maitratā' | sādhukāriṇām anumodanaṃ 'priyavāditā' | upakārasaṃpratipattiḥ 'kṛtajñatā' | śaraṇaṃ gṛharakṣitror ity abhidhānakośasmaraṇāt śaraṇam āśrayo rakṣitā vā, śaraṇam eva śaraṇyaḥ | svārthiko ’tra taddhitaḥ | atha vā śaraṇe āśrayatve rakṣitṛtve [394] vā sādhuḥ śaraṇyaḥ, tasya bhāvaḥ 'śaraṇyatā' | vidyādyanutsekaḥ 'praśāntiḥ' | kṣamādiṣu vyākhyātāni na vyākhyāyante | yathāvidhi svasya sattvanivṛttipūrvakaṃ parasvattvāpādanaṃ 'dānaṃ' | viśiṣṭamantrāvṛttiḥ 'japa:' | devatoddeśena prakṣepāntaḥ svattvatyāgo 'homaḥ' | 'saṃtoṣaḥ' vitṛṣṇatā | svīkṛtavratāparityāgaḥ ‘dṛḍhavratatvam' | 'upavratitvaṃ' daśamyādāv ekabhaktatā | vratāny upavratāni cāha vyāsaḥ |
ahiṃsā satyavacanaṃ brahmacaryam akalkatā |
asteyam iti pañcaite yamāś caiva vratāni ca ||
akrodho guruśuśrūṣā śaucam āhāralāghavam |
apramādaś ca niyamāḥ pañcaivopavratāni ca ||
āha yamaḥ |
pāṅkteyān brāhmaṇān vakṣye yebhyo dattam athākṣayam |
yaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvabhūtānāṃ manaso nirvṛtir bhavet ||
yeṣāṃ caivopaviṣṭas tu paṅktim eko ’pi pāvayet |
śataṃ śatasahasraṃ ca paśyann api samantataḥ ||
namaskṛtya dvijātibhyaḥ pravakṣyāmy aham agrataḥ |
vedaśrutonasaṃkīrṇāṃs tān me nigadataḥ śṛṇu ||
ye somapā virajaso dharmajñāḥ śāntabuddhayaḥ |
vratino niyamasthāś ca ṛtukālābhigāminaḥ ||
pañcāgnir apy adhīyāno yajurvedavid eva ca |
bahvṛcaś ca trisauparṇas trimadhur vātha yo bhavet ||
[395] 'trimadhuḥ' ṛgvedaikadeśaḥ tadvrataṃ ca |
triṇāciketā virajāḥ chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
athaśiraso ’dhyetā sarve te paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
virajā nāma mantraviśeṣas tadadhyayanāt puruṣo ’pi 'virajāḥ' | atharvaśiro nāma atharvavedaikadeśaḥ |
śiśur apy agnihotrī ca nyāyavid yaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
mantrabrāhmaṇavic caiva yaś ca syād dharmapāṭhakaḥ ||
ṛṣivratī ṛṣīkaś ca tathā dvādaśavārṣikaḥ |
brahmadeyāsutaś caiva garbhaśuddhaḥ sahasradaḥ ||
cāndrāyaṇavratacaraḥ satyavādī purāṇavit |
niṣṇātaḥ sarvavidyāsu śānto vigatakalmaṣaḥ ||
gurudevāgnipūjāsu prasakto jñānatatparaḥ |
vimuktaḥ sarvato dhīro brahmabhṛto dvijottamaḥ ||
anamitro na cāmitro maitraś cātmavid eva ca |
snātako jāpyanirataḥ sadā puṣpabalipriyaḥ ||
ṛjur mṛduḥ kṣamī dāntaḥ satyavrataparaḥ śuciḥ |
vedajñaḥ sarvaśāstrajña upavāsaparāyaṇaḥ ||
gṛhastho brahmacārī ca caturvedavid eva ca |
ete dvijātayaḥ puṇyāḥ pāṅkteyāḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ |
niyataṃ tārayanty eva havyakavye ca yojitāḥ ||
'ṛṣīkaḥ' ṛṣeḥ kiṃcidūnaguṇaḥ,
ṛṣeḥ kiṃciṅguṇair nyūna [396] ṛṣīka iti kīrtitaḥ |
iti sumantusmaraṇāt | 'dvādaśavārṣikaḥ' dvādaśavārṣikavedavratacārī | 'sahasradaḥ' gosahasrada iti medhātithiḥ | 'vimuktaḥ sarvataḥ' ity atra ṛṇād iti śeṣaḥ | 'anamitraḥ' śatrurahitaḥ | 'na cāmitraḥ' na kasyāpi śatruḥ | 'maitraḥ' maitrīrataḥ | ‘ghṛṇī’ dayāluḥ |
iti pāṅkteyāḥ |
atha paṅktipāvanāḥ |
āpastambaḥ |
apāṅktyopahatā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yair dvijottamaiḥ |
tān nibodhata kārtsnena dvijāgryān paṅktipāvanān ||
‘apāṅktayaḥ' vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇair apāṅkteyaiḥ 'upahatā' dūṣitā, 'paṅktiḥ' pariṣat, ‘yaiḥ' brāhmaṇaiḥ 'pāvyate' nirdoṣā kriyate, tān 'kārtsnena,' vakṣyamāṇān 'nibodhata' śṛṇuta | arthavādarupāṇy etāni padāni | yathā hy ekapaṅktyāṃ bhuñjāno duṣṭo ’nyān aduṣṭān api dūṣayati evaṃ paṅktipāvanaḥ svaguṇātiśayād anyakṛtam api doṣam apanudatīty asyārthaḥ | na cānenāpāṅkteyānāṃ bhojanam anujñāyate | kiṃ tarhi paṅktipāvanas tāvad avaśyam aveṣitavyaḥ |
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca |
śrotriyānvayajāś caiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
'agryāḥ' uttamāḥ | 'sarveṣu' caturṣv api, 'vedeṣu' sarvasaṃśayavyudāsena saṃpradāyataḥ svīkṛtasarvavedā ity arthaḥ | procyate vyākhyāyate vedārtho yas tāni pravacanāny aṅgāni ṣaḍaṅgo vedo yair abhyasto 'bhyasyate vā ta [397] ity arthaḥ | 'śrotriyānvayajāḥ' pitṛpitāmahādayo yeṣāṃ tādṛśā evety arthaḥ |
nanu vidvadbhyo dānam uktaṃ na ceha vidvattopāttā na ca tathā vinā paṃktipāvanatvopapattiḥ | guṇaviśeṣāpekṣaṃ hi paṃktipāvanatvaṃ na guṇāpacaye yuktam |
ucyate | vidvadabhāve kevalāya śrotriyāya dānārtham etat | asati viduṣi śrotriyāya dānaṃ gauṇam api mukhyam evety uktaṃ bhavati |
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
brahmadeyānusantāno jyeṣṭhasāmaga eva ca ||
tṛṇāciketākhyo vedabhāgo ’dhvaryūṇāṃ pītodakā jagghatṛṇā ityādiḥ tadadhyayanasaṃbandhāt puruṣo ’py atra 'triṇāciketaḥ' ucyate |
anye tu triṇāciketam adhīyānānāṃ vratam āmnātaṃ tad yena cīrṇaḥ saḥ ‘triṇāciketaḥ’| atrāpi lakṣaṇyaiva puruṣa ucyate | na caivaṃ mantavyaṃ tāvanmātreṇa paṅktipāvanatvaṃ | kiṃ tarhi, sati śrotriyatvādiguṇayoge adhiko ’yaṃ guṇo draṣṭavyaḥ | pañcāgnividyā nāma chāndogyopaniṣad iti tadvedanasaṃbandhāt puruṣo ’pi 'pañcāgniḥ' |
anye tu yasya trayaḥ tretāgnayaḥ sabhyāvasthyāv api dvau sa pañcāgnir ity āhuḥ | 'trisuparṇaḥ' mantraḥ, taittirīyake bāhvṛcye vā ye brāhmaṇās trisuparṇaṃ paṭhantītyādi paṭhyate trisuparṇākhyaṃ vrataṃ ca | ṣaḍaṅgaṃ vedaṃ vettīti ‘ṣaḍaṅgavit' | 'brahmadeyānusantānaḥ' brāhmaṇāya dānena yā dattā tasyā anusantānas tayā jātaḥ | 'jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ' ājyadohāni āraṇyakasāmāni santi jyeṣṭhasāmākhyaṃ vrataṃ ca | yadvratācaraṇena yas tad adhīte sa evam ucyate |
[398]        vedārthavit pravaktā ca brahmacārī sahasradaḥ |
śatāyuś caiva vijñeyā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
ṣaḍaṅgavittvasyoktatvāt tadaṅgair vinā svayam evātiprājñatayā yo vedārthaṃ vetti so ’tra 'vedārthavit' abhipretaḥ | 'pravaktā' vedasyādhyāpako vyākhyātā vā | ‘brahmacārī' brahmacaryāśramavān | brahmacārigrahaṇaṃ yauvanopalakṣaṇārtham ity anye | ata eva śrotriyo brahmavid yuveti yājñavalkyenokaṃ | yuvabhyo dānaṃ prathamaṃ pitṛvayasa ity eke iti gotamīye 'py uktaṃ |
pitṛvayasa iti | pitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahais tulyavayaskān tattatsthānīyān brāhmaṇān nimantrayed ity arthaḥ | sahasraśabdasya bahvarthavācitvāt 'sahasradaḥ' bahupradaḥ | 'śatāyuḥ' vṛddhavayāḥ, sa hi paripakvakapolatayā pātratām aśnute | śatam ity atra prasiddhivaśād varṣāṇi saṃkhyeyatayāvagamyante |
āha yamaḥ |
vedavidyāvratasnātā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
vratacaryāsu niratā ye kṛśāḥ kṛśavṛttayaḥ ||
atyutkrāntās tv adharmebhyas te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
satriṇo niyamasthāś ca ye viprāḥ śrutisaṃmatāḥ ||
atra mantriṇa iti vā pāṭhaḥ | tasmin pakṣe mantriṇo gāyatryādimantrajapaniratāḥ |
prāṇihiṃsānivṛattāś ca te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
agnihotraratāḥ śāntāḥ kṣamāvanto ’nasūyakāḥ |
ye pratigrahaniḥsnehās te dvijāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
satriṇo niyamasthāś ca ye viprāḥ śrutisaṃmatāḥ |
[399]        maṅgalyācārayuktāś ca te viprāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
ete vai bhūtale devāḥ pūrvaṃ devair vinirmitāḥ |
yat kiṃcit pitṛdevatvam ebhyo dattaṃ tad akĕayam ||
tathā |
vidvāṃsaḥ suvratāḥ śāntā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ |
tair bhuktaṃ vrajate devāṃs tair bhuktaṃ vrajate pitṝn |
aha śaṅkhaḥ |
ṣaḍaṅgavit trisauparṇo bahvṛco jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnir brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
brahmadeyānusantāno brahmadeyāpradāyakaḥ |
brahmadeyāpatir yaś ca brāhmaṇaḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
yajuṣāṃ pārago yaś ca sāmnāṃ yaś cātipāragaḥ |
atharvasiraso ’dhyetā brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
nityaṃ yogaparo vidvān samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
dhyānaśīlo yatir vidvān brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
athānyāṃś ca pravakṣyāmi brāhmaṇān paṅktipāvanān |
samarthān havyakavyeṣu svaguṇair abhipūjitān ||
vedavedāṅganiṣṇātā viśuddhānvayayonayaḥ |
svakarmāduḥsthitā viprāḥ svapaṅktiṃ pāvayanti hi ||
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit |
[400]        chandogaḥ somayājī ca brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
jyeṣṭhāśramaniviṣṭaś ca śatāyur jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ |
agnivit somapāś caite brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
ṛgyajuḥsāmadharmajñāḥ snātakāś cāgnihotriṇaḥ |
brahmadeyānusantānā vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
daśobhayataḥ śrotriyās triṇāciketas trimadhus trisauparṇas triśīrṣā jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ pañcāgniḥ ṣaḍaṅgavid rudrajāpy ūrdhvaretā ṛtukālābhigāmī tattvavic ceti paṅktipāvanā bhavanti |
athāpy atrodāharanti |
pacanaḥ pācanas tretā yasya pañcāgnayo gṛhe |
sāyaṃ prātaḥ pradīpyante sa vipraḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ ||
sahasrasaṃmitaṃ prāhuḥ snātakaṃ pūrvavad guṇaiḥ |
pañcāgyādiguṇair yuktaḥ śatasāhasra ucyate ||
anṛcāṃ yadi vā kṛtsnāṃ paṅktiṃ yojanam āyatām |
punāti vedavid vipro niyuktaḥ paṅktimūrdhani ||
'daśobhayataḥ śrotriyāḥ' ityanena yājñavalkyādyuktā mātṛtaḥ pitṛto ’pi daśapuruṣaśuddhir abhihitā | rudraśiro vaiśvānaraśiro ’tharvaśiraś ceti trayāṇāṃ śīrṣṇām adhyetā 'triśīrṣā' | 'rudrajāpī' namas te rudra manyava ityādi rudradhyānajapaparaḥ | ūrdhvam eva reto yasya sa 'ūrdhvaretāḥ' | sa ca naiṣṭhikabrahmacārī | gṛhasthe tu nityam ūrdhvaretastvasyāyuktatvāt | ṛtukāla eva gacchatīti 'ṛtukālābhigāmī' | sa ca dharmapatnīṣu, yaḥ [401] sadeti vyāsavacanāt | 'tattvavit' ātmatattvajñaḥ, sa ca vināpi tridaṇḍagrahaṇāt, gṛhasthāśramādāv apy ātmajñānābhyāsasyoktatvāt | pañcayajñādyartham annaṃ pacatīti 'pacanaḥ' āvasathyaḥ | pācayatīti 'pācanaḥ' sabhyaḥ | niyatapaktṛpratyāsattilakṣaṇadharma-yogād aupacārikaṃ prayojakatvam | gārhapatya āhavanīyo dakṣiṇāgnir ity agnitrayaṃ 'tretā' | ete pañcāgnayo yasya gṛhe dīpyante | vedavratācaraṇapūrvakam arthajñānaparyantaṃ gṛhītavedaḥ ‘snātakaḥ,’ tam etaṃ śamadamādibhir guṇair upetaṃ sahasrasaṅkhyakabhoktṛdvijaparikalpitapaṅktipāvanatvāt sahasrasaṃmitam ity āhuḥ | bhrūṇahatyāṃ vā ete ghnanti ye brāhmaṇās trisuparṇaṃ paṭhanti āsahasrāt paṅktiṃ punantīti | sa eva pañcāgnitvādiguṇair upetaḥ 'śatasāhasraḥ', lakṣaparimitapaṅktipāvana ity arthaḥ | 'anṛcāṃ' vedarahitānāṃ samantād yojanaparimitadeśavyāpinīṃ paṅktiṃ vedarahasyavid brahmavit punāti |
āha gautamaḥ |
paṅktipāvanāḥ | ṣaḍaṅgavit jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ triṇāciketas trisuparṇaḥ pañcāgniḥ snātako mantravid brāhmaṇavid dharmajño brahmadeyānusantāna iti |
āha viṣṇuś caivaṃ |
yathā pitryeṣu karmasv iti paṅktipāvanā evaṃ deveṣv api |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnis trisuparṇaś caturmedhī vājasaneyī ṣaḍaṅgavit brahmadeyānusantānaḥ chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago mantrabrāhmaṇavid yaś ca dharmam adhīte yasya ca daśapuruṣaṃ mātṛpitṛvaṃśaḥ śrotriyo vijñāyate vidvāṃsaḥ snātakāś caite paṅktipāvanā bhavanti |
[402] 'caturmedhī' cāturmāsyayājī | tatra hi catvāro medhā bhavanti | jyotiṣṭomayājī vā | evaṃrūpo vājasaneyīti saṃbandhaḥ | vidvāṃsa iti bahuvacanaṃ vaiduṣyasyānekarūpatvārthaṃ |
āpastambaḥ |
trimadhus trisuparṇas triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgniḥ chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago vedādhyāyy anūcānaputra ity ete śrāddhe bhuñjānāḥ paṅktipāvanā bhavanti |
baudhāyanaḥ |
trimadhus triṇāciketas trisuparṇaḥ pañcāgniḥ ṣaḍaṅgavit triśīrṣako jyeṣṭhasāmaga iti paṅktipāvanāḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
athātaḥ paṅktipāvanā bhavanti triṇāciketas trimadhus trisuparṇaś cīrṇavratachandogo jyeṣṭhasāmago vedādhyāyy anūcānaputro brahmadeyānusantānaḥ sahasrado rudrādhyāyi caturvedaṣaḍaṅgavid atharvaśiro’dhyāyī pañcāgnir vedajāpī ceti paṅktipāvanāḥ | teṣām ekaikaḥ punāti paṅktiṃ niyukto mūrddhani sahasrair athāhvayatāṃ | ‘rudrādhyāyī' śatarudrīyajapaśīlaḥ |
uśanāḥ |
tatra brāhmaṇo vedavidyāvratasnātakaḥ pañcapuruṣapāraṃparyavikhaṇḍita-cāritramaryādas trisuparṇo ’ṅgavij jeṣṭhasāmagaḥ śatavarṣāluptadharmādhyayanaḥ mantraprabhṛtijāpyapravacanapādyapārago ṛtukālābhigāmī chandasā śuddhadaśapuruṣaḥ snātaka iti paṅktipāvanāḥ |
atra viṣṇuḥ |
atha paṅktipāvanāḥ triṇāciketaḥ pañcāgnir jyeṣṭhasāmago vedapārago [403] vedāṅgasyāpy ekasya pāragaḥ purāṇetihāsavyākaraṇapāragaḥ dharmaśāstraikasyāpi pāragaḥ tīrthapūto yajñapūtas tapaḥpūtaḥ satyapūto dānapūto mantrapūto gāyatrījapanirato brahmadeṣānusantānas trisuparṇo jāmātā dauhitraś ceti pātraṃ viśeṣeṇa vayona iti |
brahmadeyā brāhmavivāhoḍhā eva santāno yasya asau 'brahmadeyānusantānaḥ' |
brahmadeyānusantāno brahmadeyāpradāyakaḥ |
brahmadeyāpatiś caiva brāhmaṇāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
ṣaḍaṅgavit jñānayogī sarvatantras tathaiva ca |
yāyāvaraś ca pañcaite vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ #
sarvatantraśālīnākhyo gṛhasthaviśeṣaḥ | yāyāvaro ’pi gṛhasthaviśeṣaḥ
ye bhāṣye vaidike kecid ye ca vyākaraṇe ratāḥ |
adhīyānāḥ purāṇaṃ ca dharmaśāstrāṇy athāpi vā ||
caturdaśānāṃ vidyānām ekasyā api pāragaḥ 1
śrāddhakalpaṃ paṭhed yaś ca sarve te paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
gārgyaḥ |
dātuṃ bhokuṃ ca jānanti ye viprāḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ |
saṃskārakarmavijñānasaṃpannāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
mahādevācanarato mahādevaparāyaṇaḥ |
vaiṣṇavo vātha yo nityaṃ sa vipraḥ paṅktipāvanaḥ ||
[404] āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
athāpy udāharanti |
atha cen mantravid yuktaḥ śārīraiḥ paṅktidūṣaṇaiḥ |
adūṣyantaṃ yamaḥ prāha paṅktipāvana eva saḥ ||
mantravidgrahaṇaṃ guṇaprakarṣopalakṣaṇārtham | tasmin sati śarīragatair doṣair yuko ’pi na dūṣayitavyaḥ | janmāntarīyaduḥkhānubhavena kṣīṇaprāyadoṣacihna-saṃbandhitvāt | yamagrahaṇaṃ stutyarthaṃ | sa hi karmavipākābhijña iti | paṅktipāvana evāsau saṃpratyanaparādhitvād iti |
iti paṅktipāvanāḥ |
atha paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ |
vṛddhamanuḥ |
yaś ca vyākurute vātha yaś ca mīmāṃsate ’dhvaram |
yaś ca vetty ātmakaivalyaṃ paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
yaḥ pāṇiniprabhṛtipraṇītaśabdānuśāsanānusāreṇa prakṛtipratyayavibhāgena śabdān vyutpādayati, yaś ca mīmāṃsāśāstravicārapariṇatatayā adhvaraṃ yajñakarma mīmāṃsate vivicya pratipādayati, yaś ca ātmakaivalyaṃ brahmādvaitaṃ vetti sākṣātkurute, ete paṅktipāvanebhyo ’pi pāvanāḥ, atiśayena puṇyatamā ity arthaḥ | ataḥ sarvebhyo ’pi pūrvapratipāditebhyaḥ prathamaṃ prayatnād ete gaveṣaṇīyā ity avagantavyam 1
matsyapurāṇe |
yaś ca vyākurute vācaṃ yaś ca mīmāṃsate ’dhvaram |
sāmasvaravidhijñaś ca paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
[405] brahmavaivarte |
vedavedāṅgavid yajvā śānto dāntaḥ kṣamānvitaḥ |
vītaspṛhas tapasvī ca paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
dhīmān alolupaḥ śāntaḥ saṃtuṣṭo yena kenacit |
samadṛṣṭiḥ satyavādī paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
samaḥ śatrau ca mitre ca samaloṣṭāśmakāñcanaḥ |
na rajyate na ca dveṣṭi paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
garuḍapurāṇe |
śāṅkhyaśāstrārthanipuṇaḥ yogaśāstrārthatattvavit |
vedāntaniṣṭhabuddhiś ca paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
priyavādī śucir maunī sarvabhūtahite rataḥ |
ekāntaśīlo dhyānī ca paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
yamaiś ca niyamaiḥ pūtaḥ pratyāhāraparāyaṇaḥ |
priyāpriyābhyām aspṛṣṭaḥ paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
prāṇāyāmaparo dhīro maitraḥ karuṇa eva ca |
adhyātmajñānapūtātmā paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
saurapurāṇe |
yo na nindati na dveṣṭi na śocati na kāṅkṣati |
ātmārāmaḥ pūrṇakāmaḥ paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
yas tu sarvāṇi bhūtāni ātmany evānupaśyati (?) |
sarvabhūteṣu cātmānaṃ sa ca pāvanapāvanaḥ ||
[406]        aśanāyāpipāsābhyāṃ śītoṣṇādibhir eva ca |
aspṛṣṭaḥ śokamohābhyāṃ paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
om ity ekākṣaraṃ brahma vyāharaty aniśaṃ śuciḥ |
ātmaikatānadhīr yo ’sau paṅktipāvanapāvanaḥ ||
yatendriyas taponiṣṭho nirāśīr aparigrahaḥ |
jñānavijñānacittātmā paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ ||
iti paṅktipāvanapāvanāḥ |
atha yogināṃ śrāddhe niyoga ucyate |
tatrāha vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
yogino bhojayen nityaṃ dṛṣṭatattvān manīṣiṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ tu dattam akṣayyaṃ bhavatīti na saṃśayaḥ ||
‘yoginaḥ’ aṣṭāṅgayogayuktān | 'tattvāni' mahadādīni, deśakālakaraṇāvasthāsattāsvarūpeṇa [broken letter] viśiṣṭāni śāstrataḥ pratyakṣataś ca yair dṛṣṭāni tān | atha vā 'tattvaṃ' āropitasaṃśayānadhyavasato ’sya dṛṣṭātmasvabhāvaḥ, sa vedāntavākyavicārasamudbodhaiḥ udarkaniścayālokanasamutpanna-jitāntaḥkaraṇajanyasākṣātkāragocarīkṛto yais tān | ata eva manīṣiṇaḥ heyopādeyavivekapūrvakaṃ heyādviṣayapañcakād upādeyātmatattvalābhopāya-niṣaṇṇās tān | bhojayed ity aviśeṣato ’bhidhānād daivapitryamānuṣyeṣu karmasv iti labhyate, prakaraṇād vā pitryesv iti | nityam iti niyamena | nātra jīvanavato ’har ahar yogitvanimittaṃ bhojanaṃ vidhīyate | kiṃ tu nimittāntarād eva prāpteṣu devapitryādiṣu bhojanātmakeṣu karmasūpasthiteṣu bhojanīyānāṃ yogināṃ ca lābhe sati yogino ’vaśyaṃ bhojanīyā yatheṣṭam anye [407] ity eṣa nityam ity asyārthaḥ | ādarārtho vā nityaśabdaḥ | phalādhikyārthaś cādaraḥ | ādhikyam āha teṣāṃ tu dattam akṣayyaṃ bhavatīti niścaya eva na saṃśaya iti |
brahmavaivartte ’pi yogipraśaṃsāpūrvakaṃ tadbhaktasya phalagauravaṃ darśitaṃ |
ṛco hi yo veda sa veda vedān
yajūṃṣi yo veda sa veda yajñān |
sāmāni yo veda sa veda brahma
yogān hi yo veda sa veda sarvam ||
sa khalu vedān veda yo ṛco veda sa eva bhuṅkte ya eva ghṛtena bhuṅkte iti ca | eṣā śrutiḥ | evam uttaratrāpi | atha ṛgbhiḥ śrāddhoktānāṃ yaḥ ṛcāṃ vettā sa sarvavedavettaiva bhavatīti śrutiḥ | 'yajūṃṣi' saṃnipatyopakārakārād upakārakāntaravargasahitadarśapūrṇamāsādiyajñavidhāyakavākyarūpāṇi smārakāṇi ca mantrarūpāṇi, 'yo veda sa yajñān veda' | 'sāmāni' jyeṣṭhasāmādīni ṛgādhāragītirūpāṇi, tāni yo veda asau (?) parabrahmajñānopāyabhūtaṃ śabdabrahma veda | viśuddhamanojanyasākṣātkāratvān mānasa ātmā yas taṃ 'veda', sa sarvaṃ 'veda' nāsya kiṃcid veditavyam avaśiṣyata ity arthaḥ |
tathā |
sarvaṃ yogīśvarair vyāptaṃ trailokyaṃ hi nirantaram |
yasmāt paśyanti te sarvaṃ yat kiṃcij jagati sthitam ||
vyaktāvyaktaṃ vaśīkṛtya sattvaṃ cāpi tataḥ param |
sac cāsac cobhayaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ sarvaṃ sattvaṃ mahātmanā ||
[408]        sarvajñajñānasṛṣṭāni mokṣādīni mahātmanām |
tasmāt teṣāṃ sadā bhaktaḥ prāpnoty unmeṣavac chubham ||
trayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ samāhāraḥ ‘trailokyaṃ', 'tat sarvaṃ yogīśvaraiḥ’
avikṣatayogatayā yogiṣv aiśvaryaṃ mahimānaṃ prāptaiḥ, ‘nirantaraṃ sarvakālaṃ’ ‘vyāptaṃ' svena rūpeṇa saha saṃbandhaṃ nītam | vyāptyanumāpakaṃ liṅgam āha | 'yasmāt paśyanti te sarvaṃ’, iti | 'sarvaṃ' vedajātaṃ, 'paśyanti' sākṣātkurvanti | tathā ca pātañjalaṃ sūtram “bhuvanajñānaṃ sūryasaṃyamāt” iti | 'vyaktam' sarvalokapratītiyogyaṃ sthūlaṃ vastu, kāryam ity arthaḥ | 'avyaktaṃ' sūkṣmaṃ, kāraṇam ity arthaḥ | atha vā 'vyaktaṃ' guṇakāryaṃ kṣityādiḥ, 'avyaktaṃ' prakṛtiḥ | 'vaśīkṛtya' santa iti śeṣaḥ | 'sattvaṃ' sattvapradhānā buddhir antaḥkaraṇaṃ vā | tasmāt paraṃ paramātmānaṃ ca paśyanti,
manasas tu parā buddhir yo buddheḥ paratas tu saḥ |
iti bhagavadvacanāt | satprayogagocaraṃ 'sat’| tadviparītaṃ ‘asat' | 'ubhayātmakaṃ' sadasat | mahatā ātmanā bhagavatā brahmaṇety arthaḥ | 'sarvvajñaḥ' īśvaraḥ, tadviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ 'sarvajñajñānaṃ' | yatas teṣāṃ mahātmanāṃ sarvajñajñānena 'sṛṣṭāni mokṣādīni' | yata evaṃ śiṣṭās te atas teṣāṃ 'bhaktaḥ' śuśrūṣādikartā, 'unmeṣavat' uttarottaram upacīyamānaṃ, 'śubhaṃ' bahuvidhaṃ phalaṃ, 'prāpnoti' |
tathā |
kriyayā gurupūjābhir yogaṃ kurvanti yoginaḥ |
tena cāpyāyayante te pitaro yogavardhanāt |
āpyāyitāḥ punaḥ somaṃ pitaro yogabhūṣitāḥ | |
āpyāyayanti yogena trailokyaṃ yena jīvati ||
[409]        pitṝṇāṃ hi balaṃ yogo yogāt somaḥ pravartate |
tasmāc chrāddhāni deyāni yogināṃ yatnataḥ sadā ||
'kriyayā' varṇāśramādhikāravihitenāgnihotrādikarmaṇā, 'gurupūjābhiḥ' parāparagurubhaktibhiś ca, viśiṣṭaṃ yogino yogam aṣṭāṅgaṃ kurvanti | tadyogajanyena cāpūrveṇa svapitṛpitāmahādīn agniṣvāttādīṃś ca 'āpyāyayante' prīṇyanti | te cāpyāyitāḥ 'somaṃ' candraṃ āpyāyayanti | yena hy āpyāyitena satā candreṇa trailokyam āpyāyyamānaṃ 'jīvati' ciram anuvartate | asyām āpyāyanaparamparāyāṃ hetur uktaḥ pitṝṇāṃ hi balam ityādinā | ato yogināṃ viśvajīvanopayogiyogānuṣṭhānavattvena sarvebhyo ’pi vaiśiṣṭhyād abhyarthanādikleśamayenātiprayatnenāpi tān āmantrya satkṛtya ca tebhyaḥ śrāddhāni deyāni |
tathā |
aśvamedhasahasrāc ca rājasūyaśatād api |
puṇḍarīkasahasrāc ca yogibhuktaṃ viśiṣyate ||
aśvamedhādayaḥ puṇḍarīkāntāḥ kratavaḥ |
somādhvaraḥ pitṛgaṇo yogādhvaraś ca candramāḥ |
śrāddhe yoginiyogas tu tasmāt kāryo vijānatā ||
tasmāt sarvaṃ paricchinnaṃ viśvam etad iyattayā |
avidyāntargataṃ yoge na ceyattāpramā matā ||
tasmād anantaphaladaṃ pradattaṃ yogināṃ haviḥ |
kāraṇānugataṃ kāryam iti tvaṃ mānyayā kṛthāḥ ||
jñānī yasya gṛhe 'śnāti jalaṃ pibati vā kvacit |
[410]        kṛtakṛtyo bhaved eṣa yajamāno na saṃśayaḥ ||
‘niyogaḥ' nimantraṇam | avidyāntargatam etad viśvaṃ sarvam eva iyattayā ‘paricchinnaṃ’ kalitam | yoge tu 'iyattāpramā' parimāṇaparikalanā, nāsti | ato ’parimitayogaviśiṣṭebhyaḥ pradattaṃ haviḥ 'anantaphaladaṃ ' aparimitaphaladam eva bhavati | atra hetuḥ | kāraṇānugataṃ kāryam iti | kāraṇadharmānuvidhāyino hi kāryadharmā dṛṣṭāḥ | ato ’parimitāt kāraṇād aparimitam eva kāryaṃ bhavitum ucitam | ity etat 'mānyathā kṛthāḥ' mānyathāmaṃsthā ity arthaḥ |
chāgaleyaḥ |
mūrttaṃ brahma hariḥ sākṣād daityārir yogavin mataḥ |
ato yogavido bhojyāḥ śrāddheṣu pitṛtṛptaye ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
jñānī yasya gṛhe ’śnāti udakaṃ vā pibed yadi |
kṛtaṃ teneha satkṛtyaṃ tāritaṃ ca kulatrayaṃ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
ṣaḍaṅgavit jñānayogī yogatattvajña eva ca |
ayācitāśī vipro yaḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi bhojayet ||
samādhiśabdavācyaśuddhātmaparāmarśarūpayogabhūmikāniṣaṇṇo jñānayogīty ucyate |
brahmavaivarte |
bahucchidraḥ purā proktaḥ paitro yajño maharṣibhiḥ |
niṣpratyūhaś ca niśchidro jāyate yogarakṣayā ||
[411] 'niṣpratyūhaḥ' yātudhānādikṛtopaghātarahitaḥ | 'niśchidraḥ' nyūnātiriktatādidoṣajanitaphalavaikalyavivarjitaḥ |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
yoginaś ca tadā śrāddhe bhojanīyā vipaścitā |
yogādhārā hi pitaras tasmāt tān pūjayet sadā ||
tad etad amāvāsyāṣṭakākutapāparāhṇādikāle | sadeti sarveṣu śrāddhaprayogeṣu |
tathā |
brāhmaṇānāṃ sahasrasya yogī tv agrāsanī yadi |
yajamānaṃ ca bhoktṝṃś ca naur ivāmbhasi tārayet |
brāhmaṇānāṃ sahasrasya paṅktāv agre ārambhe sthitam āsanaṃ yasyāsau 'agrāsanī' | tārayet saṃsṛtimahārṇavāditiśeṣaḥ |
tathā |
pitṛgāthās tathaivātra gīyante brahmavādibhiḥ |
yā gītāḥ pitṛbhiḥ pūrvam ailasyāsan mahīpateḥ ||
kadā naḥ santatāv agryaḥ kasyacid bhavitā sutaḥ |
yo yogibhuktaśeṣānnāt bhuvi piṇḍān pradāsyati ||
'ailasya mahīpateḥ' purūravasaḥ, purastāt pitṛbhir yā 'gītāḥ' kīrtitāḥ āsan bhuktaśeṣād annāt kiyad apy uddhṛtya 'naḥ' asmabhyaṃ, piṇḍān pradāsyatīti |
gāthāntaram āha āpastambaḥ |
api syān na kule jāto bhojayed yas tu yoginam |
[412]        vipraṃ śrāddhe prayatnena tena tṛpyāmahe vayam ||
iti yogināṃ śrāddhe niyogaḥ |
atha yogināṃ sarvotkṛṣṭatvam ucyate |
padmapurāṇe |
sāṅgān yaś caturo vedān yo ’dhīte sa kṛtārthavat |
janmavān karmasaṃśuddhaḥ sa śrāddhe prayato mataḥ |
tādṛśād ayutāc chreyān eko yogasamāśrayaḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
sahasraśas tu viprān vai bhojayed yān yathāgatān |
ekas tān mantravit prītaḥ sarvān arhati dharmataḥ ||
etān eva ca dharmajñān bhojayed yaḥ sahasraśaḥ |
ekas tān snātakaḥ prītaḥ sarvān arhati śṛṇvataḥ ||
saṃkalpināṃ sahasreṇa snātakānāṃ śatena ca |
yogācāryeṇa yad bhukaṃ trāyate mahato bhayāt ||
gṛhasthānāṃ sahasrāc ca vānaprasthaśatād api |
brahmacārisahasrāc ca yogī tv eko viśiṣyate ||
nāstiko vā vidharmo vā saṃkīrṇas taskaro ’pi vā |
atyantaṃ tārayen nūnaṃ yogīty āha prajāpatiḥ ||
yas tiṣṭhed ekapādena vāyubhakṣaḥ śataṃ samāḥ |
dhyānayogī paras tasmād iti brahmānuśāsanaṃ ||
‘yathāgatān' yathāprāptān, svādhyāyādhyayanatadarthajñānādiśūnyān iti yāvat | tān etān sahasraśas tu bhojitān eka eva svādhyāyatadarthavid[413]brāhmaṇe bhojitaḥ sann arhati | tāvatsaṅkhyakabrāhmaṇabhojanotpādyaṃ sukṛtaṃ janayituṃ samartha ity arthaḥ | 'snātakaḥ prītaḥ' bhojaneneti śeṣaḥ | 'saṃkalpinaḥ' nānāvratānuṣṭhānaśīlāḥ | yogācāryeṇa caikena tattulyatayā mahataḥ pāpabhayād bhojayitāraṃ 'trāyate' rakṣati |
kūrmapurāṇe |
bhojayed yoginaṃ śāntaṃ tattvajñānarataṃ yatim |
alābhe naiṣṭhikaṃ dāntam upakurvāṇakaṃ tathā ||
tasmāt yatnena yogīndram īśvarajñānatatparam |
bhojayed dhavyakavyeṣu alābhād itarān dvijān ||
vāyupurāṇe |
gṛhasthānāṃ sahasreṇa vānaprasthaśatena ca |
brahmacārisahasreṇa yogī tv eko viśiṣyate ||
nāstiko vā vikarmā vā saṃkīrṇas taskaro ’pi vā |
nānyat tu kāraṇaṃ dāne yogiṣv āha prajāpatiḥ ||
pitaras tasya tuṣyanti suvṛṣṭeneva karṣakāḥ |
putro vāpy atha vā pautro dhyāninaṃ yas tu bhojayet ||
āhośanāḥ |
yaṃ kaṃcid api yo veda yogadharmasamāśritaḥ |
samyagvarṇāśramās tebhyas tat pātraṃ paramaṃ mataṃ ||
ye samyagvarṇāśramācārāḥ, 'tebhyaḥ' 'tat' pūrvārdhenokaṃ, 'paramaṃ pātraṃ
mataṃ' ity arthaḥ | etad yoginaḥ praśaṃsā śātātapena dṛṣṭatatvān iti viśeṣitatvāt |
[414]        brahmavaivarte ca
ye tu vṛtte sthitā nityaṃ jñānino dhyāninas tathā |
iti vṛttasthānām eva jñānināṃ dhyānināṃ bhojanīyatvābhidhānāt ātmāditattvaparisthitiḥ ‘jñānaṃ' mūrtāmūrtabrahmānucintanaṃ 'dhyānaṃ' |
mahābhārate bhagavadgītāsu |
tapasvibhyo ’dhiko yogī jñānibhyaś cādhikā mataḥ |
karmibhyaś cādhiko yogī tasmāt yogī bhavārjuna || iti |
iti yogināṃ sarvotkṛṣṭatvanirūpaṇaṃ |
athānyatra yogino ’tikramadoṣam āha |
chāgaleyaḥ |
yoginaṃ samatikramya gṛhasthaṃ yadi pūjayet |
na tat phalam avāpnoti sarvaṃ gotraṃ pratāpayet ||
'gṛhasthaṃ' ayoginam | tasya karmaṇaḥ phalaṃ na labhate | tathā 'sarvaṃ gotraṃ' sarvān api svagotrodbhavān pūrvapuruṣān, 'pratāpayet' yogyatikramajanitapratyavāyena duḥkhinaḥ kuryād ity arthaḥ |
tathā |
yoginaṃ samatikramya bhojayanti parasparam |
dātā bhoktā ca narakaṃ gacchataḥ saha bāndhavaiḥ ||
atra dātṛbhoktror ubhayor api narakagamanaṃ, dvayor api yogyatikramābhiprāye satīti veditavyaṃ | ata āha parasparaṃ bhojayantīti | ayam idānīṃ bhojitaḥ san svagṛhe māṃ bhojayiṣyati kiṃ yoginety abhiprāyavān yoginaṃ dātā bhojayati | bhoktāpi tathaivānusaṃdadhāno bhuṅkta iti | dvayor api yogyatikramābhiprāyaḥ | yadā tu dātur [415] evātikramo ’bhipretas tadā na bhoktuḥ pratyavāyaḥ | abhiprāyam antareṇa pramādakṛte vyatikrame śrāddhavaikalyamātraṃ, na tadā kasyāpi nirayapāta iti |
vṛddhaśātātapo ’pi |
yoginaṃ samatikramya gṛhasthaṃ yadi bhojayet |
na tat phalam āpnoti svargastham api pātayet ||
yo gṛha eva tiṣṭhati na yoge ’sau atra 'gṛhasthaḥ' | 'svargasthaṃ' svapitṛvargam iti śeṣaḥ |
yoginaṃ tu vyatikramya pūjayanti parasparam |
bhoktāras tu sadātāro narake syuḥ sabāndhavāḥ || iti |
iti yogino ’tikrame doṣanirūpaṇaṃ |
atha gṛhasthādīnāṃ yogitvam ucyate |
tatra yogo nāma mukhyayā vṛttyā jīvaparamātmanor ekībhāvalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | sa yady api naisargikatvena sarvakṣetrajñasādhāraṇas tathāpy anādyavidyākṛtabhedābhāsatiraskṛtaḥ san na saṃsṛtimatāṃ jīvānāṃ, kiṃ tu muktānām evāsti | asya copāya ātmatattvajñānam | tadupāyaś ca yamādisamādhyanto ’ṣṭāṅgo ’pi yogaḥ | yujyate ’nena jīvaḥ paramātmanā saheti karaṇavyūtpattim āśritya mukhyayā vā vṛttyā sa yogaḥ | tadvanto gṛhasthādayo ’pi śrutisāmarthyāt yoginaḥ | na caitad vaktavyam | mokṣopāyatvāt yogasya, mumukṣor eva yogasaṃbandho na gṛhasthasyeti | maivaṃ | yato bahutaradoṣadūṣitaviṣayarasavirajyamānamanaso gṛhasthasyāpi sarvapuruṣārthātiśāyini mokṣe kāmanopapadyate| satyāṃ ca tasyāntadupāyānuṣṭhānasamarthasya viduṣo nirvyūḍhādhikāratayā yuktam eva yogānuṣṭhānam | samyaganuṣṭhitayogasya ca mākṣa iti | na ca [416] svāśramakarmānuṣṭhānāvaruddhakālasya gṛhasthasya yogābhyāsānavakāśa iti vācyam (?) | bhikṣor iva niyatakālakarmānuṣṭhānāntarāleṣu tasyāpy avakāśasaṃbhavāt | na ca turīyāśramasadhrīcīnasyaiva yogasya mokṣasādhanatvāt gṛhasthasyānarthakaṃ yogānuṣṭhānam | yataḥ smaranti |
nyāyārjitadhanas tattvajñānaniṣṭho ’tithipriyaḥ |
śrāddhakṛt satyavādī ca gṛhastho ’pi vimucyate || iti |
śrūyate ca gṛhasthānām eva janaka-jābāla-vāmadeva-prahlādaprabhṛtīnāṃ yogābhyāsān mokṣa iti | atra ca tattvajñānaniṣṭha ity ātmatattvajñānaniṣṭha ity avagantavyam | anena cārthad ātmatattvajñānopāyabhūto yamādiḥ samādhyanto ’ṣṭāṅgo ’pi gṛhasthasyokto bhavati |
tathā ca kūrmapurāṇe |
gṛhasthā naiṣṭhikāḥ śāntā vanasthā yatayo ’pi vā |
yoginaḥ paryupāsante paramātmānam ātmanīti ||
ato gṛhasthādayo ’pi yoginaḥ | evaṃ ca sati svādhyāyādiguṇasaṃpadā tulyeṣu gṛhasthādiṣv api madhye ye yoginas tatpuraḥsarebhyaḥ śrāddhāni deyānīti |
atha yater atyantaviśiṣṭatayā śrāddhaniyoga ucyate |
tatra āha manuḥ |
jñānaniṣṭhā dvijāḥ kecit taponiṣṭhās tathāpare |
tapaḥsvādhyāyaniṣṭhāś ca karmaniṣṭhās tathaiva ca ||
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratiṣṭhāpyāni yatnataḥ |
havyakavyāni deyāni sarveṣv eva caturṣv api ||
[417] 'jñāniṣṭhaḥ' parivrājakaḥ | tasya hy ātmatattvajñāne niṣṭhā prakarṣo bhavati | 'taponiṣṭhaḥ' vānaprasthaḥ | sa hi tāpasa ity ākhyāyate | grīṣme pañcatapās tu syād iti tapovidhānāt | 'tapaḥsvādhyāyaniṣṭhāḥ' brahmacāriṇaḥ | 'karmaniṣṭhāḥ ' gṛhasthāḥ | eṣām api 'kavyāni ' pitṝn uddiśya dīyamānāny annāni, 'jñānaniṣṭheṣu pratiṣṭhāpyāni ' tebhyo deyānīty arthaḥ | prayatnavacanāt tadabhāve anyebhyo ’pi deyāni | havyāni caturṣv api pratiṣṭhāpyāni | 'yathānyāyaṃ' śāstroktena vidhinā |
bārhaspatyasaṃhitāyām |
jñānaniṣṭhā hi yatayas taponiṣṭhā vanāśrayāḥ |
karmaniṣṭhā hi gṛhiṇa ādyāḥ svādhyāyaniṣṭhitāḥ ||
'ādyāḥ' brahmacāriṇaḥ |
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratipādyāni yatnataḥ |
havyakavyāni deyāni sarveṣv eva caturṣv api || iti |
yamasmṛti-kūrmapurāṇayoḥ |
prakṛter guṇatattvajño yasyāśnāti yatir haviḥ |
phalaṃ vedavidāṃ tasya sahasrād atiricyate ||
tasmād agrāsane bhojyo yajatā saṃyato yatiḥ |
śrotriyo brahmacārī vā yajñas tena na lupyate ||
yater anagrabhojane doṣam āha yamaḥ |
yasya vai yajamānasya nāgre bhuṅkte yatis tathā |
[418]        aniṣṭam adbhutaṃ tasya harate rakṣasāṃ gaṇaḥ ||
'adbhutam aniṣṭaṃ' putrakalatrādināśarūpam, tasya saṃbandhitayā rakṣasāṃ gaṇaḥ ‘harate' upāharatīty arthaḥ |
brahmavaivarte |
yāni śrāddhāni yogīndrā yatayo brahmaniṣṭhitāḥ |
alaṃkurvanti tāny eva pitṝṇāṃ tṛptaye dhruvam ||
pañcaviṃśatitattvajñān pañcatantrāśrame ratān |
yatīn āhuḥ svadhāsvāhāyogyān pātratayā budhāḥ ||
rāgadveṣavinirmukrān āptān ye yogino yatīn |
bhojayanti phalaṃ teṣām anantaṃ tatpradāyinām ||
jñānaniṣṭheṣu kavyāni pratiṣṭhāpyāni yatnataḥ |
havyāni ca yathānyāyaṃ na mithyāhaṃkṛtātmasu ||
yogācāryaṃ yatiṃ samyag yaḥ pūjayati bhaktitaḥ |
sa phalaṃ samavāpnoti kāraṇānugataṃ budhaḥ ||
mūrtaṃ brahma hariḥ sākṣād daityārir yogavit tataḥ |
ahaṃ nārāyaṇo brahmā yatidehasamāśrayaḥ ||
dhyānena satataṃ yuktaṃ sarvasaṅgavivarjitam |
daive kāryaṃ ca pitrye ca pūjayed dharmaretasam ||
chāgaleyaḥ |
[419]        gandhamālyaphalaiś caiva bhojanaiḥ kṣīrasaṃskṛtaiḥ |
pūjayec ca yatiṃ śrāddhe pitṝṇāṃ tuṣṭikārakam ||
brahmacārī yatiś caiva pūjanīyau hi nityaśaḥ |
tatkṛtaṃ sukṛtaṃ yat syāt tasmāt ṣadbhāgam āpnuyāt ||
jāpyaṃ yogaś ca yajñaś ca yatas tasmiṃs trayaṃ sthitam |
tasya praṇāmaḥ pūjā ca dattaṃ bhavati cākṣayam ||
gṛhasthasyāśramaṃ gacched brahmacārī yatis tathā |
khādyaṃ pānaṃ phalaṃ puṣpam ātmānaṃ parivedayet ||
satataṃ yogayuktānāṃ vītarāgatapasvinām |
sarvārambhanivṛttānāṃ yatīnāṃ dattam akṣayam ||
yataye vītarāgāya dattam annaṃ supūjitam |
na kṣīyate śraddhayā vai kalpakoṭiśatair api ||
atra yateḥ sarvottvamāśramāśrayatvena jñānadhyānadhurīṇatvena ca bhojanīyeṣu mukhyatayā saṃpādanaṃ kartavyam |
tathā ca vāyupurāṇe |
yatis tu sarvaviprāṇāṃ sarveṣām agrabhug bhavet |
itihāsapañcamān vedān yaḥ paṭheta dvijottamaḥ ||
anantaraṃ yateḥ so ’yaṃ niyoktavyo vijānatā |
trivedo ’nantaras tasmāt dvivedas tadanantaraḥ ||
ekavedas tataḥ paścāt nityādhyāyī tataḥ paraṃ |
[420] 'sarvaviprāṇāṃ' brahmacārigṛhasthavānaprasthānām, 'sarveṣāṃ' trimadhutrisuparṇatriṇāciketādīnām | agryaḥ mukhyaḥ bhoktā yaḥ tasmād dhi 'agrabhuk', na tu pūrvakālabhojī | yo dvijasattamaḥ setihāsāṃś caturo vedān adhīte so ’yaṃ yater anantaraṃ paścān niyojanīyaḥ | evaṃ trivedādīnāṃ paurvāparyam | 'nityādhyāyī' adhīyamānavedaḥ |
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
āmantrayed yatīn pūrvaṃ snātakān vā trikarmiṇaḥ |
tadabhāve gṛhasthāṃś ca brahmajñānaparāyaṇān ||
yājanādhyāpanadānāni trīṇy eva karmāṇi yeṣāṃ te 'trikarmiṇaḥ' | 'snātakāḥ' vānaprasthā ity arthaḥ |
āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
pitṛbhyo dadyāt pūrvedyur brāhmaṇān saṃnipātya yatīn gṛhasthān sādhūn pariṇatavayasaḥ || iti |
'saṃnipātya' nimantraṇena śrāddhakriyāsaṃbandham āpādya |
nāradasmṛtau |
yo vai yatīn anādṛtya bhojayed itarān dvijān |
vijānan vasato grāme havyam āpnoti rākṣasān ||
