yaḥ svagrāme vasato yatīn jānann api tān anādṛtyānyān gṛhasthādīn eva śrāddhe bhojayet tasya tat sakalaṃ śrāddhabhojanīyam annaṃ rākṣasān eva tarpayati na pitṝn iti | anena svagrāme vasato yateḥ śrāddhe nimantraṇīyatvam [421] uktam | śranimantritasyāpi śrāddhakāle bhikṣārthamāgatasya yaterbhe-
janī yatvamuktaṃ |
brahma-mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇayoḥ |
bhikṣārtham āgatāṃś cāpi kāle saṃyamino yatīn |
bhojayet praṇipātādyaiḥ prasādya yatamānasaḥ ||
atra nimantritasya yater nimantraṇād eva dvāradeśagatasya cātithitvenaiva nimantritabrāhmaṇapaṅktibhojanīyatve siddhe tadgrāmasthasyaiva tu pramādād animantritasya śrāddhakāle ’py upasthitasya yateḥ śrāddhe bhojanīyatvārtham idaṃ vacanaṃ | bahuvacanaṃ tu bahūnām api prāptyarthaṃ yatibhedasaṃgrahārthaṃ vā | yater upaveśane sthānaviśeṣa uktaḥ |
bārhaspatyasaṃhitāyāṃ |
śrāddhakāle yatiṃ prāptaṃ pitṛsthāne tu bhojayet |
pariveṣayet tat prathamaṃ yadi vā pārśvataḥ sthitam ||
'pitṛsthāne bhojayet' iti pramītoddeśena tyajyamānānnaṃ bhojitaṃ kuryyādityarthaḥ (1) | 'pariveśayet tat prathamaṃ' iti mukhyaṃ pitryaṃ
kuryād ity arthaḥ |
pakṣāntaram āha 'yadi vā pārśvataḥ sthitaṃ' iti nimantritabrāhmaṇapaṅktāv atithivad vā bhojayet |
atreyaṃ vyavasthā | brāhmaṇeṣu pitṛapitāmahādikalpanātaḥ pūrvam āgataṃ yatiṃ pitṛsthāne bhojayet | paścād āgataṃ tu pūrvānuṣṭitapadārthakrama-[822] saṃkalpādyavirodhāya śrāddhīyadvijasaṃnidhāv atithivat bhojayed iti | śrāddhakālāt pūrvam api prāptaṃ samyak saṃprārthanena śrāddhakālaṃ yāvad avasthāpya bhojayet |
śrāddhakālāgatasya yateḥ brahmacāriṇaś ca śrāddhīyatvenātithitvena vā bhojanīyatvam āha yamaḥ |
bhikṣuko brahmacārī vā bhojanārtham upasthitaḥ |
upaviṣṭeṣu tu (?) prāptaḥ kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet ||
bhojane phalaviśeṣaṃ kāraṇaṃ cāha chāgaleyaḥ |
bhojayec chrāddhakāle ’pi yatiṃ sabrahmacāriṇam |
viprān uddharate pāpāt pitṛmātṛgaṇān api ||
bhuñjate yatra tatrāpi yatayo brahmacāriṇaḥ |
gṛhanti pitaro devāḥ sa yāti paramāṃ gatim ||
tārayanti ca dātāraṃ putrān dārān pitṝṃs tathā |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena arcayed āśramāgatam ||
brahmacārī yatiś caiva pakvānnasvāmināv ubhau |
pacamānāḥ pacanty ete nāpacaḥ pacate punaḥ ||
brahmacāryādibhyo yater viśeṣam āha sa eva |
brahmacārisahastrais tu vānaprasthaśatair api |
gṛhasthānāṃ sahastrais tu yatir eko viśiṣyate ||
tad evaṃ sati saṃbhave śrāddhe yatayo bhojanīyāḥ | teṣām alābhe brahmacāryādayaḥ |
[423]        tathā ca brahmavaivarte |
alābhe yatibhikṣūṇāṃ vanastham api pūjayet |
tadalābhe 'py udāsīnaṃ naiṣṭhikaṃ saṃyatendriyam ||
'udāsīnaḥ' dātur asaṃbandhī |
āha śātātapaḥ "yatīn bhojayet" iti |
tatra viśeṣa ukaḥ brahmavaivarte |
muṇḍān jaṭilakāṣāyān śrāddhakāle vivarjayet |
śikhibhyo dhāturaktebhyas tridaṇḍibhyaḥ pradāpayet ||
te hi vratasthitā nityaṃ jñānino dhyāninas tathā |
devabhaktā mahātmānaḥ punīyur darśanād api ||
'muṇḍāḥ' śikhayā saha muṇḍitamastakāḥ | 'jaṭilāḥ' śaivapāśupatakālamukhādayaḥ | ‘kāṣāyāḥ' āśvatyādikaṣāyaraktavasanāḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
etān śrāddhakāle viśeṣeṇa varjayet | ye tu śikhāvanto gairikaraktavasanāḥ tridaṇḍāś ca tebhyaḥ prayatnena dadyāt | etena sarvāṇy api yogivacanāny etallakṣaṇānvitayativiṣayāṇy eveti vijñāyate |
nanu
bhaikṣeṇa vartate nityaṃ naikānnāśī bhaved vratī |
bhaikṣeṇa vratināṃ vṛttir upavāsasamā smṛtā ||
iti manunā brahmacāriṇām ekapadattānnabhojananiṣedhāt, (?)
aṣṭau bhikṣāḥ samādāya daśa dvādaśa vā yatiḥ |
akhilāḥ śoṣayet tās tu tato ’śnīyād dvijottamaḥ ||
iti saṃvartena yater bhikṣākadambakabhojanavidhānāt tayoḥ kathaṃ śrāddhe bhojanam |
[424]        atra brūmaḥ | bhikṣārthabhramaṇāśaktāv anekānnalābhāsaṃbhave parānugrahārthaṃ ca yater ekānnabhojanābhyanujñādarśanāt | tad āha kārṣṇājiniḥ |
aśakto ’nugrahārthaṃ vā yatir ekānnabhug bhavet |
vasiṣṭho ’pi |
brāhmaṇakule vā yal labheta tad bhuñjīteti |
brahmacāriṇas tv ekānnabhojanam anujajñe yājñavalkyaḥ |
brahmacarye sthito naikam annam adyād anāpadi |
brāhmaṇaḥ kāmam aśnīyāc chrāddhe vratam apīḍayan ||
brahmacāry anāpady ekasyānnaṃ nāśnīyāt | brahmacārī brāhmaṇaś cet tadārthitaḥ sann ekānnam anāpady api śrāddhe bhuñjīta | tadā vratapīḍākaraṃ madhumāṃsādi varjayitvāśnīyāt |
manur api |
vratavad devadevatye pitrye karmaṇy atharṣivat |
kāmam abhyarthito ’śnīyād vrataṃ tasya na lupyate |
'devadevatye' daivike karmaṇi, abhyarthito brahmacārī bhuṅkte | bhuñjīta katham | ‘vratavat' vratayuktam, vratāvirodhena madhumāṃsavarjam ity arthaḥ | 'pitrye' pitṛdevatye ’pi karmaṇi, bhuñjīta | 'ṛṣivat' madhumāṃsavarjam ity arthaḥ | 'kāmam' icchayā | evam asya brahmacārivrataṃ na lupyate | anyathā lupyata ity arthaḥ | ataḥ sadbhir abhyarthyamānasya tadanugraham icchato yateḥ śrāddhe bhuñjānasya na pratyavāyo bhavati | atha vaikānnabhojanajanitaṃ svalpatamaṃ pratyavāyaṃ prāyaścittarūpeṇa vartamānaiḥ prāṇāyāmādibhir yogāṅgair (?) [425] nāśayati |
nanu tathāpi śrāddhe bhojyatvena mukhyatayā madhumāṃsavidhānāt kathaṃ yater bhojanam upapadyate |
ucyate | madhumāṃsaniyamābhāvāt | tanniyame hi tadantareṇa munyannādimātreṇa śrāddhaṃ na sidhyet | na caitad iṣṭam | na cāsti śrāddhe yatiniyamaḥ | tanniyame hi yatim antareṇa śrotriyais triṇāciketādibhir gṛhasthaiḥ śrāddhaṃ na sidhyet | na caitad apīṣṭam | ato madhumāṃsavarjite śrāddhaprayoge yater bhojanam, yativarjite ca prayoge madhumāṃsopakalpanam iti na kiṃcid anupapannaṃ | madhumāṃsasya yateś ca nityavad vidher ubhayāvakāśārtham agatyā pākṣikatvam āśrīyate | atha kasmān madhumāṃsavaty api śrāddhaprayoge madhvādivarjitaṃ yater bhojanaṃ nābhyupagamyate | nirvāpapākayoḥ sarvapitrarthatvāvirodhāyeti brūmaḥ |
atha kasmāc chrāddhe yater na madhumāṃsabhojanaṃ | na yatir madhumāṃsam aśnīyād iti niṣedhād iti cet,
tan na, niṣedhasya puruṣārthatvāt kratvarthabhojanabādhe sāmarthyābhāvāt | hiṃsāniṣedhasyeva yajñīyahiṃsābādhe |
nanu paruṣārthasyāpi godohanasya kratvarthacamasabādhakatvaṃ dṛṣṭam |
satyam | yat kratvarthapadārthasādhyakāryaniṣpādakaṃ puruṣārthaṃ tatra tad vyavatiṣṭhate | na ceha kratvarthamadhumāṃsabhojanakāryaṃ puruṣārthaṃ bhojanaṃ niṣpādayitum īṣṭe | kratunā kratvīyena vā padārthena sahāsya saṃbandhābhāvāt | godohanasya tu puruṣārthasyāpi kratvīyena praṇayanena sahāśrayāśrayibhāvalakṣaṇasya saṃbandhasya vidyamānatvāt |
atha kasmān na yatir madhumāṃsam aśnīyād ity eṣa niṣedhaḥ kratvarthasya śrāddhabhojanasyaiva na syāt |
brūmaḥ | śāstraprāptaniṣedhatvāpattau duṣṭaṃ vikalpam āpādayet rāgaprāptaṃ ca madhumāṃsabhojanaṃ yater anargalam āpadyate | ubhayaniṣedho ’stv iti cet, evaṃ tarhy ekatra vidhyantarasāpekṣo ’nyatra nirapekṣa iti vaiṣamyeṇa pīḍaiva | eka eva cāyaṃ kratvarthaḥ puruṣārthaś ceti vipratiṣidhyate | ato rāgaprāptasya madhumāṃsabhojanasya puruṣārtho niṣedho na vidhiprāptasya kratūpakārakasya bhojanasya nivartakaḥ | ataḥ śrāddhe yater madhumāṃsabhojanaṃ na duṣyatīti |
atrocyate | bhojayed iti śrāddhādhikārikartṛkaṃ prayojakavyāpārātmakaṃ madhumāṃsabhojanaṃ vihitam | na tu śrāddhaniyojyayativyāpārātmakam apīti kathaṃ na niṣedhaḥ | prayojakavyāpāravidhinārthākṣiptaṃ tadvihitam eveti cet, tarhi meṣīm ubhayatomukhīṃ vā dadyād ity etadvidhākṣiptaḥ pratigraho ’pi vihita eveti na duṣyeta | kiṃ cedaṃ bhojanaṃ yadi sarvasya śrotriyāder vihitaṃ tarhi kenacid ekenāpy abhukte śrāddhavaiguṇyam āpadyeta |
atha nimantritasya vihitaṃ tarhi kimarthaṃ madhumāṃsabhakṣaṇaṃ dūṣaṇīyam | yatiṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi nimantrayet svādhikāravidhisiddhyarthaṃ pratigrahavad iti cet, yatiḥ tarhi kimarthaṃ nimantraṇam abhyupagacchet | nimantrayitur anugrahārtham iti cet, na hi kaścid ātmānam etāvati pātakapaṅke nimajjayantaṃ parānugrahaleśam icchati, iti vidālocyate | ato rāgata eva yater nimantraṇagrahaṇam avaśiṣyate | tathā ca niṣedhānupraveśān na śrāddhe ’pi madhumāṃsabhojanam | athāninditenāmantrito nāpakrāmed iti niṣedhāt nimantraṇam aṅgīkaroti, aṅgīkṛtanimantraṇasya bhuñjīraṃs te ’pi vāgyatā iti bhojanavidhānāt pariveṣitaparityāgapratiṣedhāc ca nimantraṇāṅgīkaraṇapūrvakaṃ vihitam eva yateḥ śrāddhīyamadhu-[427]māṃsabhojanam iti|
atra brūmaḥ | apakramaṇe pratyavāyamātraṃ madhumāṃsabhakṣaṇe tv āśramabhraṃśa eva bhavet | tato ’pakramaṇasyaiva nyāyyatvāt | na vāpakramaṇe pratyavāyo ’pi yatyāśramadharmaviruddhatvād anapakramaṇādividhīnāṃ yativyatiriktaviṣayatvāt | tad evam etat siddhaṃ madhumāṃsavivarjite śrāddhaprayoge yater niyogaḥ, yatiniyogavarjite ca prayoge madhumāṃsopakalpanam iti |
iti yateḥ śrāddhe niyogaḥ |
athātitheḥ śrāddhapaṅktau bhojanīyatvaṃ vaktum atithisvarūpaṃ tāvad ucyate | atra mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
ajñātakulanāmānaṃ tatkālasamupasthitam |
bubhukṣum āgataṃ śrāntaṃ yācamānam akiñcanam |
brāhmaṇaṃ prāhur atithiṃ sa pūjyaḥ śaktito budhaiḥ ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
acintyamāno nāhūto vaiśvadevam upāgataḥ |
atithiṃ taṃ vijānīyāt na punaḥ pūrvam āgatam |
acintitāgato yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate ||
śātātapo ’py āha |
priyo vā yadi vā dveṣyo mūrkhaḥ paṇḍita eva ca |
prāptas tu vaiśvadevānte so ’tithiḥ svargasaṃkramaḥ ||
manur api |
ekarātraṃ tu nivasann atithir brahmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
anityaṃ hi sthito yasmāt tasmād atithir ucyate ||
[428] brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ kṣatriyāder vyudāsārtham |
tad apy āha manur eva |
na brāhmaṇasya tv atithir gṛhe rājanya ucyate |
vaiśyaśūdrau sakhā caiva jñātayo gurur eva ceti ||
āha yamaḥ |
tithiparvotsavāḥ sarve tyaktā yena mahātmanā |
so ’tithiḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ śeṣān abhyāgatān viduḥ ||
vratī yatir vaikarātraṃ nivasann ucyate ’tithiḥ |
yasmād anityaṃ vasati tasmāt tam atithiṃ viduḥ ||
'tithiḥ' dīpālikā pratipadādiḥ | 'parvaṇi' amāvāsyādīni | 'utsavāḥ' vivāhādayaḥ | etāny annaviśeṣaprāptihetubhūtāni yena yatidharmapravaṇena satā tyaktāni so ’tithir ity arthaḥ | 'vratī' brahmacārī, 'yatiḥ' parivrājakaḥ, anayor eva vidhidharmakayor atithisaṃjñakatvam, na punar gṛhasthavanasthayoḥ | tayor abhyāgatasaṃjñakatvam uktam vāyupurāṇe |
vālakhilyo yatiś caiva vijñeyo hy atithiḥ sadā |
abhyāgataḥ pacānaḥ syād atithiḥ syād apācakaḥ || iti |
'vālakhilyaḥ' brahmacārī | 'pacānaḥ' pacamānaḥ, paktā gṛhastho vānaprasthaś cety arthaḥ | dvividhaś cātithiḥ madhyāhnāgataḥ sāyamāgataś ca |
tatra madhyāhnāgataḥ ādityapurāṇe |
madhyāhnasamaye caiva vaiśvadeve hute ’pi ca |
[429]        atithiṃ caiva saṃprāptaṃ pāṃśupādaṃ śramāturam |
taṃ pūjayeta yatnena so ’tithir brāhmaṇaḥ sadā || iti |
sāyamāgatas tu sūryoḍhaśabdavācyaḥ | tam āha pracetāḥ |
yaḥ sāyaṃ vaiśvadevānte sāyaṃ vā gṛham āgataḥ |
devavat pūjanīyo ’sau sūryoḍhaḥ so ’tithiḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sāyaṃtano vaiśvadevaḥ | na caivaṃ mantavyaṃ mādhyāhnikasāyaṃtana-vaiśvadevakāla evāgato ’tithiḥ nānyakālāgata iti | yato manur āha |
apraṇodyo ’tithiḥ sāyaṃ sūryoḍho gṛhamedhinā |
kāle prāptas tv akāle vā nāsyānaśnan gṛhe vaset || iti |
āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
apraṇodyo ’tithiḥ sāyam api vāgbhūtṛṇodakaiḥ |
sāyamāgato ’tithir annābhāve vāgbhūtṛṇodakadānato ’pi saṃpūjanīyaḥ | na punaḥ pratyākhyeyaḥ |
acintitāgatatvādilakṣaṇakasyāsya śrāddhakālopasthitasya nāmāntaram apy uktaṃ paiṭhīnasinā |
kuto ’pi śrāddhavelāyāṃ śrotriyo yadi dṛśyate |
śrāddhaṃ punāti vai yasmāt kutapas tena saṃjñitaḥ ||
kuto ’py avijñāyamānād deśād āgataḥ punāti śrāddham ity atithir eva kutapasaṃjñayocyate |
samānagrāmavāsino ’cintitāgatasyāpy atithisaṃjñakatvaṃ nāstīty uktaṃ |
[430] viṣṇudharmottare |
naikagrāmīṇam atithiṃ vidyān nābrāhmaṇaṃ tathā |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
na mitram atithiṃ kuryān naikagrāmanivāsinam |
ādityapurāṇe |
naikagrāmīṇam atithiṃ kathayanti manīṣiṇaḥ |
yat tu vāyupurāṇe,
na ghoro nāpi saṃkīrṇo nāvidyo nāviśeṣavit |
nāvasan na saṃdadhāno na sevanaparo ’tithiḥ ||
iti, tad atithibahutve śaktyabhāve ca draṣṭavyam |
yad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
yadi bahūnāṃ na śaknuyād ekasmai śīlavate dadyāt prathamam upāgataḥ syāc chrotriyas tasmā iti | tad evam avijñātagotranāmā grāmāntarato ’kasmād vaiśvadevādikāle maitryādisaṃbandharahitaḥ samāgato brāhmaṇo ’tithiḥ |
iti lakṣaṇam uktaṃ veditavyam |
ity atithisvarūpanirūpaṇam |
athātithipraśaṃsā |
śivadharmottare |
anugrahāya lokānāṃ śrāddhasaṃprekṣaṇāya ca |
caranty atithirūpeṇa devā yogāś ca bhūtale ||
'yogāḥ' yoginaḥ |
[431] ādityapurāṇe |
tasmāt gṛhāśramasthasya nānyad daivatam ucyate |
ṛte ’tither mahābhāge satyam etad vicāraya ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
dhātā prajāpatiḥ śukro vahnir vasugaṇo yamaḥ |
praviśyātithim ete vai bhuñjate ’nnaṃ nareśvara ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
devā brāhmaṇarūpeṇa caranti pṛthivīm imām |
tasmāt saṃprāptam atithiṃ prayatnena tu pūjayet ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
nātitheḥ paramo bandhur vidyate gṛhamedhinām |
gṛhān upetya kṛpayā saṃtārayati yaḥ kulaṃ ||
siddhāś caranti pṛthivīṃ kautukena yadṛcchayā |
anugṛhṇanti satataṃ dharmiṣṭhā gṛhamedhinaḥ ||
atithīnāṃ hi rūpeṇa gṛhāṅgaṇam upāgatāḥ |
tasmād atithayo mānyāḥ sarveṣāṃ sarvadā nṛpa ||
nāradīyapurāṇe |
punanti gṛhiṇāṃ gehān yadīyāḥ pādapāṃśavaḥ |
cālayaty akhilaṃ pāpaṃ yat pādakṣālanodakam ||
satkṛtiḥ praṇatir yeṣāṃ yajñād api viśiṣyate |
yeṣām annodakaṃ dattaṃ tārayaty akhilaṃ kulam ||
atithibhyaḥ paraṃ tebhyaḥ kim asti bhuvanatraye |
[432]        devānām abhivandyāḥ syur dūrād atithayaḥ śubhāḥ |
kiṃ punaḥ karmaniṣṭhānāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ mahāmate ||
kārṣṇājiniḥ |
tilā rakṣanti tv asurāt dabhī rakṣanti rākṣasān |
apaṅktyāt śrotriyo rakṣed atithiḥ sarvarakṣakaḥ ||
iti atithipraśaṃsā |
athātithiparīkṣāniṣedhaḥ |
tatra mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
na pṛcched gotracaraṇaṃ svādhyāyaṃ cāpi paṇḍitaḥ |
śobhanāśobhanākāraṃ taṃ manyeta prajāpatiṃ ||
viṣṇupurāṇe ’pi |
svādhyāyagotracaraṇam apṛṣṭvā ca tathā kulam |
hiraṇyagarbhabuddhyā taṃ manyetābhyāgataṃ gṛhī ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
rūpānvitaṃ virūpaṃ vā malinaṃ malināmbaram |
yogīndraśaṅkayā nityam atithiṃ na vicārayet ||
vāyupurāṇe ’pi |
avijñātaṃ dvijaṃ śrāddhe na parīkṣet sadā budhaḥ |
siddhā hi viprarūpeṇa caranti pṛthivīm imām ||
ūrvīṃ sāgaraparyantāṃ devā yogīśvarās tathā |
nānārūpāś caranty ete prajādharmeṇa līlayā ||
nārasiṃhapurāṇe |
[433]        na parīkṣeta caritaṃ na vidyāṃ na kulaṃ tathā |
na śīlaṃ na ca deśādīn atither āgatasya hi ||
kurūpaṃ vā surūpaṃ vā kucelaṃ vā suvāsasam |
vidyāvantam avidyaṃ vā saguṇaṃ vātha nirguṇam ||
manyeta viṣṇum evaitaṃ sākṣān nārāyaṇaṃ harim |
atithiṃ samanuprāptaṃ vicikitsen na karhicit |
vāyupurāṇe |
atithiṃ hy āgataṃ dṛṣṭvā dveṣyaṃ bandhum ivāgatam |
yādṛśaṃ tādṛśaṃ vāpi vicikitsen na jātucit ||
na hi vidyādayas tasmin pūjyatāhetavaḥ smṛtāḥ |
kevalenātithitvena sa bhavet paṅktipāvanaḥ ||
anugrahārthilokānāṃ mahātmāno mahīm imām |
caranty atithayo bhūtvā na vicāryas tatas tu te ||
guṇāguṇavicāreṇa bhavanty ete ’vamānitāḥ |
nirdahaty āś gṛhiṇaṃ tādṛśeṣv avamānanā |
ato 'tither na kartavyā kāpi caryā kadācana ||
ity atithiparīkṣāniṣedhaḥ |
athātithipūjāvidhiḥ |
tatra manuḥ |
saṃprāptāya tv atithaye pradadyād āsanodake |
annaṃ caiva yathāśakti satkṛtya vidhipūrvakam ||
hārīto ’py āha |
[434]         tasya svāgatam arghyaṃ pādyam ācamanīyam āsanaṃ pradadyād iti |
parāśaro ’pi |
atithiṃ tatra saṃprāptaṃ pūjayet svāgatādinā |
tathāsanapradānena pādaprakṣālanena ca ||
śraddhayā ca tathānnena priyapraśnādareṇa ca |
gacchataś cānuyānena prītim utpādayet gṛhī ||
yamasmṛtau |
cakṣur dadyān mano dadyād vācaṃ dadyāc ca sūnṛtām |
utthāya cāsanaṃ dadyāt sa dharmaḥ pañcalakṣaṇaḥ ||
utthāyeti śrotriyātithiviṣayaṃ |
ata evāpastambaḥ |
brāhmaṇāyānadhīyānāyāsanam annam udakam iti deyaṃ | na pratyuttiṣṭhed abhivādanīye cottiṣṭhed abhivādayet || iti |
ādityapurāṇe |
cakṣur dadyān mano dadyād vācaṃ dadyāc ca sūnṛtām |
anuvrajed upāsīta ma yajñaḥ pañcadakṣiṇaḥ ||
pādyam āsanam evātha dīpam annaṃ pratiśrayam |
dadyād atithipūjārthaṃ sa yajñaḥ pañcadakṣiṇaḥ ||
yena yena ca tuṣyeta nityam eva yathātithiḥ |
athātmanaḥ pradānena tat tat kuryād vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
śivadharmottare |
tasmād atithim āyāntam abhigacchet kṛtāñjaliḥ |
[435]        svāgatāsanapādyārghyasnānānnaśayanādibhiḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
tasmād atithim āyāntam abhigacchet kṛtāñjaliḥ|
pūjayec cārghyapādyena pādābhyañjanabhojanaiḥ ||
pipāsitāya śrāntāya saṃstnātāya bubhukṣine |
tasmai satkṛtya dātavyaṃ yajñasya phalam icchatā ||
na vaktavyaṃ sadā vipre kṣudhite nāsti kiṃcana |
tasmai satkṛtya yo dadyād apūrvo yajña ucyate ||
hīnottamātithisamavāye pūjāprakāram āha manuḥ |
āsanāvasathau śayyām anuvrajyām upāsanām |
uttameṣūttamaṃ kuryād dhīne hīnaṃ same samam || iti |
ekapaṅktyupaviṣṭānām eṣāṃ bhojane tu vaiṣamyaṃ nirākaroti hārītaḥ |
vidyātapo'dhikānāṃ ca prathamāsanam ucyate |
paṅktau sahasthitānāṃ tu bhojanādi samaṃ smṛtam || iti |
vaiṣamye doṣam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
yady ekapaṅktau viṣamaṃ dadāti
snehād bhayād vā yadi vārthahetoḥ |
vedeṣu dṛṣṭāṃ ṛṣibhiś ca gītāṃ
tāṃ brahmahatyāṃ munayo vadanti ||
'viṣamaṃ' ekasyottamam anyasya hīnam ity arthaḥ |
ity atithipūjāvidhiḥ |
athātithipūjāphalaṃ |
[436]
tatrāha manuḥ |
atithiṃ pūjayed yas tu śrāntaṃ vā dṛṣṭam āgatam |
savṛṣaṃ gośataṃ tena dattaṃ syād iti me matiḥ ||
viṣṇur api |
svādhyāyenāgnihotreṇa yajñena tapasā tathā |
nāvāpnoti gṛhī lokān yathā tv atithipūjanāt ||
ādityapurāṇe |
atithiḥ pūjito yas tu dhyāyate manasā śubham |
tat tu kratuśatenāpi phalam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
annaṃ hi datvātithaye brāhmaṇāya yathāvidhi |
pradātā sukham āpnoti devaiś cāpi prapūjyate ||
śivarahasye |
nityaṃ dadāti yaś cānnam atithibhyaḥ susaṃskṛtam |
sa yāti brahmasālokyam evam āha parāśaraḥ ||
atra mahābhārate ’tithidharmeṇa vā jayalubdhakāyāvidyamānānnatayā svaśarīrāyogyaṃ cātithyaṃ cikīrṣataḥ kapotakasya vākyam |
devatānām ṛṣīṇāṃ ca saṃvādeṣu punaḥ punaḥ |
śrutapūrvo mayā dharmo mahān atithipūjane ||
sudūrād āśayā prāptaḥ kṣuttṛṣāśramakarśitaḥ |
[437]        yaḥ pūjyate ’tithiḥ samyak sa tapaḥ kratur eva saḥ ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
satatam iha naro yaḥ pūjanaṃ cātithīnāṃ
jalatṛṇamṛduvādaiḥ sāravadbhir (?) vidadhyāt |
surasamitiṣu pūjyo devatānāṃ sadā syād
bhavati ca naraloke jāyamānaḥ samṛddhaḥ ||
ity atithipūjāphalaṃ |
athātithyam akurvato doṣaḥ kathyate |
tatra vyāsaḥ |
pathi śrāntam avijñātam atithiṃ kṣutpipāsinam |
yo na pūjayate bhaktyā tam āhur brahmaghātakam ||
ayaṃ ca brahmahatyātulyaḥ pratyavāyaḥ satyāṃ śaktāv atyantapātrībhūtasyātither atikrame vijñeyaḥ |
tathā ādityapurāṇe |
pātraṃ tv atithim āsādya śīlāḍhyaṃ yo na pūjayet |
ādatte sukṛtaṃ tasya pātakaṃ ca prayacchati ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
vaiśvadeve tu saṃprāpte na kaṃcid avamānayet |
ādāya sukṛtaṃ yāti bhagnāśas tv atithir gataḥ ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
atithir yasya bhagnāśo gṛhāt pratinivartate |
sa datvā duṣkṛtaṃ tasya puṇyam ādāya gacchati ||
[438]        'tasya' gṛhamedhinaḥ, svakīyaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ datvā tadīyaṃ puṇyam ādāya gacchati |
devalo ’pi |
atithir gṛham abhyetya yasya pratinivartate |
asaṃskṛtanirāśaś ca sa sadyo hanti satkulam ||
āha manuḥ |
āsanāśanaśayyābhir adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
nāsya kaścid vased gehe śaktito 'nārcito ’tithiḥ ||
manu-viṣṇu-śātātapāḥ |
yeṣām anaśnann atithir viprāṇāṃ vrajate gṛhāt |
te vai kharatvam uṣṭratvam aśvatvaṃ pratipedire ||
yasya caiva gṛhe vipro vaset kaścid abhojitaḥ |
na tasya pitaro devāḥ havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca bhuñjate ||
atithir yasya vai grāme bhikṣamāṇaḥ prayatnataḥ |
sa cen nirasitas tatra brahmahatyā vidhīyate ||
api śākam pacānasya śilockenāpi jīvataḥ |
svadeśe paradeśe vā nātithir vimanā bhavet ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
devātithau tu vimukhe gate yat pātakaṃ nṛpa |
tad evāṣṭaguṇaṃ puṃsāṃ sūryoḍhe vimukhe gate ||
ity ātithyam akurvato doṣanirūpaṇam |
|
atha śrāddhakālāgatasyātitheḥ śrāddhapaṅktau bhojanīyatvam ucyate |
[439]
tatrāha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇaṃ bhikṣukaṃ vāpi bhojanārtham upasthitam |
brāhmaṇair abhyanujñātaḥ śaktitaḥ pratipūjayet ||
tatkālāgataṃ bhikṣukaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ bhojayet brāhmaṇānumatena |
yamo ’pi |
bhikṣuko brahmacārī vā bhojanārtham upasthitaḥ |
upaviṣṭeṣv anuprāptaḥ kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
kāle tatrātithiṃ prāptam annakāmaṃ dvijottamam |
brāhmaṇair abhyanujñātaḥ kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet ||
yogino vividhaiḥ rūpair bhavantīty upakāriṇaḥ |
bhramantaḥ pṛthivīm etām avijñātasvarūpiṇaḥ ||
tasmād abhyarcayet prāptaṃ śrāddhakāle ’tithiṃ budhaḥ |
śrāddhakriyāphalaṃ hanti dvijendro ’pūjitā ’tithiḥ ||
asyābhojane doṣam āha śātātapaḥ |
atithir yasya nāśnāti tac chrāddhaṃ na praśasyate |
śrutavṛttavihīnaiś ca bhuktam aśrotriyaiś ca yat ||
evaṃvidhaśrāddhakartuḥ pratyavāyo bhavatīty uktam |
vāyupurāṇe |
yas tu śrāddhe ’tithiṃ prāptaṃ daive vāpy avamanyate |
taṃ vai devā nirasyanti hato yadvat parāvasuḥ ||
sarvasvenāpi tasmād dhi pūjayed atithiṃ budhaḥ |
[440]        vānaprasthagṛhasthau ca satām abhyāgatau tathā |
kālāgato yatir brahmacārī vātithisaṃjñakaḥ ||
tam avaśyaṃ śrāddhe pūjayet | yatas tasyāpūjitāraṃ śrāddhakartāraṃ devāḥ svargāl lokāt ‘nirasyanti' apanudanti | yathā hotā hotṛṣadana upaviśan nirastaḥ parāvasur iti mantreṇāsuraviśeṣaṃ parāvasuṃ nirasyati | avajñātātithike śrāddhe bhoktṝṇām api pratyavāyaṃ chāgaleya-vṛddhaśātātapāv āhatuḥ |
ātithyarahite śrāddhe bhuñjate ye ’budhā dvijāḥ |
vṛthā tenānnapākena kākayoniṃ vrajanti te ||
lobhāt pramādād vā kathaṃcid atithim avajānantaṃ yajamānam atithidharmopadeśena tatsvīkāram akārayatāṃ brāhmaṇānām ayaṃ pratyavāyaḥ | etat tv atithibhojanam atithilakṣaṇalakṣita āgate sati draṣṭavyaṃ, na punar atithivarjitaśrāddhakaraṇe doṣaśravaṇād anupasthite ’pi tasmin katham apy atithitvena parikalpyāpy anuṣṭheyam | svayaṃ parikalpitasya tallakṣaṇatvenātithitvābhāvāt |
atithitvarahitānāṃ tu kṣatriyādīnāṃ dūradeśatatkālāgatatvādinātithi-samānadharmeṇa brāhmaṇabhojanānantaraṃ bhojanīyatvam āha manuḥ |
yadi tv atithidharmeṇa kṣatriyo gṛham āvrajet |
bhuktavatsu ca vipreṣu kāmaṃ tam api bhojayet ||
atra kāmaśabdagrahaṇāt bhojanaṃ teṣām anāvaśyakam iti gamyate | yat tu,
atithitve tu varṇānāṃ deyaṃ śaktyānupūrvaśaḥ |
iti viṣṇupurāṇe atithidharmeṇa sahāgatānāṃ brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyānāṃ brāhmaṇakrameṇaikapaṅktāv evopaveśanaṃ kārayitvā bhojanaṃ dātavyam iti pratibhāti, tac chrāddhavyatiriktaviṣayam eveti veditavyam, abrāhmaṇānāṃ śrāddhe ’nvayābhāvāt | yac ca purāṇe,
śeṣān vittānusāreṇa bhojayed anyaveśmani |
iti veśmāntaraṃ nimantritāny abrāhmaṇabhojanavidhāv atitheḥ śrāddhīyapaṅktibhojanena saha viruddham iva pratibhāti, tad api tadveśmany avakāśalābhāsaṃbhavaviṣayam atithivyatiriktaśrāddhottarakālabhojanīya-brāhmaṇaviṣayaṃ vā draṣṭavyaṃ | tadāpy ādyo yaḥ so ’yaṃ śrāddhīyabrāhmaṇocchiṣṭāntopakaraṇapakṣa eva | pakṣāntare tu veśmany eveti | atithilakṣaṇarahitam evālīkātithibhāvam āviskṛtya bhojanayācanena prakārāntareṇa śrāddhe vighnakartāraṃ praty āha hārītaḥ |
caturṇāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ hanti brāhmaṇo vighnakārakaḥ |
annasyānnapateḥ paṅkter bhojanākāṅkṣiṇaṃ tathā ||
'bhojanākāṅkṣiṇāṃ' śeṣānnabhojanīyānāṃ, śrāddhe vā śrāddhārtha-pākatarpaṇīyānāṃ pitṝṇām ity arthaḥ |
ityatitheḥ śrāddhapaṅko bhojanīyatvanirūpaṇaṃ |
athaivamuktalakṣaṇānāṃ brāhmaṇānām asaṃbhave nyūnaguṇair api śrāddhaṃ nirvartayitum anukalpo ’bhidhīyate | tatra jñānotkṛṣṭrādīn utkṛṣṭatamān dvijān abhidhāyāha manuḥ |
eṣa vai prathamaḥ kalpaḥ pradāne havyakavyayoḥ |
anukalpas tv ayaṃ jñeyaḥ sadā sadbhir anuṣṭhitaḥ ||
'eṣaḥ' anantaroktaḥ, 'prathamaḥ' mukhyaḥ, 'kalpaḥ' vidhiḥ, 'havyakavyayoḥ
pradāne’ ‘ayaṃ’ tu vakṣyamāṇaḥ, 'anukalpaḥ' anu paścāt kalpanaṃ ity anukalpaḥ, mukhyābhāve ’nuṣṭhīyata ity arthaḥ | pūrvam uktkṛṣṭatamāya deyam ity uktaṃ | ataḥ paraṃ tadabhāve utkṛṣṭatarāya, tasyāpy abhāve utkṛṣṭāya, tasyāpy abhāve kiṃcid [442] utkaṣṭāya deyam iti vakṣyati | tathā pūrvam asaṃbandhine deyam ity uktaṃ | tadabhāve saṃbandhibhyo deyam ity ataḥ paraṃ vakṣyati | sa cāyam anukalpaḥ sadbhir anuṣṭhita iti praśaṃsā |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
alābhe sati bhikṣūṇāṃ bhojayed dhyāninaḥ śubhān |
asaṃbhave tu teṣāṃ vai naiṣṭhikān brahmacāriṇaḥ |
tadabhāve 'py udāsīnaṃ gṛhastham api bhojayet ||
‘bhikṣavaḥ' tridaṇḍā yatayaḥ, dhyānino vānaprasthāḥ 'udāsīnaḥ' dātur asaṃbandhī mitrāribhāvaśūnyo vā | prathamaṃ tāvat sarvotkṛṣṭatvena bhikṣava eva grāhyā iti pūrvam eva pratipāditam | tadalābhe vānaprasthāḥ | teṣām apy asaṃbhave vedapāragā naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇaḥ | teṣām apy abhāve paṅktipāvanatvādiguṇayuktagṛhasthāḥ |
brahmavaivarte |
yoginaḥ prathamaṃ pūjyāḥ śrāddheṣu prayatātmabhiḥ |
tadabhāve vedavidaḥ pāṭhamātrās tataḥ param |
viniyojyā mahān eṣa pātrasādhyo vidhir mataḥ ||
gṛhasthānām api madhye 'yoginaḥ' brahmavidaḥ mukhyāḥ | tadabhāve 'vedavidaḥ' vedārthajñāḥ | teṣām apy abhāve 'pāṭhamātrāḥ' vedasya pāṭhamātraṃ ye kurvanti |
niruktakāro 'pi vedavidāṃ praśaṃsām āha |
sthāṇur ayaṃ bhārahāraḥ kilābhūd
adhītya vedaṃ na vijānāti yo ’rtham |
yo ’rthajñaḥ sakalaṃ bhadram aśnute
nākameti jñānavidhūtapāpmā ||
[443]        kūrmapurāṇe |
sthāṇur bhāraharo vipraḥ paṭhann arthaṃ na vetti yaḥ |
sa kathaṃ pātratām eti katham arhati ketanaṃ ||
ato vidvattamāyaiva havyakavyāni dāpayet |
adhītamātravedaṃ tu tadabhāve niyojayet ||
śrotriyatvaṃ śrotriyaputratvaṃ ceti militam ubhayaṃ śraiṣṭhyahetutayā pūrvam uktam | idānīm anyatarāpāye kiṃ nyāyyam iti saṃśaye manur āha |
aśrotriyaḥ pitā yasya putraḥ syād vedapāragaḥ |
aśrotriyo vā putraḥ syāt pitā syād vedapāragaḥ ||
jyāyāṃsam anayor vidyād yasya syāc chrotriyaḥ pitā |
mantrasaṃpūjanārthaṃ tu satkāram itaro 'rhati ||
ekasya tu pitā apaṭhaḥ, svayaṃ tu sāṅgavedādhyāyī | itarasya tu pitā vedapāragaḥ, svayaṃ tu apaṭhaḥ | tayoḥ kaḥ śreyān iti saṃśayasya siddhāntam āha | anayor dvayor madhye yasya pitā śrotriyas taṃ 'jyāyāṃsaṃ' śrāddhayogyaṃ, vidyāt | 'itaraḥ' svayam eva yaḥ śrotriyaḥ, so ’pi 'mantrasaṃpūjanārthaṃ' tena ye mantrā adhītāḥ tatsaṃmānanārthaṃ, pūrvābhāve 'satkāraṃ' śrāddhe bhojanīyatvam arhati | yas tu śrotriyagotraprasūto ’pi vṛttahīno na tasya vihitākriyayā kulamātreṇa śraiṣṭhyam |
tad uktam agnipurāṇe |
kiṃ kulena viśālena vṛttahīnasya dehinaḥ |
[444]        kṛmayaḥ kiṃ na jāyante kusumeṣu sugandhiṣu ||
jātūkarṇo ’pi |
api vidyākulair yuktān vṛttahīnān dvijādhamān |
anarhān havyakavyeṣu vāṅmātreṇāpi nārcayet ||
yasya tu kule kaścid garīyān guṇo na vijñāyate na ca kaścid doṣo ’pi śrūyate svayaṃ ca vidyāvṛttasaṃpannaḥ so ’pi vikhyātavaṃśasya vidvatamasyābhāve grāhyaḥ |
āha manuḥ |
kiṃ brāhmaṇasya pitaraṃ kiṃ vā pṛcchasi mātaram |
śrutaṃ ced asti vedyaṃ vā tat mātāpitarau smṛtau ||
praśnavacanād eva saṃbandhakuladoṣāparijñānam avagamyate | 'śrutaṃ' śāstraṃ, 'vedyaṃ' śāstrāvabodhyaṃ karma, vānuṣṭheyatayā yady asti tarhi tāv eva mātāpitarau kim asya mātāpitṛvijñāneneti | vidyāvṛttayor vṛttaṃ jyāya ity āha manuḥ |
gāyatrīsāramātro ’pi varaṃ vipraḥ suyantritaḥ |
nāyantritaś caturvedī sarvāśī sarvavikrayī ||
alpataravidyo ’pi svānuṣṭhānaniṣṭhaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ, bahutaravidyo yatheṣṭaceṣṭo neti vākyārthaḥ |
matsyapurāṇe |
sāvitrīṃ japate yas tu trikālaṃ bharatarṣabha |
bhaikṣavṛttikriyāvāṃś ca sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
ketane nimantraṇe kṣamo yogyaḥ 'ketanatamaḥ' |
[445]        skandapurāṇe |
sadācāraparā nityaṃ svalpavidyā api dvijāḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ te ’pi kurvanti tṛptiṃ bhuktāḥ kulodbhavāḥ ||
kulācārasamopetān tasmāc chrāddhe niyojayet |
brāhmaṇān nṛpaśārdūla mandavidyādharān api ||
ye svalpadoṣā api bahuguṇāḥ, ye ca nirvidyā api svānuṭhānaśīlāḥ, ye ca sadoṣā api prāyaścittapūtātmānas teṣām api sarvaguṇasaṃpannapātrālābhe ’nugraha ukto mahābhārate |
śrāddhe ye ca vinirdiṣṭāḥ brāhmaṇā bharatarṣabha 1
dātuḥ pratigrahītuś ca śṛṇuṣvānugrahāt punaḥ ||
cīrṇavratā guṇair yuktā bhaveyur ye ca karṣukāḥ |
sāvitrījñāḥ kriyāvantas te rājan ketanakṣamāḥ ||
kṣatradharmiṇam apy ājau ketayet kulajaṃ dvijam |
na tv eva vaṇijaṃ tāta śrāddheṣu parikalpayet ||
agnihotrī ca yo vipro grāmavāsī ca yo bhavet |
asteyaś cātibhīruś ca sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
sāvitrīṃ japate yaś ca dvikālaṃ bharatarṣabha |
bhikṣāvṛttiḥ kriyāvāṃś ca sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
uditāstamite homī nityaṃ karmaparāyaṇaḥ |
ahiṃsraś cālpadoṣaś ca sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
[446]        gṛhasthavṛttir yaś cāpi brāhmaṇo bharatarṣabha |
saṃvṛttiḥ śuddhavṛttiś ca sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
avratī kitavaḥ stenaḥ prāṇavikray atho vaṇik |
paścāc cet pītavān somaṃ so ’pi ketanam arhati ||
arjayitvā dhanaṃ pūrvaṃ dāruṇaiḥ kṛṣikarmabhiḥ |
paścāttapo niṣeveta sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ ||
arjayitvā dhanaṃ pūrvaṃ dāruṇaiḥ kṛṣikarṣmabhiḥ |
bhavet sarvātithiḥ paścāt sa rājan ketanakṣamaḥ |
asaṃbandhināṃ śrāddhe bhojanīyatvam abhidhāya gāruḍapurāṇe |
udāsīneṣv alabdheṣu bhojyāḥ saṃbandhino ’pi hi |
mātulaḥ śyālayājyartvikśiṣyācāryādayo ’pi ca ||
'mātulaḥ' mātur bhrātā | 'śyālaḥ' patnībhrātā | 'yājyaḥ' yasyārtvijyaṃ kriyate | 'ṛtvik' yājakaḥ | 'śiṣyaḥ' adhyāpyaḥ | 'ācāryaḥ' vedādhyāpakaḥ |
āpastambaḥ |
bhojayed brāhmaṇān brahmavido yonigotramantrāntevāsyasaṃbandhān guṇahānyā tu pareṣāṃ samudetaḥ sodaryo ’pi bhojayitavyaḥ etenāntevāsino vyākhyātāḥ |
‘brahmavidaḥ' vedārthajñān adhyātmajñānaniṣṭhān vā, yonyādisaṃbandharahitān brāhmaṇān bhojayet | 'pareṣām' yonyādisaṃbandharahitānāṃ, yadi vṛttavidyādiguṇahāniḥ tadā 'samudetaḥ' sakalaguṇopetaḥ, sodaryo ’pi bhojayitavyaḥ | kim aṅga punaḥ vyavahitasaṃbandhāḥ śyālaśvaśurajāmātṛmātulādayaḥ | 'etena' samuditena [447] sodaryeṇa, 'antevāsinaḥ samīpavāsinaḥ, vyākhyātāḥ | tena bhojayed ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
śiṣyāṃś caike sagotrāṃś ca bhojayed ūrdhvaṃ tribhyo guṇavataḥ |
caśabdād yājyādyāṃś ca dvitīyacaśabdāt samānārṣeyāṃś ca | 'ūrdhvam tribhyaḥ' iti mukhyās trayas tāvad asaṃbandhina eva nimantraṇīyāḥ | asaṃbandhibhyas tribhya ūrddhvaṃ paṅktipāvanaguṇayukaṃ śiṣyādikaṃ bhojayed ity arthaḥ |         anye tv evaṃ vyācakṣate | sagotreṣu madhye tribhyaḥ puruṣebhya ūrddhvaṃ guṇavān bhojanīya iti |
atra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śiṣyān api guṇavatā bhojayet |
'guṇavataḥ' guṇaprakarṣavataḥ | apiśabdād antevāsino ’pi |
manu-kūrmapurāṇayoḥ |
mātāmahaṃ mātulaṃ ca svastrīyaṃ śvaśuraṃ gurum |
dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ bandhum ṛtvigyājyāṃś ca bhojayet ||
'mātāmahaḥ' mātuḥ pitā | 'svastrīyaḥ' bhaginyāḥ putraḥ | 'guruḥ' upādhyāyaḥ | viśaḥ prajāyā duhituḥ patir iti vyutpatyā 'viṭpatiḥ' jāmātā | atithir vā viṭpatiḥ | sa hi gṛhābhyāgataḥ san sarvāsāṃ viśāṃ lokaniṣṭha iti medhātithiḥ | śrāddhīyadvijasaṃnidhāv atithir bhojanīya ity uktaṃ | yadi tu śrāddhe bhoktāraḥ prakṛṣṭaguṇā na syuḥ śrāddhe evāsau bhojanīya ity anukalpatvaṃ |
bandhumātulaśyālādir asagotras tathā guṇī |
kāmaṃ śrāddhe ’rcayen mitraṃ nābhirūpam api tv arim ||
[448]        dviṣatā hi havir bhukaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalaṃ ||
'abhirūpam api' vidvāṃsam apīty arthaḥ | kāmam ity abhidhānād anukalpo ’yam iti gamyate |
śrāddhasaṃpada ity anuvartamāne yājñavalkyaḥ |
svasrīyaṛtvikjāmātṛyājyaśvaśuramātulāḥ |
triṇāciketadauhitraśiṣyasaṃbandhibāndhavāḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
mātāpitṛparaś caiva svasrīyaḥ sāmavedavit |
ṛtvikapurohitācāryam upādhyāyaṃ ca bhojayet ||
mātulaḥ śvaśuraḥ śyālaḥ saṃbandhī droṇapāṭhakaḥ |
ete śrāddhe niyoktavyā brāhmaṇā vedapāragāḥ ||
matsyapurāṇe |
bhojayec cāpi dauhitraṃ yatnataḥ śvaśvaraṃ gurum |
viṭpatimātulabandhum ṛtvigācāryaśyālakān ||
gurur atra śilpādiśāstropādhyāyaḥ |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
dauhitraṛtvigjāmātṛsvasrīyāḥ śvaśuras tathā |
mātāpitṛparā bhojyāḥ śiṣyasaṃbandhibāndhavāḥ ||
triṇāciketādīn abhidhāyokaṃ viṣṇupurāṇe |
ṛtviksvasrīyadauhitrā jāmātṛśvaśurās tathā |
mātulo ’tha taponiṣṭhaḥ pitṛmātṛsvasuḥ patiḥ ||
śiṣyāḥ saṃbandhinaś caiva mātāpitṛrataś ca yaḥ |
[449]        etān niyojayec chrāddhe pūrvoktān prathamaṃ nṛpa |
brāhmaṇān pitṛtuṣṭyartham anukalpeṣv anantarān ||
pūrvoktāṃs triṇāciketādīn prathamaṃ niyojayet | etān ṛtvigādīn tadanantarān anukalpeṣu niyojayet |
atra śaṅkhyadhareṇoktaṃ |
kuṣṭhakuṇitvādidoṣarahitāḥ saṃdhyopāsanamātraguṇā ete mātulādayo ’nukalpatayā niyojyāḥ | yadi tu vidvāṃsa ete bhavanti tadā mukhyakalpatvenaiveti | tad etasyāḥ kalpanāyāḥ kiṃ mūlam iti na vidmaḥ | vayaṃ tu brūmaḥ | utkṛṣṭaguṇodāsīnābhāve pratinidhīyante khalv ete | pratinidhiś ca prathamaṃ tāvat susadṛśa eveti tadalābha asadṛśa eveti | utkṛṣṭaguṇodāsīnānām utkṛṣṭaguṇā eva mātulādayaḥ susadṛśā iti prathamataḥ pratinidhīyante | tadabhāve madhyamaguṇāḥ | teṣām apy abhāve hīnaguṇā iti | evaṃ sati guṇavattaratvavedapāragatvādīni tadviśeṣaṇāny api samañjasāni syuḥ |
nanu yady ete pratinidhayas tarhi mukhyābhāve sādṛśyāt svayam eva prāpsyanti ity anukalpavacanānām ānarthakyam eva syāt |
na | pūtikavacanavan niyamārthatvopapatteḥ | yathā somābhāve bahuṣu susadṛśeṣu prāpteṣu vacanena niyamaḥ kriyate, pūtikā evābhiṣotavyā iti | evam ihāpi niyamārthāni vacanāni bhaviṣyanti | tad ukaṃ jaimininā “niyamārthaḥ kvacid vidhiḥ” iti | niyamaś caivaṃ atra yathaivotkṛṣṭaguṇānām udāsīnānām [450] abhāve prathamaṃ guṇotkarṣasādṛśyād utkṛṣṭaguṇā mātulādayaḥ prāptāḥ | evam audāsīnyasādṛśyena madhyamaguṇā udāsīnāḥ prāptāḥ tatra niyamyate mātulādaya eva grāhyā iti | evaṃ ca sati vakṣyamāṇāni saṃbandhinam atikramya vacanāny api niyamārthāni syuḥ | mukhyabrāhmaṇābhāve mātulādīn atikramyānye na grāhyā iti | tatra śvaśurān apīti | kiṃ punar nyāyenārthavādattvena neyam | amūrkhān iti tu pāṭhe sarvam anavadyam eva | atha vā śeṣam annam iṣṭaiḥ saha bhujyatām iti brāhmaṇābhyanujñāta-śrāddhopayuktaśeṣānnapratipattyarthaṃ yad iṣṭabhojanaṃ tadviṣayāṇi mūrkhānatikramavacanāni |
āha gārgyaḥ |
naikagotre havir dadyāt samānapravare tathā |
na cājñātakule dadyāt yathā kanyā tathā haviḥ ||
abhāve hy anyagotrāṇām ekagotrāṃs tu bhojayet |
asamapravarābhāve samānapravarān api ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
abhāve hy anyagotrāṇāṃ sagotrān api bhojayet |
atra sagotraśabdena yathaikagotrāḥ asaṃbandhino ’nujñāyante tathā sapiṇḍasamānodakādyāḥ saṃbandhino ’py anujñāyanta iti gamyate |
tatra viśeṣam āhātriḥ |
ṣaḍbhyas tu puruṣebhyo ’rvāk na śrāddhīyās tu gotriṇaḥ |
ṣaḍbhyas (?) tu parato bhojyā śrāddhe syur gotrajā api ||
ye ṣaḍbhyaḥ pare na bhavanti ta ihāvākśabdena gṛhyante |.[451] tataś ca gotre ṣaṭpuruṣāḥ śrāddhe na bhojanīyā iti tadvacanārthaḥ | guṇavatāṃ teṣām abhāve śrutādhyayanasaṃpannāḥ saptamādayo ’pi bhojanīyāḥ |
atra paṅktipāvanānantaraṃ baudhāyanaḥ |
tadabhāve rahasyavid ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmānīti śrāddhasya mahimā tasmād evaṃvidyaṃ sapiṇḍam apy āśayet || iti |
'rahasyavit' vedāntārthavit | yasmād ṛgyajuḥsāmāni śrāddhasya 'mahimā' mahattvāpādakāni, tasmāt 'evaṃvidyaṃ' ṛgyajuḥsāmavidaṃ, sapiṇḍam api bhojayet | tathāyaṃ sapiṇḍaśabdaḥ sāptapuruṣamātrapiṣayaḥ | tribhya ūrddhvaṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ bhojanīyatvadarśanāt | tad uktaṃ gautamena |
bhojayed ūrdhvaṃ tribhyo guṇavantam iti |
caturthād arvañcas tu na bhojanauyā eva |
tad āha sumantuḥ |
pitā pitāmahaś caiva śrāddheṣu prapitāmahaḥ |
pituḥ pitṛvyaḥ svastrīyo na bhojyāś ca sutādayaḥ ||
pituḥ sutādaya iti saə̄bandhaḥ | atra pitṛpitāmahānām uddiśyamānādhiṣṭhānabhūtatvena śrāddhabhojanaṃ niṣidhyate | na punar anyādhiṣṭhānībhūtatvenāpi pṛthak śrāddhādiṣu |
vipravad vāpi taṃ śrāddhe svakaṃ pitaram āśayet |
pitāmaho vā tat sākṣāt bhuñjīta śrāddham arcitaḥ |
ityādibhis teṣāṃ śrāddhe bhojanīyatvadarśanāt | atris tu pitrādīnāṃ guṇavattarāṇāṃ vaiśvadeve niyojyatvam uktavān |
pitā pitāmaho bhrātā putrā vātha sapiṇḍakaḥ |
na parasparam arhāḥ syuḥ na śrāddhe ṛtvijas tathā ||
ṛtvikputrādayo hy ete sakulyā brāhmaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ |
vaiśvadeve niyoktavyā yady ete guṇavattarāḥ ||
[452]        ato yadāpastambena samudetasya sodaryasya bhojanīyatvam uktaṃ tad api vaiśvadeve iti mantavyam | saguṇānām anukalpānām alābhe nirguṇānām anukalpatayā svīkāram āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
anṛśaṃsyaṃ paro dharmo yācate yat pradīyate |
ayācataḥ sīdamānān sarvopāyair nimantrayet ||
ānṛśaṃsyaṃ para utkṛṣṭo dharmas tena saguṇānām abhāve ’pi nirguṇāyāpi yācate yat pradīyate tad apy anukalpo bhavati ayācataḥ sīdamānān ayācanaśīlān kuta eva kāraṇāt sīdato nirguṇān api saguṇānām alābhe mantrayet | sarvopāyair iti yādṛśais te nimantraṇam abhyupagacchanti tādṛśair upāyair ity ādareṇa nimantraṇaṃ tad ayācanena sīdatām alābhe yācamānāya pradānam iti darśayati | evaṃvidheṣu vipreṣu sadācāravikalpadarśane ’pi nindā na kāryety āha sa eva |
yuge yuge tu ye dharmās teṣu dharmeṣu ye dvijāḥ |
teṣāṃ nindā na kartavyā yugarūpā hi te dvijāḥ ||
'yuge yuge tu ye dharmāḥ' yugānurūparāgadveṣāditāratamyanibaddhā ye ye asatyabhāṣaṇādayo duḥkhabhāvāḥ, 'teṣu dharmeṣu ye dvijāḥ' teṣu duḥsvabhāveṣu vartamānā ye dvijāḥ, yugānurūpavipreṣv api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ śreṣṭhā eva grāhyāḥ | prathamakalpaśaktasya tu anukalpānuṣṭhātur doṣam āha manuḥ |
prabhuḥ prathamakalpasya yo ’nukalpe tu vartate |
na saṃparāyikaṃ tasya durmater vidyate phalam ||
[453]        'prabhuḥ' saṃpādane samarthaḥ | 'saṃparāyaḥ' uttarakālaḥ, saṃparāyaśabdasya tadvācakatāyā amarasiṃhenābhihitatvāt | evaṃ vāyam arthaḥ | 'saṃparāye' bhaviṣyakāle bhavaṃ svargādikaṃ phalaṃ na vidyate iti |
ity anukalpanirūpaṇam |
atha saṃnihitabrāhmaṇānatikramaḥ |
tatra śātātapa-parāśarasmṛtyoḥ |
saṃnikṛṣṭam adhīyānaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ yas tv atikramet |
bhojane caiva dāne ca dahaty āsaptamaṃ kulam ||
atraikagrāmanivāsināṃ tulyaguṇānāṃ dvijānāṃ madhye svecchayā śrāddhaparyāpteṣu gṛhyamāṇeṣu daivāt keṣāṃcid atikramo 'bhavann api na doṣāya bhavatīti | buddhyā saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ nātikrāmet taṃ parityajyānyaṃ nopādadītety arthaḥ | yasmād atikrāman 'āsaptamaṃ' svaprabhṛti saptamapuruṣaparyantaṃ svakulaṃ, 'dahati' nirayāgninā bhasmīkaroti |
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate |
sannikṛṣṭam adhīyānaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ yo vyatikramet |
bhojane caiva dāneṣu dahet tripuruṣaṃ kulam ||
'tripuruṣaṃ' tripuruṣaparyantam | adhītavedasya saṃnikṛṣṭasyātikrame yo doṣa uktaḥ so ’nyasyātikrame nāstīty arthāt siddham api vyāsa-vaśiṣṭha-śātātapair hetūpanyāsena dṛḍhīkṛtam |
brāhmaṇātikramo nāsti vipre vedavivarjite |
jvalantam agnim utsṛjya na hi bhasmani hūyate ||
‘vedavivarjite’ vedādhyayanarahite vipre ’tikrānte anupātte sati tasya [454] tadatikramakṛtaprakṛtaprayojanahānipratyavāyādirūpo na kaścid doṣo jāyate | yasmān nāsau haviḥpratiṣṭhāpanayogyaḥ | yathā vahniṃ vihāya bhasmani haviḥ prakṣepayogyaṃ na bhavatīti dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
vyatikrānte na doṣo ’sti nirguṇaṃ prati karhicit |
dvividho ’tra saṃnikṛṣṭaḥ samīpagṛhavāsī śarīrasaṃbandhī mātulādiś ca |
nanu dvividhasyāpi saṃnikṛṣṭasyātikramo niṣeddhuṃ na śakyate | yasmād asaṃnikṛṣṭāpekṣayottamasya prathamakalpatvād evānatikramasiddheḥ samasya tu saṃnihitātikrame kāraṇābhāvād iti nyāyād atikramāprāpteḥ | na ceyaṃ prāptir iti vācyaṃ | yatra lāghavena nimantritatvāt tasya hīnasya tv atikramo niṣidhyamānaḥ sarvān api yogyāyogyavidhiniṣedhān bādheta | tasmād atikramasya niṣedho ’nupapannaḥ | astu tarhi nodyadād ity avatparyudāso ’yaṃ vyatikramād anyat kartavyam iti kiṃ tadanyat nimantraṇam, naitad evaṃ, niṣedhapakṣoktavikalpadūṣaṇair eva nirastatvāt | ato na paryudāseo ’pi saṃbandhiṣu vyatikramaniṣedhasya saṃbandhiniṣedhānukalpavidhibhāṃ virodhaś cāparo doṣaḥ | ato ’tikramaniṣedhasaṃbandhiniṣedhānukalpavidhīnām avirodhena vaktavyo viṣayavibhāgaḥ |
ucyate | yadā sarvottamaguṇa udāsīno na labhyate tadānukalpavidhis tadānīm eva madhyamaguṇodāsīnagrahaṇe prāpte uttamaguṇā vā madhyamaguṇā vā saṃbandhinaḥ pūrvaṃ gṛhītavyāḥ | hīnaguṇeṣu tu udāsīneṣu madhyamaguṇā vā hīnaguṇā vā saṃbandhina eva grāhyāḥ | so ’yam atikramaniṣedhasya viṣayaḥ | uktam adhīyānasya saṃnihitasyātikramo na kartavyaḥ anadhīyānasya ca kartavya iti |
[455]        idānīm anadhīyānasyāpi saṃnihitasyātikramo na kartavya ity ucyate |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
aruṇa uvāca |
evam etaj jagannātha devadeva jagatpate |
kiṃ tu yan me purā deva śrutaṃ vākyaṃ mahātmanaḥ ||
gadato nāradasyeha śṛṇu tvaṃ grahanāyaka |
gadato me suraśreṣṭha dharmam arthaṃ sukhāvaham ||
saṃnikṛṣṭaṃ dvijaṃ yas tu śuklajātiṃ priyaṃvadam |
mūrkhaṃ vā paṇḍitaṃ vāpi vṛttahīnam athāpi vā ||
nātikramen naro vidvān dāridryābhihataṃ tathā |
sapta pūrvān sapta parān puruṣān ātmanā saha ||
atikramya dvijo ghore narake pātayet khaga |
tasmān nātikramet prājño brāhmaṇān prātiveśmakān ||
saṃbandhinas tathā sarvān dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ tathā |
bhāgineyaṃ viśeṣeṇa tathā saṃbandhinaḥ khaga ||
nātikramen naras tv etān svasthitān api gopate |
atikramya mahāraudraṃ rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
saṃbandhinas tathā sarvān dauhitraṃ viṭpatiṃ tathā |
bhāgineyaṃ viśeṣeṇa tathā bandhugaṇān api ||
nātikramen naras tv etān mūrkhān api varānana || iti |
[456]        mahābhārate |
gāyatrīmātrasāro ’pi brāhmaṇaḥ pūjitaḥ khaga |
gṛhāsanno viśeṣeṇa na bhavet patitaḥ sa cet ||
yasmin brāhmaṇe gāyatrīmātram eva sarvavidyāsārabhūtaṃ brāhmaṇyasārabhūtaṃ vāsti so ’py anukalpatvena śrāddhādau pūjyatāṃ prāpnoty eva | yadi nāmaivaṃvidho bhūtvā svagṛhapratyāsannagṛhavāsī bhavet tadāsau pratyāsannatvena viśeṣataḥ pūjyatāṃ labhate | viśeṣeṇeti vacanaṃ tasyānatikramaṃ sūcayati |
ayaṃ tu bhaviṣvatpurāṇādyukto nirguṇasaṃnihitabrāhmaṇānatikramo guṇayuktāsaṃnihitabrāhmaṇālābha eva lābhe vyatikrama eva |
tathā ca mahābhārate |
yadi syād adhiko vipro dūre vṛttādibhir yutaḥ |
tasmai yatnena dātavyam atikramyāpi saṃnidhau ||
'adhikaḥ' vedādhyayanavṛttādiguṇasaṃpadā | adhikagrahaṇād yadi tu samas tadā saṃnihitātikramo na kartavya iti gamyate |
niśamyātha guṇopetaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sādhusaṃpadam |
dūrād gatvā ca satkṛtya yatnataś cāpi pūjayet || iti |
saṃnihitaṃ vedādhyayanavṛttādiguṇarahitaṃ brāhmaṇam atikramya guṇayukaṃ dūram api kṛte śrāddhādirūpe karmaṇi bhojayed ity arthaḥ |
baudhāyano ’py āha |
yasya tv ekagṛhe mūrkho dūre vāpi bahuśrutaḥ |
bahuśrutāya dātavyaṃ nāsti mūrkhe vyatikramaḥ ||
[457]        ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ’pi |
yasya tv ekagṛhe mūrkho dūrataś ca guṇānvitaḥ |
guṇānvitāya dātavyaṃ nāsti mūrkhe vyatikramaḥ ||
'vyatikramo nāsti' vyatikramadoṣo nāstīty arthaḥ | 'ekagṛhe ' ity anena caitat sūcyate evaṃvidhe viṣaye atyantasaṃnidhir apy atikramaprayojako na bhavatīti | yat tu dūrasthe guṇāḍhye saty api saṃnihitasya nirguṇasyātikramo na kartavya ity uktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
yas tv āsannam atikramya brāhmaṇaṃ patitād ṛte |
dūrasthaṃ bhojayen mūḍho guṇāḍhyaṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||
tasmāt saṃpūjayed enaṃ guṇaṃ tasya na cintayet |
kevalaṃ cintayej jātiṃ guṇān vā vitatān khaga |
tasmād āmantrayet pūrvam āsannaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ budhaḥ ||
tat pūrvoktabahutaravākyavirodhāyānatikramaṇamadhye saṃnihitānatikramadarśanārthaṃ, na tu dūrasthaguṇādyalābhe ’py anatikramārtham iti vyākhyeyam | atha vāyaṃ mūrkhān atikramya gṛhapratyāsannatatsamānādhikaraṇaśarīrapratyāsannatvaviśiṣṭabrāhmaṇaviṣayo varṇanīyaḥ | daivaviṣayatvena vā varṇanīyaḥ | tatra hi parīkṣāniṣedhamukhena guṇino gṛhasthasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt | īdṛśī vā vacanānāṃ vyavasthā | yaḥ saṃbandhaniṣedhaḥ sa pitrye, yaś cānukalpatvena vidhiḥ sa vaiśvadevike |
tad āhātriḥ |
[458]        pitā pitāmaho bhrātā putro vātha sapiṇḍakaḥ |
na parasparam arhāḥ syuḥ na śrāddhe ṛtvijas tathā ||
ṛtvikputrādayo ’py ete sakulyā brāhmaṇā dvijāḥ |
vaiśvadeve niyoktavyā yady ete guṇavattarāḥ || iti |
atha vā yaḥ saṃbandhānatikramaḥ sa śrāddhād anyasmin daive mānuṣe vā kasmiṃścid abhyudayādikālike bhojane atha vā śrāddhaprayogottarakālike śeṣānnapratipattibhojana iti sarvam anavadyam |
iti saṃnihitabrāhmaṇānatikramaḥ |
atha varjyā brāhmaṇāḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
idānīṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi varjanīyān dvijādhamān |
mahābhārate |
ata ūrdhvaṃ visargasya parīkṣāṃ brāhmaṇe śṛṇu |
'visargaḥ' visarjanaṃ tyāga iti yāvat |
atra grāhyabrāhmaṇalakṣaṇābhidhānenaiva tadanyeṣām arthād agrahaṇe siddhe ’pi yad varjanārthaṃ doṣābhidhānaṃ tat pūrvoktānāṃ guṇavatām alābhe kadācid etaddoṣarahitānāṃ grahaṇārtham | doṣavatāṃ grahaṇe dvandvaṃ siddham api prakṛtakarmavināśaṃ spaṣṭam āha satyavrataḥ |
vidhihīne phalaṃ kiṃcit durbrāhmaṇasute na ca |
durbrāhmaṇe pradattaṃ cen neha nāmutra tad bhavet ||
vidhinā yathoktadravyaiḥ putreṇa guṇavanto brāhmaṇā bhojanīyā ity āmnātam | tatra vidhihīnaṃ yathokadravyādihīnam aputreṇāpi kriyamāṇaṃ [459] śrāddhaṃ kiṃcit phalaṃ dadāty eva | vakṣyamāṇe doṣadūṣite durbrāhmaṇe tu bhojite sarvathā śrāddha (sic) vinaśyatīty arthaḥ |
yamaḥ |
ūṣare tu yathehoptaṃ bījam āśu vinaśyati |
tathā dattam anarhebhyo havyaṃ kavyaṃ ca naśyati ||
'ūṣaraṃ' kṣāramṛttikādeśaḥ |
mahābhārate |
yathoṣare bījam uptaṃ na rohen
na vāsyoptā ca prāpnuyād bījabhāgam |
evaṃ śrāddhaṃ bhuktam anarhamāṇena
neha nāmutra phalaṃ dadāti ||
vyāsa-śātātapau |
prastare vāpitaṃ bījaṃ bhinnabhāṇḍe ca dohanam |
bhasmany api hutaṃ havyaṃ tadvad dānam asādhuṣu ||
manuḥ |
anarhate yad dadāti na dadāti ya arhate |
arhānarhānabhijñānāt so ’pi dharmāc ca hīyate ||
doṣavatāṃ grahaṇe na kevalaṃ prakṛtakarmavināśaḥ kiṃ tu pratyavāyo ’pi bhavatīty etat jñāpanam api doṣābhidhānasya prayojanam | pratyavāyaś ca darśito vāyupurāṇe |
na śrāddhe bhojanīyāḥ syur ninditā brāhmaṇādhamāḥ |
yair bhuktaṃ naśyati śrāddhaṃ pitṝn dātṝṃś ca pātayet ||
'pātayet' pratyavāyena saṃyojayet |
brahmapurāṇe |
[460]        asatāṃ pragraho yatra satāṃ caivāvamānanam |
daṇḍo devakṛtas tatra sadyaḥ patati dāruṇaḥ |
dakṣaḥ |
vidhihīne tathā pātre yo dadāti pratigraham |
na kevalaṃ hi tad yāti śeṣam apy asya naśyati ||
viṣṇuḥ |
yathā plavenaupalena nimajjaty udake naraḥ |
dātṛpratigrahītārau tathaivānyau nimajjataḥ ||
tathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
vidvadbhojyāny avidvāṃso yeṣu rāṣṭreṣu bhuñjate |
tāny anāvṛṣṭim ṛṣyanti mahad vā jāyate bhayam         ||
'ṛcchanti' prāpnuvanti |
kathaṃcid guṇahīnā api doṣavantaḥ paṅktipāvanamiśritatayopaveśitā nānarthavahāḥ,
apāṅktyopahatā paṅktiḥ pāvyate yair dvijottamaiḥ |
iti manunābhihitatvāt | ata eva sumantunā kathaṃcid gṛhyamāṇānāṃ doṣavatāṃ paṅktipāvanamiśraṇam uktam |
kāṇāḥ kuṇḍāś ca maṇḍāś cāpy acarmāṇaḥ kacair vinā |
sarve śrāddhe niyoktavyā miśritā vedapāragaiḥ ||
'kāṇāḥ' ekākṣāḥ | 'kuṇḍāḥ' caṅkramaṇākṣamacaraṇaḥ | 'maṇḍāḥ' vakrajaṅghāḥ | 'acarmāṇaḥ' duścarmāṇaḥ | 'kacair vinā' khalataya ity arthaḥ |
kāśyapena tu miśraṇe viśeṣa ukaḥ |
[461]        kāṇādīn bhojayed daive śrāddhe dāne tu varjayet |
śrāddhe prasaktau vaiśvadevike bhojayet | dāne tu varjayet | paṅktipāvanāmiśriteṣu pramādād doṣavatsu bhojiteṣu doṣatāratamyavaśād enasi “guruṇi gurūṇi laghuni laghunūni” iti vacanāt yathāyathaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidheyam | etac ca vīkṣāndho na ca te kāraṇā ity etan manuślokābhiprāyaṃ varṇayatā medhātithinā samudbheditam | ayogyasya śrāddhe bhoktur api pratyavāyam āhuḥ yama-manu-śātātapāḥ |
yāvato grasate piṇḍān havyakavyeṣv amantravit |
tāvato grasate pretya dīptān ṛṣṭīn ayoguḍān ||
‘piṇḍān' grāsān | 'amantravit' vedarahitaḥ | 'dīptān' agnisaṃtaptān | ‘ṛṣṭiḥ’ āyudhaviśeṣaḥ | 'ayoguḍāḥ' ayogolakāḥ |
yamaḥ |
devatānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ ca havyakavyavināśakaḥ |
vipro ’dharmaparo mūrkho na lokān prāpnuyāt kvacit ||
tad etat sarvam abhipretya yājñavalkayenokam |
gobhūtilahiraṇyādi pātre dātavyam arcitam |
nāpātre viduṣā kiṃcid ātmanaḥ śreya icchatā ||
varjyā nirūpyanta iti pratijñātam | tatra mitrādayas tāvat manuḥ |
na śrāddhe bhojayen mitraṃ dhanaiḥ kāryo ’sya saṃgrahaḥ |
nāriṃ na mitraṃ yaṃ vidyāt taṃ śrāddhe bhojayed dvijam ||
satyām eva śrotriyatvādipūrvaguṇasaṃpadi maitryānimitena pratiṣedho ’yam | manovākkāyair anukūlaikapravṛttiḥ 'mitram' |
[462]        ‘saṃgrahaḥ’ cittānurañjanam | ‘ariḥ' śatruḥ | yo rāgadveṣayor adveṣas taṃ bhojayet |
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
tasya pretya phalaṃ nāsti śrāddheṣu ca haviḥṣu ca ||
pūrvasya pratiṣedhasyārthavādo ’yaṃ | mitraśabdo bhāvapradhānaḥ maitrīpradhānānīty arthaḥ | 'havīṃṣi' havyāni | pretyeti paralokavācakam avyayam asamānakartṛkatvena kyappratyayānupapatteḥ |
yaḥ saṃgatāni kurute mohāt śrāddhāni mānavaḥ |
sa svargāt cyavate lokāt śrāddhamitraṃ dvijādhamaḥ ||
'saṃgatāni' sakhyāni | ‘mohāt' śāstrārtham ajānan | 'svargāc cyavate' svargeṇa na saṃbadhyata ity arthaḥ | śrāddhaṃ mitraṃ mitralābhahetur yasya | dvijagrahaṇaṃ pradarśanārthaṃ, śūdreṇāpi mitrībhṛtā brāhmaṇā na grāhyā iti medhātithinā bahu prapañcitam s
sambhojanī yābhihitā paiśācī dakṣiṇā dvijaiḥ |
ihaivāste tu sā loke gaurave caikaveśmani ||
saṃbhojanī yā sahabhojanī | maitryā hi sahabhojanī pravartate |
piśācānām ayaṃ dharmo yac chrāddhe mitrasaṃgrahaḥ |
sā dakṣiṇā ihaivāste mitrārjanaphalā hi sā |
mitrārjanamātraphalā bhavatīty arthaḥ |
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
pitṛṣu daivayajñeṣu dātā svargaṃ na gacchati ||
śrāddhena yaḥ kurute saṃgatāni
[463]                na devayānena pathā sa yāti |
vinirmuktaṃ pippalaṃ bandhato vā
svargāl lokād bhraśyati śrāddhamitraḥ ||
devayānaśabdaḥ pitṛyāṇasyāpy upalakṣaṇam | vāśabda upamārthaḥ | 'pippalaṃ'
pippalaphalam |
hārītaḥ |
śrāddhena yaḥ kurute saṃgatāni
na devayānena pathā sa yāti |
vṛntāc cyutaṃ pippalaṃ bandhanād vā
lokāt svargāt cyavate śrāddhamitraḥ ||
yama-āpastambau |
saṃbhojanī nāma piśācabhikṣā
naiṣā pitṝn gacchati nota devān |
ihaiva sā bhramati kṣīṇapuṇyā
śālāntare gaur iva naṣṭavatsā ||
śrāddham adhikṛtya gautamaḥ |
na ca tena mitrakarma kuryāt |
bārhaspatyasaṃhitāyām |
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni havīṃṣi ca |
tasya pretya phalaṃ naiva syād ity anyonyabhojanāt ||
mahābhārate |
yasya mitrapradhānāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
[464]        na prīṇāti pitṝn devān na svargaṃ sa ca gacchati ||
yas tu śrāddhaiḥ kurute saṃgatāni
na devayānena pathā sa yāti |
sa vai muktaṃ pippalaṃ bandhanād vā
svargalokāc cyāvate śrāddhamitraḥ ||
tasmān mitraṃ śrāddhakṛn nādriyeta
dadyān mitrebhyaḥ saṃgrahārthaṃ dhanāni |
yaṃ manyate naiva śatruṃ na mitraṃ
taṃ madhyasthaṃ bhojayet śrāddhakalpe ||
utkṛṣṭaguṇodāsīnābhāve kadācid utkṛṣṭaguṇaṃ mitraṃ grāhyam | śatrus tv anukalpatayāpi na grāhya ity āha manuḥ |
kāmaṃ śrāddhe ’rcayen mitraṃ nābhirūpam api tv ariṃ |
dviṣatā hi havir bhuktaṃ bhavati pretya niṣphalaṃ ||
'abhirūpaṃ' yogyam |
saṃbhavaty udāsīne mitraṃ na grāhyam evety uktaṃ
bārhaspatyasaṃhitāyām |
udāsīnam atikramya yaḥ śrāddhe mitram āśayet |
parasparasya dānād dhi tasya nāmutra tat phalam ||
vāyupurāṇe |
na bhojayed ekagotrān samānapravarāṃs tathā |
etebhyo hi havir dattaṃ bhuñjate na pitāmahāḥ ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
na śrāddhe bhojayed viprān sagotrān brāhmaṇān kvacit |
[465]        sagotrair hi havir bhuktam ihaiva parivartate ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
na bhojyā yonisaṃbandhā gotrasaṃbandhinas tathā |
mantrāntevāsisaṃbandhāḥ śrāddhe viprāḥ kadācana ||
brahmavaivarte |
śiṣyāś ca ṛtvijo yājyāḥ suhṛdaḥ śatravas tathā |
śrāddheṣu śvasuraḥ śyālā na bhojyā mātulādayaḥ ||
iti mitrādayaḥ |
athānadhīyānādayaḥ |
tatra manu-vyāsa-yamāḥ |
brāhmaṇo hy anadhīyānas tṛṇāgnir iva śāmyati |
tasmai havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate ||
yathā stenādayaḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ, evam anadhīyānas tatkalpadoṣa ity evamarthaṃ punarvacanam | yathā tṛṇāgnir na śaknoti havīṃṣi paktuṃ hutamātre ca haviṣi ‘śāmyati’ kṣīṇaśaktir bhavati, evam anadhīyāno mūrkhaḥ |
yatheriṇe bījam uptvā na vaptā labhate phalam |
tathānṛce havir dadyān na dātā labhate phalam ||
īriṇaṃ' ūṣaram | ‘anṛce' vedarahite |
śātātapaḥ |
nāvidvatsu pradeyaṃ tu havyaṃ vā kavyam eva ca |
mahābhārate | [13.90.40]
[466]        yathā praśānte jvalane juhoti
tan naiva devān na pitṝn upaiti |
tathā dattaṃ nṛtyate gāyate ca
yāñ cānṛce dakṣiṇām ātṛṇeḍhi [āvṛṇoti?] ||
ubhau hinasti na bhunakti caiṣā
yā cānṛce dakṣiṇā dīyate vai |
athāyanīgartam eṣā patantī
teṣāṃ pretān pātayed devayānāt ||
'ubhau' dātṛbhokārau | 'na bhunakti' na pālayati | 'ayanau' pāpagatiḥ |
kūrmapurāṇe |
yasya vedaś ca vedau ca vicchidyete tripauruṣam |
sa vai durbrāhmaṇo nārhaḥ śrāddhādiṣu kadācana |
vyāsaḥ |
saṃdhyāhīne vratabhraṣṭe vipre vedavivarjite |
dīyamānaṃ rudaty annaṃ kiṃ mayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam ||
viṣṇuḥ |
na vāry api prayaccheta biḍālavratike dvije |
na bakavratine pāpe nāvedavidi dharmavit ||
triṣv apy eteṣu yad dattaṃ vidhinopārjitaṃ dhanam |
dātur bhavaty anarthāya paratrādātur eva ca ||
yamaḥ |
[467]        avratānām amantrāṇāṃ jātimātropajīvinām |
naiṣāṃ pratigraho deyo na śilā tārayec chilām ||
tathā |
rājadhānī yathā śūnyā yathā kūpaś ca nirjalaḥ |
yathā hutam anagnau ca tathā dantaṃ dvije ’nṛce ||
vāyupurāṇe |
prāha vedān vedabhṛto vedād yaś copajīvati |
ubhau tau nārhataḥ śrāddhaṃ putrikāpatir eva ca ||
vedaprāptaye dattavetanaḥ 'bedabhṛtaḥ' | 'vedopajīvanaṃ' ghaṭikādhyayanādidravyagrahaṇam |
mahābhārate |
anuyoktā ca yo vipro yo ’nuyuktaś ca bhārata |
nārhatas tāv api śrāddhaṃ brahmavikrayiṇau hi tau ||
yaś cādeśapūrvakaṃ hi svādhyāyam ātmano ’bravīt |
vedavikrayiṇo hy ete naitān śrāddheṣu bhojayet ||
amuṃ vargam amum adhyāyaṃ paṭhety ādiṣṭāḥ santo ye ’dhīyate te dattānuyogāḥ |
ye kurvanti yad aśnanti juhvate dadate ca yat |
vidhivat tena pāpena mahac chrubhraṃ (?) patanti te ||
abhojanīyās te sadbhis te cābhojyās tathā smṛtāḥ |
tebhyo dātā ca bhoktā ca mahādoṣān avāpnuyāt ||
svakarmakulaśuddhānāṃ satyasthānāṃ mahātmanām |
[468]        saṃsatsv avyavahāryās te strīśūdrakṛtaniścayāḥ ||
asaṃskṛtādhyāpakāś ca bhṛtakādhyāpakāś ca ye
tamo viśanti te ghoram iti vaivasvato ’bravīt ||
saurapurāṇe |
vedān ye nigadantīha lobhān mohāt phalārthinaḥ |
brahmavikrayiṇas te vai śrāddhakarmaṇi varjitāḥ ||
yamaḥ |
na pratigraham arhanti vṛṣalādhyāpakā dvijāḥ |
śudrasyādhyāpanād vipraḥ pataty atra na saṃśayaḥ ||
ity anadhīyānādayaḥ |
atha śarīradoṣiṇaḥ |
tatra vasiṣṭhaḥ |
bhojayed dvirnagnaśuklaviklavaśyāvadantakunakhivarjaṃ |
'dvirnagnaḥ' duścarmā khalatir ity apare | 'śuklaḥ' śvetrī | 'viklavaḥ' kuṣṭhī | 'śyāvadantaḥ' svabhāvata eva kṛṣṇadantaḥ | 'kunakhī' kutsitanakhaḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
na vai duṣṭān bhojayet | duścarmakunakhikuṣṭhiśvitriśyāvadantā ye cānye hīnātiriktāṅgās tān api varjayet |
'duścarmā' gajacarmarogī | kuṣṭhī prasiddhaḥ | 'śvitrī' śvetakuṣṭhavān | 'hīnaṃ' parimāṇataḥ saṅkhyayā vā nyūnaṃ, 'aṅgaṃ' avayavo yasya sa [469] ‘hīnāṅgaḥ' | evam atiriktāṅgaḥ | hīnatvaṃ cāṅgānāṃ svabhāvataḥ chedādinā vāpi bhavati | tac cobhayam api saṃgṛhyate | āmantrayed ity anuvṛttau kātyāyanaḥ |
dvirnagnaśuklaviklavaśyāvadantaviddhaviprajananavyādhitavyaṅgaśvitri-kunakhivajam |
'viddhaprajananaḥ' viddhaśiśnaḥ | 'vyādhitaḥ' duścikitsyarogaḥ, devaloktapāparogavān vā | 'vyaṅgaḥ' hīnāṅgaḥ |
brahmapurāṇe |
bhoktuṃ śrāddhe na cārhanti daivopahatacetasaḥ |
ṣaṇḍo mūkaś ca kunakhī khalvāṭo dantarogavān ||
śyāvadantaḥ pūtināsaḥ chinnāṅgaś cādhikāṅguliḥ |
galarogī ca gaḍumān sphuṭitāṅgaś ca sajvaraḥ |
khañjatūparamaṇḍāś ca ye cānye hīnarūpiṇaḥ ||
'ṣaṇḍaḥ'' ṣoḍhā pūrvoktalakṣaṇaḥ | 'mūkaḥ' vāgrahitaḥ | 'khalvāṭaḥ'
keśarahitaḥ | 'pūtināsaḥ' pūtivannāsākṣirogī | 'galarogī' galagaṇḍādikaṇṭharogavān | ‘gaḍumān' kuñjaḥ | 'khañjaḥ' kuṣṭaḥ | 'yauvane ’py ajātaśmaśruḥ 'tūparaḥ' |
skandapurāṇe |
kāṇāḥ kuṇḍāś ca maṇḍāś ca khalvāṭāḥ śyāvadantakāḥ |
kunakhāḥ kuṣṭhinaś caiva dvirnagnā viddhamehanāḥ ||
kāṇāḥ kuṇḍāś ca maṇḍāś ca mūkāndhabadhirā jaḍāḥ |
atidīrghā atihrasvā atisthūlā bhṛśaṃ kṛśāḥ ||
[470]        nirlomāno ’tilomāno gaurāḥ kṛṣṇā atīva ye |
etān vivarjayet prājñaḥ śrāddheṣu śrotriyān api ||
'jaḍāḥ' mūkamanasaḥ |
śālaṅkāyanaḥ |
aviddhakarṇair yad bhuktaṃ lambakarṇais tathaiva ca |
dagdhakarṇaiś ca yad bhuktaṃ tad vai rakṣāṃsi gacchati ||
lambakarṇalakṣaṇam (?) āha gobhilaḥ |
aviddhakarṇaḥ kṛṣṇaś ca lambakarṇas tathaiva ca |
varjanīyāḥ prayatnena brāhmaṇāḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
hārītaḥ |
hanusthalād adhaḥ karṇau lambau tu parikīrtitau |
dvyaṅgulau tryaṅgulau śastau tena śātātapo ’bravīt ||
iti śarīradoṣiṇaḥ |
atha ninditācārāḥ |
vyāsa-śātātapau |
ūṣare vāpitaṃ bījaṃ yac ca bhasmani hūyate |
kriyāhīneṣu yad dattaṃ tasya nāśo vidhīyate ||
'kriyāhīnāḥ' vihitānuṣṭhānaśūnyāḥ |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
nābrāhmaṇāya dātavyaṃ na deyaṃ brāhmaṇe ’kriye |
na brāhmaṇabruve caiva na ca durbrahmaṇe dhanam ||
abrāhmaṇādīnāṃ lakṣaṇāny ukāni saṃjñāprakaraṇe |
[471]        hārītaḥ |
vikrayī śaṃsate paṇyaṃ krayī nindati tadguṇān |
vyāvyopāyaṃ [vyājopāyaṃ?] ca kitavam aśrāddheyaṃ prajāpatiḥ |
'vyājaḥ' chalaṃ, 'kitavaḥ' dhūrtaḥ |
yamaḥ |
nāśnanti piśune devā nāśnanty anṛtavādini |
bhāryājitasya nāśnanti yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
na kharair upayātasya na raktāntaravāsasaḥ |
dyaṅgulātītakarṇasya bhuñjate pitaro haviḥ ||
'piśunaḥ' paradoṣaprakāśakaḥ | 'bhāryājitaḥ' bhāryāparatantraḥ | 'upapatiḥ' nityajāraḥ | raktam āntaraṃ vāsaḥ paridhānavastraṃ yasya sa raktāntaravāsāḥ | vedhasthāne tālapatrādipraveśena yasya karṇapāśāv aṅguladvayād adhikaṃ pravṛddhau sa ‘dyaṅgulātītakarṇaḥ' |
āpastambaḥ |
nīlīkarṣaṇakartā tu nīlīvastrānudhārakaḥ |
kiṃcin na tasya dātavyaṃ cāṇḍālasadṛśo hi saḥ ||
yaḥ kṣetre nīlībījāni nivapati sa 'nīlīkarṣaṇakartā' |
manuḥ |
avratair yad dvijair bhuktaṃ parivetrādibhis tathā |
apāṅkteyair yad anyaiś ca tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate ||
hārītaḥ |
āmapātre payo yadvad bhasmany eva hutaṃ yathā |
[472]        kṣudrakarmaṇy avṛttasthe tadvad vittaṃ praṇaśyati ||
yamaḥ |
mohād dattāni naśyanti kavyāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
viprebhyo bhasmabhūtebhyo bījāni ca tathoṣare ||
śānte yathāgnau ghṛtam ājuhoti
tad eva devān na pitṝn upaiti |
tathā dattaṃ nṛtyate gāyate ca
yāñ cānṛce dakṣiṇāṃ vai dadāti ||
kāmaṃ vipraṃ vṛttavantaṃ niyujyān
na tv evam anyebhyas tathā pradeyam |
krośaty annam anṛce dīyamānaṃ
tasmai dattaṃ yātudhānā haranti ||
tasmāt tulyaviśiṣṭebhyo dānaṃ dadyāt prayatnataḥ |
vidyātapaḥsamṛddhā hi tārayanti dvijātayaḥ ||
avivāhikajātasya havir dadyāc ca yo naraḥ |
na tasya pitaro devāḥ pratigṛhṇanti tad dhaviḥ ||
daive vā yadi vā paitre yatra bhuṅkte tu madyapaḥ |
devānāṃ ca pitṝṇāṃ ca dātus tan nopatiṣṭhate ||
madyapāya na dātavyaṃ daive pitrye tathā haviḥ |
tasmai naśyati tad dattaṃ yathā bhasmani vai hutam ||
dātus tu vidyate hasto bhoktur jihvā ca bhidyate |
[473]        jvalitān grasate grāsān śrāddhaṃ tad anṛtaṃ bhavet |
madyapaś ca niṣādaś ca yatra bhuṅkte dvijādhamaḥ ||
na tatra bhuñjate devā na pibanti havis tathā |
vṛṣalīpatibhuktāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca ||
na prīṇanti pitṝn devān dātā svargaṃ na gacchati |
hārītaḥ |
vṛṣalīpatibhuktāni śrāddhāni ca havīṃṣi ca |
devānāṃ nopatiṣṭhanti dātā svargaṃ na gacchati ||
yamaḥ |
ye hy apetāḥ svakarmabhyaḥ stenās te parikīrtitāḥ |
kavyaṃ dadāti yas tebhyas tasya tat pretya naśyati ||
jñānapūrvaṃ tu ye tebhyaḥ prayacchanty alpamedhasaḥ |
purīṣaṃ bhuñjate sarve śatavarṣāṇi suvrata |
tasmād dhi dātukāmena daivaṃ śrāddhaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
praśāntebhyaḥ pradātavyaṃ nāśāntebhyaḥ kadācana ||
naṣṭaṃ devalake dattam apratiṣṭhaṃ tu vārdhuṣe |
yat tu vāṇijyake dattaṃ neha nāmutra tad bhavet ||
bhasmanīva hutaṃ havyaṃ dattaṃ paunarbhave dvije |
somavikrayiṇe viṣṭhā bhiṣaje pūyaśoṇitam |
śleṣmā tu patite caiva kṛtaghne viṣam ucyate ||
itareṣu tv apāṅkteṣu yathodiṣṭeṣv asādhuṣu |
medastvaṅmāṃsamajjādi bhavati pratiyācitam ||
[474]        vāyupurāṇe |
vṛthā dārāṃś ca yo gacchet yājayec cāvṛto 'dhvare |
nārhaty asāv api śrāddhaṃ dvijo yaś caiva nāstikaḥ ||
ātmārthaṃ yaḥ paced annaṃ na devātithikāraṇāt |
nārhaty asāv api śrāddhaṃ patito brahmarākṣasaḥ ||
sadā raktāmbarā ye ca parivādaratāś ca ye |
arthakāmaratā ye ca na tān śrāddheṣu bhojayet ||
mahābhārate |
adhīyānaḥ paṇḍitaṃ manyamāno
yo vidyayā hanti yaśaḥ pareṣām |
brahman sa ācarati brahmahatyāṃ
lokās tasya hy etadantā bhavanti ||
sarve ca vedāḥ saha ṣadbhir aṅgaiḥ
sāṃkhyaṃ purāṇaṃ ca kule ca janma |
naitāni sarvāṇi gatir bhavanti
śīlavyapetasya narasya rājan ||
ākarṣaṇaṃ śatrunibarhaṇaṃ ca
kṛṣir vaṇijyaṃ paśupālanaṃ ca |
śuśrūṣaṇaṃ cāpy aguror ahetoḥ
kāryaṃ naitad vidyate brāhmaṇasya ||
ete dvijā ninditam ācarantaḥ
[475]                pitrye ca daive ca vivarjanīyāḥ |
tān ninditān mohavaśena gṛhṇan
mahātmanā majjayate pitṝṃś ca ||
iti ninditācārāḥ |
athāśramabāhyāḥ |
kārṣṇājiniḥ |
catvāra āśramāḥ puṇyāḥ śrāddhe daive tathaiva ca |
caturāśramabāhye tu śrāddhaṃ naiva pradāpayet ||
pracetāḥ |
āśramād ye bahirbhūtā dvijarūpeṇa rākṣasāḥ |
tebhyo havyaṃ na dātavyaṃ na hi bhasmani hūyate ||
devalaḥ |
caturāśramabāhyāya dattaṃ bhavati nisphalam |
vāyupurāṇe |
yas tiṣṭhed vāyubhakṣas tu caturāśramabāhyataḥ |
ayatir mokṣavādī ca ubhau tau paṅktidūṣakau ||
'ayatiḥ' asaṃyatendriyaḥ |
ugreṇa tapasā yuktaḥ śrotriyo ’pi bahuśrutaḥ |
anāśrame tapas tepe taṃ vipraṃ na nimantrayet ||
aupapattis tathā śākyo nāstiko vedanindakaḥ |
dhyāninaṃ ye ca nindanti sarve te paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
[476]        'aupapattiḥ' upapattivādī śuṣkatarkaiḥ śākyasāhacaryāt |
vṛthāmuṇḍāś ca jaṭilāḥ sarve kārpaṭikās tathā |
nirghṛṇān bhinnavṛttāṃś ca sarvabhikṣāṃś ca varjayet ||
muṇḍajaṭilakāṣāyān śrāddhe yatnena varjjayet |
kūrmapūrāṇe |
buddhaśrāvakanirgranthāḥ pañcarātravido jināḥ |
kāpaṭikāḥ pāśupatāḥ pāṣaṇḍā ye ca tadvidhāḥ ||
yasyāśnanti havīṃṣy ete durātmānas tu tāmasāḥ |
na tasya yad bhavec chrāddhaṃ pretya ceha phalapradam ||
anāśramī yo dvijaḥ syād āśramī vā nirarthakaḥ |
mithyāśramāś ca ye viprā vijñeyāḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
ādityapurāṇe |
anāśramī tu yo vipro jaṭī muṇḍī vṛthā ca yaḥ |
vṛthākarmaparityāgaḥ śrāddhe taṃ dūratas tyajet ||
nandipurāṇe |
brahmajñānāpadeśena ye kurvanty aśubhaṃ mahat |
sarvakarmasu varjyās te cāṇḍālā dvijarūpiṇaḥ ||
sarvāvasthā dvijā ye te brahmavidyāpadeśataḥ |
deśān nirvāsanīyās te rājñā vai dharmacāriṇā ||
padmapurāṇe |
mithyāsaṃkalpinaḥ sarve durvṛttā vā dvijātayaḥ |
mithyātattvavido varjyā dāmbhikāḥ sarvakarmasu ||
brahmopadeśaṃ kurvanti janānām arthalipsayā |
svayaṃ mūḍhās tu mūḍhānāṃ dhūrtā vai vipralambhakāḥ ||
brahmavikrayiṇaḥ pāpān prītyā vāpi na pūjayet |
varṇāśramaviruddhānāṃ karmaṇāṃ ye tu sevakāḥ ||
muṇḍān jaṭilakāṣāyāṃs tāṃs tu śrāddhe vivarjayet |
kālikāpurāṇe |
anāśramī tu yo vipro jaṭī muṇḍī vṛthā ca yaḥ |
vṛthākāṣāyadhārī yaḥ śrāddhe taṃ dūratas tyajet ||
ye saṇnyāsāpadeśena durātmāno jaḍādhamāḥ |
parityajanti karmāṇi te ’pi varjyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
adṛṣṭatattvā api ye karmamārgād bahiḥ sthitāḥ |
patitās te na saṃdehaḥ saṃtyājyāḥ sarvakarmasu ||
viṣṇupurāṇe |
puṃsāṃ jaṭādharaṇamuṇḍavatāṃ vṛthaiva
madyāśinām akhilaśaucavivarjitānām |
toyapradānapitṛpiṇḍabahiṣkṛtānāṃ
saṃbhāṣaṇād api naro narakaṃ prayāti ||
ity āśramabāhyāḥ |
atha ketanānarhāḥ |
tatra mahābhārate |
[478]        yāvantaḥ patitā viprā jaḍonmattās tathaiva ca |
daive vāpy atha vā pitrye rājan nārhanti ketanam ||
'patitāḥ' pātityahetupātakavantaḥ | 'unmattāḥ' unmādarogiṇaḥ |
śvitrī kuṣṭhī ca klībaś ca tathā pakṣahataś ca yaḥ |
apasmārī ca yaś cāndho rājan nārhati ketanam ||
'klībaḥ' napuṃsakaḥ | pakṣāghātarogeṇa karmākṣamadehārddhaḥ 'pakṣaitaḥ' |
cikitsakā devalakāḥ vṛthāniyamadhāriṇaḥ |
somavikrayiṇaś caiva rājan nārhanti ketanam ||
'devalakāḥ' mūlyena devapūjakāḥ | 'vṛthāniyamadhāriṇaḥ' dāmbhikāḥ | 'somavikrayiṇaḥ' somalatāvikretāraḥ |
gāyakā nartakāś caiva plavakā vādakās tathā |
kathakā bodhakāś caiva rājan nārhanti ketanam ||
'plavakāḥ' nāṭikāḥ | 'bodhakāḥ' vaitālikāḥ |
hotāro vṛṣalānāṃ ye vṛṣalādhyāpakās tathā |
tathā vṛṣalaśiṣyāś ca śrāddhe nārhanti ketanam ||
ye śūdrān vyākaraṇādiśāstram adhyāparyānti te vṛṣalādhyāpakāḥ | evaṃ vṛṣalaśiṣyāḥ |
yena kāmāt kṛtaḥ pūrvaṃ varṇāntaraparigrahaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ sarvavidyo ’pi rājan nārhati ketanam ||
yena brāhmaṇādikumārīr apariṇīya mukhyatayā varṇāntaraparigrahaḥ kṛtaḥ |
[479]        anagnayaś ca ye viprā mṛtaniryātakāś ca ye |
stenāś ca patitāś caiva rājan nārhanti ketanam ||
‘anagnayaḥ’ anāhitāgnayaḥ | mūlyena pretanirhārakāḥ 'mṛtaniryātakāḥ' | 'stenāḥ' caurāḥ |
aparijñātapūrvāś ca gaṇapūrvaś ca bhārata |
putrikāputrapūrvāś ca śrāddhe nārhanti ketanam ||
aparijñātāḥ pūrve pūrvajā yeṣāṃ te 'aparijñātapūrvāḥ' | gaṇeṣu saṅgheṣu pūrvā mukhyāḥ 'gaṇapūrvāḥ' | 'putrikāputrapūrvāḥ' putrikāputrasantānīyāḥ |
ṛṇakartā ca yo rājan yaś ca vārddhuṣiko dvijaḥ |
prāṇivikrayavṛttiś ca rājan nārhanti ketanam |
yo ’nāvaśyakavyayārtham ṛṇaṃ kurute saḥ 'ṛṇakarttā' | 'vārddhuṣikaḥ'
dhānyavṛddhijīvī | 'prāṇavikrayavṛttiḥ' sevakaḥ |
tripūrvaḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaś ca yavano bharatarṣabha |
akriyo brāhmaṇaś caiva śrāddhe nārhanti ketanam ||
'tripūrvaḥ' trijātaḥ | 'kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ' pūrvoktalakṣaṇaḥ | 'yavanaḥ' yavanadeśotpannaḥ |
iti ketanānarhāḥ |
athāpāṅkteyāḥ |
āhośanāḥ |
athāpāṅkteyā bhavanti | tathā jaḍabadhirāndhaśvitrikuṣṭhikuṇi-śyāvadantavārddhuṣikadevalakavaṇikṣaṇḍhagaṇābhyantaracikitsaka-paunarbhava[480]kāṇaśilpopajīvipatitasaṃprayoktṛkitavānṛtadāmbhika-paraparibhāvitādyāḥ |
vidhiniṣedhapratipattimūḍhaḥ 'jaḍaḥ' | śrotrendriyavikalpaḥ 'badhiraḥ' | acakṣuḥ 'andhaḥ' | śvetakuṣṭhavān 'śvitrī' | 'kuṣṭhī' prasiddhaḥ | kutsitahastaḥ ‘kuṇiḥ’ | svabhāvataḥ kṛṣṇadaśanaḥ 'śyāvadantaḥ' | saty anyasmin jīvikopāye dhānyavṛddhijīvanaḥ ‘vārddhuṣikaḥ' | mūlyena devatāpūjakā devadravyabhoktā vā 'devalakaḥ' | krayavikrayavyavahāropajīvī 'vaṇik' | 'ṣaṇḍhaḥ' ṣoḍhāsaṃjñāprakaraṇe kṛtalakṣaṇaḥ | 'gaṇaḥ' saṅghaḥ, ye sadaikayā kriyayā jīvanti te ’tra gaṇaśabdavācyāḥ, tadantargataś cāturvidyo ’pi brāhmaṇaḥ ‘gaṇābhyantaraḥ’ | dhanārthaṃ rujāṃ pratikartā 'cikitsakaḥ' | dharmārthinas tu tasya praśastatvād iti medhātithiḥ | punaḥpariṇītā 'punarbhūḥ', tasyā apatyaṃ 'paunarbhavaḥ' | ekenākṣṇā vikalaḥ 'kāṇaḥ' | tiryakprekṣī vā 'kāṇaḥ' | gṛhaprāsādādinirmāṇavṛttiḥ ‘śilpopajīvī' | mahāpātakisaṃsargadūṣitaḥ 'patitaḥ' | 'saṃprayoktā' dyūtakārī | chadmaprayoktā 'kitavaḥ' | asatyavadanaśīlaḥ ‘anṛtaḥ’| lokaprītyarthaṃ chadmanā dharmānuṣṭhātā 'dāmbhikaḥ' | gurutarapātakābhi-yogena paraiḥ paribhavaṃ prāpitaḥ 'paraparibhāvitaḥ' |
aha manuḥ |
ye stenāḥ patitāḥ klībā ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ |
tān havyakavyayor viprān anarhān manur abravīt ||
jaṭilaṃ cānadhīyānaṃ durvalaṃ kitavaṃ tathā |
yājayanti ca ye pūgān tāṃ ca śrāddhe na bhojayet ||
cikitsakān devalakān māṃsavikrayiṇas tathā |
[481]        vipaṇena ca jīvantaḥ varjyās te havyakavyayoḥ ||
preṣyo grāmasya rājñaś ca kunakhī śyāvadantakaḥ |
pratiroddhā guroś caiva tyaktāgnir vārddhuṣī tathā ||
yakṣmī ca paśupālaś ca parivettā nirākṛtiḥ |
brahmadviṭ parivittiś ca gaṇābhyantara eva ca ||
kuśīlavo ’vakīrṇī ca vṛṣalīpatir eva ca |
paunarbhavaś ca kāṇaś ca yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
bhṛtakādhyāpako yaś ca bhṛtakādhyāpitaś ca yaḥ |
śūdraśiṣyo guruś caiva vāgduṣṭaḥ kuṇḍagolakau ||
akāraṇāt parityaktā mātāpitror guros tathā |
brāhmair yaunaiś ca saṃbandhaiḥ saṃyogaṃ patitair gataḥ ||
āgāradāhī garadaḥ kuṇḍāśī somavikrayī |
samudrayāyī bandī ca tailikaḥ kūṭakārakaḥ ||
pitrā vivadamānaś ca kitavo madyapas tathā |
pāparogy abhiśastaś ca dāmbhiko rasavikrayī ||
dhanuḥśarāṇāṃ kartā ca yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ |
mitradhruk dyūtavṛttiś ca putrācāryas tathaiva ca ||
bhrāmarī gaṇḍamālī ca śvitry atho piśunas tathā |
unmatto ’ndhaś ca varjyāḥ syur vedanindaka eva ca ||
hastigo'śvoṣṭradamako nakṣatrair yaś ca jīvati |
[482]        pakṣiṇāṃ poṣako yaś ca yuddhācāryas tathaiva ca ||
srotasāṃ bhedako yaś ca teṣāṃ cāvaraṇe rataḥ |
gṛhasaṃveśako dūto vṛkṣāropaka eva ca ||
śvakrīḍī śyenajīvī ca kanyādūṣaka eva ca |
hiṃsro vṛṣalavṛttiś ca gaṇānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ ||
ācārahīnaḥ klībaś ca nityayācanakas tathā |
kṛṣijīvī slīpadī ca sadbhir nindita eva ca ||
aurabhriko māhiṣikaḥ parapūrvāpatis tathā |
pretaniryāpakaś caiva varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
etān vigarhitācārān apāṅkteyān dvijādhamān |
dvijātipravaro vidvān ubhayatra vivarjayet ||
‘stenāḥ’ brāhmaṇavyatiriktadravyāpahartāraḥ, itareṣāṃ tu patitapadenaivābhidhānāt | ‘patitāḥ' mahāpātakinaḥ | 'klībaḥ' napuṃsakaḥ, “mā klaibyaṃ gaccha kaunteya” iti liṅgadarśanāt | “sattvaśūnyaḥ klībaḥ” iti hariharaḥ | nāsti dattaṃ nāsti hutaṃ nāsti paraloka iti ye sthitaprajñāḥ teṣāṃ ‘vṛttiḥ’ ācāro ’śraddadhānatā 'nāstikavṛttiḥ' sā vṛttir yeṣāṃ te 'nāstikavṛttayaḥ' | uttarapadalopī samāsaḥ | atha vā nāstikyaṃ vṛttiḥ jīvanaṃ yeṣāṃ ta evam ucyante | manugrahaṇaṃ pratiṣedhādarārtham | jaṭilo brahmacārīti medhātithiḥ | sa cānadhīyāno ’prārabdhādhyayano ’tra niṣidhyate | prārabdhādhyayanasya tu brahmacāriṇo vihitatvāt | na ca sarvathādhya[483]yanasaṃbandhasya prāptir eva nāsti tat kathaṃ pratiṣedha iti vācyam, vratastham api dauhitram ityādinā prāptisaṃbhavāt | 'duvālaḥ' lohitakeśaḥ, khalatir vā durvala ity anye paṭhanti | 'durvalaḥ' vikośadhvajaḥ, bhokum akṣamo vā | ‘pūgān’ saṅghātān, yājayanti | māṃsavikrayiṇaḥ prasiddhāḥ | 'vipaṇena' lavaṇādinā pratiṣiddhena paṇyena | grāmasya rājño vā ‘preṣyaḥ’ ājñākaraḥ | ‘kunakhī' kutsitanakhaḥ | sarvakāryeṣu yo guroḥ pratiṣedhena prātikūlye vartate ’sau 'guroḥ pratiroddhā' | 'tyaktāgniḥ' tretāvarsathyayor anyatarasyāpi parityaktā | 'yakṣmī' rājayakṣmagṛhītaḥ | ājīvanārthaṃ yaḥ paśūn rakṣati sa 'paśupālaḥ' | saty adhikāre mahāyajñānuṣṭhānarahitaḥ ‘nirākṛtiḥ', vismṛtavedo vā | durākṛtir ity anye | brāhmaṇānāṃ vedasya vā dveṣṭā 'brahmadviṭ' | parivettṛparivittī, pūroktalakṣaṇau | cāraṇanartakagāyanādayaḥ 'kuśīlavāḥ' | 'avakīrṇī' viplutabrahmacaryaḥ | 'vṛṣalī' śūdrā, tasyāḥ patiḥ | adūkṣanyāyaṃ vātra manyante, vṛṣalyā eva patiḥ | 'upapatiḥ' jāyājāraḥ | sa copekṣyamāṇo yasya gṛha eva vasati | iyatā dhanenedaṃ karomīti vacanavyavasthayā vetanagrāhī 'bhṛtakaḥ' bhṛtakaś cāsāv adhyāpakaś ca 'bhṛtakādhyāpakaḥ' | evaṃ bhṛtakenādhyāpitaḥ 'bhṛtakādhyāpitaḥ | vyākaraṇādividyāsu śūdrasya śiṣyaḥ ‘śūdraśiṣyaḥ’| ‘guruḥ' śūdrasyaiva | atropasarjanībhūtasyāpi saṃbandhaḥ | vigarhitācāratvasya sarvaśeṣatvāt śūdrasyaiva ca gurutvaṃ garhitaṃ nānyat | vācā duṣṭaḥ 'vāgduṣṭaḥ' paruṣānṛtabhāṣī abhiśasto vā | kuṇḍagolakāv uktalakṣaṇau | gurur atrācāryaḥ | asati brahmahatyādidoṣaparityāgakāraṇe yo mātaraṃ pitaram ācāryaṃ ca parityajati | [484] parityāgaś ca śuśrūṣādyakaraṇam | 'gurutyāgaḥ' tasminn adhyāpanasamarthe ’dhyāpayati tattyāgenānyatrādhyayanam | brāhmaiḥ yājanādhyāpanādibhiḥ, 'yaunaiḥ kanyādānādibhiḥ saṃbandhaiḥ, yaḥ patitaiḥ saha saṃsargaṃ gataḥ, "saṃvatsareṇa patati patitena sahācaran" iti | patitapadenaiva labdhe ’pi saṃsargiṇi vatsarād arvāg api pratiṣedhārtham idaṃ | 'āgāradāhī' gṛhādidāhakaḥ | kṛtrimākṛtrimaviṣapradātā 'garadaḥ' | kuṇḍagolakayor annam aśnāti saḥ 'kuṇḍāśī' | 'kuṇḍaṃ' āḍhakaḥ, tatparimāṇānnabhoktā vā 'kuṇḍāśī' | 'somaṃ' latāviśeṣaṃ yāgārtham auṣadhārthaṃ vā yo vikrīṇāti saḥ 'somavikrayī' somasādhanakayāgavikretā vā | samudraṃ udadhiṃ yāti pravahaṇādibhir laṅghayati 'samudrayāyī' | 'bandī' stutipāṭhakaḥ | tilādīnāṃ peṣṭā 'taikaḥ' | sākṣitve ’nṛtavādī 'kūṭakārakaḥ', 'kūṭānāṃ' nāṇakādīnāṃ kartā vā | pitrā saha bhāgādinimittaṃ pūrvottarapakṣabhaṅgyā yo vivadate viruddhaṃ lapate | 'kitavaḥ' kalitaprekṣī adhyarddhadṛṣṭiḥ | kātara ity anye paṭhanti | sa eva śukapakṣasadṛśatārakaḥ | 'madyapaḥ' surāvyatiriktasyāriṣṭasya | 'pāparogī' kuṣṭhī | 'abhiśastaḥ' pātakopapātakayoḥ | 'dāmbhikaḥ' loke prasiddhaḥ | asaty api tatkāritāniścaye | 'rasavikrayī' viṣasya vikreteti medhātithiḥ | madhurādirasādhiṣṭhānānāṃ guḍalavaṇakṣaudratakraprabhṛtīnāṃ vikreteti śaṅkhadharaḥ | 'rasaḥ' pārada ity apare | agredidhiṣūpatir ity atra didhiṣūśabdasya agrepadena patipadena ca pṛthaksaṃbandhaḥ | tato 'gredidhiṣūr ekaḥ didhiṣūpatir anyaḥ | samāsāntargatasyāpy ekaśabdasya dvābhyāṃ bhinnaprasthānābhyām (?) abhisaṃbandhaḥ smṛtiśāstratvād upapadyata iti samāhitaṃ medhātithinā | mṛtasya bhrātur bhāryāyāṃ dharmeṇa niyuktāyām api yo ’nurāgavaśād [485] āliṅganacumbanādīni kuryād asakṛd vā pravartate sa 'didhiṣūpatiḥ' | jīvatas tāvat bhrātur bhāryāyām anuraktaḥ 'agredidhiṣūḥ' | atha vā vivāhārhāyāṃ jyeṣṭhāyām anūḍhāyāṃ yā kanīyasī pariṇīyate sā 'agredidhiṣūḥ', tasyāḥ patiḥ ‘agredidhiṣūpatiḥ' | mitrān druhyatīti 'mitradhruk', mitrakāryopaghātaka ity arthaḥ | dyūtaṃ vṛttir jīvikā yasya saḥ ‘dyūtavṛttiḥ' | yo dyūtakārān niyojya tebhyo ’ṃśaṃ (?) labhate so ’trābhidhīyata iti svayaṃ devitvāt kitavena sahāpaunaruktyam | putraḥ ācāryaḥ adhyāpako yasya saḥ 'putrācāryaḥ', mūrkhasyācāryatvasya putre saṃbhavāt | 'bhrāmarī 'apasmārī | 'gaṇḍamālī' prasiddhaḥ | paramarmaprakāśakaḥ 'piśunaḥ' | vātādidoṣeṇa piśācādigṛhītatvena vā anavasthitacittaḥ 'unmattaḥ' | hastyādīnāṃ damanaḥ gatiśikṣayitā 'hastigo'śvoṣṭradamanaḥ' | nakṣatragrahaṇaṃ jyotiḥśāstropalakṣaṇārtham | 'pakṣiṇāṃ' śyenādīnāṃ, ākheṭakārthaṃ poṣayitā | dhanurvedopadeṣṭā 'yuddhācāryaḥ' | yo vrīhyādisecanārthaṃ śrotāṃsi bhinanti anekadhā nayati | yaś ciraruddhapravṛddhānāṃ śrotasāṃ gatibandhārthaṃ setubandhenāvaraṇaṃ karoti | 'gṛhasaṃveśakaḥ' vāstuvidyopajīvī sthapatisūtradhārādiḥ | yaḥ saṃdhivigrahādiṣu preṣyate saḥ 'dūtaḥ' | yo mūlyena vṛkṣān ropayati sa 'vṛkṣāropakaḥ' | śvabhiḥ śunakaiḥ krīḍati 'śvakrīḍī' | śyenānāṃ krayeṇa jīvati 'śyenajīvī' | yaḥ satā asatā vā doṣeṇa kanyāṃ nindati saḥ 'kanyādūṣakaḥ', yo mithunasaṃprayogeṇa kanyām akanyāṃ karoti so ’pi kanyādūṣakaḥ | 'hiṃsraḥ' svabhāvata eva prāṇighātanirataḥ | vṛṣalebhyaḥ śūdrebhyaḥ sevādinā vṛttir [486] jīvanaṃ yasyāsau 'vṛṣalavṛttiḥ' | gaṇānāṃ devatāviśeṣāṇāṃ yājakaḥ | gaṇayāgāḥ prasiddhā iti medhātithiḥ | ācāraḥ gṛhābhyāgatānāṃ pūjāsatkārādiḥ, tena varjitaḥ 'ācārahīnaḥ' | kartavyeṣu bhagnotsāhaḥ ‘klībaḥ’| sadaiva yācanayā lokān udvejayati saḥ ‘nityayācanakaḥ | nindādibhyaḥ svārthe kap | 'kṛṣijīvo' yaḥ pratisiddhayā kṛṣyā jīvati, sā cānāpadi pratiṣiddhā āpady api svayaṃ kriyamāṇā anyataḥ kāryamāṇāpi yathoktavidhivirahitā pratiṣiddhā | kṛṣividhis tu,
rājño dattvā ca ṣaḍbhāgaṃ devatānāṃ ca viṃśakaṃ |
triṣaḍbhāgaṃ ca viprāṇāṃ kṛṣiṃ kṛtvā na doṣabhāk ||
ityādiḥ smṛtikāraiḥ pratipāditaḥ | doṣavaśāt sthalaikapādaḥ 'ślīpadī' | yasya pratyakṣeṇa doṣo na lakṣyate sadabhiś ca nindyamāno dṛśyate ’sau 'sadbhir ninditaḥ' | urabhrāḥ meṣāḥ paṇyaṃ yasya sa ‘aurabhrikaḥ' | evaṃ māhiṣikaḥ | paraḥ pūrvo yasyāḥ sā 'parapūrvā' svairiṇyādiḥ, tasyāḥ patiḥ 'parapūrvāpatiḥ' | yo mūlyena pretān niryāpayati vahati sa 'pretaniryāpakaḥ' | 'vigarhitācārāḥ' ninditācārāḥ | ‘apāṅkteyāḥ' paṅktyanarhāḥ | 'ubhayatra' daive pitrye ca karmaṇi |
atrāha sumantuḥ |
taskarakitavājapālagaṇagaṇikāśūdraprevṣyāgamyāgāmiparivettṛparivitti- paryahitaparyādhātṛpaunarbhavāndhabadhiracāraṇaklībāvakīrṇivārddhuṣika-garadāyikūṭasākṣināstikavṛṣalīpatyupahutādotsṛṣṭāgnisomavikravyavikreya- vikretṛpaustikakathakakuṇḍāśikuṇḍagolakayantrakārakāṇḍapṛṣṭhaduścarma- caṇḍaviddhaśiśnadevalakaṣaṇḍārūḍhapatita[487]prāyotthitakunakhikilāśi- śyāvadantavaṇikśilpavāditranṛtyagītatālopajīvimūlyasāṃvatsarika- mahāpathikāśmakuṭṭakahīnātirikāṅgā virāgavāsasaś cāpāṅkteyāḥ |
'taṣkaraḥ' cauraḥ | ājīvanārtham ajāḥ pālayati 'ajapālaḥ' | 'gaṇaḥ' saṅghaḥ | ‘gaṇikā' veśyā | śūdraḥ prasiddhaḥ | eteṣāṃ 'preṣyaḥ' | śūdrasevakapratiṣedhān mahāpadi varṇantarasevakasya doṣāntararahitasyānujñāvagamyate |
yadāha pracetāḥ |
mahadāpadgato vipraḥ kṣatrasevāṃ samāśrayet |
śūdrasevā na kartavyā prāṇaiḥ kaṇṭhagatair api ||
agamyāḥ parastriyo gacchatīti 'agamyāgāmī' | jyeṣṭhe bhrātary akṛtāgnyādhāne yadi kanīyān agnyāhitaḥ syāt tadāsau paryādhattety ucyate jyeṣṭhas tu paryahita iti | etac ca prāg vistareṇa nirṇītam | cāraṇo ’tra nagnācāryaḥ itas tataḥ kīrtipracāraṇāt | nartakaviśeṣasya tu cāraṇasya nṛtyopajīviśabdena vakṣyamāṇatvāt | kūṭam alīkaṃ sākṣyaṃ karotīti 'kūṭasākṣī' | pañcayajñān avidhāya yo bhuṅkte saḥ 'upahutādaḥ' | 'utsṛṣṭāgniḥ' parityaktāgniḥ | avikreyaṃ tilayavādi vikrīṇāti 'avikreyavikretā' | pustakalekhanakarmaṇā jīvati iti 'paustikaḥ', pustakavikretā vā mūlyena pustakalekhako vā | viṭajanarañjanāya bṛhatkathādikathānāṃ kathayitā 'kathakaḥ' | ākheṭanārthaṃ durgādiṣu saṅgrāmārthaṃ vā yantranirmātā 'yantrakāraḥ' | śastropajīvī 'kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ' | kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ śastrajīvīti nāmānuśāsanasmaraṇāt | anye tu kāṇḍaspaṣṭa iti paṭhanti | tasya lakṣaṇam uktaṃ prāk | 'duścarmā' duścikitsyatvagyogī | [488] 'caṇḍaḥ' chinnamehanacarmā, caṇḍi valkāpanayana iti caurādiko dhātuḥ, caṇḍayatīti caṇḍaḥ | viddhaśiśno draviḍeṣu prasiddhaḥ | tatra hi śiśnavṛṣaṇādhaḥśirāyāṃ chidraṃ vidhāya viṭāḥ suvarṇaghaṭikāghaṭitaṃ muktāphalādi viracayanti | tridaṇḍādivratam āruhya tasmāt paribhraṣṭaḥ ‘ārūḍhapatitaḥ’| saṃnyāsapūrvakaṃ maraṇāntikam anaśanaṃ 'prāyaḥ', “saṇnyāsavaty anaśane pumān prāyaḥ” iti trikāṇḍasmaraṇāt | prāyaṃ parityajyotthitaḥ | ‘kilāśī' sidhmarogī | 'vāditraṃ' ātoḍyam | 'nṛtyaṃ' nartanaṃ | ‘gītaṃ' gānam | ‘tālaḥ' cañcatpuṭādiḥ, etadvidyāprāvīṇyena yo jīvati | dhanalipsayā nakṣatrādyādeśakaḥ 'mūlyasāṃvatsarikaḥ' | uktaṃ ca manunā |
tithiṃ parasya na brūyān nakṣatrāṇi na nirdiśet || iti |
'mahāpathikaḥ' samudrapathacārī nityādhvago vā | 'aśmakuṭṭakaḥ' prāsādādinirmāṇāya pāṣāṇatakṣakaḥ | 'hīnāṅgāḥ' khañjādayaḥ | 'adhikāṅgāḥ' ṣaḍaṅguliprabhṛtayaḥ | vividhamāñjiṣṭhādirāgayuktavāsasaḥ ‘virāgavāsasaḥ’, tridaṇḍagrahaṇaṃ vinā lokavañcanārthaṃ gairikādiraktavāsaso vā |
āha ca yamaḥ |
apāṅkteyā dvijāś cānye tān me nigadataḥ śṛṇu |
yebhyo dattaṃ na devānāṃ na pitṝṇāṃ ca karmakṛt ||
kāṇāḥ kubjāś ca ṣaṇḍāś ca kṛtaghnā gurutalpagāḥ |
brahmaghnāś ca surāpāś ca stenā goghnāś cikitsakāḥ ||
rāṣṭrakāmās tathonmattāḥ paśuvikrayiṇaś ca ye |
grāmakūṭās tulākūṭāḥ śilpino grāmayācakāḥ ||
vṛṣalībhiḥ prapītāś ca śreṇīrājanyayājakāḥ |
[489]        rājabhṛtyāndhabadhiramūkakhalvāṭapaṅgavaḥ ||
kalopajīvinaś caiva brahmavikrayiṇaś ca ye |
daṇḍadhvajāś ca ye viprā grāmakṛtyakarāś ca ye ||
āgāradāhinaś caiva garadā vanadāhakāḥ |
kuṇḍāśino devalakāḥ paradārābhimarṣakāḥ ||
śyāvadantāḥ kunakhinaḥ śilpinaḥ kuṣṭhinaś ca ye |
vaṇijo madhuhantāro hastyaśvadamakā dvijāḥ ||
kanyānāṃ dūṣakāś caiva brāhmaṇānāṃ ca dūṣakāḥ |
sūcakāḥ poṣakāś caiva kitavāś ca kuśīlavāḥ ||
samayānāṃ ca bhettāraḥ pradāne ye ca vārakāḥ |
ājāvikā māhiṣikāḥ sarvavikrayiṇaś ca ye |
vaiṣṇavīṣu ca ye sakāḥ śalākādāhinaś ca ye |
dhanuḥkartā dhūtavṛttir mitradhruk śaṭha eva ca ||
iṣukartā tathā varjyo yaś cāgredidhiṣūpatiḥ |
pāṇḍurogī gaṇḍamālī yakṣmī ca bhrāmarī tathā ||
piśunaḥ kūṭasākṣī ca dīrgharogī vṛthāśramī |
pravrajyopanivṛttaś ca vṛthāpravrajitaś ca yaḥ ||
yas tu pravrajitāj jātaḥ pravrajyāvasitaś ca yaḥ |
tāv ubhau brahmacaṇḍālāv āha vaivasvato yamaḥ ||
rājñaḥ preṣyakaro yaś ca grāmasya nagarasya ca |
samudrayāyī vārttāśī keśavikrayiṇaś ca ye ||
avakīrṇī ca vīraghno gurughnaḥ pitṛdūṣakaḥ |
[490]        govikrayī ca durvālaḥ pūgānāṃ caiva yājakaḥ ||
madyapaś ca kadaryaś ca saha pitrā vivādakṛt |
dāṇḍiko bandhukībhartā tyaktātmā dāradūṣakaḥ ||
sadbhiś ca ninditācāraḥ svakarmaparivarjitaḥ |
parivittiḥ parivettā bhṛtyācāryo nirākṛtiḥ ||
śūdrācāryaḥ sutācāryaḥ śūdraśiṣyaś ca nāstikaḥ |
duṣṭas tu dārakācāryo mānakṛt tailikas tathā ||
caurā vārddhuṣikā duṣṭāḥ parasvānāṃ ca nāśakāḥ |
caturāśramabāhyāś ca ye cānye paṅktidūṣakāḥ |
ity etair lakṣaṇair yuktāṃs tān dvijān na niyojayet ||
'gurutalpagaḥ' gurubhāryagāmī | paurohityārthaṃ yajñārthaṃ vā rāṣṭraṃ vaśīkartuṃ kāmayate saḥ 'rāṣṭrakāmaḥ' | grāmaṃ kūṭayati chalayatīti ‘grāmakūṭakaḥ’| bahubhir alīkavyavahārair grāmadravyaṃ bhakṣayatīty arthaḥ | alīkatulāvyavahāreṇa vañcayitā 'tulākūṭaḥ' | vṛṣalyā prapīto ’sakṛccumbitaḥ 'vṛṣalīprapītaḥ’| 'śreṇyaḥ' suvarṇakārādyāḥ prakṛtayaḥ, tāsāṃ yājakaḥ 'śreṇīyājakaḥ' | kalayā suvarṇavṛddhyā jīvatīti 'kalopajīvī' | catuḥṣaṣṭikalopajīvī vā | brahmaṇo vedasya putrasya vā vikretā 'brahmavikrayī’| jīvikārthaṃ brahmavidyopadeṣṭā vā | yo mahāparādhī rājñā ca padādyaṅkanena kṛtadaṇḍaḥ saḥ ‘daṇḍadhvajaḥ’ ucyate | madhugrahaṇāya madhumakṣikopaghātī 'madhuhantā' | 'sūcakaḥ' paradoṣajñānopāyaprayoktā | anyena prakāśitaṃ paradoṣaṃ puṣṇāti saṃbaddhavacanena dṛḍhayati | rājādikṛtā vyavasthāḥ [491] 'samayāḥ' | ajāvikena jīvantīti 'ājāvikāḥ' | evaṃ māhiṣikāḥ | vyabhicāriṇīputrā vā | 'vaiṣṇavīṣu' indrajālādimāyāsu, 'saktāḥ' niṣaṇṇamatayaḥ | ‘śalākā' śastraṃ, 'dāhaḥ' agnikarma, tābhyāṃ ye bhaiṣajyam ācaranti te 'śalākādāhinaḥ' | apakāraśīlaḥ 'śaṭhaḥ' | 'iṣukartā' śarāṇāṃ kartā | yaḥ śāstrāvihiteṣv āśrameṣu vartate sa 'vṛthāśramī' | pravrajyāṃ vidhāsyāmīti saṃkalpya pravrajyāṃ na kurute sa 'pravrajyopanivṛttaḥ' | yas tu vairāgyādikam antareṇaiva pravrajati saḥ 'vṛthāpravrajitaḥ' | pravrajitenotpāditaḥ 'pravrajitāj jātaḥ' | vidhivat pravrajyāṃ svīkṛtya tataḥ paribhraṣṭaḥ 'pravrajyāvasitaḥ' | vārttayā kṛṣivāṇijyādirūpayā vaiśyavṛttyā yo jīvati saḥ 'vārttāśī' | yo vā nigūḍhapracāreṇa sakalalokavṛttāntam adhigamya rājñe nivedayati so ’pi vārttājīvanād vārtttāśī puruṣa iti loke prasiddhaḥ | vāntāśīti vā pāṭhaḥ | tallakṣaṇam uktaṃ saṃjñāprakaraṇe | 'keśavikrayī' cāmarādivikretā | 'vīraghnaḥ' bālahantā | avīrām iva mām ayam ity asyāḥ śruter abālām iva mām ayam iti nairuktivyākhyānāt | 'pūgayājakaḥ' samānajātīyānām api samānavyavasāyānāṃ samūhaḥ pūgas tasya yājayitā 'pūgayājakaḥ' | kadaryalakṣaṇam uktaṃ | aparādhaprayojyadaṇḍādhikṛtaḥ 'dāṇḍikaḥ' | 'bandhukībhartā' puṃścalīpatiḥ | 'tyaktātmā' kṛtātmaghātodyamaḥ | mukhasukhādinā puruṣāntarasaṃyojanena vā kulastrīṇāṃ dūṣayitā 'dāradūṣakaḥ' | vanitāntarānuraktatayā pūrvadārāṇāṃ mithyābhiyoktā vā 'dāradūṣakaḥ' | bhṛtakādhyāpako bhṛtakādhyāpito vā 'bhṛtyācāryaḥ' | evaṃ śūdrācāryaḥ | śiśūnām akṣarapāṭhako ‘dārakācāryaḥ' | dhānyabhūmyādimānavyavasāyena yo jīvati sa 'mānakṛt' | eṣu kiṃcid vyākhyātaṃ kiṃcit subodham |
[492]        śaṅkhalikhitau |
anṛk taskaro rājabhṛtyo vṛkṣāropakavṛttir garado ’gnidaḥ śvāgaṇikaḥ śūdropādhyāyo bandī ghāṇṭiko devalaḥ purohito nakṣatrādeśavṛttir brahmapuruṣo madyapo brahmavikrayī kitavaḥ kūṭakārako mānuṣapaśuvikrayiṇaś cety apāṅkteyāḥ |
anadhītavedaḥ ‘anṛk’ | śvagaṇaiḥ caratīti ‘śvāgaṇikaḥ' | yaḥ kṣudraghaṇṭikādinibandhanapuraḥsareṇa nṛtyagītādinā jīvati sa ‘ghāṇṭikaḥ’| yo vā mahatīṃ ghaṇṭāṃ vādayann eva pratigṛhaṃ bhikṣate so ’pi 'ghāṇṭikaḥ' | gūrjaradeśe ghaṇṭāla iti prasiddhaḥ | yo jīvanmuktaveṣeṇa lokaṃ pratārayan dravyam arjayati sa ‘brahmapuraṣaḥ' |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kunakhikilāśiśyāvadantāḥ pitrā vivadamānaś ca yaś ca strījitaḥ kuṣṭhī klībaḥ kitavo durvāro duścarmā surāgarbho viddhaprajananaḥ patitaḥ piśunaḥ somavikrayī yajñavedavikrayī vāṇijako grāmayājī mithyābhiśasto vṛṣalyām abhijātaḥ parivittiḥ parivindāno didhiṣūpatiḥ punarbhūputraḥ golakakuṇḍājāvikāś cauraḥ kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ sevakaś cety apāṅkteyāḥ | śrāddhe caitān na bhojayen na yajñakarmaṇi |
yasya gṛhe prādhānyena bhāryaiva sarvavyavahārān vidhatte sa ca bhītaḥ san tatpāratantryeṇa varttate sa 'strījitaḥ' |
uktaṃ ca yamena |
nāśnanti pitaro daive nāśnanty anṛtavādini |
bhāryājitasya nāśnanti yasya copapatir gṛhe || iti |
[493] surāpastrīgarbhasaṃbhūtaḥ 'surāgarbhaḥ' kṛtaprāyaścittena surāpeṇa janito vā | tīrthayātrādyarjitadharmavikretā 'tīrthavikrayī' |
śātātapaḥ |
agniṣṭomādibhir yajñair ye yajanty alpadakṣiṇaiḥ |
teṣām annaṃ na bhoktavyam apāṅkteyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
devakośopajīvī ca nāmnā devalako bhavet |
apāṅkteyaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ sarvakarmasu sarvadā ||
manuḥ |
avratair yat dvijair bhuktaṃ parivettrādibhis tathā |
apāṅkteyair yad anyaiś ca tad vai rakṣāṃsi bhuñjate ||
yamaḥ |
apāṅkteyo dahaty āśu yāvat samanupaśyati |
bhraśyate tatphalaṃ tebhyo dānaṃ cāpi vinaśyati ||
bhraśyate tatphalam iti kāmyasya bhraṃśaḥ | dānaṃ cāpi vinaśyatīti nityakarmabhraṃśaḥ |
ity apāṅkteyāḥ |
atha paṅktidūṣakāḥ |
śaṅkhaḥ |
brāhmaṇā ye vikarmasthā baiḍālavratikāḥ śaṭhāḥ |
hīnāṅgā hy adhikāṅgāś ca vijñeyāḥ paṅktidūṣakāḥ ||
kunakhī śyāvadantaś ca duścarmā vṛṣalīpatiḥ |
