padmabilvakadhustūrapāribhadrāḍharūṣakāḥ |
na deyāḥ pitṛkāryeṣu payaś cājāvikaṃ tathā ||
[565]        'pāribhadraḥ' nimbaḥ | 'āḍharūṣakaḥ' siṃhāsyaḥ |
mahābhārate |
hiṅgudravyeṣu śākeṣu mālānaṃ laśunaṃ tathā |
palāṇḍusaubhāñjanakau tathā gṛñjanakādayaḥ ||
kiṣkiṇḍajāny alābūni kṛṣṇaṃ lavaṇam eva ca |
grāmyaṃ varāhamāṃsaṃ ca yac caivāproṣitaṃ bhavet ||
kṛṣṇājājī viṭaṃ caiva śītapākī tathaiva ca |
aṅkurādyās tathā varjyā gṛhaśṛṅgāṭakāni ca ||
varjayel lavaṇaṃ sarvaṃ tathā jambuphalāni ca |
avakṣutāvaruditaṃ tathā śrāddheṣu varjayet ||
kiṣkiṇḍajāny alābūnīty atra kukuṇḍakāny alābūnīty api paṭhanti | vyācakṣate ca 'kukuṇḍakāni' chatrākasamānaprakṛtidravyāṇi piṇḍopamāni | 'kṛṣṇaṃ lavaṇaṃ' sauvarcalam | śākādisaṃskāradravyeṣu madhye hiṅgu varjanīyam | yat tu hiṅguno grāhyatvam uktam ādityapurāṇe |
madhukaṃ rāmaṭhaṃ caiva karpūraṃ maricaṃ guḍam |
śrāddhakarmaṇi śastāni saindhavaṃ tripusaṃ tathā || iti |
tad vihitapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpeneti mantavyam | anye tu tac chetahiṅguviṣayaṃ varṇayanti | "lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān varjayed" iti smṛtyantare darśanāt | anye ca śākasaṃskāradravyāntarālābhe ’nukalpatvena hiṅgūpādeyam ity āhuḥ | apare ca śākarūpādi hiṅguviṣayaṃ vadanti | hiṅgulavaṇamaricānām amiśratvena pratyakṣāṇāṃ niṣedhād iti | etat tu hiṅgunaḥ pṛthakbhūtasyānupayojyatvāl lavaṇasyaiva ca pratyakṣasya niṣedhād ayuktam |
[566]        brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
sugandhaṃ pañcaśimbaṃ ca kalāyāḥ sarva eva ca |
vāṃśaṃ karīraṃ surasamarjakaṃ bhūstṛṇāni ca |
bhakṣyatvena niṣiddhaṃ ca na deyaṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
'sugandhaṃ' gandhanākulyāḥ patrādi | niṣpāvamasūrarājamāṣamaṭhakulatthākhyāni pañcaśimbidhānyāni 'pañcaśimbam' | yac ca dharmaśāstreṣu bhakṣaṇīyatvena pratiṣiddhaṃ tad api na deyam |
kiṃ punas tad ity apekṣāyāṃ paiṭhīnasir āha |
vṛntākanālikāpautīkakusumbhāśmantakāś ceti śākā na bhakṣyāḥ | iti |
'vṛntākaṃ' śvetavṛntākam |
tathā ca devalaḥ |
kaṇḍūraṃ śvetavṛntākaṃ kumbhāṇḍaṃ ca vivarjayet |
'kaṇḍūraṃ' kaṇḍūrāyāḥ prāvṛṣe ’ṇyāparanāmadheyāyāḥ phalam | tac cātyantakaṇḍūtijanakabahuromayukaṃ bhavati | 'kumbhāṇḍaṃ' kumbhavadvartulam adighālābusadṛśaṃ phalam | 'kusumbhaṃ' kaṇṭakarahitam | smṛtyantare viśeṣitatvād iti devasvāmī |
kusumbhādīnāṃ bhakṣaṇe doṣam āhośanāḥ |
kusumbhaṃ nālikāśākaṃ vṛntākaṃ pautikaṃ tathā |
bhakṣayan patitas tu syād api vedāntago dvijaḥ ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
[567]        laśunaṃ gṛñjanaṃ cava palāṇḍuṃ kavakāni ca |
vārtākaṃ nālikālābu hy aveyāj jātidūṣitam ||
etajjātyaiva dūṣitaṃ 'aveyāt' jānīyāt |
hārītaḥ |
na vaṭaplakṣodumbaradadhitthanīpamātuliṅgāni bhakṣayet |
'dadhitthaṃ' kapitthaphalam | 'nīpaṃ' kadambapatram |
āha manuḥ |
lohitān vṛkṣaniryāsān vraścanaprabhavāṃs tathā
śailu gavyaṃ ca pīyūṣaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet ||
'śailuḥ' śleṣmātakaḥ | prasūtāyā daśāhaṃ payaḥ 'pīyūṣam' |
iti varjyani phalamūlādīni |
atha grāhyāṇi varjyani ca kṣīrādīni |
kūrmapurāṇe |
kṣīraṃ kṣīravikāraś ca pāyasaṃ śarkarā madhu |
deśaṃ śrāddheṣu yatnena sarvadā pitṛtṛptaye ||
padmapurāṇe |
annaṃ ca sadadhikṣīraṃ goghṛtaṃ śarkarānvitam |
māṃsaṃ prīṇāti vai sarvān pitṝn ity abravīd ajaḥ ||
pitaras tṛpyanti ity anuvṛtau manu-devala- kātyāyanāḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena ca |
manuḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīram auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ tathā |
[568]        āvikaṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ vivatsāyāś ca goḥ payaḥ ||
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ mṛgānāṃ māhiṣaṃ vinā |
strīkṣīraṃ caiva varjyāni sarvaśuklāni caiva hi ||
iha anirdaśāhaṃ ca goḥ kṣīram iti pāṭhe anirdaśāhaśabdānuvṛttau uṣṭrādīnām api dāśāhikaḥ pratiṣedha āśaṅkyeta | tatrātyantikatatpratiṣedhārthaṃ samācāra āśrayaṇīyaḥ | anirdaśāyā iti strīliṅgapāṭhe āśaṅkaiva nāsti | na hi taddhitāntair auṣṭram ityādibhir anirdaśāyā ity anveti | yasyāḥ prasūtāyā daśadināny anapakrāntāni sā gaur anirdaśā | uṣṭryāḥ karabhyā idam ‘auṣṭram’| ekaśaphādīnām vaḍavāprabhṛtīnāṃ idaṃ 'aikaśapham' | avikānām idam 'āvikam' | uttaratra punaḥ kṣīrāgrahaṇasam ācārāc ca auḍṣṭraikaśaphāvikānirdaśagavādikṣīrāṇi savikārāṇi pratiṣidhyante | saṃdhinyā avatsāyāś ca goḥ kṣīram eva | vṛṣabheṇa krāntā 'saṃdhinī' | medhātithis tv āha | yā ubhayoḥ kālayoḥ prāptadohā kathaṃcid anyataratra duhyate, sāyam adugdhā prātar dugdhā prātar adugdhā sāyaṃ vā dugdhā sā 'saṃdhinī' | anyā tu svalpadugdhatvān nityam ekasminn eva kāle duhyamānā atra dohaśabdasya saṃdhānāt saṃnikarṣāt saṃdhinīśabdapravṛttau saṃdhāśabdāt saṃdhiśabdād vā vrīhyādipāṭhād iniḥ | kaścid āha yā mṛtavatsā vatsāntarasaṃdhānena duhyate sā saṃdhinīti | saṃdhinīśabdasyāvasthāviśeṣavacanatvāt tadavasthāyā eva kṣīraṃ varjyam | nottarakālam api vivatsāyāḥ | mṛtavatsāyās tu gor vatsavirahajanitaśokaśāntiparyantaṃ payo varjyam | yā tu jīvadvatsāpi vatsam antareṇaiva tuṣacūkrapiṇyākādinā duhyeta tathā api vivatsāyāḥ payo varjyam| payograhaṇaṃ ca samāsāntargatasya kṣīrapadasyottaratrānvayāt | [569] āraṇyānāṃ mṛgāṇām ity atra puṃliṅgasya payaḥ prakṛtibhāvenānvayāsaṃbhavāj jātimātravivakṣāyāṃ sāmarthyāc camarīprabhṛtīnāṃ payo varjyam iti labhyate |
'māhiṣaṃ vinā' āraṇyamahiṣīkṣīram antareṇa | 'strīkṣīraṃ' mānuṣīkṣīram | tad api nityaṃ varjyam | sarvāṇi śuktāny api varjyani |
yamaḥ |
anirdaśāyā goḥ kṣīraṃ ājaṃ māhiṣam eva ca |
āvikaṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ ca yat |
varjayed gor avatsāyāḥ payaś caivānyavatsāyāḥ ||
āraṇyānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ varjayitvā tu māhiṣam |
āvikādīni varjyāni sadā svāyaṃbhuvo ’bravīt ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃdhinyanirdaśāvatsāgoḥ payaḥ parivarjayet |
auṣṭram aikaśapham aiṇaṃ āraṇyakam athāvikam ||
vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
agrāmapāśvavipayaḥ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ gomahiṣyajānām anirdaśāhānām |
gautamaḥ |
goś ca kṣīram anirdaśāyāḥ sūtake ajāmahiṣyoś ca nityam āvikam apeyam auṣṭram aikaśaphaṃ ca syandinīyamasūsaṃdhinīnāṃ ca yāś ca vatsavyapetāḥ |
'syandinī' svayam eva kṣīraṃ prasyandamānā prasyandamānayonir vā | 'yamasūḥ’ prastutayamalā |
baudhāyanaḥ |
anirdaśāhāsaṃdhinīkṣīram apeyaṃ vivatsāyāś cāvikam aikaśaphaṃ cā peyam |
[570]        āpastambaḥ |
apeyaṃ tathaiṇakaṃ paya uṣṭrīkṣīraṃ mṛgīkṣīraṃ saṃdhinīkṣīraṃ yamasūkṣīraṃ goś cānirdaśāyāḥ |
hārītaḥ |
syandinī vṛṣasyantī tasyāḥ payo na pibet | na ṛtumad bhavati | na hatavatsāyāḥ śokābhibhūtatvāt | na dugdhāyā vinā vatsāt | yathā hy aśnata ācchidyātmanāśnīyād evaṃ tat | na navasūtāyāḥ sarajaskatvāt | saptarātrād ity eke, daśarātrād ity apare māsenāpi pūyaṃ bhavatīti dharmavidaḥ |
evaṃ hy āhuḥ |
dvau māsau pāyayed vatsaṃ tṛtīye dvistanaṃ duhet |
caturthe tristanaṃ caiva yathānyāyaṃ yathābalam ||
vṛṣeṇa maithunam icchato 'vṛṣasyantī' tadīyaṃ payaḥ | 'ṛtumad bhavati' ṛtumatyāḥ saṃbandhi bhavatīty arthaḥ | anena nivṛttamaithunetarāyāḥ payo na niṣiddham iti gamyate | 'hatavatsā' mṛtavatsā | śokābhibhūtatvād iti hetuḥ | tena vismṛtavatsaśokāyāḥ payasi na pratiṣedhaḥ | asya hetuvannigadārthavādatā smṛticandrikāreṇaiva nirākṛteti tan na prapañcyāte | vatsād vineti kṣīralobhāt | jīvantam eva vatsaṃ anyato nidhāya dugdhāyāḥ payo na pivet | dṛṣṭāntena niṣiddhaṃ draḍhayati | yathā 'aśnataḥ' bhuñjānāt, 'annaṃ' 'ācchidya' haṭāt gṛhītvā, 'ātmanā' svayaṃ, aśnīyāt | evaṃ tad iti| navasūtāyāḥ payo na pibed iti saṃbandhaḥ | rajaskatvād iti hetuḥ | saptarātrād upari pūyaṃ bhavatīty eke | daśarātrād uparīty apare | māṣād uparīti [571] dharmavidaḥ manyanta iti śeṣaḥ | eteṣu saptarātrādipakṣeṣu yathā rajonivṛttivyavasthā draṣṭavyā | dharmavidagrahaṇaṃ māsānte rajonivṛttisaṃśayo ’pi nāstīti jñāpanārthaṃ tena yadā saptarātrādāv api rajonivṛttiniścayaḥ tadā payaḥpāne ’pi na dharmahāniḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
māhiṣaṃ gavyam ājaṃ ca bhakṣyaṃ kṣīraṃ tu nirdaśam |
śaṅkhaḥ |
sarvāsāṃ dvistanīnāṃ kṣīram abhojyaṃ ajāvarjam | iti |
āpastambas tu brāhmaṇavyatiriktena kāpilaṃ payo na peyam ity āha |
kṣatriyaś caiva vṛttastho vaiśyaḥ śūdro ’tha vā punaḥ |
yaḥ pibet kāpilaṃ kṣīraṃ tato ’nyo nāsty apuṇyakṛt ||
tena brāhmaṇavyatiriktaḥ kapilāyā goḥ kṣīraṃ śrāddhādikarmopayuktaśeṣam api prayatnena varjayed ity arthaḥ | brāhmaṇasya hutaśeṣavyatiriktakāpilakṣīrapāne doṣo bhaviṣyatpurāṇe darśitaḥ |
kāpilaṃ yaḥ pibet śūdro narake sa vipacyate |
hutaśeṣaṃ pibed vipro vipraḥ syād anyathā paśuḥ || iti |
hutaśeṣe pīte viprasya janmāntare ’pi vipratvaṃ bhavati | tathā |
hutaśeṣavyatiriktakāpilakṣīre pīte viprasya janmāntare paśutvaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | kṣīravarjanavidhānād eva kṣīravikārasyāpi kūrcikāder varjyatvaṃ siddham | kūrcikādivikāre ’pi kṣīrarasasya sadbhāvāt | ata eva kṣīre varjayitavye tadrasavataḥ pāyasāder api varjanaṃ kāryam iti mīmāṃsāyāṃ [572] pratipāditam | ata eva niṣiddhakṣīravikārāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇe niṣiddhātikramasya kṛtatvāt prāyaścittam āha śaṅkhaḥ |
kṣīrāṇi yāny abhakṣyāṇi tadvikārāśane budhaḥ |
saptarātravrataṃ kuryāt prayatnena samāhitaḥ || iti |
śrāddheṣu mahiṣīkṣīrasya niṣedhe ’pi vacanāntareṇa prāśastyapratipādanān mahiṣīghṛtaṃ grāhyam eva |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
śrāddheṣu mahiṣīkṣīram ajākṣīraṃ ca varjayet |
gavāṃ cānirdaśāhānāṃ saṃdhinīnāṃ payas tyajet ||
ādityapurāṇe |
na ca govyatiriktānāṃ payaḥ śrāddheṣu śasyate |
savikāraṃ gavām eva pitṝṇāṃ tṛptaye payaḥ ||
sumantuḥ |
payodadhighṛtaṃ caiva gavāṃ śrāddheṣu pāvanam |
mahiṣīṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ prāhuḥ śreṣṭhaṃ na tu payaḥ kvacit ||
devalaḥ |
ajāvimahiṣīṇāṇ tu payaḥ śrāddheṣu varjayet |
vikārān payasaś caiva māhiṣaṃ tu ghṛta (?) hitaṃ ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
mārgam āvikam auṣṭraṃ ca sarvam aikaśaphaṃ ca yat |
māhiṣaṃ cāmaraṃ caiva dhenvā goś cāpy anirdaśam ||
pitrarthaṃ me prayacchasvety uktvā yac cāpy upāhṛtam |
[573]        varjanīyaṃ sadā sadbhiḥ tat payaḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
brahmapurāṇe |
māhiṣaṃ cāmaraṃ mārgam āvikaikaśaphodbhavam |
aiṇm auṣṭraṃ yācitaṃ ca dadhikṣīraṃ tyajet ghṛtam ||
ghṛtāt phenaṃ ghṛtān maṇḍaṃ pīyūṣam atha cārdragoḥ |
saguḍaṃ maricāktaṃ tu tathā paryuṣitaṃ dadhi |
dīrṇaṃ takram apeyaṃ ca naṣṭāsvādaṃ ca phenavat ||
ayam arthaḥ | ‘ghṛtāt phenaṃ' ghṛtād uddhṛtya phenaṃ, apeyam | 'ghṛtān maṇḍaṃ' ghṛtād uddhṛtā santānikā | tathā 'ārdragoḥ pīyūṣaṃ' yāvat prasrutā gaur ārdrayonis tāvat kṣīram apeyam | tathā payodadhiguḍamaricamiśritaṃ yat paryuṣitaṃ tad apy apeyam | tathā yat takraṃ 'dīrṇam' darvīdhaṭṭanaśūnyāgnitāpādinā svato vā sphuṭitam | yac ca 'naṣṭāsvādaṃ' śuklaṃ naṣṭarasam | yac ca bahudinasthityā saṃjātaphenaṃ tad apy apeyam |
iti grāhyāṇi varjyāni ca kṣīrādīni |
atha grāhyāṇi māṃsāni |
tatra tāvat sāmānyataḥ śrāddhe māṃsadānavidhiḥ |
manuḥ |
munyannāni payo somo māṃsaṃ yac cānupaskṛtam |
akṣāralavaṇaṃ caiva prakṛtyā havir ucyate ||
pūrvaṃ vyākhyātaḥ ślokaḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ māsikaṃ śrāddhaṃ anvāhāryaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
tad āmiṣeṇa kartavyaṃ praśastena prayatnataḥ ||
[574]        tad etat pratimāsam amāvāsyāyāṃ vihitaṃ śrāddhaṃ tat pitṝṇām anvāhāryam iti munayo manyante | tad etad āmiṣeṇa māṃsena kartavyam | praśastenāpratiṣiddhena dvau māsau matsyamāṃsenetyādiviśeṣavihitena vā | ayaṃ ca mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | tadabhāve dadhighṛtasūpādi vidhāyiṣyate | māṃsaṃ ca vyañjanaṃ bhaktāder bhojyasya na punar etad eva kevalaṃ bhojyam | yena vakṣyati | "guṇāṃś ca sūpaśākādyānām" iti | tathā yāvantaś caiva yaiś cānnāni iti
smṛtyantare |
vinā māṃsena yat śrāddhaṃ kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ bhavet |
kravyādāḥ pitaro yasmād abhāve pāyasādayaḥ ||
purāṇe |
māṃsaṃ śākaṃ dadhi kṣīraṃ madhu cānyat tathaiva ca |
iti sāmānyataḥ śrāddhe māṃsadānavidhiḥ |
atha māṃsabhakṣaṇe vidhir ucyate |
manuḥ |
yat prokṣitaṃ bhaven māṃsaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca kāmyayā |
yathāvidhi niyukaś ca prāṇānām eva cātyaye ||
paśuṃ prākṣatītyādivihitaprokṣaṇādisaṃskārayuktāgnīṣomīyādipaśuprakṛtikaṃ māṃsaṃ prokṣitam ity ucyate | 'brāhmaṇakāmyayā' brāhmaṇecchayā | kāmyaśabdaḥ chāndaśaḥ |
[575]        yadā brāhmaṇāḥ prārthayanti tadā prokṣaṇārhaprakṛtikam apy aprokṣitaṃ kṛtābhakṣaṇasaṅkalpo ’pi bhakṣayet | 'yathāvidhi' yathāśāstraṃ, madhuparkayajñaśrāddheṣu ‘niyuktaḥ' niyogam aṅgīkāritaḥ māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet | ataḥ śrāddhe bhokṣya ity upetya idam nāśnāmīti nārhati vaktum | yatibrahmacāribhyāṃ tu niyuktābhyām api māṃsaṃ na bhakṣaṇīyam iti pratipāditaṃ prāk | 'prāṇānām atyaye' aśanāntarābhāvāt bhāviprāṇāpagamaniścaye devapitṛkāryopayuktaśeṣād anyad api niṣiddham api paunaḥpunyenāpi brahmacarye ’pi māṃsaṃ bhakṣayet | sarvata ātmānaṃ gopāyed iti vacanād ātmaghātapātakaparihārasyāvaśyakāryatvāc ca |
yamo ’pi |
bhakṣayet prokṣitaṃ māṃsaṃ sakṛt brāhmaṇakāmyayā |
daive niyuktaḥ śrāddhe vā niyame tu vivarjayet ||
aniyame pūrvapādatrayoktaniyamābhāve māṃsaṃ vivarjayet |
yājñavalkya-viśvāmitrau |
prāṇātyaye tathā śrāddhe prokṣitaṃ dvijakāmyayā |
devān pitṝn samabhyarcya khādan māṃsaṃ na duṣyati ||
devalaḥ |
bhakṣayann api māṃsāni śeṣabhojī na lipyate |
auṣadhārtham aśakau vā niyogād yajñakāraṇāt ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
rogī niyukto vidhināhitaṃ vipravṛtas tathā |
māṃsam adyāc caturdhaiṣā pariṣaṅkhyā prakīrtitā ||
[576]        'rogī' māṃsabhakṣaṇaikaparihāryarogavān | niyuktaḥ śrāddhādau | vidhināhitaṃ hutaśeṣam | 'vipravṛtaḥ' viprābhyarthitaḥ | 'caturddhā parisaṅkhyā', caturddhā catuḥprakāro niyama ity arthaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
vṛthāmāṃsaṃ bhakṣayaṃs tu puṣpakṛcchraṃ cared budhaḥ |
kāmyayā brāhmaṇānāṃ tu yathākāmaṃ samaśnuyāt ||
'yathākāmaṃ' yāvadiccham |
viṣṇuḥ |
asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kathaṃcana |
mantrais tu saṃskṛtān adyāt śāśvataṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ ||
'śāśvataṃ' nityaṃ vaidikam ity arthaḥ |
yajuṣā saṃskṛtaṃ māṃsaṃ bhakṣayīta yathāvidhi |
na bhakṣayed vṛthāmāṃsaṃ vṛthāmāṃsaṃ vivarjayet ||
śrāddhe māṃsaṃ samaśnīyāt yadātithye nivedayet |
iti māṃsabhakṣaṇavidhiḥ |
atha niyuktasya māṃsabhakṣaṇe pratyavāya ucyate |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
nimantrito yo nāśnāti śrāddhe māṃsaṃ dvijottamaḥ |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakamsṛcchati ||
yāvanti hatasya paśoḥ romāṇi tāvat saṅkhyāni ca varṣāṇi narake vasatīty arthaḥ |
[577]        manuḥ |
niyuktas tu yathānyāyaṃ yo māṃsaṃ nātti mānavaḥ |
sa pretya paśutāṃ yāti saṃbhavān ekaviṃśatim ||
'saṃbhavān' janmāni |
vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
niyuktas tu yadi śrāddhe daive māṃsaṃ samutsṛjet |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakam ṛcchati ||
yamaḥ |
āmantritas tu yaḥ śrāddhe daive māṃsaṃ samutsṛjet |
yāvanti paśuromāṇi tāvan narakam ṛcchati ||
iti niyuktasya māṃsabhakṣaṇe pratyavāyanirūpaṇaम |
atha praśastamāṃsaprakṛtayaḥ |
praśastenāmiṣeṇa śrāddhaṃ kuryād iti manuvacane pūrvam uktatvāt |
tatrāha śaṅkhaḥ |
tittiraṃ ca mayūraṃ ca lāvakaṃ ca kapiñjalam |
vārdhrīṇasaṃ varttakaṃ ca bhakṣyān āha yamaḥ sadā ||
‘tittiraḥ' citrapakṣaḥ | ‘mayūraḥ' śikhaṇḍī | 'lāvakaḥ' prasiddhaḥ | 'kapiñjalaḥ' śarur babhruvarṇaḥ pakṣī | 'vārdhrīṇasaḥ' kṛṣṇagrīva ityādinā dvādaśavārṣikatṛptau vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇaḥ | ‘varttakaḥ' vṛttākṛtiś citrabindulāñchitaḥ tulapucchaḥ pakṣiviśeṣaḥ |
manuḥ |
pāṭhīnarohitāv ādyau niyuktau havyakavyayoḥ |
[578]        rājīvān siṃhatuṇḍāṃś ca saśakāṃś caiva sarvaśaḥ ||
'pāṭhīnaḥ' bahudaṃṣṭro matsyaḥ, sahasradaṃṣṭraḥ pāṭhīna ity abhidhānāt | ‘rohitaḥ’ raktavarṇo matsyaḥ | etāv ubhau havyakavyaniyogena śrāddhādau bhakṣaṇīyau | 'rājīvāḥ' padmavarṇā matsyā iti kecit, rājībhiḥ rekhābhir yuktā ity apare | 'siṃhatuṇḍāḥ' siṃhamukhākṛtimukhāḥ | ‘saśalkāḥ’ pṛṣṭhapratiṣṭhitaśuktyākāraśakalāḥ, rājīvādyāḥ sarvaśo ’niyuktenāpi bhakṣyāḥ |
tathā |
śvāvidaṃ śalyakāṃ godhāṃ khaḍgaṃ kūrmaṃ śaśāṃs tathā |
bhakṣyān pañcanakhān āhur anutrāṃś caikatodataḥ ||
'śvāvidaḥ' śvabhakṣako vyāghraviśeṣaḥ | 'śalyakaḥ ' śalalīsaṃjñatanūruhaḥ śvāpadaviśeṣaḥ | 'godhā' dīrghākṛtir bileśayaḥ prāṇiviśeṣaḥ | 'khaḍgaḥ' gaṇḍakaḥ | ‘kūrmaḥ' kacchapaḥ prasiddhaḥ | śaśaḥ pañcanakhānāṃ madhye śvāvitprabhṛtīn bhakṣyān āhuḥ | smṛtyantare tu khaḍge vikalpaḥ |
tathā ca vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
khaḍge tu vivadante | iti |
uṣṭravarjitānekavibhāgasthitadantapaṅkīn go'jāvimṛgān bhakṣyān āhuḥ |
bhakṣyā ity anuvṛttau baudhāyanaḥ |
matsyāḥ sahasradaṃṣṭrā go'jāvimṛgā bṛhacchirasaḥ tittirirohitarājīvāḥ |
bhakṣyā ity anuvṛttau gautamaḥ |
matsyāśvāvikṛtāḥ |
[579]        vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
śvāvicchalyakaśaśakacchapagodhāḥ pañcanakhānāṃ bhakṣyāḥ anuṣṭrāḥ paśūnāṃ adantāś ca |
devalaḥ |
pañca pañcanakhā bhakṣyāḥ dharmataḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
godhākūrmaśaśāḥ śvāvit śalyakaś ceti te smṛtāḥ ||
bhakṣyā ity anuvṛttau baudhāyanaḥ |
pakṣiṇaḥ tittirikapotakapiñjalalāvaṛṇamayūravāraṇācāraṇāvarjyāḥ pañca viṣkirāḥ |
'kapotaḥ' prasiddhaḥ | viṣkirāṇāṃ madhye ete pañca bhakṣyāḥ | 'vāraṇācāraṇau' kapotabhedau ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
māhiṣaṃ cājam aurabhraṃ mārgaṃ rauravam eva ca |
bhakṣyamāṃsaṃ samuddiṣṭaṃ yac ca vai pārṣataṃ smṛtam |
varāhāś ca tathā bhakṣyā mahāraṇyanivāsinaḥ ||
mahiṣasyedaṃ 'māhiṣam' | urabhrasya meṣasya māṃsaṃ 'aurabhram' | mṛgasya tāmrahariṇasya 'mārgam' | ruroḥ kṛṣṇasāraṅgasya bahuśṛṅgasya vā mṛgasya māṃsaṃ 'rauravam' | pṛṣatasya binduvato mṛgasya 'pārṣataṃ' | 'varāhāḥ' śūkarāḥ |
hārītaḥ |
grāmyāraṇyānāṃ paśūnām ṛcchanti yathājameṣahiraṇyakhaḍgarurupṛṣataṛṣyababhrumahāraṇya-vāsinaś ca varāhās tathā śaśakaśalyakamedhā[580]godhākūrmavartakatittiramayūravārdhrīṇasa- lāvakakurarakapiñjalān saśalkāṃś ca matsyān nyāyopapannān bhakṣayet |
iti praśastamāṃsaprakṛtayaḥ |
atha māṃsabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhaḥ |
manuḥ |
asaṃskṛtān paśūn mantrair nādyād vipraḥ kadācana |
mantrais tu saṃskṛtān adyād vaidikaṃ vidhim āsthitaḥ ||
prokṣaṇādisaṃskāramantareṇa ye nihitās tān nādyāt | ye tu vaidikair mantraiḥ saṃskṛtās tān eva bhakṣayet |
pulastyo ’pi |
varjayed dūrataḥ śrāddhe yad aprokṣitam āmiṣam |
rājñānutpāditaṃ yac ca vyādhinābhihataṃ ca yat ||
‘aprokṣitaṃ' prokṣaṇādirahitaṃ māṃsam, śrāddhe brāhmaṇādir datā varjayet | rājñā tu mṛgavyena svayaṃ yad anutpāditam | vyādhinābhihatāt paśor yad gṛhītaṃ tat sarvaṃ varjyam | ayam aprokṣitapratiṣedho grāmyapaśuviṣayaḥ | āraṇyās tu prokṣaṇamantareṇāpi praśastā eva |
brahmapurāṇe |
āraṇyānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ prokṣaṇaṃ brahmaṇā kṛtam |
ata eva tu te bhakṣyā brāhmaṇakṣatriyādibhiḥ ||
vyāso ’pi |
[581]        prajānāṃ hitakāmena agastyena mahātmanā |
āraṇyāḥ sarvadevatyāḥ prokṣitāḥ sarvaśo mṛgāḥ |
tasmād aprokṣitā eva prayojyās te sadā budhaiḥ ||
āraṇyānāṃ paśūnāṃ agastyena brahmaṇā ca sarvārthatayā prāg eva prokṣaṇasya kṛtatvād anyair na kartavyam ity arthaḥ |
atra kaścid āha | asaṃskṛtān paśūn nādyād ity ayaṃ phalaviśeṣakāmasya paryudāsavacano na pratiṣedhaḥ | ataḥ phalārthitvābhāve ’saṃskṛtam api māṃsaṃ bhakṣaṇīyam eveti |
tan na | yataḥ pratiṣedhatve saṃbhavati paryudāsatvaṃ phalakalpanā ceti na nyāyyam |
na māṃsabhakṣaṇe doṣo na madye na ca maithune |
pravṛttir eṣā jantūnāṃ nivṛttis tu mahāphalā ||
ityādivacanoktanyāyād iti cet |
na, asya vacanasya mantrarahitaśūdrādiviṣayatvenaivopapatteḥ | ato vipro mantrasaṃskṛtam eva māṃsam adyān nānyat | yena hi māṃsena devāś ca pitaraś cārcyante tan mantrasaṃskṛtam eva bhavati | devapitrarcanasaṃbaddhasyaiva brāhmaṇabhojyatvena vihitatvāt |
tad uktaṃ manunā |
devān pitṝn samabhyarcya khādan māṃsaṃ na doṣabhāg || iti |
vidhipūrvakabhakṣaṇe guṇam abhidhāyāvidhipūrvakabhakṣaṇe doṣam āha sa eva |
ya stu bhakṣayate māṃsaṃ vidhiṃ hitvā piśācavat |
sa loke ’priyatāṃ yāti vyādhibhiś caiva pīḍyate ||
tathā |
[582]        yajñāya jagadhir māṃsasyety eṣa daivo vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
tato ’nyathā prākṛtas tu rākṣaso vidhir ucyate ||
'jagdhiḥ ' bhakṣaṇam |
viṣṇuḥ |
nādyād avidhinā māṃsaṃ vidhijño 'nāpadi dvijaḥ |
jagdhvā tu vidhinā māṃsaṃ pretya tair arcyate tu saḥ ||
taiḥ paśubhir arcyate |
hārīta-śātātapau |
viṣachadmahataṃ māṃsaṃ vyādhibhir jagdhitaṃ ca yat |
na praśaṃsanti vai śrāddhe yac ca mantravivarjitam ||
‘viṣeṇa’, ‘chadmanā' kūṭayantrādiprakāreṇa, hatasya tathā vyādhibhiḥ siṃhavyāghravyatiriktaśvāpadaiś ca hatasya paśor yan māṃsaṃ yac ca mantravarjitaṃ tac chrāddhādau na praśaṃsanti arhaṃ na manyante |
āpastambaḥ |
hiṃsārthenāsinā chinnaṃ tad abhojyaṃ vidur budhāḥ |
iti māṃsabhakṣaṇapratiṣedhaḥ |
atha niṣiddhamāṃsaprakṛtayaḥ |
tatrāha manuḥ |
kravyādaḥ śakunīna sarvān tathā grāmanivāsinaḥ |
anirdiṣṭāṃś caikaśaphān ṭiṭṭibhāṃś ca vivarjayet ||
[583]        kalaviṅkaṃ plavaṃ haṃsaṃ cakrāhvaṃ grāmakukkuṭam |
sārasaṃ rajjuvālaṃ ca dātyūhaṃ śukasārike ||
pratudān jālapādāṃś ca koyaṣṭinakhaviṣkirān |
nimajjataś ca matsyādān saunaṃ vallūram eva ca ||
bakaṃ caiva balākāṃ ca kākolaṃ khañjarīṭakam |
matsyādān viḍvarāhāṃś ca matsyān eva ca sarvaśaḥ ||
kravyaṃ māṃsam adantīti 'kravyādaḥ' kevalamāṃsabhakṣakāḥ kākagṛdhrādayaḥ | ‘grāmanivāsinaḥ' akravyādo ’pi pārāvatādayaḥ | 'ekaśaphāḥ' aśvāśvataragardabhādayaḥ | ‘anirdiṣṭāḥ śrutau bhakṣyatvena noktāḥ | ye tūkās te tatra bhakṣyāḥ | yathā “tvāṣṭraṃ vāḍvam ālabheta gauraṃ vā gardabhaṃ prajākāmasya ca māṃsam aśnīyāt” iti | 'ṭiṭṭibhāḥ' atyuccataraśabdopalakṣitāḥ | kadutkāṇanāmānaḥ pakṣiviśeṣāḥ 'ṭiṭṭibhāḥ' | 'kalaviṅkaḥ' grāmacaṭako nigame dṛṣṭaḥ | tasya grāmavāsitvena prasiddhā satyām api puṃliṅgaśabdena punas tatpratiṣedhaḥ tatstriyāś cābhyanujñānārtham | anye tu kalaviṅkaśabdasya caṭakajātimātravacanatvaṃ manyamānāḥ āṇyanivṛttyarthaṃ tatpratiṣedhaṃ varṇayanti | 'plavaḥ' jalakukkuṭaḥ | 'haṃsaḥ' marālaḥ | 'cakrāhvaḥ' cakravākaḥ | plavahaṃsacakravākānāṃ varjyamānajālapādapratiṣedhāt siddhe pratiṣedhe nityārthaṃ vacanam | ataś cāṣādīnāṃ vikalpena bhakṣaṇaṃ gamyate | grāmagrahaṇād āraṇyakukuṭo bhakṣyaḥ | 'sārasaḥ' puṣkarāhvayaḥ | sa ca dīrghagalajaṅgho nīlāṅgaḥ pakṣī | 'rajjuvālaḥ' rajjusadṛśadīrghakeśaḥ pakṣī | [584] rajudāla iti tu pāṭhe vṛkṣakukkuṭako jñeyaḥ | 'dātyūhaḥ' kālakaṇṭhaḥ | 'śukaḥ' kīraḥ | 'sārikā' 'sārikā' evaṃ nāmaprasiddhā | pratudya prahatya cañcvā ye bhakṣayantīti 'pratudāḥ' | jīvajīvakabhṛṅgāhvaladvārakokilahāritakapotaśatapacādayaḥ | 'jālapādāḥ' jālasadṛśapādāḥ | cāṣādayaḥ | 'koyaṣṭiḥ' āraṇyapakṣiviśeṣaḥ | nakhair vikīrya bhakṣayanti ye te 'nakhaviṣkirāḥ' vātīkavartīraktavartmakakubhopacakrāhvacakorakurarāhvakrakacakaraṇān arddhagirivarttakaśārapadedrābhavāḍādayaḥ | nimajya ye matsyān bhakṣayanti jalavāyasaprabhṛtayaḥ te ’pi varjyaḥ | yatra māṃsavikrayārthaṃ paśavo hanyante sā sūnā | āpaṇo māṃsasyety eke | sunāyāṃ bhavaṃ ‘saunam' | 'vallūraṃ' saṃśoṣya sthāpitaṃ chāgādimāṃsam | bakaḥ prasiddhaḥ | 'balākā' vikaṇṭakā | 'kākolaḥ' kṛṣṇakākaḥ | 'khañjarīṭaḥ' khañjanaḥ | yo varṣāsu śikhodgamenāhṛṣyo bhavati | bakabalākākākolānāṃ nimajjan matsādaiḥ saha paunaruktyaṃ parihartuṃ ye kadācid unmajjya na bhakṣayanti ye ca kevalān matsyān evādanti tatra makarādayo matsyādaśabdena gṛhyante | viḍvarāhapratiṣedhād āraṇyasyābhyanujñā | pūrvasūtrasthitasya grāmanivāsiśabdasya prakaraṇāt pakṣiviśeṣaṇatve siddhe viḍvarāhaviṣayātvābhāvād apaunaruktyam | 'matsyāḥ' mīnāḥ | 'sarvaśaḥ' sarvadā | utsargo ’yam |
yamaḥ |
matsyān aśalkān vai sarvān vedādhyāyī vivarjayed iti |
'aśalkān' pṛṣṭhabhāge śalkarahitān | 'vedādhyāyī' traivarṇikaḥ |
[585]        devalaḥ |
balāko haṃsadātyūhabhṛṅgarājabakacitrakāḥ |
ulūkakuraraśyenagṛdhrakukkuṭavāyasāḥ ||
cakoraḥ kokilo rajjuvālakaś cāṣamadgukau |
pārāvatakapotau ca na bhakṣyāḥ pakṣiṇaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
'bhṛṅgarājaḥ' tenaiva nāmnā prasiddhaḥ | 'citrakaḥ' mayūraḥ | 'ulūkaḥ' kauśikaḥ ghūkaś ca | 'kuraraḥ' krośakaḥ | 'cakoraḥ' candrikāharo lohitacañcucaraṇacakṣuḥ pakṣī | 'kokilaḥ' kuhūkaṇṭhaḥ | 'cāṣaḥ' kikīdiviḥ | 'madguḥ' jalavāyasaḥ |
devalaḥ |
abhakṣyāḥ paśujātīnāṃ gokharoṣṭrāś ca kuñjarāḥ |
siṃhavyāghrarkṣaśarabha-arpājagarakās tathā ||
ākhusūkaramājāranakulagrāmyasūkarāḥ |
śvasṛgālakapidvīpigolāṅgūlakamarkaṭāḥ || iti |
‘kharaḥ’ gardabhaḥ | ‘ṛkṣaḥ' bhalluḥ | 'śarabhaḥ' aṣṭacaraṇaḥ siṃhaśatruḥ | 'ākhuḥ' sthūlonduraḥ | 'nakulaḥ' babhruḥ | 'sṛgālaḥ' jambukaḥ | 'kapiḥ' plavaṅgaḥ | 'dvīpī' mahāvyāghraḥ | 'golāṅgūlaḥ' gopucchasadṛśapuccho vānaraviśeṣaḥ | 'markaṭaḥ' lohitamukhaḥ śākhāmṛgaḥ | atroktāḥ pañcanakhāḥ śvāvitprabhṛtipañcanakhavyatiriktapañcanakhopalakṣaṇārthāḥ | ata eva na bhakṣayed ity anuvṛttau āha manuḥ "sarvān pañcanakhāṃs tathā" iti |
iti niṣiddhamāṃsaprakṛtayaḥ |
[586]        atha kālaviśeṣāvacchedena tṛptikarāṇi dravyāṇy ucyante |
tatrāha manuḥ |
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ prīyante vidhivat pitaro nṛṇām ||
dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākuneneha pañca tu ||
ṣaṇmāsān chāgamāṃsena pārṣatenātha sapta tu |
aṣṭāv aiṇena māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu ||
daśa māsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ |
śaśakūrmayos tu māṃsena māsān ekādaśaiva tu ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena vā |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkaḥ khaḍgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpyante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ ||
śrāddhavidhim āśritya dattatilādidravyeṇaikavāraṃ māsam ekaṃ pitaras tṛptyanuvṛttyā prīyante | atra dvijātikartṛkasya mukhyasya śrāddhasya brāhmaṇabhojanātmakatvād annākāṅkṣāyāṃ tatprītiyogyānāṃ tilavrīhyādīnāṃ tṛptisādhanatvena vidhīyamānānāṃ dṛṣṭadvāreṇa sādhanatvopapattaye svavikārabhūtānnadvāreṇaiva tṛptisādhanatvaṃ teṣāṃ ca tat prathamaṃ samuccitānām eva bhojanasya lokataḥ samuccitanānāvidhānnaphalamūlādisādhyatvāvagamāt | alābhe tu tṛptikaratvayogyānnaprakṛtibhūtasyaikasyāpi śrāddhīyabhojanasādhanatvam | laukikopāyasādhanakaṃ hi bhojanaṃ samuccitālābhe [587] ekenāpi dṛśyate | evaṃ cāmaśrāddhe ’pi samuccayāsamuccayau veditavyau, tatrāpi teṣāṃ bhojyānnaprakṛtibhāvānupasandhānenaivopakalpitatvāt | uktanyāyād annadvāreṇaiṣāṃ sādhanatvam āmaśrāddhe ca vacanād apodyata iti naiṣa doṣaḥ |
annadvāreṇaivaiṣāṃ śrāddhasādhanatvam uktaṃ pracetasā |
haritamudgakṛṣṇamāṣaśyāmākapriyaṅgugodhūmekṣuvikārāṃś
ca dadyāt | iti |
vidhīyante cāpūpapāyasādīny annāni śrāddhe | tāni ca prakṛtidravyasāṅkṣāni | ato vrīhipuroḍāsayor iva tilavrīhyādīnām annānāṃ prakṛtivikārabhāvenānvayasiddhiḥ | na vikalpo doṣabāhulyāt |
kātyāyanavacane cānnībhūtānām evaiteṣāṃ tṛptisādhanatvaṃ sphuṭaṃ bhaviṣyati | saty api samuccaye adbhir mūlaphalena veti vikalpavacanaṃ brāhmaṇatṛptiparyāptānna-sādhanībhūtavrīhyādyalābhe 'nukalparūpapakṣāntarābhiprāyeneti kātyāyanavyākhyāne ca darśayiṣyate |
mātṛptikarāṇi tu mūlaphalāni mārkaṇḍeyena prapañcitāni |
vidāryais tu parūṣais tu bisaiḥ śṛṅgāṭakais tathā |
kambukaiś ca tathā kandaiḥ karkandhubadarair api ||
pālevatair ārukaiś ca akṣoṭaiḥ panasais tathā |
kākolyā kṣīrakākolyā tathā piṇḍālukaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
lājābhiś ca sadhānābhiḥ trapusaiś cārucir bhaṭaiḥ |
sarṣapārājaśākābhyām iṅgudai rājajambubhiḥ ||
priyālāmalakair mukhyaiḥ phalgubhiś ca vilambakaiḥ |
vaṃśāṅkurais tālakandaiḥ cukrikākṣīrikādhavaiḥ ||
[588]        vocaiḥ samocair lakucais tathā vai bījapūrakaiḥ |
muñjātakaiḥ padmaphalair bhakṣyabhojyais tu saṃskṛtaiḥ ||
rāgaṣāḍavacoṣyaiś ca cijātakasamanvitaiḥ |
dattais tu māsaṃ prīyante śrāddheṣu pitaro nṛṇām ||
'kandaḥ' sūraṇaḥ | arśoghnaḥ sūraṇaḥ kanda ityabhidhānāt | 'badaraṃ' badarīphalam | 'kākolīkṣīrakākolyau' gauḍadeśe prasiddhau | sarṣapeti strīliṅgatayā nirdeśaḥ chāndasaḥ | 'rājaśākaṃ' rājavṛkṣākhyaṃ śākam | 'iṅgudī' tāpasataruḥ | 'rājajambuḥ' jambuviśeṣaḥ | 'mukhyāny āmalakāni’ sthūlāmalakāni | 'phalgūni' kṣudrāmalakāni | 'vilambakāni' paṭolāni | 'vaṃśāṅkuraḥ' prasiddhaḥ | 'kṣīrikā' phalādhyakṣam | rājādanaḥ phalādhyakṣe kṣīrikāyām ity amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt | 'mocaṃ' kadalam | 'lakucaḥ' likucaḥ | sa ca jambīraphalatulyakharvaphalavān gulmaviśeṣaḥ | ‘bījapūraḥ’ mātuliṅgaḥ | 'muñjātaṃ' gauḍadeśe prasiddhaṃ phalam | ‘padmaphalaṃ’ padmabījakośakarṇiketi yāvat | mudgāḍhakyādīni māṃsāni vā mūlaphalādīni niṣkāṣya gṛhītā rasāḥ śarkarāmadhvādidravyasaṅgena madhūrīkṛtāḥ 'rāgāḥ' | amladravyasaṃyogenāmlīkṛtās tu 'ṣāḍavāḥ' ucyante | 'coṣyāṇi' vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇāni | 'trijātakaṃ' lavaṅgailāpatrakāṇāṃ samāhāraḥ | etair dravyair māsam ekaṃ pitaraḥ prīyante | evam aniṣiddhena matsyamāṃsena dvau māsau | 'hariṇaḥ' tāmramṛgaḥ tasya māṃsaṃ ‘hāriṇam’ | urabhro meṣaḥ tasya māṃsaṃ 'aurabhram' | śakuniḥ pakṣī | sa cātrāraṇyakukkuṭatittirādir aniṣiddha eva vivakṣitaḥ | tasya māṃsaṃ 'śākunaṃ' | 'chāgaḥ' bastaḥ | pṛṣataḥ citramṛgaḥ tasyedaṃ 'pārṣatam' | eṇaḥ [589] kṛṣṇamṛgaḥ | tasyedam aiṇam, "eṇaḥ kṛṣṇamṛgo jñeyas tāmro hariṇa ucyate" ity abhidhānāt | ye tv aṣṭāv aiṇyamāṃseneti paṭhanti tatra hariṇīmāṃsenety ucyate | ‘ruruḥ’ bahuśṛṅgo mṛgaḥ | tasya māṃsaṃ 'rauravam' | 'varāhaḥ' araṇyasukaraḥ, grāmasūkarasya niṣiddhatvāt | 'mahiṣaḥ' lulāpaḥ | 'amiṣaṃ' māṃsaṃ | atra "māhiṣāṇi ca māṃsāni śrāddheṣu parivarjayet" iti viṣṇupurāṇadarśanāt māhiṣamāṃsasya vihitapratiṣiddhatvena vikalpo veditavyaḥ | sa ca "tad āmiṣeṇa kartavyaṃ praśastena prayatnataḥ" iti "vinā māṃsena yat śrāddhaṃ kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ bhavet" ityādivacanāvagatāvaśyakāṅgabhāvasya māṃsāntarasyābhāve mahiṣasya grahaṇaṃ kartavyam anyadā nety ekarūpa iti kecit | tad vicāraṇīyaṃ | apraśastasya hy avagatatvena sarvathā pratiṣedhe gṛhyamāṇasya praśastahariṇorabhrāchāgādimāṃsebhyo 'dhikaṃ māhiṣyam ucyamānaṃ viruddhaṃ syāt | ‘śaśaḥ' prasiddhaḥ | 'kūrmaḥ' kacchapaḥ | śrutasambandhabalīyastvān māhiṣeṇa payaseti sambandho na māṃseneti |
śaṅkhadharas tv āha |
gavyaṃ prakaraṇān māṃsam eva |
ata eva vyāsaḥ |
gavayasya tu māṃsena tṛptir māsaṃ sadaiva tu |
māsānekādaśa prītiḥ pitṝṇāṃ māhiṣeṇa tu |
gavye tu datte śrāddheṣu saṃvatsaram ihocyate || iti |
tan na kalau gavālambhasya niṣiddhatvāt lokavidviṣṭatvāc ceti | payasā siddham odanaṃ 'pāyasaṃ' payovikāro dadhyādi ca | 'vārdhrīṇasaḥ' chāgaviśeṣaḥ pakṣiviśeṣo vā |
[590]        tathā ca nigama-viṣṇudharmottarayoḥ |
triḥ pibantv indriyakṣīṇaṃ śvetaṃ vṛddham ajāpatim |
vārdhrīṇasaṃ tu taṃ prāhur yājñikāḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
yasya pibato jalaṃ karṇau spṛśataḥ sa kila karṇābhyāṃ mukhena pibatīti 'triḥpibaḥ' | 'kṣīṇendriyaḥ' saṃbhogākṣamaḥ | 'ajāpatiḥ' aja ity arthaḥ | pāṭhāntare |
triḥ pibantv indriyakṣīṇaṃ yūthasyāgracaraṃ tathā |
rakaṃ varṇena rājendra ajaṃ vārdhrīṇasaṃ viduḥ ||
tathā nigama eva |
kṛṣṇagrīvo raktaśirāḥ śvetapakṣo vihaṅgamaḥ |
sa vai vārdhrīṇasaḥ prokta ity eṣā naigamī śrutiḥ ||
kālaśākādīni dhānyaprakaraṇe vyākhyātāni | iha sarvatra prītyatiśayo vivakṣitaḥ | na tu yathāśruta eva kālaḥ | tathā sati dvādaśavarṣaparyantaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptatvāt śrāddhākaraṇe prāpte sati na “prativarṣaṃ yatnāt kāryaṃ” ity etad vacanaṃ viruddhyata iti hariharādayaḥ |
āha kātyāyanaḥ |
atha tṛptir grāmyābhir oṣadhībhir māsaṃ tṛptir āraṇyābhivir vā tadalābhe mūlaphalairadbhir vā sahānnenottarās tarpayanti | chāgosameṣā ālabdhavyāḥ śeṣāṇi krītvā labdhā vā svayaṃ mṛtānāṃ vāhṛtya pacen māsadvayaṃ mātsyaiḥ, trīn māsān hāriṇamṛgamāṃsena, caturaḥ śākunena, pañca rauraveṇa ṣaṭ chāgena, sapta kaurmeṇa, aṣṭau vārāheṇa, nava meṣamāṃsena, daśa māhiṣeṇa, ekādaśa pārṣatena, saṃvatsaraṃ gavyena pāyasena payasā vā, vārdhrīṇasasya [591] māṃsena ca dvādaśavarṣāṇi akṣayā tṛptiḥ | khaḍgakālaśākalohachāgo mahāśalko varṣāsu maghāsu ca śrāddhaṃ hasticchāyāyāṃ ca |
‘grāmyābhiḥ' śāliyavagodhūmamuṅgādyābhiḥ, 'oṣadhībhiḥ' sakṛddattābhir māsaṃ tṛptir bhavati | 'āraṇyābhiḥ' araṇyauṣadhībhiḥ māsam eva tṛptiḥ | yat tv āraṇyānām eva smṛtyantare tṛptisādhanatvam uktaṃ, tad vānaprasthādikartṛkaśrāddhaviṣayam | 'tadalābhe' grāmyāraṇyānām oṣadhīnām alābhe, mūlaphalair adbhiś ca māsam eva tṛptiḥ | mūlādīnāṃ kevalānāṃ tṛptisādhanatvaṃ nirākartum āha sahānneneti | 'uttarāḥ' mūlaphalādayaḥ | oṣadhīnāmu pariṣṭān nirdiṣṭatvād uttarāḥ annena sahaiva dattāḥ satyas tarpayanti na kevalāḥ, tadannopasarjanībhūtā eva tṛptikāritvena loke prasiddhāḥ |
nanv oṣadhīnām alābhe mūlādīnāṃ pradeyatvam annasāhityaṃ ceti vipratiṣiddham |
maivaṃ, iha hi saṃpūrṇatṛptisādhanībhūtānnasaṃpādanaparyāptānām oṣadhīnām alābhe svalpānnasahitānāṃ mūlādīnāṃ tṛptisādhanatvam ucyata ity avirodhāt |
atha cottarāḥ saty anne sahānnena, asati tu tasmin kevalā ity anusaṃdheyaṃ |
atha caivaṃ vyākhyā | grāmyauṣadhisiddhānnālābhe mūlaphalair adbhiś ca sahitāḥ ‘uttarāḥ' āraṇyā oṣadhyaḥ, 'annena' annadvārā, māsaṃ tarpayanti | yady api grāmyauṣadhisiddhānnasāhityaṃ śākatvena mūlādīnām asty eva tathāpy āraṇyānāṃ mandarasatvāt śākasāhityam apekṣitataram iti prādhānyena tatroktam iti mantavyam | annādibhir māṃsaṃ tṛptim uktā māṃsair dvimāsādititṛptiṃ pratipādayiṣyaṃs tadupādānavidhiṃ tāvad āha | chāgosrameṣā ālabdhavyā [592] ityādi | ‘usraḥ' anaḍvān | prokṣaṇādisaṃskārapūrvakaṃ saṃjñapanaṃ 'ālambhaḥ' | etat paśutrayavyatiriktānāṃ tu paśūnāṃ prokṣaṇādisaṃskāram antareṇāpi svayaṃ pareṇa vā hatānāṃ krayādyupāyasaṃpāditaṃ māṃsaṃ śrāddhādāv upayojanīyam |
tathā cāhatuḥ hārīta-śātātapau |
kṣatriyais tu mṛgavyena vidhinā samupārjitam |
śrāddhakāle praśaṃsanti siṃhavyāghrahataṃ ca yat ||
manuḥ |
krītvā svayaṃ vāpy utpādya paropahṛtam eva vā |
devān pitṝṃś cārcayitvā khādan māṃsaṃ na duṣyati ||
gautamaḥ |
vyālahatādṛṣṭadoṣavākpraśastāny abhyukṣyopayuñjīteti |
āhośanāḥ |
tatra vrīhiyavair māṣair arcitā māmaṃ pitaras tṛptā bhavanti, māsadayaṃ mātsyena māṃsena, trayaṃ hariṇamṛgamāṃsena, caturo māsān kṛṣṇasāraṅgena, pañca śākunena, ṣaṭ chāgena, sapta pārṣatena, aṣṭau varāheṇa, nava ruruṇā, meṣeṇa daśa, ekādaśa kūrmeṇa, pāyasena payasā gavyena saṃvatsaraṃ, vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī | khaḍgamāṃsenānantyam api codāharanti |
khaḍgaś ca kālaśākaṃ ca lohachāgas tathaiva ca |
mahāśalkaś ca mannaṃ (?) pitryenantyāya (?) kalpyate ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
[593]        haviṣyānnena vai māsaṃ pāyasena tu vatsaram |
mātsyahāriṇakaurabhraśākunachāgapārṣataiḥ ||
aiṇarauravavārāhaśāśair māṃsair yathākramam |
māsavṛddhyābhitṛpyanti dattair iha pitāmahāḥ ||
khaḍgāmiṣaṃ mahāśalkaṃ madhu munyannam eva ca |
lohāmiṣaṃ kālaśākaṃ māṃsaṃ vārdhrīṇasasya ca |
yad dadāti gayāsthaś ca sarvam ānantyam aśnute ||
'haviṣyaṃ' haviryogyaṃ tilavrīhyādi, tatprakṛtikenānnena sakṛd dattena māsaparyantaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptir bhavati | tata ūrdhvaṃ tu yathākramam ekaikamāsavṛddhyā mātsyād ārabhya śāśaparyantair māṃsais tṛptir bhavati | matsyā aniṣiddhā rohitādayaḥ | ‘śāśaṃ’ śaśamāṃsam | 'munyannaṃ' nīvārādi |
yamaḥ |
adbhir mūlaphalaiḥ śākaiḥ puṇyair vrīhiyavais tilaiḥ |
prīṇāti māsaṃ dattena śrāddheneha pitāmahān ||
matsyaiḥ prīṇāti māsau dvau trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
śalyakaś caturo māṣān rurūḥ prīṇāti pañca ca ||
śaśaḥ prīṇāti ṣaṇ māsān kūrmaḥ prīṇāti sapta tu |
aṣṭau māsān varāhas tu meṣaḥ prīṇayate nava ||
māhiṣaṃ daśa māsāṃs tu gāvayaṃ rudrasaṃmitān |
gavyaṃ dvādaśamāsāṃs tu payaḥ pāyasam eva vā ||
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃse tu tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī |
ānantyāya bhaved dattaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ pitṛkṣaye |
[594]        pitṛkṣayo gayā jñeyā tatra dattaṃ mahāphalam ||
kālaśākaṃ ca khaḍgaś ca lohachāgas tathaiva ca |
mahāśalkāś ca matsyāś ca pitrye ’nantyāya kalpitāḥ ||
yat kiṃcin madhusaṃyuktaṃ tad ānantyāya kalpayet |
upākṛtaṃ tu vidhinā mantreṇānnaṃ tathākṛtaṃ ||
gajayo nāmāraṇyo gavā sahātyantasadṛśaḥ paśus tasya māṃsaṃ 'gāvayam' | 'rudrasaṃmitān' ekādaśeti yāvat | 'pitṛkṣaye' pitṛṇāṃ kṣayo gṛhaṃ, tac ca gayāsaṃjñakaṃ tīrtham | 'upākaraṇaṃ' vaidikasaṃskāraviśeṣaḥ |
devalaḥ |
adbhir mūlaphalaiḥ śākair vrīhimāṣayavair api |
māsaṃ tṛpyanti pitaraḥ śrāddheṣv iha niveditaiḥ ||
tuṣyanti matsyair dvau māsau trīn māsān rurubhir mṛgaiḥ |
śākunaiś caturo māsān pañca tṛpyanti pārṣataiḥ ||
śaśaiḥ ṣānmāsikī tṛptiḥ kūrmaiḥ syāt saptamāsikī |
aṣṭau māsān varāhena nava tṛpyanty ajena ca ||
daśa māhiṣamāṃsena tṛpyanty ekādaśāvikaiḥ |
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena vā ||
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃse tu tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī |
ānantyāya bhaved dattaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ pitṛkṣaye ||
mahāśakalino matsyāś chāgo vā sarvalohitaḥ |
kālaśākakam ity etad ānantyāya prakīrtitam ||
[595]        āha viṣṇuḥ |
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlalaphalaḥ śākaiḥ priyaṅgunīvāraiḥ mudgaiḥ godhūmaiś ca māsaṃ prīyante | dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena, trīn māsān hāriṇena tu, caturaś caurabhreṇa, pañca śākunena, ṣaṭ chāgena, sapta rauraveṇa, aṣṭau pārṣatena, nava gāvayena, daśa māhiṣeṇaikādaśa tūpareṇājena, saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena ca tadvikārair vā | kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkā vā vārdhrīṇasamāṃsaṃ khaḍgamāṃsam akṣayyāya |
atra pitṛgītā gāthā bhavanti |
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkā māṃsaṃ vārdhrīṇasasya ca |
viṣāṇavarjyā ye khaḍgā āsūryaṃ tān labhemahi ||
'gāthāḥ' ślokaviśeṣāḥ | 'śsūryaṃ' yāvac candrasūryādipadārthānām avasthānaṃ, tāvatkālaṃ tān vayaṃ labhemahīti pitaraḥ prārthayante |
viṣṇudharmottare |
khaḍgā viṣāṇaiḥ parivarjitā ye
teṣāṃ hi māṃsena bhavaty anantam |
āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ghṛtena māṃsaṃ prīṇāti | kālaśākena dvimāsaṃ, yavāgvapūpakṛśareṇa trimāsaṃ, matsyaiś caturo māsān, ājeṇa māṃsena pañca, pāyasena ṣaṇmāsān, śākunena sapta māsān, aṣṭau māsān aiṇeyena nava māsān, pārṣatena daśa māsān, gavyena ekādaśa māsān, māhiṣeṇa dvādaśa māsān, chāgena khagena trayodaśa māsān, sarvalohachāgenānantyam | iti |
brahmapurāṇe |
māsaṃ tṛptiḥ pitṝṇāṃ tu haviṣyānnena jāyate |
[596]        māsadvayaṃ matsyamāṃsais tṛptiṃ yānti pitāmahāḥ ||
trīn māsān hāriṇaṃ māṃsaṃ vijñeyaṃ pitṛptaye |
prīṇāti caturo māsān śaśīyaiḥ piśitaiḥ pitṝn ||
śākunaṃ pañca vai māsān ṣaṇ māṣān śūkarāmiṣam |
chāgalaṃ sapta vai māsān aiṇaṃ caivāṣṭamāsikīm ||
karoti tṛptiṃ nava vai rurumāṃsaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
gavayasya māṃsaṃ tṛptiṃ karoti daśamāsikīm ||
tathaikādaśa māsāṃs tu aurabhraṃ pitṛtṛptidam |
saṃvatsaraṃ tathā gavyaṃ payaḥ pāyasam eva ca ||
vārdhrīṇasāmiṣaṃ lohaṃ kālaśākaṃ tathā madhu |
duhitrāmiṣam annaṃ ca dattāny ātmakulodbhavaiḥ ||
anantāṃ vai prayacchanti tṛptiṃ gaurīsutas tathā |
pitṝṇāṃ nātra saṃdeho gayāśrāddhaṃ ca bho dvijāḥ ||
'duhitrāmiṣaṃ' khaḍgamāṃsam |
kūrmapurāṇe |
vrīhibhiś ca yavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
śyāmākaiś caṇakaiḥ śākaiḥ nīvāraiś ca priyaṅgubhiḥ |
godhūmaiś ca tilair mudgair māsaṃ prīṇayate pitṝn ||
dau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīn māsāna hāriṇena tu |
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākuneneha pañca tu ||
ṣaṇmāsān mṛgamāṃsena pārṣateneha sapta vai |
aṣṭāv eṇasya māṃsena rauraveṇa navaiva tu ||
[597]        daśa māsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ |
śaśakacchapayor māṃsaṃ māsānekādaśaiva tu ||
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena tu |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī ||
kālaśākaṃ mahāśalkāḥ khaḍgalohāmiṣaṃ madhu |
ānantyāyaiva kalpyante munyannāni ca sarvaśaḥ ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
guṇaiś ca sūpaśākādyair nānābhakṣais tathaiva ca |
annaṃ tu sudadhikṣīragoghṛtaṃ śarkarānvitam ||
māsaṃ prīṇāti vai sarvān pitṝn ity āha bāhujaḥ |
dvau māsau matsyamāṃsena trīn māsān hāriṇena tu ||
aurabhreṇātha caturaḥ śākunenātha pañca vai |
sapta lohasya māṃsena tathāṣṭāv eṇajena tu ||
pṛṣatasya tu māṃsena tṛptir māsān navaiva tu |
daśa māsāṃs tu tṛpyanti varāhamahiṣāmiṣaiḥ ||
śaśakūrmasya māṃsena māsān ekādaśaiva tu |
saṃvatsaraṃ tu gavyena payasā pāyasena vā ||
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī |
kālaśākena cānantyaṃ khaḍgamāṃsena caiva hi ||
yat kiṃcin madhusaṃmiśraṃ gokṣīraghṛtapāyasam |
dattam akṣavyam ity āhuḥ pitaraḥ pūrvadevatāḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ prīyante śrāddhena tu pitāmahāḥ ||
matsyais tṛpyanti dvau māsau trīn māsān hāriṇena tu |
śaśas tu caturo māsān pañca prīṇāti śākunam ||
varāheṇa tu ṣaṇmāsān chāgalaṃ saptamāsikam |
aṣṭamāsikam ity ukaṃ yac ca vai pārṣataṃ bhavet ||
rauraveṇa tu prīyante nava māsān pitāmahāḥ |
gavayasya tu māṃsena tṛptiḥ syād daśamāsikī ||
kūrmasyaiva tu māṃsena māmān ekādaśaiva tu |
śrāddham evaṃ vijānīyāt gavyaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ bhavet ||
tathā gavyasamāyuktaṃ pāyasaṃ madhusarpiṣā |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī ||
ānantyāya bhaved dattaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ pitṛkṣaye |
gayāyām akṣayaṃ śrāddhaṃ japahomas tathaiva ca ||
pitṛkṣaye hi sā proktā tasmāt tatrākṣayaṃ smṛtam |
kṛṣṇachāgas tathā loha ānantyāyaiva kalpyate ||
mahābhārate |
tilair vrīhiyavair māṣair adbhir mūlaphalena vā |
dattena māsaṃ prīyante śrāddhena pitaro nṛpa ||
dvau māsau tu bhavet tṛptir matsyaiḥ pitṛgaṇaiḥ saha |
trīn māsān āvikenāhuś caturmāsān śaśena tu ||
ājena māsān prīyante pañca vai pitaro nṛpa |
[599]        vārāheṇa tu ṣaṇmāsān sapta vai śākunena ca ||
māsān aṣṭau pārṣatena rauraveṇa navaiva tu |
gavayasya tu māṃsena tṛptir māsān daśaiva tu ||
māsān ekādaśa prītiḥ pitṝṇāṃ māhiṣeṇa tu |
gavyena datte śrāddhe tu saṃvatsaram ihocyate ||
yathā gavyaṃ tathā yuktaṃ pāyasaṃ sarpiṣā saha |
vārdhrīṇasasya māṃsena tṛptir dvādaśavārṣikī ||
ānantyāya bhaved dattaṃ khaḍgamāṃsaṃ pitṛkṣaye |
kālaśākaṇ ca lohaṃ cāpy anantaṃ chāgam ucyate ||
gāthāś cāp yatra gāyanti pitṛgītā yudhiṣṭhira |
api naḥ sa kule jāto yo no dadyāt trayodaśīm ||
āpo mūlaphalaṃ māṃsam annaṃ cāpi pitṛkṣaye |
yat kiṃcin madhusaṃmiśraṃ tad ānantyāya kalpyate ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
aprāptau khaḍgamāṃsasya tathā vārdhrīṇasasya ca |
madhunā saha dātavyaṃ pāyasaṃ pitṛtṛptaye ||
tenāpi vārṣikī tṛptiḥ pitṝṇāṃ copajāyate |
abhāve cāpi tasyāpi śiśumārasamudbhavam ||
māṃsaṃ tu tṛptaye proktaṃ vatsaraṃ māsavarjitam |
tadabhāve varāhotthaṃ daśamāsapratuṣṭidam ||
āraṇyamāhiṣotthena tṛptiḥ syān navamāsikī |
ruroś caivāṣṭamāsān vai eṇasya saptamāsikī ||
[600]        chāgasya māsaṣaṭkaṃ ca śaśakasya ca pañca vai |
caturaḥ śalyakasyoktās trayo viṣkirikasya ca ||
māsadvayaṃ ca matsyasya māṃsaṃ kapiñjalasya ca |
nānyeṣāṃ yojayen māṃsaṃ pitṛkārye kathaṃcana |
eteṣām api cābhāvāt pāyasaṃ ca narādhipa |
yojayed iti śeṣaḥ |
iti kālaviśeṣāvacchedena tṛptikaraṇāni dravyāṇi |
athānnāni |
tatra dhānyaphalamūlādīnāṃ sākṣād abhyavahāryabhakṣabhojyalehyapeyacoṣyarūpasvavikāra-prakṛtibhāvena śrāddhasaṃbandhāt dhānyādīnāṃ grāhyatvavarjyatvapratipādanaṃ tadvikāragrāhyatvavarjyatvaparyantam iti grāhyadravyasādhyasyānnasya grāhyatvaṃ varjyasādhyasya ca varjyatvam iti siddham eva | ato yad grāhyadravyavikārāṇāṃ sāmānyato grāhyatvaṃ vacanaiḥ pratipādyate tat spaṣṭatvārtham | yac ca viśeṣato vikāraviśeṣāṇām apūpapāyasādīnāṃ tadanyebhyo vikārebhyo ’pi praśastataratvapratipādanārtham iti vivekavyaṃ |
tatra grāhyāṇy ucyante |
āha pracetāḥ |
pāyasatilakṛśarabrahmasuvarcalāharitamudgakṛṣṇamāṣaśyāmākapriyaṅguyava-godhūmekṣuvikārāṃś ca dadyāt | tilataṇḍulasiddhodanaḥ 'kṛśaraḥ’ 'brahmasuvarcalāḥ' ādityabhaktāḥ | vikāraśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ prakṛtibhiḥ saṃbadhyate | vihitadravyaprakṛtikābhyavahāryāśeṣavikāropalakṣaṇārtham iti |
devalaḥ |
[601]        tato 'nnaṃ bahusaṃskāraṃ naikavyañjanabhakṣyavat |
coṣyapeyasamṛddhaṃ ca yathāśakty upakalpayet ||
‘bahusaṃskāraṃ' sūpakāraśāstropadiṣṭaiḥ samṛddhataragṛhajanapratisiddhair hiṅgumaricailākarpūrādiyuktijanyair abhijñaramanīyair atiśayaviśevair yukam | 'naikavyañjanabhakṣyavat' vyajyatenenānnasya rasa iti 'vyañjanaṃ' sūpaśākādikam | dantair avakhaṇḍya yad bhujyate tad 'bhakṣyam' | yasya oṣṭhadaśanarasanādibhir nipīḍitasya saphūtkāreṇa mukhamārutena raso niṣpīyate tat 'coṣyaṃ' yathekṣukhaṇḍādikam | 'peyaṃ' dravaikasvabhāvaṃ pānakādi | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitad bhojyaleyayoḥ | yat tv anatikāṭhinyād atiprayatnavat khaṇḍanānapekṣaṃ carvayitveva nigīryate tat 'bhojyam' | yat tu sāndradravasvabhāvatayā daśanānapekṣeṇa rasanāvyāpāreṇāśyate tat 'lehyam' | 'yathāśakti' śaktim anatikramya | 'upakalpayet' saṃpādayet |
brahmapurāṇe |
guḍaśarkaramatsya.eṇḍyo (?) deyaṃ phāṇitamurmuraṃ |
gavyaṃ payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ ca tilasaṃbhavam ||
pākād ghanībhūto golākāreṇopanibaddhekṣurasaḥ 'guḍaḥ' | ikṣurasapākaprabhava eva śvetavarṇacūrṇātmako vikāraḥ 'śarkarā' | utkṛṣṭaḥ śarkarābhedaḥ 'matsyaṇḍī' | īṣatkvathitasyekṣurasasya drava eva vikāraḥ 'phāṇitam' | guḍamaricailāmiśro godhūmasthūlacūrṇavikāraḥ 'murmuraḥ' | tailam ityādi, tilasaṃbhavam eva tailaṃ deyaṃ nānyat | ayaṃ ca śrāddhārthapradīpapravarttakasya śrāddhīyaśākapākārthasyābhyaṅgodvartanārthasya ca tailasya niyamāyopadeśo ’nusandheyaḥ |
[602]        tathā |
pāyasaṃ śālimudgāḍhyaṃ modakādīṃś ca bhaktitaḥ |
pūrikāṃ ca rasālāṃ ca gokṣīraṃ ca niyojayet ||
yāni cābhyavahāryāṇi svādusnigdhāni bho dvijāḥ |
īṣaduṣṇakaṭūny eva deyāni śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
'modakaḥ' laḍḍukaḥ | 'pūrikā' anenaiva nāmnā prasiddhaḥ apūpaviśeṣaḥ | śarkarātvagelānāgakesaramadhumaricamiśram anuddhṛtasnehabhāgaṃ vastre saṃghṛṣya gālitaṃ godadhi 'rasālā' |
tad uktam āyurvede |
sitācaturjatamadhūṣaṇāḍhyaṃ
dadhi praghṛṣṭaṃ sarasaṃ rasāleti |
tathā |
mṛṣṭasnigdhāni yāni syur īṣatkaṭvamlakāni ca |
svādūni devabhojyāni tāni śrāddhe niyojayet ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
lājān madhuyutān dadyāt saktūn śarkarayā saha |
dadyāc chrāddhe prayatnena śṛṅgāṭabisasebukān ||
kiṃcid ārdrīkṛtya bhṛṣṭāḥ śālayaḥ 'lājāḥ' | śarkarāyutāni bhṛṣṭayavapiṣṭāni 'saktavaḥ' |
vāyupurāṇe |
bhakṣyān vakṣye karambhaṃ ca īṣṭakā ghṛtapūrakāḥ |
kṛśaraṃ madhu sarpiś ca payaḥ pāyasam eva ca |
[603]        snigdham uṣṇaṃ ca yo dadyād agniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
karambhasya vihitapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpaḥ | iṣṭakākṛtiḥ khaṇḍeṣṭakākhyo bhakṣyaviśeṣaḥ 'iṣṭakā' | ghṛtākadugdhāloḍitaiḥ śāligodhūmapiṣṭasārair vihitāpūpaviśeṣāḥ 'ghṛtapūrāḥ' | 'sarpiḥ' ājyaṃ, tac ca gavyaṃ māhiṣaṃ vā | 'payaḥ' kṣīraṃ, tac ca gavyam eva | 'snigdhaṃ' snehadravyasaṃskṛtam | 'uṣṇaṃ' īṣaduṣṇam | 'agniṣṭomaḥ' jyotiṣṭomasyādyā saṃsthā |
uṣṇaṃ dadyād ity asyāpavādaḥ saurapurāṇe |
vividhaṃ pāyasaṃ dadyād bhakṣyāṇi vividhāni ca |
lehyaṃ coṣyaṃ yathākāmam uṣṇam eva phalaṃ vinā |
vividhāny api māṃsāni pitṝṇāṃ pitṛpūrvakam ||
atrāgnipakvāt phalād anyat phalaṃ vineti boddhavyam | pakvasya kaduṣṇasyaiva svādutvāt tathāvidhasyaiva dānaṃ yuktam | upalakṣaṇaṃ caitad apakvabhakṣyāṇāṃ kandamūlādīnām |
ata eva kūrmapurāṇe |
uṣṇam annaṃ dvijātibhyo dātavyaṃ śreya icchatā |
anyatra phalamūlebhyaḥ pānakebhyas tathaiva ca ||
‘pānakāni’ guḍamaricaśarkarailākarpūrādisaṃskṛtāpakvadrākṣākadalādiphaladravāḥ |
bhaviṣyottare |
uṣṇam annaṃ tu viprebhyaḥ śraddhayā vinivedayet |
anyatra phalamūlebhyaḥ pānapuṣpādikāṃs tathā ||
[604]        vāyupurāṇe |
dadhi gavyam asaṃsṛṣṭaṃ bhakṣān nānāvidhān api |
datvā na śocati śrāddhe varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca ||
‘asaṃsṛṣṭaṃ' asaṃpṛktam | mahiṣyādikṣīravikāraiḥ apavitradravyāntareṇa vā bhakṣavaṭakamaṇḍakamodakādayo lokasiddhaprakārakā eva viśiṣṭataraśrāddhācaraṇasamutpāditaparipuṣṭapāralaukikasukhotpādakasukṛtarāśitvād akṛtānyabahupuṇyo ’pi svargasukhābhāvanimittakaṃ śokaṃ na karoti |
tathā |
ghṛtena bhojayed viprān ghṛtaṃ bhūmau samutsṛjet |
śarkarākṣīrasaṃyuktāḥ pṛthukā nityam akṣayāḥ ||
syuś ca saṃvatsaraṃ prītā varkarair meṣakaiṇakaiḥ |
saktūlājāṃs tathā pūpān kulmāṣān vyañjanaiḥ saha ||
sarpiḥsnigdhāni sarvāṇi dadhnā saṃskṛtya bhojayet |
śrāddheṣv etāni yo dadyāt pitaraḥ prīṇayanti tam ||
ghṛtaṃ bhūmau samutsṛjed ity asya ghṛtabāhulye tātparyam | tathā ghṛtaṃ pariveṣyaṃ yathā pātram āpūrya bhūmāv apy upasarpati | aṅgārabhṛṣṭakaṇiśagalitā dhānyakaṇāḥ ‘pṛthukāḥ’ | 'varkaraiḥ' taruṇaiḥ | ‘meṣaiḥ' urabhraiḥ | 'pūpāḥ' pūpalikādibahubhedāḥ | ardhakhinnāni dhānyāni 'kulmāṣāḥ' |
kūrmapurāṇe |
atha piṇḍāvaśiṣṭānnaṃ vidhivad bhojayed dvijān |
māṃsāny apūpān vividhān dadyāt kṛsarapāyasam ||
pūpaśākaphalān īkṣūn payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
[605]        annaṃ caiva yathākāmaṃ vividhaṃ bhojyapeyakam ||
yad yad iṣṭaṃ dvijendrāṇāṃ tat tat sarvaṃ nivedayet |
dhānās tilāṃś ca vividhān śarkarā vividhās tathā ||
athety agnaukaraṇakālīnapiṇḍapradānānantaram |
kālikāpurāṇe |
pāyasaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ khaṇḍena ca vimiśritaṃ |
ādau piṇḍān samuddiśya dvijebhyas tadanantaram ||
dadyād iti śeṣaḥ |
devalena tu pradeyasyānnasya tilādisāhityam uktam |
atha piṇḍāvaśiṣṭānnaṃ bhojayet prayato dvijān |
bhojanaiḥ satilaiḥ snehair bhakṣyapūpavimiśritaiḥ ||
mitrāyaṇīyasūtre tu pariveṣaṇīyasya madhvādisāhityam apy uktam | tilavan madhumac cānnaṃ sāmiṣaṃ ca dadyād iti |
pradeyasyānnasya prabhūtatvaṃ tu pippalādasūtre pratipāditam |
māṃsaiḥ śākair māṣair madhunā ghṛtena dadhnā payasā ca prabhūtam annam iṣṭaṃ dadyāt |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tatas tilair māṣaiḥ śākaiḥ pūpaiḥ kṛṣarapāyasāpūpair lājair bhakṣyaiḥ pānair madhunā ghṛtena dadhnā payasā ca prabhūtam iṣṭham annaṃ dadyāt |
tata ity agnaukaraṇaśeṣapratipattyuttarakālam | yac ca jīvataḥ pitrāder bhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ priyam āsīt tat pitṛtāṃ gatāya tasmai śrāddhe deyam |
tad āha kārṣṇājiniḥ |
[606]        yad iṣṭaṃ jīvataś cāsīt tad dadyāt tasya yatnataḥ |
sutṛptau dustaraṃ mārgaṃ tato yāti na saṃśayaḥ ||
pāyasādyannaviśeṣadāne phalaviśeṣāḥ | tatra brahmavaivarte |
sadānnaṃ pāyamaṃ sarpir madhu mūlaphalāni ca |
bhakṣāṃś ca vividhān datvā paratreha ca modate ||
navaśasyāni yo dadyāc chrāddhaṃ satkṛtya yatnataḥ |
sarvān bhogān avāpnoti pūjyate ca divaṃ gataḥ ||
bhakṣyabhojyāni cānyāni pralehyāny aparāṇi ca |
sarvaśreṣṭhāni yo dadyāt sarvaśreṣṭho bhaven naraḥ |
vaiśvadevyaṃ ca saumyaṃ ca khaḍgamāṃsaṃ varaṃ haviḥ ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
tilān ikṣūṃs tathā bhojyaṃ śrāddhe satkṛtya dāpayet |
mitrāṇi labhate loke strīṣu saubhāgyam eva ca ||
bhagavatīpurāṇe |
sarveṣām eva cānnānāṃ paramānnaṃ ca pāyasam |
sarvānnadaḥ sa tu prokto yena dattaṃ tu pāyasam |
pāyasena pitṝn prīṇan prīṇāti sudhayā naraḥ ||
kapilāpāyasaṃ gavyaṃ payaḥ sarpis tathāmiṣam |
madhu vety akṣayāṃ tṛptiṃ pitṝṇāṃ sādhayanti hi ||
matsyapurāṇe |
yat kiṃcin madhunā miśraṃ gokṣīraghṛtapāyasaiḥ |
[607]        dattam akṣayyam ity āhuḥ pitara pūrvadevatāḥ ||
yat kiṃcid bhakṣyabhojyaṃ madhumiśritaṃ gokṣīraghṛtapāyasaiḥ sahitam akṣayyam ity arthaḥ |
payasā prāpnuyāc caiva gokṣīraṃ gosave phalam |
sarpiṣā tu śubhaṃ cakṣuḥ ṣoḍaśāhaphalaṃ labhet |
madhunā tu trirātrasya phalaṃ ca samavāpnuyāt ||
annaṃ naraḥ prāpnuyāc chraddadhānaḥ
sarvaiḥ kāmair bhojayed yas tu viprān |
sarvārthadaḥ sarvaviprābhivandyaḥ
phalaṃ bhuṅkte sarvamedhasya nityam ||
iti grāhyāṇy annāni |
atha varjyāny annāni |
tatrāha śāṭyāyaniḥ |
varjyam annaṃ tridhā proktam ādyam āśrayagarhitam |
jātito garhitaṃ yac ca yac ca bhāvādidūṣitam ||
abhojyānnaṃ vijānīyād annam āśrayagarhitam |
laśunādikam annaṃ yat jñeyaṃ jātivigarhitam |
duṣṭaṃ bhāvakriyāvasthāsaṃsargais tu tṛtīyakam ||
abhojyānnam iti tac ca paribhāṣāprakaraṇe vakṣyate | tatra kāraṇagatajātiduṣṭatayā varjyani varjyadhānyābhidhānenaivābhihitāni bhāvakriyāsaṃsargāvasthāduṣṭatayā varjyāni tūcyante |
viśvāmitraḥ |
jātikriyāduṣṭam asatsaṃsargaduṣṭaṃ sakṛlleṣaṃ na bhakṣayet |
aha gobhilaḥ |
[608]        atiśuktogralavaṇaṃ virasaṃ bhāvadūṣitam |
rājasaṃ tāmasaṃ caiva havyakavyeṣu varjayet ||
'atiśuktaṃ' yat svayam anamlaṃ dravyāntareṇa saṃpṛṣṭaṃ vā kālavaśād utpannātikutsitarasam | 'ugralavaṇaṃ' lavaṇādhikyena mukhodvegakaram | 'virasaṃ' svabhāvato ’timandarasaṃ, kālād apagatasvābhāvikaramaṃ vā | 'bhāvadūṣitaṃ' kārpaṇyakutsākutsitasārūpyāropādibhir antaḥkaraṇavikārair dūṣitam | 'rājasaṃ' rajoguṇasya kāryaṃ kāraṇaṃ vā | evaṃ tāmasam |
tac cobhayam uktaṃ bhagavadgītāsu |
kaṭvamlalavaṇātyuṣṇatīkṣṇarūkṣavidāhinaḥ |
āhārā rājasasyeṣṭā duḥkhaśokāmayapradāḥ ||
yātayāmaṃ gatarasaṃ pūtiparyuṣitaṃ ca yat
ucchiṣṭam api cāmedhyaṃ bhojanaṃ tāmasapriyam ||
ataḥ sāttvikam evānnaṃ praśastam | tad api tatraiva darśitaṃ |
āyuḥsattvabalārogyasukhaprītivivardhanāḥ |
rasāḥ snigdhāḥ sthirā hṛdyā āhārāḥ sāttvikapriyā iti ||
'havyakavyeṣu' havyakavyasādhyeṣu karmasu |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
āsanārūḍham annādyaṃ pādopahatam eva ca |
amedhyād āgataiḥ spṛṣṭaṃ śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ ca yat ||
dviḥkhinnaṃ paridagdhaṃ ca tathaivāgrāvalehitam |
śarkarākīṭapāṣāṇaiḥ keśair yac cāpy upadrutam ||
piṇyākaṃ mathitaṃ caiva tathātilavaṇaṃ ca yat |
[609]        siddhāḥ kṛtāś ca ye bhakṣyāḥ pratyakṣalavaṇīkṛtāḥ ||
vāgbhāvaduṣṭāś ca tathā duṣṭaiś copahatā api |
vāsasā copadhūtāni varjyāni śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
‘āsanārūḍhaṃ’ sākṣād āsanasyoparisthāpitam, bhūvyatirikrādhikaraṇasthāpitapātrasthitaṃ vā | 'annādyaṃ' adanārham annam | 'pādopahataṃ' sākṣāt bhājanadvārā vā pādasparśadūṣitam | 'amedhyād āgataiḥ' apavitrasparśadūṣitaiś cetanācetanaiḥ spṛṣṭam | 'śuktam' pūrvam uktam | 'paryuṣitaṃ' rātryantaritam | 'dviḥkhinnaṃ' vāradvayaṃ pakvam | yad annam avaśrayaṇāntaṃ pacikriyayā siddhaṃ san mārdavāya punar udakaṃ ninīyāvastrāvaraṇāntam eva pacyate tat dviḥkhinnam | na tu yat siddham eva punar auṣṇyanirvāhārtham agnisaṃnidhau sthāpyate, “atyuṣṇaṃ sarvam annaṃ syāt' iti vacanāt | ata eva punar vilāpyamānam ājyam api na dviḥkhinaṃ | yasya tu sūraṇāder adhiśrayaṇadvayenaiva siddhiḥ prasiddhā tasya tāvad avayava eka evāsau pāka iti tatrāpi na dviḥpakvatā | 'paridagdhaṃ' dravāṃśasaṃśoṣaṇāt bhāṇḍāgninā paripluṣṭam | 'agrāvalehitaṃ’ agre uparibhāge mājīrādibhir avalīḍham, pūrvam anyenāsvāditaikadeśaṃ vā | 'śarkarāḥ' śvetāśmajātiviśeṣaḥ | 'kīṭāḥ' pipīlikādayaḥ | 'pāṣāṇaḥ'
dṛṣat | ‘keśaḥ' kacaḥ | etair dūṣitaṃ antaḥpraviśyābhakṣyatāṃ nītam | 'mathitam' āloḍitaṃ dadhi | 'atilavaṇam' atimātralavaṇayuktam | 'siddhāḥ kṛtāś ca ye bhakṣyāḥ' yeṣu tailapakvagodhūmacūrṇamayavarttiśoṣitaphalaphāṇiparpaṭakādirūpeṣu bhakṣyeṣu cirakālasthiteṣv api paryuṣita[610]vyavahāro nāsti te bhakṣyāḥ siddhāḥ kṛtā ity ucyante | 'pratyakṣalavaṇīkṛtāḥ' bhoktari paśyati lavaṇena saṃyojitāḥ | 'vāgduṣṭāḥ' nindādirūpayā vācā dūṣitāḥ | 'duṣṭaiś copahatāḥ' śvaśūkarakākakukkuṭādibhir nagnapatitapāṣaṇḍādibhiś ca darśanasparśanādigocarīkṛtāś ca ye bhakṣyāḥ | 'vāsasā copadhūtāni' yeṣām upari makṣikāpagamārtham auṣṇyanivṛttaye vā prasaṅgāntareṇa vā vastram avadhūyate tāny annāni tathocyante |
manuḥ |
keśakīṭāvapannaṃ ca padā spṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ |
bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitaṃ caiva saṃspṛṣṭaṃ cāpy udakyayā ||
patatriṇāvalīḍhaṃ ca śunā saṃspṛṣṭam eva ca |
gavā cānnam upaghrātaṃ ghuṣṭānnaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||
śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ caiva śūdrasyocchiṣṭam eva ca |
vṛthākṛśarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpam eva ca |
anupākṛtamāṃsaṃ ca devānnāni havīṃṣi ca ||
keśakīṭair avapannaṃ saṃspṛṣṭaṃ ‘keśakīṭāvapannam' | keśagrahaṇaṃ nakharomnāṃ dūṣikādimalānāṃ ca pradarśanārtham | kīṭagrahaṇaṃ kṣudrajantūnāṃ kṛmipataṅgānām | kīṭeṣu ca makṣikādayo mṛtā eva dūṣayanti na jīvantaḥ, anye tu jīvanto ’pi | ‘kāmataḥ’ buddhipūrvaṃ, kāmakāreṇa yat pādaspṛṣṭam, ataḥ pramādaspṛṣṭe na doṣaḥ | 'bhrūṇahā ' brahmahā, tenāvekṣitaṃ nipuṇam avalokitam | pradarśanārthaṃ caitad anyeṣāṃ mahāpātakinām | spṛṣṭasya tu pratiṣedhaḥ snānavidhānād eva siddhaḥ | 'udakyā' rajasvalā, tayā spṛṣṭasyaiva pratiṣedho nāvekṣitasya | udakyāspṛṣṭasyāpi snānavidhānād eva pratiṣedhasiddhau [611] prakṣālitam api tad annaṃ bhojyam ity etadarthaṃ spṛṣṭagrahaṇam | 'patatrī' pakṣī sa ca samācārāt kravyādo gṛdhravāyasādiḥ, na tu śukahaṃsādiḥ | 'ghuṣṭānnaṃ' bhujyatām atra ye kecid arthina iti yad udghuṣya dīyate | yad vānyasmai pratiśrutyānyasmai dīyate | pratijñāne hy ayaṃ dhātuḥ padyate | 'śūdrasya' śūdrasvāmikaṃ yad annam | ‘ucchiṣṭaṃ' bhuktāvaśiṣṭam | tilaiḥ saha siddhaudanaḥ ‘kṛśaraḥ' | 'saṃyāvaḥ' tilasarpirguḍādisahitagodhūmaśakalanirmito bhakṣyaviśeṣaḥ sūdeṣu prasiddhaḥ | payasi siddhaudanaḥ 'pāyasaḥ' | 'apūpaḥ' asnehapakvo godhūmavikāraḥ | vṛthāśabdaḥ sarvatrānuṣajyate | yad ātmārthaṃ kriyate devān pitṝn atithīn vānuddiśya tad vṛthā | 'anupākṛtam' aprokṣitaṃ māṃsam | 'devānnāni' devasvībhūtāny annāni | 'havīṃṣi' devatārtham upakalpitāni |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsaṃ keśakīṭasamanvitam |
śuktaṃ paryuṣitocchiṣṭaṃ śvaspṛṣṭaṃ patitekṣitam ||
udakyāspṛṣṭasaṃghuṣṭaparyāyānnaṃ ca varjayet |
goghātaṃ śakunocchiṣṭaṃ padā spṛṣṭaṃ ca kāmataḥ ||
tathā |
vṛthākṛsarasaṃyāvapāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīḥ |
'anarcitaṃ' arcārhāya yad avajñayā dīyate | 'vṛthāmāṃsaṃ' devādyarcanaśiṣṭaṃ na bhavati, ātmārtham eva yat sādhitaṃ, tac ca prāṇātyayādivyatirekeṇa varjayet | 'śvaspṛṣṭaṃ' śunā spṛṣṭam | anyasaṃbandhavyapadeśena yad dīyate [612] tat ‘paryāyānnam’| paryācāntam iti pāṭhe parigatam ācāntaṃ gaṇḍūṣagrahaṇaṃ yasmin bhujyamāne tat 'paryācāntam' | pārśvācāntam iti
pāṭhe ekasyāṃ paṅktau pārśvasthe ācānte na bhoktavyaṃ, bhasmodakādivicchedo (?) na kṛtaś cet | ‘śakunaḥ' kravyādapakṣī | snehapakvo godhūmavikāraḥ 'śaṣkulī' |
yamaḥ |
udakyayā ca saṃspṛṣṭaṃ gavāghrātaṃ ca yad bhavet |
kākakukkuṭasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śuktaṃ ca kṛmisaṃyutam |
abhojyaṃ ca dvijātīnāṃ dharmarājavaco yathā ||
yavāgūṃ kṛśaraṃ caiva pāyasāpūpaśaṣkulīḥ |
ṛjīṣapakvamāṃsaṃ ca matsyān apy anupākṛtān ||
varjayet sarvaśuktāni devānnāni havīṃṣi ca |
snehena tu samāyuktaṃ naiva sarvaṃ prayojayet ||
tathā |
avadhūtam avajñātaṃ saroṣaṃ vismayānvitam |
guror api na bhoktavyam annaṃ saṃskāravarjitam ||
avaghuṣṭaṃ yad ucchiṣṭaṃ vāgduṣṭam api yad bhavet |
aśraddhayā hutaṃ dattam abhojyaṃ ca dvijātibhiḥ ||
tathā |
bhinnabhāṇḍe na bhuñjīta na rātrau dadhisaktukān |
divā dadhitthadhānāṣu rātrau ca dadhisaktuṣu |
śleṣmātake tathā lakṣmīr nityam eva kṛtālayā ||
[613]        yavāgūprabhṛtīny ātmoddeśena sādhitāny abhakṣyāṇi | anavaśrāvitoviraladrava odanaḥ 'yavāgūḥ' | 'ṛjīṣaṃ' piṣṭapacanabhāṇḍam | anagnika uṣmā ‘ṛjīṣaṃ’ tena dhānyarāśyādigatoṣmapakvam iti smṛticandrikāyām | 'avadhūtaṃ' vāsasā | 'dadhitthaṃ' kapittham | 'dhānāḥ' bhṛṣṭayavāḥ |
devalaḥ |
abhojyaṃ prāhur āhāraṃ śuktaṃ paryuṣitaṃ ca yat |
anyatra madhusaktubhyāṃ bhakṣyebhyaḥ sarpiṣo guḍāt ||
nāśnīyāt payasā naktaṃ bhuktaṃ cen na svapen niśi |
na kṣīram utsṛjet prāptaṃ pavitraṃ hi payaḥ smṛtam ||
gautamaḥ |
uddhṛtasnehavilayanapiṇyākamathitaprabhṛtīni cānnavīryāṇi nāśnīyāt |
'vilayanaṃ' bhaktamaṇḍaḥ |
āpastambaḥ |
vilayanaṃ mathitaṃ piṇyākaṃ madhumāṃsaṃ varjayet | kṛṣṇadhānyaṃ ca śūdrānnaṃ ye cānye nāsya saṃmatāḥ | ahaviṣyam anṛtaṃ krodhaṃ yena ca krodhaḥ smṛtim icchan yaśo medhāṃ svargaṃ buddhiṃ dvādaśaitāni varjayet |
tathā |
aprayatopahatam annam aprayatam abhojyam | aprayatena śūdreṇopahatam abhojyam | yasmiṃś cānne keśaḥ syād anyad vāmedhyam amedhyair avaspṛṣṭaṃ kīṭo vāmedhyaḥ | sadā mūlakaliṅgaṃ vā padā copahataṃ śivayā śunā vāpy apātreṇa vā dṛṣṭaṃ śucā vopahataṃ bhuñjānaṃ śvā śūdro vā upaspṛśed anarhadbhir vā samānapaṅktau [614] bhuñjāneṣu vā yatrānutthāyocchiṣṭhaṃ prayacched ācāmed vā kutsayitvā vā yatrānnaṃ dadyuḥ gobhir avaghrātam anyair vāmedhyaiḥ |
tathā |
nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyāt tathā rasānāṃ māṃsaṃ madhulavaṇāni parihāpya tailasarpiṣī tūpayojayet | udake badhāya kṛtānnaṃ paryuṣitam apy adyāt tac cānādyaṃ śuktaṃ cety aparāṇi ca pṛthukataṇḍulakarambhatarujasaktuśākamāṃsapiṣṭakṣīravṛkṣauṣadhi-vanaspatimūlaphalavarjaṃ śuktaṃ cāpariyogam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
tatrāpeyāny abhakṣyāṇi varjayed amedhyapatitacaṇḍālapukkasarajasvalā-kuṇapakuṣṭhisaṃspṛṣṭāni |
tathā |
nāpaṇīyam annaṃ śnīyān na dviḥpakvaṃ na śuktaṃ na paryuṣitam anyatra rāgacukrakhāḍavadadhiguḍagodhūmayavapiṣṭavikārebhyaḥ |
aśnīyād ityanuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
na rajasvalādattaṃ na puṃścalyā na kruddhayā na malavadvāsasā nāparayā dvārāpannaṃ na dviḥpakvaṃ na śuktaṃ na paryuṣitam anyatra guḍapiṣṭasaktusnehagorasatailādiṣu pakvānnatailadadhyannapānaṃ nāvakṣutānnaṃ na jugupsitam |
gautamaḥ |
nityam abhojyaṃ keśakīṭāvapanaṃ rajasvalākṛṣṇaśakunipadopahataṃ bhrūṇaghnāvekṣitaṃ gavāghrātaṃ bhāvaduṣṭaṃ śuktaṃ kevalam adadhi punaḥsiddhaṃ paryuṣitāny aśākabhakṣyasnehamāṃsamadhūni |
abhojyam ity anuvṛttau vaśiṣṭhaḥ | [615]
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhāvaduṣṭaṃ sakṛllekhaṃ punaḥsiddham āmam ṛjīṣapakvaṃ ca kāmaṃ tu dadhnā ghṛtena vābhighāritam upabhuñjīta |
tathā |
ucchiṣṭam aguror na bhojyaṃ samucchiṣṭam ucchiṣṭopahataṃ ca yad vasanakeśakīṭopahataṃ ca |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
nādyāc chāstraniṣiddhaṃ tu bhakṣyabhojyādikaṃ dvijaḥ |
māṃsaṃ vigarhitaṃ caiva taṃ bahuvidhaṃ tathā |
atyamlaṃ śukam ākhyātaṃ ninditaṃ brahmavādibhiḥ ||
yamaḥ |
ghṛtaṃ vā yadi vā tailaṃ vipro nādyān nakhacyutam |
yamas tad āśuci prāha tulyaṃ gomāṃsabhakṣaṇaiḥ ||
hastadattāś ca ye snehā vyañjanaṃ lavaṇāni ca |
dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhanti bhuktvā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam ||
ekena pāṇinā dattaṃ śudradattaṃ ca yad bhavet |
ghṛtaṃ tailaṃ ca lavaṇaṃ pāṇīyaṃ pāyasaṃ tathā ||
varjyam iti śeṣaḥ | darvyādy anantardhāya sākṣād dhastenaiva yad dattaṃ tad dhastadattam | tathā hastena dattaṃ śaudraṃ yad api kim ānīyatāṃ tad api śītalaṃ śaudraṃ śūdraiḥ pariviṣṭaṃ bhavati | atha vā śaudraṃ śūdrocitaṃ śrāddhāyogyam iti yāvat | yad apy odanādyannaṃ kiṃ tat tad artham ānīyatām iti pṛṣṭvā ānītaṃ tad api śaudram |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
[616]        surālaśunasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ pīyūṣādisamanvitam |
saṃsargāt duṣyate tad dhi śūdrocchiṣṭavad ācaret ||
iti varjyānnāni |
athaiteṣu varjyatvenokteṣv anneṣu keṣāṃcit pratiprasavaḥ kriyate |
tatrāha manuḥ |
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ ca śukteṣu sarvaṃ ca dadhisaṃbhavam |
yāni caivābhiṣūyante puṣpamūlaphalaiḥ śubhaiḥ ||
tathā |
yat kiṃcit snehasaṃyuktaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyam agarhitam |
tat paryuṣitam apy ādyaṃ haviḥśeṣaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||
cirasthitam api tv ādyam asnehāktaṃ dvijātibhiḥ |
yavagodhūmajaṃ sarvaṃ payasaś caiva vikriyāḥ ||
'dadhisaṃbhavaṃ' udaśvinmadhukilāṭakūrcakādi | śuktāny ucyante | yāni madhurarasāny api kālātyayena dravyāntarasaṃsargeṇa vātyamlībhāvam āpadyante, yathāmradrākṣādīni, yāni tu svabhāvato 'mlāni dāḍimāmalakajambīrādīni tāni naiva śuktāni yāni cāprāptapākāny āmrādīni | na hy ayam amlaparyāyaḥ śuktaśabdaḥ | teṣu ca kevalāni kālataḥ śuktāni pratiṣidhya dravyāntarair mūlādibhiḥ miśritāny anujñāyante | tathā ca gautamenoktam kevalam adadhīti |
udakena saṃsṛjya parivāsanaṃ 'abhiṣavaḥ', puṣpādibhiḥ saha yāny abhiṣūyante | 'bhakṣyaṃ' dhānāśaṣkulyāni | kharaviśadabhojyam odanādi | agarhitam anapetagandhavarṇarasaṃ tac cet snehasaṃyuktaṃ sat paryuṣitaṃ tadā bhakṣaṇīyam | [617] abhighāraṇasnehasaṃyuktasyāpi haviḥśeṣasya pṛthaggrahaṇam ādarārtham | 'cirasthitaṃ’ dvitrirātryantaritam | 'yavagodhūmajaṃ' apūpasaktvādi, asnehāktam api bhojyam | 'payasaḥ’, ‘vikriyāḥ' vikārāḥ dadhimathitādayaḥ |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
annaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ snehāktaṃ cirasaṃsthitam |
asnehā api godhūmayavagorasavikriyāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
dadhi bhakṣyaṃ śuktam api yac caiva dadhisaṃbhavam |
ṛcīṣapakvaṃ bhakṣyaṃ syāt sasarpiṣkam iti śrutiḥ ||
tathā |
yavagodhūmajaṃ sarvaṃ vikārāḥ payasaś ca ye |
rāgakhāḍavacukrādyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhavet ||
apūpāś ca karambhāś ca dhānāvaṭakasaktavaḥ |
śākamāṃsāni pūpaṃ ca sūpaṃ kṛśaram eva ca ||
yavāgūṃ pāyasaṃ caiva yac cānyat snehasaṃyutam |
sarvaṃ paryuṣitaṃ bhojyaṃ śuktaṃ ca parivarjayet ||
vaśiṣṭthaś |
apūpadhānākarambhasaktuvaṭakatailapāyasaśākāni vinā śuktāni varjayet | anyāṃś ca kṣīrayavapiṣṭavikārān |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kāmaṃ dadhnā ghṛtenābhighāritaṃ paryuṣitānnaṃ bhuñjīta ye hy atra śuktaparyuṣitādīnāṃ pratiprasavās teṣāṃ puruṣārthaikarūpatvān na śrāddhānaviṣayatvam | [618] na uṣṇaṃ na svinnaṃ dadyāt na dviḥpakvaṃ dadyāt | prātaḥ pārkaṃ samārabhed iti śraddhadina eva pākārambhavidhānāc chuddhaparyuṣitādiprasaktyabhāvāc ca |
yaś ca nāpaṇīyam annam aśnīyād ity asya yamenāpavāda uktaḥ |
apūpāḥ saktavo dhānās takraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ madhu |
etat puṇyeṣu bhoktavyaṃ bhāṇḍalepo na ced bhavet || iti |
so ’yam apūpasaktudhānāvyatirikteṣu saṃbhavatprasaṅgeṣu takrādiṣv avatiṣṭhate |
iti varjyānnapratiprasavāḥ |
atha grāhyāṇy udakāni |
āha manuḥ |
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vitṛṣṇā yatra gaur bhavet |
avyāptāś ced amedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ |
bhūmistham udakaṃ śuddhaṃ vitṛṣṇā yatra gaur bhavet |
avyāptaṃ ced amedhyena tadvad eva śilāgatam ||
bhūmiṣṭham udakaṃ śuddhec chuci toyaṃ śilāgatam |
gandhavarṇarasair duṣṭair varjitaṃ yadi tad bhavet ||
uśanāḥ |
āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatā vitṛṣṇā yatra gaur bhavet |
avyāptāś ced amedhyena gandhavarṇarasānvitāḥ ||
devalaḥ |
avigandhā rasopetā nirmalāḥ pṛthivīgatāḥ |
[619]        akṣīṇāś caiva gopānād āpaḥ śuddhikarāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
uddhṛtā vā praśasyante śuddhaiḥ pātrair yathāvidhi |
ekarātroṣitās tās tu tyajed āpaḥ samuddhṛtāḥ ||
akṣudrāṇām apāṃ nāsti prabhūtānāṃ ca dūṣaṇam |
stokānām uddhṛtānāṃ ca kaśmalair dūṣaṇaṃ bhavet ||
tatra kṣudrataḍāgāni nadyo vātha sarāṃsi ca |
kaśmalāśuciyuktāni tīrthataḥ parivarjayet ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
śuci gotṛptikṛt toyaṃ prakṛtisthaṃ mahīgatam |
aduṣṭān abhidhāya vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
kṣitisthāś caiva yā āpo gavāṃ tṛptikarāś ca yāḥ |
parisaṅkhyāya tāḥ sarvā śucīn āha prajāpatiḥ ||
śucir adbhir iti cānuvṛttau vasiṣṭaḥ |
pradarād api yā gos tarpaṇāya syur na varṇarasaduṣṭās tābhir yaś ca na syur
aśubhāgamāḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
āpo rūparasagandhavatyaḥ pariśuddhā jīvacarmakaraṇḍair anabhyuddhṛtāḥ bhūmigatā medhyā yatra gaur vitṛṣā bhavet |
paiṭhīnasiḥ | [620]
gandhavarṇaśuddhā āpo bhūmigatāḥ vitṛṣṇā yatra gaur bhavet | viparyastaṃ mahodakam api varjyam |
yamaḥ |
prapām araṇye ca ghaṭaṃ ca kūpe
droṇyāṃ jalaṃ kośagatās tathāpaḥ |
kṛte ’pi śūdrāt tad apeyam āhur
āpagato bhūmigataṃ pibet tu ||
hārītaḥ |
bhūmiṣṭhāḥ puṇyā āpo ’śubhavarjam athāpi brāhmaṇaṃ devānāṃ vāg eṣā matsyāpaḥ prāviśaṃs te devā adbhyo vācam aicchaṃs tān āpo ’bruvan yad asmākaṃ prajābhyo yad anyebhyo ’śubhaṃ tat pāvayadhvaṃ tato vācaṃ dāsyāma iti tatheti devāḥ pratyāhus tā agniṃ prāviśaṃs tāḥ agnipūtā abhavaṃs tasmād etā yatra tiṣṭhanti tad dahanti tāḥ vāyuṃ prāviśaṃs tā vāyupūtā abhavaṃs tasmād etā yato yanti tato dārayanti tāḥ somaṃ prāviśaṃs tāḥ somapūtā abhavaṃs tasmād etā snigdhāḥ śaityarasavatyaḥ kalpyante | asāv ādityo brahmā yajñaḥ sarvā devatās tā anvahaṃ devagavādibhīraśmibhir apāyayaṃs tasmād etā ahani śuddhāḥ pūtāḥ śuddhau kalpyante | tasmād yad yan mīmāṃsyaṃ syāt tat tad adbhiḥ saṃspṛśec chuddhir bhavati | rātrāv etāś cāpo varuṇaṃ prāviśaṃs tasmān na rātrau gṛhṇīyād dhāmno dhāmna ity agnim upariṣṭād dhārayan gṛhṇīyāt |
kātyāyanaḥ |
apo niśi na gṛhīyād gṛhṇann api kadācana |
vidhṛtyāgnim uparyāsāṃ dhāmno dhāmna itīrayan ||
astam ita ādityam udakaṃ gṛhīyād āpadi rātrau yadi gṛhīyād agnim upari dhārayan dhāmno dhāmno rājann iti yajur japan gṛhṇīyāt |
[621]        baudhāyanaḥ |
astamita āditya udakaṃ gṛhṇīyān na gṛhṇīyād iti mīmāṃsan tena gṛhṇīyād ity eke āhur brahmavādino gṛhṇīyād ity etad apare yāvad udakaṃ gṛhṇiyāt tāvat prāṇam āyacched agnir ha vā udakaṃ gṛhṇāti |
uśanāḥ |
nadyaḥ kūpataḍāgāni sarāṃsi saritas tathā |
asaṃvṛtāny adoṣāṇi manuḥ svāyambhuvo ’bravīt ||
parāśaraḥ |
anyair api kṛtān doṣān kūpasetupatheṣu ca |
teṣu vegeṣu yat toyaṃ samyak kṣetrapatheṣu ca |
pratigrāhyāś ca peyāś ca na duṣṭān manur abravīt ||
iti grāhyāṇy udakāni |
atha varjyāṇy udakāni |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
durgandhi phenilaṃ varjyaṃ tathā vai palvalodakam |
na labhet yatra gaus tṛptiṃ naktaṃ yac caiva gṛhyate ||
yan na sarvāya cotsṛṣṭaṃ yac cābhojyaṃ nipānajam |
tad varjyaṃ salilaṃ tāta sadaiva pitṛkarmaṇi ||
'yat' kūpādikaṃ, tatkartṛbhiḥ sarvaprāṇyupajīvanārthaṃ 'notsṛṣṭaṃ" na parityaktaṃ, tat salilaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi vacyam | kūpasamuddhṛtaṃ paśvādipeyodakadhāraṇārtho jalāśayaḥ ‘nipānaṃ' 'tajjaṃ' tatra sthitam api, salilaṃ varjyanīyam |
yogayājñavalkyaḥ |
[622]        agrāhyas tv agrimā āpo nadyāḥ prathamavegagāḥ |
prakṣobhitāś ca kenāpi yāś ca tīrthāt tathoddhṛtāḥ ||
'agrimāḥ' prathamavṛṣṭisaṃbhūtāḥ, tā nadyāḥ kaluṣataraikadvitryādidinasthāyivegagatā eva na grāhyāḥ | nivartamāne tu prathamavege prasādavatyo grāhyāḥ | dvitīyādivege ca vegānivṛttāv api grāhyāḥ | nadīvyatiriktvāśayasaṃbhṛtās tv agrimāḥ daśarātram agrāhyāḥ |
tathā ca vacanam |
ajā gāvo mahiṣyaś ca brāhmaṇyaś ca prasūtikāḥ |
daśarātreṇa śudhyanti bhūmīṣu ca navodakam ||
yāś ca kenacit saṃkṣobhya kaluṣīkṛtāḥ yāś ca tīrthāj jalāvataraṇamārgād uddhṛtās tā api na grāhyāḥ |
vyāsaḥ |
nadyā yac ca paribhraṣṭaṃ nadyā yac ca vivarjitam |
gatapratyāgataṃ yac ca tat toyaṃ parivarjayet ||
grīṣmādyūṣmasaṃśuṣyajjalān nadīpravāhāt pṛthagbhūya palvale sthirībhūtaṃ ‘nadyāḥ paribhraṣṭam’| yac ca svalpanirjhararūpeṇa pṛthakbhūya punar mahāpravāheṇa saha saṃsargaṃ na labhate tat ‘nadyā vivarjitam' | sakṛd asakad vā svato vā kāraṇāntareṇa vā yat pratilomaṃ syandate tat 'gatapratyāgatam', tat parivarjayet |
marīciḥ |
nabhonabhasyayor madhye sarvā nadyo rajasvalāḥ |
tāsu snānaṃ na kurvīta devarṣipitṛtarpaṇam ||
[623]        tarpaṇaśabdaḥ śrāddhādyupalakṣaṇārtham | nabhāś ca nabhasyaśvca nabhonabhasyau śrāvaṇabhādrapadau tayor madhye tayor antaraṃ tāvat kālam iti yāvat | rajasvalāśabdo gauṇyā vṛtyā pavitratvaviparyayaṃ vadan tajjalasya karmārhatāṃ vārayati |
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
ādityaduhitā gaṅgā plakṣajātā sarasvatī |
rajasā nābhibhūyante ye cānye nadasaṃjñakāḥ ||
'ādityaduhitā' yamunā | 'gaṅgā' bhāgīrathī | 'plakṣajātā sarasvatī' kurukṣetre sthitā satī |
smṛtyantare |
kālindī narmadā gaṅgā plakṣajātā sarasvatī |
etās tu puṇyāḥ saritaḥ saṃkṣobhaparivarjanāt |
vaimalyaguṇasaṃyogād rajo nābhibhavaty uta ||
gaṅgā dharmadravaḥ puṇyā yamunā ca sarasvatī |
antargatarajoyogāt sarvaheṣv api cāmalāḥ ||
nigamaḥ |
pratisroto rajoyogo rathyājalaniveśanam |
gaṅgāyāṃ na praduṣyanti sa hi dharmadravaḥ svayam ||
iti varjyāṇy udakāni |
athābhyukṣaṇāharaṇam |
tatra śāṭyāyaniḥ |
tataḥ sūryam upasthāya samyag ācamya ca svayam |
[624]        abhyukṣaṇaṃ samādāya saṃjitātmā gṛhaṃ vrajet ||
ācamyety atra dvir iti śeṣaḥ |
tathā ca vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
tapaḥsiddhe dvir ācamyābhyukṣaṇārtham athāharet |
na vinābhyukṣaṇaṃ jātu vidhijñaḥ kiṃcid ācaret ||
āharaṇetikartavyatām āha śāṭyāyaniḥ |
sopānatkaḥ sadarbhaḥ sadaśottarīyaḥ samāhartā |
‘sopānatkaḥ’ upānadyugalārūḍhaḥ | 'sadarbhaḥ' darbhapavitrayuktapāṇiḥ | ‘sadaśottarīyaḥ’ daśāyuktottarīyavastrāvṛtaḥ, 'samāhartā' pātrastham abhyukṣaṇārthaṃ jalam āhared ity arthaḥ |
pracetāḥ |
anuguptā apo gṛhya pracārārthaṃ gṛhaṃ vrajet |
savane prokṣaṇaṃ kuryād gṛhītena sadā śuciḥ ||
'anuguptāḥ' anyenādṛṣṭāḥ | 'apaḥ' jalam | 'gṛhya' gṛhītvā | 'pracārārthaṃ' gṛddanirvarttyakarmānuṣṭhānārtham | 'savane' prātarādikāle | gṛhaṃ gacchet | gatvā ca tena jalena karmārthadravyadeśānāṃ śucir bhūtvā prokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt |
uśanāḥ |
sravantyādiṣv athācamya sopānatko hy asaṃspṛśan |
āgataḥ sodapātras tu yatnena śucir eva saḥ ||
tenodakena dravyāṇi prokṣyācamya punar gṛhe |
tataḥ karmāṇi kurvīta nityaṃ vai yāni kānicit ||
śātātapaḥ |
[625]        bahir nadyādiṣv ācāntaḥ sodakaḥ kaṃcid aspṛśat |
rathyāgato ’pi yatnena śucir eva hi mānavaḥ ||
gargas tu jalāharaṇe niyamāntaram āha |
trisaṃdhyaṃ vāgyato vāri guptam āhṛtya śodhayet |
āhṛtya vārā dravyāṇi deśaṃ ca prokṣya śodhayet || iti |
'vāgyataḥ' maunī |
gobhilaḥ |
purā prāduṣkaraṇavelāyāḥ sāyaṃ prātar anuguptā apo haret
paricaraṇīyā vā || iti |
agniviharaṇaṃ ‘prāduṣkaraṇaṃ’, tasya ‘velā’ kālaḥ tasmāt, 'purā' pūrvakālam |
atra mukhyāmukhyāni jalāharaṇapātrāṇi |
āha yogiyājñavalkyaḥ |
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ mukhyaṃ pātraṃ prakīrtitam |
tadalābhe smṛtaṃ pātraṃ sravate yan na dhāritam ||
śāṭyāyaniḥ |
agryaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ raupyaṃ dāravaṃ mṛnmayaṃ dṛḍham |
tāmraṃ patrapuṭaṃ puṇyaṃ pātram abhyukṣaṇāya vai ||
'patrapuṭaṃ' praśastapatranirmitaṃ jalāharaṇayogyaṃ pātram ity arthaḥ | etac ca pātrāntarālābhe veditavyam |
tad āha āpastambaḥ |
savālābhe tu pātrāṇāṃ parṇapātraṃ vidhīyate |
pātraṃ tad api vijñeyaṃ sravate yan na dhāritam ||
[626]        varjyapātrāṇy āha āpastambaḥ |
saivālavālukādūrvātṛṇaparṇāyasair api |
abhyukṣaṇaṃ na gṛhīyād āpastambo ’bravīn muniḥ ||
parṇaniṣedho ’tra pātrāntarasadbhāvaviṣayaḥ | anāpāditapuṭākṛtiviṣayo vānusaṃdheyaḥ |
nalikābhinnapātreṇa kāṃsyapātreṇa caiva hi |
prāṇyaṅgaphalajenāpi kuryān nābhyukṣaṇaṃ dvijaḥ ||
'nalikayā' veṇvādimayyā 'bhinnena' sphuṭitena, 'kāṃsyapātreṇa' ghoṣapātreṇa, ghoṣam iti nighaṇṭau kāṃsyanāma | 'prāṇyaṅgena' śaṅkhaśuktyādirūpeṇa | 'phalajena' nālikerabilvādimayena |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
saivālatṛṇaparṇādyair asaṃskārāmbubhājanaiḥ |
sikatāvastralepaiś ca na kuryāt prokṣaṇaṃ budhaḥ ||
ādiśabdena nalikāprāṇyaṅgādi gṛhyate |
dravyaśuddhiprakaraṇoktasaṃmārjanādisaṃskārarahitaiḥ 'ambubhājanaiḥ' jalapātraiḥ, sikatābhir vastreṇa vā saṃgṭahītāni 'sikatāvastralepāni' |
yamo ’pi |
pātrādirahitaṃ toyam uddhṛtaṃ savyapāṇinā |
na tena prokṣaṇaṃ kuryād vastraniṣpīḍanena ca ||
'pātrādirahitaṃ' śaivalādyāhṛtam | 'savyapāṇinā' vāmena pāṇinā | anena dakṣiṇapāṇinā samāhṛtaṃ toyaṃ prokṣaṇārham ity abhyanujñātam | pātrasthitaṃ savyapāṇināpy āhṛtam abhyanujñā |
[627]        śātātapaḥ |
yat pātrarahitaṃ toyaṃ dhṛtaṃ savyena pāṇinā |
na tena prokṣayed dravyaṃ vastraniṣpīḍanena ca ||
nādhovastraikadeśena śuddhyartham apa āharet |
athānīte tu savyena prokṣayed dakṣiṇena tu ||
śāṭyāyaniḥ |
nāhared ekajātistu nāvṛto na ca jalpakaḥ |
na pāṇinā na vastreṇa jalam abhyukṣaṇāya vai ||
'ekajātiḥ' śūdraḥ,
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyas trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
caturtha ekajātis tu śūdronānyas tu pañcamaḥ ||
iti manusmaraṇāt | 'jalpakaḥ' amaunī |
abhyukṣaṇāharaṇānantarakṛtyam āha pracetāḥ |
saṃgṛhyābhyukṣaṇaṃ pāyāt sopānatko gṛhaṃ prati |
tad anutko gṛhaṃ prāpya sarvānuṣṭhānam ācaret ||
abhyukṣaṇodapātraṃ tu na tāvat sthāpyate kvacit |
yāvan nācamanaṃ dattaṃ prokṣitā na galantikā ||
anenāhṛtyābhyukṣaṇaṃ kvacid anidhāyaiva kenacid dattena jalāntareṇācamya saṃprokṣyābhyukṣaṇapātraṃ nidadhyād ity ukaṃ bhavati |
śāṭyāyanir api |
ādāya prokṣaṇaṃ pātraṃ na nidadhyād gṛhaṃ gataḥ |
akṛtvācamanaṃ bhūmāv ācāntaḥ prayato bhavet ||
ācamanam akṛtvā prokṣaṇaṃ bhūmāvasthāpayann ācāntaḥ prayato bhavatīty arthaḥ |
[628]        ācamanānantaraṃ pulastyaḥ |
tena dravyāṇy aśeṣāṇi prokṣyācamya punar gṛhe |
tataḥ karmāṇi kurvīta satkriyāś ca dvijottamāḥ ||
atra gṛhe punar ācamya tena dravyāṇi prokṣyety anvayaḥ |
prokṣaṇīyāni dravyāṇy āha gargaḥ |
trisaṃdhyaṃ vāgyatā vāri guptam āhṛtya śodhayet |
homopahārabhoge ha dravyātmaparicārakān ||
yamo ’pi |
abhyukṣet tu prayatnena pracārādhyuṣitaṃ gṛham |
madhyāhne caiva saṃdhyāyāṃ na cānabhyukṣite yajet | ||
gargaḥ |
gocarmamātram abbindu gāṃ śodhayati pātitaḥ |
samūḍhām aśucīnāṃ tu yatra lepo na vidyate ||
apāṃ binduḥ 'abbinduḥ', 'gāṃ' bhūmim, 'samūḍhām' kṛtaparisamūhanāṃ apasāritāvakarām iti yāvat |
uktābhyukṣaṇāsamarthaṃ praty āha śāṭyāyaniḥ |
nadyādau samyag ācāntaḥ saṃyato gṛham āgataḥ |
uddhṛtya maṇikāt toyaṃ tathābhyukṣaṇam ācaret ||
gṛhe vā samupaspṛśya kṛtvā svarṇakuśodakam |
kṛtvācamanam ācāntaḥ punaḥ prokṣaṇam ācaret ||
samyag dvir ācamya saṃyato nadyādeḥ svayaṃ śiṣyādibhir vā prokṣaṇam āhared [629] ity ekaḥ pakṣaḥ | tathaiva samupaspṛśya gṛhe sthitāt maṇikād abhyukṣaṇatoyam āhared ity aparaḥ pakṣaḥ | pūrvavad ācamanaṃ kṛtvā gṛha eva yatra kutracit sthitam udakam ādāya nikṣiptasuvarṇakuśaṃ kṛtvā punar ācāntas tadabhyukṣaṇārtham upakalpayed ity anyaḥ |
tathā |
āgneyaṃ prathamaṃ proktaṃ viprahastād dvitīyakam |
tṛtīyam udakasthānāc caturthaṃ maṇikāt smṛtam ||
nadyādeḥ svayam āhṛtaṃ prathamaṃ, anyena śucinā brāhmaṇena tata evānītaṃ dvitīyaṃ, gṛhasthitād eva kūpāder jalasthānāt gṛhītaṃ tṛtīyaṃ, maṇikād upāttaṃ caturtham | bṛhajjalasaṃśrayaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ maṇikaḥ |
pracetāḥ |
vaiśvānareṇa yat kiṃcit kurute prokṣaṇaṃ dvijaḥ |
gaṅgātoyasamaṃ sarvaṃ vadanti brahmavādinaḥ ||
‘vaiśvānaram' āgneya |
ityabhyukṣaṇāharaṇaṃ |
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaśrīmahādevīyasakalaśrīkaraṇādhipati-
paṇḍitaśrīhemādriviracite caturvargacintāmaṇau pariśeṣakhaṇḍe śrāddhakalpe prakṣepyadravyanirūpaṇaṃ nāma aṣṭamo ’dhyāyaḥ ||
[630]
atha navamo ’dhyāyaḥ |
śrāddhopakaraṇāni |
śailān kailāsayantī bhuvi balisadane śeṣayantī bhujaṅgān
ākāśe bhānumantaṃ śiśirakaratulāṃ prāpayantī sitimnā |
yat kīrttiḥ kautukena tribhuvanam aṭani spaṣṭapuṇyaḥ sa eṣaḥ
śrīhemādriḥ purastād upakaraṇagaṇaṃ śrāddhayuktaṃ bravīti ||
athānyāny api śrāddhopaprakaraṇāny ucyante |
tatra kuśās tāvat | tatrāpy utpattipūrvaṃ yas teṣāṃ mahimā tatra śatapathaśrutiḥ |
yā vai vṛtrād bībhatsamānā āpo dhanva dṛbhantya udāyaṃs te dabhā abhavan yad dṛbhantya udāyaṃs tasmād dabhās tā haitāḥ śuddhā medhyā āpo vṛtrābhiprakṣaritā yad dharbhā iti |
'vṛtrāt' tvaṣṭṛputrāt, 'bībhatsamānāḥ' bibhyatyaḥ, 'āpaḥ' udakāni, 'dhanva' marusthalaṃ, 'dṛbhantyaḥ' saṃdṛbhyamānāḥ, 'udāyan' udapatan, 'śuddhāḥ' nirdoṣāḥ | 'medhyāḥ' yajñiyāḥ | 'avṛtrābhiprakṣaritāḥ' vṛtreṇa kāluṣyam aprāpitāḥ |
āha gobhilaḥ |
kuśamūle sthito brahmā kuśamadhye tu keśavaḥ |
kuśāgre śaṅkaraṃ vidyāt sarve devāḥ samantataḥ ||
darbhān prakṛtya śatapathaśrutiḥ |
agram iva vai devānāṃ madhyam iva manuṣyāṇām |
mūlam iva pitṛṇām || iti |
hārito ’pi |
[631]        kuśahastena yaj japtaṃ dānaṃ caiva kuśaiḥ saha |
kuśahastas tu yad bhuṅkte tasya saṅkhyā na vidyate ||
kuśapūtaṃ bhavet snānaṃ kuśenopaspṛśed dvijaḥ |
kuśena coddhṛtaṃ toyaṃ somapānena saṃmitam ||
kuśamālāṃ tu yaḥ kaṇṭhe samāvahati sarvadā |
lipyate na sa pāpena padmapatram ivāmbhasā ||
kare kaṇṭhe śikhāyāṃ ca karṇayor ubhayor api |
pavitradhārako yaś ca na sa pāpena lipyate ||
kauśikaḥ |
kuśāsanaṃ sadā pūtaṃ yatīnāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |
kuśāsanopaviṣṭasya sidhyate yoga uttamaḥ ||
yathā puṣkaraparṇeṣu apāṃ lepo na dṛśyate |
evaṃ pavitrahastasya na lopo vidyate kvacit ||
hārītaḥ |
japahomaharā hy ete asurāvyaktarūpiṇaḥ |
pavitrakṛtahastasya vidravanti diśo daśa ||
gobhilaḥ |
vajro yathā surendrasya śūlaṃ haste harasya ca |
cakrāyudhaṃ yathā viṣṇor evaṃ viprakare kuśa iti ||
atha kuśavidhiḥ |
āha kauśikaḥ |
śucau deśe śucir bhūtvā sthitvā pūrvottarāmukhaḥ |
[632]        oṃkāreṇaiva mantreṇa kuśāḥ spṛśyā dvijottamaiḥ ||
utpāṭanamantras tatraiva |
virañcinā sahotpannaḥ parameṣṭhīti sargajaḥ |
nuda pāpāni sarvāṇi mama svastikaro bhava ||
smṛtyantare ’pi tam eva mantram abhidhāya |
evaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya tataḥ pūrvottarāmukhaḥ |
huṃphaṭkāreṇa mantreṇa sakṛc chittvā samuddharet ||
kārṣṇījiniḥ |
bhuvaṃ tu śithilīkṛtya khanitreṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ |
ādadyāt pitṛtīrthena huṃ phaṭ huṃ phaṭ sakṛt sakṛt ||
atra kālaniyamam āha hārītaḥ |
māse nabhasyam āvāsyā tasyāṃ darbhoccayo mataḥ |
ayātayāmās te darbhā niyoktavyāḥ punaḥ punaḥ |
nārdrān lunīyād rātrau tu lunīyād vāpi saṃdhyayoḥ ||
'nabhaḥ' śrāvaṇaḥ | yātayāmaṃ' anekatra prayogānarham | 'ayātayāmāḥ ' upayuktāḥ athānekaśaḥ kriyāntaropayogayogyā iti bhāvaḥ | ataś caite upayuktāḥ | athāpavādasthānaṃ vihāya kriyāntare ’pi punaḥ punar upayojyāḥ | kālāntare gṛhītās tu sakṛd apy upayuktayā tayā matayā kriyāntarānahīḥ |
śālaṅkāyanaḥ |
nabhomāsasya darśe tu śucir darbhān samāharet |
ayātayāmās te darbhā niyojyāḥ syuḥ punaḥ punaḥ ||
[633]        yad api sāṅkhyavacanam |
dabhāḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ mantrāḥ brāhmaṇāś ca viśeṣataḥ |
ayātayāmāny etāni niyojyāni punaḥ punaḥ ||
yac ca gṛhyapariśiṣṭe |
darbhāḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ mantrāḥ brāhmaṇa havir agnayaḥ |
ayātayāmāny etāni niyojyāni punaḥ punaḥ || iti ||
tad api pūrvavacanānusāreṇa nabho’māvāsyāgṛhītadarbhaviṣayam eva mantavyam |
mārkaṇḍeyo ’py amīṣāṃ sāpavādām ayātayāmatām āha |
sapavitreṇa hastena kuryād ācamanakriyām |
nocchiṣṭaṃ tat pavitraṃ tu bhutocchiṣṭaṃ tu varjayet ||
yena pavitreṇa sahācamanaṃ kṛtaṃ tad ucchiṣṭaṃ na bhavati, yena tu saha bhojanaṃ kṛtaṃ tad ucchiṣṭatayā tyājyam | etac ca granthirahitapavitraviṣayam,
granthir yasya pavitrasya na tenācamanaṃ caret
iti hārītasmaraṇāt | etad api brahmagranthimatpavitraviṣayam,
na brahmagranthinācāmen na ca kāśādibhiḥ kvacit |
ity ādityasmaraṇāt |
hārītaḥ |
ācāmet prayato nityaṃ pavitreṇa dvijottamaḥ |
nocchiṣṭaṃ ca bhavet tat tu bhuktaśeṣaṃ tu varjayet ||
‘pavitreṇa’ saha pavitrayuktapāṇir ity arthaḥ | 'tat' pavitraṃ, ucchiṣṭaṃ na bhavati, karmānarhaṃ na bhavatīty arthaḥ | 'bhuktaśeṣaṃ' bhojanāṅgatāṃ nītam, 'varjayet' tyajet | etac ca śucer ācāmato veditavyaṃ 'prayataḥ', iti [634] vacanāt | anye tu karmaṅgācamanaviṣayam etad ity āhuḥ |
tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
sapavitraḥ sadarbho vā karmāṅge pitṛkarmaṇi |
aśūnyaṃ tu karaṃ kṛtvā sarvatrācamanaṃ caret |
nocchiṣṭāt tat sadarbhaś ca muktocchiṣṭaṃ tu varjayet ||
atra pavitraśabdena granthimatpavitram ity eke |
āha śātātapaḥ |
jape home tathā dāne svādhyāye pitṛkarmaṇi |
aśūnyaṃ tu karaṃ kuryāt suvarṇarajataiḥ kuśaiḥ ||
jape home tathā dāne svādhyāye pitṛtarpaṇe |
savyāpasavyau kurvīta sapavitrau karau budhaḥ ||
kaśyapaḥ |
snāne home jape dāne svādhyāye pitṛtarpaṇe |
sapavitrau sadabhau vā karau kurvīta nānyathā ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
kuśapāṇiḥ sadā tiṣṭhed brāhmaṇo dambhavarjitaḥ |
sa nityaṃ hanti pāpāni tūlarāśim ivānalaḥ ||
purāṇe ’pi |
kauśeyaṃ bibhṛyān nityaṃ pavitraṃ dakṣiṇe kare |
bhuñjānas tu viśeṣeṇa gṛhītān na ca śodhanam ||
[635]        'kauśeyaṃ pavitraṃ ' kuśapavitram |
atrir api |
ubhābhyām eva pāṇibhyāṃ viprair darbhapavitrake |
dhāraṇīye prayatnena brahmagranthisamanvite ||
brahmayajñe jape caiva brahmagranthir vidhīyate |
bhojane vartulaḥ proktaḥ evaṃ dharmo na hīyate ||
dviguṇīkṛtānāṃ darbhaśikhānāṃ pāśaḥ pradakṣiṇam ardhaveṣṭanaṃ vidhāya pañcādbhāgena yadā praveśyate tadā vartulagranthiḥ | yadā sa eva prādakṣiṇyena samagraveṣṭanaṃ vidhāya purobhāgena praveśyate tadā brahmagranthiḥ |
darbhadhāraṇāyāṅguliniyamaḥ skandapurāṇe darśitaḥ |
anāmikādhṛto darbho jyeṣṭhānāmikayāpi vā |
ubhābhyām anāmikābhyāṃ dhārye darbhapavitrake ||
smṛtyantare |
anāmikābhyām evaite pavitre granthisaṃyute |
dhārayed dakṣiṇenaiva pāṇinā vā kuśagrahaḥ ||
'anāmikā' upakaniṣṭhikā |
pavitralakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
anantargarbhitaṃ sāgraṃ kauśaṃ dvidalam eva ca |
prādeśamātraṃ vijñeyaṃ pavitraṃ yatra kutracit ||
pavitrakaraṇaprakāram āha yogayājñavalkyaḥ |
pavitre stheti mantreṇa dve pavitre ca kārayet |
nāntargarbhe kuśachinne kauśe prādeśasaṃmite ||
[636]        ‘nāntargarbhe' garbhe śikhārahite | 'kuśachinne' kuśemāveṭṣya chinne | ‘kauśe' kuśamaye | “pavitrestho vaiṣṇavyo” iti chedanamantraḥ |
atra viśeṣa ukto devīpurāṇe |
pavitrestha iti chinnaṃ mūladeśe kuśadvayam |
kuśatrayeṇa prādeśamātre te staḥ pavitrake ||
āpastambo ’pi |
samau sāgrau dabhau prādeśamātrau pavitre kurute | pavitrestho vaiṣṇavī vā punar manasā punātv iti tṛaṇaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ vāntardhā yac chinatti na nakheneti |
yajñapārśvo ’pi |
yā oṣadhīmantreṇa kṛtvāṅguṣṭāṅguliparvaṇi |
chindyāt prādeśamātrṃ tu pavitraṃ viṣṇudaivatam ||
na nakhena na kāṣṭhena na lohena na mṛṇmayaiḥ |
nakhena tu bhaved vyādhiḥ kāṣṭhenārtho na siddhyati ||
āyasena bhaven mṛtyuḥ mṛṇmayaiḥ kalaho dhruvam |
vṛttagranthimatpavitralakṣaṇam uktaṃ gāruḍapurāṇe |
śraddhaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya śikhāṃ pāśaṃ praveśayet |
vaiṣṇavenaiva mārgeṇa vṛttagranthau pavitrake ||
'vaiṣṇavo mārgaḥ' paścādbhāgaḥ |
brahmagranthimatpavitralakṣaṇam api tatraivoktam |
saṃtyajya vaiṣṇavaṃ mārgaṃ brahmamārgaviniḥsṛtam |
sakṛt pradakṣiṇīkṛtya pavitram abhidhīyate |
tad brahmagranthimat proktaṃ pavitraṃ vedavādibhiḥ ||
[637]        pavitrakartur abhimukhaḥ pradeśa˙ 'brahmamārgaḥ' |
pavitradarbhaśikhāsaṅkhyāpi tatraivoktā |
saptabhir darbhapiñjūlaiḥ kuryād brāhmaṃ patricakam |
pañcabhiḥ kṣatriyasyaiva caturbhis tu tathā viśaḥ |
dvābhyāṃ śūdrasya vihitam antarāṇāṃ tathaiva ca ||
mārkaṇḍeyas tu |
caturbhir darbhapiñjūlair brāhmaṇasya pavitrakam |
ekaikaṃ nyūnam uddiṣṭaṃ varṇe varṇe yathākramam ||
sarveṣāṃ vā bhaved dvābhyāṃ pavitraṃ granthitaṃ na vā |
tribhis tu śāntike kāryaṃ pauṣṭike pañcabhis tathā |
caturbhiś cābhicārākhyaṃ kurvan kuryāt pavitrakam ||
darbhalakṣaṇam āha kauśikaḥ |
saptapatrāḥ śubhā darbhās tilakṣetrasamudbhavāḥ |
te praśastā dvijātīnāṃ daive pitrye ca karmaṇi ||
aprasūtāḥ smṛtā darbhāḥ prasūtās tu kuśāḥ smṛtāḥ |
samūlāḥ kutapāḥ proktāś chinnāgrās taṇamaṃjñitāḥ ||
'aprasūtāḥ' asaṃjātaprasavāḥ apuṣpitā ity arthaḥ |
hārīto ’pi |
darbhagraṃ daivam ity ukaṃ samūlāgraṃ tu paitṛkam |
tatra mūlaṃ vinā ye tu te kuśāḥ kutapāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
acchinnāgrān sapatrāṃś ca samūlān komalān śubhān |
pitṛdevajapārthaṃ ca samādadyāt kuśān dvijaḥ ||
