[638]        etat prastarādivyatiriktaviṣayam |
tathā ca brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
haritā vai sapiñjūlāḥ snigdhāḥ puṣṭāḥ samanvitāḥ |
ratnimātrapramāṇena pitṛtīrthena saṃskṛtāḥ ||
chinnamūlā grahītavyāḥ prastarārthaṃ kuśottamāḥ |
agnikārye ca yāge ca samūlān parivarjayet ||
'sapiñjūlāḥ' apṛthakkṛtadalāḥ | baddhamuṣṭikāraparimāṇaṃ ratniḥ, tayā saṃskṛtāḥ pitṛtīrthagrahaṇenotpāditātiśayāḥ |
sumantuḥ |
acchinnāgrāḥ śubhā darbhāḥ pitrye tu haritāḥ śubhāḥ |
amūlā devakāryeṣu prayojyāś ca japādiṣu |
saptapatrāḥ kuśāḥ śastā daive pitrye ca karmaṇi ||
āha kātyāyanaḥ |
haritā yajñiyā darbhāḥ pītakāḥ pañcayajñikāḥ |
samūlāḥ pitṛdevatyāḥ kalmāṣā vaiśvadevikāḥ |
hrasvāḥ pracaraṇīyāḥ syuḥ kuśā dīrghāś ca barhikāḥ ||
‘pañcayajñiyāḥ’ pañcayajñārhāḥ | 'pracaraṇaṃ' anuṣṭhānaṃ, tadarhāḥ 'pracaraṇīyāḥ', 'pitṛdevatyāḥ' pitrathaḥ |
tathā ca yamaḥ |
samūlas tu bhaved darbhaḥ pitṝṇāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
bahirlūnāḥ sakṛlllūnāḥ sarvatra pitṛkarmaṇi ||
'bahirlūnāḥ' upamūlalūnāḥ |
[639]        evaṃ ca yad uktaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe,
upamūle tathā lūnāḥ śrāddhārthaṃ tu kuśāḥ smṛtāḥ |
tathā śyāmākanīvārau dūrvā ca samudāhṛtā || iti |
yad api gobhilenopamūlalūnāḥ pitṛkāryesv iti, tad ekoddiṣṭetaraśrāddhaviṣayam ity avagantavyam |
atra purāṇe |
sapiṇḍīkaraṇaṃ yāvad ṛjubhiś ca kuśaiḥ kriyāḥ |
samūlaiś cāpi kartavyāḥ sāgraiḥ śucisamāhitaiḥ ||
sapiṇḍīkaraṇād ūrdhvaṃ dviguṇair vidhivad bhavet |
tilakṣetrodbhavair darbhaḥ prayatnād dviguṇīkṛtaiḥ ||
pitṝṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ devatānāṃ yadṛcchayā |
paitryaṃ mūlena madhyena nāraṃ dānaṃ prayatnataḥ |
devaṃ karma kuśāgreṇa kartavyaṃ bhūtim icchatā ||
'yadṛcchayā' aniyamenety arthaḥ |
nāradīyapurāṇe |
tarpaṇādīni kāryāni pitṝṇāṃ yāni kānicit |
tāni syuḥ dviguṇair darbhaḥ saptapatrair viśeṣataḥ ||
atra varjyan āha hārītaḥ |
citau darbhāḥ pathi dabhā ye darbhā yajñabhūmiṣu |
prastarāsanapiṇḍeṣu ṣaṭ kuśān parivarjayet ||
brahmayajñe ca ye darbhāḥ ye darbhāḥ pitṛtarpaṇe |
dhṛtā mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ teṣāṃ tyāgo vidhīyate ||
[640]        apūtā garbhitā dabhā ye ca darbhā nakhais tathā |
kvathitān agnidagdhāṃś ca kuśān yatnena varjayet ||
'garbhitāḥ' garbhadalayuktāḥ | 'kvathitāḥ' śaṭitāḥ |
purāṇe ’pi |
piṇḍārthaṃ ye kṛtā dabhā yaiḥ kṛtaṃ pitṛtarpaṇam |
mūtrocchiṣṭadhṛtā ye ca teṣāṃ tyāgo vidhīyate ||
śāṭyāyanaḥ |
yaiḥ kṛtaḥ piṇḍanirvāpaḥ śrāddhaṃ vā pitṛtarpaṇam |
bhojane ca malotsarge ye dhṛtās tān parityajet ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
piṇḍārthaṃ ye smṛtā darbhās tarpaṇārthe tathaiva ca |
dhṛtaiḥ kṛte tu viṇmūtre tyāgas teṣāṃ vidhīyate ||
tyāgāpavādam āha hārītaḥ |
nīvīmadhye tu ye dabhā brahmasūtre ca ye kṛtāḥ |
pavitrāṃs tān vijānīyād yathākāyas tathaiva te ||
gautamo ’py āha |
nīvīmadhye sthitā ye ca yajñasūtre sthitās tathā |
ucchiṣṭamūtrādyutsarge na teṣūpatir bhavet ||
vāmahaste sthite darbhe na pibed dakṣiṇena tu |
vastrādinopagrahaṇe na doṣaḥ pibato bhavet ||
hārītaḥ |
samitpuṣpakuśādīni śrotriyaḥ svayam āharet |
[641]        śūdrāhūtaiḥ kuśair vipraḥ karma kurvan vrajaty adhaḥ ||
kuśapuṣpāṇi samidha auṣadhaṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |
niṣedhe ’pi ca gṛhṇīyād amāvāsyāhani dvijaḥ ||
kuśālābhe pratinidhim āha śaṅkhaḥ |
kuśābhāve dvijaśreṣṭhaḥ kāśaiḥ kurvīta yatnataḥ |
tarpaṇādīni karmāṇi kāśāḥ kuśasamāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
atha śatapathaśrutiḥ |
yajño ha devebhyo ’pacakrāma so ’śvo bhūtvāparāṅ āvavarta tasya devā anuhāya bālān abhipedus tān ālulupus tān ālupya sārdhaṃ sanyāsus tata etā oṣadhayaḥ samabhavat padaś ca bālā iti |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe brahmavaivarttapurāṇeṣu |
pūrvaṃ kīrttimatāṃ śreṣṭho babhūvāsau prajāpatiḥ |
tasya bālā nipatitā bhūmau kāśatvam āgatāḥ ||
tasmān medhyāḥ sadā kāśāḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi pūjitāḥ |
piṇḍanirvapaṇaṃ teṣu kartavyaṃ bhūtim icchatā |
prajāpuṣṭidyutiprajñākīrtikāntisamanvitāḥ |
bhavanti rucirā nityaṃ vipāpmāno vivarjitāḥ ||
piṇḍapradānaṃ kāśeṣu ye kurvanti yathāvidhi |
viṣṇuḥ |
kuśābhāve kuśasthāne kāśaṃ dūrvāṃ vā dadyāt |
yajñapārśvaḥ |
kuśābhāve tu kāśāḥ syuḥ kāśāḥ kuśasamāḥ smṛtāḥ |
[642]        kāśābhāve grahītavyā anye dabhā yathākramam ||
'anye dabhāḥ' darbhakāryakarāḥ | te cātriṇā prokā daśadarbheṣu |
kuśāḥ kāśā yavā dūrvā uśīrāś ca sakundurāḥ |
godhūmā vrīhayo muñjā daśadarbhāḥ sabalvajāḥ ||
‘uśīraḥ' bālakaḥ | 'kunduruḥ' kunda iti nāmnā prasiddhaḥ tṛṇaviśeṣaḥ | ‘balvajaḥ’ kāśasadṛśastṛṇaviśeṣaḥ yasya rajjubhiḥ pāmaraiḥ khaṭvā vitanyate |
barhiḥ praty āha yamaḥ |
kuśāḥ kāśās tathā dūrvā yavā vrīhaya eva ca |
balvajāḥ puṇḍarīkāṇi saptadhā barhir ucyate ||
uśanāḥ |
piṇḍabarhiḥ kuśāḥ kāśā dūrvā muñjāś ca balvajāḥ |
muñjekṣuvrīhigodhūmayavakundurubālakāḥ ||
ekādaśyāṃ trayodaśyāṃ maghākṛttikayor api |
piṇḍadānaṃ na kartavyaṃ durvāsu śriyam icchatā |
piṇḍabarhiṣi tu balbajaniṣedham āha gobhilaḥ |
barhir upamūlalūnaṃ pitṛbhyas teṣām alābhe śūkatṛṇaśarabalbajavarjaṃ sarvatṛṇāni |
'śūkaṭaṇaṃ' śūkavant tṛṇam |
atrāpastambasūtram |
samūlaṃ sakṛd ācchinnaṃ barhir āharanti | sakṛd ācchinnāni vā tṛṇāny
upamūlaṃ ditānīti |
'upamūlaṃ' mūlasamīpe, 'ditāni' chinnāni |
[643]        niṣiddhāni tṛṇāny uktāni vāyupurāṇe |
sadā śrāddhe kṣupāś caiva balvajās taravas tathā |
vīraṇāś ca lavāś caiva varjyā varṣāś ca nityaśaḥ |
evamādīny athānyāni tṛṇaṇi parivarjayet ||
iti kuśanirūpaṇam |
atha tilāḥ |
tatra teṣām utpattis tāvan nirūpyate vārāhapurāṇe |
dharaṇy uvāca |
kathaṃ tilāḥ samutpannāḥ kena cotpāditāḥ prabho |
pitṝṇāṃ vallabhāḥ kasmād iti me vakum arhasi ||
śrīvarāha uvāca |
brahmakṣetre kurukṣetre devā brahmapurogamāḥ |
adityā vasavaḥ sādhyāḥ bhāskarā marutośvinau ||
viśvedevāś ca uṣitā rudrāś cendro bṛhaspatiḥ |
ārebhire mahāyajñaṃ gosavaṃ saṃsitavratāḥ ||
āhūtās tatra vai devāḥ sarvaśo varavarṇini |
ṛṣayaś ca jitātmāno nānādeśanivāsinaḥ ||
adhvaryūdgātṛhotāras tatra brāhmaṇapuṅgavāḥ |
svāhākāravaṣaṭkārair adhvaraṃ taṃ vitenire ||
ājyāhūtibhir agryābhir mantraputābhir eva ca |
sāmidhenīsamiddheṣu dīyamāneṣu vahniṣu |
[644]        vicitrāṇāṃ tathānnānāṃ pradānair anivāritaiḥ ||
yathākāmābhisampannais tarpyamāṇeṣu bhoktṛṣu |
gobhūhiraṇyavāsobhir dīyamānair yadṛcchayā ||
anārttibhūmideveṣu nīyamāneṣu kṛtsnaśaḥ |
dakṣiṇābhir anantābhiḥ prīteṣv ṛtvikṣu caiva hi ||
samāgateṣu deveṣu tatra sarveṣu kevalam |
rājñā somena sahitā ājagmuḥ pitaras tadā ||
yajñasaṃbhārasaṃbhrāntair nāhūtās te purāmaraiḥ |
atha te pitaraḥ śrutvā tāyamānaṃ tu gosavam ||
sāmena rājñā sahitāḥ samājagmus tvarānvitāḥ |
tān dṛṣṭvā savanasthāne vilakṣā bhītamānasāḥ ||
procuḥ prāñjalayaḥ sarve gamyatāṃ gamyatām iti |
tataḥ sasomāḥ pitaraḥ krodhasaṃraktalocanāḥ ||
pratyūcur vepamānāṅgāḥ kim asmābhir ihāgataiḥ |
pūrvam asmān avajñāya yuṣmābhir vitataḥ kratuḥ ||
vāṅmātreṇāpi vai devāḥ kiṃ na saṃbhāvitā vayam |
śapsyāmahe tataḥ sarvān yajamānān saṛtvijaḥ ||
atha te vibudhā bhītā rakṣety ūcuḥ pitāmaham |
uvācātha viriñco ’pi kaśyapaṃ tair dhruvaṃ tadā ||
sasomakān pitṛgaṇān sāntvaya tvaṃ mahāmune |
kaśyapo ’pi mahābhāgo nideśāt parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
[645]        praṇamya somaṃ rājānaṃ bhaktyā pitṛgaṇān api |
tuṣṭāva vividhaiḥ stotrair ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃbhavaiḥ ||
tam ūcuḥ pitaraḥ sarve tuṣṭās tava mahāmune |
na ca śāpapradānasya vighnaḥ kāryas tvayādhunā ||
akṛtvā daṇḍam eteṣām avalepavatāṃ mune |
nāsmākaṃ manasaḥ śāntir ato ’smān mā nivāraya ||
abravīt kaśyapo ’thaitān kṣamadhvaṃ yogavittamāḥ |
yajñabhāgād api śreṣṭhaṃ bhavadbhyo ’nnaṃ dadāmy aham ||
ity ūcivān muniḥ svebhyo gātrebhyo 'py assṛjat tadā |
tilān kṛṣṇān atiślakṣān sumṛṣṭān amṛtād api ||
sṛṣṭavān atha romabhyas tilān śvetān munīśvaraḥ |
teṣām adhipatiṃ somaṃ rājānaṃ samakalpayat ||
prakalpya tilarāsīn vai pitṝn provāca kaśyapaḥ |
idam annaṃ mayā dattaṃ pitaraḥ pratigṛhyatām ||
ākalpaṃ tṛptaye vo 'stu tyajyatāṃ śāpasaṃbhramaḥ |
athātaḥ pitaraḥ sarve prītāḥ somapurogamāḥ |
tilānāṃ darśanenaiva paramāṃ tṛptim āgatāḥ |
ūcuḥ prasannamanasas trātāḥ smo munipuṅgava |
kiṃ kāryaṃ yajñabhāgena tilā eva bhavantu naḥ ||
vayaṃ śāpān nivṛttāḥ smas tridaśāḥ santu nirbhayāḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ tilā eva deyā naḥ śraddhayānvitaiḥ ||
nāmṛatenāpi naḥ kṛtyaṃ tilān yadi labhāmahe |
[646]        yas tarpaṇaṃ ca śrāddhaṃ ca tilapūtaṃ kariṣyati ||
bhakṣyāṃs tilamayāṃś cāpi bhaktyā naḥ saṃpradāsyati |
deyadravyāṇi cānyāni pradāsyanti tilodakaiḥ ||
tasya prajāṃ dhanaṃ svargaṃ prītā dāsyāmahe vayam |
ity uktvāntardadhus te vai pitaro divyayoginaḥ ||
hiraṇyagarbhapramukhā yayur devā yathāgatam |
ity evaṃ somadevatyā gosave devanirmitāḥ |
tilāḥ kṛṣṇāś ca śuklāś ca kim anyac chrotum arhasi ||
matsyapurāṇe |
viṣṇor dehasamudbhūtāḥ kuśāḥ kṛṣṇatilās tathā |
dharmasya rakṣaṇāyālam etat prāhur divaukasaḥ ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
viṣṇoḥ svedāt tilā jātāḥ kuśā romabhya eva ca |
śrāddhasya rakṣaṇārthāya iti prāhur maharṣayaḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
tilā viṣṇoḥ samudbhūtās tatas te paramaṃ haviḥ |
tilaiḥ śrāddhāc ca homāc ca syāt jagatprītir bhārgava ||
atha tilānāṃ mahimavidhir ucyate |
ādityapurāṇe |
durvāsā uvāca |
kathaṃ tilāḥ samutpannāḥ kathaṃ caivāvatāritāḥ |
evaṃ me saṃśayaṃ deva chettum arhasy aśeṣataḥ ||
[647]                bhānur uvāca |
śṛṇuṣvāvahito vipra sarvaṃ vakṣyāmy aśeṣataḥ |
purā kṛtayuge śaśvad anāvṛṣṭyāntilakṣaye ||
kṣutkṣāmakaṇṭhās tṛṣitāḥ pitaraḥ sarva āgatāḥ |
tapaś ceruś ca vipulaṃ tilārthe nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
divyavarṣasahasraṃ tu nirāhārās tapasvinaḥ |
parivṛttaḥ sarvagaṇaiḥ sarvair devair viśeṣataḥ ||
gandharvaiś cāraṇair yakṣaiḥ siddhaiḥ kinnarapannagaiḥ |
prajāpatiḥ pitṛpatiḥ sākṣād devapitāmahaḥ ||
parituṣṭo ’smi vo dadmi brūta yac cepsitam hi vaḥ |
pitara ūcuḥ |
tilān dehi mahābhāga kāṅkṣitān vai na saṃśayaḥ |
tilair vinā na jīvāmo nātilas tiṣṭhate hṛdi ||
brahmā uvāca |
gacchadhvaṃ vai tilā dattāḥ sarveṣāṃ vo dvijottamāḥ |
parituṣṭāś ca pitaraḥ tilān ādāya niryayuḥ ||
tataḥ pitṝṇam āhāraḥ sarvas tilamayo ’bhavat |
tilāmṛtena saṃtuṣṭāḥ pitaro nirṛtiṃ yayuḥ ||
tilān yas tu prayaccheta pitṝṇāṃ tṛptikāmyayā |
agniṣṭomasahasrasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
vaiśākhyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ ca tilān kṣaudreṇa saṃyutān |
yaḥ prayacched dvijāgryebhyaḥ sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ||
[648]        kṛṣibhāgī ca dhanavān jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
yaḥ śraddadhānaḥ śrāddheṣu guḍena tilamodakān ||
sitayā vāpi matsyaṇḍyā kṛtvā bhaktyā prayacchati |
tasya tṛptāḥ pitṛgaṇāḥ prayānti paramāṃ gatim |
tasyaivāyuḥ prayacchanti svargam ārogyam eva ca |
tilais tu kṛśarān kṛtvā dadyāc ca tilaśaṣkulīḥ ||
dadāti yaḥ pitṝṇāṃ vai tasya loko ’kṣayo mataḥ |
yaś cākṣayatṛtīyāyāṃ tilapātrāṇi yacchati ||
ayane cottare caiva tasya puṇyam anantakam |
yo dharmarājāya tilān dadāti śraddhayā naraḥ ||
maharṣeḥ kaśyapasyaiva gātrebhyaḥ prasṛtās tilāḥ |
tato divyaṃ gatā bhāvaṃ pradāneṣu tilāḥ prabho ||
mahābhārate |
tilāc chrāddhe praśaṃsanti dānam etad anuttamam |
tilā nityaṃ pradātavyā yathāśaktyā dvijarṣabha ||
atrāha satyavrataḥ |
jarttilās tu tilāḥ proktāḥ kṛṣṇavarṇā vanodbhavāḥ |
jarttilāś caiva te jñeyā akṛṣṭotpāditāś ca ye ||
āpastambo ’pi |
aṭavyāṃ ye samutpannā akṛṣṭaphalitās tathā |
te vai śrāddhe pavitrās tu tilāst e na tilās tilāḥ ||
ye ’ṭavyām utpannā akṛṣṭāyāṃ ca bhūmau phalitās te jarttilākhyās tilāḥ [649] śrāddhakarmaṇi praśastatamāḥ | ye tu grāmyāḥ kṛṣṭhe kṣetre phalitāḥ prasiddhās tilās te na tilāḥ | nāyaṃ teṣāṃ tilarūpasya niṣedhaḥ, aśakyatvāt | nāpi tilakāryakāritvaniṣedhaḥ, laukikatatkāryakāritvaniṣedhasya pratyakṣādibādhitatvāt | nāpi vaidikakāryakāritvasya niṣedhaḥ, vacanavirodhāt |
tathā ca brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
yatis tridaṇḍī karaṇaṃ rājataṃ pātram eva ca |
dauhitraḥ kutapaḥ kālaḥ chāgaḥ kṛṣṇājinaṃ tathā ||
gaurāḥ kṛṣṇās tathāraṇyās tathaiva vividhās tilāḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ tṛptaye sṛṣṭā daśaite brahmaṇā svayam || iti |
atra tridaṇḍīti yater viśeṣaṇaṃ | rājatam iti pātrasya | kutapa iti kālasya | gaurās tilāḥ kṛṣṇās tilāḥ āraṇyās tilā iti viśeṣaṇabhedāt trayaḥ padārthaḥ | evaṃ ca daśasaṅkhyā ghaṭate | asmiṃś ca vacane sarvaprakārāṇāṃ tilānāṃ vaidikakāryakāritvābhidhānān na prasiddhānāṃ tilānāṃ tatkāritvaniṣedho ghaṭate | ataḥ “na tilās tilāḥ" iti "apaśavo vā anye go ’śvebhyaḥ” itivad vidhīyamānajarttilapraśaṃsārthaṃ nindāmātram iti |
iti tilanirūpaṇam |
atha yavāḥ |
tatra teṣām utpattiḥ śatapathaśrutau |
puruṣaṃ ha vai devāḥ | agre paśum ālebhire tasyālabdhasya medho ’pacakrāma so ’śvaṃ praviveśa te ’śvam ālabhanta tasyālabhdhasya medho ’pacakrāma sa gāṃ praviveśa te gām ālabhanta tasthālabdhasya medho 'pacakrāma so ’viṃ praviveśa [650] te ’vim ālabhanta tasyālabdhasya medho ’pacakrāma so ’jaṃ praviveśa te’ jam ālabhanta tasyālabdhasya medho ’pacakrāma | sa imāṃ pṛthivīṃ praviveśa | taṃ khananta ivānvīṣus tam anvavindaṃs tāv imau vrīhiyavau tasmād apy etāv etarhi khananta ivaivānuvindanti sa yāvad vīryavad dha vā’asaite sarve paśavaḥ ālabhdhāḥ syus tādvīryavad dhāsya havir eva bhavati ya evam etad vedeti |
etān sarvān paśūn ālabhya yajamānasya yāvat phalaṃ tāvad vrīhibhir yavair vā yajamānasya phalaṃ syād iti vacanārthaḥ |
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
prajākāmaḥ purā tvaṣṭā tuṣṭāva parameṣṭinam |
tato gotrabhidaḥ śatruṃ vṛtraṃ putram ajījanat ||
jātamātraḥ sa lokānāṃ mahākāyo mahābalaḥ
bhayaṃ saṃjanayāmāsa bhuvanagrāsalālasaḥ ||
adhastād ūrdhvatas tiryag iṣumātraṃ hi sarvaśaḥ |
pratyakṣaṃ vardhate tasya śarīram amitaujasaḥ ||
sa viśvaṃ vyānaśe vīraḥ svalpakenāpy anehasā |
jighatsur yugapad viśvaṃ sa hi pitrā nivāritaḥ ||
pūrvahne bubhuje devair dīyamānaṃ baliṃ balī |
madhyandine ca manujair aparāhṇe pitṛvrajaiḥ ||
evaṃ sa sarvam annādyam ekākī grasate tadā |
eka eva papau somam eka evāharad dhaviḥ ||
tato bṛhaspatir devaiḥ saṃbhṛto yajñasaktibhiḥ |
[651]        cikṣepa kṣepaṇāyāsya jvalantam aśaniṃ śanaiḥ ||
sa dambho ’liprahāreṇa durdhareṇa dharātale |
vṛtraḥ papāta pātālakuharāṇi pradārayan ||
tataḥ praskannam annādyarasaṃ sarvaṃ vasundharā |
dadhāra jagadādhārā dhārābhir abhipātitam ||
atha tasmān mahauṣadhyo jajñire yajñasādhanam |
miśrānnarasajātatvād dhīyamānā yavākhyayā ||
tato vṛtrāsurabadhāt parituṣṭā divaukasaḥ |
yavān ālokya paramaṃ paritoṣam upāyayuḥ ||
ūcuḥ sarve pramuditāḥ sudhāhārāḥ parasparam |
āraṇyānām oṣadhīnāṃ grāmyāṇām apy ayaṃ rasaḥ ||
rājñaḥ somasya sāro ’yam āvirbhūto yavātmanā |
vṛtrāsurasya vīryeṇa parameṇopabṛṅhitāḥ ||
ete pavitram asmākam eta eva paraṃ haviḥ |
ete pīyūṣamadhurāḥ sarvadā prīṇayantu naḥ ||
haviryajñāḥ pravartantām etair amṛtabhojinām |
karmaṇy ete niyujyantāṃ śāntike pauṣṭike ’pi ca ||
yat kiṃcid devadevatyaṃ śraddhāvadbhiḥ pradīyate |
tad etan miśritair eva salilaiḥ saṃpradīyatām ||
śrāddheṣu vaiśyadevāni yāni karmāṇi kānicit |
etair eva vidhīyantāṃ tāni śrotriyapuṅgavaiḥ ||
yavair arthā yavair dānaṃ yavair akṣā yavā haviḥ |
[652]        vaiśvadeve tu yatra syāt tṛptis tatrāstu śāśvatī ||
ity uktvā vibudhāḥ prītāḥ svāni dhāmāni bhejire |
pūrvavad bhuvanāny āsan pākaśāsanaśāsane ||
atha yavānāṃ mahimā |
tatra śatapathaśrutiḥ |
devāś ca vā asurāś ca | ubhaye prājāpatyāḥ paspṛdhire tato devebhyaḥ sarvā evauṣadhaya īyur yavā haivebhyo neyuḥ | tad vai devā aspṛṇvata | ta etaiḥ sarvāḥ sapatnānām oṣadhīr ayuvata yad ayuvata tasmād yavā nāma | te hocuḥ | hanta yaḥ sarvāsām oṣadhīnāṃ rasas taṃ yaveṣu dadhāmeti sa yaḥ sarvāsām oṣadhīnāṃ rasa āsīt taṃ yaveṣv adadhus tasmād yatrānyā oṣadhayo snāyanti tad ete modamānā vardhanta evaṃ hy eṣu rasam adadhus tatho’ evaiṣa etaiḥ sarvāḥ sapatnānām oṣadhīr yute iti |
purā devāsuravigrahe pravṛttibalibhir asurair devebhyaḥ sarva evauṣadhayo ’pahṛtya nītāḥ | kevalaṃ rakṣoghnaśaktiviśeṣavadbhyo yavebhyo bibhyadbhir asurais ta eva netuṃ nāśakyanta | tato devā devānām eva mahinā sarvān apy asurān vidrāvya sarvā apy oṣadhīr ātmanā saha dadhur ataḥ sarvataś ca yavā iti vākyārthaḥ |
atha kṛṣṇājinam |
tatra śatapathaśrutiḥ |
sadyaḥ sa yajñas tat kṛṣṇājinam iti |
tathā | [653]
śreyāṃsaṃ vā eṣa upādhirohati yo yajñaṃ yajño hi kṛṣṇājinam iti |
trailokyātmakatvenāpi kṛṣṇājinasya stutis tatraiva |
yadyu ekaṃ bhavati tad eṣāṃ lokānāṃ rūpaṃ tad enam eṣu lokeṣv adhidīkṣayati yāni śuktāni tāni divo rūpaṃ yāni kṛṣṇāni tāny asyai yad ivetarathā yāny eva kṛṣṇāni tāni divorūpaṃ yāni śuktāni tāny asyai yāny eva babhrūnīva harīṇi tāny antarikṣasya rūpaṃ tad enam eṣu lokeṣv adhidīkṣayatīti |
śuklānītyādiṣu romāṇīti śeṣaḥ | 'asyai' pṛthivyāḥ | 'babhrūṇi' kaḍārāni, ‘harīṇi’ hariṇavarṇāni |
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
bhartṛyajña uvāca |
tataḥ śaktyā purā sṛṣṭaḥ sṛṣṭikāmena vedhasā |
āsīt kratukriyādakṣo dakṣo nāma prajāpatiḥ ||
śatam ekottaraṃ kanyāḥ sa prāsūta mahātapāḥ |
tāś ca dharmyeṇa vidhinā surebhyaḥ pratyapādayat ||
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa |
pīyūṣabhānave caiva prītātmā saptaviṃśatim ||
anyebhyo matimān anyās tāḥ kanyāḥ sa ca dattavān |
sutā dākṣāyaṇī nāma sarvābhyo ’pi yavīyasī ||
tena tribhuvaneśāya pradattā kṛttivāsase |
sa kadācit kratuṃ kartum ārebhe dharmakarmaṭhaḥ ||
saṃbhṛtāśeṣasaṃbhāraḥ puruhūtapurogamān |
surān ākārayāmāsa tena tatra mahotsave ||
[654]        jāmātaras tu satkṛtya kṛttivāsasamantarā |
āhūtās tena sarve ’pi gīrvāṇagaṇavandinā ||
abhavan ṛtvijas tatra marīcyādyā maharṣayaḥ |
tataḥ pravitate tasminn adhvare bahudakṣiṇe ||
yakṣān suparvaṇaḥ sarvān gandharvān uragān ṛṣīn |
paśyantī dīvyato ’śeṣān vasantī bhavane pituḥ ||
dayitaṃ svam anālokya parimlānamukhāmbujā |
dākṣāyaṇī kṣaṇāt kṣoṇīṃ vilikhantī yadā tadā ||
upetya pitaraṃ sādhvī sādhvasākulalocanā |
ālambamānā mandākṣaṃ mandākṣaram uvāca sā ||
tāta nātaḥ paraḥ kaścid utsavo bhavitā tataḥ |
kasmād asmākam īśo ’sāv etasminn api vismṛtaḥ ||
adyāpi jagatāṃ kartā madbhartāhūyatām iha |
taṃ vinā yajñapuruṣaṃ yajño ’pi tava kīdṛśaḥ ||
ity ukto bālayā dakṣaḥ kopavisphuritādharaḥ |
uvāca vatse bībhatsadarśano ’sau nirākṛtiḥ ||
kāpālikaḥ kathaṃkāraṃ praveśyo yajñabhūmiṣu |
iha tena vayaṃ putri jāmātrā’patrapāmahe ||
pativratānām etāsāṃ paśyantīnāṃ puraḥ katham |
asāv amaṅgalācāraḥ samāneyo digambaraḥ ||
iti roṣeṇa paruṣaṃ dakṣe ’nalpaṃ prajalpati |
pidhāya karṇau tanvaṅgī smarantī smaravairiṇam |
[655]        visasarja tadā prāṇān na vācaṃ śokasaṃyutā ||
tato jagaty aśeṣe ’pi hāhākāro mahān abhūt |
ākarṇya tam aparṇāyā vṛttāntaṃ tripurāntakaḥ |
śucāḥ saṃpīḍyamāno ’pi krodhasyaivābhavad vaśaḥ ||
atha kṣaṇena saṃnaddhāṃ kruddhāṃ gaṇapatākinīm |
puraskṛtya purāṃ bhettā pratasthe duḥsthayaṃ jagat ||
tataḥ pravavṛte yuddhaṃ sārdhaṃ vibudhasainikaiḥ |
dakṣasya pakṣapātena prāptair nārāyaṇādibhiḥ ||
atha rudragaṇaiḥ ruddhāṃ samare ’maravāhinīm |
vavarṣādhikasaṃbaddhāḥ śarāsārair nirantaram ||
ghoraiḥ śastrāstrasaṃpātaiḥ praharan pṛtanād vayam |
sasmāra yogaśakiṃ svām anādinidhanaḥ śivaḥ ||
tataḥ kṣaṇāt parābhūtāḥ prabhutā diviṣadbhaṭāḥ |
kāṃ diśaṃ kāṃ diśaṃ jagmur ākrāntas te samantataḥ ||
asahanta na hantāraṃ nākinas te pinākinam |
tenāvindanta govindapramukhā na tu dhīratām ||
atha yajñapatiṃ trastaṃ kṛṣṇasāravapurdharam |
palāyamānam adrākṣīt bhagavān bhaganetrahā ||
vivyādha tripuravyādhastaṃ śareṇa triparvaṇā |
ciccheda ca śiro vyomni tad enan mṛgaśīrṣakam ||
adyāpi dṛśyate loke lokeśasya yaśodbhavam |
[656]        bhūmau makhakuraṅgasya kabandhaṃ nipapāta yat ||
taṃ vāstupuruṣaṃ prāhur vāstuśāstraviśāradāḥ |
aṅgād gaṅgādharas tasya kṛttim utkṛtya kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
sasarja dhūrjaṭiḥ puṇyaṃ kṛṣṇājinam anuttamam |
abravīc ca virupākṣo dīkṣāsādhanam uttamam ||
idam astu samastānāṃ dānānām api cādhikaṃ |
saṃnidhāpayitā yac ca śrāddhe kṛṣṇājinaṃ naraḥ ||
prāpsyanti pitaras tasya tṛptim ākalpakālikīm |
ity uktvā vāmadevas tu devadāruvanaṃ yayau ||
ity evam idam utpannaṃ medhyaṃ kṛṣṇājinaṃ purā |
etac chrāddhe prayatnena saṃnidhāpyaṃ manīṣibhiḥ ||
etasya saṃnidhānena pitṝṇām akṣayā gatiḥ |
āstīrya dakṣiṇagrīvam etad uttaralomakam |
sarvān śrāddhasya saṃbhārān tasyopari niveśayet ||
brahmavaivarte |
kṛṣṇājinasya sāṃnidhyaṃ darśanaṃ dānam eva ca |
rakṣoghnaṃ brahmavarcasyaṃ paśūn putrāṃś ca tārayet ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
saṃsparśanāt kṛṣṇamṛgājinasya
sarve padārthaḥ śucayo bhavanti |
tasmāt prayatnena niveśanīyaṃ
śrāddhapradeśeṣv idam āgamajñaiḥ ||
iti kṛṣṇajinanirūpaṇam |
atha rajataṃ |
tatra tāvad utpattir nandipurāṇe |
atha devo virūpākṣaḥ pratyālīḍhaṃ samāśritaḥ |
sthānakaṃ śaram ādāya sarvadevamayaṃ tadā ||
ākṛṣya mandaraṃ cāpaṃ vāsukiṃ jyāvanāmitam |
tripurasya vadhārthāya nirnimeṣair vilocanaiḥ ||
tato ’śrukaṇa ekasmād apatat tasya locanāt |
tasmād rūpyaṃ samabhavat saśākhaṃ piṭapojvalam ||
dvitīyād apatan netrād aśrubindus tu vāmakāt |
tasmād rajatam utpannaṃ pitṛkarmasu śaṃsitam ||
atra pitṛkarmasvitiviśeṣaṇād daivakarmasu rajatasya niṣedho gamyate | ata eva matsyapurāṇe rajataṃ prakṛtyoktam |
śivanetrodbhavaṃ yasmāt tasmāt tat pitṛvallabham |
amaṅgalaṃ ca yajñeṣu devakārye vivarjitam |
rajataṃ dakṣiṇām āhuḥ pitṛkāryeṣu sarvadā || iti |
taittirīyaśākhāyām |
devāsurāḥ saṃyatā āsaṃs te devā vijayam upayanto ’gnau vāmaṃ vasu saṃnyadadhante damu no bhaviṣyati yadi no jeṣyantīti tad agnir nyakāmayata tenāpākrāmat te devā vijityāvarurutsamānā anvāyaṃs tadaṃsva sahasāditsanta so ’rodīt yad arodīt tad rudrasya rudratvaṃ | yad aśraśīr yat tad rajataṃ [658] hiraṇyam abhavat tasmād rajataṃ hiraṇyam adakṣiṇyam aśrujaṃ yo barhiṣi dadāti purāsya saṃvatsarād gṛhe rudanti tasmād barhiṣi na deyam || iti |
asyārthaḥ | 'devāś cāsurāś cānyonyāḥ, 'saṃyatāḥ' saṃgrāmodyatāḥ, purā babhūvus tatra vijayakāmāḥ santo devāḥ saṃgrāmabhūmiṃ pratiṣṭhamānāḥ 'vāmaṃ' ramaṇīyatamaṃ, ‘vasu’ dhanaṃ, svakīyaṃ, rakṣaṇaṇayāgnihaste nyadadhur yadi nāmāsurair jitā vayaṃ bhavema tadaitat dhanam asmākaṃ nirvāsāya bhaviṣyatīty anusaṃdhāya, tad idaṃ dhanam agnir atilubdhatayā mamaivedam astv iti nitaram akāmayata, sa tatropāyaṃ vicāryaṃ tasmād deśāt 'apākrāmat' apalāyata | athāsurān vijitya devās tad dhanaṃ 'avarurūtsamānāḥ' avaroddhum avāpnum icchantaḥ, agniṃ ‘anvāyan' agacchan 'tad asya' agneḥ sakāśāt 'sahasā' balena, 'āditsanta' ādātum aicchan, tadāsāv agnir arodīt tato ’sau rudranāmābhavat | tad asya rudataḥ ‘yad aśru' locanajalaṃ, 'aśīryata' bhūmāv apatat, tad rajatam abhavat | tasmāt 'barhiṣi' devadevatye karmaṇi, rajataṃ na deyam iti |
atha rajatamahimā |
nandipurāṇe |
rajatena samāyuktaṃ yad yac chrāddheṣu kiṃcana |
tat tad akṣayam ity uktaṃ rahasyaṃ pitṛbhiḥ svayam ||
athātra rajatābhāve varaṃ rajatasaṃśrayam |
kṛtvā śrāddheṣu prīṇāti pitṝn abdāni ṣoḍaśa ||
skandapurāṇe |
yaḥ prayacchati viprāya rajataṃ cātinirmalam |
[659]        sa vidhūyā pāpāni svargaloke mahīyate |
rūpakaḥ subhagaḥ śrīmān iha loke ca jāyate ||
brahmavaivarte |
rajatasya kathā vāpi darśanaṃ dānam eva ca |
anantam akṣayaṃ svargyaṃ rājataṃ dānam eva ca |
pitṝn etena dānena satputrās tārayanty uta ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
dhātūṃs tu sṛjatā pūrvaṃ raupyaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ svayaṃbhuvā
tena tad vihitā śrāddhe dakṣiṇā pitṛtṛptaye ||
alābhe sati rūpyasya nāmāni parikīrtayet |
tuṣyanti pitaro rājan kīrtanād rajatasya ca ||
na hi rūpyāt paraṃ kiṃcit pitṝṇāṃ vidyate priyam |
rūpyaṃ dṛṣṭvaiva pitaro yānti prītim anuttamām ||
nāradīyapurāṇe |
ya icchet paramāṃ tṛptiṃ pitṝṇāṃ cākṣayāṃ gatim |
tena śrāddheṣu yatnena rūpyaṃ deyaṃ dvijottama ||
rūpyaṃ hastena dātavyaṃ yat kiṃcit pitṛdaivatam |
tena syuḥ pitaras tṛptā yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
rajataṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāc chrāddhakarmaṇi yo naraḥ |
pitṝn etena tena syur iṣṭāḥ pitṛpitāmahāḥ ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
tarjanyā rajataṃ dhṛtvā pitṛbhyo yat pradīyate |
[660]        anto ’sti paramāṇūnāṃ tasyānto naiva vidyate ||
rājatāny arghyapātrāṇi kārayet pitṛkarmaṇi |
tathā bhojanapātrāṇi kuryād rūpyamayāni tu ||
pariveṣaṇapātrāṇi piṇḍapātrāṇi caiva hi |
rūpyeṇaiva prakurvīta pitṛbhaktiparo naraḥ ||
evaṃ kṛtaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ yatra kutra sthitena hi |
tad gayāśrāddham eva syān nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
rajataṃ dakṣiṇaṃ dattvā śrāddheṣu prayato naraḥ |
ātmānaṃ ca pitṝṃś caiva nayati brahma śāśvatam ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
ruṣyaṃ pitṝṇām ativallabhaṃ tat
dattvā naro vallabhatām upaiti |
somasya loke labhate sa tāvad
dhruve nibaddhā ṛṣayo hi yāvat ||
'dhruve nibaddhā ṛṣayaḥ' saptarṣisaṃjñakā ṛṣayaḥ | rajatadānam atra dakṣiṇādirūpeṇa śrāddhabrāhmaṇebhyo veditavyam | rajatabhājanapraśaṃsā tu bhājanaprakaraṇe vakṣyate |
iti rajatanirūpaṇam |
atra hiraṇya |
tatra śatapathaśrutiḥ |
aśvasya vā ālabdhasya reta udakrāmat tat suvarṇaṃ hiraṇyam abhavat || iti |
tathā | [661]
agni vā āpo ’bhidadhyau mithunyābhiḥ syām iti tāḥ saṃbabhūva tāsu retaḥ prāsiñcat tad dhiraṇyam abhavat tasmād etad agnisaṃkāśam agner hi retas tasmād apsu bindanty apsu hi prāsiñcat tasmād enena na dhāvayati na kiṃcana karoty apayaśo devaretasaṃ hi tad yaśaś caivenam etat samarddhayati |
skānde kāśīkhaṇḍe |
bhagavān uvāca |
maheśvarasya yad retaḥ pavitraṃ śukram uttamam |
samākṛṣṭaṃ purā gauryā durādharṣatamāt pateḥ ||
devatānāṃ hitārthāya tad agnau prākṣipat purā |
mukhenaivāpibad retaḥ śukraṃ caiva pinākinaḥ ||
tato jvalati vai vahniḥ kāṣṭham adhyasyito yathā |
tad auṣṇyena parīto ’sau gaṅgāyāṃ prākṣipat punaḥ ||
kvāthitvā ca punar gaṅgā sasamudrasaridgaṇā |
bhūmyāṃ nikhanitā sātha parvatair abhyapātayat ||
taptāyaḥpiṇḍavadbhūmiḥ sadvīpakulaparvatā |
tatas tad aiśvaraṃ retaḥ svarasena sapāvakam ||
dagdhvā samastaṃ bhuvanaṃ yātaṃ śaravanaṃ prati |
tataḥ skandaḥ samabhavat skandasaṃparki yaj jalam ||
tat svacchaṃ svarṇamabhavadvipruṣo yāḥ samutthitāḥ |
svedakāś cāpatan merāv āsīd ādyantasaṃyutaḥ ||
vṛkṣagulmalatāyukto meruḥ paramakāñcanam |
dadhitthaḥ samabhūt prānte jambūpakvakvacid gireḥ ||
tasya mūlena saṃyukto vyavardhata mahātaruḥ |
[662]        tasya parvatamātrāṇi phalāni laghujīvanaḥ ||
mahāsvādūni nyapatan sarvartuṣv aviśeṣataḥ |
teṣāṃ rasaughān mahataḥ sarid āsīt samudragā ||
jāmbūnadauti vikhyātā purāṇe taralārkikā |
jājvalyamānā satataṃ vahnijvālāsamauṣṇataḥ ||
draṣṭuṃ na śakyate kaiścid daśayojanam āyatā |
tīramṛt tadrasaṃ prāptā hematvam abhigacchati ||
tatsaṃsaktakasaṃsaktā tadrasād bhūṣitāpi vā |
mṛttikā gṛhyate loke variṣṭhaṃ svarṇam icchati ||
tat saṃsaktaṃ yad apy anyat kāñcanaṃ parikīrtyate |
etat pavitraṃ hemābhūt purā dharmyam anuttamam ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
himavadduhitā pūrvam umānāmneti viśrutā |
pūrvajanmani sā cāsīd dakṣasya tanayā satī ||
dakṣakopāc ca tatyāja satī cātmakalevaram |
himavadduhitā jajñe loke gaurīti viśrutā ||
dattā tenāpi sā tasmai praṇipatya pinākine |
vivāhaṃ kārayāmāsa vidhivad duhitus tadā ||
vaivāhikena vidhinā juhāvāgniṃ pitāmahaḥ |
homaṃ ca kurvatas tasya vikāro mānmatho ’bhavat ||
rūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā tadā devyā retaskannaṃ mahātmanaḥ |
padbhyāṃ ca mṛditaṃ tena bahudhā samapadyata ||
[663]        tatrotpannā mahātmāno bālakhilyā marīcipāḥ |
agnau ca śeṣam apatat tac cābhūj jvalanaprabham (?) ||
jājvalyamānadīptyā ca agnim adhyagataṃ tathā |
śaśaṃsire tato devā dṛṣṭvā putraṃ vibhāvasoḥ ||
jagṛhuḥ śirasā caiva puṣyatvāt te mudānvitāḥ |
vibhūṣaṇaṃ ca devāś ca cakrur uttamatejasaḥ ||
atha hiraṇyamahimā |
tatra śatapathaśrutiḥ |
devarūpam amṛtaṃ hiraṇyam iti |
skānde kāśikhaṇḍe |
īśitur hṛdayāj jātaṃ bhoktṝṇāṃ ca priyaṃ dhanam |
samudrasya tathā meror jambūnadyās taṭasya ca ||
tasmād devamanuṣyāṇāṃ pavitraṃ priyam uttamam |
yat pavitraṃ pavitrāṇāṃ tad dhiraṇyam udāhṛtam ||
śirasā dhāryate devair īśvareṇāpi kiṃ paraiḥ |
dhāraṇāt sparśanād dānād darśanāt kīrtanād api ||
liṅgārcena samaṃ puṇyaṃ mayāpi triyate yataḥ |
haraṇān nāśanād dveṣād avamānād ayaṃ tataḥ ||
mahat prajāyate hema śiro naṣṭaṃ hi puṣkalam |
tasmād eva hy abhūd aṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ tv ādisargikam ||
ato hemamayaṃ kuryād aṅgulīyakasaṃjñakam |
pavitraṃ paramaṃ hy etad anāmāṅgulisaṃjñakam ||
[664]        anyāni ca pavitrāṇi kuśadūrvātmakāni vai |
hemātmakapavitrasya hy ekāṃ nārhanti vai kalām ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
jāmbūnadaṃ tad devānām indragopakasaṃnibham |
pitṝṇāṃ candraraśmyābhaṃ daityānāṃ śabalopamam ||
nāgānāṃ tat śukābhaṃ syān nārīṇāṃ pītam ucyate |
vahnipurāṇe suvarṇaṃ prakṛtya rāmā uvāca |
etat pavitram atulaṃ saṃbhūtam iha viśrutam |
śambhor bījaṃ paraṃ hy etad apatyaṃ jātavedasaḥ ||
sahajaṃ kārtikeyasya rudraśukraṣamudbhavam |
pavitratvāt suraiḥ sarvair dhāryate mukuṭādiṣu ||
agnis tu devatāḥ sarvāḥ suvarṇaṃ ca tadātmakam |
tasmāt suvarṇaṃ dadatāṃ prītāḥ syuḥ sarvadevatāḥ ||
daśa pūrvān parāṃś caiva narakāt tārayanti te |
suvarṇaṃ ye prayacchanti provācedaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ ||
sarvān kāmān prayacchanti ye prayacchanti kāñcanam |
marīcir bhagavān pūrvaṃ pitāmahasuto ’bravīt ||
yaḥ suvarṇaṃ naro nityaṃ brāhmaṇebhyaḥ prayacchati |
sa ciraṃ virajā vidvān devavad divi modate ||
sarveṣām eva dānānām ekajanmānugaṃ phalam |
hāṭakaṃ tv iti gaurīṇāṃ saptajanmānugaṃ phalam ||
kṛtvāpi sumahat pāpaṃ jātarūpaṃ dadāti yaḥ |
[665]        sa sadyas tena pāpena mucyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
suvarṇaṃ paramaṃ dānaṃ suvarṇaṃ dakṣiṇā parā |
pavitrāṇāṃ pavitraṃ ca dānānāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
pavitraṃ paramaṃ loke suvarṇena samaṃ kvacit |
na vidyate dravyajātaṃ dānaṃ vā bhūṣaṇaṃ tathā ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gṛhādike puṇyaphalaṃ bhaven mūlyānusārataḥ |
tasmāt sarvapradānānāṃ hiraṇyam adhikaṃ smṛtam ||
tathā santānikādīnāṃ hemnāṃ saṃpadyate kriyā |
tathā na gṛhadānena hiraṇyam adhikaṃ tataḥ ||
vedavyāsaḥ |
sarvān kāmān prayacchanti ye yacchanti kāñcanam |
evaṃ hi bhagavān atriḥ pitāmahasutābravīt ||
pavitram atha vā puṇyaṃ pitṝṇām akṣayaṃ ca tat |
suvarṇaṃ manujendreṇa manunā saṃprakīrttitaṃ ||
manuḥ |
bhūmidaḥ sarvam āpnoti dīrgham āyur hiraṇyadaḥ |
saṃvartaḥ |
hiraṇyado mahāvṛddhiṃ dīrgham āyuś ca vindati |
viṣṇuḥ |
suvarṇadānenāgnisālokyam āpnoti |
[666]        viśvāmitraḥ |
yatrāste likhitā gehe svarṇadānasya saṃśrutiḥ |
rakṣobhūtapiśācās taṃ na paśyanti tu pārthiva ||
devīpurāṇe |
jñātvā mānaṃ suvarṇādeḥ pātreṣu pratipādayet |
kāmān iṣṭān avāpnoti vidhiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ ||
vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
tasmād agnimukhāḥ sarvadevatā iti śuśruma |
brahmaṇo hi prasūto ’gnir agner api ca kāñcanam ||
tasmād eva prayacchanti hiraṇyaṃ sarvadaivatam |
tasya vai vaidhasā lokā gacchataḥ paramāṃ gatim |
svargaloke ca rājyena so ’bhiṣicyeta bhārgava ||
suvarṇam ity anuvṛttau kālikāpurāṇe |
pūtam etat paraṃ puṇyaṃ surakāryeṣu nityaśaḥ |
dhārayanti yataḥ sarve tv amalārthaṃ malojjhitam ||
ataḥ sarvāṇi pāpāni pātrebhyo vidhipūrvakam |
dattaṃ punāti kāle ca deśe ca harasaṃnidhau ||
mahābhārate bhīmaṃ prati pitṛvākyam |
vedopaniṣade caiva sarvakarmasu dakṣiṇam |
sarvakratuṣu coddiṣṭā bhūmigāvo ’tha kāñcanam ||
tataḥ śrutis tu paramā suvarṇaṃ dakṣiṇeti vai |
tad idaṃ samyag ārabdhaṃ tvayādya bharatarṣabha ||
[667]        kiṃ tu bhūmer gavāṃ cārthe suvarṇaṃ dīyatām iti |
evaṃ vayaṃ ca dharmajñāḥ sarve cāsmatpitāmahāḥ |
tāritā vai tariṣyanti pāvanaṃ paramaṃ hi tat ||
daśa pūrvān daśaivānyāṃs tathā saṃtārayanti te |
suvarṇaṃ ye prayacchanti evaṃ te pitaro ’bruvan |
rāmaṃ prati vasiṣṭhavākyāni |
sarvaratnāni nirmathya tejorāśisamanvitam |
suvarṇam ebhyo viprendra ratnaṃ paramam uttamam ||
etasmāt kāraṇād devā gandharvoragarākṣasāḥ |
manuṣyāś ca piśācāś ca prayatnād dhārayanti tat ||
mukuṭair aṅgadayutair alaṃkāraiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ |
suvarṇaracitair atra virājante raghūttama ||
tasmāt sarvapavitrebhyaḥ pavitraṃ paramaṃ smṛtam |
pṛthivīṃ gāṃ ca datveha tathānyad api kiṃcana ||
viśiṣyate suvarṇasya dānaṃ paramakaṃ vibho |
akṣayaṃ pāvanaṃ caiva suvarṇam iha kathyate ||
prayacched dvijamukhyebhyaḥ pāvanaṃ hy etad uttamam |
suvarṇam eva sarvatra dakṣiṇāsu vidhīyate |
suvarṇaṃ ye prayacchanti sarvadās te bhavanty utaḥ ||
nandipurāṇe |
kṛṣṇalāḥ pañca māṣas tu māṣaiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ smṛtam |
suvarṇam ekaṃ yaddānāt svargalokam avāpnuyāt ||
[668]        suvarṇaṃ paramaṃ dānaṃ suvarṇaṃ dakṣiṇā parā |
etat pavitraṃ paramam etat svastyayanaṃ mahat ||
daśapūrvān parān vaṃśyān ātmānaṃ ca viśāmpate |
api pāpaśataṃ kṛtvā datvā vipreṣu tārayet ||
suvarṇaṃ ye prayacchanti narāḥ śuddhena cetasā |
devatās te na saṃdehaḥ samastā iti naḥ śrutam ||
agnir hi devatāḥ sarvāḥ suvarṇaṃ ca hutāśanaḥ |
tasmāt suvarṇaṃ dadatā dattāḥ sarvāś ca devatāḥ |
agnyabhāve ca kurvanti vahnisthāneṣu kāñcanam |
sarvavedapramāṇajñāḥ śrutiśāstranidarśanāt ||
ye tv enaṃ kṣālayitvāgnim ādityodayanaṃ prati |
dadyur vai vratam uddiśya sarvakāmān avāpnuyuḥ ||
'vratam udiśya' vratam anusaṃdhāyety arthaḥ |
suvarṇadaḥ svargaloke kāmān iṣṭān samaśnute |
virajo ’mbarasaṃvītaḥ pariyāti yatas tataḥ ||
vimānenārkavarṇena bhāsvareṇa virājitaḥ |
apsarogaṇakīrṇena bhāsvatā svena tejasā |
haṃsabarhiṇayuktena kāmagena narottamaḥ |
divyagandhavahaḥ svarge parigacchann itas tataḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
[669]        agner apatyaṃ prathamaṃ suvarṇaṃ
bhūr vaiṣṇavī sūryasutāś ca gāvaḥ |
lokās trayas tena bhavanti dattāḥ
yaḥ kāñcanaṃ gāṃ ca mahīṃ ca dadyāt ||
skānde prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
dravyaṃ pṛthivyāṃ paramaṃ suvarṇaṃ
hṛṣyanti devā yadi dīyate tat |
sūrye ’pi tasmai suciraṃ vimānaṃ
dadāti tāvad bhramate ’tra yāvat ||
iti hiraṇyanirūpaṇaṃ |
atha pātrāṇi |
tatrārghādyarthapātrāṇi hārītaḥ |
kāṃsyapārṇarājatatāmrapātrāṇy arghyodakadhāraṇāni kharvāṇy upakalpyāni |
'kharvāṇi' hrasvāni |
baijavāpaḥ |
khādiraudumbarāṇy arghyapātrāṇi śrāddhakarmaṇi |
apy aśmamṛṇamayāni syur api parṇapuṭās tathā || iti |
‘khādiraṃ’ bālapatratarudārumayam | 'audumbaraṃ' jantuphalavṛkṣanirmitaṃ,
tāmramayaṃ vā | 'parṇapuṭāḥ' yajñīyavṛkṣaparṇanirmitāś camasākṛtayaḥ pātraviśeṣāḥ |
kātyāyanaḥ | [670]
sauvarṇarājataudumbarakhaḍgamaṇimayānāṃ pātrāṇām anyatameṣu mṛṇmayavarjaṃ yāni vā vidyante palāśapatrapuṭeṣu vā |
‘khaḍgamayaṃ' gaṇḍakaśṛṅganirmitam | atra mṛṇamayavarjanaṃ sati saṃbhave | asaṃbhave tu tad apy upādeyaṃ taijasāśmamayamṛṇmayeṣv ity āśvalāyanavacanāt | athāśmamṛṇmayāni syur iti baijavāpavacanāt śākhābhedena vyavasthite vā mṛṇmayavarjanopādāne ’vagantavye |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
dauhitraṃ khaḍgaśṛṅgaṃ tu lalāṭe yat tu dṛśyate |
tasya śṛṅgasya yat pātraṃ dauhitram iti kīrtitam ||
matsyapurāṇe |
pātraṃ vanaspatimayaṃ tathā parṇamayaṃ punaḥ |
jalajaṃ vāpi kurvīta tathā sāgarasaṃbhavam |
sauvaṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ pitṝṇāṃ pātram uttamam ||
puṣpaṃ vinā ye phalanti te vanaspatayaḥ tad dārumayaṃ 'vanaspatimayam' | parṇamayam ity atra parṇāny api vanaspatīnām eva | 'jalajaṃ' kamalinīpatram | ‘sāgaramayaṃ’ śaṅkhaśuktyādi |
rajatasya kathā vāpi darśanaṃ dānam eva vā |
rājatair bhājanair deyam api vā rajatānvitaiḥ ||
vāry api śraddhayā dattam akṣayyāyaiva kalpyate |
yac cārghyapiṇḍabhojye ’pi pitṝṇāṃ rajataṃ matam ||
śivanetrodbhavaṃ yasmāt tasmāt tat pitṛvallabham |
amaṅgalaṃ ca yajñeṣu devakāryeṣu varjitam |
[671]        evaṃ pātrāṇi saṃkalpya yathālābhaṃ vimatsaraḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
pālāśe brahmavarcasyam aśvatthe rājyabhāgitā |
sarvabhūtādhipatyaṃ ca plakṣe nityam udāhṛtam ||
'plakṣaḥ' jaṭī |
puṣṭiḥ prajāś ca nyagrodhe buddhiḥ prajñā dhṛtiḥ smṛtiḥ |
'nyagrodhaḥ' vaṭaḥ |
rakṣoghnaṃ ca yaśasyaṃ ca kāryāḥ pātram ucyate |
'kāśmarī' śrīparṇī |
saubhāgyam uttamaṃ loke madhūke samudāhṛtam |
'madhūkaḥ' guḍapuṣpaḥ |
phalgupātreṇa kurvāṇaḥ sarvān kāmān avāpnuyāt |
'phalguḥ' kākodumbarikā |
parāṃ dyutim athārke ca prākāmyaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
'arkaḥ' rūpikā |
bilve lakṣmīs tapo medhā nityam āyuṣyam eva ca |
kṣetrārāmataḍāgeṣu sarvasasyeṣu caiva hi ||
varṣaty ajasraṃ tatraiva parjanyo veṇupātrataḥ |
eteṣāṃ labhate puṇyaṃ sauvarṇe rājate tathā ||
palāśaphalgunyagrodhaplakṣāśvatthavikaṅkatāḥ |
udumbaras tathā bilvaś candano yajñiyāś ca ye |
saralo devadāruś ca śālo ’tha khadiras tathā |
[672]        ete hy arghyādipātrāṇāṃ yonayaḥ syur aśeṣataḥ |
yas tair dadyāt sa yajñānāṃ sarveṣāṃ phalam āpnuyāt ||
'vikaṅkataḥ' sruvakṣaḥ, ‘saralaḥ' pītadruḥ, 'śālaḥ' sarjakaḥ | arghyādīty ādiśabdenāvanejanādipātrāṇi tilakuśādiśrāddhopakaraṇadhāraṇāthīni pātrāṇi ca gṛhyante |
tasminn eva purāṇe |
rājataṃ rajatāktaṃ vā pitṝṇāṃ pātram ucyate |
rajatasya kathā vāpi darśanaṃ dānam eva ca ||
anantam akṣayaṃ svargyaṃ rājataṃ dānam eva ca |
pitṝn etena dānena satputrās tārayanty uta ||
rājate hi sudhāṃ dugdhvā pātre ’smin pṛthivī purā |
sudhākṣayārthibhis tāta tasmin dattaṃ tato ’kṣayam ||
atha bhojanapātrāṇi |
vāyupurāṇe |
pātraṃ vai taijasaṃ dadyān manojñaṃ śrāddhabhojane |
pātraṃ bhavati kāmānāṃ vidyānāṃ ca dhanasya ca ||
'taijasaṃ' suvarṇarajatakāṃsyatāmramayam |
rājataṃ kāñcanaṃ caiva dadyāc chrāddheṣu yaḥ pumān |
datvā sa labhate dātā prākāmyaṃ dhanam eva ca ||
rājatair bhājanair eṣām api vā rajatānvitaiḥ |
vāry api śraddhayā dattam akṣayyāyopakalpyate ||
tatra yady apy aviśeṣeṇa rājatāni sauvarṇani ca pātrāṇi śrāddhe [673] vihitāni tathāpi vaiśvadevike sauvarṇāni pitre rājatānīti guṇaprakarṣārthaṃ vyavasthām āhātriḥ |
bhojane haimarūpyāṇi deve pitrye yathākramam |
viṣṇuḥ |
taijasāni ca pātrāṇi dadyād viśeṣato rājatāni |
atra haimapātrābhāve hārītaḥ |
rājatakāṃsyapārṇatāmrapātrāṇi brāhmaṇabhojanārthāni mahānti kāryāṇi bhojanārthopakalpitasarvānnadhāraṇaparyāptaṃ mahattvamātram atrānusaṃdheyam | kāṃsyapātraṃ tu dvipañcāśatpalaparimitakāṃsyanirmitam eva grāhyaṃ tadasaṃbhave yathopapannam |
tathāca atriḥ |
pañcāśatpalikaṃ kāṃsyaṃ hy adhikaṃ bhojanāya vai |
gṛhasthais tu sadā kāryam abhāve hemarūpyayoḥ ||
'gṛhasthair' iti gṛhasthāśramibhir ity arthaḥ | parṇamayaṃ cātra bhojanapātraṃ palāśapatram eva grāhyam | tadvyatiriktasya patrapātrasyātriṇā pratiṣiddhatvāt |
tad yathā |
pālāśebhyo vinā na syur anyapatrāṇi bhojane |
iti viśeṣitatvād dārvādiṣu tu na pratiṣedhaḥ | parṇapātrapatre ’pi ghṛtādidravyadhāraṇārthaṃ, sati saṃbhave taijasāni saṃpādyāni | ghṛtādidāne taijasāni pātrāṇīti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | teṣām alābhe palāśapatranirmitāḥ puṭakāḥ ghṛtādidravyadhāraṇārthaṃ viniyojyāḥ |
vārāhapurāṇe |
[674]        sauvarṇānīha pātrāṇi saṃpādyāni prayatnataḥ |
tadabhāve tu rūpyāṇi kāṃsyāni tadasaṃbhave ||
palāśaparṇajāni syuḥ śrāddheṣu dvijabhojane |
anyāny api hi pātrāṇi dārujāny api jānatā ||
yathopapannaṃ kāryāṇi mṛṇmayāni na tu kvacit |
nāyasāny api kurvīta paittalāni na caiva hi |
na ca sīsamayānīha śasyante trapujāny api ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
pātre tu mṛṇamaye yas tu śrāddhe bhojayate pitṝn |
tatra dātā purodhāś ca bhoktā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
pātre tu mṛṇamaye yo vai śrāddhe bhojayate pitṝn |
sa yāti narakaṃ ghoraṃ bhoktā ca sapurohitaḥ || iti |
aṅgirāḥ |
na jātīkusumāni dadyān na kadalīpatram || iti |
atha pariveṣaṇapātrāṇi |
hārītaḥ |
kāñcanena tu pātreṇa rājataudumbareṇa vā |
dattam akṣayatāṃ yāti khaḍgapātreṇa caiva hi ||
viṣṇuḥ |
ghṛtādidāne taijasāni pātrāṇi khaḍgapātrāṇi phalgupātrāṇi vā praśastāni |
[675]        atra ca gāthā bhavati |
sauvarṇarājatābhyāṃ ca khaḍgenaudumbareṇa vā |
dattam akṣayatāṃ yāti phalgupātreṇa vāpy atha ||
atra viśeṣam āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
daive sauvarṇāni śreṣṭhāni pitrye tu rājatāni pariveṣaṇapātrāṇīti |
manuḥ |
rājatair bhājanair eṣām api vā rajatānvitaiḥ |
vāry api śraddhayā dattam akṣayyāyopakalpate ||
avaśyaṃ cedaṃ pariveṣaṇaṃ darvyādinā pātreṇaiva vidheyaṃ sākṣād dhastena pariveṣaṇasya niṣedhaśravaṇāt |
kūrmapurāṇe |
na dadyād riktahastena pratyakṣaṃ lavaṇaṃ tathā |
na cāyasena pātreṇa na caivāśraddhayānvitaḥ ||
kāñcanena tu pātreṇa rājataudumbareṇa ca |
dattam akṣayatāṃ yāti khaḍgena ca viśeṣataḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ |
pratyakṣalavaṇaṃ na dadyād istena na ghṛtavyañjanādi dadyāt |
pātrādyabhāve tṛṇena parṇena vā hastam antardhāya pradeyam ity āha vṛddhaśātātapaḥ|
hastadattāś ca ye snehā lavaṇaṃ vyañjanāni ca |
dātāraṃ nopatiṣṭhanti bhoktā bhuñjīta kilbiṣam ||
tasmād antaritaṃ deyaṃ parṇenaiva tṛṇena vā |
[676]        pradadyān na tu hastena nāyasena kadācana ||
vṛddhaśātātapa-laghuhārīta-vasiṣṭhāḥ |
āyasena tu pātreṇa yad annaṃ saṃpradīyate |
bhoktā viṣṭhāśanaṃ bhuṅkte dātā ca narakaṃ vrajet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
sīsakāyasapātrāṇi rītiśeṣāṇi pātrāṇi cety ayajñiyāni |
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
nāpavitreṇa hastena na naikena vinā kuśam |
nāyase nāyasenaiva śrāddhe tu pariveṣayet ||
'apavitreṇa' durlepasaṃsargādinā apūtena | 'āyasena' ayomayadarvyādinā | 'āyase' ayomayapātre ’pi na pariveṣayet |
atha pacanabhāṇḍāni
camakārakhaṇḍe |
svarṇabhāṇḍeṣu kurvīta pitṝṇaṃ pākam uttamam |
tenāsau bhāgyam āyuṣyaṃ labhate cākṣayāṃ śriyam ||
rājateṣu tu bhāṇḍeṣu kurvan pākaṃ tu paitṛkam |
pitṝṇāṃ kurute tṛptiṃ yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
pacamānas tu bhāṇḍeṣu bhaktyā tāmramayeṣu tu |
samuddharati vai ghorāt pitṝn duḥkhamahārṇavāt ||
taijasānām abhāve tu piṭhare mṛṇmaye tu yaḥ |
nave śucau prakurvīta pākaṃ pitrartham ādarāt |
tasya tuṣyanti pitaraḥ prītā yacchanti vāñchitam ||
[677]        nāgarakhaṇḍe |
śucīni pitṛpākārthaṃ piṭharāṇi prakalpayet |
sauvarṇāny atha raupyāṇi tāmrakāṃsyodbhavāni ca ||
mārttikāny api bhavyāni nūtanāni dṛḍhāni ca |
na kadācit paced annam ayaḥsthālīṣu paitṛkam ||
ayaso darśanād eva pitaro vidravanti hi |
ayajñiyam amaṅgalyaṃ loham āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
deveṣu caiva pitryeṣu garhitaṃ sarvakarmasu |
kālāyasaṃ viśeṣeṇa ninditaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi ||
phalānāṃ caiva śākānāṃ chedanārthāni yāni tu |
mahānase ’pi śastrāṇāṃ teṣām eva hi saṃnidhiḥ ||
iṣyate netarasyātra śastramātrasya darśanam |
śrāddhadeśe tu viduṣāṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītim icchatā |
mahānasopayuktānām api kāryaṃ nidarśanam |
ayaḥśaṅkumayaṃ pīṭhaṃ pradeyaṃ nopaveśanam ||
ādityapurāṇe |
paced annāni susnātaḥ pātreṣu śuciṣu svayam |
svarṇādidhātujāteṣu mṛṇmayeṣv api vā budhaḥ ||
acchidreṣu vilepeṣu tathānupahateṣu ca |
nāyaseṣu na bhinneṣu dūṣiteṣv api karhicit ||
pūrvaṃ kṛtopayogeṣu mṛṇmayeṣu tu na kvacit |
pakvānnasthāpanārthaṃ tu śasyante dārujāny api ||
[678]        darvyādīny api kāryāṇi yajñiyair eva dārubhiḥ |
iti pātranirūpaṇam |
atha gandhāḥ |
garuḍapurāṇe |
yas tu gandhān pitṝṇāṃ vai dadyād āmodito nṛpa |
gandharvāpsarasāṃ lokān sa prāpya divi modate ||
gandhā yathopacāreṣu pitṝṇām ativallabhāḥ |
na tathā vidyate hy anyad ato deyāḥ prayatnataḥ ||
viśveṣām api devānām atyantaṃ prītihetavaḥ |
gandhās tasmāt prayatnena gandhais tān api pūjayet ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
yaḥ śrāddheṣu pradadyād vai gandhān atimanoharān |
svarge varāṅgarāgās tam āliṅganti surāṅganāḥ ||
yo ’nulimpati gātrāṇi śrāddhaṃ kurvan dvijanmanām |
devāś ca pitaraś caiva tasya tuṣyanti sarvadā ||
bhagavatīpurāṇe |
yaś candanena śuddhena suślakṣṇena sugandhinā |
anulimpati vai viprān sa kadācin na tapyate ||
tenaivāgurumiśreṇa paraṃ saubhāgyam aśnute |
aguros tu vilepena bhuṅkte bhogān amānuṣān ||
yaḥ śrīkhaṇḍaṃ sakarpūraṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pratipādayet |
[679]        yaśaḥ prāpnoti vipulaṃ sa pṛthivyāṃ mahāmatiḥ ||
yaḥ kuṅkumasamopetaṃ dadāti malayodbhavam |
kevalaṃ kuṅkumaṃ vāpi rūpavān sa prajāyate ||
dadyān malayajaṃ divyaṃ yas tu kastūrikānvitam |
kasturīṃ kevalāṃ vāpi sa prāpnoti mahāśriyam ||
yo yakṣakardamaṃ datte śrāddheṣu śraddhayānvitaḥ |
sa bhūpatitvam āsādya mahendra iva modate ||
yas tu śrāddhe daded gandhān nānākusumavāsitān |
sa sarvakāmasaṃpannaḥ svarge vaimāniko bhavet ||
yakṣakardamalakṣaṇam
karpūrāgurukakkoladarpakuṅkumacandanaiḥ |
yakṣakardama ity ukto gandhaḥ svarge ’pi durlabhaḥ ||
iti viṣṇudharmottaroktam anusaṃdheyam |
viṣṇuḥ |
candanakuṅkumakarpūrāgurupadmakāny anulepanāya |
mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
candanāgurukarpūrakuṅkumāni pradāpayet |
aśvamedham avāpnoti pitṝṇām anulepanāt ||
marīciḥ |
karpūrakuṅkumopetaṃ sugandhisitacandanam |
daivike tv atha vā pitrye gandhadānaṃ praśasyate ||
brahmapurāṇe |
śvetacandanakarpūrakuṅkumāni śubhāni ca |
vilepanārthaṃ dadyāt tu yac cānyat pitṛvallabham ||
[680]        kuṣṭhaṃ māṃsī bālakaṃ ca tvakkuṣṭhī jātipatrakam |
nālikośīramustaṃ ca granthiparṇaṃ ca dumbaram |
murā cety evamādīni gandhayogyāni paitṛke ||
atha varjyā gandhāḥ |
viṣṇupurāṇe |
śrāddhe na deyā pālaṅkyā tathā niṣpāvakodbhavāḥ |
'pālaṅkyā' mukundākhyaṃ gandhadravyam,
bālakaṃ cātha pālaṅkyā mukundaḥ kundakundurū |
iti trikāṇḍīsmaraṇāt |
nṛsiṃhapurāṇe |
śrāddheṣu viniyoktavyā na gandhā jīrṇadārujāḥ |
śuklībhāvaṃ samāsādya na ca paryuṣitāḥ kvacit |
na vigandhāś ca dātavyā bhuktaśeṣā viśeṣataḥ ||
nāradīyapurāṇe |
jīrṇaṃ vikṛtagandhaṃ ca gandhaṃ śrāddhe na yojayet |
pitṛkarmaṇi tajjñena pradeyaṃ na ca varvaraṃ ||
brahmapurāṇe |
pūtīkaṃ mṛganābhiṃ ca rocanāṃ raktacandanam |
kālīyakaṃ joṅgakaṃ ca turuṣkaṃ cāpi varjayet ||
'pūtīkaṃ' sugandhitṛṇaviśeṣaḥ, "pūtikaṃ karañjakāṣṭhaṃ vā prakīryaḥ pūtikarajaḥ pūtikaḥ kalikāraka" ity amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt| ‘mṛganābhiḥ’ kasturī, tasyāś ca vihitapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpaḥ | 'rocanā' gorocanā | 'raktacandanaṃ' prasiddham | ‘kālīyakajoṅgake' gandhadravyaviśeṣau | kālīyakaṃ ca [681] kālānusāryaṃ cātha samānārthakaṃ, "vaṃśakāgururājārhalohaṃ kṛmijajoṅgakam" iti sugandhadravyaprakaraṇe amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt | 'turuṣkaḥ' sihlakaḥ |
iti gandhanirupaṇam |
atha puṣpāṇi |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
pitṛbhyo yaḥ sumanasaḥ sumandhas tatparāyaṇaḥ |
sadā dadyād dvijātibhyaḥ so ’pi yāti divākaram ||
vāyupurāṇe |
pitṛbhyo yas tu mālyāni sugandhīni ca dāpayet |
sadā dadyāt śriyā yuktaḥ so ’pi yāti divākaram ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
yas tu śrāddhe dvijāgryāṇāṃ puṣpāṇi pratipādayet |
sugandhīni manājñāni tasya syāt puṇyam akṣayam ||
sugandhipuṣpagandhena prītiṃ yānti pitāmahāḥ |
prīyamāṇāś ca yacchanti dātuḥ kāmān yathepsitān ||
ataḥ puṣpaiś ca patraiś ca mañjarībhir athāpi vā |
sukumāraiḥ kiśalayair yavadūrvāṅkurair api |
saṃpūjanīyāḥ pitaraḥ śreyaḥkāmena sarvadā ||
skandapurāṇe |
gandhamālyapriyā nityaṃ pitaraḥ śrāddhadevatāḥ |
ato gandhaiś ca puṣpaiś ca pūjayet tān sugandhibhiḥ ||
śreṣṭhāni sarvabhogeṣu puṣpāṇīha paratra ca |
[682]        ato bhaktyā pradeyāni pitṝṇāṃ kusumāni vai ||
viparītaphalaṃ loke yad asatkṛtya dīyate |
ataḥ satkṛtya saṃpūjya dānam icchanti sūrayaḥ ||
na prasūnair vinā pūjā kṛtā puṇatamā bhavet |
ataḥ śrāddhaparo vidvān śrāddhena prīṇayet pitṝn ||
saṃpūjayīta prathamaṃ puṣpaiḥ sarvottamaiḥ sadā |
aparyuṣitarūpaiś ca tathānupahatair api |
kṛmikīṭādyaviddhaiś ca śucibhiḥ prokṣitair api ||
padmapurāṇe |
yavanīvāramudgāś ca śuklāḥ sumanasas tathā |
vallabhāni praśastāni pitṝṇām iha sarvadā ||
nandipurāṇe |
puṣpajātir yadā sṛṣṭā tadā prāk śatapatrikā |
sṛṣṭā tena ca mukhyā sā śrāddhakarmaṇi sarvadā ||
vāyu-brahmāṇḍapurāṇyoḥ |
śuklāḥ sumanasaḥ śreṣṭhās tathā padmotpalāni ca |
gandhavarṇopapannāni yāni cānyāni kṛtsnaśaḥ ||
atra padmotpalāni śukletarāṇy api deyāni |
atra |
jalajāni raktāny api dadyāt |
prabhāsakhaṇḍa-mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇayoḥ |
jātyaś ca sarvā dātavyā mallikā śvetayūthikā |
[683]        jalodbhavāni sarvāṇi kusumāni ca campakam ||
‘jātyaḥ’ mālatyaḥ | ‘mallikā' mudgarākhyāni puṣpāṇi | ‘śvetayūthikā' hemakusumāni |
dhānyaphalaśākāny abhidhāya brahmapurāṇe |
śākam āraṇyakaṃ caiva dadyāt puṣpāṇy amūni ca |
jātīcampakarodhrāṃś ca mallikābāṇabarbarīḥ ||
cūtāśokāṭarūṣaṃ ca tulasīṃ tilakaṃ tathā |
pārantīṃ śatapatraṃ ca gandhanepālikām api ||
kujakaṃ tagaraṃ caiva mṛgamāraṃ ca ketakīm |
yūthikām atimuktāṃ ca śrāddhayogyāni bho dvijāḥ ||
kamalaṃ kumudaṃ padmaṃ puṇḍarīkaṃ ca yatnataḥ |
indīvaraṃ kokanadaṃ kalhāraṃ ca niyojayet ||
‘campakaḥ' cāmpeyaḥ | ‘rotraḥ' gālavaḥ | 'bāṇaḥ' ārttagalaḥ | 'barbarī' karvarī | ‘cūtaḥ' āmraḥ | ‘aśokaḥ' vañjulaḥ | ‘āṭarūṣaḥ' siṃhāsyaḥ | ‘tulasī' surasā | asyāś ca śrāddhe varjanīyatvam uktaṃ smṛtyarthasārakāreṇa | tat tu bahusmṛtīhāsapurāṇeṣv adṛṣṭatvān nirmūlam iti jānīmaḥ | 'tilakaḥ' kṣurakaḥ | ‘pārantī' śephālikāsadṛśapuṣpam | 'śatapatraṃ' prasiddham | 'nepālikā' navamālikā | 'kujakaṃ' kubjakam | 'tagaraṃ' gandhatagaram | 'mṛgamāraṃ” śrāvaṇikāpuṣpam | 'atimuktakaṃ' mādhavīlatāpuṣpam | kamalādayaḥ paṅkajabhedāḥ prasiddhāḥ |
atha varjyāni puṣpāṇi |
[684]
tatra vāyu-brahmāṇḍapurāṇayoḥ |
japādi sumanā bhaṇḍī rūpikā ca kuraṇṭakaḥ |
puṣpāṇi varjanīyāni śrāddhakarmaṇi nityaśaḥ ||
yāni gandhād apetāni ugragandhāni yāni ca |
varjanīyāni puṣpāṇi śrāddhakarmaṇi nityaśaḥ ||
yāni gandhād apetāni ugragandhāni yāni ca |
varjanīyāni puṣpāṇi bhūmim anvicchatā sadā ||
'japā' rudrapuṣyam | ādiśabdaḥ prakāravacanaḥ, tena japākusumasadṛśānāṃ karavīrādikusumānāṃ varjyatvam | 'sumanā' jātiḥ | "sumanā mālatījātir" iti smaraṇāt | 'bhaṇḍī' mañjiṣṭhā | 'rūpikā' arkaḥ | 'kuraṇṭakaḥ' pītapuṣpamahāsahā | jātīkusumaniṣedho ’tra na nityaḥ nityaniṣedhe “jātyaś ca sarvā dātavyāḥ” iti pūrvoktavacanasya nirviṣayatvam āpadyate |
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
jātīpuṣpasamāyuktaṃ tac chrāddhaṃ vyarthatāṃ vrajet ||
āha aṅgirāḥ |
na jātīkusumāni dadyān na kadalīpatram || iti |
kadalīpatram atra bhojanapātratayā prāptaṃ niṣidhyate ||
kratur āha |
asurāṇāṃ kule jātā jātī pūrvaparigrahe |
tasyā darśanamātreṇa nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ ||
‘pūrvaparigrahe' pūrvajanmani dehaparigrahāvasare, yady api trīṇi ha vai yajñasyodarāṇi atra hy evāvayanti ata evodvayatīti vartamānāpadeśatayārthavādasarūpe [685] vākye arthavādanirvāhārthaṃ gāyatryādiṣv āvāpodvāpavidhikalpanavad atra nindārthavādarūpe vākye niṣedhavidhiḥ kalpyate tathāpi pākṣika evāyam api pratiṣedha iti na mārkaṇḍeyavacanaṃ nirviṣayaṃ kiṃ tv aniṣiddhapuṣpāntarālābhaviṣayam | pratiṣedhadvayaṃ tu tallābhaviṣayam iti mantavyam |
āha śaṅkhaḥ |
ugragandhāny agandhāni caityavṛkṣodbhavāni ca |
puṣpāṇi varjanīyāni raktavarṇāni yāni ca |
vanodbhavāni deyāni raktāny api viśeṣataḥ ||
‘ugragandhāni’ ahṛdyagandhāni | 'caityavṛkṣaḥ' śmaśānavṛkṣaḥ |
saurapurāṇe |
abjād anyad rakapuṣyaṃ śiśumāraṃ tathaiva ca |
nīrasāny api sarvāṇi bhakṣabhojyāni yāni ca |
etāni naiva deyāni sarvasmin śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
varjayed ity anuvṛttau viṣṇuḥ |
ugragandhāni kaṣṭakijātāni raktāni ca puṣpāṇi |
tathā |
sitāni sugandhāni kaṇṭakijātāni api |
padma-matsyapurāṇayoḥ |
padmabilvārkadhustūrapāribhadrāṭarūṣakāḥ |
na deyāḥ piḍhakāryeṣu payaś cājāvikaṃ tathā ||
padmapatraṃ sthalajaṃ pratiṣidhyate, jalajasya vihitatvāt | 'bilvaḥ' [686] śrīphalaḥ | 'dhustūraḥ' unmattaḥ | 'pāribhadraḥ' raktastavakayukto mandāraḥ | 'āḍharūṣaḥ ' siṃhāsyaḥ |
iti puṣpanirūpaṇam |
atha dhūpāḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
dhūpaṃ gandhaguṇopetaṃ dadat pitṛparāyaṇaḥ |
labhate strīsv apatyāni iha cāmutra janmani ||
guggulādīṃs tathā dhūpān pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
saṃyujya madhusarpirbhyāṃ so ’gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
agnipurāṇe |
gandhapuṣpair alaṃkṛtya brāhmaṇān śrāddhakarmaṇi |
tadāmodena saṃtuṣṭāḥ pitaro bhuñjate haviḥ ||
ādityapurāṇe |
dhūpair manoramair divyais tataḥ saṃprīṇayet pitṝn |
prītās te hy akhilaiḥ kāmair dātāraṃ prīṇayanty uta ||
gandham āghrāya dhūpasya manaḥprahlādanaṃ param |
pitṝṇām akṣayā prītir jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
nāsti dhūpasamaṃ kiṃcit pitṝṇāṃ prītikārakam |
ataḥ śrāddhe pradātavyo dhūpaḥ puṇyam abhīpsubhiḥ ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
āmodinā tu dhūpena tataḥ saṃdhūpayed dvijān |
prayānti tṛptim akṣayyāṃ tena sarve pitāmahāḥ ||
[687]        brahmavaivarte |
candanāguruṇī cobhe tamālośīrapatrakam |
dhūpaṃ ca guggulaśreṣṭhaṃ turuṣkaṃ dhūpam eva ca ||
dhūpāś cātra ghṛtamadhusaṃyuktā deyāḥ, “madhughṛtasaṃyutāṃ guggulaṃ dadyād" iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt |
śaṅkho 'py āha |
dhūpārthe guggulaṃ dadyāt ghṛtayukaṃ madhūtkaṭam |
candanaṃ ca tathā dadyāt karpūraṃ kuṅkumaṃ śubham ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
guggulādīṃs tathā dhūpān pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
saṃyuktān madhusarpirbhyāṃ so ’gniṣṭomaphalaṃ labhet ||
brahmapurāṇe |
candanāguruṇī cobhe tathaivośīrapadmakam |
turuṣkaṃ guggulaṃ caiva ghṛtākaṃ yugapad dahet ||
tathā |
guggulaṃ candanaṃ caiva śrīvāsam aguru tathā |
dhūpāni pitṛyogyāni ṛṣe guggulam eva ca ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
dhūpo guggulajo deyas tathā candanasārajaḥ |
agaruś ca sa karpūras turuṣkaś ca tathaiva ca ||
dhūpabhedās tu bhaviṣyatpurāṇoktā grāhyāḥ |
tad yathā |
[688]        aguru candanaṃ mustā sihlakaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ tathā |
samabhāgaṃ tu kartavyaṃ dhūpo ’yam amṛtāhvayaḥ ||
śrīkhaṇḍaṃ granthisahitam aguruṃ sihlakaṃ tathā |
mustām induṃ ca bhṛteśaṃ śarkarāṃ ca dahed iha ||
ity eṣo ’nantadhūpaś ca kathito devasattama |
kṛṣṇāguruṃ sihlakaṃ ca bālakaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ tathā ||
candanaṃ tagaraṃ mustā prabodhaḥ śarkarānvitaḥ |
karpūraṃ candanaṃ kuṣṭham uśīraṃ sihlakaṃ tathā ||
granthikaṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ bhīmaṃ kuṅkumaṃ gṭañjanaṃ tathā |
harītakī tathośīraṃ yakṣadhūpa udāhṛtaḥ ||
tṛṣaṇaṃ sihlakaṃ vipra śrīkhaṇḍam aguruṃ tathā |
karpūraṃ ca tathā mustāṃ śarkarāṃ satvacaṃ dvija ||
ity eṣa viṣayo dhūpaḥ svayaṃ devena nirmitaḥ |
karpūraṃ candanaṃ māṃsī tvakpatrailā (?) lavaṅgakam ||
aguru sihlakaṃ dhūpaṃ prājāpatyaṃ pracakṣate |
śrīkhaṇḍaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ karpūraṃ sihlakaṃ tathā ||
vyañjanaṃ śarkarā kṛṣṇaṃ mahāṅgaṃ sihlakaṃ tathā |
mahāṅgo ’yaṃ smṛto dhūpaḥ priyo devasya sarvadā ||
devīpurāṇe |
bālārālasamāyuktaḥ tathā kundurusaṃyutaḥ |
śrīveṣṭaguggulakṛto dhūpaḥ pañcaraso mataḥ ||
kālottare |
[689]        karpūradārvagurubhī raktacandanasaṃyutaiḥ |
dhūpaḥ samudgako nāma sarvadaivatavallabhaḥ ||
ete cāmṛtādayo dhūpāḥ prāṇyaṅgadravyaparihāreṇopakalpya śrāddhe niyojyāḥ, “śrīvarjaṃ sarvaṃ na dhūpārthe taile ghṛte ca dadyāt” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt |
devalo ’py āha |
ye hi prāṇyaṅgajā dhūpā hastavātāhatāś ca ye |
na te śrāddhe niyoktavyā ye ca ke cogragandhayaḥ |
ghṛtaṃ na kevalaṃ dadyāt duṣṭaṃ vā tṛṇaguggulam ||
iti dhūpanirūpaṇam |
atha dīpāḥ |
vāyupurāṇe |
dīpaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ prayataḥ sadā yas tu prayacchati |
gatim apratimaṃ cakṣus tasmāt sa labhate śubham ||
tejasā yaśasā caiva āyuṣā ca balena ca |
bhuvi prakāśo bhavati bhrājate ca triviṣṭape |
apsarobhiḥ parivṛto vimānāgre sa modate ||
brahmapurāṇe |
śrāddhadīpapradānena tejasvī jāyate naraḥ |
rūpavān dhanavān bhogī sukham aiśvaryam aśnute ||
tasmād dīpaḥ sadā deyo yajamānena vai pitṝn |
dīpahīnaṃ dadac chrāddham andhe tamasi majjati ||
[690]        pratibhoktṛdvijaṃ dīpāḥ sthāpanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ |
pitṛyāṇeṣu mārgeṣu sa hy ālokaḥ prakāśate ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
śraddhayā śrādadeśeṣu dīpadaś cakṣur uttamam |
labhate nātra saṃdehaḥ kāntimāṃś ca prajāyate ||
dīpaḥ prakāśaḥ paramaḥ prakāśo jñānam uttamam |
ataḥ śrāddhe dadad dīpaṃ jñānavān abhijāyate ||
ya icched vimalaṃ jñānaṃ tamonirmūlanaṃ param |
sa bhaktyā pitṛdevebhyo dīpam eva prayacchatu ||
yaḥ svena tejasā viśvaṃ pradātā yena kṛtsnaśaḥ |
vāñchati vyāpyamānaṃ vai śrāddhe dīpaprado ’stu saḥ ||
yathā nihanti dhvāntāni dīpaḥ prajvalito ’bhitaḥ |
tathaiva sarvapāpāni śrāddhe datto nihanti saḥ ||
skandapurāṇe |
sthāpyāḥ pratidvijaṃ dīpāḥ śvetasūtrajavarttayaḥ |
gavyena māhiṣeṇāpi ghṛtena bhṛtabhājanāḥ ||
atha vā tilatailena pūritā vimalārciṣaḥ |
pitṝn uddiśya dātavyāḥ pratyekaṃ te yathāvidhi ||
tenālokena pitaro dīvyante divi sarvataḥ |
dātur iṣṭān prayacchanti kāmān kāmāvasāyinaḥ ||
padmapurāṇe |
ghṛtaprajvalitaṃ dīpaṃ pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
[691]        sa virājeṣu lokeṣu caran na pratihanyate ||
gavāṃ ghṛtena tatrāpi yo dīpayati dīpakam |
gavām eva sa lokeṣu lokeśaiḥ saha modate ||
ghṛtābhāve tu yo dīpaṃ tilatailapravartitaṃ |
prayacchati pitṝṇāṃ vai sālokyaṃ tasya bhānunā ||
alābhe tilatailasya snehaiḥ prāṇyaṅgavarjitaiḥ |
yaḥ śrāddhe dīpadaḥ so ’pi tamas tarati dustaram ||
kālikāpurāṇe |
śvetavarttikṛtān dīpān gavyasarpiḥprapūritān |
dīpikāṃ dhātusaṃbhūtāṃ sāradārumayīm api ||
alābhe mṛnmayīṃ vāpi manojñām adhisaṃsthitām |
yo dadāti pitṛbhyas tu tasya puṃyaphalaṃ śṛṇu ||
tejasā yaśasā kāntyā prajñayā parayā śriyā |
bhrājamānaḥ sa hi svarge vimānavaram āsthitaḥ ||
ramate bhuvane divye ratnālokasamākule |
abhyarcya yaḥ pitṝṇāṃ vai kuryād ārātrikaṃ naraḥ ||
sa hi nīrājyate nāke divyair apsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ |
dhātvādinirmitāṃ ramyāṃ dīpikāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
pitṝn uddiśya yo dadyāt sa bhaved bhājanaṃ śriyaḥ |
yo dhūpadahanaṃ pātraṃ pātram ārātrikasya ca |
dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ prayatas tasya svarge ’kṣayā gatiḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ |
[692]        dīpārthaṃ snehadravyeṣūpakalpanīyam |
marīciḥ |
ghṛtād vā tilatailād vā nānyadravyāt tu dīpakam || iti |
‘anyadravyaṃ' vasāmedorūpaṃ vivakṣitam | na punar atasīkusumbhādibījasaṃbhavaṃ snehadravyam |
yata āha śaṅkhaḥ |
ghṛtena dīpo dātavyas tv atha vāpy oṣadhīrasaḥ |
vasāmedodbhavaṃ dīpaṃ prayatnena vivarjayet ||
'vasā' pākāt samuddhṛto māṃsasnehaḥ | medas tu hṛdayakamalācchādako māṃsaviśeṣaḥ | pākāt samuddhṛtas tadīyasneho medaśabdena lakṣyate | medaḥśabdo ’kārānto py asti, medinītyādiṣu darśanāt |
athācchādanam |
padmapurāṇe |
saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādyair dadyād ācchādanaṃ tataḥ |
adhautaṃ sadaśaṃ nūnaṃ niśchidram amalīmasam ||
tasyābhāve tu deyaṃ syāt savarṇaiḥ kṣālitaṃ tu yat |
pradeyaṃ pitṛkāryeṣu kārudhautaṃ na jātucit ||
bhagavatīpurāṇe |
adharīyottarīyārtham uddiśyaikaikam ādarāt |
vāsoyugaṃ pradātavyaṃ pitṛkṛtye vipaścitā |
niṣkrayo vā yathāśakti vastrālābhe pradīyate ||
skandapurāṇe |
[693]        mahādhanāni vāsāṃsi pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
dhanadhānyasamṛddho ’sau suveṣaś ca prajāyate ||
rūpavān subhagaḥ śrīmān vanitānāṃ ca vallabhaḥ |
āyurārogyasaṃpannaḥ kīrtiṃ vindati cāmalām ||
candrikājālaśubhrāṇi yaḥ kṣaumāṇi prayacchati |
sa cāndramasam āsādya lokaṃ dīvyati devavat ||
datvā kṣaumāṇi raktāni sūryalokaṃ samaśnute |
pītāni tāni datvā vai yāti lokaṃ madhudviṣaḥ |
citrāṇi datvā māhendre loke nityaṃ mahīyate ||
paṭṭasūtramaye datvā vāsasī pitṛtatparaḥ |
rūpasaubhāgyasaṃpanno rājarājo bhaved iha ||
kausumbhāny api vāsāṃsi pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
sa nākapṛṣṭhe ramate divyair bhogaiḥ samāśatam ||
kārpāsasūtrajaṃ vāsaḥ susūkṣmaṃ cātiśobhanam |
yo dadāti pitṝṇaṃ vai so ’nantaṃ sukham āpnuyāt ||
mañjiṣṭhādyaiḥ śubhai rāgai rañjitaṃ vā manoramam |
pradāya pitṛdevebhyaḥ paramām ṛddhim ṛcchati ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
bahumūlyāni vāsāṃsi kṣaumādīni mahāmatiḥ |
śrāddhe viśrāṇayed yas tu tasya viṣṇuḥ prasīdati ||
yaḥ kañcukaṃ tathoṣṇīṣaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pratipādayet |
dvandvodbhavāni duḥkhāni sa kadācin na paśyati ||
[694]        dadāti yaḥ prasannātmā paṭān kañcukabandhanān |
vimuktaḥ pāpabandhebhyaḥ so ’gryāṃ vindati saṃpadam ||
strīṇāṃ śrāddheṣu sindūraṃ dadyuś caṇḍātakāni ca |
nimantritābhyaḥ strībhyo ye te syaḥ saubhāgyasaṃyutāḥ ||
'caṇḍātakāni' strīparidhānocitāny aṃśukāni |
vastrasthāpanabhāṇḍāni savastrāṇi prayacchati |
yaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ sa saṃpadbhiḥ sarvābhir abhipūryate ||
śītavātasamudbhūtāṃ pīḍāṃ vārayituṃ kṣamān |
yaḥ prāvārānatighanān viśālān sudṛḍhānnavān ||
dadyāt pitṛbhyas tasyeha dvandvaduḥkhaṃ na vidyate |
ūrṇāyulomaracitān suviśālān mahāpaṭān ||
vicitrān vividhai rāgair vātaprāvaraṇocitān |
prayacchati pitṝṇāṃ yaḥ sa sadārogyavān bhavet ||
vāyupurāṇe |
kauśeyaṃ kṣaumakārpāsaṃ dukūlam ahataṃ tathā |
śrāddhe śvetāni yo dadyāt kāmān āpnoti puṣkalān ||
tathā |
vāso hi sarvadevatyaṃ sarvadevair abhiṣṭutam |
vastrābhāve kriyā na syād yajñadānādikā kvacit ||
tasmād vastrāṇi deyāni śrāddhakāle viśeṣataḥ |
dadat tv etāny avāpnoti yajñadānatapāṃsi ca ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
ācchādanaṃ ca yo dadyād ahataṃ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
āyuḥ prakāmam aiśvaryaṃ rūpaṃ ca labhate tu saḥ ||
ahatalakṣaṇam āha pracetāḥ |
īṣaddhautaṃ navaṃ caiva sadaśaṃ yatra dhāritam |
ahataṃ tad vijānīyāt sarvakarmasu pāvanam ||
'īṣaddhautaṃ' akārudhautam |
ata eva vṛddhamanuḥ |
svayaṃ dhautena kartavyā kriyā dharmyā vipaścitā |
na tu nejakadhautena nopabhuktena vā kvacit ||
‘nejakaḥ’ rajakaḥ | svayaṃgrahaṇād eva nejakanivṛttau punar nejakapratiṣedho ’nyenāpi savarṇena dhautasya kriyārhatvajñāpanārthaḥ |
śrāddham adhikṛtya brahmavaivarte |
kṣaumakauśeyapatrāṇāṃs tathā prāvarakaṃ balāt |
ajinaṃ rauravaṃ yat syād aurṇikaṃ sṛgalomakam ||
datvā hy etāni viprebhyo bhojayitvā yathāvidhi |
prāpnoti śraddadhānas tu vājapeyasya yat phalam ||
bahyonāryyaḥ surūpāśca putrā mṛtyāśca kiṅkarāḥ |
vaśe tiṣṭhanti bhūtāni vapurvindatyanāmayaṃ ||
lakṣmīṃ nāśayatyāśu tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā |
bhrājate ca vimānāgre nakṣatreṣviva candramāḥ ||
nityaśrāddheṣu yo dadyādvastraṃ pitṛparāyaṇaḥ |
sarvān kāmānavāpnoti rājyaṃ svargaṃ tathaiva ca ||
[696]                ādityapurāṇe |
na kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ dātavyaṃ vāsaḥ kārpāsasaṃbhavam |
pitṛbhyo nāpi malinaṃ nopabhuktaṃ kadācana |
na chidritaṃ nāpadaśaṃ na dhautaṃ kāruṇāpi ca ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
kārpāsaṃ naiva dātavyaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ kṛṣṇam aṃśukam |
kṛṣṇaṃ vāpi pradātavyam anyat kārpāsasaṃbhavāt ||
nāmāpi na grahītavyaṃ nīlīraktasya vāsasaḥ |
darśanāt kīrtanān nīlyā nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
yajñopavītaṃ dātavyaṃ vastrābhāve vijānatā |
pitṝṇāṃ vastradānasya phalaṃ tenāśnute ’khilam ||
śātātapaḥ |
yuvā suvāsā iti vastraṃ dadyāt | abhāve yajñopavītam || iti |
athedaṃ yajñopavītaṃ śūdreṇa svapitṛbhyo deyaṃ na veti vicāryate | tatra tāvad deyam ity ucyate, sāmānyena śrāddhe deyatvābhidhānāt | na ca śūdrasya yajñopavītābhāvād aśakter adānam iti vācyaṃ, krayasaṃpāditasya dānasaṃbhavāt | na ca krītasyāhavanīyatvābhāvavat krayotpāditasya brahmasūtrasya kretāraṃ prati yajñopavītatvābhāva iti vācyam | ādadhīteti kartṛgāmikriyāphalapratipādakātmanepadasāmarthyāvagatādhātṛpuruṣaikasaṃbandhād āhanīyatvotpattyanurodhāt | krītasyāhavanīyatvābhāvāvarṇyate | nanu [697] yajñopavītavidhāv ātmanepadam asti | vidyamānam apy avivakṣitaṃ bhaviṣyati yajñopavītadānavidhidarśanāt | na ca śūdraśrāddhe na dātavyaṃ yajñopavītam ity apavādo dṛśyate | nanu yena pitaraḥ prīyante tat pitṛbhyo deyam, śrāddhīyasya dānasya pitṛprītyarthatvāt | na ca yajñopavītaṃ śūdrapitṝn prīṇayati | na tāvad bhūṣaṇatvena hārakeyūrādivat varjanikabhūṣaṇapratyayavedyatvābhāvenābhūṣaṇatvāt | yas tu paricayaviśeṣaprasūtavāsanopanīto ’sya bhūṣaṇabhāvaḥ so ’pi traivarṇikān pratyeveti na śūdrapitṛtṛptaye prabhavati | maivaṃ | yad vihitam apratiṣiddhaṃ vā śrāddhe dīyamānaṃ brāhmaṇān prīṇayati tad etad adhiṣṭhānatayoddeśyān pitṝn | na tu yaj jīvantaṃ prīṇayati tad eva pitṛtvaṃ gatam apīti niyamo ’sti | ko jānīte janmāntare kāṃ yoniṃ gato ’sāv iti | ata eva strīśrāddhe strībhūṣaṇāni na dīyante, yajñopavītāni dīyante | na ca parivrājakaśrāddhe yajñopavītaṃ na dīyate, alāvupatrādi vā dīyate | na ca yatibrahmacāriśrāddhe madhumāṃsaṃ na dīyate, yena jīvatā yat pāyasādikaṃ varjitam āsīt tat tadīyaśrāddhe na dīyate | nanv anaucityād anupayogāt tad api na deyam eveti cet | śiṣṭācāre tathānupalambhāt | kiṃ cedam ācchādanasthāne yajñopavītaṃ vidhīyate | ācchādanena ca śūdrāṇām apy upayogo ’sty eva | ataḥ śūdraśrāddhe kāmaṃ yajñopavītaṃ deyam | ācchādanapratinidhitvavyatirekeṇa gandhādivat vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa yad vihitaṃ tad api deyam eva, apavādābhāvāt |
ity ācchādananirūpaṇam |
atha yajñopavītam |
[698]
ādityapurāṇe |
pitṝn satkṛtya vāsobhir dadyād yajñopavītakam |
yajñopavītadānena vinā śrāddhaṃ tu niṣphalam |
tasmād yajñopavītasya dānam āvaśyakaṃ smṛtam ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
dadyād yajñopavītāni pitṝṇāṃ prītaye sadā |
śraddhāvān dhārmikas tena jāyate brahmavarcasi ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
yo brāhmaṇena śucinā brahmasūtraṃ vinirmitam |
pitṛbhyo yacchati prājñaḥ sa bhaved vedapāragaḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
upavītaṃ tu yo dadyāc chrāddhakarmaṇi dharmavit |
pāvanaṃ sarvaviprāṇāṃ brahmadānasya tat phalam ||
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
sitasūkṣmeṇa sūtreṇa racitaṃ mantrapūrvakam |
upavītaṃ dadac chrāddhe medhāvān api jāyate ||
cāmīkaramayaṃ divyaṃ pitṝṇām upavītakam |
dattvā cāmīkaramayair vimānair divi dīvyati ||
rājatāny upavītāni pitṝṇāṃ dadataḥ sadā |
āyuḥ prajā ca tejaś ca yaśaś caivābhivardhate ||
pūrvaṃ vastrālābhe tatpratinidhitvena yajñopavītadānam uktam | atra tu saty api vastre gandhapuṣpādivan mukhyatayaiva yajñopavītaṃ deyatvena vidhīyata [699] iti gamyate | pitṝn satkṛtya vāsobhir ityādiṣu samuccayadarśanāt | tathā ca nyubjapātraṃ kṛtvāha vaijavāpaḥ |
tasyopari kuśān datvā pradadyād devapūrvakam |
gandhapuṣpāṇi dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ vastropavītakam ||
ity atropavītasya deyatvena vidhānāt gandhapuṣpādivan mukhyatayaiva yajñopavītaṃ deyatvena vidhīyata iti gamyate |
iti yajñopavītanirūpaṇam |
atha daṇḍayogapaṭṭau |
viṣṇudharmottare |
daṇḍān śrāddheṣu yo dadyāt pitṛprītyai mahātmavān |
kadācit taṃ na bādhante vyāpadaḥ śvāpadodbhavāḥ ||
daṇḍāṃś ca yogapaṭṭāṃś ca yogibhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
kāmais tam abhivardhante pitaro yogavittamāḥ ||
pālāśān vaiṇavān vāpi yas tu daṇḍān yathocitam |
vratibhyo ’tha gṛhasthebhyo yatibhyaḥ pitṛtuṣṭaye ||
dadāti yogapaṭṭāṃś ca paṭṭasūtrādinirmitān |
sa yogināṃ kule bhūtvā yogirājaḥ prajāyate ||
ādityapurāṇe |
vaiṇvān sudṛḍhān daṇḍān hṛdyān saṃnataparvaṇaḥ |
pitṛprītikṛte datvā na bhayaṃ jātu vindati ||
āyasena khanitreṇa mūladeśe pariṣkṛtān |
mṛtkuśānāṃ yathākālaṃ khananeṣu kṣamān bhṛśam ||
[700]        kevalān vātha daṇḍān vā yaḥ śrāddhe pratipādayet |
tasya śraddhāṃ ca medhāṃ ca śaucam āstikyam eva ca |
iha janmani cānyatra prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ ||
skandapurāṇe |
yogapaṭṭān mṛdūn bhavyān yo vai pitṛparāyaṇaḥ |
viśrāṇyati viprebhyaḥ sa yogaṃ vindate dhruvam ||
yoginām upayuktāni vastūny anyāni yāni ca |
tāni datvā pitṝn prīṇan sadyas tarati duṣkṛtam ||
padārthān yas tu daṇḍādīn yoginām upayoginaḥ |
dadāti śraddhayā yuktaḥ sa sukhīha paratra ca ||
āha jātūkarṇyaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ datvā dvijāgryāṇāṃ pitrye karmaṇi bhaktimān |
adaṇḍanīyo bhavati yamasyāpi na saṃśayaḥ ||
āha śālaṅkāyanaḥ |
pradāya vaiṇavīṃ yaṣṭiṃ nūtanāṃ sudṛḍhāṃ mṛdum |
ślakṣṇam anulbaṇagranthiṃ dvijāya śrāddhabhojine ||
vijayo jāyate nityaṃ na paśyati parājayam |
sā ced bhavati kalmāṣī sarvaṃ tarati kalmaṣam ||
iti daṇḍayogapaṭṭī |
atha kamaṇḍalvādijalapātrāṇi |
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
[701]        śrāddheṣu bhaktisaṃyukto jalapūrṇaṃ kamaṇḍalum |
dadyād abhinavaṃ ramyaṃ dvijebhyaḥ pitṛtṛptaye ||
pitaro dvādaśābdāni tena syur abhitarpitāḥ |
dātā ca sukhakṛt sarvaṃ sarvato ’py abhivindati ||
agnipurāṇe |
sarveṣu pitṛdeveṣu pradhānaṃ dvayam eva hi |
annaṃ ca salilaṃ ceti paraṃ tṛptikare hy ubhe ||
annād api varaṃ nūnaṃ jalam eva na saṃśayaḥ |
na bādhate kṣudhā tādṛk yathā pīḍyate tṛṣā ||
vināpy annaṃ kadācit tu prāṇinaḥ prāṇituṃ kṣamāḥ |
kṣaṇārdham api jīvanti tṛṣārttā naiva dehinaḥ ||
mūrcchām upāgatāś caiva saṃsiktāḥ śiśirair jalaiḥ |
labhante cetanāṃ sadyas tasmāt saṃjīvanaṃ hi tat ||
ato ’rthaṃ salilaṃ deyaṃ pramītānāṃ viśeṣataḥ |
parāṃ nirvṛtim āyānti salilāpyāyanena te ||
paratantrāḥ pare loke prāṇinaḥ pīḍitās tṛṣā |
salilāny abhivāñchanti (?) dīyamānāni vaṃśajaiḥ ||
ato ’rthaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ pitṝṇāṃ salilaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
jalapūrṇāni pātrāṇi pradeyāni ca parvasu ||
grāme dharmaghaṭā deyā brāhmaṇebhyo ’nuvāsaram |
tathākṣayatṛtīyāyāṃ pūrṇān kumbhān pradāpayet ||
aśvatthasecanaṃ kāryaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptaye 'nvahaṃ |
[702]        śrāddhe kamaṇḍalūn dadyāt pūritān himavāriṇā ||
evaṃ nānāprakāraṃ yaḥ pitṝṇāṃ yacchate jalaṃ |
sarvakāmaiḥ samaṃ tṛptaś ciraṃ svarge ’bhimodate ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
kamaṇḍaluṃ jalāpūrṇaṃ datvā śrāddhe samāhitaḥ |
pūrṇakāmair aśeṣaiś ca so ’kṣayāṃ labhate gatim ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
kamaṇḍaluṃ ca yo dadyād brāhmaṇe vedapārage |
madhukṣīrasravā dhenur dātāram anugacchati ||
cakrabaddhanṃ tu yo dadyāc chrāddhakāle kamaṇḍalum |
kāñcanena vimānena kiṅkiṇījālamālinā |
ramate cirarātrāya sutṛpto merumūrdhani ||
vāyupurāṇe |
yaḥ kāñcanamayaṃ divyaṃ prayacchati kamaṇḍalum |
dhenuṃ sa labhate divyāṃ ghaṭaprasṛtidohanām ||
narasiṃhapurāṇe |
dadyāt pitṝṇām āpūrṇaṃ yaḥ kamaṇḍalum ambhasā |
ghṛtakulyāmadhyagataṃ madhukulyānuyāti tam ||
svarṇādidhātughaṭitaṃ yaḥ pradadyāt kamaṇḍalum |
pitṛbhyaḥ sa ciraṃ bhogair modate kāñcanācale ||
yo dadāti pitṝṇāṃ hi rājataṃ vai kamaṇḍalum |
saṃpannaḥ sakalair aṅgaiḥ sa rājā dhārmiko bhavet ||
[703]        kamaṇḍaluṃ tāmramayaṃ śrāddheṣu pradadāti yaḥ |
sa mahatyā śriyā yuktaḥ kule mahati jāyate ||
kāṣṭhena nirmitaṃ yas tu nālikeram athāpi vā |
dadyāt kamaṇḍaluṃ śrāddhe sa śrīmān abhijāyate ||
carmaṇā nirmitaṃ vāripātraṃ nānāvidhaṃ tu yaḥ |
pratipādayati śraddhe sa sukhī jāyate ciram ||
yo mṛttikāviracitān śrāddheṣu karakān navān |
prayacchati mahāmedhāḥ sa duḥkhaṃ naiva vindati ||
skandapurāṇe |
yas taḍāgāṃs tathā vāpīr api kūpān prapās tathā |
utsṛjet pitṛtṛptyarthaṃ brahmalokaṃ sa gacchati ||
maṇikān ambhasā pūrṇān pradadyād vā galantikām |
pradadyāt karakān vāpi yadi vā karapatrikāḥ ||
śrāddhakāle yathāśakti yo ’kṣayaṃ vindate sukham ||
vāyupurāṇe |
datvā pavitraṃ yogibhyo jantuvāraṇam ambhasaḥ |
śrāddhe niṣkasahasrasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
iti kamaṇḍalvādijalapātrāṇi |
atha chatram |
vāyupurāṇe |
śreṣṭhaṃ chatraṃ ca yo dadyāt puṣpamālādiśobhitam |
prāsādo hy uttamo bhūtvā gacchantam anugacchati ||
[704]        bṛhadviṣṇupurāṇe |
chatraṃ vicitraṃ yo dadyāt pitṝṇāṃ śreyasānvitaḥ |
sa vindati na saṃdehaḥ saṃtāpaṃ kutracin na hi ||
padmapurāṇe |
yo vai prayacchati chatram amukasyedam astv iti |
tena saṃtāritas tāpāt sa bhaved āyugakṣayāt ||
bhagavatīpurāṇe |
varṣātapatrāṇayogyam ātapatraṃ prayacchatā |
samuddhṛtāḥ syuḥ pitaro dustarāt duḥkhavāridheḥ ||
nāradīyapurāṇe |
dadyāt pitṝṇāṃ yaḥ chatraṃ vṛṣyātapanivāraṇam |
mahābhūtasamudbhūtāṃ naiva pīḍāṃ sa paśyati ||
brahmavaivarte |
chatraṃ narapater yogyaṃ nānāśobhāsamanvitam |
chatravāhāya vṛttiṃ ca parikalpya yathocitam ||
pradadyād vipramukhyāya pitṝn uddiśya yaḥ svakān |
sa praśāsti naraḥ kṛtsnam ekachatraṃ mahītalam ||
chatraṃ śataśalākaṃ yaḥ sitavastropaśobhitam |
pitṝṇāṃ prayato dadyāt so ’pi rājā bhaved iha ||
mayūrapicchair bahubhir nirmitaṃ rucirākṛti |
chatraṃ dadāti yas tasya vihāro nandane vane ||
yaḥ pradadyāl laghuchatraṃ ramyam ātapavāraṇam |
[705]        śrāddhakāle sa manujo na kvacit paritapyate |
yo dṛṣṭivāraṇaṃ chatraṃ varṣākāle prayacchati ||
svapitṝṇāṃ hitārthāya sa duḥkhair nābhibhūyate |
iti chatranirūpaṇam |
athopānatpāduke |
vāyupurāṇe |
śrāddheṣūpānahau dadyād brāhmaṇebhyo viśeṣataḥ |
divyaṃ sa labhate cakṣur vājiyuktān rathāṃs tathā ||
brahmapurāṇe |
upānadyugalaṃ yas tu śrāddhakarmaṇi dharmavit |
ekaikasmai dvijāgryāya pitrarthaṃ saṃprayacchati ||
pitṝṇāṃ tat pare loke vimānam upatiṣṭhate |
dātāpi svargam āpnoti suyuktair vaḍavārathaiḥ ||
devīpurāṇe |
upānahau prayacchanti ye pitṛbhyaḥ samāhitāḥ |
aśvadānaṃ kṛtaṃ tais tu nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||
ādityapurāṇe |
prataptabālukāḥ kecit kecid aṅgārasaṅkulāḥ |
lohakaṇṭakasaṃkīrṇāḥ kecid vyālasamākulāḥ ||
yamalokasya panthānaḥ pramītānāṃ sudustarāḥ |
upānaddānam evaikaṃ teṣu tārayituṃ kṣamam ||
upānahau svayaṃ datvā dvijebhyo vai yathāvidhi |
[706]        yebhyo vā vaṃśajair datte panthānaṃ te taranti tam ||
etasmāt kāraṇān nityaṃ śrāddhe dānam upānahām |
purāṇajñāḥ praśaṃsanti dānam anyasamaṃ na tat ||
saurapurāṇe |
nirmāya sudṛḍhe dadyād adurgandhena carmaṇā |
na nyūne nātirikre ca pādayoḥ sukhade mṛdū |
upānahau brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ sukhahetave |
prīyante pitaras tasya prītā yacchanti vāñchitam ||
vāyupurāṇe |
ṛjū pūrṇe tu yo dadyāt pāduke śrāddhakarmaṇi |
śobhanaṃ labhate yānaṃ pādayoḥ sukham eva ca ||
nandipurāṇe |
yaḥ pāduke pradadyāt tu pitṛtṛpyartham ādarāt |
tasya puṇyeṣu lokeṣu bhaved apratighā gatiḥ ||
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
dhātvādinirmitaṃ dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ pādukāyugam |
yas tasya devalokeṣu gatir vaimānikī bhavet ||
gajadantakṛte yas tu pāduke saṃprayacchati |
sa vai citrāṇi yānāni labhate pretya ceha ceti ||
yaḥ pāduke prayacchet tu sāradārumaye śubhe |
pitṛbhyaḥ so ’pi medhāvī sukham atyantam aśnute ||
ity upānatpādukānirūpaṇam |
athāsanāni |
[707]
brahmapurāṇe |
āsanāni ca ramyāṇi pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
sa āste suciraṃ kālaṃ tridaśair abhipūjitaḥ ||
devīpurāṇe |
pīṭhāny atimanojñāni pitṝṇāṃ pradadāti yaḥ |
tasya pīṭheśvarī nityaṃ varān yacchati vāñchitān ||
ādityapurāṇe |
suślakṣṇāni viśālāni pradadāty āsanāni yaḥ |
śrāddheṣu tasya bhavane sadāste padmasambhavā ||
gāruḍapurāṇe |
prayacchaty āsanaṃ yas tu pitṛbhyaḥ sumahāmatiḥ |
svairaṃ vairavinirmukraś ciram āste triviṣṭape |
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
cāmīkaramayaṃ śrāddheṣv āsanaṃ yas tu dāpayet |
tasyāsanaṃ merupṛṣṭhe samīpe parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
yaḥ pitṝṇāṃ sughaṭitaṃ dadyād rājatam āsanam |
sa svarge sukham āsīnaḥ krīḍate kālam akṣayam ||
yena tāmramayaṃ dattam āsanaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi |
sa vai divyāsanārūḍho na hi pracyavate ciram ||
pradadyād āsanaṃ yas tu nirmitaṃ sāradārubhiḥ |
tasya nākagatasyoccaiḥ kalpyate bhavyam āsanam ||
[708]        viṣṇudharmottare |
yas tu bhadrāsanaṃ cāru pitṝṇāṃ pratipādayet |
sa vai siṃhāsanāsīnaś cyavate na cirād api ||
yas tv āsanaṃ vastramayaṃ haṃsapicchaiḥ susaṃbhṛtam |
prayacchati mahīpālās tam āsīnam upāsate ||
tūlaiḥ pūrṇodaraṃ vāstram āsanaṃ yo nivedayet ||
pitṝṇām ādarād enaṃ pratyāsīdanti saṃpadaḥ ||
yaḥ paṭṭagaddikāḥ śrāddhe gaddikāś ca manoharāḥ |
dadāti tasya hi svarge kalpyate divyam āsanam |
pitṝn uddiśya yogibhyo datvā kuśamayīr bṛśīḥ |
sarvasaṅgavinirmuko viśuddhātmā sa jāyate ||
caitrāsanāni citrāṇi pitṛbhyaḥ pratipādayet |
nīrogaḥ subhagaḥ śrīmān puruṣaḥ saṃprajāyate ||
yas tv āsanopayogārthaṃ pradadyāt kambalān navān |
aṣṭāṅgayogasaṃsiddhis tasya puṃso ’bhijāyate ||
yas tṛṇair mṛdubhiḥ ślakṣṇaṃ nirmāya śubham āsanam ||
dadyāc chrāddheṣu tasyāśu sthirāḥ syuḥ sarvataḥ śriyaḥ |
ity āsananirūpaṇam |
atha śayyāstaraṇapracchādanapaṭopadhānāni |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
śayyām āstaraṇopetām uttaracchadasaṃyutām |
upadhānena saṃyuktāṃ pitruddeśena yo dadet ||
[709]        modante pitaras tasya sukhinaḥ śāśvatīḥ samāḥ |
dātāpi svargam āsādya vimānaṃ divyam āsthitaḥ |
sevyate suranārībhir gīyamānaś ca kinnaraiḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
yo mañcakaṃ ca dadyād vai cāru tūlikayānvitam |
viśuddhācchādanapaṭaṃ ramaṇīyopadhānakam ||
pitṛbhyaḥ śraddhayā yuktaḥ śrāddhakāla upasthite |
sa triviṣṭapam āsādya divyabhogasamanvitam ||
sahasraiḥ suranārīṇāṃ ramate parivāritaḥ |
kālakṣayād ihāgatya nārīṇāṃ vallabho bhavet ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
śayyāṃ satūlikāṃ dadyāt pitṛkarmaṇi yo naraḥ |
ramayanty anuraktās taṃ svarge sāraṅgalocanāḥ ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
mañcakaṃ tūlikāyuktaṃ nirmitaṃ dārubhiḥ śubhaiḥ |
bhaktyā viprāya prayato dvijebhyaḥ pitṛkarmaṇi |
agryair apsarasāṃ vṛndair aśrāntaṃ paricaryate ||
āndolakaṃ sāstaraṇaṃ sopadhānaṃ prasundaram |
dhātujābhiḥ suramyābhiḥ śṛṅkhalābhiś ca saṃyutam ||
mattavāraṇaśobhāḍhyaṃ grathitaṃ mṛdubhiḥ paṭaiḥ |
dadāti pitṛkāryeṣu yo hi śraddhāparāyaṇaḥ ||
gandharvāpsarasāṃ loke gīyamāno nirantaram |
[710]        sa bhuṅkte vividhān bhogāṃs tridaśair api durlabhān ||
paṭṭān kāṣṭhamayān yaś ca śayaneṣūpayoginaḥ |
datvā svāstaraṇopetān mattavāraṇalāñchitān |
varṣāyutaṃ ca ramate loke vai viśvakarmaṇaḥ ||
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
dārubhir ghaṭitān tasyaiḥ svarṇapatrair alaṅkṛtām |
śayyāṃ dadāti yas tasya vāsaḥ śakrasya veśmani ||
bhūṣitāṃ pittalamayaiḥ patrachedair yathocitām |
śrāddhe śayyāṃ prayacched yaḥ sūryaloke sa rājate ||
gajadantamayaṃ divyaṃ śrāddhe datvā tu mañcakam |
gatvā cāndramasaṃ lokaṃ śaradām ayutaṃ vaset ||
paṭṭasūtramayaiḥ paṭṭair grathitāṃ ca dadāti yaḥ |
śayyāṃ pitṛbhyo medhāvī devīlokaṃ sa gacchati ||
kārpāsasūtrajaiḥ paṭṭaiḥ sudṛḍhāṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
candrasya bhavane so ’pi kāmān bhuṅkte yatheśitān ||
kṛtāṃ śanamayaiḥ paṭṭaiḥ sūtrair vā babajair api |
datvā janmāntare jātaḥ striyo vindati sundarīḥ ||
haṃsapicchamayīṃ tūlīṃ pitṛbhyaḥ pradadāti yaḥ |
gandharvāpsarasāṃ loke modate ’sau yathāsukham ||
kārpāsanirmitāṃ tūlīṃ datvā mṛdvīṃ suśobhanām |
upadhānena saṃyuktāṃ lakṣmīvān jāyate naraḥ ||
haṃsapicchamayaṃ ramyam upadhānaṃ dadāti yaḥ |
[711]        kīrttimān jāyate nityaṃ sukhānām api bhājanam ||
kṣaumaṃ vā paṭṭasūtraṃ vā yo dadyād uttarachadam |
lāvaṇyena samāyukto jāyate ’sau janapriyaḥ ||
yaḥ prayacched uttarapaṭaṃ sūkṣmakārpāsasūtrajam |
tasyāyur vipulaṃ loke prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ ||
mṛducarmamayīṃ dadyāt yo naraḥ paṭṭagaddikām |
so ’pi śriyā samāyukto nīrogo jāyate bhuvi ||
vicitraiś carmabhir yuktaṃ racitaṃ mṛdubhis tṛṇaiḥ |
pradāyāstaraṇaṃ śrāddhe na duḥkhaṃ paśyati kvacit ||
dadyād āstaraṇaṃ yas tu nirmitaṃ mṛdubhis tṛṇaiḥ |
śrāddhakāle tu yogibhyaḥ sa duḥkhair nābhibhūyate ||
iti śayyāstaraṇapracchādanapaṭopadhānāni |
atha cāmaravyajanadarpaṇakeśaprasādhanāni |
bṛhadviṣṇupurāṇe |
cāmaravyajanaṃ caiva bhaktyā yaḥ pratipādayet |
sa vai divaspater loke bahukālaṃ mahīyate ||
rājopakaraṇaṃ hy etat pitṝṇām api vallabham |
ato vidvān prayatnena tebhyo dadyād atandritaḥ ||
pradāya darpaṇaṃ tebhyaś cakṣuṣmān abhijāyate |
keśaprasādhanaṃ datvā tathā kūrcapramādhanam ||
supuṣṭāṃ rūpasāmagrīṃ labhate so ’nyajanmani |
saurapurāṇe |
[712]        cāmaraṃ tālavṛntaṃ ca śvetachatraṃ ca darpaṇam |
datvā pitṝṇām etāni bhūmipālo bhaved iha ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
keśaprasādhanīṃ ramyāṃ dadyāt kaṅkatikāṃ tu yaḥ |
ādarśaṃ nirmalaṃ caiva pūrṇacandrasamākṛtim |
śrāddhakāle prasannātmā tasya svarge ’kṣayā gatiḥ ||
gajadantanirmitaṃ keśasaṃskārasādhanaṃ 'kaṅkatikā' |
vārāhapurāṇe |
cāmarīdhenulāṅgūlaśvetabālasamudbhavam |
cāmīkaravicitreṇa tathā daṇḍena nirmitam ||
pitṛbhyaś cāmaraṃ datvā svarge strībhis tu vījyate |
tad eva kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu datvā bhūmipatir bhavet ||
mayūrapicchanirmāṇaṃ hemadaṇḍaṃ tu cāmaram |
pratipādya pitṛbhyas tu rājarājo ’bhijāyate ||
bālair dīrghair atiśvetair aśvabāladhisaṃbhavaiḥ |
nirmitaṃ cāmaraṃ śrāddhe datvā māṇḍaliko bhavet ||
kṛṣṇāśvabālaracitaṃ cāmaraṃ yas tu yacchati |
so ’pi puṇyena teneha dhanī bhavati dharmabhāk ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
dvijebhyaś cāmaraṃ dadyāt sthāpayed cāmarālaye |
pitṝn uddiśya yas tasya pitaraḥ sarvakāmadāḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
[713]        vyajanaṃ tālavṛntaṃ ca datvā viprāya saṃskṛtam |
prāpnuyāt sparśayuktāni sugandhīni mṛdūni ca ||
'tālavṛntaṃ' vastravyajanam |
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
nānākaṣāyadhautena nirmitaṃ mṛducarmaṇā |
citrarāgair anekaiś ca svarṇadaṇḍena śobhitam ||
dantidantamayenāpi daṇḍena suvirājitam |
vyajanaṃ ye prayacchanti te vai gatvāmarāvatīm |
nārībhir abhivījyante nityaṃ satkaṅkaṇaiḥ karaiḥ ||
mayūrapicchaghaṭitaṃ vyajanaṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
pitṛbhyaḥ so’ pi dehānte skandaloke mahīyate ||
racitaṃ bālakenātha yad vośīreṇa nirmitam |
pradāya vyajanaṃ śrāddhe manastāpaṃ na vindati ||
vetrapaṭṭena racitaṃ vastrair anyair athāpi vā |
prayacchet tālavṛntaṃ yaḥ sa bhūpālo na saṃśayaḥ ||
tālīdalair viracitaṃ kṛtaṃ bhūrjatvacātha vā |
pradāya vyajanaṃ śrāddhe mahad ārogyam aśnute ||
kṛtaṃ ca vidalacchedaiḥ susūkṣmaiś caiva gumphitam |
datvā pitṛbhyo vyajanam anantaṃ sukham aśnute ||
gāruḍapurāṇe |
samarpya darpaṇaṃ bhaktyā pitṛbhyaḥ pitṛkarmaṇi |
bhuvi prakāśo bhavati yaśobhir abhipūjitaḥ ||
[714]        bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
darpaṇaṃ kaladhautena nirmalena sunirmitam |
pratipādya pitṛbhyo vai lokaṃ cāndramasaṃ vrajet ||
vimalenāpi kāṃsyena pañcāśatpalikena tu |
kalpitaṃ darpaṇaṃ datvā tejasvī jāyate dhruvam ||
triṃśatpalena kāṃsyena kṛtam ādarśamaṇḍalam |
pañcaviṃśatijaṃ vāpi datvā vai kāntimān bhavet ||
yo darpaṇaṃ viracitaṃ kāṃsyasya daśabhiḥ palaiḥ |
pratipādayate so ’pi labhate cakṣur uttamam ||
skandapurāṇe |
keśaprasādhanaṃ datvā karidantavinirmitam |
pitṛkarmaṇi dharmātmā so ’śvinor lokam aśnute ||
tālasya nālikerasya veṇor vetrasya vā punaḥ |
śalākābhir viracitaṃ datvā keśaprasādhanam |
subhagaś ca suveśaś ca niścitaṃ jāyate naraḥ ||
dāruṇā nirmitaṃ datvā keśasaṃskārasādhanaṃ
prāpnoti sundarān keśān dīrgham āyuś ca vindati ||
varāharomaracitāṃ śmaśruvaiśadyakāriṇīm |
pitṛbhyaḥ kūrcikāṃ datvā surūpaḥ subhago bhavet ||
iti cāmaravyajanadarpaṇakeśaprasādhananirūpaṇam |
athānnamadhuphalapātrapātrādhārapatadgrahatāmbūlatatpātrāṇi |
śrāddham adhikṛtya brahmavaivarte |
[715]        taruṇādityasaṅkāśaṃ vimānaṃ sārvakāmikam |
annado labhate tisraḥ kanyākoṭyas tathaiva ca ||
annadānāt paraṃ dānaṃ vidyate neha kiṃcana |
annād bhūtāni jāyante jīvanti ca na saṃśayaḥ ||
jīvadānāt paraṃ dānaṃ na kiṃcid iha vidyate |
annāj jīvati trailokyaṃ tena tat paramaṃ bhavet ||
annāl lokāḥ pratiṣṭhante lokadānasya tat phalam |
anaṃ prajāpatiḥ sākṣāt tena sarvam idaṃ tatam |
tasmād annasamaṃ dānaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
bahuprakārāṇy annāni mṛṣṭāni ca mṛdūni ca |
phalāni caiva svādūni yathākālodbhavāni ca ||
nivedayet pitṛbhyo yaḥ saṃbhṛtya svayam ādarāt |
sa pitṝṇāṃ prasādena brahmasāyujyam aśnute ||
nāradīyapurāṇe |
bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ ca lehyaṃ ca peyaṃ coṣyaṃ tathaiva ca |
pañcaprakāraṃ hy aśanaṃ dravyagandharasānvitam ||
drākṣākadalanāraṅgasahakārādikāni ca |
varṇagandharasasparśasaṃpannāni phalāny api |
samarpayec chrāddhakāle mantrajihveṣu yatnataḥ ||
tena tṛptiṃ parāṃ yānti pitaraḥ sapitāmahāḥ |
kāmān asmai prayacchanti durlabhān anyakarmaṇā ||
[716]        yas tu bhojanapātrāṇi pitṛbhyaḥ pratipādayet |
sauvarṇarājatādīni tathā kāṃsyamayāny api |
sa pumān pātratām eti sarvāsām api saṃpadām ||
dhātvādinirmitā bhuktipātrādhārās tripādikāḥ |
utsṛjya svapitṛprītyai prāpayed brāhmaṇālayam |
sa jñātīnām aśeṣāṇām ādhāratvaṃ prapadyate ||
patadgrahaṃ dhātumayaṃ ṣṭhīvanācamanādiṣu |
upayuktaṃ śrāddhakāle pitṛbhyaḥ parikalpya tu |
dvijasya bhavane sthāpya śriyam āpnoti puṣkalām ||
vahnipurāṇe |
annaṃ brahma samuddiṣṭam akhilaiḥ śrutipāragaiḥ |
tac ca bhūriguṇopetaṃ devaloke ’pi durlabham ||
tat pitṝṇām atiprītyā yaḥ prayacchati bhūriśaḥ |
aśeṣakleśanirmuktaḥ sa yāti brahma śāśvatam ||
tāmbūlaṃ yo dadātīha śucikarpūrasaṃyutam |
aiśvaryaṃ so ’tivipulaṃ paratreha ca vindati ||
yaś cūrṇaparṇapūgādisthāpanārthāni kṛtsnaśaḥ |
dadyāt tāmbūlapātrāṇi pūgādyaiḥ pūritāni ca ||
tathā karpūrabhāṇḍaṃ ca karpūreṇābhipūritam |
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ hi bhuṅkte bhogān bhuvi sthitaḥ ||
padmapurāṇe |
annaṃ hi prāṇināṃ prāṇāḥ annam evāmṛtaṃ divi |
[717]        annadaḥ prāṇadaḥ prokto hy annado ’mṛtadas tathā ||
tasmāt putreṇa kartavyaṃ pitrarthaṃ bhūribhojanam |
bhūryannadātā puruṣo na mantavyo hi mānuṣaḥ |
tasya nirdagdhapāpasya nivāso vaiṣṇave pure ||
phalāni ca sugandhīni pakvāni madhurāṇi ca |
mṛdvīkādīni yo dadyāt pitṛbhyaḥ praṇayānvitaḥ |
sa svarge nandanodyānaphalapīyūṣabhāg bhavet ||
utkṛṣṭeḥ kramukaiś caiva nāgavallīdalair api |
kalpitaṃ ghanasārādisugandhidravyasaṃyutam |
datvā pitṛbhyas tāmbūlam anantāṃ labhate śriyam ||
nānnāt parataraṃ kiṃcit prāṇināṃ vidyate priyam |
tāmbūlāc ca paraṃ nāsti bhogeṣu pravaraṃ yataḥ ||
tenaitad ubhayaṃ deyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tṛptim icchatā |
ārogyam annado yāti bhogī tāmbūlado bhavet ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
jāmbūnadamayaṃ divyaṃ vimānaṃ sūryasaṃnibham |
divyāpsarobhiḥ saṃkīrṇam annado labhate ’kṣayam ||
bhagavatīpurāṇe |
annaṃ hi prāṇināṃ prāṇāḥ suprasiddham idaṃ vacaḥ |
tasmād annaprado yas tu prāṇān eva dadāti saḥ ||
tasmād annaṃ pradātavyaṃ pitṝṇām api bhūriśaḥ |
ārogyam annnado yāti dīrgham āyuś ca vindati ||
[718]        sahyādrikhaṇḍe |
oṣadhīnāṃ hi sarvāsāṃ paramo vai raso madhu |
sarvānnānām ayaṃ sāro viśruto madhusaṃjñayā ||
devānām ca pitṝṇāṃ ca tad etad ativallabham |
ataḥ śrāddhaṃ na kartavyaṃ yat kiṃcin madhunā vinā ||
madhunā rahite śrāddhe nirāśāḥ pitaro gatāḥ |
devāś ca pitaraś caiva madhunā tarpitā dhruvam ||
sudhārasena tṛpyanti yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam |
kathaṃcid yadi viprebhyaḥ pradattaṃ bhojane madhu |
piṇḍās tu naiva dātavyāḥ kadācin madhunā vinā ||
plutena madhusarpirbhyāṃ yair dattāḥ pāyasena vai |
piṇḍāḥ pitṛbhyas te paitrāt samuttīrṇā ṛṇārṇavāt ||
yair vā maghātrayodaśyāṃ madhunā pāyasena ca |
vihitaḥ piṇḍanirvāpaḥ kṛtakṛtyās tu te narāḥ ||
annādyair madhusaṃpṛktaiḥ saktubhir vā madhuplutaiḥ |
yair nirūptā naraiḥ piṇḍā gayāśīrṣe mahātmabhiḥ ||
