kalpakoṭiśataṃ sāgraṃ nānābhogasamanvitāḥ |
te svīyaiḥ pūrvajaiḥ sārdhaṃ brahmaloke nivāsinaḥ ||
madhu madhv iti vai śrāddhe triḥsamuccaritaṃ vacaḥ |
śrāvaṇān madhunāmnas taiḥ pitaraḥ syuḥ sutarpitāḥ ||
ato ’rthaṃ madhu śaṃsanti dharmajñāḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ vāpi kāṃsyenāpy atha nirmitam ||
[719]        śaktyā bhojanapātraṃ tu viśālaṃ cātinirmalam |
ghṛtādipātrair laghubhir yutaṃ svarṇādisaṃbhavaiḥ ||
hemādidhātusaṃbhūte sthitam ādhārayantrake |
patadgrahābhidhānena malapātreṇa cānvitam ||
yo dadāti dvijāgrebhyaḥ pitṝṇām idam astv iti |
suciraṃ sa divi sthitvā samrāḍ bhavati bhūtale ||
vāyupurāṇe |
pātraṃ vai taijasaṃ dadyān manojñaṃ śrāddhabhojinām |
pātraṃ bhavati kāmānāṃ vidyānāṃ ca dhanasya ca ||
rājataṃ kāñcanaṃ caiva dadyāc chrāddheṣu yaḥ pumān |
datvā sa labhate dānāt prākāmyaṃ dhanam eva ca ||
raupyaṃ rajatayukaṃ vā pitṝṇāṃ pātram ucyate |
pitṝn etena dānena satputrās tārayanty uta ||
lavaṇena tu pūrṇāni śrāddhe pātrāṇi dāpayet |
rasāḥ samupatiṣṭhanti bhakṣyaṃ saubhāgyam eva ca ||
tilapūrṇani yo dadyāt pātrāṇīha dvijanmanām |
tile tile niṣkaśataṃ sa dadāti na saṃśayaḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
surabhidravyatailais tu gandhavadbhis tathaiva ca |
pūrayitvā tu pātrāṇi śrāddhe satkṛtya dāpayet ||
gandhavahā mahānadyaḥ sukhāni vividhāni ca |
dātāram upatiṣṭhanti yuvatyaś ca pativratāḥ ||
[720]        narasiṃhapurāṇe |
phalāni kadalādaīni pitṝṇāṃ yaḥ prayacchati |
pitṛbhaktasya loke ’smin saphalaṃ tasya jīvitam ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
vṛkṣair manoharaphalair latābhiś ca samākulān |
ārāmān ye prayacchanti pitṛbhyo jalapūrvakam |
te cakravartino bhūtvā praśāsanti vasundharām ||
ye puṣpavāṭikāṃ vāpi vṛkṣaiḥ katipayair yutām |
prayacchanti pitṛbhyas te bhūmipālā na saṃśayaḥ ||
ye ’py ekaṃ phalitaṃ vṛkṣaṃ latāmaṇḍapam eva vā |
prayacchanti pitṛprītyai brāhmaṇānāṃ mahātmanāṃ |
bahuputrā bahudhanās te dṛśyante mahītale ||
ye tu krūtvātha labdhvā vā phalāny ādāya bhaktitaḥ |
pitṝṇāṃ saṃprayacchanti dhaninas te ’pi niścitam ||
skandapurāṇe |
ye tu pūgadrumān bhavyān tāmbūlīmaṇḍapān api |
pitṛbhyo dadate teṣām apsarobhiḥ saha sthitiḥ ||
pūrṇaṃ kramukapālībhiḥ kramukāvāpabhājanam |
pūgavicchedaniryantrair yutaṃ kālāyasodbhavaiḥ ||
parṇasthāpanapātraṃ ca pūrṇaṃ parṇaiḥ samujjvalaiḥ |
tathā karpūrapātraṃ cety evamādīni kṛtsnaśaḥ (?) |
samāhṛtya dvijāgrebhyaḥ śrāddheṣu pratipādayet |
[721]        tāmbūlagrahaṇārthaṃ ca dravyaṃ saṃvatsarocitam |
pūrṇe saṃvatsare bhūyaḥ sarvam eva pradāpayet ||
evaṃ yaḥ kurute tasya pitaraḥ prītamānasāḥ |
āyuḥ prajāṃ dhanaṃ tasmai prayacchante sukhāni ca ||
ity annamadhuphalapātrapātrādhārapatadgrahatāmbūlatatpātranirūpaṇam |
atha hiraṇyālaṅkārayānāni |
nandipurāṇe |
sarveṣām api dānānāṃ hiraṇyam adhikaṃ smṛtam |
prabhavanti hi sarve ’pi padārthās tu hiraṇyataḥ ||
ato hiraṇyaṃ dātavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |
ataḥ saṃpūjya gandhādyair vastrādyair abhipūjya ca ||
hiraṇyaṃ saṃpradātavyam idam asmai svadheti hi |
dakṣiṇādau tu rajataṃ pitre karmaṇi śasyate ||
alaṃkārāḥ pradātavyā yathāśaki hiraṇmayāḥ |
keyūrahārakaṭakamudrikākuṇḍalādayaḥ ||
strīśrāddheṣu pradeyāḥ syur alaṅkārās tu yoṣitām |
mañjīramekhalādāmakarṇikākaṅkaṇādayaḥ ||
hāramāṇikyavaidūryamuktāgārutmatādibhiḥ |
ratnair viracitāḥ svacchair alaṃkārā manoharāḥ |
pitṛbhyaḥ saṃpradātavyā nijavittānurūpataḥ ||
yānāni śivikāgantrīturaṅgādīni yatnataḥ |
śrāddhe deyāni viduṣā svasāmarthyānusārataḥ ||
[722]        annāni ca vicitrāṇi svādūni salilāni ca |
dātavyāni yathākāmaṃ pitṝn modayatā sadā ||
evaṃ yaḥ kurute śrāddhe śraddhayā dhārmikottamaḥ |
prakṣīṇāśeṣapāpasya tasya saṃśuddhacetasaḥ |
vicchinnakleśajālasya muktir evāmalaṃ phalam ||
ādityapurāṇe |
hiraṇyam amṛtaṃ sākṣād devarūpam iti śrutiḥ |
hiraṇyadātā śrāddhe syāt pitṝṇām amṛtapradaḥ ||
ato hiraṇyaṃ dātavyaṃ pitṝṇāṃ prītaye sadā |
rajataṃ vā yathāśakti teṣāṃ tad ativallabham ||
pitṝn saṃpūjya cācchādya hiraṇyaṃ pradadāti yaḥ |
tulādānasamaṃ puṇyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
rajatasya pradānena gosahasraphalaṃ labhet |
dakṣiṇārthaṃ pṛthag deyaṃ svarṇaṃ rūpyam athāpi vā |
tenāsya vardhate lakṣmīr āyur dīrghaṃ ca vindati ||
vāyupurāṇe |
rajatasya kathā vāpi darśanaṃ dānam eva ca |
viśeṣād akṣayaṃ svargaṃ rājataṃ dānam ucyate ||
bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
yo dadāti suvarṇaṃ vai rūpyaṃ vā pitṛpūjakaḥ |
[723]        tasya divyāyutaṃ pūrṇaṃ nivāsaḥ kāñcanācale ||
dadāti rajataṃ vāpi yaḥ pitṝṇāṃ mahāmatiḥ |
tasyāpi vardhate vaṃśaḥ pitṝṇāṃ hi prasādataḥ ||
alaṃkārāṃs tathānarghān pitṝṇāṃ bhaktipūrvakam |
yo dadāti mahābhāgaḥ surastrīṇāṃ suvallabhaḥ ||
jāmbūnadena śuddhena yaḥ kṛtvā bhūṣaṇāni vai |
prayacchati dvijāgryebhyaḥ śrāddhakāle viśuddhadhī |
yaśobhāgī bahudhanaḥ sa naro jāyate bhṛśam ||
alaṃkārān kṛtān dadyād yas tu ratnair mahādhanaiḥ |
sa bhūmipālo bhavati sarvasāmantavanditaḥ |
tataḥ kāle bahutare viṣṇoḥ sālokyam āpnuyāt ||
gāruḍapurāṇe |
alaṃkāraṃ mahāmulyaṃ pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
tasya puṇyavataḥ puṇyaṃ gaṇyate kena sattama ||
yas tu yānāni ramyāṇi sukhahetuni vāhane |
dadyāt pitṛbhyas tasya syād vimānaiśvaryam adbhutam ||
skandapurāṇe |
mūrdhālaṃkaraṇaṃ datvā śrāddhe bahudhanocitam |
mūrdhābhiṣikto bhavati pṛthivyāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||
karṇabhūṣaṇadānena niścitaṃ syād bahuśrutaḥ |
kaṇṭhālaṃkṛtidātā tu vāgmī syān madhurasvaraḥ ||
[724]        medhāvī jāyate vidvān dattair hṛdayabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
jāyate bāhubhūṣābhiḥ pradattābhir mahābalaḥ ||
hastālaṃkaraṇaṃ datvā dātā bhavati viśrutaḥ |
sa viśvavandyo bhavati yo dadyāt pādabhūṣaṇam ||
svargacyutānām etāni phalāny uktāni sūribhiḥ |
pitṛbhūṣaṇadānasya svargo mukhyatamaṃ phalam |
rajatair bhūṣaṇair dattair na muktir api durlabhā || ||
brahmapurāṇe |
samādiṣṭaṃ (?) viśiṣṭaṃ ca tat tad deyaṃ pitṝn prati |
tathāpy annaṃ jalaṃ vastraṃ bhūṣaṇāni viśeṣataḥ |.
yānāny api pradeyāni pitṝṇāṃ parituṣṭaye ||
matsyapurāṇe |
śrāddhe vāsāṃsi deyāni śayanāny āsanāni ca |
dātavyānīha viduṣā yānāni tu viśeṣataḥ ||
padmapurāṇe |
sudurgamāḥ pramītānāṃ panthānaḥ pāralaukikāḥ |
yānadānād ṛte te vai na śakyante vilaṅghituṃ |
ato yānāni deyāni pitṛbhyo vaṃśajanmabhiḥ ||
viṣṇudharmottare |
śivikāṃ yaḥ prayacchet tu sarvopakaraṇair yutām |
dolāvāhanakarmabhyo vṛttiṃ saṃvatsarocitām ||
varṣaparyāptam aśanaṃ kuṭumbārthaṃ dvijasya tu |
[725]        chatrapradānam apy evaṃ kartavyaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi ||
bhūyo ’pi sarvaṃ dātavyaṃ varṣānte pitṛtuṣṭaye |
prativarṣaṃ sakṛd vāpi ya evaṃ kurute naraḥ |
vaimānikatvaṃ prāptasya tasyāvṛttir na vidyate ||
yas tu citragatiṃ dadyāt turagaṃ lakṣaṇānvitam |
śrāddheṣu tasya dehānte sūryaloke ’kṣayā sthitiḥ ||
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
dadyān mataṅgajaṃ yas tu yuvānaṃ cārulakṣaṇam |
sa loke lokapālānām ekaikam ayutaṃ vaset ||
tejasvinaṃ cārugatiṃ lakṣaṇyaṃ yas turaṅgamam |
dadyāt pitṛbhyo vijayaḥ tasyāpratihato bhuvi ||
rathaṃ dadāti yo ramyaṃ yugmair yukaṃ turaṅgamaiḥ |
ukṣabhir vā mahākāyais taruṇaiḥ sarvalakṣaṇaiḥ |
māhāhaveṣu kutrāpi na tasya syāt parājayaḥ ||
gantrīṃ vā śakaṭaṃ vāpi lauhacakrākṣakūvarām |
datvā pitṝṇāṃ prāpnoti dhanardhim atibhūyasīm ||
brahmavaivarte |
śrāddhe saṃmodate dattaiḥ syandanais tu sukhāvahaiḥ |
rājabhiḥ pūjyate vāpi dhanadhānyaiś ca vardhate ||
iti hiraṇyālaṃkārayānanirūpaṇam |
atha gomahiṣībalīvardoṣṭrādayaḥ |
matsyapurāṇe |
[726]        śrāddhe gāvo mahiṣyaś ca balīvardās tathaiva ca |
pradātavyā mahoṣṭrāś ca yac cānyad vastu śobhanam ||
padmapurāṇe |
gomahiṣyādayo deyāḥ padārthaḥ śrāddhakarmaṇi |
prītiḥ śrāddheṣu kartavyā pitṝṇāṃ sārvakāmikī ||
nānāpadārthair yaḥ śrāddhe pitṝṃs tu paritoṣayet |
padārthās tasya sarve ’pi nityaṃ gṛham upāsate ||
bṛhadviṣṇupurāṇe |
taruṇīṃ sukhasaṃdohyāṃ jīvavatsāṃ payasvinīm |
dadāti dhenuṃ viprebhyaḥ tuṣṭim uddiśya paitṛkīm |
yas tasya vā diviṣṭasya sarvakāmadughā bhavet ||
suśīlāṃ lakṣaṇvatīṃ savatsāṃ bahudohanām |
datvā pitṛbhyaḥ kapilāṃ ghaṣṭācāmarabhūṣitām ||
muñjavalkād yatheṣīkā pṛthag bhavati nirmalā |
evaṃ sa sarvapāpebhyaḥ pṛthag bhavati nirmalaḥ ||
ayutānāṃ śataṃ sāgraṃ brahmaloke mahīyate |
dadāti yaḥ sumahiṣīm avyaṅgāṅgīm akopanām |
bhūrikṣīrāṃ guṇavatīṃ sāpatyāṃ bahusarpiṣām ||
kṣīrasya sarpiṣo dadhnaḥ paripūrṇā divi hradāḥ |
pitṝṇām upatiṣṭhante yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam |
dātāpi svargam āpnoti varṣāṇām ayutāni ṣaṭ ||
yas tu dhuryān balīvardān pṛṣṭhībhāravahān api |
[727]        aviddhanāsikān dadyād akṣuṇavṛṣaṇāṃs tathā ||
vṛṣarūpaḥ svayaṃ dharmas tasya sākṣāt prasīdati |
prasanne bhagavaty asmin kiṃ kiṃ nāma na labhyate ||
kramelakān bhāravahān bahuyojanagāminaḥ |
ye ’laṃkṛtya prayacchanti rājānas te na saṃśayaḥ ||
ajāś caivāvayaś caiva mahiṣā bhāravāhinaḥ |
pitṛbhyaḥ saṃpradātavyāḥ sarvapāpakṣayārthinā |
nāradīyapurāṇe |
datvā pitṛbhyaḥ śvetāṃ gāṃ śvetadvīpe mahīyate |
pradāya dhenuṃ kṛṣṇāṅgīṃ yamalokaṃ na paśyati ||
pītavarṇāṃ tu gāṃ datvā na śocati kṛtākṛte |
pradāya rohiṇīṃ dhenuṃ sarvaṃ tarati duṣkṛtam |
nīlāṃ tu surabhīṃ datvā vaṃśachedaṃ na vindati ||
anyena yena kenāpi dhenuṃ varṇena lakṣitām |
datvā pitṛbhyo jayati nāke sukham anuttamam ||
balīvardān balavato yo dadāti dhurandharān |
samagraṃ bhūmidānasya phalam āpnoti mānavaḥ ||
pṛṣṭhe vahanti ye bhāram ukṣāṇo vahanakṣamāḥ |
tān prayacchan pitṝṇāṃ tu dhanāḍhyo jāyate bhṛśam ||
devīpurāṇe |
mahiṣīṃ puṣkalakṣīrāṃ pitṛbhyaḥ śastalakṣaṇām |
yaḥ prayacchati nāke ’sya sudhādhārā pravartate ||
[728]        mahiṣaṃ bhāravoḍhāraṃ yaḥ pitṝṇāṃ nivedayet |
tasya puṃso bhagavatī mahiṣaghnī prasīdati ||
skandapurāṇe |
uṣṭrīr vegavahā yas tu dadyād uṣṭrān athāpi vā |
tasya svargaṃ prayātasya gatir naiva vihanyate ||
paśūn ajāvikādīṃś ca yas tu śrāddhe prayacchati |
prajayā paśubhiś caiva gṛhaṃ suparipūryate ||
iti gomahiṣībalīvardoṣṭrādinirūpaṇam |
atha bhūgṛhapustakābhayāni |
brahmapurāṇe |
yathāśaktyā pradātavyā bhūmiḥ śrāddhe vipaścitā |
pitṝṇāṃ saṃpade sā hi sarvakāmaṃ prasūyate ||
bhūmipradaṃ hi puruṣaṃ prāhuḥ sarvapradaṃ janāḥ |
sarvapradaḥ pare loke sarvān kāmān samaśnute ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
bhūmipradānena samaṃ dānam anyat na vidyate |
ataḥ pitṝṇāṃ dātavyā bhūmir bhūtim abhīssatā ||
bhūmidānena pitaraḥ sarvān kāmān avāpya vai |
modante brahmaṇā sārdhaṃ yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
bhagavatīpurāṇe |
[729]        bhūmer bhavanti dhānyāni bhūmer vastrāṇi caiva hi|
bhūmer gāvo mahiṣyaś ca varāśvā varavāraṇāḥ ||
udyānāni ca ramyāṇi nadyaḥ kūpāḥ sarāṃsi ca |
ātapatrāṇi yānāni śayanāny āsanāni ca ||
annapānāni hṛdyāni gandhamālyāni caiva hi |
rūpyaṃ ca kanakaṃ caiva ratnāni vividhāni ca ||
alaṃkārā manojñāś ca yac cānyat sukhasādhanam |
prabhavas tasya sarvasya bhūmir eva na saṃśayaḥ ||
ato ’rthaṃ pitṛbhaktena mūmir deyā prayatnataḥ |
pitṝṇām akṣayaṃ tṛptim icchatā sārvakāmikīm ||
bhūmipradasya pitaro vairiñcaṃ lokam āsthitāḥ |
vicaranti vimānāgryair yathākāmapracāribhiḥ ||
bhūmer dātā ca puruṣaḥ pradhvastāśeṣakalmaṣaḥ |
prayāti brahmasālokyaṃ yad gatvā na nivartate ||
gāruḍapurāṇe |
ya icched akhilair bhāgaiḥ pitṝṃs tarpayituṃ naraḥ |
bhūmis tena pradātavyā pitṝn uddiśya dhīmatā ||
bhūmidānān naraḥ svarge krīḍate śāśvatīḥ samāḥ |
kālakṣayād ihāgatya bhūpatiḥ syāt pratāpavān ||
gṛhāṇi ca vicitrāṇi pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchati |
jāmbūnadamayaṃ divyaṃ yathākāmagamaṃ śubham |
sarvasaṃpatsamopetaṃ vimānaṃ so ’dhirohati ||
[730]        pustakāny api yaḥ śrāddhe pitṛbhyaḥ pratipādayet |
arhaṇāṃ labhate so ’pi sabhāyāṃ parameṣṭhinaḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
pitṝn bhaktyā samabhyarcya bhūmiṃ dadyāt samāhitaḥ |
bhūmidānena saṃtṛptā vidanty ārtiṃ na te kvacit ||
grāmaṃ vā kharvaṭaṃ vāpi pitṛbhyaḥ pradadāti yaḥ |
śakrasya bhavanaṃ gatvā yāvad indraṃ sa modate ||
śrāddhe dadāti yaḥ kṣetraṃ daśalāṅgalasaṃmitam |
pañcalāṅgalikaṃ vāpi yad vā gocarmamātrakam ||
alābhe dvihalaṃ vāpi halamātram athāpi vā |
lāṅgalaiḥ sabalīvardair yoktratotrādisaṃyutaiḥ ||
anyaiś caivopakaraṇai rajjuphālādibhir yutam |
vājapeyasya yajñasya sa phalaṃ prāpnute naraḥ |
kāle svargāt paribhraṣṭo bhūpatir dhārmiko bhavet ||
jālīnām atha vekṣūṇāṃ yavagodhūmayor api |
māṣamudgatilānāṃ ca kṣetram utpattihetu yat |
pitṝṇāṃ tan naro datvā viṣṇoḥ sāyujyam āpnuyāt ||
punar mānuṣyam āyāto dhanadhānyasamanvitaḥ |
tejasā yaśasā yukto vidvān vāgmī ca jāyate ||
gṛhaṃ pakveṣṭakacitaṃ sudhābhir dhavalīkṛtam |
mattavāraṇaśobhāḍhyaṃ gavākṣadvārabhittimat ||
[731]        anekabhūmisaṃyuktam ekabhūmikam eva vā |
pitṛbhyo yo dadātīha sa yāti brahmaviṣṭapaṃ ||
prāsādā yatra sauvarṇā nānāmaṇivibhūṣitāḥ |
tatrāsau ramate nityaṃ nṛtyavāditragāyanaiḥ |
divyaṃ varṣāyutaśataṃ tatraiva parivartate ||
datvā gṛhaṃ pitṛbhyas tu tṛṇacchannam athāpi vā |
labhate ’gryāṇi veśmāni strīmanti dhanavanti ca ||
prāsādān keśavādīnāṃ nirmāya sumanoharān |
pitroḥ pitāmahādibhyo yas tu śreyaḥ samarpayet ||
vaikuṇṭhādiṣu lokeṣu svakīyaiḥ pitṛbhiḥ saha |
modate ’sau mahābhāgo yāvad indrāś caturdaśa ||
pustakāni suvācyāni sacchāstrāṇāṃ dadāti yaḥ |
brāhmaṇānāṃ kule yajvā jāyate ’sau bahuśrutaḥ ||
brahmavaivarte |
śrāddhakāle guṇavatāṃ viprāṇāṃ samupasthite |
dadyāc chāstrāṇi dharmyāṇi smṛtiṃ medhāṃ ca vindati ||
camatkārakhaṇḍe |
yaḥ pustakāni viprāṇāṃ pitrarthaṃ vinivedayet |
sa vidvān jāyate vāgmī medhāvī ca bahuśrutaḥ ||
pustakāni pitṛbhyas tu vedāntānāṃ dadāti yaḥ |
sa śrotriyānvaye bhūtvā jāyate vedavittamaḥ ||
datvā vyākaraṇaṃ tu syāc chaśvat brahmavidāṃ varaḥ |
[732]        mīmāṃsāyāḥ pradānena somayājī bhaven naraḥ ||
pradāya nyāyaśāstrāṇi bhaved vidvattamaḥ pumān |
purāṇadātā bhaktaḥ syāt purāṇapuruṣe harau ||
manvādidharmaśāstrāṇāṃ dānād bhavati dhārmikaḥ |
kalāśāstrapradānena kalāsu kuśalo bhavet ||
yaḥ śrāddhadivase vidvān prāṇinām abhayaṃ dadet |
bhayaṃ na tasya kiṃcit syād iha loke paratra ca ||
saurapurāṇe |
yad yasya bhayam utpannaṃ svato vā parato ’pi vā |
śrāddhakarmaṇi saṃprāpte tat tasyāpanayet sudhīḥ ||
rājataś caurato vāpi vyālāc ca śvāpadād api |
saṃjātāṃ tu hared bhītiṃ pitṛkarmaṇi śaktitaḥ ||
ekataḥ kratavaḥ sarve sarvasvavaradakṣiṇāḥ |
ekato bhayabhītasya prāṇinaḥ prāṇarakṣaṇam ||
ato ’rthaṃ sarvakāleṣu dadyād abhayadakṣiṇām |
śrāddhakāle viśeṣeṇa sa hi dharmaḥ paro mataḥ ||
yathā hy abhayadānena tuṣyanti prapitāmahāḥ |
na tathā vastrapānānnaratnālaṃkārakāñcanaiḥ ||
etasmād abhayaṃ deyaṃ śrāddhakāle vijānatā |
abhayasya pradātāro bhayaṃ vindanti na kvacit ||
janmamṛtyubhayābhāvād abhayaṃ mokṣa ucyate |
[733]        mokṣam eva naro yāti prāṇinām abhayapradaḥ ||
śrāddham adhikṛtya brahmavaivarte |
jīvitasya pradānād dhi nānyad dānaṃ viśiṣyate |
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena deyaṃ prāṇābhirakṣaṇam ||
ahiṃsā sarvadevatyaṃ pavitraṃ sarvapāvanam |
dānaṃ hi prāṇinām āhur dānānāṃ paramaṃ budhāḥ ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
yaḥ śrāddhe hy abhayaṃ dadyāt prāṇināṃ jīvitaiṣiṇām |
aśvadānasahastreṇa rathadānaśatena ca |
dantināṃ ca sahasreṇa abhayaṃ vai viśiṣyate ||
yāni ratnāni medinyāṃ vāhanāni striyas tathā |
kṣipraṃ prāpnoti tat sarvaṃ śrāddhakāle ’bhayaṃ dadat ||
bandhamokṣaṃ tu yaḥ kuryāt śrāddhakāle tu mānavaḥ |
na tasya gadituṃ śakyaṃ sukṛtaṃ sādhukarmaṇaḥ ||
vāmanapurāṇe |
bandīkṛtās tu ye kecit svayaṃ vā yadi vā paraiḥ |
yena kenāpy upāyena yas tān mocayate naraḥ |
pitaras tasya gacchanti śāśvataṃ padam avyayam ||
bandino mācayet tasmāt pitṛkāryeṣu yo naraḥ |
sa yāti viṣṇusāyujyaṃ nirmuktaḥ karmabandhanāt ||
iti bhūmigṛhapustakābhayanirūpaṇam |
[734]
athāñjanābhyañjanasūtrāṇi |
brahmavaivarte |
añjanābhyañjane caiva pitṛbhyaḥ pratipādayet |
sutraṃ cābhinavaṃ sūkṣmaṃ piṇḍānām upari nyaset ||
tatra traikakudaṃ śastam añjanaṃ pitṛkarmaṇi |
tilatailodbhavād dīpāt kajjalaṃ vāpi yaddhṛtam ||
'trikakut' nīlaparvataḥ, tatra bhavaṃ 'traikakudaṃ' sroto ’ñjanākhyanīlaparvataśilācūrṇajam ity arthaḥ |
tilatailena dātavyaṃ tathaivābhyañjanaṃ budhaiḥ |
kṣaumaṃ kāpīsajaṃ vāpi pitṝṇāṃ sūtram iṣyate ||
asāv aṅkṣva tathābhyaṅkṣvety añjanādīni dāpayet |
asāv etat ta ity evaṃ sūtraṃ cāpi nivedayet ||
ity evam añjanaṃ datvā cakṣuṣmān jāyate naraḥ |
abhyañjanapradānena labhate rūpam uttamam |
labhed vastrāṇy anantāni piṇḍasūtrapradānataḥ ||
vāyupurāṇe |
añjanābhyañjane caiva piṇḍanirvapaṇe tathā |
aśvamedhaphalenaiva saṃmitaṃ mantrapūrvakam |
kriyāḥ sarvā yathoddiṣṭāḥ prayatnena samācaret ||
prabhāsakhaṇḍa-vāyupurāṇayoḥ |
śreṣṭham āhus trikakudam añjanaṃ nityam eva ca |
[735]        dīpāt kṛṣṇatilodbhūtatailajād yac ca dhāritam ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
apasavyaṃ pitṛbhyas tu dadyād añjanam uttamam |
nāmāny uccārya sarveṣāṃ vastrārthaṃ sūtram eva ca ||
peṣayitvāñjanaṃ samyak vedyā uttarato budhaḥ |
gṛhya tad darbhapiñjūlais tribhiḥ kuryād yathāvidhi ||
tathā |
ekaṃ pavitraṃ haste syāt pitṝṇāṃ tu sakṛt sakṛt |
tailaṃ pātreṇa dātavyaṃ piṇḍebhyo ’bhyañjanaṃ hi tat ||
tathā |
kṣaumaṃ sūtraṃ navaṃ dadyāc chaṇakārpāsajaṃ tathā |
patrorṇaṃ paṭṭasūtraṃ ca kauśeyaṃ ca vivarjayet ||
‘patrorṇaṃ' dhautakauśeyam |
varjayet tu daśāḥ prājño yady etā hastavastrajāḥ |
na prīṇanti tathaitān hi dātuś cāpy aphalaṃ bhavet ||
āpastambasūtre tu vastradaśā api dātavyā ity uktam |
etāni vaḥ pitaro vāsāṃsy ato nānyat pitaro mayoṣṭeti vāsaso daśāṃ chittvā nidadhāty ūrṇāsukāṃ vā pūrve vayasy uttarāyuṣi svaṃ lometi |
'ūrṇāsukāḥ' meṣalomadaśāḥ | 'uttare vayasi' pañcāśadvarṣebhya ūrdhvam | svaṃ loma uraḥsaṃbhavam iti śeṣaḥ, “pañcāśata ūrdhvam uroloma yajamānasya” iti śāṭyāyanivacanāt |
kātyāyanasūtre | [736]
etad va ity apāsyati sūtrāṇi pratipiṇḍam ūrṇādaśā vā vayasy uttare yajamānalomāni veti |
brahmapurāṇe |
dadyāt krameṇa vāsāṃsi daśā vā śvetavastrajāḥ |
tato vayasi vṛddhe ca svāni lomāny athāpi vā ||
kṣaumaṃ sūtraṃ navaṃ dadyāc chāṇaṃ kārpāsam eva vā |
patrorṇanīlaraktāktakauśeyāni vivarjayet ||
nāgarakhaṇḍe |
sūtraṃ ca pratipiṇḍaṃ tu dadyāt teṣu pṛthak pṛthak |
anyat tu sarvapiṇḍeṣu satataṃ viniyojayet ||
na truṭyati yathā tantus tathā tan nikṣipec chanaiḥ |
virodham ācaret teṣāṃ troṭanāc ca parasparam ||
tac ca raktaṃ pradātavyaṃ rogair vā kuṅkumena vā |
raktasūtravihīnaṃ yat tat te śrāddhaṃ bhaved iti ||
brahmaṇaivaṃ varo dattaḥ kavyabhakṣāya rakṣase |
evaṃ dadāti yaḥ sūtraṃ so ’kṣayāṃ labhate śriyam ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
añjanābhyañjane gandhaṃ sūtrapraṇayanaṃ tathā |
kuśaiḥ punarbhavaiḥ kurvan aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
ity añjanābhyañjanasūtranirūpaṇam |
atha prakīrṇakam |
prabhāsakhaṇḍe |
[737]        loke śreṣṭhatamaṃ sarvam ātmanaś ca priyaṃ sadā |
tat tat pitṝṇāṃ dātavyaṃ tad evākṣayam icchatā |
vāyupurāṇa-brahmāṇḍapurāṇayoḥ |
gandhaiḥ puṣpais tathā dhūpais tathāhutibhir eva ca |
phalamūlanamaskāraiḥ pitṝṇāṃ prayataḥ śuciḥ |
pūjāṃ kṛtvā dvijān paścāt tarpayed annasaṃpadā ||
śrāddhakāleṣu niyataṃ vāyubhūtāḥ pitāmahāḥ |
āviśanti dvijān śreṣṭhān tasmād etad bravīmi te ||
kūrmapurāṇe |
pradadyād gandhamālyādi dhūpādīni ca śaktitaḥ |
tatraiva purāṇe viśvān devān prati pitṛvākyam |
puṣpair gandhais tathānnena yuṣmān agre 'rcayiṣyati |
agre datvā tu yuṣmākam asmākaṃ dāsyate punaḥ |
evaṃ kṛte kṛtaṃ samyak sarvam eva bhaviṣyati ||
vāyupurāṇe |
vastrair annapradānaiś ca bhakṣyapeyais tathaiva ca |
gobhir aśvais tathā grāmaiḥ pūjayed dvijasattamān ||
bhavanti pitaraḥ prītāḥ pūjiteṣu dvijātiṣu |
tasmād anena vidhinā pūjayeta dvijān sadā ||
vārāhapurāṇe |
[738]        gandhāḥ puṣpāṇi tāmbūladhūpadīpākṣatāni ca |
pitṛkṛtye praśastāni svādvannaṃ salilaṃ himam ||
etāni śraddhayopetaḥ pitṛbhyo yo nivedayet |
sa eva dharmaśīlo me priyakārī vasundhare ||
tathā |
upaveśyāsane śubhraṃ chatraṃ tatra prakalpayet |
āvaraṇārthaṃ ca tat chatraṃ brāhmaṇāya pradāpayet |
tato vastrāṇi ramyāṇi sarvāṇy ābharaṇāni ca ||
viṣṇupurāṇe pitṛvākyam |
ratnavastramahāyānaṃ sarvabhogādikaṃ vasu |
vibhave sati viprebhyo yo ’smān uddiśya dāsyati |
sa bhuktvā nikhilān bhogān ante svargaṃ gamiṣyati |
narasiṃhapurāṇe |
upānacchatravastrāṇi bhuktipātraṃ kamaṇḍalum |
śayanāsanayānāni darpaṇavyajanāni ca ||
annaṃ susaṃskṛtaṃ gandhaṃ tāmbūlaṃ dīpacāmaram |
pitṛbhyo yaḥ prayacchet tu śraddadhāno vimatsaraḥ |
sa śokamohau tarati prāpya jñānam anuttamam ||
nandipurāṇe |
annodake dhūpadīpavastramālyānulepanam |
gomahiṣyaś ca karabhabalīvardādikaṃ tathā ||
ādarśavyajanachatraśayanāsanapādukāḥ |
[739]        śivikā rathagantrīkā mukhavāsāś ca śobhanāḥ ||
manojñāḥ paṭavāsāś ca sugandhāś cūrṇamuṣṭayaḥ |
aṅgāradhānikāḥ śīte yogapaṭṭāś ca yaṣṭayaḥ ||
kaṭisūtrāṇi śikyāni mekhalāś caiva kambalāḥ |
yajñopavītaṃ pīṭhāni kaupīnaṃ kuṇḍikās tathā ||
vastūny etāny athānyāni pitṝṇāṃ prītaye budhaiḥ |
yathopayogaṃ deyāni brāhmaṇebhyo yathāvidhi ||
evaṃ dadāti yo bhaktyā yathālābhaṃ yathādhanam |
so ’śvamedhasya yajñasya phalaṃ prāpnoti mānavaḥ ||
skandapurāṇe |
alaṃkārān bahuvidhān kāñcanena vinirmitān |
ranair viracitāṃś cāpi kṣaumādīny aṃśukāny api ||
karpūrāgurukastūrīkuṅkumādīni yāny api |
gandhadravyāṇi ramyāṇi śrīkhaṇḍaśakalāny api ||
karpūrādeś ca bhāṇḍāni tāmbūlāvapanaṃ tathā |
dīpapātraṃ dhūpapātraṃ tathā bhojanabhājanam |
bhājanādhārayantrāṇi patadgrāhas tathaiva ca ||
āndolakān mañcakāṃś ca mṛdulā haṃsatulikā |
kaṭāhās tāmradhātūtthās tāmrajāḥ kalaśās tathā ||
carukā darvikā lauhī bhāṇḍāni vividhāni ca |
tāmrādidhātusaṃbhūtāḥ gadūkāḥ karakās tathā ||
bhadrapīṭhāni pīṭhāni gaddikāś cāsanāni ca |
[740]        vastrapūrṇāś ca mañjūṣā manojñāś ca vihaṅgikāḥ ||
mīmāṃsādeś ca śāstrasya pustakāni navāni ca |
saudhāni (?) kamanīyāni vāpīkūpādikaṃ tathā ||
grāmān kṣetrāṇi kedārān udyānāny atha vāṭikāḥ |
vṛkṣān latāvitānāni pūgādidrumavāṭikāḥ ||
śakaṭān sopakaraṇān lāṅgalāni ca bhūriśaḥ |
musalolūkhalaṃ dātraṃ śilā manthānamaṇḍakam ||
tathāñjanaśalākāś ca keśānāṃ ca prasādhanam |
etān padārthān anyāṃś ca caturāśramakāṅkṣitān ||
ātmanaś ca priyān dadyāt pitṝṇāṃ yuktam mānasaḥ (?) |
vibhavasyānusāreṇa deśakālānurūpataḥ ||
etān dadyāt tu yaḥ samyak padārthān bhogasādhanān |
na tasya durlabhaṃ kiṃcid iha loke paratra ca ||
rāmāyaṇe |
samatīte daśāhe tu kṛtaśauco vidhānataḥ |
cakre dvādaśakaṃ śrāddhaṃ trayodaśakam eva ca ||
tataś coddiśya pitaraṃ brāhmaṇebhyo dadau dhanam |
mahārhāṇi ca ratnāni gāś ca vāhanam eva ca ||
atra bharata iti śeṣaḥ |
brahmavaivarte |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi dānāni saphalāni ca |
tāraṇāni pitṝṇāṃ vai svargyāṇy ātmasukhāni ca ||
[741]        loke śreṣṭhatamaṃ yac ca ātmanaś cāpi yat priyam |
sarvaṃ pitṝṇāṃ dātavyaṃ teṣām evāśiṣorthinā ||
vāpīsaṃkrīḍanaṃ datvā mahāsatraphalaṃ labhet |
vanaṃ puṣpaphalopetaṃ datvā gomedham aśnute ||
tathā |
dadyād yaḥ śiśire vahniṃ bahukāṣṭhaṃ prayatnataḥ |
kāyāgnidīptiprākāśyaṃ rūpaṃ saubhāgyam eti saḥ ||
indhanāni tu yo dadyād dvijebhyaḥ śiśirāgame |
nityaṃ jayati saṃgrāme śriyā yuktaś ca dīpyate ||
tathā |
śaraṇaṃ sarvasaṃpūrṇaṃ saśayyāsanabhojanam |
śrāddhe datvā yatibhyas tu nākapṛṣṭhe mahīyate ||
‘śaraṇam' āvāsaḥ |
muktāvaidūryavāsāṃsi ratnāni vividhāni ca |
vāhanāni ca divyāni jāyante tasya koṭiśaḥ ||
gandharvāpsarasas taṃ ca gāyante vādayanti ca |
sumahajjvalanaprakhyaṃ sarvakāmasamanvitam ||
sūryacandraprabhaṃ divyaṃ vibhānaṃ labhate ’kṣayam |
apsarobhiḥ parivṛtaṃ kāmagaṃ tu manojavam ||
vasate sa vimānāgre stūyamānaḥ samantataḥ |
divyaiḥ puṣyaiḥ prasiñcanti cūrṇamuṣṭibhir eva ca ||
kanyāyuvatyas tāsāṃ ca hāsābharaṇanisvanaiḥ |
[742]        sukhinas te vibudhyanti satataṃ hi manoramaiḥ |
aśvadānasahastreṇa rathadānaśatena ca |
dantidānasahasrāc ca yogiṣv āvastho varaḥ ||
aśvamedhasahasreṇa rājasūyaśatena ca |
puṇḍarīkasahasreṇa yogiṣv āvastho varaḥ ||
tathā |
bhojanāgrāsanaṃ datvā atithibhyaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ |
sarvayajñakratūnāṃ ca phalaṃ prāpnoty anuttamam ||
kṣipram atyuṣṇam akliṣṭaṃ svādu dattaṃ bubhukṣite |
savyañjanaṃ sadā snigdhaṃ satkṛtya ca prayatnataḥ ||
pratiśrayaṃ sadā dadyād atithibhyaḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ |
devāvāsaṃ pratiṣṭhanti divyātithyaiḥ sahasraśaḥ ||
sarvāṇy etāni yo dadyāt pṛthivyām ekarāḍ bhavet |
tribhir dvābhyām athaikena dānena tu sukhī bhavet ||
dānāni paramo dharmaḥ sadbhiḥ satkṛtya pūjitaḥ |
trailokyasyādhipatyaṃ hi dānād eva dhruvaṃ sthitiḥ ||
rājyabhraṣṭas tu rājā syād adhanaḥ svottamaṃ dhanam |
kṣīṇāyur labhate cāyuḥ pitṛbhaktaḥ sadā naraḥ ||
yān kāmān manasā cec chettāṃs tasya pitaro daduḥ |
yāni ratnāni medinyāṃ vāhanāni striyas tathā |
kṣipram āpnoti tat sarvaṃ pitṛbhakas tu mānavaḥ ||
mahābhārate |
annapānāśvagovastrachatraśayyāsanāni ca |
pretaloke praśastāni dānāny aṣṭau viśeṣataḥ ||
dāsīdāsān alaṃkārān bhogopakaraṇāni ca |
datvā pitṛbhyaḥ prāpnoti sāmrājyaṃ neha saṃśayaḥ ||
tathā |
ity uktvā dharmarājas tu vāsudevasya dhīmataḥ |
mātulasya ca vṛddhasya rāmādīnāṃ tathaiva ca |
śrāddhāny uddiśya sarveṣāṃ cakāra vidhivat tadā ||
dadau ratnāni vāsāṃsi grāmān aśvān rathān api |
strīś caiva dvijamukhyebhyas tathā śatasahasraśaḥ ||
alaṃkārāṃs tathāśvāṃś ca gāś ca kāmyā varastriyaḥ |
ādiśyādiśya viprebhyo dadau sa nṛpasattama ||
droṇaṃ sātyaṃ ca bhīṣmaṃ ca somadattaṃ ca vāhnikam |
duryodhanaṃ ca rājānaṃ putrāṃś caiva pṛthak pṛthak ||
jayadrathapurogāṃś ca suhṛdaś caiva sarvaśaḥ |
ādiśyādiśya viprebhyo dadau sa nṛpasattama ||
tathā sa putrapautrāṇāṃ pitṝṇām ātmanas tathā |
gāndhārasya mahātejāḥ pradadau caurdhvadehike ||
śrāddham evopakramya viṣṇudharmottare pitṛgāthāḥ |
api syāt sa kule ’smākaṃ kaścit puruṣasattamaḥ |
dadyāt kṛṣṇājinaṃ yo naḥ svarṇaśṛṅgaṃ vidhānataḥ ||
api syāt sa kule ’smākaṃ kaścit puruṣasattamaḥ |
[744]        prastūyamānāṃ yo dhenuṃ dadyād brāhmaṇapuṅgave ||
api syāt sa kule ’smākaṃ kaścit puruṣasattamaḥ |
kūpārāmataḍāgānāṃ vāpīnāṃ yaś ca kārakaḥ ||
āha baudhāyanaḥ |
vāpīkūpataḍāgāni vṛkṣān ārāmam eva ca |
śālīkṣukṣetrakedārāḥ samṛddhāḥ puṣpavāṭikāḥ ||
śrāddheṣu datvā prayataḥ pitṝn ātmānam eva ca |
uddharaty enaso duḥkhād yāvad ābhūtasaṃplavam ||
tad evam eteṣv abhihiteṣu pradeyeṣu yāni pradeyāni śrāddhaprayogāntargatārcanāṅgabhūtāni tāni tat tat pitṛnāmagotrasaṃkīrtanapūrvakaṃ pitṛbhya eva deyāni | yāni punar anyāni gobhūhiraṇyakṛṣṇājinaprabhṛtīni śrāddhakāle svapitṝṇāṃ śreyaḥsiddhyarthaṃ dānakhaṇḍoktaprakāreṇa brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradeyāni | tatra nimittāntarāpekṣā na kartavyā śrāddham evādhikṛtya teṣāṃ vidhānāt | tāni caivaṃ pradattāni pitṛṇām ātmanaś ca viśiṣṭasvargādiphalaprāpyarthaṃ bhavantīti |
iti prakīrṇakam |
atha dakṣiṇādravyāṇi |
brahmapurāṇe |
yad yad iṣṭatamaṃ loke yac cāsya dayitaṃ gṛhe |
dakṣiṇārthaṃ tu tad deyaṃ tasya tasyākṣayārthinā ||
matsyapurāṇe |
satilaṃ nāmagotreṇa dadyāc chaktyātha dakṣiṇām |
[745]        gobhūhiraṇyavāsāṃsi yānāni śayanāni ca ||
dadyād yad iṣṭaṃ viprāṇām ātmanaḥ pitur eva vā |
vittaśāṭyena rahitaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ prītim ācaran ||
'nāmagotreṇa' saṃpradānasyātmanaś ca nāmagotroccāraṇena,
nāmagotre samuccārya saṃpradānasya cātmanaḥ |
saṃpradeyaṃ prayacchanti kanyādāne tu puṃstrayam ||
iti vyāsasmaraṇāt | 'śaktyā' svaśaktyanusāreṇa, 'pitṛbhyaḥ prītimācaran' paitreṇa karmaṇā pitṝn prīṇayan |
āha jamadagniḥ |
satilaṃ nāmagotreṇa dadyāc chaktyātha dakṣiṇām |
hiraṇadhanavāsāṃsi dharmyāṇi śayanāni ca ||
vahnipurāṇe |
bhaktyātha dakṣiṇā deyā śrāddhakarmaṇi śaktitaḥ |
grāmān kṣetrāṇy athārāmān vicitrāḥ puṣpavāṭikāḥ ||
bahubhaumāni ramyāṇi śayanāni gṛhāṇi ca |
suvarṇaratnavāsāṃsi rajataṃ bhūṣaṇāni ca ||
anaḍuho mahiṣyaś ca vidhidhāny [vividhāny?] āsanāni ca |
pāḍukā dāsadāsīś ca chatravyajanacāmaram ||
lāṅgalān śakaṭān gantrīr gṛhopakaraṇāni ca ||
yena yenopayogo ’sti viprāṇām ātmanas tathā |
tat tat pradeyaṃ śrāddheṣu dakṣiṇārthaṃ hitaiṣiṇā ||
[746]        yathā yathā hi guṇavad dravyaṃ bhūri yathā yathā |
jāyate phalabhūyastvaṃ śrāddhakartus tathā tathā ||
vāyupurāṇe |
dhenuṃ śrāddheṣu yo dadyāt gṛṣṭiṃ kumbhopadohanām |
gāvas tam upatiṣṭhanti gavāṃ puṣṭhiś ca jāyate ||
śayanāsanāni tathā bhūmayo vāhanāni ca |
śrāddheṣv etāni yo dadyād aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ||
sarpiḥpūrṇāni pātrāṇi śrāddhe satkṛtya dāpayet |
kumbhādidohadhenūnāṃ bahvīnāṃ phalam aśnute |
yatheṣṭāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt puṇḍarīkaphalaṃ labhet ||
'puṇḍarīkaḥ' kratuviśeṣaḥ |
ramyam āvasathaṃ datvā rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet |
kūpārāmataḍāgāni kṣetraghoṣagṛhāṇi ca |
gāṃ datvā modate svarge nityam ācandratārakam ||
svāstīrṇaṃ śayanaṃ datvā śrāddhe ratnavibhūṣitam |
pitaras tasya tuṣyanti svarge cānantyam aśnute ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
sauvarṇaraupyapātrāṇi manojñāni śubhāni ca |
hastyaśvarathayānāni samṛddhāni gṛhāṇi ca |
upānatpāḍukācchatracāmarāṇy ajināni ca ||
yajñeṣu dakṣiṇā puṇyā seti saṃcintayet tadā |
daridro ’pi yathāśakti dadyād vipreṣu dakṣiṇām ||
[747]        saiveyam iti buddhiḥ kāryety arthaḥ |
saurapurāṇe |
bahvībhir dakṣiṇābhir yaḥ śrāddhe prīṇayati dvijān |
sa pitṝṇāṃ prasādena yāti svargam anantakam |
aśaktas tu yathāśaktyā śrāddhe dadyāt tu dakṣiṇām |
adakṣiṇaṃ tu yac chrāddhaṃ hriyate tad dhi rākṣasaiḥ ||
yajñopavītam atha vā hy atidāridrapīḍitaḥ |
pradadyād dakṣiṇārthaṃ vai tena syāt karma sadguṇam ||
iti dakṣiṇādravyanirūpaṇam |
iti śrīmahārājādhirājaśrīmahādevīyasamastakaraṇādhipati-
paṇḍitaśrīhemādriviracite caturvargacintāmaṇau
pariśeṣakhaṇḍe śrāddhakalpe śrāddhopakaraṇaṃ nāma
navamo ’dhyāyaḥ || ● ||
[748]
atha daśamo’dhyāyaḥ |
gaṇakagaṇasahasrair apy aśakyo yadīyaḥ
kalayitum akhilānāṃ rāśir uccair guṇānām |
kṛtasukṛtasamṛddhiḥ so ’tha hemādrisūriḥ
kathayati paribhāṣāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇy aśeṣām ||
atha paribhāṣā |
tatra sarvaśāstropasaṃhāraḥ tadartham idaṃ cintyate | kiṃ svit śākhākalpasūtragṛhyeṣu yāvaddharmakaṃ śrāddham uktaṃ tāvanmātram eva kartavyam uta manvādismṛtivihitasarvadharmopasaṃhāreṇeti | tatra yadi tāvanmātradharmakaṃ kriyate tadā śākhākalpasūtragṛhyeṣu tāvanmātropadeśo 'rthavān anyathānarthakaḥ syāt | nanu yadi smṛtivihitadharmopasaṃhāraḥ kriyate tadā smṛtaye ’rthavatyaḥ anyathānarthikāḥ syur iti samānanyāyatayā smārtadharmakalāpopasaṃhāro ’pi niravadyam āsīd iti | na ca prātisvikaśākhākalpasūtragṛhyaśūnyān puruṣān prati smṛtayo ’rthavatyo bhaviṣyantīti vācyam | prātisvikaśākhādiśūnyānāṃ traivarṇikānām abhāvāt | na ca śūdrān praty arthavatyo bhaviṣyantīti vācyam | traivarṇikādhikārikopanayanādhyayanāgnihotrādikarmopadeśapradhānatvāt smṛtīnāṃ traivarṇikān praty api tāsām arthavattvena bhāvyam | maivaṃ | karmaviśeṣe śūdrān pratyevārthavattvaṃ kalpyatām | atha vā [749] traivarṇikān praty api bahudharmakaśrāddhāntaropadeśenopayogitvaṃ varṇyatām | ataḥ svaśākhākalpasūtragṛhyoktaśrāddhaprayogas tāvān eva kartavyo na smārtapadārthapraveśenātirecyaḥ | atirecyamāno hi prayogavidhyabhimatau kramakālau hitvānyāvāsādayet (?) | ata eva svaśākhākalpasūtragṛhyoktaśrāddhaprayogaḥ paraśākhādyuktapadārthapraveśena na bṛṃhaṇīyaḥ |
kiṃ ca | kalpasūtragṛhyāṇām adhyetṛviśeṣasaṃbandhitvaniyamāl liṅgaliṅginoś caikāśrayatvāt kartṛviśeṣāśritena kalpasūtreṇa liṅgena tadāśritaiva liṅgini śrutiḥ kalpyate | ataḥ svasvakalpasūtragṛhyoktānāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇāṃ vyavasyaiva |
kiṃ ca | śākhāntaragṛhyāntarādau vidhīyamānasya paunaruktyādidoṣaparihārasyāśakyatvāt karmāntaratve tadanvitasya dharmajātasyātideśam antareṇa śākhāntarīyakarmasaṃbandhānupapattau sarvopasaṃhāro niṣpramāṇakaḥ | na copapadāsaṃbhavena vyavasthānupapattiḥ | tattacchākhāgṛhyādhyetṛtvalakṣaṇasya tattacchākhīyatābhimānitvarūpasya copapadasya kartṛviśeṣakasya saṃbhavāt | na ca pratividhivākyam upapadakalpane gurvī kalpaneti vācyam | sarvān api vidhīnidamādinā kenacit sarvanāmnā parāmṛśya yugapadupapadānvayasya kalpyamānatvāt |
maivam | paramparāgatasvaśākhāparityāgena śākhāntarādhyayanakartāpi hi svakulaparamparāgataśākhāgṛhyoktadharmān evānutiṣṭhati | dvitrivedādhyāyī ca vedāntarāntargataṃ svaśākhāntaram adhīyāno ’pi svaśākhāgṛhyoktān eva dharmān ācarati | anadhītavedo ’pi śrāddhādikaṃ karma kurvan śākhāviśeṣagṛhyaviśeṣopadiṣṭām eva saraṇim āśrayata iti na tattacchākhāgṛhyadhyetṛtvalakṣaṇam [750] upapadaṃ yāvadācāram anuvartate tacchākhīyatvābhimāno ’pi tacchākhādhyetṛtvabhrama eva vācyaḥ | na cāsau svaśākhām adhītavato ’nadhītavato vā saṃbhavati | prathame tacchākhādhyetṛtvajñānasya satyatvāt, dvitīye tādṛśasya jñānasyaivāsaṃbhavāt | atas tayoḥ śākhāgṛhyācāraviśeṣānusaraṇaṃ nopapadyeta | anyaśākhādhyetur api ca vātādidoṣavaśopajātaśākhāntarādhyetṛtvabhramasya tacchākhāgṛhyānusaraṇaṃ prasajyeta | doṣam antareṇa samutpannabhramasyāyaṃ niyama iti cet | na, kāraṇam antareṇa bhramasyāsaṃbhavāt | athāhāryāroparūpabhramasya doṣam antareṇāpi saṃbhava ity ucyate | tarhi tasya sarvatra saṃbhavād avyavasyaiva prāpnoti | gauṇe ca śākhāntaraīyatvavyapadeśe tasya vidyamānatvāt paraśākhāgṛhyoktavidhigrahaṇaprasaṅgaḥ | ataḥ kartṛviśeṣaṇībhūtopapadāsaṃbhavāt sarvaśākhākalpasūtragṛhyasmṛtyācāraprāptānāṃ śrāddhakarmaṇāṃ sarvān prati sādhāraṇyam eva na punar vyavastheti | naitad evaṃ | śākhāviśeṣādhyetṛ antatijatvānapāyāt tacchākhīyatvam asty eva | ataḥ svaśākhākalpasūtragṛhyoktadharmasātrayuktam eva śrāddhaṃ kartavyaṃ nādhikasmārtapadārthapraveśena paraśākhākalpasūtragṛhyoktapadārthapraveśena vātirecyam iti sthitaṃ |
atrocyate | ananyathāsiddhapratyabhijñābalād aśeṣaśākhāpratyayam ekam evāgnihotrādikarmeti śākhāntarādhikaraṇe sthāpitaṃ “ekaṃ vā saṃyogarūpacodanākhyāviśeṣāt" | sarvāsv api śākhāsu jyotiṣṭomādikarmaṇaḥ saṃyogarūpacodanākhyānām aviśeṣāt pratyabhijñāyamānasyaikatvaṃ niścīyate | saṃyogo ’tra prādhānyāt phalasaṃbandho vivakṣitaḥ | rūpaṃ cāntaraṅgatvāt karmasvarūpanirūpakatvāc ca dravyadevatā | codanā ca [751] dhātvarthasahitabhāvanāviṣayo vidhiḥ | ākhyā tu jyotiṣṭomādināmadheyam | etad eva sūtraṃ vyācakṣāṇāḥ prāhur ācāryāḥ |
sarvatra pratyabhijñānāt saṃjñārūpaguṇādibhiḥ |
ekakarmatvavijñānaṃ na śākhāsv apagacchati || iti |
na cātra kiṃcid bhedakaṃ kāraṇam asti | śākhāntaroktasya bhedaprayojakatvānavadhāraṇāt | prakaraṇāntara iva parasparāsaṃnidhānaṃ bhedakam iti cet | na, ekasyādhyetuḥ śākhāntarādhyayanābhāvāt tatpratipādyamānajyotiṣṭomādikarmānupalambhe bhedāvabhāsāsaṃbhavāt | kutaḥ punar ekasya śākhāntarādhyayanābhāvaḥ | itthaṃ yathaivākṛtir ekasyāṃ vyaktau samāpyate tathā svādhyāyatvam ekaikasyāṃ śākhāyāṃ tataś ca svādhyāyo ’dhyetavya ity atrādhītena svādhyāyena karmavabādhaṃ bhāvayed ity ukteḥ svādhyāyasya karmāvabodhanaṃ pratyupādīyamānatvād vivakṣitaikasaṃkhyaikā śākhādhyetavyeti gamyate | kutas tarhi vedāntarādhyayanam api vedān adhītya vedau vetyādivacanair iti brūmaḥ
kiṃ ca | vedāntare karmaṇaḥ sākāṅkṣatvāt karmāvabodhaṃ prati vedānāṃ samuccaye saty ekasya naikavedādhyayanam | ekavedāntargate tu śākhāntare 'pi paripūrṇadharmakaṃ śākhāntaranirapekṣam eva jyotiṣṭomādikarmāvagamyate | atas tulyakāryatvād ekavedāntargataikaśākhādhyayananiyame śākhānāṃ vikalpa iti |
tad āhur ācāryīḥ |
svādhyāyagrahaṇe naikā śākhā hi parigṛhyate |
ekārthānāṃ vikalpaś ca karmaikatve bhaviṣyati || iti |
nanu yady ekasyaikavedāntargatān ekaśākhādhyayanābhāvāc chākhāntarīyakarmāpratītis tarhi kutaḥ pratyabhijñāpi bhavet | atha svayam anadhīyamāne ’pi parair adhīyamāne śākhāntare samākarṇyamāne tatpratipādyamānakarmavāgatir bhavatīti pratyabhijñopapadyata ity ucyate tarhi tathaiva bhedāvagatir api bhaviṣyati |
[752]        ucyate | karmasvarūpagataviśeṣābhāvāt pratyakṣabhedapratītyabhāve nāmarūpādyaviśeṣanibandhanāparokṣapratyabhijñotpattau parasparāsaṃnidhānaprayuktānumānajanyabhedapratīter utpattipratibandhāt | astu vā bhedabuddhyabhāvasahakṛtāsvarūpapratītir evābhedapramātmakapratyabhijñākāryakaratvād apratyabhijaiva satī gauṇī pratyabhijñā | guṇāntaraṃ tarhi bhedakam astu |
na, tasyaikasminn eva karmaṇi vikalpenānvayopapatteḥ | nanūtpattivākyaśiṣṭe guṇe sati guṇāntarānavakāśād vājina iva guṇāntare karmabheda eva | maivaṃ, dvayor utpattivākyaśiṣṭhayos tulyabalatve saty ekasmin karmaṇi vikalpasaṃbhavāt |
nanu yadi sarvāsu śākhāsv ekam eva karma pratipādyate tadā punaruktatāprasaṅgaḥ | atas tatparihārārtham abhyāsanyāyena śākhāntare karmabhedo vācyaḥ | maivaṃ | yatra tāvanmātrārthatvena punaḥ śrutiḥ prayojanaśūnyā bhavet tatra bhedam āpādayati | śākhāntare tu guṇāntarārthatvena praśaṃsārthatvena vā prayojanavatī satī na bhedaṃ karoti | na ceyaṃ punaḥ śrutiḥ | yata āhuḥ |
ekaśākhānibaddhānāṃ jñāyate hi punaḥ śrutiḥ |
kaṇṭhādicaraṇānāṃ tu pratiśākhaṃ sakṛc chrutiḥ ||
atraikaśākhādhyayananiyamādyenaikaśākhāgatam agnihotrādi śrutaṃ nāsau śākhāntaragataṃ śṛṇoti | kadācit parair adhīyamānaṃ śākhāntaragataṃ śṛṇvann api ca parapratipatyartham evaitad ity adhyavasyati | ataḥ puruṣāntarapratipatyarthatvāc chākhāntarasya na punaḥ śrutitvam | api ca śākhānāṃ yugapadvṛtter na punaḥ śrutikalpanā tarhi tatraikaśākhāvat krameṇādhyayanasthitiḥ | [753] krameṇādhyayane hi kācit punaḥ śrutitvam āpadyamānā karma bhedaṃ karoti | atra tu yugapacchruteḥ kā punaḥ śrutir bhavet | ato yathaikaśākhāgataṃ karma deśāntarapuruṣāntarādiṣu punaḥ śrūyamāṇam api pratipādyabhedān na punar uktam evam eva śākhāntareṣv iti | yat tv ekayaiva śākhayā sarve pratipadyantām anarthakaṃ śākhāntaram iti | tacchākhāntarāṇām akṛtrimatvāt pratiśākhaṃ cādhyetṛniyamasyānāditvād anupālambha eveti | ata evaitad api na vācyam | yathā kṛtasya karaṇāsaṃbhavas tathā vihitasya vidhānāsaṃbhavo ’pīty ekasyāṃ śākhāyāṃ vihitasya śākhāntare tāvad vidhānāsaṃbhavaḥ | śākhānāṃ yugapad upasthānāc cānuvādāsaṃbhavo ’pīty ato bheda eveti | yatas tattacchākhādhyāyinaḥ pravartyān pratipādyāṃś ca paruṣān prati pravṛttibhedena pratipattibhedena ca vihitasyāpi vidhānaṃ na virudhyate | tad evaṃ karmaikatve sthite yatra kvacanāpadiṣṭair dharmaiḥ saṃbadhyamānam agnihotrādikaṃ śākhāntarādhyetṛbhir apy anuṣṭhīyamānaṃ na caikaṃ pratiśiṣyata iti nyāyāt taddharmayuktam anuṣṭheyam iti sarvaśākhopasaṃhāre sthite sarvaśākhāgṛhyakalpasūtrasmṛti-purāṇetihāsācārāvagatadharmamātrayuktam eva sarvaiḥ śrāddhaṃ karttavyam iti sthitam |
ata evaikāṃ kām api śākhāṃ puraskṛtya kalpasūtrāṇi racayanto munayaḥ svaśākhoktadharmamātropanibandhanenāparituṣyantaḥ śākhāntarīyadharmasaṃgrahaṃ kurvāṇā dṛśyante |
nanu sarvaśākhāgṛhyādiṣu karmaikatve ’pi tattacchākhāgṛhyādhyetṛpuruṣabheda-vyavasthayā tattacchākhāvidhyuktetikartavyatābhedavyavasthopapatteḥ na sarvaśākhopasaṃhāraḥ |
[754]        maivaṃ | īdṛgvidhāyāṃ vyavasthāyāṃ śrutiliṅgādirūpamānābhāvāt | na ca śrutyādivat kenacic chākhāntaraṃ vyavasthāmānatvenābhyupagatam |
nanv asti tāvat svādhyāyo 'dhyetavya iti vacanād ācāraparamparāgatapuruṣaviśeṣāṇāṃ śākhāviśeṣādhyayanavyavasthā | ato vyavasthitapramāṇopanīyamānaṃ prameyam api tadvad vyavasthātum arhati | tathā hi | yathā dṛṣṭe saṃbhavati nādṛṣṭakalpanā nyāyyeti svādhyāyādhyayanavidheḥ karmāvabodhārthatvena dṛṣṭārthatvam iṣyate | evam eva śākhāviśeṣādhyayanavyavasthāyā api dṛṣṭārthatvāya kartṛviśeṣavyavasthita-karmāvabodhārthatvam eṣṭavyam |
ucyate | prajñāviśeṣaśālināṃ svavedavat paravedādhyayanasyāpy anujñātatvāt paravedāntargataśākhāyāṃ ca vyavasthābhāvād avyavasyaikaśākhādhyayanaṃ kartavyam | tad api karmāvabodhārthaṃ bodho ’py anuṣṭhānārtha iti śākhāntarīyakarmānuṣṭhāne kuto vyavasthā | na hy adṛṣṭārthaṃ dvitīyavedādhyayanam, adhyayanavidhivaiṣamyāpatteḥ |
anyebhyas taddharmopadeśārtham anyaśākhādhyayanaṃ dṛṣṭārtham evāstu | na, adhītena vedena karmavabodhaṃ kuryād iti vidhyartho nirṇītaḥ | na tu karmavabodhaṃ kārayed iti |
yat tu svaśākhāgatādhikāryantarakartavyakarmavidhāyakavedapradeśādhyayanaṃ kriyate tat karmavabodhārthaṃ samagraśākhādhyayane vihite sati nāntarīyakatveneti na doṣaḥ |
atha parādhyāpanārtham adhyāpanavidhinā dvitīyavedādhyayanaṃ prayojitaṃ nādhyayanavidhineti na vaiṣamyam | na cādṛṣṭārthatvam iti cet | tan na | ekatrādhyayanavidhiprayuktam adhyayanam anyatrādhyayanavidhiprayuktam iti vairūpyaṃ [755] prasajyeta | na ca sarvatrādhyayanavidhiprayuktam adhyayanam iti vācyam | adhyayanavidhyānarthakyaprasaṅgāt |
kiṃ ca | adhyayanavidhiprayukte paravedādhyayane prajñāviśeṣaśālinoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayor vedāntarādhyayanaṃ na prāpnoti tayor anadhyāpakatvāt | ato na śākhāgṛhyādivyavasthayā karmaṇāṃ vyavasthā |
yat tūktam, sarvaśākhopasaṃhāre kramakālau virudhyete iti | tan na | prayogavidhir hi śrutismṛtyācārādipramāṇaprāpitasarvapadārthapratīcāṃ kṛtvā prayogaṃ vidhatte | na punaḥ svasaṃnidhisamāmnātapadārthamātraprāpyiparituṣṭaḥ sahasaiveti | ata eva vedavedyādyantarā praviśataḥ smārtasnānācamanādipadārthasyāpi (?) vā kāraṇāgrahaṇe prayuktāni pratīyerann ity atra prayogavidhiparigraheo varṇitaḥ | kiṃ ca padārthānāṃ dharmaḥ kramo na hīnapramāṇaprāpitam api padārthaṃ bādhituṃ kṣamate |
api ca kasyāṃcic chākhāyāṃ sāmānyam evopadiṣṭaṃ na viśeṣaḥ | tatrānuṣṭhānakāle ’vaśyaṃ svabuddhyādhyavasya kaścid viśeṣo grahītavyaḥ | sa ca gṛhyamāṇaḥ kadācic chākhāntaropaviṣṭo gṛhyate | tatra yadi śākhāntarānusāreṇa sa eva gṛhyate tadā kiṃ nāmāsamañjasaṃ kṛtaṃ syāt | yo ’pi tatsāmānyānantargato bhinnadvāratayā samuccayenaikaprayoganiveśaṃ labhamānaḥ svaśākhoktapadārthāvirodhī śākhāntarīyaḥ padārthaḥ sp 'py apūrvasyānitāpāyaparimāṇatvādviruddha pramāṇāntarānivāritapraveśo gṛhyamāṇo na kiñcid dūṣayet | yas tu svaśākhoktaviruddhaḥ śākhāntarīyaḥ so ’pi pramāṇasyāpakṣapātitvād ekadvārakatve sati tulyabalatvād vikalpanyāyena gṛhyamāṇo nātīvānaucityena naiyāyikānāṃ saṃmataḥ |
[756]        ataḥ sarvaśākhākalpasūtragṛhyasmṛtipurāṇetihāsācārāvagatān padārthān upasaṃhṛtya prayogo ’nuṣṭheya iti siddham |
atrārthe liṅgadarśanaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
rāmeṇa bhojitā viprā munyuktena yathāvidhi |
vaidikī ca kṛtā sarvā kriyā yā mānavī smṛtā |
purāṇokto vidhiś caiva vaiśvadevikapūrvakaḥ ||
'munyuktena' munibhiḥ preritena | 'vaidikī' vedavihitapiṇḍapitṛyajñasaṃbandhinī, piṇḍapitṛyajñavad upacāraḥ pitrya ity atideśena śrāddhe prāpitā | 'mānavī' manupraṇītaśāstravihitā, 'smṛtā' anyair api hārīta-gautama-prambhṛtibhir dharmaśāstrakārair vedārtham anusmṛtyopadiṣṭā sargapratisargādipañcalakṣaṇavanti brāhmyādīni 'purāṇāni' teṣu 'uktaḥ' pratipāditaḥ | 'vidhiḥ' anuṣṭhānaprakāraḥ | evaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇy upasaṃhṛtya rāmeṇa śrāddhaṃ kṛtam ity arthaḥ |
atrāha kārṣṇājiniḥ |
ātmatantreṣu yatrokaṃ na kuryāt pāratantrikam |
viśeṣāḥ khalu sāmānyā vedoktā vedavādibhiḥ ||
'ātmatantre' śākhāgṛhyādirūpe svakīye śāstre, yad anuṣṭheyapadārthasvarūpaṃ noktaṃ tat parakīyatantrokram api kuryāt | yato vedoktāḥ sarve viśeṣāḥ sarveṣām anuṣṭātṝṇāṃ sāmānyāḥ sādhāraṇa iti vedavādibhir nirṇītam |
yat punar etadviruddhaṃ vaijavāpāyanasya vacanam,
bahvalpaṃ vā svagṛhyokaṃ yasya karma pracoditam |
tasya tāvati śāstrārthe kṛte sarvakṛto bhavet ||
iti [757] tat tāvat kecid evaṃ vyācakṣate yad garbhādhānacauḍopanayanavivāhādikaṃ karma gṛhyeṣu samyag uktaṃ manvādismṛtigrantheṣu cāsamyag uktaṃ tatra smṛtyapekṣā na kartavyā | yat tu śrāddhāṣṭakādikaṃ smṛtiṣu samyag gṛhye cāsamyaguktaṃ tatra smṛtyapekṣā kartavyaiveti | ataḥ svagṛhyoktasya smṛtyapekṣā nivāryate |
na tu paragṛhyādyapekṣāpīti sarvopasaṃhāranyāyasya vacanasya ca vratasa ca viṣayavyavastheti |
nanv asamyaguktatvaṃ nāma svalpadharmakasyābhidhānaṃ tatrāpy alpaṃ vety abhidhānāt smṛtyapekṣā vāryata eveti kutas tathāvidhe tadapekṣoktā | naitad evaṃ asamyaktvaṃ nāmātra karmasvarūpaniṣpādakena saṃnipātinā dṛṣṭārthena dharmeṇa vinābhidhānam | samyaktvaṃ ca svarūpaniṣpādakadharmābhidhānena sampūrṇatvam | alpatvaṃ ca tadabhidhānasamāptāv anyatroktādṛṣṭaikārthadharmānabhidhānam | ato ’samyagabhidhāne yuktā smṛtyapekṣeti |
tad idaṃ vyākhyānam ayuktaṃ gṛhyoktasya smṛtimātrāpekṣāniṣedhavacane svaśabdānarthakyaprakhaṅgaḥ |
astu tarhy evam | yat svagṛhye samyaguktaṃ tatra paragṛhyasmṛtyādyapekṣā na kartavyā | yat tu samyag anuktaṃ tatra kartavyā | ata evaikavedāntargatānāṃ śākhānāṃ karmāvabodhe vikalpo 'ṅgīkṛtaḥ vedānāṃ ca samuccaya iti |
tad api na yuktaṃ | śākhāntaroktadharmopasaṃhāranyāyasyāsamyaguktakarmaviṣayatve vedāntaravad asamyagabhidhāyakaśākhāyā api śākhāntarādhikaraṇe ’nudāharaṇatvam eva prasajyeta | na caikavedāntargatā kācid asti tādṛśi śākhety adhikaraṇavaiyarthyaṃ cāpadyeta | ata evaṃ vyākhyeyam, svaśākhāgṛhyokte svalpadharmake karmaṇi paraśākhoktādhikadharmopasaṃhāraṃ kṛtvaiva tāvat [758] prayogo ’nuṣṭheyaḥ | yadi kadācid daivān mānuṣād vā pratibalāt paraśākhāgṛhyoktādhikakarmopasaṃhāro na śakyate kartuṃ tadā svaśākhāgṛhyamātroktasvalpam ayuktam api kartavyam | tāvati ca kṛte sarvaśākhāgṛhyopasaṃhāreṇa yat samagraśāstrārtharūpaṃ kriyate tadvad eva tad api samagraśāstrārtharūpam eva tena kṛtaṃ bhavatīty anukalpo ’yam iti |
anye tu punaḥ svagṛhyoktadharmaviruddhaparagṛhyadharmānuṣṭhānaniṣedhaparam idaṃ vacanaṃ varṇayanti | tan na | nyūnādhikadharmaviṣayatvasya vacane pratyakṣeṇopalambhāt viruddhadharmāṇāṃ vaikalpikatvād ekopādāne ’nyahānāt prayogaparimāṇasāmye sati bahvalpaṃ vety anupayogāt |
yac ca viruddhaśākhāntarīyadharmopasaṃhāraniṣedhakam idaṃ laugākṣivacanam,
ūno vāpy atirikto vā yaḥ svaśākhodito vidhiḥ |
tena santanupādyajñaṃ na kuryyāt pāratantrikam ||
iti, tad apy anukalpatvena svaśākhāmātroktagrahaṇe kriyamāṇe yadarthāt paraśākhīyāgrahaṇaṃ prāptaṃ tadanuvādapūrvakam anukalpavidhiparam eva | na tu śākhāntarīyadharmopasaṃhāra-niṣedhaparam ity evaṃ vyākhyeyam |
yat tv aparaṃ laugākṣivākyam,
yaḥ svaśākhāṃ parityajya paraśākhāṃ samācaret |
apramāṇamṛṣiṃ kṛtvā so ’ndhe tamasi majjati ||
iti, tac chākhāntarādhyayananiṣedhakam astu |
atha prakaraṇādiparyālocanayā yadi śākhāśabdas taduktakarmaparas tad evaṃ vyākhyeyam | svaśākhoktaparityāgena śākhāntarīyagrahaṇe doṣo ’yaṃ na punaḥ svaśākhoktena saha samuccitaparaśākhīyagrahaṇe, parityajyeti vacanāt | atha vā paraśākhoktasyāpi grahaṇe kriyamāṇe tadaviruddhasvaśā[759]khoktadharmaparityāgasyāprasaktatvāt svaśākhīyaviruddhaparaśākhīyagrahaṇaniṣedhaparam idaṃ vacanaṃ niścīyate | atra svagṛhyamūlabhūtavedo gṛhyakāro vā vyavasthita ācāro vā darśanād ṛṣir ucyate, taṃ 'apramāṇaṃ kṛtvā' apramāṇatvena parikalpya 'andhe tamasi' kṛmikīṭapataṅgapipīlikā-sthāvarādiyonivaiśeṣike tamoguṇe ’vidyāyāṃ pāpmani nirayaviśeṣe vā, nimajjatīty arthaḥ | aviruddhāni tu paraśākhāmnātāni sarvāṇy apy aṅgāny upasaṃhṛtya karmānuṣṭheyam |
tad uktaṃ bhaviṣyatpurāṇe |
yan nāmnātaṃ svaśākhāyāṃ pārakayam avirodhi yat |
vidvadbhis tad anuṣṭheyam agnihotrādikaṃ yathā || iti |
iti sarvaśāstropasaṃhāranirṇayaḥ |
athānusamayasya nirṇayaḥ |
sa cāyaṃ dvividhaḥ | padārthānusamayaḥ kāṇḍānusamayaś ceti | dvividham api caitam ādriyante yājñikāḥ karmaṇi | tathā hy āgneyāgnīṣomīyayoḥ puroḍāśayoḥ kapālopadhānādhiśrayaṇodvāsanādīnāṃ padārthānām evānusamayam icchanti | aśvapratigraheṣṭāv api svalpasaṅkhyakeṣu puroḍāśeṣv evam eva śatasahasrādisaṅkhyakeṣu tu teṣu kāṇḍānusamayam aṅgīkurvate | tathā paśugaṇe upākaraṇādīnāṃ paśudharmāṇāṃ padārthānusamayam āśrayante | yūpagaṇe cāñjanādīnāṃ yūpadharmāṇāṃ kāṇḍānusamayam | ataḥ sandehaḥ, kva padārthānusamayaḥ kva kāṇḍānusamaya iti | kiṃ ca padārthānusamaye ’pi cikīrṣite kiyān ekaḥ padārtha ity evaṃ padārthaparimāṇe kvacit saṃśerate | yathā muṣṭikapālāvadānājanavapanapāvaneṣu, yathā cāvaghāte [760] kṛṣṇājināstaraṇolūkhalādhivartanādiṣu phalīkaraṇānteṣu karmasu, kiṃ ca kaścit kriyātmā kaścit padārthāvayavībhūto 'py anyatra padārthatām aśnuvāno dṛśyate | yathā carupuroḍāśasānnāyyādiṣu pradānaṃ tasya caturavat tasya padārthāvayavabhūtaṃ caturavattamātraṃ sānnāyyavikṛtibhūte py agnīṣomīye paśau svatantraikapadārthatāṃ prāptam agnīṣomīyavikṛtibhūte sampratipannadevatāke paśugaṇe 'py evam eva | vipratipannadevatāke tu pradānāntacaturavattapadārthāvayavatām evāgatam | ata iha śrāddhe ’pi nimantraṇādivisarjanānteṣu śrāddhabhoktṛdvijasaṃbaddheṣv avanejanādipratipattyanteṣu piṇḍasaṃbaddheṣv āsanādinyubjīkaraṇānteṣv arghyapātrasaṃbaddheṣu prakṣālanasthāpanapariyantrakādiṣu bhojanapātrasaṃbaddheṣu kuśayavatilagandhapuṣpādiprakṣepeṣu maṇḍalasaṃbaddheṣu kva padārthānusamayaḥ kva kāṇḍānusamaya iti vicārya nirṇetavyam |
tatra tāvad ayam utsargo yat padārthānusamayenānuṣṭānam | ato brāhmaṇādiṣu pratyekaṃ saṃnipatyopakārakaṃ padārtham āvartya tenaiva nyāyenānyam āvartayet na tu pratyekaṃ padārthakāṇḍam iti |
nanu pitṛtṛptikāmaḥ putrādikāmo vā brāhmaṇān bhojayet piṇḍān pradadyād ity atra tāvat karmavibhaktinirdiṣṭā api brāhmaṇaḥ piṇḍāśca bhūtabhāvyupayogāntararahitatvād dhomadānābhyām iva saktugāvau bhojanena pradānena vā na cikīrṣyete | ato yais tu dravyaṃ na cikīrṣvyata iti nyāyāt tayoḥ pradhānakarmatāphalavat saṃnidhyāmnātaphalarahitakarmabhyo ’nyāni ca pradhānakarmāṇy adhikāravākaye karaṇatvenopādīyante | ataś ca brāhmaṇabhojanena piṇḍapradānena vā pitṛtṛptiṃ putrapaśusvargāni [761] vā bhāvayed iti vacanavyaktir jāyate | tatra na kiṃcid apītikarttavyatāśūnyaṃ karaṇaṃ bhavatīti kṛtvā karaṇābhidhāyinā padena sahetham itītikarttavyatābhidhāyakam api padaṃ saṃbadhyate | ataḥ phalakaraṇetikarttavyatānām ekakālatvaṃ prayogavidhināvagamyate | śabdavṛttaṃ tāvad evaṃ yad uta trayāṇām ekakālatvaṃ vastv apy evam eva | tathā hi | yadi tāvad bhūtakālaṃ bhāvyaṃ bhaviṣyatkāle karaṇetikartavyate tataḥ karaṇetikartavyatātvam eva na syāt | na hi paścādbhāvikaraṇam itikartavyatvaṃ vā bhavati | tathā bhaviṣyati bhāvye bhūtayoś ca karaṇetikartavyatayor asāv eva doṣaḥ | na hi vinaṣṭasya karaṇatvam itikartavyatātvaṃ vā saṃbhavati | karaṇetikartavyatayor mitho bhinnakālatve ’py ayam eva doṣaḥ | na hi vinaṣṭam anāgataṃ vānugrāhakam anugrāhyaṃ vā bhavitum arhati | tad evaṃ prayogavidhinā pradhānakālatvam aṅgānāṃ bodhitaṃ |
nanv evam aśakyārthopadeśaprasaṅgaḥ | na hy ekena kartā yugapad anekānuṣṭānaṃ kartuṃ śakyate | satyaṃ | kiṃ tv atra yāvantaṃ viprakarṣam antareṇānuṣṭhānam aśakyaṃ tāvān viprakarṣo ’bhyupagamyate | kva tarhi prayogavidhibodhitam ekakālatvam | maivaṃ | yataḥ prayogavacanaṃ yāvataḥ padārthān anuṣṭhāpayati tāvadbhir avarjanīyatayā yāvān kālo vyāpyate tāvān ekaḥ kālaḥ bhavaty eva sarveṣāṃ sāhityaṃ vidhīyate na tv ekasmin kṣaṇe, aśakyārthavidhiprasaṅgāt | ataḥ pratyāsattir eṣāṃ sāhityam | sā ca śrutyarthādibhiḥ yasya yāvatī pramitā tasya tāvatī kriyate | ato nānavasthitatāsyāḥ vyarthatā vā śrutyarthādeḥ | tad evaṃ mahāprayogavacanaḥ sarveṣāṃ pradhānānām aṅgasāhityaṃ bodhayann ekaśmin pradhāne ’vāntaraprayogavacanān [762] parikalpayan svayaṃ vā tadavasthatām āviṣkṛtyaikaikasya pradhānasya svasvāṅgasāhityaṃ brūte | evam avāntarāvāntaraprayogavacanatvam abhyupetyāṅgānām api pratyekaṃ svasvāṅgavargasāhityaṃ pratipādayati | tathā caikaikasya brāhmaṇasya piṇḍasya pātrasya vā paradharmānuṣṭhānavyavadhānarahitasvāṅgavarga-sahitasyānuṣṭhāne prāpte kāṇḍānusamaya eva nyāyyaḥ na padārthānusamaya iti | atrābhidhīyate | brāhmaṇān bhojayet piṇḍān pradadyāt pātrāṇy āsādayed ityādau dvandvasamāsārthaikaśeṣavaśāt prājāpatyaiś carantītivat brāhmaṇādīnāṃ parasparasahitānām evānuṣṭhānaṃ gamyate | tac ca tadaṅgabhūtapadārthānuṣṭhānadvārakam eva na svarūpataḥ | tatra yady ekasmin brāhmaṇādau samagram apy aṅgakāṇḍam anuṣṭhāyāparasminn apy anuṣṭhīyeta tad ekasyānuṣṭhāne ’pavṛkte saty aparasyārambhāt sāhityāsiddhiḥ | yadi punar ekaṃ padārtham ekasmin kṛtvānantara eva kṣaṇe tam evānyasminn api kuryāt tadā brāhmaṇadīnām anyonyasāhityam āpāditaṃ bhavati |
nanu prayogavacanānugrahāya parasparasāhityavacanaṃ sarvārthatayā tantrānuṣṭhīyamānārādupakārakāṅgamātraviṣayam astu |
maivaṃ | kālpanikaṃ hi prayogavacananibandhanaṃ sāhityaṃ, na tadbalāt śrutapratyakṣasāhityabādhaḥ saṅkoco vāpi kartuṃ nyāyyaḥ |
tasmāt padārthānusamaya evautsargika iti sthitam |
nimantraṇādīnāṃ kāṇḍānusamaye ca bahubādha ity aparo doṣaḥ | tathāhi pūrvedyur ekasya nimantraṇaṃ kṛtvā yadi tasyaivānyedyur amyoṣakalpanaparyantaṃ padārthakāṇḍaṃ kṛtvā taṃ tathaivāvasyāpyānyasya nimantraṇādi kartuṃ pravartate tadā pūrvedyur brāhmaṇān nimantrayed iti sarvanimantraṇsya pūrvadina[763]saṃbandhāvagatiḥ, yāvatprayogaṃ prayogadeśe kartṛsthitiḥ, śaucaṃ, kutapāparāhṇādikālaḥ, pākoṣṇatā, sakṛt sarvasaṃbandhayogyānāṃ praiṣādīnāṃ tantratā, āvṛtyādiprasaṅgāt prayogaprāṃśubhāvaḥ, samācāraś cety etat savaṃ bādhyeta | tatra tāvan nimantraṇīyasamīpopagamanasya nimantraṇākṣiptasya pṛthakpadārthatvavirahād anarthakāvṛttiprasakter anavasthānān nimantraṇāsiddher nimantraṇāntasyānusamayaḥ | nimantraṇasyāpi niyamaśrāvaṇāntasya kevalasyānusamaye nimantraṇīyadvijānām anyonyaṃ dūre ’vasthānād upagamanadvaye prayogavidhyapekṣitaprāṃśubhāvabhaṅgaprasaṅgaḥ |
kiṃ ca |
dakṣiṇa jānum ālabhya tvaṃ mayātra nimantritaḥ |
evaṃ nimantrya niyamān śrāvayet paitṛkān budha ||
ity atraikavacanopāttaikaikanimantraṇasya ktvāśrutyā pūrvakālatām uktvā niyamaśrāvaṇasyāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavartitocyate sāpi bādhyeta |
na caitad vaktavyam |
akrodhanaiḥ śaucaparaḥ satataṃ brahmacāribhiḥ |
bhavitavyaṃ bhavadbhiś ca mayā ca śrāddhakarmaṇi ||
iti devalopadiṣṭe niyamaśrāvaṇavākaye bahuvacanāt saṃmilitānāṃ yugapanniyamaśrāvaṇena saha saṃbandho ’vagamyamānaḥ kevalasya nimantraṇasya niyamaśrāvaṇāntasya vānusamayena yujyata iti | bahuvacanasya pūjārthatvopapatteḥ | śrāddhadinapūrvāhṇe prāvṛttikakramānurodhāya nimantraṇakrameṇa viprebhyaḥ padārthanusamayena dantakāṣṭhaṃ pradāya tailodvarttanapānīyādisnānīyadravyapradānāni kāṇḍānusamaya iva samuditāny ekaikasmin brāhmaṇe kuryāt | tathā hi kṛṣṇājināstaraṇādiphalīkaraṇāntāni karmāṇi taṇḍula-[764]niṣpattiphalakāvaghātabhāvanākṣiptatvena tadavayavībhūyaikapadārthatāṅgatāni santi nānābījeṣṭau santānādāv ekaikasmin bīje kāṇḍānusamaya iva rumuditāny eva kriyante | tathā tailādipradānāny api snānabhāvanākṣiptatvenaikapadārthībhūtatvāt samuditāny evaikaikasmin brāhmaṇa iti |
ākāraṇam api nimantraṇakrameṇa padārthānusamayenaiva svāgatapraśnasya tantrānuṣṭhānabhramaṃ nirākartuṃ padārthānusamayam upadiśati mārkaṇḍeyaḥ |
snātaḥ snātān samāhūtān svāgatenārcayet pṛthak || iti |
yavagandhādyarcanadravyaprakṣepās tv ekaikasmin maṇḍale lāghavāt tantreṇaiva tāvata kartavyāḥ | pṛthaganuṣṭhānapakṣe 'py arcanabhāvanākṣiptatvenaikapadārthābhāvād ekaikasmin maṇḍale kāṇḍānusamaya iva samudāyavanto ’nuṣṭheyāḥ | caraṇaprakṣālanācamanapradānāsanopaveśanāni padārthānusamayena | āsanakṣaṇāvāhanāgha- (?) gandhapuṣpadhūpadīpācchādanānāṃ padārthānāṃ kṛtsnaṃ kāṇḍaṃ vaiśvadevikeṣu dvijeṣv apavarjya paitṛkeṣv ārambhaṇīyam | yājñavalkya-matsyapurāṇayos tathā vacanāt |
vacanāt tu parivyāṇāntam añjanādīty atra jaiminināpi pūgagaṇe vacanāt kāṇḍānusamayo varṇitaḥ so ’trāpi vacanād bhavitum arhati |
tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
pāṇipracālanaṃ datvā viṣṭarārthaṃ kuśān api |
āvāhayed anujñāto viśvedevāsa ity ṛcā ||
yā divyā iti mantreṇa haste 'py arghyaṃ viniḥkṣipet |
datvodakaṃ gandhamālyaṃ dhūpadānaṃ sadīpakam ||
apasavyaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā pitṝṇām apradakṣiṇam |
[765]        dviguṇāṃs tu kuśān datvā hy uśantas tvety ṛcā pitṝn ||
āvāhya tad anujñāto japed āyantu nas tataḥ |
yavārthas tu tilaiḥ kāryaḥ kuryād arghyādi pūrvavat || iti |
iha vaiśvadevikeṣu dvijeṣv āsanādyācchādanāntapadārthakāṇḍasya parisamāptim uktvā paitṛkeṣu tadārambhaḥ pratipāditaḥ |
matsyapurāṇe ’pi |
viśvān devān yavaiḥ puṣpair abhyarcyāsanapūrvakam |
pūrayet pātrayugmaṃ tu sthāpya darbhapavitrake ||
śan no devīty apaḥ kuryād yavo 'sīti yavāṃs tathā |
gandhapuṣpaiś ca saṃpūjya vaiśvadevaṃ prati nyaset ||
viśvedevāsa ity ābhyām āvāhya vikired yavān |
gandhapuṣpair alaṃkṛtya yā divyety arghyam utsṛjet |
abhyarcya tair anujñātaḥ pitṛkāryaṃ samārabhet || iti |
tad evam āsanādyācchādanāntāḥ padārtha vaiśvadevike paitṛke ca vacanāt kāṇḍānusamayenānuṣṭhīyamānāḥ | athaikaikavargāntargateṣu brāhmaṇeṣu padārthānusamayenaiva nirvartanīyāḥ pariyantropakalpanapātrāvasthāpana-tilāvakiraṇopastaraṇapariveṣaṇāni sarveṣv api dvijeṣu padārthānusamayenaiva kāryāṇi | pariviṣṭānnaprokṣaṇābhimantraṇāṅguṣṭhaniveśanāni devatoddeśahavistyāgāś ca pradānāntāvadānavad (?) ekapadārthībhūtāḥ samuditā ekaikasmin brāhmaṇe kāṇḍānusamayavad anuṣṭheyāḥ | ekaikaśo ’nye pratipātraṃ somavat pradhānāvṛttiḥ | sakṛtprayogayogyānāṃ tantrateti brāhmaṇeṣu | avanejanādīny ekaikaśaḥ | añjanābhyañjanavāsāṃsi tristrir ekaikasmin [766] nirvāpamuṣṭipuroḍāśakapālāgneyādyavadānadīkṣitābhyañjanādivat piṇḍāñjanādīnām api saṅkhyāviśeṣaviśiṣṭānām eva vihitatvād iti piṇḍeṣu |
athānekāgnaukaraṇānuṣṭhānapakṣe ’nnoddharaṇāt prabhṛty avadānāt prāk sarvaṃ padārthānusamayena kṛtvā pradānāntenaiva caturavattenānusametavyam | daivatāny avadāya na tāvaty eva hotavyaṃ sauviṣṭakṛtāny avadyatīty agnīṣomīyapaśāv atideśāgataṃ caturavattasya pradānāntattvaṃ yathā vacanād apodyate tathātra kutaścid apavādābhāvād ity agnaukaraṇe | atha pātreṣu sarvam ekaikaśa iti |
ity anusamayanirṇayaḥ |
atha bhojyābhojyānnāḥ nimantvaṇaprakaraṇe vacyante | anindyenāmantrito nāpakrāmed iti | tatrānindyā bhojyānnāḥ nindyā abhojyānnāḥ | atas tadvivekaḥ kriyate | tatra gautamaḥ |
praśastānāṃ svakarmasu dvijātīnāṃ brāhmaṇo bhuñjīta |
yamaḥ |
śrotriyāṇāṃ sadāśnīyād doṣas teṣāṃ na vidyate |
vikalpopahatānāṃ ca nāśnīyān naiva dāpayet ||
paratra paralokaśāstrārtheṣu sadartheṣu sadasadvikalpair nityam avyavasthitacittavṛttayo vikalpāpahatāḥ |
yama-hārīta-jamadagni-paiṭhīnasayaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya sadāśnīyāt kṣatriyasya ca parvasu |
prakṛteṣu ca vaiśyasya śūdrasya na kadācana ||
[767]        amṛtaṃ brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ kṣatriyasya payaḥ smṛtam |
vaiśyasya tv annam evānnaṃ śūdrānnaṃ rudhiraṃ smṛtam ||
brāhmaṇānnaṃ kṣatriyānnaṃ vaiśyānnaṃ śaudram eva ca |
tāṃ tāṃ yoniṃ vrajed vipro bhuktvānnaṃ yasya vai mṛtaḥ ||
'sadā' kālaniyamam antareṇa | 'parvasu' praśastadivaseṣu, śraddhayā śrāddhādyarthaṃ nimantritaḥ kṣatriyasya gṛhe ’śnīyāt 'prakṛteṣu' prakaraṇeṣu vivāhādyutsaveṣv iti yāvat | 'amṛtam ityādiḥ pūrvasyārthavādaḥ | 'tāṃ tāṃ yoniṃ vrajed" ityanenānindyayor api kṣatriyavaiśyayor annam asakṛn na bhoktavyam iti gamyate |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
brāhmaṇakṣatriyaviśāṃ yasyānne jaṭharasthe mriyate tām eva yoniṃ vrajatīti |
āpastambaḥ |
trayāṇāṃ kṣatriyaprabhṛtīnāṃ samāvṛttena bhokravyaṃ prakṛtyā brāhmaṇasya bhoktavyaṃ kāraṇād abhojyaṃ yad aprāyaścittaṃ karmasacate prāyaścittavati karmaṇi caritanirveśasya bhoktavyaṃ sarvavarṇānāṃ svadharme vartamānānāṃ bhoktavyaṃ śūdravarjam ity eke |
'prakṛtyā' utsargataḥ | 'kāraṇāt' nimittāt | 'aprāyaścittaṃ karma' prāyaścittānarham abhyastaṃ mahāpātakādi | 'āsacate' saṃbadhyate | 'prāyaścittavati' prāyaścittayogye tu karmaṇi | 'caritanirveśasya' kṛtaprāyaścittasya |
vasiṣṭaḥ |
[768]        śraddadhānasya bhoktavyaṃ caurasyāpi viśeṣataḥ |
na tv eva bahuyājyasya yaś copanayate bahūn ||
caurasyāpīti śraddhāpraśaṃsārtham |
tathā ca manuḥ |
śrotriyasya kadaryasya vadānyasya ca vārddhuṣeḥ |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan ||
tān prajāpatir āha sma mā kṛḍhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
śraddhāpūtaṃ vadānyasya hatam aśraddhayetarat ||
yo dhanavān apy atilobhād ātmānaṃ dharmakṛtyaṃ poṣyavargaṃ ca na puṣṇāti sa ‘kadaryaḥ’| ‘vadānyaḥ’ bahupradānaśīlaḥ | niṣiddhavṛddhyupajīvī 'vārddhuṣikaḥ' | 'mīmāṃsitvā' vicārya | ekaḥ śrotriyo ’pi san kadaryaḥ | itaraś ca vārddhuṣir api san vadānyaḥ | anayor annaṃ tulyatvena kalpayato devān nivārya vadānyasyaiva śraddhāpūtatvena śreṣṭhyaṃ prajāpatiḥ samarthitavān ity arthaḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
śucer aśraddhadhānasya śraddhadhānasya cāśuceḥ |
mīmāṃsitvobhayaṃ devāḥ samam annam akalpayan ||
tān prajāpatir ity āha mākṛḍhvaṃ viṣamaṃ samam |
hatam aśraddadhānasya śraddhāpūtaṃ viśiṣyate ||
yamaḥ |
avaliptasya mūrkhasya durvṛttasya ca durmateḥ |
annam aśraddhadhānasya yo bhuṅkte sa tu bhrūṇahā ||
[769]        śvaśurānnaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte sa bhuṅkte pṛthivīmalam |
nṛṇām āhur malaṃ cānnaṃ sarvam anne pratiṣṭhitam ||
duṣkṛtaṃ hi manuṣyasya annam āśritya tiṣṭhati |
yo yasyānnaṃ samaśnāti sa tasyāśnāti kilbiṣam ||
‘avaliptaḥ' garvitaḥ | ‘mūrkhaḥ' anadhītavedaḥ | 'duṣṭavṛttaḥ' durācāraḥ | 'durmatiḥ' krūrāśayaḥ | śvaśurānam iti sarvadā śvaśurānnabhojanasyaiṣa pratiṣedhaḥ |
anyeṣām api bhojyānnānāṃ gṛhe nityaṃ na bhoktavyam ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
parapākarucir na syād anindyāmantraṇād ṛte |
‘parapākaruciḥ' parapākābhilāṣukaḥ |
manur apy āha |
upāsate ye gṛhasthāḥ parapākam aśuddhayaḥ |
tena te pretya paśutāṃ vrajanty annādyadāyinām ||
atrānāpadīti śeṣaḥ |
tad āha yamaḥ |
svapāke vartamāne yaḥ parapākaṃ niṣevate |
so ’śvatvaṃ śūkaratvaṃ ca gardabhatvaṃ ca gacchati ||
parapākena puṣṭasya dvijasya gṛhamedhinaḥ |
iṣṭaṃ dattaṃ tapo ’dhītaṃ yasyānnaṃ tasya tad bhavet ||
yasyānnena tu bhuktena bhāryāṃ samadhigacchati |
yasyānnaṃ tasya te putrā annād retaḥ pravartate ||
[770]        hārīta-jamadagnī |
brāhmaṇānnena dāridryaṃ kṣatriyānnena preṣyatām |
vaiśyānnena tu śūdratvaṃ śūdrānnair narakaṃ vrajet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gṛhamedhī nānyasyānnaṃ kāṅkṣed ity udāharanti |
parapākam upāśnanti ye dvijā gṛhamedhinaḥ |
śvabhāsarāsabhānāṃ te yoniṃ gacchanti durdhiyaḥ ||
upa samīpaṃ gatvā ye ’śnanti animantritā eva svayaṃ yācitvāśnantīty arthaḥ |
bhojyānnayor api brāhmaṇaśūdrayoḥ kadācid abhojyānnatām āhur yama-śātātapa- chāgaleyāḥ |
abhojyaṃ brāhmaṇasyānnaṃ vṛṣalena nimantritam |
tathaiva vṛṣalasyānnaṃ brāhmaṇena nimantritam ||
brāhmaṇānnaṃ dadac chūdraḥ śūdrānnaṃ brāhmaṇo dadat |
ubhāv etāv abhojyānnau bhuktvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ caret ||
'nimantritaṃ' nimantritabhoktṛkaṃ, bhukvā tad annam iti śeṣaḥ |
anyān apy abhojyānnān āha manuḥ |
nāśrotriyatate yajñe grāmayājahute tathā |
striyā klībena ca hute bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ kvacit ||
'aśrotriyaḥ' anadhīyānaḥ, tena 'tate' prārabdhe, yajñe | ṛtvigbhir vāśrotriyais tatena bhuñjīta brāhmaṇaḥ | 'grāmayājaḥ' bahuyājakaḥ, tena, strīklībābhyāṃ ca yatra hūyate, yatra yajñe bhartā dāridryopahataḥ strī ca saudāyikena dhanena jñātibalena vā darpitā satī pradhānaṃ bhavati sa striyā huta iti medhātithiḥ |
[771]        mattakruddhāturāṇāṃ ca na bhuñjīta kadācana |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca viduṣā ca jugupsitam ||
yāvan madādiyoga eteṣāṃ tāvad abhojyānnāḥ | anye tu bāhulyaṃ manyante | bāhulyena yaḥ klībo bhavati tadannaṃ na bhoktavyam | evaṃ krodhapradhānasvabhāvasya | 'āturaḥ' dīrgharogī, 'gaṇaḥ' saṅghātaḥ, tasyānnaṃ gaṇānnam | bhrātṝṇāṃ tv avibhaktānāṃ na gaṇavyapadeśaḥ,
bhrātṝṇām avibhaktānām eko dharmaḥ pravartate |
iti vacanāt |
ekaś ca dharmas teṣām ātithyādikriyaiveti taddharme varṇitam | 'gaṇikā' veśyā | yad bhakṣyam api viduṣā 'jugupsitaṃ' ninditam |
stenagāyanayoś cānnaṃ takṣṇor vārdhuṣikasya ca |
dīkṣitasya kadaryasya baddhasya nigaḍasya ca ||
'gāyanā' atra gītopajīvī | anyasya tūparāntakādigānaṃ vihitam eva | 'takṣā' bardhakiḥ | 'dīkṣitaḥ' dīkṣaṇīyeṣṭyādijanitadīkṣākhyasaṃskāravān | dīkṣākhyasaṃskāravattātrāvabhṛtheṣṭeḥ prāg eva, avabhṛtheṣṭyā tatsaṃskārāpagamāt | evaṃ ca dīkṣaṇīyeṣṭyanuṣṭhānaprabhṛtyavabhṛtheṣṭy-anuṣṭhānaparyantaṃ dīkṣitasyābhojyānnatvam anayā smṛtyoktam iti yady api pratibhāti tathāpi saṃsthite ’gnīṣomīye yajamānasya gṛhe ’śitavyam ityādiśrutyā saty api dīkṣākhyasaṃskāre ’gnīṣomīyapaśuyāgasamāpteḥ prāg eva bhojyānnatvāvagamāt tadavirodhāya tatparyantam evābhojyānnatvam anayā smṛtyādyuktam iti mantavyam |
āpastambas tu pūrvam apy agnīṣomīyavapāhomād yajñārthaṃ dravye pṛthakṛte yad avaśiṣṭaṃ tad eva dīkṣitasyāpi bhoktavyam ity uktavān | na dīkṣitasya [772] vasanaṃ paridadhīta nāsya pāpaṃ kīrtayet nānnam aśnīyāt yajñārthe vā nirdiṣṭe śeṣād bhuñjīran saṃsthite vāgnīṣomīye hutāyāṃ vā vapāyām iti | śatapathe tu dīkṣitasyāṅgulīvisarjanāt pūrvam abhojyānnatvaṃ taduttarakāle ca bhojyatvaṃ sārthavādam uktam | tasmād asmān nāśnanti mānuṣo hi bhavati tasmād asyātra nāma gṛhṇanti mānuṣo hi bhavaty atha yat purā nāśnanti yathā haviṣo hutasya nāśnīyād evaṃ tat tasmād dīkṣitasya nāśnīyād athātrāṅgulīr visṛjata iti | 'nigaḍaḥ' nigaḍitaḥ | baddhanigaḍayor iyān viśeṣaḥ | eko vāṅmātreṇāvaruddhaḥ, aparo rajjvādinā niyantritaḥ |
abhiśastasya ṣaṇḍasya puṃścalyā dāmbhikasya ca |
cikitsakasya mṛgayoḥ krūrasyocchiṣṭabhojinaḥ |
ugrānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca paryācāntam anirdaśam ||
'abhiśastaḥ' patanīyakarmakāritvenābhiyuktaḥ | 'ṣaṇḍaḥ' napuṃsakaḥ | 'puṃścalī' vyabhicāriṇī | 'dāmbhikaḥ' khyātilābhādiprayojanābhisandhānenaiva dharmānuṣṭhātā | 'cikitsakaḥ' cikitsāvṛttiḥ, asamyakcikitsaka itaro ’pi | ‘mṛgayuḥ' mṛgavyādhaḥ, ya ākheṭakārthaṃ māṃsavikrayārthaṃ vā prāyo mṛgān hanti | 'krūraḥ' anṛjuprakṛtir duḥprasāda iti yāvat | 'ucchiṣṭabhojī' parabhuktojjhitānnabhojanaśīlaḥ | 'ugraḥ' jātiviśeṣaḥ, "śūdrākṣatriyayor ugra" ity amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt | paruṣabhāṣaṇādinā duḥsahasannidhir vā | 'sūtikānnaṃ' sūtikām uddiśya yat kṛtaṃ tatkulīnair api tan na bhojyam | 'anirdaśaṃ' daśāhaṃ yāvad aśaucinām annaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ | tena yady api kṣatriyādīnāṃ daśāhād ūrdhvam apy aśaucaṃ tathāpi daśaivāhāni na bhojyam | sūtakānnam iti pāṭhāntare sūtakaśabdena tadvantaḥ puruṣā [773] ucyante, te ca daśāham abhojyānnāḥ | anirdaśagrahaṇam aśaucānivṛttyupalakṣaṇārthaṃ tena yasya yāvān aśaucakālaḥ sa tāvatkālam abhojyānnaḥ | prathamapāṭhe vyākhyānāntaram | sūtikāyāḥ prasūtāyā gavāder annam adanīyaṃ dugdhādi anirdaśam anatikrāntadaśāhaṃ na bhojyam | 'pari' madhye, ‘ācāntaṃ' ācamanaṃ yasya tatparyācāntam ācamanavyapetam (?) ity arthaḥ | arddhabhuktaḥ kenacit kāraṇena yadācāmati tadā bhuktocchiṣṭaṃ punar nāśnitavyas |
anarcitaṃ vṛthāmāṃsam avīrāyāś ca yoṣitaḥ |
dviṣadannaṃ nagaryannaṃ patitānnam avakṣutam ||
arcanīyebhyo yad avajñayā dīyate tad anarcitaṃ, na tu suhṛdādeḥ | 'vṛthāmāṃsaṃ' devārcanaśiṣṭaṃ yan māṃsaṃ na bhavati ātmārthaṃ yat sādhitam | avīrā strī yasyā na bhartā nāpi putraḥ | 'dviṣan' śatruḥ | 'nagarī' rājavyatirikto nagarasvāmī | 'avakṣutaṃ' yasyopari kṣavathuḥ kṛtaḥ |
piśunānṛtinoś cānnaṃ kratuvikrayikasya ca |
śailūṣatantuvāyānnaṃ kṛtaghnasyānnam eva ca ||
'piśunaḥ' yo viśrabdham arthaṃ kathitaṃ bhinatti | parokṣaṃ parachidravādī vā | ‘anṛtī' kṛtakoṭasākṣyaḥ | 'kratuvikrayikaḥ' kratur yajñaḥ taṃ kṛtvā vikrīṇīte kratuphalaṃ madīyaṃ tavāstv iti mūlyena tad dadāti | yady api paramārthataḥ krator vikrayo nāsti tathāpi yasyaivaṃvidhabhrānyā vipralambhena vā pravṛttis tadannasya pratiṣedhaḥ | 'śailūṣaḥ' naṭabhāryaḥ, 'tantuvāyaḥ' sūcīśilpopajīvī | 'kṛtaghnaḥ' kṛtam upakāraṃ vināśyopakartur apakāre varttate yaś ca tasya na pratyupakaroti |
karmārasya niṣādasya raṅgāvatarakasya ca |
[774]        suvarṇakartur venasya śastravikrayiṇas tathā ||
'karmāraḥ' lohakāravṛttijīvī | śūdrāyāṃ brāhmaṇāj jātaḥ 'niśādaḥ' | 'raṅgāvatarakaḥ' naṭagāyanakebhyo’nyo mallādiḥ | yo vā kutūhalena pratiraṅgam upatiṣṭhate | 'venaḥ' vāditrajsvanaḥ | 'śastravikrayī' kṛtasya khaḍgāder akṛtasya vā ayaso vikretā |
śvavatāṃ śauṇḍikānāṃ ca cailanirṇejakasya ca |
rajakasya nṛśaṃsasya yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
ākheṭakādyarthaṃ ye śuno bibhrati te 'śvavantaḥ' | 'śauṇḍikāḥ' madyavyasaninaḥ, tatpaṇyajīvino vā | cailaṃ vastraṃ nirṇenektīti 'cailanirṇejakaḥ' vastradhāvaka ity arthaḥ | ' rajakaḥ' vāsasāṃ nīlādirāgakaraḥ | ‘nṛśaṃsaḥ’ nṝn manuṣyān śaṃsati stauti yo loke vandīti prasiddhaḥ, atha vā nirdayāśayo nṛśaṃsaḥ | 'upapatiḥ' jāraḥ, yasya bhāryāyā gṛhe jāro 'pi vartate |
puṣyanti ye copapatiṃ strojitānāṃ ca sarvaśaḥ |
daśāhikaṃ ca pretānnam atuṣṭikaram eva ca ||
pūrvaśloke bhartrā avidito bhāryāījāro gṛhe vasatīty uktam | atra tu gṛhād anyatrāpi vasantaṃ bhāryājāraṃ viditvāpi yaḥ kṣamate, na bhāryāyā nigrahaṃ karoti, nāpi jārasya, tadannasya pratiṣedhaḥ | ato yeṣām aviditā gṛhāntaravāsinaś ca bhāryājārās te bhojyānnāḥ | 'strījitāḥ' yeṣāṃ gṛheṣu bhāryaiva kartrī hartrī ca, svayaṃ parijanasya dhanasya vā [775] neśate | daśāhikaṃ pratiṣedhati, tad eva pretānnaṃ, pretam uddiśya yad annaṃ kṛtam | yasyāśaucaṃ nāsti suhṛdbāndhavādeḥ tasyāpi kāruṇyāc caturthīśrāddhādipravṛttasya yad annaṃ tan na bhoktavyam | "daśāhikaṃ nāvamikaṃ caturthīśrāddhaṃ" ityādinā rāmāyaṇe varṇitam | varṇitam anyair api gṛhyakāraḥ | 'atuṣṭhikaraṃ' yasmin bhujyamāne cittatuṣṭir na bhavati |
āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
adattāny agnihīnasya nānnam adyād anāpadi |
kadaryabaddhacaurāṇāṃ klībaraṅgāvatāriṇām ||
vaiṇābhiśastavārddhuṣyagaṇikāgaṇadīkṣiṇām |
cikitsakāturakruddhapuṃścalīmattavidviṣām ||
krūrograpatitavrātyadāmbhikocchiṣṭabhojinām |
avīrāstrīsvarṇakārastrījitagrāmayājinām ||
śastravikrayikamāratantuvāyaśvavṛttinām |
nṛśaṃsarājarajakakṛtaghnavadhajīvinām ||
cailadhāvasurājīvasahopapativeśmanām |
piśunānṛtinoś caiva tathā cākrikavandinām |
eṣām annaṃ na bhoktavyaṃ somavikrayiṇas tathā ||
adattānīti pūrvavākyāvayavaḥ, śrautasmārtāgnyadhikārarahitasya śūdrasya pratilomajasya ca tathā saty apy adhikāre ’gniparigraham akurvāṇasyotsṛṭāgneś cānnam anāpadi na bhuñjītety arthaḥ | ananārthād āpady agnihīnasyānnam adyād ity ukaṃ bhavati | 'cauraḥ' brahmasvavyatiriktaparadravyāpahartā, 'vaiṇaḥ' vīṇāvādanajīvī | ‘vārdhaṣyaṃ’ niṣiddhavṛddhyupajīvanaṃ, tena ca tadvān [776] lakṣyate | 'dīkṣī' dīkṣitaḥ | 'vidviṭ' śatruḥ | 'patitaḥ' brahmatyādimahāpātakakartā | 'vrātyaḥ' patitasāvitrīkaḥ | 'śvavṛttī' śvavṛttavān sevaka iti yāvat | yad vā śunāṃ vṛtam asti yasmin sa śvavṛttī śunakavān | 'rājā' janapadapuraparipālakaḥ | yaḥ prāṇivadhena jīvati sa 'vadhajīvī' | 'cailadhāvaḥ' vastraprakṣālakaḥ | 'surājīvī' surāvikrayī | 'sahopapativeśma' (?) nijabhāryājārasahitagṛhaḥ | 'somavikrayī' somalatāvikretā | 'cākrikaḥ' tailikaḥ | 'vandī' parastutipāṭhakaḥ | eṣāṃ kadaryatvādidoṣaduṣṭānāṃ traivarṇikāṇām annaṃ kadaryatvādidoṣaduṣṭasvāmisaṃbandhāt paramparayā duṣṭaṃ na bhoktavyam ity arthaḥ | yady api dīkṣitānnasyāśrayadoṣābhāvān na kṛtaduṣṭatvabhāvas tathāpi tasya vacanabalād evābhojyatvam |
yamaḥ |
naṭanartakatakṣāṇaś carmakāraḥ suvarṇakṛt |
sthāyukāḥ ṣaṇḍagaṇikā abhojyānnāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
gāndharvo lohakāraś ca saucīkas tantuvāyakaḥ |
cakropajīvī rajakaḥ kitavas taskaras tathā ||
dhvajī mānopajīvī ca śūdrādhyāpakajīvakau |
kulālaś citrakarmā ca vārddhuṣī carmavikrayī ||
'naṭaḥ' svayaṃ nṛtyakartā | 'nartakaḥ' nṛtyakriyopadeṣṭā | 'sthāyukāḥ' grāmādhikṛtāḥ, "sthāyuko ’dhikṛto grāma" ity amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt | 'gāndharvaḥ' gāyanaḥ | 'saucīkaḥ' sūcīśilpopajīvī | 'tantuvāyaḥ' kuvindaḥ | 'cakropajīvī' śākaṭikaḥ | 'kitavaḥ' dyūtakāraḥ | [777] 'dhvajī' madyavikrayī | 'mānopajīvī' dhānyādimānakarmaṇā yo jīvati | 'dāropajīvīti kvacit pāṭhaḥ | tatra dārārjitenārthena yo jīvati sa tathā |
sumantuḥ |
abhiśastapatitapaunarbhavabhrūṇahapuṃścalyaśuciśastrakāratailikacākrikadhvaji-suvarṇakāralekhakapaṇḍakabandhakīgaṇagaṇikānnāni cābhojyāni | saukarikavyādhaniṣyandarajakaburuḍacarmakārā abhovyānnā apratigrāhyāḥ |
'cākrikaḥ' vaitālikaḥ, "vaitālikā bodhakarāś cākrikā ghāṇṭikārthakāḥ" ity amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt | 'lekhakaḥ' citrakaraḥ | ‘paṇḍakaḥ' paṇḍaḥ | 'bandhakī' puṃścalī | 'saukarikaḥ' yaḥ sūkaraiś carati | 'vyādhaḥ' mṛgavadhajīvī | ‘vuruḍaḥ’ śūrpapiṭakādivaṃśapātrakatā
vasiṣṭaḥ|
cikitsakamṛgayupuṃścalīdāṇḍikastenābhiśastaśaṭhapatitānām annam anādyaṃ tathā kadaryadīkṣitabaddhāturasomavikrayitakṣakarajakaśauṇḍisaunikavārdhuṣika-carmāvakṛtānām | śūdrasya cāsvabhūtasya upapater yaś copapatiṃ manyate yaś ca gṛhān dahet yaś ca vadhārhān upahanyāt | ko bhokṣyata iti cāvakruṣṭaṃ gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ceti |
athāpyudāharanti |
nāśnanti śvavato devā nāśnanti vṛṣalīpateḥ |
bhāryājitasya nāśnanti yasya copapatir gṛhe ||
'dāṇḍikaḥ' yo daṇḍaṃ gṛhītvā rājādīnām agrata caran manuṣyān utsārayati sa 'dāṇḍikaḥ' | 'śaṭhaḥ' anṛjuhṛdayaḥ | 'takṣakaḥ' vardhakaḥ | [778] 'asvabhūtasya' yaṃ prati svasvāmisambandho nāsti | yaḥ svabhāryāyā upapati 'manyate' anumanyate | śvāno vidyante yasyāsau śvavān tasya
'śvavataḥ' |
gautamaḥ |
utsṛṣṭhapuṃñcalyabhiśastānapadeśyadāṇḍikatakṣakakadaryabandhanikacikitsaka-mṛgayuviṣacāryucchiṣṭabhojigaṇavidviṣāṇānām apaṅktyānāṃ prāgdubālād annaṃ nādyāt | vṛthānnācamanotthānavyapetāni samāsamābhyāṃ viṣamasame pūjitān arcitaṃ ca |
'utsṛṣṭaḥ' svajātibahiṣkṛtaḥ | 'anapadeśyaḥ' avijñātakulaśīladeśādiḥ | 'bandhanikaḥ' vāgurādibandhanaiḥ prāṇihantā | 'viṣacārī' yo viṣavidyayā jīvati | 'vidviṣāṇaḥ' śatruḥ | apaṅktayaprakaraṇe duvālāt pūrvaṃ yāvantaḥ kathitāḥ eteṣām annaṃ nādyāt | 'ācamanavyapetaṃ' anācāntena dattam | 'utthānavyapetaṃ' abhyutthānādisatkāraśūnyam | tathā samena viṣamaṃ, viṣameṇa samaṃ, pūjārheṇa cānarcitaṃ nāśitavyam |
āpastambaḥ |
saṅghānnam abhojyaṃ parikruṣṭaṃ ca sarveṣāṃ ca śilpajīvānāṃ ye ca śastreṇa jīvanti ye cānnibhir bhiṣag vārdhuṣikā dīkṣito’krītarājako 'gnīṣomīyasaṃsthāyām eva hutāyāṃ vā vapāyāṃ dīkṣitasya bhoktavyam | yajñārthe vā nirdiṣṭe śeṣād bhuñjīrann iti brāhmaṇaṃ klībo rājapreṣakaro haviryājī cāvidhinā pravrajito yaś cāgnīn apāsyet yaś ca sarvān varjayet sarvānnīno śrotriyo nirākṛtir vṛṣalīpatir matta unmatto baddho vaṇik pratyupaviṣṭo yaś ca pratyupaviṣṭayate tāvantaṃ kālam | ke asyānno ya īpsed iti kāṇvaḥ | puṇya iti kautsaḥ | yaḥ kaścid dadyād iti vārṣyāyaṇiḥ | yadi hi rajaḥ sthāvaraṃ puruṣeṇa bhoktavyam apy aced (?) annadānena nirdeṣo bhavati śuddhā bhikṣā bhoktavyaikataḥkuṇikau (?) kaṇṭhakutsau tathā pauṣkarasādiḥ | [779] sarvato ’py annaṃ vārṣyayaṇiḥ | puṣyasyepsyato bhoktavyaṃ puṣyasyāpy anīpsato na bhoktavyaṃ yaḥ sarvān varjayate kasyāpi gṛhe nāśnāti |
‘sarvānnīnaḥ' sarvānnabhojī, “sarvānnīnas tu sarvānnabhojī gṛdhnus tu gardhanaḥ” iti amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt |
ślokāpastambaḥ |
dvāv evāśramiṇau bhojyau brahmacārī tathā gṛhī |
muner annam abhojyaṃ syāt sarveṣāṃ liṅgināṃ tathā ||
'muniḥ' vānaprastho yatiś ca | 'liṅgināṃ' śrutibāhyaliṅgadhāriṇām |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
bhītaruditākranditāvakruṣṭāvakṣutaparibhuktavismitonmattāvadhūtarājapurohitānnāni varjayet |
punaḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
vidviṣāṇasya nāśnīyāt brahmavidyāpakāriṇaḥ |
śrāddhasūtagaṇānnāni paribhūtāni yāni ca ||
devalaḥ |
patitānnam abhojyānnam apāṅkteyānnam eva ca |
śūdrānnaṃ kutsitānnaṃ ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||
vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
anirdaśāhe prasave niyogād bhuktavān dvijaḥ |
kṛmir bhūtvā sa dehānte tāṃ viṣṭhāṃ samupāśnute ||
yamaḥ |
yas tu prāṇān vimuñcet tu bhuktvā śrāddhaṃ navaṃ dvijaḥ |
[780]        agrāhyāsu sughorāsu tiryagyoniṣu pacyate ||
yas tu prajāyate garbho bhuktvā śrāddhaṃ navaṃ dvijāt |
na sa vidyām avāpnoti kṣīṇāyuś caiva jāyate ||
tathā |
bhuktaṃ rājabhṛtasyānnaṃ caurasyānnaṃ tathaiva ca |
sūtake mṛtake cānnaṃ svargastham api pātayet ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
parāśauce naro bhuktvā kṛmiyonau prajāyate |
bhuktvānnaṃ mriyate yasya tasya jātau prajāyate ||
āpastambaḥ |
yasya kule mriyate tatrānirdaśe na bhoktavyam | tathānutthitāyāṃ sūtikāyām
antaḥśave ca |
aṅgirāḥ |
janmaprabhṛtisaṃskāre bālasyānnasya bhojane |
asapiṇḍair na bhoktavyaṃ śmaśānānte viśeṣataḥ ||
śātātapaḥ |
yasya nāśnanti vai devāḥ pitaraś ca tathātithiḥ |
vṛthā pākaḥ sa vijñeyo na tasyādyāt kathaṃcana ||
āpastambaḥ |
dviṣan dviṣato vā nānnam aśnīyād doṣeṇa vā mīmāṃsyamānasya mīmāṃsitasya vā tathā pāpmānaṃ hi tasya bhakṣayatīti vijñāyate |
manu-vasiṣṭhau |
[781]        annāder bhrūṇahā mārṣṭiṃ patyau bhāryāpacāriṇī |
gurau śiṣyaś ca yājyāś ca steno rājani kilbiṣam ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
annāder bhrūṇahā mārṣṭi patyau bhāryāpacāriṇī |
gurau śiṣyaś ca yājyaś caḥ steno rājani kilbiṣam ||
aṅgirāḥ |
aprajānāṃ tu nārīṇāṃ nāśnīyāj jātu tadgṛhe |
mohād vā yas tu bhuñjīta sa pūyaṃ narakaṃ vrajet ||
'nārīṇāṃ' duhitṝṇām |
tathā ca chāgaleyaḥ |
aprajāyās tu kanyāyā nāśnīyāj jātucid gṛhe |
gāndharve caiva rājanyaḥ kuryād vai gamanāgamam ||
brahmadeyāṃ na vai kanyāṃ datvāśnīyāt kadācana |
atha bhuñjīta mohātmā pūrvaṃ sa narakaṃ vrajet ||
brāhmādivivāhacatuṣṭayapariṇītā 'brahmadeyā' |
yamaḥ |
adhītya caturo vedān sāṅgopāṅgān viśeṣataḥ |
narendrasya gṛhe bhuktvā kṛmiyonau prajāyate ||
rājānnaṃ harate tejaḥ śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
vaiśyānnaṃ sūtikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati ||
hārītaḥ |
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
[782]        gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokān api nikṛntati ||
ya icchec chuddham ātmānaṃ iṣṭān lokāṃś ca veditum |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca dūrataḥ parivarjayet ||
ayaṃ ca svadharmavyapetasya rājño ’nnabhojane pratiṣedha iti draṣṭavyam,
prajāpālanahīnānāṃ rājñām ucchāstravartinām |
annaṃ na jātu bhoktavyaṃ bhuñjāno rauravaṃ vrajet ||
iti chāgaleyasmaraṇāt |
āha manuḥ |
rājānnaṃ teja ādatte śūdrānnaṃ brahmavarcasam |
āyuḥ suvarṇakārānnaṃ yaśaś carmāvakartinaḥ ||
kārukānnaṃ prajāṃ hanti balaṃ nirṇejakasya ca |
gaṇānaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca lokebhyaḥ parikṛntati ||
pūyaṃ cikitsakasyānnaṃ puṃścalyās tv annam indriyam |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ śastravikrayiṇo malam ||
ya ebhyo ’nye tv abhojyānnāḥ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
teṣāṃ tvagasthilomāni vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ||
'pūyaṃ' pūyatulyaṃ, cikitsakasyānnaṃ bhojane | ebhyaḥ pratipadanirdiṣṭebhyo ye ’nye abhojyānnā asmin prakaraṇe paṭhitās teṣāṃ yad annaṃ tat tvagasthiromatulyam | yas tadīyāyāṃ tvaci bhuktāyāṃ doṣaḥ sa evānne 'pīti |
āha paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gaṇānnaṃ gaṇikānnaṃ ca duṣkṛtaṃ, vārdhuṣer viṣṭhā sāṃvatsaraghāṇṭikagrāma-kauṭānnaṃ viṣaḥ, bandhakīnāṃ retaḥ, bhiṣakśalyakṛtoḥ pūyaṃ, parivitti[783]parivividānaviddhaprajananavṛṣalīpatididhiṣūpati-agredidhiṣūpati-punarbhūputrāṇāṃ rudhiraṃ patitānāṃ ca duṣkṛtahetutvād duṣkṛtam |
'sāṃvatsaraḥ' muhūrttopadeśajīvī, 'grāmakauṭaḥ' grāme 'py ananyādhīnaḥ takṣā, “kauṭatakṣo ’nadhīnakaḥ” ity amarasiṃhenābhidhānāt | 'grāmakūṭaḥ' iti tu pāṭhe grāmavāsinām anekopadravair ātmambhariḥ 'grāmakūṭaḥ' |
yamaḥ |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ rudhiraṃ brahmaghātinaḥ |
pūyaṃ cikitsakasyānnaṃ bandhakyās tv annam indriyam ||
malaṃ hi rajakasyānnaṃ carmakārasya kilbiṣam |
yakṣmā tathānnaṃ śauṇḍasya ghāṇṭhikasya tathaiva ca ||
dūtare ye tv abhojyānnās teṣām annaṃ vivarjayet |
teṣāṃ tvagasthiromāṇi vadanty annaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ ||
'yakṣmā' rogaḥ | 'śauṇḍaḥ' surāvikrayī |
hārītaḥ |
viṣṭhā vārdhuṣikasyānnaṃ bhiṣajāṃ caiva śoṇitam |
reto ’nnaṃ bandhakīnāṃ tu gaṇānnam iha duṣkṛtam ||
duṣkṛtaṃ patitasyānaṃ tāskaraṃ pūyaśoṇitam |
sūtikānnaṃ rajaḥ prāhuḥ śvaviṣṭhā śūdrayājake ||
'rajaḥ' ārtavam |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gṛhamedhī na śūdrānnam aśnīyād asmād brahmavarcasaṃ balaṃ tejaḥ iti vyapakrāmati |
hārītaḥ |
[784]        śūdrānnena tu bhukrena jaṭharasthena yo mṛtaḥ |
sa vai kharatvam uṣṭratvaṃ śūdratvaṃ copagacchati ||
vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
śūdrānnenodarasthena yaḥ kaścin mriyate dvijaḥ |
sa bhavet sūkaro grāmyas tasya vā jāyate kule ||
śūdrānnarasapuṣṭāṅgo hy adhīyāno ’pi nityaśaḥ |
juhvad vāpi japan vāpi gatim ūrddhvāṃ na vindati ||
śūdrānnena ca bhuktena maithunaṃ yo ’dhigacchati |
yasyānnaṃ tasya te putrā na ca svargrāhako bhavet ||
yamaḥ |
śūdrānnenodarasthena brāhmaṇo yas tyajet tanum |
āhitāgnis tathā yajvā sa śūdragatim āpnuyāt ||
yaś cāhitāgnir vipras tu śūdrānnān na nivartate |
pañca tasya praṇaśyanti hy ātmā brahma trayo ’gnayaḥ ||
aṅgirāḥ |
ṣaṇmāsān yo dvijo bhuṅkte śūdrasyānnaṃ vigarhitam |
sa ca jīvan bhavec chūdro mṛtaḥ śvā cābhijāyate ||
śūdrasya na kadācanetyādibhiḥ sakṛd api śūdrānnasyābhojyatve siddhe ṣaṇmāsavacanaṃ doṣādhikyapratipādanārtham |
vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
aśauce yas tu śūdrasya sūtake vāpi bhuktavān |
sa gacchen narakaṃ ghoraṃ tiryagyonau ca jāyate ||
[785]        āśaucādivacanam api doṣātiśayārtham eva |
aviśeṣeṇoktasya śūdrānnapratiṣedhasya viṣayaviśeṣam āha manuḥ |
nādyāc chūdrasya pakvānnaṃ vidvān aśrāddhino dvijaḥ |
ādadītāmam evāsmād avṛttāv ekarātrikam ||
'aśrāddhinaḥ' śrāddhaśabdena pākayajñādikriyā śūdrasya vihitopalakṣyate | tena taduktasatkriyānuṣṭhāyinaḥ sacchūdrād anyasya yat pakvam annaṃ tan nādyāt | 'aśraddhinaḥ' iti vā pāṭhaḥ aśraddadhānasyety arthaḥ | 'āmaṃ' apakvaṃ dhānyataṇḍulādi, tac ca 'aikarātrikaṃ' ekāhaparyāptaṃ na bahu | 'avṛttau' prakārāntareṇa vṛttyabhāve sati |
na śūdrānnam aśnīyād ity ukaṃ tatra pratiprasavārtham āhatur manu-viṣṇū |
ardhikaḥ kulamitraṃ ca gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
'ardhikaḥ' yo bhūmikarṣaṇādinā sasyārdhabhāgī | 'kulamitraṃ' pitṛpitāmahādikramāyātam | 'gopālaḥ' gavāṃ pālanena yo jīvati | 'dāsāḥ' garbhadāsādayaḥ | dāsagopālau saṃbandhiśabdau | yo yasya dāsaḥ yaś ca yasya gāḥ pālayati sa tasya bhojyānnaḥ | 'nāpitaḥ' kṣaurakarmā | ahaṃ
tvaccharaṇaḥ tvayi saṃbaddho vatsyāmīty evaṃ vāṅmanaḥkāyakarmabhir ātmānaṃ yo nivedayati | śudreṣv iti nirddhāraṇe saptamī | tena śūdrāṇaṃ madhye ardhikādayo bhojyānnāḥ nānye śūdrāḥ |
ātmanivedanam api vyaktīkaroti manuḥ |
yādṛśo ’sya bhaved ātmā yādṛśaṃ ca cikīrṣitam |
yathā copacared enaṃ tathātmānaṃ nivedayet |
[786]        yatkulīno yac ca veṣo yacchilpaś ca cikīrṣitam ||
anena kāryeṇa tvām aham āśrito dharmeṇānyena vā rājakularakṣādinā prayojanena yathā copacarec chilpenānena tvāṃ seve pādamardanādinā gṛhakṛtyakāritvena vā, evaṃ sarvasmin nivedite ātmā nivedito bhavati | anye tu ātmā vai putra nāmāsīty apatyavacanam ātmaśabdaṃ manyamānāḥ yasya śūdrasya kāmataḥ pravṛttau duhitā vivāhyate tasyānena bhojyānnātocyata ity āhuḥ | tad ayuktaṃ | na tāvad ayam ātmaśabdo duhitari spaṣṭaṃ prayuktaḥ, putraśabdaś ca puṃsy eva prasiddhataraḥ | na ca parokṣaśabdopadeśena kiṃci tprayojanam asti | tathā hi sati etāvad eva vaktuṃ yuktaṃ dadyād duhitaraṃ ca ya iti |
anye tv ardhikādigrahaṇaṃ saṃbandhiśūdropalakṣaṇārthaṃ (?) varṇayanti tena pāraśavasyāpi śūdrasya bhojyānnatā siddhā bhavati |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
śūdreṣu dāsagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
bhojyānnā nāpitaś caiva yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
'ardhasīrī' saṃbhūyasamutthāpitayā halaparyāyasīropalakṣitakṛṣiphalabhāgagrāhī |
yamaḥ |
kulamitraṃ cārdhasīrī gopālo dāsanāpitau |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
dāsanāpitagopālakulamitrārdhasīriṇaḥ |
ete śūdreṣu bhojyānnā yaś cātmānaṃ nivedayet ||
[787]        devalaḥ |
svadāso nāpito gopaḥ kumbhakārakṛṣīvalau |
brāhmaṇaiś caiva bhojyānnāḥ pañcaite śūdrayonayaḥ ||
chāgaleyaḥ |
gopanāpitakumbhakārakulamitrārdhikaniveditātmāno bhojyānnāḥ |
śūdrādhikāre gautamaḥ |
paśupālakṣetrikakulasaṃgatakārayitṛparicārakā bhojyānnāḥ |
'paśupālaḥ' gopālaḥ | 'kṣetrikaḥ' ardhasīrī | 'kārayitā' nāpitaḥ | 'paricārakaḥ' dāsaḥ |
sumantuḥ |
kṛṣigopālakṣetrikaparicārakanāpitaniveditātmānaś ca bhojyānnāḥ |
śāṭyāyaniḥ |
kulamitraṃ kulaputro bhakṣadaḥ śiṣyakaḥ suhṛt |
bhaved yasya sukhaṃ lābhe bhaye trātā ca yo bhavet |
ete tu śūdrā bhojyānnā mano yatra vibhāvyate ||
'kulaputraḥ' dāsaḥ | anye tu kulakarṣakaḥ tatputraḥ kulaputra ity āhuḥ | 'vibhāvyate' prasīdati |
iti bhojyānnanirūpaṇam |
atha dravyaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
tatrāśuddhir nāma dravyādeḥ sparśanādyanarhatāpādako doṣaviśeṣaḥ | śuddhis tu saṃskāraviśeṣotpāditā tannivṛttiḥ | śuddhim ata eva ca sakalakarmopayogayogyatvam |
[788]        tad uktaṃ brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
śrautaṃ smārtaṃ tathā karma kartavyam adhikāriṇā |
śucinā sādhanaiḥ śuddhaiḥ samyak śraddhānvitena ca || iti |
