ato yathāśāstraṃ śucinā śucibhir eva dravyaiḥ pracaraṇīyam | nanv atra kiṃ śāstreṇa, lokavyavahāreṇa prasiddhatvāt śuddhyaśuddhyoḥ, vyavaharaty eva hi loko jugupsitamūtrapurīṣādisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ dravyam aśuddham iti prakṣālanādyapanītagandhalepaṃ ca śuddham iti | satyaṃ | kiṃ tv ayaṃ lokavyavahāraḥ kvacid eva jugupsitasparśopahata eva dravye na sarvatra | sāmānyataś cāsau vyavahāro na dravyadeśakālāvasthādikṛtabheda-śuddhyaśuddhitāratamyaviśeṣeṇāpi | ataḥ sarvatra viśeṣataś ca śuddhāśuddhoḥ śāstrād eva viniścayaḥ | yas tu caṇḍālasparśādāv api viśeṣataś ca lokavyavahāraḥ sa tu śāstramūlikām eva siddhim āśritya yāgadānahomādiṣu śreyaḥsādhanatvavyavahāravat | kathaṃ punaḥ kāryārthapareṇa dharmaśāstreṇa śuddhyāśuddhirūpasya siddhārthasyāvagatiḥ śrūtārthāpattyeti brūmaḥ | tathā hi yathā darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajetety atra kṣaṇikānāṃ yāgānāṃ śrūyamāṇakālāntarabhāvisvargasādhanatvānyathānupapattyā sukṛtāpūrvasiddhiḥ, yathā vā kṣaṇikasyāvagoraṇasya śrutakālāntarabhāviśatayātanāsādhanatvānyathānupapattyā dūritā pūrvasiddhiḥ | tathā mūtrapurīṣādispṛṣṭena na vyavahartavyaṃ prakṣālanaprokṣaṇādi-saṃskāravatā tu tena vyavahartavyam iti śrutavyavahāraniṣedhatadabhyanujñānyathānupapattyā dravyādigataśuddhyaśuddhirūpasiddhārthakalpanā na virudhyate | atha vā śuddhyaśuddhismṛtīnāṃ siddhārthaparatvam [789] evāstu | tāsāṃ ca tādṛśam eva vedavākyaṃ mūlaṃ kalpyate | smaryamāṇārthānusāriṇī hi kalpanā | nāsti niyamaḥ kāryārthenaiva vedavākyena bhāvyam iti |
nanu siddhārthānāṃ smṛtīnāṃ śabdasmṛtivad anindyaprathamavṛddhavyavahāra-mūlatvasambhavāt kiṃ vedamūlatvakalpanayā | maivaṃ | vyavahāramūlatve vidhyānarthakyaṃ syāt | kathaṃ tarhi “sādhubhir bhāṣitavyam asādhubhir na” iti pāṇinismṛter arthavattvam | kathaṃ ca svatvaviṣaye “svāṃśaṃ haret" ityasya vidheḥ | ucyate | yā tāvat sādhubhāṣaṇaniyamasmṛtiḥ sā na pāṇineḥ kiṃ tu kalpasūtrakārāṇāṃ | yā ca "svāṃśaṃ haret” iti smṛtiḥ, na tatra vidhir asti yasyānarthakyaṃ syāt | na ca liṅśruter vidhikalpanā, prāptakālādāv api liṅsaṃbhavāt | na cātra vidheyam asti, aṃśaharaṇasya rāgataḥ prāpteḥ | hared iti ca vidhāv aharaṇe pratyavāyaḥ syāt | svāṃśam eva hared iti niyame parāṃśaṃ na vā hared iti parisaṃkhyāyāṃ bhrātṛbhir abhyanujñātam api vibhāgarūpeṇādhikam aṃśaṃ gṛhṇan pratyaveyāt | yadi ceyaṃ pratiṣedharūpā parisaṃkhyā syāt tadā pratiṣedhātikramakṛtaṃ pratyavāyam abhyupagamya yaḥ kaścid ananujñātam api parāṃśaṃ gṛhṇīyāt tatra tasya svatvaṃ prasajyeta | na hi tadā parigrahād anyat svatvakāraṇaṃ kiṃcit syāt | tathā ca cauryādināpy arjyeta dhanaṃ | ataś ca svāṃśaṃ gṛhṇīyād ity eṣā smṛtiḥ svāṃśa eva svatvaṃ nānyatrety etāvanmātrapratipādanaparādhyavasīyate | ata eva gautamaḥ svatvam evāha | “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu” ityādi | tad evam anindyaprathamavṛddha-vyavahārasiddhe ’rthe nāsti vidhiḥ | iha tu prakṣālitenaiva vyavahartavyam iti niyame vidhir dṛśyate | ato vedamūlatvakalpanā |
[790]        nanu tarhi vyavahāraniyamo ’stu vedamūlaḥ na śuddhisvarūpam | maivaṃ | śrutārthāpattikalpitena vākyena svarūpasiddhisaṃbhavāt | ataḥ śuddhyaśuddhī ubhe api śāstrāvaseye | tad uktam | “śuddhyaśuddhī hy adṛṣṭatvād vijñāyete yathāśruti” iti | tad evaṃ vasāśukram ityādibhir aśuddhivacanair aśuddhir gamyate | jalenaiva viśuddhyatītyādibhiś ca śuddhir avasīyate | ato ’ṣṭakādivad vidhimūle eva śuddhyaśuddhī | etāvāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ | aṣṭakādayaḥ pradhānavidhimūlāḥ śuddhyaśuddhī saṃskāravidhimūle iti | śuddhivacanaiś cāśuddhir avagamyate | śuddhir hi mūtrādyapaghātenāśuddhānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ saṃskārarūpā kartavyatayā vidhīyate, na punar evam eva prayujyamānānām | na hi suvarṇādayo bhāvāḥ svabhāvenāśuddhāḥ | yena prayogakāle śuddhim apekṣeran | athādṛṣṭārtho dṛṣṭaprayogāśrayaḥ saṃskāro vidhīyate prāṅmukhateva bhojane | tan na | tathā sati hi śuddhivacanaṃ virudhyeta | ye tu pātrāṇāṃ bhojanārambhe saṃmārjanaprakṣālane te samācārato na punar asyāḥ śuddhismṛteḥ | ataḥ siddhaṃ śuddhyaśuddhyoḥ śāstragamyatvam iti |
iti śuddhyaśuddhyoḥ śāstrīyatvanirūpaṇam |
athādhyātmaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
tatrośanāḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ śaucādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmas tatra dvividhaṃ śaucaṃ bāhyam ābhyantaraṃ ca | tatrābhyantaraśaucaṃ nāma yad buddhyahaṅkāraninditebhyo mano nivartayet | bāhyam api gandhalepasnehopaghāte ’dbhir aniṣṭarūpasparśagandhanāśe prokṣaṇābhyukṣaṇāc chucir ity aśucipratiṣedhaḥ |
[791]        punar uśanāḥ |
tatra hṛdayaśaucam anutaptānāṃ sādhutā, karmaśaucam agarhitakarmaṇyatā, śarīraśaucaṃ mṛdbhir adbhiś ca, vācām apriyānṛtanaiṣṭhūryavarjanaṃ, manaso mithyāsaṃkalpapratiṣedhaḥ, buddher jñānam |
baudhāyanaḥ |
athātaḥ śaucādhiṣṭhānam |
adbhiḥ śudhyanti gātrāṇi buddhir jñānena śuddhyati |
ahiṃsayā ca bhūtātmā manaḥ satyena śuddhyati ||
manaḥśuddhir antaḥśaucaṃ, bahir vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |
punar baudhāyanaḥ |
śrūyate dvividhaṃ śaucaṃ yac chiṣṭaiḥ paryupāsitam |
bāhyaṃ nirlepanirgandham antaḥ śaucam ahiṃsanam ||
punar baudhāyanaḥ |
kālo 'gnir manasaḥ śuddhir udakāny anulepanam |
avijñānaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ śaucam ucyate ||
deśaṃ kālaṃ tathātmānaṃ dravyaṃ dravyaprayojanam |
upapattim avasthāṃ ca parijñāya yathāvidhi ||
śaucajñaḥ kuśalo dharme dharmepsuḥ śaucam ācaret |
śaucam ity anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
pañcavidham ābhyantaraṃ bhavati mānasaṃ cākṣuṣaṃ ghrāṇaṃ
vācyaṃ svādyaṃ ceti |
punar hārītaḥ |
anilānalasomasūryāṃśukāñcanarajatabhasmatoyatāmraiḥ |
[792]        ajamukhabālagomayarajaādyaiḥ sparśaguṇair iha medhyatāṃ nayati ||
kulaśrutaśīlaśaucaśuddho dhanajanavastrabhājanaiś ca vidvān |
atamorajāḥ sattvabhāvaparamam acalaṃ padam āpnuvīteti ||
śuddhir ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
ātmayājināṃ śarīrasyādbhir mṛdbhiś ca, jñānena buddheḥ, tapasā pāpānāṃ santānajānāṃ mātṛpitṛkāṇāṃ doṣāṇāṃ yamaniyamamantravratopavāsādibhiḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
kālo 'gnir manasas tuṣṭir udakāny avalekhanam |
avijñānaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ ṣaḍvidhā śuddhir iṣyate ||
manu-viṣṇū |
jñānaṃ tapo ’gnir āhāro mṛnmayo vāry upāñjanam |
vāyuḥ karmārkakālau ca śuddhikartṝṇi dehinām ||
sarveṣām eva śaucānām arthaśaucaṃ paraṃ smṛtam |
yo ’rthe śucir hi sa śucir na mṛdvāriśuciḥ śuciḥ ||
kṣānyā śudhyanti vidvāṃso dānenākāryakāriṇaḥ |
pracchannapāpā jāpyena tapasā vedavittamāḥ ||
śodhyaṃ śuddhyati mṛttoyair nadī vegena śuddhyati |
rajasā strī manoduṣṭā saṃnyāsena dvijottamāḥ |
adbhir gātrāṇi śudhyanti manaḥ satyena śuddhyati ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
kālo ’gniḥ karma mṛd vāyur mano jñānaṃ tapo jalam |
[793]        paścāttāpo nirāhāraḥ sarve ’mī śuddhihetavaḥ ||
akāryakāriṇāṃ dānaṃ vego nadyāś ca śuddhikṛt |
śodhyasya mṛc ca toyaṃ ca saṃnyāso vai dvijanmanām ||
tapo vedavidāṃ kṣāntir viduṣāṃ varṣmaṇo jalam |
japaḥ pracchannapāpānāṃ manasaḥ satyam ucyate ||
bhūtātmanas tapovidye buddher jñānaṃ viśodhanam |
kṣetrajñasyeśvarajñānād viśuddhiḥ paramā matā ||
yamaḥ |
satyaṃ śaucaṃ tapaḥ śaucaṃ śaucamindriyasaṃyamaḥ |
sarvabhūtadayā śaucaṃ śaucam adbhis tu pañcamam ||
tathā |
kālāgnimanasā śuddhir udakena tathā mṛdā |
avijñānaṃ tu bhūtānāṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ śuddhilakṣaṇam ||
tathā |
gātrāṇy adbhir viśudhyanti nadī vegena śudhyati |
rajasā śudhyate nārī saṃnyāsena dvijottamaḥ ||
tathā |
gātrāṇy adbhir viśudhyanti manaḥ satyena vāg dhiyā |
bhūtātmā tapasā buddhir jñānena kṣamayā budhaḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gātraṃ mṛdambhaṣā śudhyec cittaṃ bhūtātiśaṃsanāt |
vidyayā tapasā dehī matir jñānena śudhyati ||
[794]        svādhyāyenānutāpena homena tapasaiva ca |
dhyānena kṣetravāsena dāneneha parikṣayaḥ ||
tathā |
parāpavādāśravaṇaṃ parastrīṇām adarśanam |
etac chaucaṃ śrotradṛśor jihvāśuddhir apaiśunam ||
aprāṇivadham asteyaṃ śucitvaṃ pādahastayoḥ |
asaṃśleṣaḥ parastrīṇāṃ śārīraṃ śaucam iṣyate ||
tathā |
sabāhyābhyantaraṃ śaucam etad uktam aśeṣataḥ |
sarvasyāsyādhikaṃ proktam arthaśaucaṃ svayaṃbhuvā ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ucchiṣṭopeto ’bhyukṣaṇāc chucir viṇmūtraṃ saṃspṛśyonmṛjyācamya prayato bhavati triḥ prakṣālya ca taṃ pradeśam |
viṣṇuḥ |
prāṇinām api sarveṣāṃ mṛdbhir adbhis tu kārayet |
atyantopahatānāṃ ca śaucaṃ nānyad atandrinaḥ ||
manu-viṣṇū |
viṇmūtrotsargaśuddhyarthaṃ mṛdvāry ādeyam arthavat |
daihikānāṃ malānāṃ ca śuddhiṣu dvādaśasv api ||
vasā śukram asṛṅ majjā mūtraviṭkarṇaviṭnakhāḥ |
śleṣmāśrudūṣikā ksvedo dvādaśaite nṛṇāṃ malāḥ || ||
[795]        devalaḥ |
tathā śarīre śrotrebhyo malaviṣyandavisravaḥ |
annādīnāṃ pradeśaś ca syād aśucir viśeṣataḥ ||
patitāśucyamedhyānāṃ sparśanāc cāśucir bhavet |
svapnād vastraviparyāsāt kṣutāt buddhipariśramāt ||
uktāvācamanaṃ śuktam anṛtaṃ krūram eva ca |
īpsāviddhaṃ dhanurlakṣyaṃ dṛṣṭvā vāpy aśucir bhavet ||
pralepasnehagandhānām aśucau cāpakarṣaṇam |
śaucalakṣaṇam ity āhur mṛdambhogomayādibhiḥ ||
lepe snehe ca gandhe ca vyapakṛṣṭe ca dūrataḥ |
paścād ācamanaṃ cāpi śaucārthaṃ vakṣyate vidhiḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
atra śārīraṃ duṣṭābhiśastapatitatiryaggandhavarṇopahatānāṃ saṃsparśaḥ | sparśāc cāsya svedatoyapūyaśoṇitacchardilālāniṣṭhīvitareṇukardamocchiṣṭajalaviṇmūtrapurīṣādibhir bāhyaśarīropaghātādibhir upapūtābhir adbhir mṛdbhiś ca bhasmagomayauṣadhimantra- maṅgalācāraviddhaiḥ prayuktair bāhyaśarīropaghātāt pūto bhavati |
viṣṇuḥ |
nābher adhastāt prabāhuṣu ca kāyikair malaiḥ surābhir madyair vopahatau mṛttoyais tad aṅgaṃ prakṣālyātandritaḥ śudhyet | anyatropahatau mṛttoyais tad aṅgaṃ prakṣālya snānena cakṣuḥṣūpahatas tūpoṣya snātvā pañcagavyena daśanacchadopahataś ca |
aṅgirāḥ |
ūrddhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā yad aṅgam upahanyate |
tatra snānam adhastāt tu kṣālanenaiva śudhyati ||
indriye tu praviṣṭaṃ syād amedhyaṃ yadi karhicit |
mukhe ’pi saṃspṛśya malaṃ tatra snānaṃ viśodhanam ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
rathyākardamatoyena ṣṭhīvanādyena vā tathā |
nābher ūrddhvaṃ tataḥ spṛṣṭvā sadyaḥ snānena śudhyati ||
yamaḥ |
sakardame tu varṣāsu praviśya grāmasaṃkaram |
jaṅghābhyāṃ mṛttikās tisraḥ padbhyāṃ ca dviguṇāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
manuḥ |
divākīrtim udakyāṃ ca sūtikāṃ patitaṃ tathā |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyati ||
ācamya prayato nityaṃ japed āśucidarśane |
sauryān mantrān yathotsāhaṃ pāvamānīś ca śaktitaḥ |
nāraṃ spṛṣṭvāsthi sasnehaṃ snātvā vipro viśudhyati ||
ācamyaiva tu niḥsnehaṃ gāṃ spṛṣṭvā vīkṣya vā ravim |
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret |
acāmed eva bhuktvānnaṃ snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam ||
‘divākīrtiḥ’ caṇḍālaḥ, ‘tatspṛṣṭī' caṇḍālādispṛṣṭena yaḥ spṛṣṭaḥ | [707] snānaṃ cātra sacelam | 'vānto viriktaḥ' saṃjātavamanavirekaḥ | sa yadi bhojanād anyadivase tarhi snātvā ghṛtaprāśanaṃ, bhojanadivase ced ācamanamātraṃ kuryāt | maithunānantaramṛtau garbhaśaṅkāyāṃ snānam,         ṛtau tu garbhaṃ śaṅkitvā snānaṃ maithuninaḥ smṛtam |
anṛtau tu yadā gacchec chaucaṃ mūtrapurīṣavat ||
iti śātātapasmaraṇāt |
tathā |
ajīrṇe ’bhyudite vānte śmaśrukarmaṇi maithune |
duḥsvapne durjanasparśe snānamātraṃ vidhīyate ||
śunā caiva śvapākena mṛtanirhārakeṇa vā |
spṛṣṭamātras tu kurvīta sacelaṃ plāvanaṃ jale ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
duḥsvapradarśane caiva vānte vā kṣurakarmaṇi |
maithune kaṭadhūme ca sadyaḥ snānaṃ vidhīyate ||
udakyāpatitāntyānāṃ sparśe snānaṃ vidhīyate |
vānto viriktaḥ snātvā tu ghṛtaprāśanam ācaret ||
tathā |
patitaṃ sūtikām antyaṃ śavaṃ spṛṣṭvā tu kāmataḥ |
snātvā sacelaṃ spṛṣṭvāgniṃ ghṛtaṃ prāśya viśudhyati ||
śavaspṛśaṃ divākīrtiṃ citiṃ yūpaṃ rajasvalām |
spṛṣṭvā pramādato vipraḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā viśudhyati ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
udakyāśucibhiḥ snāyāt saṃspṛṣṭas tair upaspṛśet |
[798]        abliṅgāni japec caiva gāyatrīṃ manasā sakṛt ||
upaspṛśir atra snānārthaḥ, udakyādispṛṣṭaḥ snāyāt | ‘taiḥ’ udakyādispṛṣṭaiḥ, saṃspṛṣṭo ’pi snāyād iti vacanārthaḥ |
aṅgirāḥ |
śavaspṛśaṃ tathodakyāṃ sūtikāṃ patitaṃ tathā |
spṛṣṭvā snānena śudhyanti sacelena na saṃśayaḥ ||
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ vyaṅgam unmattaṃ śavam antyajam |
sūtakiṃ sūtikāṃ nārīṃ rajasā ca pariplutām ||
śvakukkuṭavarāhāṃś ca grāmyān saṃspṛśya mānavaḥ |
sacelaṃ saśirāḥ snātvā tadānīm eva śudhyati ||
devalaḥ |
śvapākaṃ patitaṃ vyaṅgam unmattaṃ śavahārakam |
aśuddhān svayam apy etān aśuddhaś ca yadi spṛśet ||
viśudhyaty upavāsena punaḥ kṛcchreṇa vā punaḥ |
upaspṛśyāśucispṛṣṭaṃ tṛtīyaṃ vāpi mānavaḥ ||
hastau pādau ca toyena prakṣālyācamya śudhyati |
sākṣāc caṇḍālādinā yaḥ spṛṣṭaḥ sa prathamaḥ | caṇḍālādispṛṣṭena spṛṣṭo dvitīyaḥ tenāpi spṛṣṭas tṛtīyaḥ | eteṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ snānena śuddhiḥ | vakṣyamāṇagautamavacano 'syārthasya vyaktam eva pratīyamānatvāt | tṛtīyaṃ yaḥ [799] spṛśati tasya pāṇipādaprakṣālanapūrvakam ācamanam eva | yāni tu tṛtīyasyācamanavidhāyīni vacanāni tāny aśaktaviṣayāṇīti veditavyam |
tathā |
mānuṣāsthi vasāṃ viṣṭhām ārtavaṃ mūtraretasī |
majjānaṃ śoṇitaṃ cāpi parasya yadi saṃspṛśet ||
snātvā pramṛjya lepādīn ācamya sa śucir bhavet |
tāny eva svāni saṃspṛśya pūtaḥ syāt parimārjanāt ||
chāgaleyaḥ |
caṇḍālaṃ patitaṃ spṛṣṭvā sacelo jalam āviśet |
śavaṃ tatspṛṣṭinaṃ caiva citiṃ yūpaṃ rajasvalām ||
parāśara-baudhāyanau |
caityavṛkṣaś citir yūpaś caṇḍālaḥ somavikrayī |
etāṃs tu brāhmaṇaḥ spṛṣṭvā sacelo jalam āviśet ||
duḥsvapnaṃ yadi paśyet tu vānte ca kṣurakarmaṇi |
maithune kaṭadhūme ca snānam ity abhidhīyate ||
śvakākair upalīḍhasya nakhair vilikhitasya ca |
adbhiḥ pracālanaṃ śaucam agninā copacūlanam ||
hārītaḥ |
śvapacamūṣṭikapretahārakaśavāni saṃspṛśya devīr āpa ity etābhir antarjale snātaḥ pūto bhavaty ajīrṇavāntaśmaśrukarmāyonipaśudivāmaithunagamanena ca |
āha gautamaḥ | [800]
patitacaṇḍālasūtikodakyāśavaspṛṣṭitatspṛṣṭyupasparśanaṃ tadbāndhavaiḥ sahāśrupātaṃ kṛtvā snānenākṛte ’sthisaṃcaye sacelasnānena |
viṣṇuḥ |
citādhūmāvasecane sarve varṇāḥ snānam ācareyuḥ | maithune duḥsvapne vamanavirekayoś ca śmaśrukarmaṇi kṛte ca śavaṃ spṛṣṭvā rajasvalāṃ śvacaṇḍālayūpāṃś ca bhakṣyavarjaṃ pañcanakhaśavaṃ tadasthi sasnehaṃ ca sarveṣv eteṣu sthāneṣu pūrvavastraṃ nāprakṣālitaṃ bibhṛyāt |
atha brahmacaryānuvṛttau paiṭhīnasiḥ |
skandane chardane sacelasnānam anudakaghṛtaprāśanaṃ ca | kākakeśamalasparśane sacelaṃ snānam anudakamūtrapurīṣakaraṇe sacelasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca asneham asthi saṃspṛśya acailasnānam | sasneham asthi saṃspṛśya sacelasnānaṃ mahāvyāhṛtihomaś ca kharoṣṭracaṇḍālasparśane sacelanānam |
kaśyapaḥ |
atha sāṃsparśikaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | caṇḍālaśmaśānamṛtahārarajasvalāsūtikāsaṃsparśane uditāstamite skanditvākṣispandane karṇakrośane citārohaṇe yūpasaṃsparśane caiva prāyaścittaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedya sacelaṃ snātvā punar manojapāt mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ saptājyāhutīr juhuyāt tataḥ śuddhir bhavati |
yavanaḥ | śvānaṃ śvapākaṃ pretadhūmaṃ devadravyopajīvinaṃ grāmayājakaṃ yūpaṃ citiṃ citikāṣṭhaṃ samadyabhāṇḍaṃ sasnehaṃ mānuṣāsthi śavaspṛṣṭaṃ rajasvalāṃ mahāpātakinaṃ śavaṃ spṛṣṭvā sacelavāsā ambho ’vagāhya uttīryāgnim upaspṛśet | gāyatryaṣṭaśataṃ japet | ghṛtaṃ prāśya punaḥ snātvā trir ācamet |
śātātapaḥ | [801]
śunāghrātāvalīḍhasya nakhair vilikhitasya vā |
adbhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ proktam agninā copacūlakam ||
āpastamba-baudhāyanau |
śunopahataḥ sacelo ’vagāhet | prakṣyālya ca taṃ deśam agninā saṃspṛśya punaḥ prakṣālya pādau cācamya prayato bhavati |
vṛddhaśātātapaḥ |
aśuciṃ saṃspṛśed yas tu eka eva sa duṣyati |
taṃ spṛṣṭvānyo na duṣyet tu sarvadravyeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
yasya sparśe ācamanamātraṃ vihitaṃ so ’trāśuciśabdenocyata iti bhāṣyakāraḥ | yady apy atrāśucispṛṣṭasparśinaḥ sarvathā nirdoṣatvam ucyate tathāpi samācārād ācamanaṃ vidheyam |
saṃvartaḥ |
śvavarāhakharān uṣṭrān vṛkagomāyuvānarān |
kākakukkuṭagṛdhraṃ ca spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret ||
vyāsaḥ |
bhāsavānaramārjarakharoṣṭrānāṃ śunāṃ tathā |
sūkarāṇām amedhyaṃ vai spṛṣṭvā snāyāt sacelakam ||
hārītaḥ |
ṣaṇḍakaṃ kukkuṭaṃ kākaṃ śvaśṛgālaśivāvṛkān |
citiyūpaśmaśānāni viḍvarāhakharāśucīn |
avakīrṇinam antyaṃ ca spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ vidhīyate ||
mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe |
[802]        abhojyasūtikāṣaṇḍamājārākhuśvakukkuṭān
patitāpaviddhacāṇḍālamṛtahārāṃś ca dharmavit ||
saṃspṛśya śudhyate snānād udakyām antyasūkarau |
tadvac ca sūtikāśaucaṃ dūṣitai puruṣāv api ||
ṣaṭtriṃśanmate ’pi |
buddhān pāśupatān jainān lokāyatikakāpilān |
vikarmasthān dvijān spṛṣṭvā sacelo jalam āviśet |
kapilāṃ gāṃ tu saṃspṛśya prāṇāyāmo ’dhiko mataḥ ||
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
śaucān (?) pāśupatān spṛṣṭvā lokāyatikanāstikān |
vikarmasthān dvijān śūdrān savāsā jalam āviśet ||
saṃvartaḥ |
śūdrocchiṣṭaṃ dvijaḥ spṛṣṭvā ucchiṣṭaṃ śūdram eva vā |
śucim apy avagāhyainaṃ sacelaṃ snānam ācaret ||
chāgaleyaḥ |
spṛṣṭvā devalakaṃ caiva savāsā jalam āviśet |
viṣṇuḥ |
nābher adhaḥ karāgraṃ vā śunā yady upahanyate |
prakṣyālya tad avakṣālya punar ācamya śudhyati ||
nābher ūrddhvṃa śunā spṛṣṭo lipto 'medhyena vā punaḥ |
pramṛjya mṛdbhir aṅgāni sacelaṃ snānam arhati ||
[803]        śātātapaḥ |
rajakaś carmakaś caiva vyādhajālopajīvinau |
nirṇejakaḥ śauṇḍikaś ca naṭaḥ śailūṣakas tathā ||
mukhebhagas tathā śvā ca vanitā sarvavarṇagā |
cakrī dhvajī vadhyaghātī grāmyakukkuṭasūkarau ||
ebhir yad aṅgaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ syāc chirovarjaṃ dvijātiṣu |
toyena kṣālanaṃ kṛtvā ācāntāḥ prayatā matāḥ ||
jātūkarṇyaḥ |
ūrddhvaṃ nābheḥ karau muktvā yad aṅgaṃ spṛśate khagaḥ |
snānaṃ tatra prakurvīta śeṣaṃ prakṣālya śudhyati ||
kūrmapurāṇe
ucchiṣṭo ’dbhir anācāntaś cāṇḍālaṃ spṛśate dvijaḥ |
pramādād vai japet snātvā gāyatryaṣṭasahasrakam ||
cāṇḍālapatitādīṃs tu kāmād yaḥ saṃspṛśed dvijaḥ |
ucchiṣṭas tatra kurvīta prājāpatyaṃ viśuddhaye ||
viṣṇuḥ |
ucchiṣṭena tu saṃspṛṣṭe snānaṃ yena vidhīyate |
tenaivocchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭe prājāpatyena śudhyati ||
saṃvartaḥ |
nīlīnīlivikārāṃś ca mānuṣāsthīny api dvijaḥ |
caṇḍālapatitacchāyāṃ spṛṣṭvā snānaṃ samācaret ||
skandapurāṇe |
[804]        spṛṣṭvā rudrasya nirmālyaṃ savāsā āplutaḥ śuciḥ |
śāṭyāyaniḥ |
aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ divā parvaṇi maithunam |
kṛtvā sacelaṃ snātvā (?) tu vāruṇībhiś ca mārjayet ||
aṅgirāḥ |
jñānaṃ rajasvalāyās tu caturthe ’hani nirdiśet |
kuryād rajasi nirvṛtte śaucārthaṃ tu tataḥ punaḥ |
rajasy uparate sādhvī jñānena strī rajasvalā ||
rajonivṛttyanantaraṃ punaḥ jñānena sādhvī devādikarmayogyety arthaḥ | yat punas tasyaiva vacanam,
śuddhā bhartuś caturthe ’hni snātā nārī rajasvalā |
daive karmaṇi pitrye ca pañcame ’hani śudhyati ||
tad rajonivṛttikālopalakṣaṇārtham |
yad āha śaṅkhaḥ |
sādhvācārā na tāvat syāt snātāpi strī rajasvalā |
yāvat pravartamānaṃ hi rajo naiva nivartate ||
'sādhvācārā' karmayogyā | sparśayogyā tu snānād anantaraṃ bhavaty eva | tad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
rajasvalā caturthe ’hni snānāc chuddhim avāpnuyāt |
yadā tu rogeṇa rajā niḥsarati tadā tu na tenāśuddhir ity āha śaṅkhaḥ |
rogeṇa yad rajaḥ strīṇām atyarthaṃ tu pravartate |
aśucyo naiva tās tena yena vaikārikaṃ hi tat ||
[805]        evam akāle ’pi rajodarśane draṣṭavyam |
tathā ca chāgaleyaḥ |
akāle yad bhavet strīṇāṃ raktam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
kāle tu yad rajaḥ proktaṃ tasmāt tatraiva sā śuciḥ ||
ity adhyātmaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
atha taijasādidravyaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
tatrāha manuḥ |
eṣa śaucasya vaḥ proktaḥ śārīrasya vinirṇayaḥ |
nānāvidhānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śuddheḥ śṛṇuta nirṇayam ||
taijasānāṃ maṇīnāṃ ca sarvasyāśmamayasya ca |
bhasmanādbhir mṛdā caiva śuddhir uktā manīṣibhiḥ ||
nirlepaṃ kāñcanaṃ bhāṇḍam adbhir eva viśudhyati |
abjam aśmamayaṃ caiva rājataṃ cānupaskṛtam |
agneś cāpāṃ ca saṃyogād dhemarūpyaṃ ca saṃbabhau ||
tasmāt tayoḥ svayonyaiva nirṇeko guṇavattaraḥ |
tāmrāyaḥkāṃsyaraupyāṇāṃ trapuṇaḥ sīsakasya ca |
śaucaṃ yathārhaṃ kartavyaṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ ||
dravāṇāṃ (?) caiva sarveṣāṃ śuddhir utpavanaṃ smṛtam |
prokṣaṇaṃ saṃhatānāṃ ca dārvāṇāṃ caiva takṣaṇam ||
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi |
camasānāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu ||
carūṇāṃ sruksruvāṇāṃ ca śuddhir uṣṇena vāriṇā |
[806]        sphyaśūrpaśakaṭānāṃ ca muśalolūkhalasya ca ||
adbhis tu prokṣaṇaṃ śaucaṃ bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsayām |
prākṣālanena tv alpānām adbhir eva vidhīyate ||
celavac carmaṇāṃ śuddhir vaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca |
śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca dhānyavac chuddhir iṣyate ||
kauśeyāvikayor ūṣaiḥ kutapānām ariṣṭakaiḥ |
śrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ ||
kṣaumavac chaṅkhaśṛṅgāṇāṃ asthidantamayasya ca |
śuddhir vijānatā kāryā gomūtreṇodakena vā ||
prokṣaṇāt tṛṇakāṣṭhānāṃ palālaṃ ca viśudhyati |
mārjanopāñjanair veśma punaḥpākena mṛnmayam ||
yamaḥ |
suvarṇasyātha rūpyasya yajñeṣu camasasya ca |
yatīnāṃ caiva pātrāṇāṃ adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
tathā |
rajatasya suvarṇasya tāmrasya trapuṇas tathā |
rītyayaḥkāṃsyasīsānāṃ bhasmanā śaucam ucyate ||
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ śaṅkhānām upalasya ca |
abjānāṃ caiva sarveṣām adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
nadī śudhyati vegena dāravaṃ takṣaṇena ca |
phalamātrasya sarvasya vālarajjvā pragharṣaṇam ||
abhyukṣya tṛṇakāṣṭhānāṃ vaiṇave gomayena ca |
mṛnmayānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ punardāho viśodhanam ||
dravyāṇāṃ saṃhatānāṃ ca śuddhir adbhir mṛdā tathā |
somapānāṃ (?) tu yat pātram adbhis tacchuddhim āpnuyāt ||
dhānyānāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhir adbhiḥ prakṣālanena vā |
celavac carmaṇāṃ śuddhir vaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
śākamūlaphalānāṃ ca puṣpāṇām āsanasya ca |
ūrṇākārpāsikānāṃ ca śuddhir adbhir mṛdā saha |
dukūlapaṭṭakauśeyāḥ śudhyanty adbhiḥ sadaiva tu ||
tathā |
kutapānām ariṣṭais tu śrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭayoḥ |
kṛṣṇājinānāṃ bilvaiś ca bālānāṃ mṛdbhir ambhasā ||
gomūtreṇāsthidantānāṃ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ |
pāṃśukaṃ parivartena mṛttikābhiś ca chādanam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
ambhasā hemarūpyāyaḥkāṃsyaṃ śuddhyati bhasmanā |
amlāmbhasā tāmraraityaṃ punardāhena mṛnmayam ||
śākamūlaphalānāṃ tu śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu |
dārubhūmyos takṣaṇena prokṣaṇena tṛṇodake ||
vastravaidalacarmādeḥ śuddhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ smṛtam |
atidṛṣṭvāsya tanmātratyāgaś chitvānyaśuddhaye |
abhyukṣaṇena śuddhiḥ syāt bahūnāṃ dhānyavāsavām ||
[808]        prakṣālayed yajñapātrāṇy ambhasoṣṇena yatnataḥ |
gṛhasya mārjanaṃ lepaḥ śuddhiḥ plāvo dravasya tu |
śākamūlaphalānāṃ tu śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
sauvarṇarājanābjānām ūrddhapātragrahāśmanām |
śākarajjumūlaphalavāsovidalacarmaṇām ||
pātrāṇāṃ camasānāṃ ca vāriṇā śuddhir iṣyate |
carusruksruvasasnehapātrāṇy uṣṇena vāriṇā ||
sphyaśūrpājinadhānyānāṃ musalolūkhalānām |
takṣaṇaṃ dāruśṛṅgāsthnāṃ govālaiḥ phalasaṃbhuvām ||
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi |
sauṣair udakagomūtraiḥ śuddhyaty āvikakauśikam ||
saśrīphalair aṃśupaṭṭaṃ sāriṣṭaiḥ kutapaṃ tathā |
sagaurasarṣapaiḥ kṣaumaṃ punaḥpākān mahīmayam ||
tathā |
trapusīsakatāmrāṇāṃ kṣārāmlodakavāribhiḥ |
bhasmādbhiḥ kāṃsyalohānāṃ śuddhiḥ plāvo dravasya tu ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
amlodakena tāmrasya sīsasya trapuṇas tathā |
kṣāreṇa śuddhiḥ kāṃsyasya lohasya ca vinirdiśet ||
muktāphalapravālānāṃ śuddhiḥ prakṣālanena tu |
abjānāṃ caiva bhāṇḍānāṃ sarvasyāśmamayasya ca ||
[809]        śākarajjumūlaphalavaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
mārjanaṃ yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇinā yajñakarmaṇi |
uṣṇāmbhasā tathā śuddhiṃ sasnehānāṃ vinirdiśet ||
śayanāsanayānānāṃ sphyaśūrpakaṭakasya ca |
śuddhiḥ saṃprokṣaṇāt jñeyā palālendhanayos tathā ||
sasarjikṣāratoyena vāsasāṃ śuddhir iṣyate |
bahūnāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhir dhānyānāṃ ca vinirdiśet ||
śuddhir dravāṇāṃ vijñeyā nityam utpavanena tu |
prokṣaṇāt saṃhatānāṃ ca dāravāṇāṃ ca takṣaṇāt ||
siddharthakānāṃ (?) kalkena śṛṅgadantamayasya ca |
govālaiḥ phalapātrāṇām asthnāṃ syāc chṛṅgavattayā ||
niryāsānāṃ guḍānāṃ ca lavaṇānāṃ tathaiva ca |
kusumbhakuṅkumānāṃ ca ūrṇākārpāsayos tathā |
prokṣaṇāt kathitā śuddhir ity āha bhagavān yamaḥ ||
tathā |
mṛnmayaṃ bhājanaṃ sarvaṃ punaḥpākena śudhyati |
devalaḥ |
hemasphāṭikaraupyāṇāṃ yajñeṣu camasasya ca |
yatīnāṃ caiva pātrāṇām adbhiḥ śaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
tathā |
tāntavaṃ malinaṃ pūrvam adbhiḥ kṣāraiś ca śodhayet |
aṃśubhiḥ śoṣayitvā vā vāyunā vā samāharet ||
[810]        ūrṇāpaṭṭāṃśukakṣaumadukūlāvikacarmaṇām |
alpāśauce bhavec chuddhiḥ karmabhiḥ śoṣaṇādibhiḥ ||
tāny evāmedhyayuktāni niṣicyāt kṣārasarṣapaiḥ |
dhānyāriṣṭakakalkaiś ca rasaiś ca phalavalkalaiḥ ||
tūlikām upadhānāni puṣparaktāmbarāṇi ca |
śoṣayitvātape kiṃcit karair unmārjayen muhuḥ ||
paścāc ca vāriṇā prokṣya ’śucīny evam udāharet |
tāny apy atimaliṣṭhāni yathāvat pariśodhayet ||
lohānāṃ dahanāc chuddhiḥ bhasmanā gomayena ca |
dahanāt śvasanād vāpi śailānām ambhasāpi vā ||
kāṣṭhānāṃ takṣaṇāc chuddhir mṛdgomayajalair api |
mṛnmayānāṃ tu pātrāṇāṃ dahanāc chuddhir iṣyate ||
sarvadravāṇāṃ pakṣāṇāṃ lavaṇasya guḍasya ca |
nānyac chaucaṃ parityāgād aśuddhānām iti smṛtiḥ ||
toyābhāve parasparśe bhūmisaṃveśane ’pi ca |
kuṇḍikāyāḥ parityāgo dahanaṃ vāpadi smṛtam ||
dravyāṇām avaśiṣṭānāṃ toyāgnibhyāṃ viśodhanam |
śodhanārthaṃ hi sarveṣām ubhayaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ smṛtam ||
śvapāko yat spṛśed dravyaṃ mṛnmayaṃ dravam eva vā |
pakvaṃ vā bhojyakalkaṃ vā tat sarvaṃ parivarjayet ||
ucchiṣṭāśucibhiḥ spṛṣṭam adravaṃ śodhyate ’mbhasā |
ūnaṃ vāpi prabhūtaṃ vā śodhayet prokṣaṇādibhiḥ ||
[811]        viṣṇuḥ |
sauvarṇarājatābjamaṇimayānāṃ nirlepānām adbhiḥ śuddhir aśmamayānāṃ ca camasānāṃ grahāṇāṃ ca, carusruksruvāṇām uṣṇenāmbhasā yajñakarmaṇi | yajñapātrāṇāṃ pāṇisaṃmārjanena sphyaśūrpaśakaṭamūśalolūkhalānāṃ prokṣaṇena ca | śayanayānāsanānāṃ ca bahūnāṃ dhānyājinarajjutāntavavaidalamūtrakārpāsavāsasāṃ ca śākamūlaphalapuṣpāṇāṃ ca tṛṇakāṣṭhapalānāṃ ca eteṣāṃ prakṣālanenālpānām ūṣaiḥ, kauśeyāvikayor ariṣṭakaiḥ, kutapānāṃ śrīphalaiḥ, aṃśupaṭṭānāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ, kṣaumāṇāṃ śṛṅgāsthidantamayānāṃ ca padmākṣaiḥ, mṛgalomikānāṃ tāmrarītitrapusīsamayānām amlodakena, bhasmanā kāṃsyalohayoḥ, takṣaṇena dāravāṇāṃ, govālaiḥ phalasaṃbhavānāṃ, prokṣaṇena saṃhatānāṃ, utpavanena dravāṇāṃ guḍādīnām ikṣuvikārāṇāṃ, prabhūtānāṃ gṛhanihitānāṃ mṛdvāryagnidānena, sarvalavaṇānāṃ ca punaḥpākena, mṛnmayānāṃ dravyavat kṛtaśaucānāṃ devārthānāṃ bhūyaḥ pratiṣṭhāpanena, asiddhasyānnasya yanmātram upahataṃ tanmātraṃ parityajya śeṣasya kaṇḍanaprakṣālane kuryāt |
hārītaḥ |
athārthaśaucam adbhiḥ kāñcanarājatānām | tattadguṇavarṇasnehavaivarṇyopahatānāṃ yavagodhūmakalāyamāsagomayacūrṇair mārjanaṃ śodhanam adbhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ syād amlalavaṇābhyāṃ tāmrāṇāṃ, bhasmanā kāṃsyānāṃ, śailatailagharṣaṇaiḥ kāṣṇāyasānāṃ, sikatāvaghātanagharṣaṇaiḥ śailānāṃ, śailāvagharṣaṇamārjanair maṇimayānāṃ, nirlekhanair dārumayānāṃ, punaḥpākān mārttikānāṃ | gomūtragomayabilvair vaidalānāṃ, gobālarajjvā sodakayā phalapātrāṇām | [812] mārjanaṃ kamaṇḍalūnāṃ yatipātrāṇāṃ ca | kṣāroṣābhyāṃ kārpāsaśaṇamayānāṃ, putrajīvakāriṣṭakaiḥ kṣaumadukūlānāṃ, putrajīvakodasvidbhiḥ cīrāṇāṃ, śrīphalaiḥ śvetasarṣapaiḥ kauśeyānāṃ, mudasvidvalmīkamṛdā sarṣapair ūṛṇānāṃ, snehasaktukulmāṣodvartanaiḥ gurūṇāṃ |
gautamaḥ |
dravyaśuddhiḥ parimārjanapradāhatakṣaṇanirṇejanāni taijasamārttikadāravatāntavānāṃ, taijasavad upalamaṇiśaṅkhaśuktīnāṃ, dāruvad asthibhūmyoḥ, āvapanaṃ ca bhūmeḥ, cailavad rajjuvidalacarmaṇāṃ | utsargo vā | taijasamārttikadāravatāntavānāṃ bhasmaparimārjanapradāhatakṣaṇadhāvanāni, taijasavad upalamaṇīnāṃ | maṇivac chaṅkhaśuktīnām | dāruvad asthnāṃ | rajjuvidalacarmaṇāṃ cailavac chaucaṃ | govālaiḥ phalamayānāṃ | gaurasarṣapakalkena kṣaumānāṃ | tathā adbhir eva kevalaṃ kāñcanaṃ pūyate tathā rājatam |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
ata ūrddhvaṃ śaucavidhiḥ | kāruhastaḥ śuciḥ | tathā karasya dravyāṇi ca prokṣitāni śucīni  ākarajātānāṃ tv abhyavaharaṇīyānāṃ ghṛtenābhidhāritānāṃ śuddhiḥ, plavanena snehānāṃ, saṃhatānāṃ mṛdbhir adbhiḥ, dravāṇām utpavanaṃ, śuṣkāṇām uddhṛtadoṣāṇāṃ saṃskāraḥ | pariplāvitānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ tatas tyāgaḥ | cailānāṃ mṛdbhasmagomūtrakṣārodakaiḥ | cailavad dhānyānāṃ pakṣacāmaracarmatṛṇabālavetra- valkalajānāṃ ca | suvarṇarajatānāṃ mṛdbhir adbhiḥ, bhasmanā taijasānām abhyavaharaṇīyānāṃ, punardāho mṛnmayānāṃ, parilekhanaṃ vaiṇvānāṃ, takṣaṇaṃ dāravāṇāṃ, dāruvad asthidantaśṛṅgāṇāṃ, taijasavad upalamaṇiśaṅkhaśuktīnām |
[813]        baudhāyanaḥ |
taijasānāṃ pātrāṇām asakṛn mṛdbhasmabhiḥ parimārjanam anyatamena vā tāmrarajatasuvarṇānām amlaiḥ | amatrāṇāṃ dahanam | vaiṇvānāṃ gomayena | phalamayānāṃ govālarajjvā | kṛṣṇājinānāṃ bilvataṇḍulaiḥ | kutapānām ariṣṭhaiḥ | ūrṇavānām ādityena | kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapakalkena | mṛdā cailānām | cailavac carmaṇām | taijasavad upalamaṇīnām | dāruvad asthnām | kṣaumavac chaṅkhaśṛṅgaśuktidantānāṃ payasā vā cakṣurghrāṇānukūlyād vā |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
camasānāṃ cocchiṣṭagandhahiraṇyarajatatāmrāṇām alepānām adbhiḥ, salepānāṃ gomayena | bhinnam abhinnam iti na doṣaḥ | kārṣṇāyasānāṃ sikatābhiḥ | dāhena mṛnmayānām | adbhiḥ śailānām | trapusīsakānāṃ gomayatuṣaiḥ | kāṃsyaṃ bhasmanā | kāṣṭhamayaṃ takṣaṇena | kṣīrodakābhyāṃ śaṅkhaśuktī | vastrāṇāṃ nirṇejanena | cailavad upalamaṇīnām | kṛṣṇājinānāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ | gavāvibastājinānāṃ bilvataṇḍulaiḥ | kutapānām ariṣṭhaiḥ |
uśanāḥ|
kāṣṭhānāṃ parilekhanam | phalamūlapuṣpabhūmitṛṇadārupalāladhānyānām abhyukṣaṇam | rajjuvaiṇavayajñacarmakārpasikakṣaumadukūlacīnāṃśukapaṭṭakauśeyorṇakānām adbhir eva prakṣālanam | suvarṇarajatatāmralohatrapusīsakānām adbhir eva bhasmasaṃyuktābhiḥ, maṇimayānām adbhir eva mṛtsaṃyuktābhiḥ, taijasānāṃ cocchiṣṭānāṃ bhasmanā triḥ prakṣālanam | phalamayānāṃ govālarajjvā | vaiṇavānāṃ gomayena | kṛṣṇājinānāṃ bilvataṇḍulaiḥ | kutapānām ariṣṭaiḥ | kauśeyānāṃ gaurasarṣapakalkena | mṛdbhiś cānyeṣāṃ vāsasām |
sumantuḥ |
maṇikanakarajataśaṅkhaśuktyupalapravālānāṃ pramārjanaṃ śuddhiḥ | vastravidalarajju-carmaṇām adbhiḥ prakṣālanam | bhūmyasthikāṣṭhānāṃ takṣaṇam | mṛtpātralohānām agninottāpanam | sīsatrapukāṃsyatāmrāyasakuddālānām udakabhasmabhyāṃ triḥpramārjanāc chuddhiḥ | govālarajjvā puṣpaphalānām | anyatra śakunocchiṣṭebhyas teṣām adoṣaḥ | śvadaṣṭānāṃ ca māṃsādīnāṃ sruksruvāṇām anyeṣāṃ ca bhāṇḍāṇāṃ uṣṇena vāriṇā śaucam | taijasānāṃ ca bhṛjatucarmacauraveṇuphalavalkalānām adbhiḥ prakṣālanam |
kaśyapaḥ |
atha pātrāṇāṃ śaucavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | parilekhanaṃ dārupātrāṇām | aupalānāṃ gharṣaṇam | mṛnmayānāṃ punardāhaḥ | rītīnāṃ parimārjanam | bhasmanā kāṃsyatrapusīsakatāmrāyasānām | sikatābhir dantaśṛṅgaśaṅkhaśuktimaṇīnām | vastrarājatasauvarṇānām adbhiḥ śaucam | tṛṇakāṣṭharajjubhūrjaśāṇakṣaumacīracarma - veṇuvedalapatravalkalānāṃ cailavac chaucam | atyantopahatānāṃ parityāgo vidhīyate |
hārītaḥ |
payasā dantānām | krītānām avahanananiṣyavanaiḥ | vrīhiyavagodhūmānāṃ gharṣaṇadalanapeṣaṇaiḥ | śividhānyānāṃ khaṇḍanavimṛśanaprakṣālanaiḥ | phalīkṛtānāṃ gharṣaṇaprakṣālanaparyanikaraṇaiḥ | śākamūlaphalānāṃ bhūsthagrahaṇaprakṣālanaiḥ ikṣukāṇḍānāṃ ca | vidhividhānāt yajñadravyahaviṣyasya | utpavanam eva snehānām | punaḥpākaḥ kṛtalavaṇānām | pukkasādibhiḥ spṛṣṭānāṃ bhūsthānāṃ tṛṇakāṣṭhānām ādityadarśanāc chaucam |
[815]        āpastambaḥ |
anāprīte mṛnmaye bhoktavyam āprītaṃ ced abhidagdhe parimṛṣṭaṃ lohaṃ prayataṃ vilikhitaṃ dārumayaṃ yathāgamaṃ yajñe tāpanīyam aśnīyāt |
tathā |
rasānām amāṃsamadhulavaṇānaīi parihāpya telasarpiṣī tūpayojayed udake ’vadhāya |
śātātapaḥ |
gavāghāteṣu kāṃsyeṣu śūdrocchiṣṭeṣu vā punaḥ |
daśabhir bhasmabhiḥ śuddhiḥ śvakākopahateṣu ca ||
śaṅkhalikhitau |
camasānām adbhir yajñasomaikaśaucam adhvareṣu camasavad yatipātrāṇāṃ yatisamayapātrāṇāṃ bhasmodakena parigharṣayet |
baudhāyanaḥ |
vacanāt yajñe camasapātrāṇāṃ na somenocchiṣṭā bhavantauti śrutiḥ |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
yatipātrāṇām adbhiḥ śaucam |
uśanāḥ |
yajñe camasapātrāṇāṃ neocchiṣṭaṃ na somenocchiṣṭaṃ bhavatīti śrutiḥ |
baudhāyanaḥ |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ gavāṃ panthās tṛṇāni ca |
mārutārkeṇa śudhyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca ||
ātmaśayyāsanaṃ vastraṃ jāyāpatyaṃ kamaṇḍaluḥ |
[816]        śucīny ātmana etāni pareṣām śucīni tu ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
svabhāryāśiśuvastraṃ svam upavītaṃ kamaṇḍaluḥ |
ātmanaḥ kathitaṃ śuddhaṃ na tu śuddhaṃ parasya tu ||
duṣṭābhiśastapatitatiryaggandhavarṇopahatānīty anuvṛttau hārītaḥ |
pānaśayanāsanāni parihāryaṇy eke manyante | tatra varṇaviśeṣāc chuṣkamalinasaṃsargadarśanāt pādopasargād vyādhisaṃkramaṇayogāc ca | tasmāt pṛthak śaucāḥ śreyāṃsa iti | svānupapattau śucyantarvāsaḥ samāmananti, sacailaṃ saṃsparśe snānam evaṃ hy āha | āsanaṃ śayanaṃ pānam antardhāya samācaret |
baudhāyanaḥ |
asaṃskṛtānāṃ bhūmau nyastānāṃ tṛṇānāṃ prakṣālanaṃ narokṣāhṛtānām abhyukṣaṇam | evaṃ kṣudrasamidhāṃ mahatāṃ kāṣṭhānām upaghāte prakṣyālyāvaśoṣaṇam, dārumayānāṃ pātrāṇām ucchiṣṭasamatvārabdhānām ullekhanam ucchiṣṭalepopahatānām eva takṣaṇam | mūtrapurīṣalohitaretaḥprabhṛtibhir utsargaḥ mṛnmayānāṃ pātrāṇām ucchiṣṭasamanvārabdhānām avajvalana (?) ucchiṣṭalepopahatānāṃ punar dahanam | mūtrapurīṣalohitaretaḥprabhṛtibhir utsargaḥ | tad etad anyatra nirdeśāt yathaitad agnihotre ca dharmocchiṣṭe ca dadhidharme ca kuṇḍapāyinām ayane cotsargiṇām ayane ca dākṣāyaṇyajñe ceḍādau ca cakṣuścakre ca bṛhaccakre ca brahmodaneṣu ca teṣu sarveṣu darbhair adbhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ sarveṣu ca somabhakṣyeṣv adbhir eva mārjāraloḍhe prakṣālanaṃ mūtrapurīṣalohitaretaḥprabhṛtibhir ūtsargaḥ | taijasānāṃ pātrāṇām ucchiṣṭopahatānāṃ tu triḥ aptakṛtvaḥ [817]  parimārjanaṃ parimārjanadravyāṇi gośakṛnmṛdbhasmāni | mūtrapurīṣalohitaretaḥprabhṛtyupahatānāṃ punaḥkaraṇaṃ gomūtre vā saptarātraṃ pariśāyanaṃ | mahānadyāṃ caivam aśmamayānām alābubilvavaidalānāṃ govālaiḥ parimārjanam | nalaveṇuśarakuśacyutānāṃ gomayenādbhir iti prakṣālanam | vrīhīṇām upaghāte prakṣālyāvaśoṣaṇam | taṇḍulānām utsarga eva |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
kāṣṭhaṃ tṛṇaṃ cāṇḍālādispṛṣṭam ādityadarśanāt pūtaṃ bhavati |
āpastambaḥ |
gūḍhaḥ prastare vāsaḥ spṛśann anyān aprayatān aprayato manyeta tayos tṛṇakāṣṭheṣu nikhāteṣu prākṣya vāsa upayojayet |
baudhāyanaḥ |
parihitam adhirūḍhaprakṣālitaprāvaraṇaṃ nāpallulitaṃ manuṣyasaṃyutaṃ
devatrāyuñjate |
aṅgirāḥ |
śaucaṃ sahasraromāṇāṃ vāyvagnyarkenduraśmibhiḥ |
retaḥspṛṣṭaṃ śavaspṛṣṭam āvikaṃ naiva śudhyati ||
śayanāsanayānāni romabaddhāni yāni tu |
vastrāṇy etāni sarvāṇi saṃhatāni pracakṣate ||
cailānām ity anuvṛttau śaṅkhalikhitau |
sarveṣām āpo mṛdariṣṭakeṅgudabilvataṇḍulasarṣapakalkakṣāragomūtra-
gomayādīni śaucadravyāṇi saṃhatānāṃ prokṣaṇam ity eke |
[818]        manuḥ |
yāvan nāpaity amedhyākto gandho lepaś ca tatkṛtaḥ |
tāvan mṛdvāri deyaṃ syāt sarvāsu dravyaśuddhiṣu ||
gautamaḥ |
lepagandhāpakarṣaṇe śaucam amedhyaliptasyādbhir mṛdā ca |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
amedhyāktasya mṛttoyaiḥ śuddhir gandhāpakarṣaṇāt |
vākśastam ambunirṇikram ajñātaṃ ca sadā śuciḥ ||
manu-vasiṣṭha-baudhāyanāḥ |
trīṇi devāḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇānām akalpayat |
adṛṣṭam adbhir nirṇitaṃ yac ca vācā praśasyate ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
śucir ity aśucir iti pratiṣedho vāgduṣṭāni varjayet vākpraśastāni ca kurvīta |
hārītaḥ |
upaghātavaśaṃ śaucaṃ pratighātalakṣaṇaṃ gandhalepāpakarṣaṇaṃ dravyasya bhāvaśuddhiḥ śaucaṃ bāhyārtheṣu |
vṛddhaśātātapa-hārītau |
śodhitānāṃ tu pātrāṇāṃ yady ekam aśucir bhavet |
iti taijasādidravyaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
athātyantopahatadravyaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
tatra viṣṇuḥ |
śārīrair malaiḥ surābhir madyair yad upahataṃ tad atyantopahatam | atyantopahataṃ sarvaṃ lohabhāṇḍam agnau prakṣiptaṃ śudhyeta | maṇimayam aśmamayam abjaṃ ca saptarātraṃ mahīnikhananena | śṛṅgadantāsthimayaṃ saṃtakṣaṇena | dāravaṃ ca mṛnmayaṃ ca jahyāt | atyantopahatasya prakṣālitasya trir avadya tat chindyāt |
hārītaḥ |
atyantopahatānāṃ sarvataijasānām agninā śaucam | mṛnmayacarmaṇāṃ tyāgaḥ | tanmātracchedanam ity eke vāsasām | navavāsamāṃ dhāraṇaṃ hi śaucam ity ācāryāḥ | tasmāt sarvavāsasām āplavanād eva śuddhiḥ |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
taijasānāṃ kuṇaparetomūtrapurīṣopahatānām āvarttanam ullekhanaṃ bhasmanā triḥsaptakṛtvaḥ parimārjanam ucchedanaṃ tanmātracchedanaṃ vā |
baudhāyanaḥ |
mūtrapurīṣāsṛakkuṇapaspṛṣṭānāṃ pātrāṇāṃ pūrvoktānām anyatamena triḥsaptakṛtvaḥ parimārjanam ataijasānām evambhūtānām utsargaḥ |
punar baudhāyanaḥ |
kṣaumāni vāsāṃsi teṣām alābhe kārpāsikāny aurṇikāni bhavanti | mūtrapurauṣalohita-retaḥprabhṛtyupahatānāṃ mṛdārdbhir iti prakṣālanaṃ, vāsovat tārṇavalkānāṃ valkalavat kṛṣṇājinānām |
śaṅkha-vasiṣṭha-kaśyapāḥ |
madyair mūtrapurīṣaiś ca lseśmapūyāśruśoṇitaiḥ |
saṃspṛṣṭaṃ naiva śudhyeta punaḥpākena mṛnmayam ||
etair eva tathā spṛṣṭaṃ tāmrasauvarṇarājatam |
[820]        śudhyaty āvarttitaṃ paścād anyathā kevalāmbhasā ||
yamaḥ |
alābudārupātrāṇāṃ vaidalānāṃ tathaiva ca |
atyantopahatānāṃ ca parityāgo vidhīyate ||
tathā |
atra mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ retasā rudhireṇa vā |
cailaṃ yad upahanyeta punaḥ prakṣālayet tu tat ||
yady ambhasā na śudhyet tu vastraṃ copahataṃ dṛḍham |
chedanaṃ tasya dāho vā yanmātram upahanyate |
mṛttikākṣārapātena aśuddhaṃ tat parityajet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
vastravaidalacarmādye śuddhiḥ prakṣālanaṃ smṛtaṃ |
atiduṣṭasya tanmātraṃ tyajec chittvātmāśuddhaye ||
uśanāḥ |
mūtrapurīṣaretoraktopahatānām atyantotsargaḥ
devalaḥ |
dūṣitaṃ varjitaṃ duṣṭaṃ kaśmalaṃ ceti liṅginām |
caturvidham amedhyaṃ ca sarvaṃ vyākhyāsyate punaḥ ||
śucy apy aśucisaṃspṛṣṭhaṃ dravyaṃ dūṣitam ucyate |
abhakṣyabhojyapeyāni varjitāni hi vakṣyati ||
nyaṅkuḥ patitacaṇḍālau grāmyasūkarakukkuṭau |
śvā ca nityaṃ visarjyāḥ syuḥ ṣaḍ ete dharmataḥ samāḥ ||
[821]        savraṇaḥ sūtakī sūtī mattonmattarajasvalāḥ |
mṛtabandhur aśuddhaś ca varjyany aṣṭau svakālataḥ ||
svedāśrubindavaḥ pheṇo nirastaṃ nakharoma ca |
ārdracarmāsṛg ity etat duṣṭam āhur dvijātayaḥ ||
mānuṣāsthi kapho viṣṭhā reto mūtrārttavāni ca |
kuṇapaṃ pūyam ity etat kaśmalaṃ cety udāhṛtam ||
dūṣitaprokṣaṇenāpi śuddhis tūktā vidhānataḥ |
duṣṭair mārjanasaṃskāraiḥ kaśmalaiḥ sarvathā bhavet ||
iti atyantopahate dravye śuddhyupāyāḥ |
atha bhūśuddhyupāyāḥ |
tatrāha manuḥ |
saṃmārjanenāñjanena sekenollekhanena ca |
gavāṃ ca parivāsena bhūmiḥ śudhyati pañcadhā |
yamaḥ |
khananāt pūraṇād dāhāl lepanād abhigharṣaṇāt ||
gobhir ākramaṇāt kālād bhūmiḥ śudhyati saptabhiḥ ||
devalaḥ |
pañcadhā vā caturddhā vā bhūr amedhyā viśudhyati |
duṣṭā dvidhā tridhā vāpi śodhyate malinaikadhā ||
dahanaṃ khananaṃ bhūmer upalepanavāpane |
parjanyamārjanaṃ ceti śaucaṃ pañcavidhaṃ smṛtam ||
[822]        prasūte garbhiṇī yatra mriyate yatra mānuṣaḥ |
caṇḍālair uṣitaṃ yatra yatropanyasyate śavam ||
viṇmūtre vahatī yatra kuṇapaṃ yatra dṛśyate |
evaṃ kalmaṣabhūyiṣṭhā bhūr amedhyeti lakṣyate ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgādyair dūṣitā yatra medinī |
drapsaiḥ prakarṣocchiṣṭair vā vāntair vā duṣṭatāṃ vrajet ||
nakhadantatanūjatvaktuṣapāṃsurajomalaiḥ |
bhasmapaṅkatṛṇair vāpi pracchannā malinā bhavet ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
ghanāyā bhūmer upaghāte upalepanaṃ suṣirāyāḥ karṣaṇāṃ, ācchinnāyā medhyam āhṛtya pracchādanaṃ, caturbhiḥ śodhyate bhūmiḥ gobhir ākramaṇāt khananād dahanād abhigharṣaṇāt pañcamāc copalepanāt ṣaṣṭhāt kālāt |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
bhūmis tu saṃmārjanaprokṣaṇopalepanollekhanair yathāsthānaṃ doṣaviśeṣāt prāyatyam upaiti | athāpy udāharanti |
khananād dahanād varṣād gobhir ākramaṇād api |
caturbhiḥ śodhyate bhūmiḥ pañcamād upalepanāṃ || iti |
śuddhyanuvṛttau śaṅkha-likhitau |
karṣaṇam upalepanam adbhir abhyukṣaṇaṃ kālo dāhaś ca bhūmeḥ |
hārītaḥ |
khananāt pūraṇād dāhāl lepanād abhigharṣaṇāt |
gobhir ākramaṇāt kālāt bhūmir etair viśudhyati ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
[823]        bhūśuddhir mārjanād dāhāt kālād gokramaṇāt tathā |
sekād ullekhanāl lepāt gṛhaṃ mārjanalepanāt ||
viṣṇuḥ |
mārjanopāñjanair veśma prokṣaṇena ca pustakam |
mārjanenāñjanenāpi sekenollekhanena ca ||
dāhena ca bhuvaḥ śuddhir vāsenāpy atha vā gavām |
gāvaḥ pavitraṃ maṅgalyaṃ goṣu lokaḥ pratiṣṭhitaḥ ||
gāvo vitanvate yajñaṃ gāvaḥ sarvāghasūdanāḥ |
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ sarpir dadhi ca rocanā ||
ṣaḍaṅgam etat paramaṃ maṅgalyaṃ sarvadā gavām |
śṛṅgodakaṃ gavāṃ puṇyaṃ sarvāghaviniṣūdanam ||
manu-baudhāyanau |
bhūmes tu saṃmārjanaprokṣaṇopalepanopastaraṇair yathāsthānaṃ doṣaviśeṣāt prāyatyam | yathātrāpy udāharanti |
gocarmamātram abbindur goḥ śodhayati pātitaḥ |
samūḍham asamūḍhaṃ vā yatra lepo na dṛśyate ||
gautamaḥ |
dāruvad asthibhūmyor āvapanaṃ ca bhūmeḥ
śaṅkhaḥ|
mārjanād veśmanāṃ śuddhiḥ kṣitau śodhanatakṣaṇam |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
gṛhasya mārjanaṃ lepaḥ śuddhiḥ plāvo dravasya tu |
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇāvasathe bhūmir devāgāre tathaiva ca |
medhyāṃ caiva sadā manye gavāṃ goṣṭhe ca yā bhavet ||
parāśaraḥ |
rathyākardamatoyāni nāvaḥ panthās tṛṇāni ca |
sparśanān na praduṣyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca || i
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu udyānopavaneṣu ca |
triḥsaptamārjanaṃ kṛtvā evaṃ śaucaṃ vidhīyate ||
yājñavalkya-viṣṇū |
rathyākardamatoyāni spṛṣṭāny antyaśvavāyasaiḥ |
mārutārkeṇa śudhyanti pakveṣṭakacitāni ca ||
tathā |
panthānaś ca viśudhyanti sūryāṃśumārutaiḥ |
śātātapaḥ |
valmīkād utkarānāl lepāj jalāc ca pathivṛkṣayoḥ |
kṛtaśaucāvasiṣṭāś ca na grāhyāḥ saptamṛttikāḥ |
śucau deśe ca saṃgrāhyāḥ sakaṇṭāśmādivarjitāḥ ||
iti bhūśuddhyupāyāḥ |
atha pakvānnaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
tatra śātātapaḥ |
[825]        keśakīṭakṛtāghrātaṃ vāsasopahataṃ ca yat |
klībābhiśastapatitaiḥ sūtikodakyanāstikaiḥ ||
dṛṣṭaṃ vā syād yad annaṃ tu tasya niṣkṛtir ucyate |
abhyukṣya kiṃcid uddhṛtya tad bhuñjītāviśaṅkitaḥ ||
bhasmanā vāpi saṃspṛśya saṃspṛśed ulmukena vā |
suvarṇarajatābhyāṃ ca bhojyaṃ ghrātam ajena vā ||
ghṛtaṃ sapāyasaṃ kṣīraṃ tathaivekṣuraso guḍaḥ |
śūdrabhāṇḍasthitaṃ takraṃ tathā madhu na duṣyati ||
keśādīnāṃ vāsontānāṃ sūtikodakyayoś ca dvandvaikavadbhāvaḥ | ‘udakyā' rajasvalā | ‘niṣkṛtiḥ' śuddhiḥ | yāvan makṣikādisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ tat saṃnihitena kiyatāpi sahoddhṛtya vakṣyamāṇamantraiḥ salilenābhyukṣya tato bhasmanolmukena vā saṃspṛśya tataḥ suvarṇena rajatena vā saṃspṛśet, ajena vā ghrāpayet | 'pāyasāḥ' payovikārāḥ dadhyādayaḥ |
atrābhojyam ity anuvṛttau vaśiṣṭhaḥ |
yad rasanakeśakīṭopahataṃ ca kāmaṃ tu keśakīṭān uddhṛtyādbhiḥ prokṣya bhasmanāvakīrya vācā praśastam upabhuñjīta, nirdoṣam idaṃ pūtam idaṃ idānīṃ savṛttam iti vācā praśaṃsanīyam |
baudhāyanaḥ |
tvakkeśakīṭaromanakhāny ākhupurīṣāṇi dṛṣṭvā taddeśapiṇḍānnam uddhṛtyādbhir abhyukṣya bhasmanāvakīryābhighārya punar api prokṣya vācā praśastaṃ oṃ bhūr bhuvaḥ svar ity upaṣṭāt dhyātvā punar evam upabhuñjīta |
[826]        'ākhupurīṣaṃ' mūṣakasya śakṛt | taddeśasthitaṃ grāsamātram annam uddhṛtyāvātādibhiḥ saṃskuryāt |
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
keśakīṭagavāghrātam annaṃ hiraṇyena bhasmanā spṛṣṭaṃ prokṣitaṃ ca |
upabhuñjīteti śeṣaḥ |
yamaḥ |
śuktāni hi dvijo ’nnāni na bhuñjīta kadācana |
prakṣālayitvā nirdoṣāṇyāpaddharmo yadā bhavet ||
masūramāṣasaṃyuktaṃ tathā paryuṣitaṃ ca yat |
tat tu prakṣālitaṃ kṛtvā bhuñjīta hy abhighātitam ||
'māṣāḥ' rājamāṣāḥ |
tathā |
makṣikākeśam anneṣu patitaṃ yadi dṛśyate |
mūṣakasya pūrīṣaṃ vākṣutaṃ yac cāvadhūnitam |
bhasmanā spṛśya vāśnīyād abhyukṣya salilena vā ||
kṣutenākrāntam ‘ākṣutaṃ' | 'avadhūnitaṃ' vastreṇeti śeṣaḥ |
tathā |
avakṣutaṃ keśapataṅgakīṭair udakyayā vā patitaiś ca dṛṣṭam |
alātabhasmāmbuhiraṇyachāgaiḥ saṃspṛṣṭam annaṃ manur āha bhojyam ||
tathā |
vākyaśastāni bhuñjīta vāgduṣṭāni vivarjayet |
śucīni annapānāni vākśastāni na saṃśayaḥ ||
[827]        tathā |
makṣikā maśakā daṃśā ghuṇāḥ sūkṣmāḥ pipīlikāḥ |
āmiṣāmedhyasevī ca naite kīṭā vipattayaḥ ||
manu-viṣṇū |
pakṣijagdhaṃ gavā ghrātam avadhūtam avakṣutam |
dūṣitaṃ keśakīṭaiś ca mṛtprakṣepeṇa śudhyati ||
yājñavalkāḥ |
goghrāte ’nne tathā keśamakṣikākīṭadūṣite |
salilaṃ bhasma mṛd vāpi prakṣeptavyaṃ viśuddhaye ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
goghrāte ’vakṣute cānne makṣikākeśadūṣite |
mṛd bhasma salilaṃ tatra prakṣeptavyaṃ viśuddhaye ||
sumantuḥ |
keśakīṭakṣutaniṣṭhīvitavaco’bhihataṃ śvabhir āghrātaṃ prekṣitaṃ vā dadhi paryuṣitaṃ punaḥ siddhaṃ caṇḍālāvekṣitam abhojyam annam anyatra hiraṇyodakaspṛṣṭād iti |
uśanāḥ |
ajāghrāṇenāghrātānāṃ snehaghṛtaguḍamadhulavaṇānāṃ pākena |
śaṅkhalikhitau |
ākarajānāṃ tv abhyavaharaṇīyānāṃ ghṛtenābhidhāritānāṃ śuddhiḥ punaḥ pacanam eva, snehānāṃ snehavad rasānāṃ saṃhatānām adbhiḥ, dravāṇām utpavanaṃ, śuṣkāṇām uddhṛtadoṣāṇāṃ saṃskāraḥ, pariplāvitānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ tatas tyāgaḥ |
āpastambaḥ |
[828]
parokṣam annaṃ saṃskṛtam agnāv adhiśrityādbhiḥ prokṣet tad annaṃ pavitram ity ācakṣate |
hārītaḥ |
aniṣṭagandhopaghrāṇaśravaṇadarśane keśakīṭapipīlikābhir annādyopaghāte kāñcanarajatavaidūryapravālagovālājinadarbhāṇām ekatamenādbhiḥ saṃspṛṣṭena mantraprokṣaṇaṃ paryagnikaraṇam ādityadarśanāt pūtaṃ bhavati | bahvannopaghāte brāhmaṇamatāt tadantar apanīya ajam avaghrāpya pūtaṃ bhavati | tyāgo rasamayānām |
baudhāyanaḥ |
siddhahaviṣāṃ mahatāṃ śvavāyasaprabhṛtyupahatānāṃ taddeśe piṇḍam uddhṛtya pavamānaḥ suvarjana ity etenānuvākenābhyukṣaṇam, madhūdake payovikāre ca pātrāntaranayane śuddhiḥ | evaṃ tailasarpiṣī | ucchiṣṭasamanvārabdhe tūdake ’vadhāyopayojayet |
‘mahatāṃ' bhūyasām | ‘taddeśe' upaghātadeśe | 'pavamānaḥ suvarjana" ityādir jātavedāmūrjayantyā punātv ityanto ’nuvākas taittirīyāṇāṃ prasiddhaḥ | mādhyandinādibhis tu “punantu mā pitaraḥ somyāsa" ityādibhiḥ pavanaprakāśakair yajurbhir abhyukṣaṇaṃ vidheyam | 'ucchiṣṭasamanvārabdhe' ucchiṣṭasaṃspṛṣṭe, 'avadhāya' pātrāntare nidhāya |
laugākṣiḥ |
payodadhivikārādi śuci pātrāntarasthitam |
pāvanotpavanābhyāṃ ca paryagniprakaraṇena ca ||
'pāvanaṃ' vastrādigālanam | 'utpavanaṃ' darbhapavitreṇa "savitus tvā prasava utpunāmi" ity anena mantreṇa yājñikaprasiddhaprakāreṇa saṃskaraṇam |
[829]        yamaḥ |
āmamāṃsaṃ ghṛtaṃ kṣaudraṃ snehāś ca phalasaṃbhavāḥ |
mlecchabhāṇḍasthitā dūṣyā niṣkrāntāḥ śucayaḥ smṛtāḥ ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
ghṛtadadhipayastakrāṇām ākarabhāṇḍasthitānām adoṣaḥ |
tathā|
ādhāradoṣe tu nayet pātrāt pātrāntaraṃ dravam |
ghṛtaṃ tu pāyasaṃ kṣīraṃ tathaivekṣuraso guḍaḥ |
śūdrabhāṇḍasthitaṃ takraṃ tathā madhu na duṣyati ||
tathā |
śrapaṇaṃ ghṛtatailānāṃ plāvanaṃ gorasasya ca |
bhāṇḍāni plāvayed adbhiḥ śākamūlaphalāni ca ||
siddham annaṃ tathā sarpiḥ kṣīraṃ ca dadhi cāmbu ca |
eteṣām avalīḍhānāṃ tejasā śuddhir iṣyate ||
kākamārjāranakulasarpamūṣakapakṣibhiḥ
saṃskṛtaṃ tu yadā hy annam avalihyeta kenacit ||
suvarṇavarṇatāmrolkair govālaiḥ rajatena ca |
spṛṣṭam ekatareṇāpi bhojyaṃ ghrātam ajena vā ||
'śrapaṇena' pākena, ghṛtatailayoḥ śuddhiḥ | 'plāvanena' bhāṇḍasya bahijpradeśe salilapūraṇena, gorasānāṃ śuddhiḥ kāryā | 'tejasā śuddhiḥ' paryagnikaraṇam | suvarṇavarṇatvaṃ tāmrasya viśeṣaṇam | iyaṃ ca śuddhiḥ kṛmikeśādikaṃ dūṣakam uddhṛtya kāryā |
brahmāṇḍapurāṇe |
[830]        dravadravyāṇi bhūrīṇi pariplāvyāni cāmbhasā |
sasyāni vrīhiyavaś caiva śākamūlaphalāni ca |
tyaktvā tu dūṣitaṃ bhāgaṃ plāvyāny atha jalena vā ||
viṣṇuḥ |
droṇād abhyadhikaṃ siddham annam upahataṃ na duṣyati | tasyopahatamātram apāsya gāyatryābhimantritaṃ suvarṇāmbhaḥ kṣipet | vastasya ca darśayed agneś ca |
ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaṃ palaśatadvayaṃ 'droṇaḥ' | idaṃ ca droṇādhikaparimāṇakathanaṃ kākaśunakaviḍvarāhāvalehanādimahopaghāteṣv anusaṃdheyam | keśakīṭasvalpopaghāte tu svalpaparimāṇasyāpi yathoktā śuddhir vidheyā |
parāśaraḥ |
śṛtaṃ droṇādhikasyānnaṃ śvakākair upaghātitam |
na tyājyaṃ tasya śuddhyarthaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo nivedayet ||
kartavyaṃ vandanaṃ teṣām annasaṃskārakarmaṇi |
vedavedāṅgavidviprā dharmaśāstrabahuśrutāḥ ||
uccārya dharmaśāstraṃ tu ṛṣer vaktrād viniḥsṛtam |
munivaktracyutān dharmān gāyanto dharmapāṭhakāḥ ||
ebhir ya ukto dharmo vai sevyaḥ syān netaras tathā |
droṇādhikasya śuddhis tu dharmaśāstreṣu paṭyate ||
śvakākair avalīḍhaṃ tu droṇānnaṃ na vivarjayet |
grāsam uddhṛtya tanmātraṃ yac ca lālāśritaṃ bhavet ||
suvarṇodakenābhyukṣya jvalanena tu tāpayet |
hutāśajvālayā spṛṣṭaṃ suvarṇasalilena ca |
[831]        viprāṇaṃ mantraghoṣeṇa pūtaṃ bhojyaṃ ca tad bhavet ||
droṇān nyūnaparimāṇasya tv annasya śunakādyavalehamahopaghātadūṣitasya tyāga eva kartavya iti sa evāha |
śvavāyasagavādyais tu jagdham annaṃ yadā bhavet |
sneho vā goraso vāpi tatra śuddhiḥ katham bhavet ||
annaṃ parityajet tatra snehasyotpavanena tu |
aśvatthalatayā śuddhir gorasasya vidhīyate ||
devalo ’pi |
avalīḍhaṃ śvamārjāradhvāṃkṣakukkuṭamūṣakaiḥ |
bhojane nopayuñjīta tad amedhyaṃ hi sarvataḥ ||
caṇḍālaspṛṣṭasya droṇādhikasyāpi tyāga eva kartavya ity āha sa (?)eva |
cāṇḍālena śunā vāpi dṛṣṭam annam ayajñiyam |
cāṇḍālādibhir ucchiṣṭaṃ spṛṣṭaṃ bhūyo ’pi varjayet ||
yamaḥ |
devadroṇayāṃ vivāheṣu yajñeṣu prasaveṣu ca |
kākaiḥ śvabhiś ca yat spṛṣṭaṃ tad annaṃ na vivarjayet ||
tanmātram annam uddhṛtya śeṣaṃ saṃskāram arhati |
ghanānāṃ prokṣaṇāc chuddhir dravāṇām api tāpanāt |
saṃsparśanād bhavec chuddhir apām agner dhṛtasya ca ||
chāgena mukhasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śucitvena vinirdiśet ||
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
[832]        devadroṇyāṃ vivāheṣu yajñeṣu prakṛteṣu ca |
kākaiḥ śvabhiś ca saṃspṛṣṭam annaṃ tatra vivarjayet ||
tanmātram annam uddhṛtya śeṣaṃ saṃskāram arhati |
dravāṇāṃ plāvanenaiva ghṛtānāṃ prokṣaṇena ca ||
chāgena mukhasaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śucitvena hi tad bhavet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
tīrthe vivāha yātrāyāṃ saṃgrāme deśaviplave |
nagare grāmadāhe ca spṛṣṭāspṛṣṭaṃ na duṣyati ||
gokule kanduśālāyāṃ talacakrekṣuyantrayoḥ |
amīmāṃsyāni śaucāni strībhir ācaritāni ca ||
śātātapaḥ |
gokule kanduśālāyāṃ tailacakrekṣuyantrayoḥ |
amīmāṃsyāni śaucāni strīṣu bālāntareṣu ca ||
hārītaḥ |
kandupakvāni tailāni pāyasaṃ dadhi saktavaḥ |
etāni śūdrānnabhujo bhojyāni manur abravīt ||
iyaṃ tu yathoktā śuddhir annāntarasaṃpādanāśaktirūpāyām āpady eva kartavyā | anāpadi duṣṭam annaṃ parityajyānnāntaram eva saṃpādanīyam |
yac ca nagnādīn abhidhāyoktaṃ vāyupurāṇe |
annaṃ paśyeyur ete tu yadi vai havyakavyayoḥ |
utsraṣṭavyaṃ pradhānārthaṃ saṃskāras tv āpadi smṛtaḥ ||
nagnādibhir dṛṣṭam annaṃ ‘utsraṣṭavyaṃ' parityājyam | 'pradhānārthaṃ' [833] agnaukaraṇabrāhmaṇabhojanapiṇḍapradānātmakapradhānasaṃpattyartham anyad annaṃ saṃpādyam | āpadi tu nagnādidṛṣṭam annaṃ notsraṣṭavyam | kiṃ tu tasyaiva pavitrīkaraṇaṃ kartavyam | tad uktaṃ brahmapurāṇe |
haviṣāṃ saṃskṛtānāṃ tu pūrvam evāpavarjanam |
mṛtsaṃpṛktābhir adbhiś ca prokṣaṇaṃ tu viśuddhaye ||
siddhārthakaiḥ kṛṣṇatilaiḥ kāryaṃ cāpy avakīraṇam |
dvijasūryagnivastānāṃ darśanaṃ ca prayatnataḥ ||
asyārthaḥ | 'haviṣāṃ' havyakavyānāṃ, pākena saṃskṛtānāṃ nagnādidarśane sati 'apavarjanam eva' parityāga eva, kartavya ity eṣaḥ mukhyaḥ kalpaḥ | āpadi tu doṣāpagamahetubhūtasaṃskāra- siddhaye praśastamṛttikāsaṃpṛktābhir adbhiḥ prokṣaṇaṃ vidheyam | tathā gaurasarṣapaiḥ kṛṣṇatilaiś cāvakīraṇaṃ duṣṭahavyakavyayor upari prakṣepaṇaṃ kāryam | tathā dvijasūryagnichāgānāṃ darśanaṃ kāryam | evaṃ yathā havyakavye śudhyatas tathā prayatitavyam iti | atha vā jamadagninoktām annaśuddhiṃ kuryāt |
śuddhavatyo ’tha kūṣmāṇḍāḥ pāvamānyas taratsamāḥ |
pūtena vāriṇā darbhair annadoṣān apānudet ||
śuddhavatyādimantrābhimantritam udakaṃ darbhair upādāya nagnādivīkṣaṇādiprabhavadoṣāpanodanārthaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | eto tv indraṃ stavām etyādyās tisra ṛcaḥ śuddhavatyaḥ | yad devā devahelanam ityādyās tisraḥ kūṣmāṇḍyo yajurvede prasiddhāḥ | yad anti yac ca dūraka ityādyāḥ pāvamānyaḥ | taratsamandī dhāvataīyādyās taratsamāḥ |
iti pakvānna dupāyāḥ |
athodakaśudbhutapāyāḥ |
tatrāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ucchiṣṭaṃ malinaṃ klinnaṃ yac ca viṣṭhānulepitam |
adbhiḥ śudhyati tat sarvam apāṃ śuddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet ||
sūryenduraśmipātena mārutasparśanena ca |
gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa śudhyanty āpa iti sthitiḥ ||
viṣṇuḥ |
mṛtapañcanakhāt kūpād atyantopahatāt tathā |
āpaḥ samuddharet sarvāḥ śeṣaṃ śāstreṇa śodhayet ||
vahiprajvālanaṃ kṛtvā kūpe pakveṣṭakācite |
pañcagavyaṃ nyaset paścān navatoyasamudbhave ||
jalāśayeṣv ayālpeṣu sthāvareṣu mahītale |
kūpavat kathitā śuddhir mahatsu ca na dūṣaṇam ||
parāśaraḥ |
vāpīkūpataḍāgebhya āpo grāhyās tu sarvaśaḥ |
paścāt paśyed amedhyaṃ tu pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
asthicarmāvasiktena śvamārjārais tu mūṣakaiḥ |
dūṣitaṃ tad bhavet toyaṃ tathādhastāt tu śodhanam ||
asthicarmāvapatitaṃ kharavānarasukaraiḥ |
uddhared udakaṃ sarvaṃ śodhanaṃ parimārjanam ||
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu dūṣiteṣu kathaṃcana |
uddhṛtya vai kumbhaśataṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
[835]        khanikūpataḍāgeṣu saṃdigdheṣu viśeṣataḥ |
toyaṃ hṛtvā ghaṭaśataṃ pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
pādukopānaviṇmūtraṃ kūpe yadi nimajjati |
ṣaṣṭhikumbhān samuddhṛtya pañcagavyena śudhyati ||
dūṣitaṃ ca malaiḥ klinnaṃ yac ca viṣṭhānulepitam |
adbhiḥ śudhyati tat sarvam apāṃ śuddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet ||
gavāṃ mūtrapurīṣeṇa somasūryāgnir aśmibhiḥ |
mārutasya tu vegena āpaḥ śuddhim avāpnuyuḥ ||
ity udakaśuddhyupāyāḥ |
athāśuddhyapavādāḥ |
tatra manu-viṣṇū |
nityam āsyaṃ śuci strīṇāṃ śakuniḥ phalapātane |
prasrave ca śucir vatsaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ ||
vaśiṣṭhātri śātātapa- baudhāyanāḥ |
vatsaḥ prasravane medhyaḥ śakuniḥ phalapātane |
striyaś ca ratisaṃsarge śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ ||
uśanāḥ |
rahasye strīmukhaṃ śuci | śucayaḥ phalapātane pakṣiṇaḥ | pratravane vatsaḥ |
mṛgagrahaṇe śvā medhyatīty anuvṛttau paiṭhīnasiḥ |
vatsaḥ prasravane, śvā mṛgagrahaṇe, śakuntāḥ phalapātane, strīmukhaṃ ratisaṃgame |
[836]        śaṅkhaḥ |
nārīṇāṃ caiva vatsānāṃ śakuntānāṃ śunāṃ mukham |
ratau prasravaṇe vṛkṣe mṛgayāyāṃ tathā śuci ||
ākarāḥ śucayaḥ sarve śakuniḥ phalapātane |
vatsaḥ prasravaṇe medhyaḥ śvā mṛgagrahaṇe śuciḥ ||
hārītaḥ |
vatsaḥ prastravaṇe medhyaḥ śvā mṛgagaṇe śuciḥ |
ākarāḥ śucayaḥ sarve śakuniḥ phalapātane ||
manu-viṣṇū |
śvabhir hatasya yan māṃsaṃ śuci tan manur abravīt |
kravyādbhiś ca hatasyānyaiś caṇḍālādyaiś ca dasyubhiḥ ||
śucītyanuvṛttau yājñavalkyaḥ |
tathā māṃsaṃ śvacaṇḍālakravyādādinipātitam |
vasiṣṭhaḥ |
śvahatāś ca mṛgāḥ dhanyāḥ pātitaṃ ca khagaiḥ phalam |
bālair anuparikrāntaṃ strībhir ācaritaṃ ca yat ||
prasāritaṃ ca yat paṇyaṃ ye doṣāḥ strīmukheṣu ca |
maśakair makṣikābhiś ca nilīnaṃ nopahanyate ||
yamaḥ |
yāḥ syur bhūmigatā hy āpo yac ca māṃsaṃ śvabhir hatam |
strīṇāṃ mukheṣu ye doṣā na doṣā manur abravīt ||
tathā |
bālakair yat parikrāntaṃ strībhir ācaritaṃ ca yat |
maśakair makṣikābhiś ca nilīnaṃ nopahanyate |
kṣitisthāś caiva yā āpo gavāṃ tṛptikarāś ca yāḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
raśmir agnir ajaṃ chāyā gaur aśvo vasudhānilaḥ |
vipruṣo makṣikāḥ sparśe vatsaḥ prasravane śuciḥ ||
tathā |
kāruhastaḥ śuciḥ paṇyaṃ bhaikṣyaṃ yoṣinmukhaṃ tathā |
yamaḥ |
ātmaśayyāsanaṃ vastraṃ medhryaṃ vā maladūṣitam |
brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣyaṃ nityaṃ medhyam iti sthitiḥ ||
tathā |
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ strīmukhaṃ kutapaṃ kṣuram |
na dūṣayanti vidvāṃso yajñeṣu camasaṃ tathā ||
gaur aśvo vipruṣaś chāyā makṣikāḥ śalabhāḥ śukāḥ |
ajo hastī raṇe śastraṃ raśmayaś candrasūryayoḥ ||
bhūmiragnaurajeā vāyurāpeo dadhi ghṛtaṃ pathaḥ |
sarvāṇy etāni śuddhāni sparśe medhyāni nityaśaḥ ||
tathā |
śucir agniḥ śucir vāyuḥ pavitrā ye bahiścarāḥ |
āpaś ca śucayo nityaṃ panthāḥ saṃcaraṇāc chuciḥ |
tathā |
[838]        āpaḥ śuddhā bhūmigatāḥ śucir nārī pativratā |
śucir dharmaparo rājā saṃtuṣṭo brāhmaṇaḥ śuciḥ ||
manu-baudhāyanau |
nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇyaṃ yac ca prasāritam |
brahmacārigataṃ bhaikṣyaṃ nityaśuddham iti sthitiḥ ||
manu-śātātapau |
makṣikā vipruṣaś chāyā gaur aśvaḥ sūryaraśmayaḥ |
rajā bhūr vāyur agniś ca sparśe medhyāni sarvadā ||
viṣṇuḥ |
nityaṃ śuddhaḥ kāruhastaḥ paṇyaṃ yac ca prasāritam |
brāhmaṇāntaritaṃ bhaikṣyam ākarāḥ sarva eva tu ||
makṣikā vipruṣaś chāyā gaur aśvaḥ sūryaraśmayaḥ |
rajo bhūr vayur agniś ca mārjāraś ca sadā śuciḥ |
sumantuḥ |
strībālamaśakamakśikāchāyā.āsanaśayanayānāmbuvipruṣo nityaṃ medhyāḥ | ghṛtakadvalāgārāṇām atyājyānām adoṣaḥ |
hārītaḥ |
makṣikā vipruṣo nāryo bhūmis toyaṃ hutāśanaḥ |
mārjāraś caiva darvī ca mārutaś ca sadā śuciḥ ||
śaṅkhaḥ |
śuddhaṃ nadīgataṃ toyaṃ sarva eva tathākarāḥ |
śuddhaṃ prasāritaṃ paṇyaṃ śuddham aśvājayor mukham |
[839]        mukhavarjaṃ ca gauḥ śuddhā mārjāraś caṅkrame śuciḥ ||
bṛhaspati-parāśarau |
aduṣṭā santatā dhārā vātoddhūtāś ca reṇavaḥ |
striyo vṛddhāś ca bālāś ca na duṣyanti kadācana ||
makṣikā vipruṣo nārī bhūmir āpo hutāśanaḥ |
mājāraś caiva darvī ca mārutaś ca sadā śuciḥ ||
śaṅkha-likhitau |
bālastrīparigatāny agāreṣu na duṣyanti, dhūmāgnirajāṃsi vāyvīritāni vipruṣaḥ chāyā makṣikā iti sparśe medhyāḥ |
uśanāḥ |
kāruhastaḥ śucir nityaṃ paṇyaṃ yac ca prasāritam |
ākarāḥ somapānaṃ ca vācā yac ca praśasyate ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
adūṣyāḥ santatā dhārā vātoddhūtāś ca reṇavaḥ |
ākarāḥ śucayaḥ sarve varjayitvā surākaram ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
ākarāḥ śucayaḥ sarve paṇyaṃ yac ca prasāritam |
rajasā strī manohṛṣṭyā nadī vegena śudhyati ||
punaḥ paiṭhīnasiḥ |
gaur ajo ’śvarajo vāyur makṣikā veti spardhe medhyāni |
śātātapaḥ |
reṇavaḥ śucayaḥ sarve vāyunā samudīritāḥ |
anyatra rāsabhājāviśvasamūhanivāsām ||
[840]        uśanāḥ |
gajāśvarathanāgānāṃ praśastā reṇavaḥ sadā |
apraśastāḥ samūhānyāḥ śvāvirāsabhavāsayām ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
pure reṇuguṇṭhitaśarīrasvapratipūrṇanetravadanaś ca nagare vasan niyatātmā siddhim avāpsyatīti naitad asti |
rathāśvagajadhānyānāṃ gavāṃ caiva rajaḥ śubham |
praśastaṃ samūhanyāḥ śvājāvikharavāsasām ||
śātātapaḥ |
apāṃ nocchiṣṭadoṣo ’sti madhunā na ca sarpiṣaḥ |
na phalānāṃ na cekṣūṇāṃ nākarāṇāṃ na yoṣitām ||
bālakair yat parikrāntaṃ strībhir ācaritaṃ ca yat |
maśakair makṣikābhiś ca nilīnaṃ nopahanyate ||
paiṭhīnasiḥ |
madhusarpiḥphalabālastrīṇām anucchiṣṭam |
devalaḥ |
śucipūtaṃ svayaṃ śuddhaṃ pavitraṃ ceti kevalam |
medhyaṃ caturvidhaṃ loke prajānāṃ manur abravīt ||
tathā |
navaṃ cānirmalaṃ vāpi śucīti dravyam ucyate |
śodhitaṃ tu yadānyena tat pūtam abhidhīyate ||
[841]        svayam eva hi yad dravyaṃ kevalaṃ dhanyatāṃ gatam |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ vāpi svayaṃ śuddham iti smṛtam ||
anyad dravyaira dūṣyaṃ yat svayam anyāni śodhayet |
havyakavyeṣu pūjyaṃ yat tat pavitram iti smṛtam ||
atha sarvāṇi dhānyāni dravyāṇy ābharaṇāni ca |
avarjyabhakṣyabhojyāni śucīny etāni kevalam ||
varjite nikhiladravye ’śucisaṃjñā pravartate |
tasminn eveha dharmajña śuddhatā na punar bhavet ||
tasmāc chuddhaṃ tu karmaṇyaṃ śucīty āhur dvijātayaḥ |
nirmalaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ dravyaṃ kriyārhaṃ pūtam ucyate ||
vasatiś camaso yānaṃ vāhanaṃ sādhanāni ca |
kṣuro naur āsanaṃ ceti svayaṃ śuddham iti smṛtam ||
śiśavaś ca svayaṃ śuddhā yoṣitaś cānṛtau tathā |
brahmahatyā hi nārīṇām ṛtukālena saṃspṛśet ||
ākarā hi svayaṃ śuddhā dravyāṇām iti nirṇayaḥ |
krītaṃ ca vyavahāribhyaḥ paṇyaṃ śuddham iti smṛtam ||
aduṣṭaṃ vākpraśastaṃ hi svayaṃ śuddhaṃ ca kevalam |
trīṇy etāni viśuddhāni bhagavān manur abravīt ||
ajāśvā mukhato medhyā gāvā medhyās tu pṛṣṭhataḥ |
taravaḥ puṣpitā medhyā brāhmaṇāś caiva sarvadā ||
[842]        bhasma kṣaudraṃ suvarṇaṃ ca sadarbhaḥ kutapās tilāḥ |
apāmārgaśirīṣārkāḥ padmam āmalakaṃ maṇiḥ ||
mālyāni sarṣapā dūrvāḥ sadā bhadrāḥ priyaṅgavaḥ |
akṣatā rocanā lājā haridrāś candanaṃ yavāḥ ||
palāśakhadirāśvatthatulasīdhātakīvaṭāḥ |
etāny āhuḥ pavitrāṇi brāhmaṇa havyakavyayoḥ ||
pauṣṭikāni malaghnāni śodhanāni ca dehinām |
tathāpo ’gniḥ śakṛn mṛc ca pavitrāṇi viśeṣataḥ |
sarvāśaucaviśuddhayarthaṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvataḥ sadā ||
tathā |
kaśmalaiḥ samiddho ’gnir durmanuṣyair adūṣitaḥ |
sarveṣām apy aśaucānāṃ samarthaḥ śodhanāya saḥ ||
dravyāṇām agnidagdhānāṃ medhyatvam upadiśyate |
jvālābhimṛṣṭapātrāṇāṃ śucitvaṃ niyataṃ bhavet ||
agner vṛṣalamuktasya grahaṇaṃ nāsty anāpadi |
śvapāko vṛṣalo bhoktṛbrāhmaṇo ’gnir anāhitaḥ ||
caṇḍālāgner amedhyāgneḥ sūtikāgneś ca karhicit |
patitāgneś citāgneś ca na śiṣṭair grahaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
gośakṛcchuddhadeśasthā śmaśānādyacitā śivā |
agrāmyā mṛd bhavec chuddhā ślakṣṇā viṇmūtravarjitā ||
manu-viṣṇū |
ūrdhvaṃ nābher yāni khāni tāni medhyāni sarvaśaḥ |
[843]        yāny adhastāny amedhyāni dehāc caiva malāś cyutāḥ ||
baudhāyanaḥ |
nābher adhaḥ saṃsparśaṃ karmayukto varjayet | ūrdhvaṃ vai puruṣasya nābher medhyam āvācīnam amedhyam iti śrutiḥ |
viṣṇu-yājñavalkyau |
ajāśvaṃ mukhato medhyaṃ na gaur na narajā malāḥ |
uśanāḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ nābheḥ puruṣaḥ, gaur medhyaḥ pṛṣṭhe, purastād ajāḥ, striyaḥ sarvato hṛdayam āsām aśūci |
yamaḥ |
medhyā rahogatā nārī strīmukheṣu ca vāruṇī |
sparśane na tu duṣyanti vāto gandharasāḥ striyaḥ ||
strīṇāṃ mukharasaś caiva gandho niśvāsa eva ca |
mukhato gaur amedhyā syān medhyo ’jo mukhataḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pṛṣṭhato gaur gajaḥ skandhe sarvato ’śvaḥ śucis tathā |
nṝṇāṃ mūtrapurīṣe tu amedhye mala eva ca ||
goḥ purīṣaṃ ca mūtraṃ ca nityaṃ medhyam iti sthitiḥ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pādau śucau brāhmaṇānām ajāśvasya mukhaṃ śuci |
gavāṃ pṛṣṭhāni medhyāni sarvagātrāṇi yoṣitām ||
romodbhede śaśī bhuṅkte gandharvaḥ kucadarśane |
analas tu rajo bhuṅkte striyo medhyās tu nānyathā ||
[844]        balātkāropabhuktā vā caurahastagatāpi vā |
svayaṃ vipratipannā vā atha vā vipramāditā ||
atyantadūṣitāpi strī na parityāgam arhati |
sarveṣāṃ niṣkṛtiḥ proktā nārīṇāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ ||
striyaḥ pavitram atulaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhicit |
māsi māsi rajas tāsāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati ||
somaḥ śaucaṃ dadāv āsāṃ gandharvāś ca śubhāṃ giram |
pāvakaḥ sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ medhyā vai sarvato hy ataḥ ||
yamaḥ |
brāhmaṇāḥ pādato medhyā gāvo medhyās tu pṛṣṭhataḥ |
ajāśvaṃ mukhato medhyaṃ striyo medhyās tu sarvataḥ ||
trīṇy āhuḥ pātakāny āsāṃ strīṇāṃ sarvavido janāḥ |
bhartur vadho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanam ||
yāni kānicid anyāni kurvanti bhuvi yoṣitaḥ |
sarvāṇy āsāṃ svakarmaṇi devair dattavarāḥ striyaḥ ||
pūrvaṃ nāryaḥ surair bhuktāḥ somagandharvavahnibhiḥ |
taiś ca dattavarā hy etā naiva duṣyanti karhicit ||
somas tāsāṃ dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram |
hutāśaḥ sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ tena niṣkasamāḥ striyaḥ ||
yamātrī |
[845]        na strī duṣyati jāreṇa nāgnir dahanakarmaṇā |
nāpo mūtrapurīṣābhyāṃ na dvijo vedakarmaṇā ||
gāvo na duṣyanty amṛtaṃ vahatyaḥ
striyo na duṣyanti paropabhuktāḥ |
āpo na duṣyanti mahītalasthāḥ
viprā na duṣyanti pratigraheṇa ||
vaśiṣṭha-baudhāyanau |
na strī duṣyati jāreṇa na vipro vedakarmaṇā |
svayaṃ vipratipannā vā yadi vā vipramāditā ||
balātkāropabhuktā vā caurahastagatāpi vā |
na tyajed dūṣitāṃ nārīṃ nāsyās tyāgo vidhīyate ||
puṣpakālam upāsīnā ṛtukālena śudhyati |
striyaḥ pavitram atulaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhicit |
māsi māsi rajo hy āsāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati ||
pūrvaṃ striyaḥ surair bhuktāḥ somagandharvavahnibhiḥ |
taiś ca dattavarā hy etā naiva duṣyanti karhicit ||
tāsāṃ somo dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram |
agniś ca sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ tasmāt niṣkasamāḥ striyaḥ ||
trīṇi striyaḥ pātakāni loke dharmavido viduḥ |
bhartur vadho bhrūṇahatyā svasya garbhasya pātanam ||
atriḥ |
balātkāropabhuktā vā caurahastagatāpi vā |
[846]        śudhyeta rajasā nārī nadī vegavatī yathā ||
svayaṃ vipratipannā vā atha vā vipramāditā |
atyantadūṣitā nārī nāsyās tyāgo vidhīyate ||
striyaḥ pavitram atukhaṃ naitā duṣyanti karhicit |
māsi māsi rajeo hy āṣāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati ||
pūrvaṃ striyaḥ surair bhuktāḥ somagandharvavahnibhiḥ |
somas tāsāṃ dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram |
pāvakaḥ sarvamedhyatvaṃ tasmān niṣkasamāḥ striyaḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
somaḥ śaucaṃ dadau strīṇāṃ gandharvaś ca śubhāṃ giram |
pāvakaḥ sarvamedhyatvaṃ medhyā vai yoṣito hy ataḥ ||
vyabhicārād ṛtau śuddhir garbhe tyāgo vidhīyate |
bhartṛgarbhavadhādau ca tathā mahati pātake ||
stryadhikāre paiṭhīnasiḥ,
tṛīṇi pātakāni bhartṛvadho brahmavadhaḥ svagarbhapātanaṃ ceti | ṛtau māsi māsi śudhyantīty āha |
māsi māsi rajas tāsāṃ duṣkṛtāny apakarṣati |
puṣpakālam upāsitvā ṛtukālena śudhyati ||
somas tāsāṃ dadau śaucaṃ gandharvaḥ śikṣitāṃ giram |
agniś ca sarvabhakṣyatvaṃ tasmān niṣkasamāḥ striyaḥ ||
yamaḥ |
balātkāropabhuktā vā caurahastagatā ca yā |
